### [1] [IceCube Takes Flight with Pelican - A First Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31851)
*David Schultz,Maclean Mansfield-Parisi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: IceCube中微子天文台将数据传输从GridFTP和x.509证书认证迁移到Pelican平台、Open Science Data Federation与WLCG令牌，并通过自定义令牌签发器和HTCondor改造实现与现有POSIX存储和权限体系的兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有GridFTP与x.509证书认证方式逐渐过时，而通用云计算基础设施已普遍转向Pelican、OSDF和令牌认证；但直接套用现成方案无法适配IceCube既有的数据存储结构与权限模型，也难以让科学家便捷使用，因此需要定制化迁移。

Method: 编写自定义WLCG令牌签发器，以支持带有自定义用户与组权限的POSIX文件系统；对HTCondor进行多项修改（包括自定义credmon），以便利基于令牌的作业提交；整体迁移到Pelican平台与Open Science Data Federation。

Result: 迁移初期因若干Pelican问题而经历波折，但这些问题随后得到解决；基于Pelican的系统已在多个方面证明优于GridFTP。

Conclusion: 在保留既有存储与权限结构的前提下，通过自定义令牌签发与HTCondor集成，IceCube成功完成向Pelican/OSDF/WLCG令牌体系的迁移，并取得优于GridFTP的实际效果。

Abstract: The IceCube Neutrino Observatory has removed GridFTP and x.509 certificate authentication for data transfers, migrating to the Pelican Platform, the Open Science Data Federation, and WLGC tokens. While this is a common solution on the computing infrastructure we use, we required several customizations to work with our existing data storage structure and make it easier for scientists to use. We wrote a custom WLCG token issuer to support our POSIX filesystem with custom user and group permissions across the entire filesystem. We also made several modifications to HTCondor to ease job submission using tokens, including a custom credmon. After an initially bumpy transition due to several now-resolved Pelican issues, the Pelican-based system has already proven superior to GridFTP in several ways.

</details>


### [2] [Towards Simple Models of Complex SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32055)
*Robert Chang,Teng Jiang,Wonsup Yoon,Daehyeok Kim,Sam Kumar,George Varghese*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZRAM 模型，用平台图与程序图配对及三个指标评估 SmartNIC 上的放置方案，预测可行性与瓶颈，并以 DDoS 检测为例总结出七种设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云厂商将加密、遥测等处理卸载到 NIC，但异构 SmartNIC（如 BlueField-3 内含 eSwitch、DPA、ARM 核与固定功能加速器）难以编程和性能预测。设计者希望在写代码前就能低成本地知道哪些实现方式可行、瓶颈在哪，以及有哪些提升性能的设计模式。

Method: 提出 ZRAM 模型：将处理区域及其通道构成的平台图，与任务及其流量比例构成的程序图相结合；由设计者或编译器选择放置方案，把程序图映射到平台图；再从该映射直接计算 capability、roofline、capacity 三个指标，判定放置是否可行并指出瓶颈资源。

Result: 以 DDoS 检测器为主要案例，并简要验证决策树推理与 RDMA 遍历两个应用；提炼出七种 SmartNIC 编程设计模式，其中包括关键的 sifting 模式；模型可推广到 Intel IPU E2200 与 AMD Pensando Salina 400 等其他 SmartNIC。

Conclusion: ZRAM 为在异构 SmartNIC 上低成本、先于编码地评估设计方案与瓶颈提供了起点，并开启了编译器与硬件设计等新的研究方向。

Abstract: Cloud vendors push ambitious in-network processing (e.g., crypto, telemetry) onto the NIC to offload servers even as link rates climb to terabit speeds. Vendors have responded with heterogeneous SmartNICs. For example, NVIDIA BlueField-3 interposes---between the wire and the host CPUs---a line-rate eSwitch, a multithreaded Data-Path Accelerator, general-purpose ARM cores, and a sea of fixed-function accelerators. These devices are notoriously hard to program, and harder still to predict. Applications can be implemented in many ways, with each choice potentially hitting a different bottleneck. A designer ideally needs to know---cheaply, and before a line of code is written---feasible choices and their bottlenecks, and design patterns to improve performance.
  Our paper offers a starting point to answer these questions using what we call the ZRAM model. It pairs a platform graph of processing zones and their channels with a program graph of tasks and their traffic fractions. The application designer or a compiler chooses a placement that maps the program graph onto the platform graph. Three metrics computed directly from this mapping---capability, roofline, and capacity---score the placement, deciding its feasibility and naming the bottleneck resource. We use a DDoS detector as a primary case study, and briefly explore two other applications, decision-tree inference and RDMA traversal. We distill seven design patterns for programming SmartNICs including a key one we call sifting. ZRAM generalizes to other SmartNICs such as Intel IPU E2200 and AMD Pensando Salina 400, and opens a new research agenda that includes compilers and hardware design.

</details>


### [3] [Empowering Hybrid Attention Models on NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32114)
*Yinyuan Zhang,Daliang Xu,Xiaolong Huang,Wangsong Yin,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HA-NPU 是首个在不修改算法的前提下，让混合注意力 LLM 在边缘 NPU 上高效推理的系统，通过三级数据流重组实现最高 35.95 倍 LA 内核加速与 36.14 倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力模型（如 Qwen3.5、Kimi 系列）因内存与计算效率高，非常适合端侧推理，与边缘 NPU 形成协同优势。但在边缘 NPU 上直接执行这类模型无法获得预期收益：线性注意力层的访存效率低下与架构不匹配导致 prefill 阶段成为瓶颈。

Method: HA-NPU 不改动底层算法，而是在三个层级重组线性注意力组件的数据流：(1) 核心级——按 head 维度划分工作负载并融合相互依赖的算子，消除跨核全局内存访问；(2) 算子级——重排执行顺序以即时消费中间张量，大幅降低本地缓冲区压力；(3) 张量级——采用数据流感知的布局规划，最小化矩阵与向量处理单元间的转换开销。

Result: 相比竞争基线，HA-NPU 实现最高 35.95 倍线性注意力内核加速、36.14 倍能耗降低，端到端请求延迟最高提升 2.03 倍。源码将在 GitHub 公开。

Conclusion: HA-NPU 证明无需算法改动即可在边缘 NPU 上高效运行混合注意力 LLM，为端侧 LLM 推理提供了可行的系统级优化路径。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models have emerged as a crucial architecture for Large Language Models (LLMs) (e.g., the Qwen3.5 and Kimi series). Their memory and computational efficiency make them highly attractive for on-device inference, forming a promising synergy with edge Neural Processing Units (NPUs). However, naive execution of these hybrid models on edge NPUs fails to deliver these benefits, often bottlenecking the prefill stage due to severe memory-system inefficiencies and architectural mismatches within the linear attention (LA) layers. We present HA-NPU, the first system to enable efficient hybrid attention LLM inference on edge NPUs without modifying the underlying algorithms. HA-NPU enhances execution efficiency by reorganizing the dataflow of the LA components across three levels: (1) At the core level, it partitions workloads by the head dimension and fuses dependent operators, eliminating cross-core global memory accesses; (2) At the operator level, it reorders execution to consume intermediate tensors immediately, drastically minimizing local-buffer pressure; (3) At the tensor level, it employs dataflow-aware layout planning to minimize transformation overhead between matrix and vector processing units. Compared to competitive baselines, HA-NPU achieves up to 35.95$\times$ LA kernel speedup and 36.14$\times$ energy reduction, delivering up to 2.03$\times$ faster end-to-end request latency. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yinyuanzhang/HA-NPU

</details>


### [4] [REBASE: Device-Cloud Experience Coherence for GUI Agents Across App Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32130)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang,Yanxiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: REBASE 是一种让手机 GUI agent 的经验在应用更新后仍保持可用的协议，通过在云端重放经验、设备端逐步验证，并仅在失败时以递增证据量请求云端修复，且修复以版本为键服务于整个设备群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机上的 GUI agent 会复用此前记录的动作路径及云端缓存经验。但应用更新后部分经验会悄然失效，导致 agent 成功率下降且无法及时察觉不匹配：在九组真实应用版本对上，携带旧版本经验的 agent 比无经验者成功率低 10.1 个百分点，且要在执行错误步骤后 3 步才察觉。

Method: 提出 REBASE 协议，保持设备端与云端经验副本在版本更新后的—致性：版本变化时，云端在新版本上重放其副本，设备端在执行每个记录步骤前逐一验证；若某步仍失败，设备端按递增证据量向云端发送证据，从无障碍子树差分开始，直到云端重新推导出可执行的补丁，并以版本为键，使一次修复可服务整个设备群。

Result: 在两台 Jetson 设备上，REBASE 在一个 episode 内将陈旧经验的成功率恢复到与基于新版本记录的—新鲜经验—相同水平，相比向云端上传截图，字节数减少 708 倍、云端调用减少 47.3 倍，设备能耗比无经验运行时低 2.5 倍。

Conclusion: REBASE 能有效修复应用更新导致的经验失效问题，以极低的通信与能耗代价，在单次 episode 内恢复经验可用性，并借助版本键控的云端修复实现设备群范围的复用。

Abstract: A graphical user interface (GUI) agent that ships on a phone runs a small model and reuses experience: action paths recorded on earlier runs and cached from the cloud. When an app updates, part of this experience becomes silently wrong. On nine real app version pairs, an agent carrying experience recorded on the old version succeeds 10.1 points less often than one carrying none, and it does not notice the mismatch until 3 steps after acting on it. We propose REBASE, a protocol that keeps the device copy and the cloud copy of the experience coherent across updates. When a version changes, the cloud replays its copy on the new version and the device verifies each recorded step before executing it; when a step still fails, the device sends the cloud evidence in increasing size, starting from an accessibility-subtree diff, until the cloud re-derives an executable patch, keyed by version so that one repair serves the whole fleet. On two Jetson devices, REBASE restores the success rate of stale experience to that of fresh experience recorded on the new version within one episode, at 708x fewer bytes and 47.3x fewer cloud calls than uploading screenshots to the cloud, and 2.5x less device energy than running without experience.

</details>


### [5] [ThreadShift: Transparent Thread-Level Offloading on Transient Cloud Resources Using MPKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32153)
*Antoine Murat,Clément Burgelin,Rachid Guerraoui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ThreadShift 是一个系统，能够透明地将未修改 Linux 应用中的计算密集型线程卸载到廉价的瞬时云资源上，并在资源被回收时保持正确性。它通过线程粒度的细粒度检查点，利用 Memory Protection Keys 高效追踪内存依赖，实现快速增量检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬时云资源可显著节省成本，但其不可预测性（随时可能被回收）使其难以用于无法安全重启的应用。现有方法要么需要修改应用，要么依赖粗粒度检查点，其开销限制了收益。

Method: ThreadShift 在未修改的 Linux 应用上透明运行，将计算密集型线程卸载到瞬时资源。核心挑战是在存在跨线程依赖的情况下对单个线程进行检查点。它通过高效追踪内存依赖、识别每线程内存写操作、并在机器间维护一致性来实现。三项对 Memory Protection Keys (MPKs) 的新颖用法使这些机制高效，从而支持快速增量检查点而无需暂停整个应用。

Result: 在 x86-64 Linux 上实现并在机器学习推理、密码学任务和内存数据处理等工作负载上评估。ThreadShift 卸载线程最快仅需 164 微秒，检查点速度比 CRIU 快最多三个数量级，在通用云上将部署成本降低最多 56%。

Conclusion: 通过在未修改应用中实现线程粒度卸载与快速增量检查点，ThreadShift 使应用能够安全利用廉价瞬时云资源，兼顾正确性与成本收益。

Abstract: Transient cloud resources offer significant cost savings, but their unpredictability makes them hard to use for applications that cannot be safely restarted after reclamation. Existing approaches require application changes or rely on coarse-grained checkpointing, whose cost limits its benefits.
  We present ThreadShift, a system that transparently offloads compute-intensive threads of unmodified Linux applications to cheap transient resources while preserving correctness under reclamation. By operating at thread granularity, ThreadShift enables fast, fine-grained checkpointing and offloads only the threads that benefit from transient execution.
  The main challenge is to checkpoint individual threads despite cross-thread dependencies. ThreadShift does so by efficiently tracking memory dependencies, identifying per-thread memory writes, and maintaining coherence across machines. Three novel uses of Memory Protection Keys (MPKs) make these mechanisms efficient, enabling fast incremental checkpoints without pausing the entire application.
  We implement ThreadShift on x86-64 Linux and evaluate it on workloads including machine-learning inference, cryptographic tasks, and in-memory data processing. ThreadShift offloads threads in as little as 164 us, performs checkpointing up to three orders of magnitude faster than CRIU, and reduces deployment cost by up to 56% on commodity clouds.

</details>


### [6] [SparSP: Exploiting Communication Sparsity for Sequence-Parallel Video DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32197)
*Desen Sun,Xinrui Zhong,Yuke Wang,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 SparSP，一种面向稀疏视频扩散模型的稀疏序列并行通信系统，将注意力稀疏性视为通信原语，通过三项协同设计在多 GPU 服务器上提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型计算开销巨大，需要在多 GPU 服务器上扩展推理。但在 PCIe 连接的商品级 GPU 上带宽有限，扩展效率难以提升。稀疏注意力虽能显著降低计算量，但其对通信的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: SparSP 共设计 token 分布、通信路由与异步执行三部分：1）依赖感知放置（Dependency-Aware Placement）依据稀疏注意力模式分配序列块；2）需求导向 KV 路由（Demand-Directed KV Routing）将 KV 块直接传输到请求 GPU，避免中间转发；3）解耦传输运行时（Decoupled Transfer Runtime）将通信与 GPU 计算分离，减少资源争用并提升有效带宽。

Result: 注意力性能提升 1.38–1.5×；在三种代表性服务器和三种视频扩散模型上实现平均 1.17×（最高 1.69×）端到端加速；通信量降低 12.54–23.05%；相比 NCCL 平均带宽提升 1.53–1.76×。

Conclusion: 将注意力稀疏性作为通信原语进行协同设计，可有效缓解 PCIe 带宽瓶颈，显著提升稀疏视频扩散模型的多 GPU 推理效率与带宽利用率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become the dominant architecture for video generation. Their substantial computational cost motivates scaling inference across multi-GPU servers, yet efficient scaling remains challenging on commodity GPUs connected via PCIe, whose bandwidth is limited. Although sparse attention substantially reduces computation, its implications for communication remain underexplored.
  This paper argues that attention sparsity should be treated as a communication primitive. We present SparSP, an efficient sparse sequence parallel communication system that co-designs token distribution, communication routing, and asynchronous execution for sparse video diffusion models. First, Dependency-Aware Placement distributes sequence blocks according to diffusion models' sparse attention patterns. Second, Demand-Directed KV Routing transfers KV blocks directly to requesting GPUs without intermediate relays. Third, a Decoupled Transfer Runtime separates communication from GPU computation to reduce resource contention and maximize effective bandwidth. Our evaluation shows that SparSP improves attention performance by 1.38- 1.5$\times$, achieves an average 1.17$\times$ (up to 1.69$\times$) end-to-end speedup across three representative servers and three video diffusion models, and reduces communication volume by 12.54-23.05%. Moreover, we achieve an average 1.53-1.76$\times$ bandwidth improvement over NCCL.

</details>


### [7] [No-Restart Elasticity in an Adaptive Runtime System for Cloud-Native HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32217)
*Aditya Bhosale,Laxmikant Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重启的Charm++运行时弹性伸缩机制，通过保留存活进程将伸缩开销降至毫秒级，并消除GPU弹性对检查点守护进程和监督式进程管理器的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用打折的spot实例运行HPC应用需要在运行时动态调整资源分配（中断前收缩、在替换容量上扩展）。现有弹性机制把伸缩实现为“完全拆除进程 + 在新处理器数上冷启动”，重启阶段占总开销高达95%，且随节点数增长，在GPU上因CUDA上下文初始化而显著更高。因此需要一种避免重启的伸缩方法。

Method: 为Charm++运行时设计无重启伸缩机制：存活进程永不退出；一次伸缩操作由三部分组成——与轻量级外部协调者进行成员关系协商、将UCX通信端点与新集群视图进行协调（reconciliation）、以及返回到运行时初始化路径顶部并保留活跃应用状态。同时提供启动器无关的bootstrap机制以摆脱监督式进程管理器。

Result: 在4–32个实例规模下，单次伸缩操作开销（不含任何伸缩模型都需要的负载均衡步骤）从数秒降至CPU上8–15ms、GPU上7–11ms。由于进程存活，GPU设备状态原地保留，无需GPU弹性所需的检查点守护进程。与现有spot实例管理框架集成后，八次同时中断的端到端开销降至CPU运行时的0.2%、GPU的0.6%；使作业能以低于1%开销进行伸缩的速率提升7倍。

Conclusion: 无重启伸缩通过保留存活进程与原地保留GPU状态，大幅降低spot实例HPC弹性开销，并去除对检查点守护进程和监督式进程管理器的依赖，使频繁、低开销的作业伸缩成为可能。

Abstract: Exploiting discounted spot instances for HPC requires an application to change its resource allocation at runtime, shrinking ahead of an interruption and expanding onto replacement capacity. Existing elasticity mechanisms implement rescaling as a full process teardown followed by a cold restart at the new processor count, and the restart stage accounts for up to 95% of the total overhead, growing with node count and increasing substantially on GPUs due to CUDA context initialization. In this paper, we present a no-restart rescaling mechanism for the Charm++ runtime system in which surviving processes never exit: a rescaling operation consists of a membership negotiation with a lightweight external coordinator, reconciliation of the UCX communication endpoints with the new cluster view, and a return to the top of the runtime initialization path that preserves live application state. This reduces the cost of a rescaling operation, excluding the load balancing step that any rescaling model requires, from multiple seconds to 8--15ms on CPUs and 7--11ms on GPUs, at 4 to 32 instances. Because processes survive, GPU device state persists in place, eliminating the checkpointing daemons previously required for GPU elasticity, and a launcher-independent bootstrap mechanism removes the dependence on supervised process managers, which are incompatible with spot instance interruptions. Integrated with an existing spot instance management framework, the mechanism cuts the end-to-end overhead of eight simultaneous interruptions to 0.2% of runtime on CPUs and 0.6% on GPUs, and raises the rate at which a job can be rescaled below 1% overhead by a factor of seven.

</details>


### [8] [RR-Evict: Fine-Grained Prefix Cache Eviction beyond LRU for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32278)
*Zaifeng Pan,Chris Wu,Zhengding Hu,Xinwei Qiang,Zhongkai Yu,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RR-EVICT，一种细粒度前缀缓存淘汰策略，通过轮询方式在各空闲agent轨迹间分散淘汰，减少返回agent的TTFT尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent长时任务中，前缀缓存虽避免重复prefill，但缓存占用随上下文长度和并发增长。作者发现"近因同步"现象：对某agent缓存历史的一次访问会同时刷新其所有缓存节点，导致LRU淘汰集中在少数agent的私有历史上。当某agent被完全淘汰后返回时，需重算几乎全部上下文，造成TTFT巨大离群值。

Method: RR-EVICT按轮询顺序访问各agent，从每个agent尾部淘汰一个chunk，从而在容量有限时保留更多agent的可复用前缀。返回的agent可复用部分历史，只需重算较小的缺失后缀。该策略无需预测未来到达或工具延迟。

Result: 在SGLang中实现RR-EVICT，并在colocated及prefill-decode-disaggregated服务下评估对话与编码agent负载。相比LRU，P99 TTFT最多降低75.4%，P99未缓存prompt token最多降低65.7%。

Conclusion: RR-EVICT通过细粒度、跨agent均匀分散的缓存淘汰，有效缓解了近因同步导致的TTFT离群问题，显著提升agent长任务服务的尾部延迟和缓存复用效率，且不依赖未来预测。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute long-horizon tasks through repeated model calls interleaved with tool execution and user interaction. As each call extends the history accumulated in previous turns, prefix caching avoids repeated prefill of the agent's entire context. However, the aggregate cache footprint grows with context length and concurrency, forcing serving systems to reclaim cached KV tensors. We identify recency synchronization: accesses to an agent's cached history refresh its cache nodes together, causing least recently used (LRU) eviction to concentrate on a few agents' private histories. When a fully evicted agent returns, it must recompute nearly its entire accumulated context, producing a large time-to-first-token (TTFT) outlier even if most other requests retain substantial cache reuse. We present RR-EVICT, a fine-grained prefix-cache eviction strategy that distributes reclamation across idle agent trajectories. RR-EVICT visits agents in round-robin order and evicts a tail chunk from each, preserving reusable prefixes for more agents when capacity remains for idle state. Returning agents can reuse these partial histories and recompute smaller missing suffixes. The policy requires no prediction of future arrivals or tool latency. We implement RR-EVICT in SGLang and evaluate conversational and coding-agent workloads under colocated and prefill-decode-disaggregated serving. Compared with LRU, RR-EVICT reduces P99 TTFT by up to 75.4% and P99 uncached prompt tokens by up to 65.7%.

</details>


### [9] [AgentReplay: Token-Wise Trace Replay Is Essential for Fair Serving System Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32283)
*Zaifeng Pan,Michael Wang,Chris Wu,Zhengding Hu,Xinwei Qiang,Zhongkai Yu,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 AgentReplay 框架，通过记录并重放 token、专家选择、请求依赖和工具耗时，消除 LLM 智能体服务评测中的工作负载变异，实现公平的性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需执行模型推理与工具调用交错的多轮工作流，其服务效率评测困难：相同任务会产生不同执行轨迹，生成 token 变化会改变后续提示、工具调用与推理轮次，难以区分系统优化与工作负载波动。贪心解码无法保证输出完全一致，仅重放序列长度又会丢失影响前缀缓存与 MoE 路由的 token 信息。

Method: 提出 AgentReplay，一个可配置的智能体服务轨迹记录与重放框架。记录输入/输出 token、专家选择、请求依赖关系和工具时延；重放时强制使用记录的输出 token 并正常执行自回归计算，可选控制 MoE 专家选择与工具延迟。不同系统可在保持各自批处理、调度和并行策略的前提下执行同一记录负载，并将轨迹生成与性能评测分离，支持较小模型重放较强模型采集的长程轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，AgentReplay 的逐 token 重放能有效消除贪心解码与逐长度重放无法避免的工作负载变异，从而在不同服务配置之间实现更公平的性能比较。

Conclusion: AgentReplay 为智能体服务提供了可控、可复现的负载重放方案，解决了轨迹变异带来的评测不公问题。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-turn workflows with interleaved model inference and tool calls, making efficient serving increasingly important. However, evaluating serving optimizations is challenging because identical tasks can produce different execution trajectories. Changes in generated tokens can alter subsequent prompts, tool calls, and reasoning turns, making it difficult to distinguish system improvements from workload variation. Greedy decoding does not guarantee identical outputs, while replaying only sequence lengths loses token information that affects prefix caching and mixture-of-experts (MoE) routing. To address these problems, we propose AgentReplay, a configurable trace record-and-replay framework for agent serving. AgentReplay records input/output tokens, expert selections, request dependencies, and tool durations. During replay, it forces the recorded output tokens while performing normal autoregressive computation, with optional controls for MoE expert selection and tool delays. This allows different systems to execute the same recorded workload while retaining their own batching, scheduling, and parallelization decisions. We further separate trajectory generation from performance evaluation, enabling compatible smaller models to replay long-horizon traces collected with more capable models. Our experiments show that token-wise replay in AgentReplay effectively eliminates workload variation that greedy decoding and length-wise replay cannot avoid, enabling fairer performance comparisons across serving configurations.

</details>


### [10] [ASCEND: Personal AI Agents for Autonomous Scientific Computing Across HPC Clusters and GPU Workstations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32868)
*J. Paul Liu,Uthpala Herath,Andrew Petersen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ASCEND是一个AI智能体接口，将智能体运行在研究者本地电脑上，通过多路复用认证连接访问Slurm集群和GPU工作站，无需设施级服务，仅需各资源账号。四个案例验证了其在故障恢复、结果复现、代码并行化和漏洞发现方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学计算要求研究者将计算意图转化为环境配置、资源请求和可执行作业，再从调度器状态和应用日志中诊断失败，流程繁琐且门槛高。作者希望构建一种AI驱动的智能体接口来降低这一复杂度，同时保证凭证安全（语言模型远程托管且不持有凭证）。

Method: ASCEND将智能体部署在研究者本地笔记本电脑上，通过多路复用的认证连接访问Slurm管理的集群和GPU工作站；站点特定的执行策略由本地执行的工具进行检查；语言模型托管在远程且不持有凭证。系统不需要设施级服务，仅需每个资源的账号，并可通过公共安装器链接额外的Slurm集群或工作站。通过四个录制案例进行评估，另有针对策略层的预设定评估。

Result: 案例1：智能体在人为植入的张量设备故障上闭合了失败恢复循环，完成提交、诊断、修复和重新提交并保留作业级产物。案例2：从作者发布的预报中复现了天气预测模型的已发表评估，与已发表曲线在z500上一致度达2.1%、t850达2.4%，同时发现论文正文中的单位差异和发布数据中的初始化场差异。案例3：在逐位一致要求下对12,693行地球物理求解器进行并行化，将墙钟运行时间从约12小时减至约2小时。案例4：该逐位一致要求暴露了已发布求解器中两处未定义行为，均已修复并上报上游。策略层评估中，部署的验证器拒绝了30个构造违规中的29个，剩余1个保持待批准状态，同时拒绝了14个合法请求中的3个。

Conclusion: ASCEND展示了无需设施级服务、凭证安全的AI科研计算智能体在故障恢复、结果复现、代码并行化与漏洞发现方面的实用价值。自主性在作者监督下行使，端到端恢复基准测试仍待完成。

Abstract: Traditional scientific computing requires researchers to translate computational intent into environment configuration, resource requests, and executable jobs, then diagnose failures from scheduler state and application logs. We present ASCEND (Autonomous Scientific Computing Engine and Novel Discovery), an AI-powered agent interface that runs the agent on the researcher's own laptop, reaching Slurm-managed clusters and a GPU workstation over a multiplexed authenticated connection, with site-specific execution policies checked by locally executed tools; the language model is hosted remotely and holds no credentials. No facility-scale service is required: an account on each resource is sufficient, and the public installer lets users link additional Slurm clusters or workstations of their own. We report four recorded cases: (1) the agent closed a failure-recovery loop on a planted tensor-device fault, submitting, diagnosing, repairing and resubmitting with job-level artifacts preserved; (2) it reproduced the published evaluation of a weather-forecasting model from the author's released forecasts, agreeing with the published curves to 2.1% (z500) and 2.4% (t850) while identifying a unit discrepancy in the paper's prose and an initialization-field discrepancy in its released data; (3) it parallelized a released 12,693-line geophysical solver under a bit-for-bit identity requirement, reducing wall-clock runtime from about twelve hours to about two; (4) that requirement exposed two instances of undefined behaviour in the published solver, both repaired and reported upstream. Separately, a pre-specified evaluation of the policy layer found the deployed validator rejected 29 of 30 constructed violations and held the remaining one for approval, while denying 3 of 14 legitimate requests. Autonomy was exercised under author supervision; an end-to-end recovery benchmark remains outstanding.

</details>


### [11] [Time Semantics and Liveness Artifacts in Adversarial Consensus Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32972)
*Nasit S Sony,Xianzhong Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性共识模拟器中时间建模对超时敏感协议的影响，比较EventCoupled与RoundTick两种计时模型，发现EventCoupled将调度器活动与协议时间耦合会导致调度行为影响故障检测器超时，从而人为抬高领导者不稳定率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性共识模拟器不仅需要消息投递模型，还需要时间模型。对于超时敏感协议，若将协议时间推进与调度器活动耦合，通信调度可能影响故障检测器的超时时间，进而扭曲模拟结果的真实性。

Method: 在VeriProtocol平台上，使用受控的Multi-Paxos和Raft实验。主要Multi-Paxos研究对比EventCoupled计时（每次调度机会都推进协议时间）与RoundTick计时（将单个调度事件与超时推进解耦）。通过匹配轨迹分析和有针对性的心跳延迟控制实验来识别机制。

Result: 在N=5时，将有限消息延迟预算从K=6增加到K=14，EventCoupled的领导者不稳定率从5%升至100%；而对应的140次RoundTick执行中未出现额外有效领导者选举。K=0时两种模型均未产生额外选举。匹配轨迹分析表明：EventCoupled下扰动通信的调度活动也直接推进故障检测器状态；RoundTick下类似调度活动不意味着等价的协议时间推进。心跳延迟控制实验证实RoundTick并未抑制超时驱动的选举。

Conclusion: 时间建模方式会显著影响对抗性共识模拟中观察到的协议行为。EventCoupled计时会产生调度活动与超时机制的虚假耦合，导致夸大领导者不稳定率；RoundTick解耦了调度与协议时间，更真实地反映超时驱动的选举行为。

Abstract: Adversarial consensus simulators require not only a model of message delivery but also a model of time. For timeout-sensitive protocols, coupling protocol-time progression to scheduler activity can allow communication scheduling to influence when failure detectors expire.
  We study this interaction in VeriProtocol using controlled Multi-Paxos and Raft experiments. In the primary Multi-Paxos study, we compare EventCoupled timing, where every scheduler opportunity advances protocol time, with RoundTick timing, which decouples individual scheduler events from timeout progression. At N=5, increasing the bounded message-delay budget from K=6 to K=14 increased the observed EventCoupled leadership-instability rate from 5\% to 100\% across the tested grid, while no additional valid leader elections occurred in any of the corresponding 140 RoundTick executions. At K=0, neither model produced additional elections.
  Matched-trace analysis identifies the mechanism: under EventCoupled timing, scheduler activity used to perturb communication also directly advances failure-detector state. Under RoundTick, comparable scheduler activity does not imply equivalent protocol-time progression. A targeted heartbeat-delay control confirms that RoundTick does not suppress timeout-driven elections; an additional election remains reachable when leader contact is unavailable across sufficient logical time.

</details>


### [12] [Simple and Fast Signature-Free Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32985)
*Giuliano Losa,Xuechao Wang,Zhuolun Xiang,Qianyu Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出两个免签名区块链共识协议，实现最优好情况提交延迟、乐观响应性、两消息延迟区块时间及每视图O(n^2)通信，并实例化Generic Simplex构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免签名协议可避免后量子签名的高昂成本。论文旨在设计兼具高效率（低延迟、乐观响应性）与低通信复杂度（O(n^2)）的免签名区块链共识协议。

Method: 提出Generic Simplex——一种由新抽象"view agreement（视图一致性）"参数化的区块链共识构造。基于该构造实例化两个最终同步的免签名协议，分别针对f<n/3（三消息延迟）和f<n/5（两消息延迟）的容错阈值。

Result: 两个协议均达到最优好情况提交延迟、乐观响应性、无推测下仅两消息延迟的区块时间，以及每视图O(n^2)通信。同一构造还涵盖Simplex、Minimmit，以及一个新的同步免签名协议（f<n/4时两消息延迟最优提交）。

Conclusion: Generic Simplex为免签名区块链共识提供统一构造框架，通过view agreement抽象兼容多种协议（含Simplex、Minimmit及新同步协议），在延迟与通信上达到最优。

Abstract: Signature-free protocols avoid the cost of post-quantum signatures. We present two simple signature-free blockchain consensus protocols for eventual synchrony with optimal good-case commit latency (three message delays for \(f<n/3\) and two for \(f<n/5\)), optimistic responsiveness, a block time of only two message delays without speculation, and \(O(n^2)\) communication per view. They instantiate Generic Simplex, a blockchain consensus construction parameterized by a new abstraction called view agreement. The same construction also captures Simplex, Minimmit, and a new synchronous signature-free protocol with optimal good-case commit latency of two message delays for \(f<n/4\).

</details>


### [13] [Tessera: Demand-Driven KV Cache Management for Retrieval-Augmented LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32999)
*Fei Fang,Chung-Hsiang Lo,Yi Liu,Yifan Hua,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Tessera 是一个分离式服务系统，将检索作为 KV 复用的控制平面，通过协调缓存管理与请求路由，在 RAG 和智能体记忆工作负载中显著降低首 token 时延（TTFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 与基于检索的智能体记忆都会把检索内容注入 LLM 提示，但相同内容可能在不同提示位置或不同前文下反复出现，无法通过常规前缀缓存复用。作者的表征分析发现，在智能体记忆工作负载中，超出匹配前缀的重复记录占注入记忆 token 的 70% 以上。可组合 KV 复用能应对这类情况，但在线服务带来管理难题：重复单元的 KV 状态可能尚未存在、已被驱逐或位于其他节点。

Method: 提出 Tessera，一种分离式服务系统，让检索成为 KV 复用的控制平面。由于检索能在模型执行前暴露所需的上下文单元，Tessera 得以结合当前需求、检索历史、KV 驻留位置和生成负载来协调缓存管理与请求路由。生成节点并发准备本地缓存、远程缓存和缺失的状态，同时将新计算的状态保留在请求关键路径之外。

Result: 在 RAG 和智能体记忆工作负载中，与 SGLang 以及使用 EPIC 的 LMCache 相比，在相同请求速率下平均 TTFT 最高降低 3.6 倍；在基线已饱和的请求速率下仍能维持较低 TTFT，同时答案质量与底层组合策略相当。

Conclusion: 通过把检索用作 KV 复用的控制平面，Tessera 有效解决了在线服务中可组合 KV 复用的状态管理问题，在保持答案质量的同时大幅提升服务性能与可扩展性。

Abstract: RAG and retrieval-based agent memory both inject retrieved content into LLM prompts, as document chunks and recalled memory records, respectively. The same content can recur across requests at different prompt positions or after different preceding contexts, preventing reuse through conventional prefix caching. Our characterization finds that records recurring outside the matching prefix account for over 70% of injected memory tokens in agent-memory workloads. Composable KV-reuse methods enable reuse in such cases, but online serving introduces a management problem: a recurring unit's KV states may not yet exist, may have been evicted, or may reside on another node. We present Tessera, a disaggregated serving system that makes retrieval the control plane for KV reuse. By exposing the context units needed before model execution, retrieval allows Tessera to combine current demand with retrieval history, KV residency, and generation load to coordinate cache management and request routing. Generation nodes concurrently prepare locally cached, remotely cached, and missing states, while retaining newly computed states off the request's critical path. Across RAG and agent-memory workloads, Tessera lowers mean TTFT by up to 3.6x over SGLang and LMCache with EPIC at matched request rates, and sustains low TTFT at rates where the baselines saturate, while matching the answer quality of the underlying composition policy.

</details>


### [14] [Hierarchical Secure Distributed Linearly Separable Computation with Arbitrary Heterogeneous Data Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33092)
*Ziting Zhang,Chenyi Sun,Kai Wan,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究三层分层网络中的安全分布式线性可分离计算，提出支持用户掉线与合谋的安全方案，在部分区域达到最优双层通信速率，其余区域达到2倍阶最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三层分层网络中，用户经中继与中心服务器通信，且数据分配任意异构，需同时防范服务器与中继获取用户输入信息，并应对服务器/中继与用户子集合谋，因此需要设计兼顾安全与通信效率的编码方案。

Method: 针对任意异构数据分配，每个用户计算所持数据集的中间结果并向关联中继发送掩码消息，中继处理后转发给服务器；设置两类安全约束（对服务器安全、对中继安全），并考虑服务器或中继与部分用户合谋的情形，提出可容忍用户掉线与合谋的安全方案。

Result: 当 Kc=1（退化为分布式梯度编码）时，所提方案在一个区域达到最优双层通信速率，在另一区域达到因子2以内的阶最优通信速率。当 Kc>1 时，将构造扩展到无掉线设定下的多维线性可分离任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为三层分层网络下任意异构数据分配的安全分布式线性可分离计算提供了有效编码方案，并在通信速率上取得最优或阶最优保证，同时拓展到多维任务场景。

Abstract: This paper studies secure distributed linearly separable computation over a three-layer hierarchical network, where clustered users communicate with a central server through relays. The server aims to recover Kc linear combinations of K intermediate outcomes, where each intermediate outcome is a separable function of one dataset. We consider a more general setting with arbitrary heterogeneous data assignment across users, where ''arbitrary'' means that the data assignment is given in advance (which can be in any form) and ''heterogeneous'' means that the users may hold different numbers of datasets. Under this assignment, each user computes the intermediate outcomes of its assigned datasets and sends masked messages to its associated relay. The relays subsequently process and forward the received messages to the server. We impose two security constraints: (i) security against server, requiring the server to learn only the desired task function without gaining any additional information about users' inputs; and (ii) security against relays, ensuring each relay learns nothing about users' inputs. Moreover, the server or any relay may collude with a subset of users. For Kc=1, the underlying computation reduces to distributed gradient coding. We propose a secure scheme tolerating user dropouts and user collusion, achieving the optimal two-layer communication rates in one regime and order-optimal communication rates within a factor of 2 in the other regime. For Kc>1, we extend the proposed construction to multi-dimensional linearly separable tasks under the no-dropout setting.

</details>


### [15] [Splitting Prompt Prefill from Response Replay for Context-Parallel Long-Context LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33133)
*Yubing Bao,Zhihui Lu,Qiang Duan,Yuedong Xu,Sen Liu,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AugTree 是一种面向长上下文 LLM 强化学习训练中"重放阶段"的上下文并行执行方案，通过将重放拆分为 prompt-prefill 与 response-replay 两阶段并动态选择通信语义，在保持精确训练语义的同时显著加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 策略的强化学习训练需要在更新后的策略下重新评估成组采样响应，这形成了一种与预训练差异极大的"更新阶段注意力负载"：每个组共享一个长 prompt，并分叉出多条响应分支。标准上下文并行（CP）将每个 prompt-response 对展平为线性序列，导致同一 prompt 的 KV 状态在每个响应分支上被重复计算，或在网络中反复轮转，造成大量冗余开销。

Method: 作者提出 AugTree，一种围绕重放阶段构建的 CP 执行方案。它将重放分为两个阶段：(1) prompt-prefill 阶段，只计算一次共享 prompt 的 KV 状态；(2) response-replay 阶段，在一组有界重放通道（replay lanes）上调度相互独立的响应分支。重放阶段实例化两种通信语义，由一个轻量在线规划器在 GPU 派发前枚举 CP 度、调度与放置来决定：当响应占主导时，在响应本地通道内轮转 KV 分片；当 prompt 占主导时，将响应 query 移动到固定的 prompt-KV 持有者处并配合部分 softmax 归约。共享 prompt 状态保持完全可微，响应损失可反传至该状态，累积的 prompt 梯度沿原始 prefill 图传播，从而保持精确的训练语义，而非采用脱离式的推理风格 KV 缓存。

Result: 在四个真实后训练工作负载、多达 64 个加速器上，AugTree 将平均训练阶段步时间相比动态 CP 提升 1.18×（最高 2.23×），相比带 prompt 复用的 Megatron ring CP 基线提升 2.63×，相比不带复用的基线提升 7.08×。

Conclusion: AugTree 通过针对 RL 重放阶段分离 prompt 复用与响应调度，并在运行时自适应选择通信语义，有效解决了共享长 prompt 在标准 CP 下的 KV 重复计算问题，同时保持端到端可微的精确训练语义，在多种真实负载上取得了显著的训练加速。

Abstract: Training long-context LLM policies with RL requires re-evaluating groups of sampled responses under the updated policy, an update-stage attention workload that differs sharply from pre-training: each group shares one long prompt that fans out into multiple response branches. Standard context parallelism (CP) flattens each prompt--response pair into a linear sequence, so the same prompt key--value (KV) states are recomputed---or repeatedly rotated through the network---once per response branch. We present \textbf{AugTree}, a CP execution scheme built around this replay stage. AugTree separates the replay into two phases: a prompt-prefill phase that computes the shared prompt KV state once, and a response-replay phase that schedules the independent response branches over a bounded set of replay lanes. The replay phase instantiates two communication semantics, chosen by a lightweight online planner that enumerates CP degrees, schedules, and placements before GPU dispatch: rotating KV shards within response-local lanes when responses dominate, and moving response queries to stationary prompt-KV owners with a partial-softmax reduction when prompts dominate. The shared prompt state remains fully differentiable---response losses backpropagate into it and the accumulated prompt gradients propagate through the original prefill graph---so AugTree preserves exact training semantics rather than performing detached, inference-style KV caching. On four real post-training workloads and up to 64 accelerators, AugTree improves average training-stage step time by 1.18$\times$ over dynamic CP (up to 2.23$\times$), 2.63$\times$ over a Megatron ring CP baseline with prompt reuse, and 7.08$\times$ over the baseline without reuse.

</details>


### [16] [MpFA: Hardware-Efficient Train-Free QK4V8 FlashAttention Kernels on Blackwell GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33135)
*Chencheng Deng,Jianbin Fang,Dezun Dong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MpFA是一个面向Blackwell GPU的免训练FlashAttention内核，采用QK4PV8混合精度与秩一平滑补偿，在长上下文推理中显著提升吞吐并恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理使GPU服务栈进入注意力瓶颈阶段，算力与内存均被softmax-GEMM流水线主导。Blackwell的FP4 Tensor Core虽提供高矩阵乘吞吐，但全FP4注意力受限于非矩阵乘开销（softmax后在线量化、张量/共享内存数据搬运、softmax路径竞争），无法转化为端到端加速。

Method: 提出免训练的MpFA FlashAttention内核。基于硬件特征分析采用混合精度：QK用NVFP4、PV用FP8（QK4PV8），保留低比特QK吞吐同时避免FP4 PV的转换与缩放开销。引入秩一平滑补偿（以额外Tensor Core MMA实现）恢复精度而不加重softmax流水线。并通过细粒度异步流水线、张量内存复用和面向prefill/decode的自适应并行划分提升性能。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200、16K–128K上下文中，MpFA的prefill吞吐优于最先进BF16/FP8基线；在Llama-3.1-8B与Qwen3-14B上端到端输出吞吐相较BF16 FA4提升2.81倍。在五个基准套件与两个模型上，秩一补偿以约2.0%内核开销恢复62.5%的精度损失。

Conclusion: MpFA表明，在Blackwell上通过混合精度（QK4PV8）配合轻量秩一补偿与流水线优化，可有效绕开全FP4注意力的非矩阵乘瓶颈，在长上下文推理中同时实现显著加速与可接受的精度恢复，为低比特注意力内核设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference pushes modern GPU serving stacks into an attention-bound regime, where both compute and memory are dominated by the softmax-GEMM pipeline. On NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs, FP4 Tensor Cores offer high matmul throughput, but we find that fully FP4 attention often fails to translate this throughput into end-to-end speedups due to non-matmul costs: online quantization after softmax, tensor/shared-memory data movement, and contention on the softmax path. We present MpFA, a training-free FlashAttention kernel optimized for Blackwell. Guided by hardware characterization, MpFA uses mixed precision: NVFP4 for QK and FP8 for PV (QK4PV8). This preserves low-bit QK throughput while avoiding the conversion and scaling overheads of FP4 PV. To recover accuracy without further stressing the softmax pipeline, MpFA introduces rank-one smoothing compensation implemented as an additional Tensor Core MMA. MpFA further improves performance with a fine-grained asynchronous pipeline, tensor-memory reuse, and adaptive parallel partitioning across prefill and decode. On an NVIDIA B200 and across 16K-128K contexts, MpFA improves prefill throughput over state-of-the-art BF16/FP8 baselines and increases end-to-end output throughput by 2.81$\times$ over BF16 FA4 across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B. Across five benchmark suites and two models, rank-one compensation recovers 62.5% of the accuracy loss with about 2.0% kernel overhead.

</details>


### [17] [PackServe: SLO-Aware Request Scheduling for Agentic LLM Serving at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33224)
*Zhiyuan Tan,Dejiang Zhu,Jingzhe Jiang,Yihao Zheng,Yang Tian,Tao Wang,Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PackServe 是一个面向代理式 LLM 服务的调度器，在满足 TPOT 延迟 SLO 的同时通过请求打包降低 GPU 资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模集群服务代理式 LLM 时，调度需同时保证长共享前缀的 KVC 复用、token 级延迟 SLO 和低 GPU 资源占用。现有调度器难以兼顾：请求合并会牺牲缓存局部性并加剧 prefill/decode 干扰，损害 SLO 达成率与资源效率。

Method: PackServe 使用紧凑的白盒模型预测 prefill/decode 干扰下的延迟，据此在保持 KVC 复用和 SLO 约束的前提下，将请求打包到更少的服务实例上，用可用的延迟余量换取更高的单实例吞吐。

Result: 在 64 块 NVIDIA H20 GPU 上，30ms 与 50ms TPOT 目标下分别比 SOTA 调度器少用 16.8% 和 24.6% 的 GPU 小时，且满足 TPOT 目标；已在超过 1000 块 GPU 的生产集群部署，资源占用较原生产调度器降低 34.7%。

Conclusion: PackServe 通过延迟预测驱动的请求打包，在满足延迟 SLO 的同时显著降低资源成本，并在生产规模验证了有效性。

Abstract: Request scheduling is a key challenge in large-scale clusters serving agentic large language model (LLM) workloads. An effective scheduler must preserve key-value cache (KVC) reuse across long, shared prefixes, meet token-level latency service-level objectives (SLOs), and minimize GPU resource footprint. Existing schedulers struggle to reconcile these requirements: request consolidation can sacrifice cache locality and increase prefill/decode interference, compromising both SLO attainment and resource efficiency. We present PackServe, a scheduler designed to reduce resource costs while meeting latency SLOs for agentic LLM serving. PackServe uses compact white-box models to predict latency under prefill/decode interference. Guided by these predictions, it packs requests onto fewer serving instances while preserving KVC reuse and SLO constraints, trading available latency headroom for improved per-instance throughput. Evaluation on 64 NVIDIA H20 GPUs shows that PackServe uses up to 16.8% and 24.6% fewer GPU-hours than state-of-the-art schedulers under 30-ms and 50-ms TPOT targets, respectively, while meeting the target TPOT objectives. PackServe has also been deployed in our production cluster comprising over 1000 GPUs, where it reduces the resource footprint by 34.7% compared with the original production scheduler.

</details>


### [18] [CascadeEP: Asynchronous Expert Execution for MoE Prefill under Attention Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33252)
*Jin Qin,Tiancheng Hu,Shiyan Wang,Junhao Hu,Zexin Jian,Yuzheng Wang,Haoyu Li,Chunwei Xia,Ying Liu,Pixian Zhan,Di Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Huimin Cui,Tao Xie,Chenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ASYNCEP 是一个面向 MoE 预填充的分布式执行引擎，通过异步 EP、streamFFN 与机会式专家权重获取三项机制缓解注意力副本负载不均问题，在多个模型上实现最高 1.48x 的 p95 TTFT 加速与 1.17x 吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 服务常采用数据与专家并行（DEP）：注意力副本处理不同请求批次，路由专家分片于 EP 组。预填充阶段各注意力副本完成分发的时间不同，但同步 EP 需等待所有副本的路由输入就绪才开始专家 FFN 计算，造成等待。同时请求调度器既要均衡负载又要复用共享提示前缀的 KV 缓存以避免重复预填充，这两者在持有匹配前缀的副本已过载时会冲突，导致残余的注意力不均衡。

Method: 本文提出分布式执行引擎 ASYNCEP，包含三种机制：1）异步 EP，允许专家计算在所有注意力副本的 token 就绪前即开始；2）streamFFN，将就绪 token 分批，在提前执行与 FFN 计算效率之间取得平衡；3）机会式专家权重获取（OEWF），让较快的副本拉取专家权重并执行其他 GPU 上尚未开始的工作。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash、DeepSeek-V4-Pro 和 GLM-5.3 上进行评测，p95 首 token 时延（TTFT）最高加速 1.48x，推理吞吐最高提升 1.17x。

Conclusion: ASYNCEP 通过打破同步 EP 的等待瓶颈并利用副本间速度差进行工作窃取，有效缓解了 MoE 预填充中的注意力负载不均衡，在时延与吞吐上均取得显著收益，且适用于多种主流 MoE 模型。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) serving commonly deploys data and expert parallelism (DEP): attention replicas run distinct request batches while routed experts are sharded across an expert-parallel (EP) group. During prefill, attention replicas finish dispatch at different times, but synchronous EP delays expert feed-forward network (FFN) computation until routed inputs from all replicas are ready. Request schedulers seek to balance load while reusing the key-value (KV) cache of shared prompt prefixes to avoid redundant prefill computation. These goals can conflict when a replica holding a matching prefix is already overloaded, leaving residual attention imbalance. We present ASYNCEP, a distributed execution engine for MoE prefill. ASYNCEP proposes three mechanisms. Asynchronous EP allows expert computation to start before tokens from all attention replicas are ready. streamFFN batches ready tokens to balance early execution with FFN computation efficiency. Opportunistic expert weight fetching (OEWF) allows a faster replica to fetch expert weights and execute unstarted work from other GPUs. We evaluate ASYNCEP on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and GLM-5.3, and our results show that ASYNCEP achieves up to 1.48x speedup in p95 time-to-first-token (TTFT) and improves the inference throughput by up to 1.17x.

</details>


### [19] [AgentLoop: Runtime Control of Slot-closed Execution Loops for Tool-augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33315)
*Wanyi Zheng,Minxian Xu,Kan Hu,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentLoop，为工具增强型 LLM 智能体提供槽位闭合的执行循环运行时控制，通过显式运行时信号判断任务是否完成，显著降低冗余执行与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型 LLM 智能体的执行循环缺乏评估任务完成度的显式运行时信号，导致在运行时上下文不再变化后仍继续推理或调用服务，且已收集的证据未被综合成完整答案，造成资源使用效率低下。

Method: AgentLoop 将开放式智能体迭代转化为状态驱动的执行控制：维护紧凑的运行时状态，采用模型辅助的结构化验证来检查答案完整性与缺失证据，并在相邻 LLM/工具轮次上施加有界稳定性与低增益信号，最终选择三种动作之一——继续调用、答案综合或终止迭代。其中"槽位闭合"指请求所需的信息槽已被充分的运行时证据覆盖，且未解决的槽在循环停止前被显式识别。

Result: 实验表明 AgentLoop 减少了冗余执行与上下文增长，相较基线总 token 成本最多降低 88.44%，平均服务调用次数最多减少 76.85%。消融研究显示以槽位为中心的控制路径起核心作用，禁用后执行深度增加且准确率大幅下降。

Conclusion: 结果表明，高效的工具体增强型智能体可从显式运行时信号中受益，用以判断何时进一步的 LLM/工具迭代不再带来有用的上下文或受支持的证据。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents are becoming an important execution unit in service computing, but existing agent loops still lack explicit runtime signals for assessing task completion. The challenge lies in the fact that an agent may continue reasoning or invoking services even after the runtime context has stopped changing, while evidence already collected remains unsynthesized into a complete answer, which leads to inefficiency in resource usage. To address these challenges, this paper presents AgentLoop, which provides runtime control of slot-closed execution loops for tool-augmented agents. Slot closure means that the information slots required by a request have been covered by sufficient runtime evidence, and that unresolved slots are explicitly identified before the loop stops. AgentLoop converts open-ended agent iteration into state-driven execution control: it maintains a compact runtime state, uses model-assisted structured verification to check answer completeness and missing evidence, and applies bounded stability and low-gain signals over neighboring LLM/tool rounds before selecting one of three actions: Continue Invocation, Answer Synthesis, or Terminate Iteration. Experiments show that AgentLoop reduces redundant execution and context growth, with total token cost reduced by up to 88.44% and average service invocations reduced by up to 76.85% against baselines. The ablation study further shows that the slot-centered control path plays a central role, since disabling it increases execution depth and substantially reduces accuracy. Overall, the results suggest that efficient tool-augmented agents can benefit from explicit runtime signals for deciding when further LLM/tool iterations no longer add useful context or supported evidence.

</details>


### [20] [OLED-MoE: Accelerating MoE-Based dLLM Inference via Inter-Iteration Locality-Aware Expert Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33385)
*Jingyuan Xiao,Jiayue Wang,Yitao Hu,Xinning Wang,Shi Chen,Ziqi Gong,Zhengchao Wang,Guotao Yang,Sheng Chen,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OLED-MoE 将 MoE 专家卸载的优化目标从迭代内预取转为迭代间专家保留，利用路由重叠与 token 置信度预测复用，并结合 CPU-GPU 协同执行，大幅降低 TPOT 并提升缓存利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半自回归扩散大模型（dLLM）通过分块去噪提升解码并行度，但引入 MoE 层后专家参数量超出显存受限 GPU 的容量，需要专家卸载。现有 MoE 服务系统面向自回归解码，采用迭代内逐层预取，而 dLLM 的分块路由会扩大每次迭代的活跃专家工作集，使预取难以按时完成且误预测代价高，最终退化为按需加载，导致解码延迟高。

Method: 提出 OLED-MoE 专家卸载系统。核心思想是将优化目标从迭代内预取转向迭代间专家保留，基于相邻去噪迭代间存在强路由重叠、且 token 置信度可指示专家复用可能性的洞察。采用置信度引导的迭代间预测，将高价值专家保留在 GPU 显存中而不增加额外预取流量；并通过 CPU-GPU 协同执行弥补不可避免的缓存缺失，同时考虑动态专家计算负载与预测的未来复用。

Result: 在多种 dLLM 负载下，相比最先进的卸载系统，OLED-MoE 将每输出 token 时间（TPOT）降低 1.23x-7.93x，专家缓存利用率提升 1.44x-4.23x。仅使用 40% 的专家 GPU 显存即可接近全驻留性能，在专家显存占用减少 60% 的情况下 TPOT 仅高 23%。

Conclusion: OLED-MoE 通过迭代间专家保留与置信度引导预测，有效解决了 dLLM+MoE 场景下专家卸载的预取失效问题，在显著降低显存占用的同时保持接近全驻留的推理性能。

Abstract: Semi-autoregressive diffusion large language models (dLLMs) improve decoding parallelism through iterative block-wise denoising, but scaling them with mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers introduces a large expert parameter footprint that exceeds memory-constrained GPU capacity. Expert offloading is a natural remedy, yet existing MoE serving systems target autoregressive decoding and rely on intra-iteration layer-wise prefetching: while computing one layer, they predict and load experts for subsequent layers. Under dLLM inference, block-wise routing expands the active expert working set within each iteration, making such prefetches difficult to complete in time and costly when mispredicted. Consequently, existing prefetch-based solutions often degenerate into on-demand expert loading with high decoding latency.
  We propose OLED-MoE, an expert offloading system that shifts the optimization target from intra-iteration prefetching to inter-iteration expert retention. Its key insight is that adjacent denoising iterations exhibit strong expert routing overlap, and token confidence indicates which experts are likely to be reused. OLED-MoE uses confidence-guided inter-iteration prediction to retain high-value experts in GPU memory without introducing extra prefetch traffic. It further compensates unavoidable cache misses through CPU-GPU cooperative execution, jointly considering dynamic expert computation load and predicted future reuse. Across diverse dLLM workloads, OLED-MoE reduces time per output token (TPOT) by 1.23x-7.93x and improves expert cache utilization by 1.44x-4.23x over state-of-the-art offloading systems. Notably, OLED-MoE approaches full-residency performance while using only 40% of the expert GPU memory, incurring merely 23% higher TPOT despite a 60% reduction in expert memory footprint. OLED-MoE's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/flashserve/OLED-MoE.

</details>


### [21] [Toward System-of-Systems Integration for Composable Cloud-HPC-Edge AI Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33427)
*Sumit Rakesh,Rajkumar Saini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将现代AI平台视为"系统的系统"(SoS)，提出"可组合集成"框架，通过接口、契约、映射、引用、策略上下文和运行证据实现跨系统协调，同时保留各系统原生控制平面，形成"使用上趋同、控制上联邦"的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI平台由基础设施栈与运营模型组合而成，这些栈基于不同假设设计（云服务、HPC、云原生编排、数据与制品系统、托管连接、可观测性、边缘/信息物理环境）。现有工作虽展示了部分栈之间的有效桥接，但缺乏跨独立控制系统的通用组合推理方式。

Method: 提出将此类平台视为系统的系统(SoS)，并采用"可组合集成"方法进行跨系统协调。具体贡献包括：对等的成员系统视图；区分成员系统与组件、本地依赖、边界外独立系统的边界测试；局部、共享、受限的责任模型；七个集成表面；一个代表性跨系统工作流。

Result: 给出了一套用于推理跨独立控制系统组合的概念框架，包括成员系统视图、边界测试、责任模型、七个集成表面以及跨系统工作流示例。

Conclusion: 总结出证据分类与研究方向，用于评估互操作性、治理、可观测性、故障隔离、演进与复用，最终导向"使用趋同、控制联邦"的可组合AI平台。

Abstract: Modern AI platforms increasingly combine infrastructure stacks and operating models designed around different assumptions, including cloud-style service platforms, HPC workload-management systems, cloud-native orchestration, data and artifact systems, managed connectivity, observability, and edge or cyber-physical environments. Existing work demonstrates effective bridges between selected stacks, but a general way to reason about composition across independently controlled systems remains underdeveloped. We argue that such platforms can be usefully viewed as systems of systems (SoS) when independently useful systems retain their own control, management, lifecycles, policies, and failure semantics while contributing to a higher-level AI platform capability. We frame composable integration as an approach to cross-system coordination based on interfaces, contracts, mappings, references, policy context, and operational evidence, while preserving native control planes and avoiding dependence on a single topology or orchestration stack. The resulting direction is converged in use and federated in control. The paper presents a peer constituent-system view, a boundary test for distinguishing constituent systems from components, local dependencies, and independently useful systems outside the current SoS boundary, a local, shared, and scoped responsibility model, seven integration surfaces, and a representative cross-system workflow. It concludes with evidence classes and research questions for evaluating interoperability, governance, observability, fault containment, evolution, and reuse.

</details>


### [22] [Adaptive Client Clustering and Coordination for Federated Learning Workflow Management in Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33544)
*Jieping Luo,Qiyue Li,Yuxuan Chen,Hang Qi,Jiaying Yin,Jingjin Wu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出A-CoDa，一个面向依赖式联邦学习流（dependent FL flows）的自适应聚类协调框架，通过LDD聚类、FedMIX参与机制与DAG调度，在边缘网络中降低端到端完成时间并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习正从孤立训练任务转向受管学习服务，在网络化边缘环境中，依赖式FL服务流需协调异构客户端、非IID数据、波动通信延迟与优先级约束任务，并满足服务级完成要求。这些耦合因素使参与者管理成为影响时间目标性能与学习稳定性的关键。

Method: A-CoDa包含三部分：(1) 基于标签分布散度(LDD)的贪心均衡聚类，构建统计一致且感知规模的客户端分组作为可扩展管理抽象；(2) FedMIX不确定性感知的簇内/簇间参与机制，按损失-延迟-不确定性效用对客户端排序，并依训练进度与延迟状况自适应控制跨簇探测；(3) 依赖感知DAG调度器，按层编排任务执行，兼顾并行性与优先级约束。此外提供收敛分析，将结果表述为损失域充分设计界，关联误差下界与通信轮数。

Result: 收敛分析将可达误差下界与充分通信轮数显式关联到LDD诱导的采样不匹配、残余分布偏移、本地SGD漂移、随机方差与自适应探测预算。在手写体、可穿戴传感、商品图像与医学影像任务上的实验表明，A-CoDa在依赖式FL工作流下能有效缩短端到端完成时间并保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: A-CoDa通过聚类抽象、不确定性感知参与与依赖感知调度，有效应对依赖式联邦学习流中的协调与完成时间挑战，在多种任务上验证了其兼顾效率与精度的有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is increasingly deployed as a managed learning service rather than as a set of isolated training jobs. In networked edge environments, dependent FL service flows must coordinate heterogeneous clients, non-IID data, fluctuating communication latency, and precedence-constrained tasks under service-level completion requirements. These coupled factors make participant management central to both time-totarget performance and learning stability. This paper proposes A-CoDa, an adaptive clustered coordination framework for managing dependent FL flows. A-CoDa first uses label-distribution divergence (LDD)-based greedy-balanced clustering to construct statistically coherent and size-aware client groups, which serve as a scalable management abstraction. Building on this structure, we design FedMIX, an uncertainty-aware intra-/inter-cluster participation mechanism that ranks clients by a loss-latency-uncertainty utility and adaptively controls cross-cluster probing according to training progress and latency conditions. A dependency-aware DAG scheduler then orchestrates layer-wise task execution so that parallelism and precedence constraints are jointly respected. We further provide a convergence analysis that frames the result as a sufficient loss-domain design bound, explicitly relating the attainable error floor and sufficient communication rounds to LDDinduced sampling mismatch, residual distribution shift, local-SGD drift, stochastic variance, and adaptive probing budgets. Experiments on handwriting, wearable-sensing, product-image, and medical-imaging tasks evaluate A-CoDa under dependent FL workflows and demonstrate its effectiveness in reducing end-toend completion time while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [23] [EfficientAgent: What Makes KV Cache Offloading Work for Concurrent Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33762)
*Kunming Shao,Jierun Chen,Jiangnan Yu,Xiao-Hui Li,Chaofan Tao,Yanli Wang,Huanxin Lin,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Chi Ying Tsui,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 EfficientAgent，依据“复用工作集”来估计与管理主机内存层，从而让 LLM 智能体场景下的 KV 状态卸载稳定获益；在 SWE-bench Verified 上减少 93% 重算 token、端到端时间降低 39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体每轮会重发完整对话，系统通常用 KV 缓存避免重算，并在 GPU 显存不足时把缓存卸载到主机内存。但对智能体而言，卸载效果不一致：同一编码任务在不同部署下有时加速、有时变慢、有时无变化，即使加载一个 token 比重新计算便宜数倍。根本原因是缓存必须存活到再次被使用；当一个智能体等待工具时，服务器会处理其他智能体上下文，因此只有当主机层能容纳整个智能体池的可复用上下文（即复用工作集）时前缀才会被复用。主机层太小会不断写入在被读取前就被驱逐的状态。

Method: 作者提出 EfficientAgent，按复用工作集来设定并管理主机层。用栈距离模型基于智能体历史估计工作集，从而确定主机层容量，并在实验前预测出卸载开始获益的容量点。运行时策略分为两种情况：当主机层过小时，停止写入被驱逐上下文的大规模重填，转而继续扩展仍被缓存的 prefix；当主机层足够大时则写入全部内容。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 编码智能体上，按估计工作集设定主机层可将重算 prompt token 减少 93%，端到端时间降低 39%。使用小型固定主机层时，该策略减少 35% 重算；使用大型主机层时，可避免“始终过滤写入”造成的 4.3 倍开销增长。跨三种 GPU 类型和两个模型，当 GPU 每字节主机带宽对应的算力较低且主机层能容纳工作集时，卸载才获益。

Conclusion: KV 状态卸载对智能体并非普遍有效，其收益取决于主机层是否能容纳复用工作集以及 GPU 算力与主机带宽的比值。通过工作集感知的容量设定与运行时写入策略，可以稳定实现显著的重算节省与端到端加速。

Abstract: LLM agents resend their whole conversation on every turn, and most of it was already processed on the previous turn. Serving systems avoid recomputing it by caching its key-value (KV) state and, when GPU memory runs out, by offloading that state to host memory. For agents, offloading gives inconsistent results: on the same coding-agent workload it speeds up one deployment, slows down another, and changes nothing on a third, even where loading a token back is several times cheaper than recomputing it. The reason is that cached state must survive until it is used again. While one agent waits for its tool, the server processes the contexts of all other agents, so an agent's prefix is reused only if the host tier holds the reusable context of the whole agent pool, which we call the reuse working set. A smaller tier keeps writing state that is evicted before anyone reads it. We present EfficientAgent, which sizes and manages the host tier by this working set. A stack-distance model estimates the working set from agent histories to size the host tier; its predictions, made before the experiments, located the capacity at which offloading starts to pay. When the tier is too small, a runtime policy stops writing large refills of evicted context and keeps extending prefixes that are still cached; when the tier is large enough, it writes everything. On SWE-bench Verified coding agents, a host tier sized to the estimated working set cuts recomputed prompt tokens by 93% and end-to-end time by 39%. With a small fixed tier, the policy cuts recomputation by 35%; with a large tier, it avoids the 4.3-fold increase caused by always filtering writes. Across three GPU types and two models, offloading pays off when the GPU has little compute per byte of host bandwidth and the host tier holds the working set. Code is available at https://github.com/KunmingSHAO/efficientagent_release.

</details>


### [24] [Performance vs Portability in Heterogeneous HPC Environments: Why Pre-execution Benchmarking is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33847)
*Mindaugas Macernis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究利用Podman作为用户级工具，对以预编译可执行文件形式分发的量子化学软件进行基准测试，比较容器化与系统原生执行在CPU优化构建和多计算节点下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算通常使用Kubernetes编排，而高性能计算（HPC）工作负载则由Slurm等批处理调度器管理，二者遵循不同范式。随着共享计算资源需求增长，这些环境之间的互操作性需求日益凸显，因此需要评估在HPC系统中以用户可访问方式部署科学应用容器的可行性和性能表现。

Method: 采用Podman作为用户可访问工具，对量子化学软件（以预编译可执行文件形式分发）进行基准测试。在CPU优化构建和多个计算节点上，对比容器化执行与系统内原生执行的表现，且不进行额外的网络优化。

Result: 性能取决于软件编译方式、启动开销和执行配置。容器准备过程可能主导短任务工作流，而长时间运行的计算则需要监控计算进度。

Conclusion: 在HPC系统上以容器部署科学应用时，进行针对特定工作负载的基准测试和用户级监控十分重要。

Abstract: Cloud computing and high-performance computing (HPC) typically follow different paradigms: cloud services are often orchestrated using Kubernetes, whereas HPC workloads are managed through batch schedulers such as Slurm. Growing demand for shared computational resources increases the need for interoperability between these environments. This study uses Podman as a user-accessible tool to benchmark quantum chemistry software distributed as precompiled executables. Containerized and on-system execution are compared across CPU-optimized builds and multiple compute nodes, without additional network optimizations. The results demonstrate that performance depends on software compilation, startup overhead, and execution configuration. Container preparation can dominate short-task workflows, while long-running calculations require monitoring of computational progress. These findings highlight the importance of workload-specific benchmarking and user-level monitoring when deploying scientific applications in containers on HPC systems.

</details>


### [25] [Validating Memory-Optimal Transformer Kernels on Real Hardware: From Formal Derivation to Measured Performance Across Two HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33916)
*Lenore M. Mullin,Gaetan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过在两台HPC集群（CPU与GPU）上的实测性能，验证了基于数学阵列（MoA）推导出的transformer核函数的内存最优代价函数，并发现与修复了GPU回归问题、揭示了拓扑结构对实际代价的巨大影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随论文I-IV已从形式上推导出注意力前向、反向、融合、解码及完整块的核函数，并将其作为硬件无关规范（DNF）经gamma转换为机器特定实现（ONF），且已在机器精度上对标PyTorch验证。本文动机是进一步用真实硬件性能来核验这些理论预测的代价函数是否成立。

Method: 在Purdue Anvil和NCSA Delta两个HPC集群上，对CPU和GPU环境进行实测性能对比，将测得的性能与MoA推导的代价函数预测相比较；通过性能分析（profiling）诊断GPU回归问题中的原子操作争用，并对ONF实现进行针对性重写进行验证。

Result: 三项突出结果：(1)识别并修复了GPU回归——融合前向+反向虽被证明可避免实例化O(n^2)中间量，但起初因原子争用反而比朴素实现更慢，profiling确认原子指令多出2.00x，针对性ONF重写后最多获得2.5x加速；(2)相同推导因拓扑不同产生显著不同的实际代价，一台集群有535x的NUMA局部性惩罚，另一台则仅有<3x的超订代价；(3)报告一个部分解决的异常——相同的指称计算在CPU上C比Fortran快，在GPU上则Fortran比C快，被缩小到一个主导核函数和一个内存延迟停顿机制（3.17x时间差对应3.35x停顿差）。

Conclusion: 将硬件特定优化视为在已验证的固定DNF基础上的常规ONF重写，是一种可扩展AI至演进硬件的候选方法论，无需重新推导正确性。

Abstract: We validate memory-optimal cost functions for transformer kernels derived via the Mathematics of Arrays (MoA). Companion Papers I-IV formally derive kernels for attention forward, backward, fused forward+backward, decode, and the complete block (RMSNorm, gated MLP) as a hardware-independent specification (DNF) transformed to a machine-specific realization (ONF) via gamma, with verification to machine precision against PyTorch. This paper checks those predictions against measured performance on two HPC clusters (Purdue Anvil, NCSA Delta) across CPU and GPU.
  Three results stand out. (1) We identify and fix a GPU regression: fusing forward+backward, proven to avoid materializing an O(n^2) intermediate, initially ran slower than naive on GPU due to atomic contention. Profiling confirmed 2.00x more atomic instructions; a targeted ONF rewrite reversed it, yielding up to 2.5x speedup. (2) Identical derivations produce markedly different real costs by topology: 535x NUMA-locality penalty on one cluster vs <3x oversubscription on another, showing optimal deployment is a function of the machine's array structure. (3) We report a partially resolved anomaly: identical denotational computations run faster in C than Fortran on CPU but faster in Fortran than C on GPU, narrowed to one dominant kernel and one memory-latency stall mechanism (3.17x time gap matches 3.35x stall gap).
  We treat hardware-specific optimization as a routine ONF rewrite with fixed, verified DNF, a candidate methodology for scaling AI onto evolving hardware without re-deriving correctness.

</details>


### [26] [Kafila: Serving Large Language Models on a Trusted Set of Heterogeneous Commodity Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34045)
*Murtaza Rangwala,Richard O. Sinnott,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出Kafila系统，通过在受限信任的异构设备群上精确划分大语言模型并组网，显著提升流水线推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小组或朋友圈的消费级电脑算力不足以单独运行大语言模型，而现有开放蜂群系统依赖任意节点加入、冗余放置和绕过慢节点，无法用于仅限可信机器的封闭群体。封闭会话必须让所有被接纳的设备都参与工作，且流水线速度受最慢设备限制，因此需要在服务开始前就做好精确的模型划分。

Method: 提出Kafila协议：在NAT后方组建环形拓扑，优先直连、必要时中继；规划器测量各设备的内存带宽、容量与可达性，针对固定环形顺序精确划分模型，并将含嵌入和输出投影的头部与划分方案一起确定而非预先固定。

Result: 在三个设备群、从共享局域网到横跨两大洲的五台设备上测试，Kafila将最慢流水线阶段缩短至GPipe均匀划分的5.2倍以内、exo内存比例划分的3倍以内；使75%至87%的已投入硬件参与工作（对比方案低于一半），并能运行均匀划分无法部署的模型。共享网络下吞吐为均匀划分的1.56倍、内存比例划分的1.25倍；四个并发用户时优势扩大至3.2倍。

Conclusion: Kafila通过测量驱动的精确模型划分和自适应组网，在可信异构消费设备群上实现了高效的流水线并行推理，且并发用户场景下性能优势会随负载放大而非衰减。

Abstract: Between them, the members of a research group or a circle of friends own several consumer computers, none large enough to run a capable large language model. Existing systems pool such capacity across open swarms anyone may join, which a group admitting only trusted machines cannot use. Bounding membership removes what they depend on: a swarm holds each part of the model on several peers and routes around a slow one. A bounded session must use every device it admits. Its pipeline advances at the pace of whichever device received a share it cannot serve quickly, so the division has to be right before serving begins. We propose Kafila, whose protocol assembles a ring from behind NATs, preferring direct paths and relaying where traversal fails, while its planner measures each device's memory bandwidth, capacity and reachability, divides the model exactly for a fixed ring order, and places the head, which holds the embedding and output projection, together with that division rather than beforehand. On machines with different capabilities across three fleets, from a shared LAN to five devices spanning two continents, Kafila shortens the slowest pipeline stage by up to $5.2\times$ against the even split of pipeline parallelism, as in GPipe, and up to $3\times$ against the memory-proportional split of personal-device inference, as in exo, keeps 75 to 87 per cent of the committed hardware doing work where those divisions fall below half, and serves a model no uniform split can place on the fleet at all. What that is worth to a user depends on how much of a token is computation rather than network. Where the members share a network the same division returns $1.56\times$ the throughput of a uniform split and $1.25\times$ of a memory-proportional one, and under four concurrent users that lead compounds to $3.2\times$ rather than fading, each user served at almost the rate of one.

</details>


### [27] [Schedule Repair for DAG Workflows under Link Disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34048)
*Mohammadali Khodabandehlou,Jared Coleman,Bhaskar Krishnamachari,Kevin Chan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究网络化IoT系统中DAG工作流的"调度修复"问题，提出从"等待"到"全局重调度"的一系列修复策略谱系，并在100个工作流实例、5种CCR、多种中断过程下评估，发现没有单一修复范围总是最优，修复范围应随中断过程和修复成本自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DAG工作流调度通常假设网络静态或稳定，但在对抗与竞争环境中，链路会因移动性、干扰和阻塞而退化或失败，导致原调度部分失效。因此需要研究：当链路中断使部分调度失效时，应对多少调度进行修复（reschedule）。

Method: 作者提出一个修复策略谱系，按修复范围（repair scope）区分：等待中断结束、绕路重路由数据、仅局部重调度受影响任务、全局重调度所有待处理任务。每个策略与一个oracle对比，并按移动调度的程度收取与决策延迟成比例的代价。评估覆盖100个工作流实例，包括合成任务图、RIoTBench流水线和WfCommons科学工作流，每个实例在5种通信计算比（CCR）下运行，并使用具有不同相关结构的中断过程进行干扰。

Result: 结果显示没有单一修复范围占优：重路由几乎能消除孤立故障（而等待会带来30%代价）；在阻塞式黑障下，全局修复与oracle差距在4%以内；在无记忆链路抖动下，对更大且通信密集的工作负载，等待更优（类似路由抖动抑制）；自愈式移动中断更青睐耐心而非反应；计入修复延迟后，大范围修复首先受到侵蚀。

Conclusion: 修复范围应随中断过程和修复成本自适应调整，而不应由调度器固定。这意味着调度器需要具备根据链路中断特性动态选择修复策略的能力。

Abstract: Schedules for directed acyclic graph (DAG) workflows in networked IoT systems are typically computed assuming a static or generally stable network. In contested and adversarial environments, this assumption is not valid. Links degrade and fail due to mobility, interference, and jamming. We study schedule repair: when a link disruption invalidates part of a schedule, how much of it should be rescheduled? We introduce a spectrum of repair policies that vary in repair scope, how much of the pending schedule each may move: wait out the disruption, reroute data around it, reschedule only the affected tasks locally, or reschedule all pending tasks globally. We evaluate each against an oracle and charge every repair a decision latency proportional to the extent to which it moves. Across 100 workload instances spanning synthetic task graphs, RIoTBench pipelines, and WfCommons scientific workflows, each run at five communication-to-computation ratios (CCRs) and disrupted by processes with deliberately different correlation structure, we find that no single scope wins: rerouting nearly erases isolated failures that cost waiting 30%, global repair comes within 4% of the oracle under jamming blackouts, waiting is favored under memoryless link flapping for larger and communication-heavy workloads (the scheduling analog of route-flap damping), self-healing mobility outages reward patience over reaction, and accounting for repair latency erodes large scopes first. We conclude that the scope of the repair should be adapted to the disruption process and the repair cost, rather than fixed by the scheduler.

</details>


### [28] [SlideDP: Scaling Host-Resident LLM Fine-Tuning Across Multiple GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34162)
*Ruijia Yang,Shiyuan Lin,Yulong Ao,Zhiyu Li,Yingli Zhao,Xianduo Li,Yonghua Lin,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出同步数据并行运行时 SlideDP，通过权威主机状态与流水线化传输/聚合/更新，缓解多卡共享主机资源竞争，实现超出显存的全参数微调并显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主机驻留层流式传输使全参数 LLM 微调可突破显存限制，但数据并行各 rank 争抢共享主机资源：复制式传输放大流量，强扩展下计算窗口缩小导致主机工作暴露。

Method: 面向共享主机多 GPU 系统的同步数据并行运行时：维护单一权威主机状态；将通信路径与状态布局解耦；跨 rank 与分块流水线化参数下发、梯度聚合与 CPU 更新；用解析步时模型刻画资源瓶颈与流水线暴露，并依据运行时测量在显存预算下调度通信、分块与激活策略。

Result: 匹配批次扫描中，相较 SlideFormer、MegaTrain、ZeRO-Offload 取得 1.46–2.64× 几何平均吞吐比；四张 H100 上 Qwen3-14B 在小批次接近 GPU 驻留 FSDP2 吞吐，大批次单步超 1M token 并超出 FSDP2 实测峰值 11.2%；支持同模型 256K token 序列，并在四张 RTX 4090 上微调 Qwen2.5-72B。

Conclusion: SlideDP 通过解耦主机状态与通信路径并流水线化跨 rank 工作，有效缓解共享主机资源竞争，在无需 GPU 驻留全参数的前提下逼近甚至超越 GPU 驻留方案的吞吐，兼顾长序列与大模型微调。

Abstract: Host-resident layer streaming enables full-parameter LLM fine-tuning beyond GPU memory, but data-parallel ranks compete for shared host resources. Replicated transfers amplify traffic, while strong scaling can expose host work as computation windows shrink. We present SlideDP, a synchronous data-parallel runtime for shared-host multi-GPU systems. It maintains one authoritative host state, decouples communication routes from state layout, and pipelines parameter delivery, gradient aggregation, and CPU updates across ranks and chunks. An analytical step-time model characterizes resource bottlenecks and pipeline exposure; runtime measurements guide communication, chunking, and activation policies under a GPU memory budget. In matched-batch sweeps, SlideDP achieves geometric-mean throughput ratios of 1.46-2.64$\times$ over SlideFormer, MegaTrain, and ZeRO-Offload. On four H100s, SlideDP approaches GPU-resident FSDP2 throughput for Qwen3-14B at a smaller batch size. With a larger batch, it processes over 1M tokens per step and exceeds FSDP2's measured peak throughput by 11.2%. Separately, it supports 256K-token sequences for the same model and fine-tunes Qwen2.5-72B on four RTX 4090 GPUs. Project page: https://github.com/RegiaYoung/SlideDP.

</details>


### [29] [Arachne: Learning to Plan Parallel Training on Dynamic Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34244)
*Taeyoon Kim,Yonguk Song,Seoyeong Choy,Hexiao Duan,Dong Li,Seo Jin Park,Myeongjae Jeon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Arachne提出一种基于学习的规划器，将并行规划问题简化为对流水线结构的搜索，从而在异构GPU集群上快速生成高吞吐训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享GPU基础设施上训练大模型面临两大挑战：GPU可用性随租户需求动态变化，以及数据中心持续引入新GPU代际导致硬件异构性累积。异构GPU类型和节点规模带来巨大搜索空间，规划器必须激进剪枝以保持可解性，同时还需在集群配置变化时迅速给出高吞吐方案。

Method: Arachne通过基于学习的规划器，将完整规划问题归约为对流水线结构的搜索。它将规划决策封装在结构化模板中，并在多样化的集群配置上离线学习如何从模板构造规划。该设计有效的关键在于：结构决策是并行规划中性能最关键的核心，一旦结构固定，其余决策可通过规则或小型带价格候选集确定。

Result: 在具有不同GPU类型和节点规模的集群上，针对三种不同规模模型，Arachne的吞吐量达到或超过五种现有规划器找到的最佳方案，稠密模型最高提升84.5%，MoE模型最高提升4.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过将规划问题聚焦于结构搜索并离线学习模板构造，Arachne能够在动态、异构的共享GPU集群上快速生成高吞吐并行训练方案，显著优于现有规划器。

Abstract: Training large machine learning models on shared GPU infrastructures faces two challenges: (1) GPU availability shifts dynamically with varying resource demands from tenants, and (2) hardware heterogeneity accumulates as datacenters continuously adopt new GPU generations. Due to the vast search space induced by heterogeneous GPU types and node sizes, training planners must prune it aggressively to remain tractable, yet must also derive high-throughput plans promptly as cluster configurations change. Arachne achieves this goal through a learning-based planner that reduces the full planning problem to a search over pipeline structures. Arachne encapsulates planning decisions in a structural template and learns to construct plans from templates over diverse cluster configurations offline. This design is effective because structural decisions constitute the performance-critical core of a parallelism plan, while the rest follows by rule or from a small priced candidate set once the plan structure is fixed. Evaluation shows that Arachne matches or exceeds the best plan found by five existing planners across clusters with varying GPU types and node sizes for three models of different sizes by up to 84.5% in throughput on dense models and 4.6x on MoE models.

</details>


### [30] [HyDra: Demystifying and Taming Dynamic Context Parallelism at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34318)
*Zihao Fan,Yunzhuo Liu,Bo Jiang,Changgang Zheng,Lin Zheng,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyDra，一个可扩展的负载驱动动态上下文并行（DCP）系统，通过负载均衡调度与嵌套 CP 引擎，显著缓解长上下文训练中的计算偏斜、流水线气泡与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文训练的序列长度跨越多个数量级，动态上下文并行（DCP）按序列长度分配 CP 度。现有 DCP 系统要么不可扩展，要么在主流模型上表现差，生产中只能用 Megatron-Core（Mcore）DCP。但 Mcore DCP 按显存设定 CP 度，显存随长度线性增长而注意力计算二次增长，导致相同 token 数下计算量不等。在 11K+ GPU、256K 上下文的实际训练中，各 rank 的微批时间差达 5 倍，造成 46% 流水线气泡和 13% 数据并行气泡。

Method: HyDra 是负载驱动的可扩展 DCP 系统：1）调度器通过将所有 rank 拉向同一负载目标来均衡计算，且均衡使该目标可闭式求解；2）使用惰性堆而非整池扫描来放置序列；3）均衡要求 CP 度大于显存所需，故 CP 引擎在浅层外环中嵌套内层 Ulysses 组，使更高 CP 度同时降低计算与通信。

Result: 512 GPU 测试台上，32K 上下文吞吐较 Mcore DCP 平均提升 1.18 倍，256K 时提升 2.48 倍；2,048 GPU 生产中，流水线气泡从 36% 降至 14%，调度时间减少 2.3 倍，吞吐较 Mcore DCP 提升 1.10–1.43 倍（平均 1.25 倍），较静态 CP 提升 1.33–1.90 倍（平均 1.59 倍）。

Conclusion: HyDra 通过负载驱动的动态上下文并行，在多个规模上一致地提升长上下文训练吞吐并降低气泡与调度开销，验证了以负载均衡替代单纯显存驱动 CP 度分配的有效性。

Abstract: Long-context training runs on sequences whose lengths span orders of magnitude, and dynamic context parallelism (DCP) gives each sequence its own CP degree. Existing DCP systems either do not scale or perform poorly on mainstream models, leaving Megatron-Core (Mcore) DCP as the only option at production scale. Mcore DCP, however, sizes each degree to fit memory, which grows linearly with length while attention grows quadratically, so comparable token counts hide unequal computation. In our production 256K-context training job on more than 11K GPUs under Mcore DCP, per-rank microbatch times differ by up to 5x at similar token counts. The skew leads to a 46% pipeline bubble and a 13% data-parallel bubble. We present HyDra, a scalable load-driven DCP system. Its scheduler balances computation by pulling every rank toward one load target, and balance in turn makes that target solvable in closed form. It places sequences with lazy heaps rather than whole-pool scans. That balance asks for CP degrees larger than memory requires, so its CP engine nests an inner Ulysses group in a shallow outer ring, letting a higher degree lower computation and communication together. Evaluation at both scales shows consistent gains. On a 512-GPU testbed, HyDra raises throughput over Mcore DCP by 1.18x on average at 32K context and 2.48x at 256K. On a 2,048-GPU production job, it shrinks the pipeline bubble from 36% to 14%, cuts scheduling time by 2.3x, and raises throughput by 1.10-1.43x (avg. 1.25x) over Mcore DCP and 1.33-1.90x (avg. 1.59x) over static CP.

</details>


### [31] [VarioPath: Workload-Aware All-to-All Communication for PCIe GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34340)
*Yao Fei,Jin Fang,Size Zheng,Gongming Zhao,Hongli Xu,Jiacheng Zhu,Zhijing Xin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VarioPath是一个针对PCIe GPU系统的AlltoAllv调度框架，通过离线路由分析器与在线需求感知调度器结合，实现低开销、高效的通信调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCIe GPU系统在MoE模型推理中日益普及，但其无专用scale-up互联，intra-node与inter-node流量均经PCIe层级传输，导致链路带宽竞争；叠加流量分布倾斜与动态需求，使AlltoAllv调度困难。现有方案要么不适配PCIe系统，要么调度合成开销过大，实用性差。

Method: VarioPath由两部分组成：离线拓扑感知分析器记录无竞争的传输模式为AlltoAllv通道，并利用拓扑对称性构建紧凑目录以加速搜索；在线需求感知调度器将每次AlltoAllv调用的需求分解到一系列通道上，适应快速变化且倾斜的流量，同时保持低规划开销。

Result: 在四个平台（最多256 GPU）上评测，相比FAST平均加速5.88倍，相比DeepEP加速1.72倍；端到端实验中，Qwen3推理延迟最多降低27.2%，Wan2.1生成延迟降低6.1%。

Conclusion: VarioPath通过离线通道目录与在线需求分解相结合，有效解决了PCIe GPU系统上AlltoAllv的链路竞争与调度开销问题，在多种平台和端到端推理任务中取得显著加速。

Abstract: AlltoAllv communication is a critical primitive in distributed large-model inference, particularly for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. The growing adoption of PCIe GPU systems for cost-efficient inference makes AlltoAllv performance on these systems increasingly important. Without a dedicated scale-up interconnect (e.g., NVLink or Infinity Fabric), PCIe GPU systems carry both intra-node and inter-node traffic through the PCIe hierarchy, where concurrent transfers can contend for PCIe link bandwidth. This link contention, compounded by skewed traffic distributions and dynamic traffic demand, makes efficient AlltoAllv scheduling challenging. Existing approaches are either poorly suited to PCIe GPU systems or incur substantial schedule synthesis overhead that reduces their practicality in real-world deployments.
  We present VarioPath, an efficient AlltoAllv scheduling framework for PCIe GPU systems. It combines an offline topology-aware analyzer with an online demand-aware scheduler. The analyzer records contention-free transfer patterns as AlltoAllv channels and exploits topology symmetry to build a compact catalog for efficient search. The online scheduler decomposes each AlltoAllv invocation's demand across a sequence of channels, adapting to rapidly changing and skewed traffic while incurring low planning overhead. Evaluation on four platforms (up to 256 GPUs) shows average AlltoAllv speedups of 5.88x over FAST and 1.72x over DeepEP. End-to-end experiments show that VarioPath reduces Qwen3 inference latency by up to 27.2% and Wan2.1 generation latency by 6.1%.

</details>


### [32] [Before Agents Act: Assurance-Aware Semantic Scheduling for Evidence Acquisition in Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34376)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将工具使用型代理的基础设施变更审批中的"证据获取"建模为在法定人数、多样性、时效性、截止时间与资源约束下的联合见证选择与调度问题，提出AAS方法，并通过仿真验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型代理可发起具有重大影响的基础设施变更，但准入所需的证据可能在其它检查运行期间失效，或依赖共享故障域，因此需要解决证据获取的时效性与约束问题。

Method: AAS（Assurance-Aware Semantic Scheduling）结合整数规划选择、调度感知的时间调度、有界诊断扩展以及凭证感知的修复机制；并给出分派时新鲜度与有限诊断扩展所需假设的形式化结果。

Result: 在三个生成的基础设施工作负载中，AAS产生1,075/1,200个有效候选（约束感知前向调度为647/1,200），过期候选从440降至12。纠正后的超时干预找到18/20个可准入案例（静态方案为0/20），凭证复用成本更低。在20个需认证分解割的构造案例中，细化每次都能恢复与神谕匹配的可行方案。

Conclusion: 结果为受控仿真结果：有界神谕共享一个时间搜索组件，且向实际部署系统的迁移尚未验证。

Abstract: Tool-using agents can initiate consequential infrastructure changes, yet evidence required for admission may expire while other checks run or depend on a shared fault domain. We formulate evidence acquisition as joint witness selection and scheduling under quorum, diversity, freshness, deadline, and resource constraints. Assurance-Aware Semantic Scheduling (AAS) combines integer-program selection, dispatch-aware temporal scheduling, bounded diagnostic expansion, and receipt-aware repair. Formal results state the assumptions needed for dispatch-time freshness and finite diagnostic expansion. In three generated infrastructure workloads, AAS produces 1,075/1,200 valid candidates versus 647/1,200 for constraint-aware forward scheduling; stale candidates fall from 440 to 12. Paired sensitivity studies reuse the same instances and operation latency draws across parameter settings. A corrected timeout intervention finds 18/20 admissions with repair or full resynthesis versus 0/20 for a static plan, with lower committed cost when receipts are reused. On 20 constructed cases requiring a certified decomposition cut, refinement recovers an oracle-matching feasible plan every time. These are controlled simulation results; the bounded oracle shares a temporal search component, and transfer to deployed systems remains untested.

</details>


### [33] [DPS: Dual-Mode Precision LLM Serving with Semi-Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34380)
*Xuan Truong Nguyen,Tien Son Pham,Tuan Duc Chu,Wookeun Jung,Thanh Tuan Dao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DPS是一种双精度LLM服务系统，将权重内存变为弹性资源：正常负载服务全精度模型，KV压力下切换到嵌套低精度变体并将权重内存重用于KV缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统只虚拟化和优化KV缓存内存，但把模型权重内存视为执行期间固定不变。近期多精度模型表示研究允许单一存储模型支持全精度和低精度执行，使有效权重占用变为运行时依赖，从而在突发负载（KV缓存需求临时激增决定吞吐和SLO）下产生优化机会。

Method: 提出DPS双精度LLM服务系统，基于Semi-Unified Memory（SUM）：将权重区域划分为持久低精度子区域和共享区域，共享区域在残差权重张量与KV缓存块之间交替，并保持与分页KV缓存管理的兼容性。实现在vLLM之上。

Result: 在密集和MoE模型以及多种生产负载轨迹上评估，相较Static FP16，持续吞吐量提升2.1–3.3倍，有效pass@1最高提升41个百分点，同时保持FP16级别精度。

Conclusion: 通过将权重内存转为弹性资源并动态切换精度，DPS在突发负载下显著提升吞吐与精度表现，验证了权重内存弹性化设计的有效性。

Abstract: Existing LLM serving systems virtualize and optimize KV-cache memory, but treat model-weight memory as fixed throughout execution. Recent work on multi-precision model representations challenges this design by allowing a single stored model to support both full-accuracy and lower-precision execution, making the effective weight footprint runtime-dependent. This creates an opportunity under bursty workloads, where temporary spikes in KV-cache demand often determine throughput and SLO compliance. We present DPS, a dual-precision LLM serving system that turns weight memory into an elastic resource: under normal load, DPS serves the full-accuracy model; under KV pressure, it switches to a nested, lower-precision variant and repurposes unused weight memory for KV cache blocks. DPS is built on Semi-Unified Memory (SUM), which partitions the weight region into a persistent lower-precision sub-region and a shared region that alternates between residual weight tensors and KV-cache blocks, preserving compatibility with paged KV-cache management. We implement DPS on top of vLLM and evaluate it across both dense and MoE models and various production workload traces. Our results show that \sysname improves sustained throughput by $2.1$--$3.3\times$ and effective pass@1 by up to $+41$\,pp over Static FP16, while preserving FP16-class accuracy.

</details>


### [34] [Semantics, Workflows, and Infrastructure: Understanding Agent Serving at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34432)
*Yihao Zheng,Jingzhe Jiang,Dejiang Zhu,Zhiyuan Tan,Yang Tian,Tao Wang,Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对某大规模生产级通用智能体平台的11.7M请求、为期两周的追踪数据进行了端到端分析，覆盖任务发起、工作流执行与推理基础设施三个层级，揭示会话请求量高度偏斜、同级逻辑请求执行重叠罕见、跨任务边界的上下文复用等特征，并讨论部署启示与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
