Motivation: LLM智能体应用以推理请求、工具调用和用户交互构成的工作流执行，生产级服务需理解应用行为如何塑造推理需求并指导高效执行。现有表征研究多提供请求级工作负载测量或智能体执行分析，但缺少连接任务发起、工作流执行与推理基础设施的端到端视角。

Method: 基于某大规模生产级通用智能体平台为期两周、共1170万条请求的追踪数据，底层推理基础设施由超过1万块GPU组成。作者从三个相互连接的层级进行表征：任务级发起语义、工作流级执行模式、基础设施级服务需求。

Result: 测量揭示多项工作负载规律：跨会话的请求量高度偏斜、逻辑同级请求之间执行重叠罕见、以及上下文跨任务边界复用等。基于这些观察，作者分析了对部署的影响并识别出开放问题。

Conclusion: 研究提供了智能体服务的端到端工作负载视图，为面向智能体服务系统的高效部署与未来研究提供了实证依据和方向指引。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents execute applications through a workflow of inference requests with tool calls and user interactions. Serving these applications at production scale requires understanding how application behavior shapes inference demand and for guiding efficient execution. Recent characterization studies provide request-level workload measurements and agent execution analysis. However, an end-to-end view connecting task initiation, workflow execution, and inference infrastructure remains unexplored. In this paper, we analyze a two-week trace of 11.7 million requests from a large-scale production platform for general-purpose agents, backed by inference infrastructure comprising over 10k GPUs. We characterize the platform at three connected levels: task-level initiation semantics, workflow-level execution patterns, and infrastructure level serving demands. Our measurements reveal workload patterns such as highly skewed request volumes across sessions, rare execution overlap among logical sibling requests, and context reuse across task boundaries. Building on these observations, we analyze deployment implications and identify open problems to guide future research on agent serving systems.

</details>


### [35] [AgentWare: Automating the Lifecycle of Agentic Applications across the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34586)
*Michalis Kasioulis,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentWare——一个 AgenticOps 框架，实现 Edge-to-Cloud 环境下智能体应用的自动化配置、部署、可观测性与评估全生命周期管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Edge-to-Cloud 连续体上部署 LLM 驱动的智能体应用面临硬件异构、部署复杂、可观测性有限及缺乏系统化评估方法等挑战；现有方案分别处理开发、可观测性或基准测试，难以覆盖分布式智能体应用的完整生命周期。

Method: AgentWare 提供端到端生命周期流水线：自动准备异构执行环境，将用户定义的智能体实现转化为分布式应用，跨连续体部署组件，并统一采集执行轨迹、基础设施遥测与评估指标。框架还通过 LLM-as-a-Judge 工作流支持自动化语义评估，并生成涵盖正确性、性能、资源利用与能耗的可复现报告。

Result: 通过一个分布式图书助手智能体在真实 Edge-to-Cloud 基础设施上的多种部署与模型配置实验，验证了 AgentWare 的适用性。

Conclusion: AgentWare 支持对分布式智能体应用进行系统化实验与评估，同时显著降低部署、插桩与分析所需的人工成本。

Abstract: Deploying LLM-enabled agentic applications across the Edge-to-Cloud continuum remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, deployment complexity, limited observability, and the lack of systematic evaluation methods. Existing solutions address agent development, observability, or benchmarking separately, offering limited support for the full lifecycle of distributed agentic applications. This paper presents AgentWare, an AgenticOps framework that automates the provisioning, deployment, observability, and evaluation of agentic applications across Edge-to-Cloud infrastructures. AgentWare introduces an end-to-end lifecycle pipeline that automatically prepares heterogeneous execution environments, transforms user-defined agent implementations into distributed applications, deploys agent components across the continuum, and performs unified collection of execution traces, infrastructure telemetry, and evaluation metrics. The framework further supports automated semantic evaluation through LLM-as-a-Judge workflows and generates reproducible reports covering correctness, performance, resource utilization, and energy consumption. We demonstrate the applicability of AgentWare through a distributed book assistant agent deployed across real Edge-to-Cloud infrastructure under multiple deployment and model configurations. The results show that AgentWare enables systematic experimentation and evaluation of distributed agentic applications while significantly reducing the manual effort required for deployment, instrumentation, and analysis.

</details>


### [36] [Nereus: Adaptive Parallelism for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34645)
*Songlin Jiang,Tuo Shi,Sitong Zhang,Zeke Wang,Mario Di Francesco,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nereus是一个成本感知的运行时系统，通过低开销控制器和全局转换图，将LLM强化学习后训练任务动态适配为高效执行计划，显著降低延迟并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的RL后训练需在GPU集群上协调生成、推理、训练多个模型。运行过程中资源可用性、序列长度、内存压力和阶段瓶颈会变化，导致初始执行计划变慢甚至不可行。而动态适配共享GPU的模型面临挑战：判断新计划是否值得转换成本、复用分布式状态、跨模型与阶段协调GPU传输。

Method: Nereus采用低开销控制器选择内存可行的全局计划，并通过基于运行任务校准的成本模型决定是否进行转换。它将模型-阶段副本（某阶段的某个模型）的分布式状态表示为Elastic Model Unit，并使用全局转换图来排序这些单元的转换与GPU传输操作。

Result: 基于真实数据构建的trace中，在线TP/PP适配相对初始固定TP/PP布局（含DP扩展）将平均步延迟降低27.7%。在达到1024 GPU的1000步运行中，六次转换仅占总运行时间的0.079%。Nereus将端到端8B PPO吞吐量相比OpenRLHF提升2.14–7.27倍，相比Verl提升1.10–1.47倍（跨多种集群）。

Conclusion: Nereus通过成本感知的自适应运行时有效解决了RL后训练中执行计划随运行变化的适配问题，以极低的转换开销实现了显著的延迟降低与吞吐提升，优于现有系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for large language models (LLMs) coordinates multiple models across generation, inference, and training on GPU clusters. Several factors may change during a run, including resource availability, sequence length, memory pressure, and stage bottlenecks. As a consequence, an execution plan that was initially suitable can then become slow or even infeasible over time. However, adapting a job whose models share GPUs entails significant challenges: deciding whether a new plan is worth the transition cost, reusing the job's distributed state, and coordinating GPU transfers across models and stages.
  Nereus targets these challenges as a cost-aware runtime that adapts RL post-training jobs into efficient execution plans. Its low-overhead controller selects a memory-feasible global plan and admits the transition using a cost model calibrated against the running job. To estimate and execute a transition, Nereus represents the distributed state of each replica of a model-stage (one model in one stage) as an Elastic Model Unit. It then employs a global transition graph to order the transformations and GPU transfers of these units. In a trace built from real data, online TP/PP adaptation reduces average step latency by 27.7% relative to the initial fixed TP/PP layout with DP scaling. In a 1,000-step run reaching 1,024 GPUs, six transitions consume 0.079% of total run time. Nereus improves end-to-end 8B PPO throughput by 2.14--7.27$\times$ over OpenRLHF and by 1.10--1.47$\times$ over Verl across diverse clusters.

</details>


### [37] [Distributed Lower Bounds via Automatic Self-Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34753)
*Alkida Balliu,Francesco d'Amore,Dennis Olivetti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明“自归约轮消除”是经典轮消除的特例，从而将其推广为通用技术，并给出多种图问题的Ω(√log n)随机下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轮消除技术虽革新了分布式图问题硬度理解，却无法给出ω(log log n)轮的随机下界；而Khoury与Schild新提出的“自归约轮消除”虽突破了该限制，却仅针对极大匹配，缺乏通用性。

Method: 作者证明自归约是经典轮消除的一个特例，引入新的算法误差度量方式，并在该度量下扩展经典轮消除框架，识别出一大类问题，使其改进可完全黑盒地自动获得更强的随机下界。

Result: 在新误差度量下，经典轮消除可给出ω(log log n)的随机下界；作为应用，得到正则2-着色图上极大匹配、1/k-整数匹配与极大H-填充问题的Ω(√log n)随机下界。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了自归约轮消除，证明其本质属于经典轮消除，为一大类图问题提供了自动化的更强随机下界工具。

Abstract: The development of round elimination into a general-purpose technique [PODC 2019] marked a turning point in our understanding of the hardness of many graph problems in the distributed setting and led to several breakthrough results. However, the round elimination technique seems unable to yield randomized lower bounds of $ω(\log \log n)$ rounds as a function of the number $n$ of nodes.
  Very recently, Khoury and Schild [FOCS 2025] introduced a new technique called round elimination via self-reduction, which bypasses the limitations of classical round elimination. Using this approach, the authors show that any randomized algorithm for maximal matching requires $Ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ rounds in the LOCAL model. Their elegant technique is, in some respects, similar to classical round elimination while being fundamentally different in others. However, it is tailored specifically to maximal matching rather than being applicable to a broad class of problems.
  In this paper, we show that self-reduction is, in fact, a special case of classical round elimination, thereby turning it into a general-purpose approach. In particular, we introduce a new way to measure the error of an algorithm and show that, under this new measure, classical round elimination can indeed yield $ω(\log \log n)$ randomized lower bounds. More specifically, we identify a large class of problems for which this improvement is entirely black-box: once a problem is shown to belong to the class, stronger randomized lower bounds follow automatically from the classical round-elimination framework. As an application, we prove $Ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ randomized lower bounds for a range of graph problems, namely, maximal matching on regular $2$-colored graphs, $\frac{1}{k}$-integral matching, and maximal $H$-packing.

</details>

*Zijian Dai,Sen Han,Youhui Bai,Shannon Wang,Kan Wu,Jingkai Huang,Yuhang Wang,Jing Li,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Long-video generation with diffusion transformers (DiTs) produces extremely long token sequences, making attention a dominant inference bottleneck. Dynamic sparse attention reduces computation, but its realized speedup remains limited because irregular query-row execution degrades L2 cache locality and increases HBM traffic. We present WaveAlign, a lightweight, cache-aware query-row reordering framework for dynamic sparse attention. WaveAlign formulates row ordering as an optimization problem and approximates it with two stages. The first stage derives a low-rank SVD representation of sparse-mask rows and groups query rows with similar K/V access patterns, increasing K/V overlap among concurrently scheduled rows. The second stage exploits streaming GPU scheduling by sorting rows within each wave in descending order of their K/V-block counts, so that short rows from the current wave are followed by long rows from the next. This aligns K/V accesses across wave boundaries and enables shared blocks to be reused before eviction. An adaptive skip module avoids unprofitable reordering. By only permuting query and mask rows, WaveAlign preserves sparse-attention semantics and requires no changes to existing methods or backend kernels. Across two GPU architectures, two video DiTs, and four sparse-attention methods, WaveAlign raises the L2 cache hit ratio from 28.48%--36.35% to 79.38%--89.06%, reduces HBM read traffic by up to 92.11%, and achieves up to 1.25x kernel and 1.17x end-to-end generation speedup without quality loss.

</details>


### [39] [Accelerator Choice Is Not Enough: AlphaFold2 Inference on Cloud TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34818)
*Lorenzo Pazienza,Ihab El Bani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AlphaFold2虽可跨CPU/GPU/TPU无缝编译运行，但实测表明软件层（默认单芯片执行、批处理策略、缺乏分片标注）决定了用户实际获得的硬件收益，且加速比仅对单次测量campaign成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaFold2用JAX编写，同一份推理代码可在CPU、GPU和TPU上原样编译运行，这种可移植性容易让人误以为硬件加速器选择是用户唯一的关键决策。作者希望验证：真正决定用户实际获得多少算力的，是软件层而非硬件本身。

Method: 在同一测量campaign中运行同一AlphaFold2推理工作负载，对比Colab CPU运行时、NVIDIA T4 GPU与专用八芯片Cloud TPU v5e切片；测试默认执行路径、jax.vmap批处理、jax.pmap跨芯片映射与自动分片，并对新输入形状下首次调用的trace进行分析，五周后复跑以检验可复现性。

Result: TPU单芯片稳态每次调用0.47 s，T4为13.1 s（同campaign）；但默认路径仅用八芯片之一，按标价计算闲置容量使切片单次预测成本与GPU相当。jax.vmap批处理吞吐不超过单查询；jax.pmap跨芯片映射在匹配网格上实现8芯片达单芯片6.5–7.9倍吞吐；自动分片未改变每芯片占用，与复制一致，可能因AlphaFold2无分片标注。首次调用追踪中约四分之三时间花在JAX tracing与编译而非执行。五周后复跑，两个云端基线均未复现，GPU偏差约两倍。

Conclusion: 硬件加速比并非AlphaFold2用户实际收益的决定因素，软件层（默认执行路径、并行策略、分片标注）才是关键；且上述硬件比率仅对特定一次测量campaign成立，跨时间复现性差，提示性能结论需谨慎对待。

Abstract: AlphaFold2 is written in JAX, so the same inference code compiles and runs unchanged on CPUs, GPUs and Google Cloud TPUs. That portability makes the accelerator look like the main decision a user has to make. We show that it is not. Running one AlphaFold2 inference workload across a Colab CPU runtime, an NVIDIA T4 GPU and a dedicated eight-chip Cloud TPU v5e slice, we find a large hardware advantage for the TPU, 0.47 s per call in steady state on a single chip against 13.1 s on the T4 in the same measurement campaign, and three ways in which the software layer decides how much of it a user actually gets. The default execution path uses one chip of the eight, and at list prices the idle capacity makes the slice cost about as much per prediction as the GPU. Batching with jax.vmap never exceeds single-query throughput, while mapping queries across chips with jax.pmap gives eight chips 6.5-7.9x the throughput of one on a matched grid; automatic sharding leaves the per-chip footprint unchanged, consistent with replication, most plausibly because AlphaFold2 carries no sharding annotations. Our retained trace analysis of a first call at a new input shape reports about three quarters of the traced span in JAX tracing and compilation rather than execution. Reruns five weeks later reproduced neither cloud baseline, the GPU one off by roughly a factor of two, so the hardware ratio above is specific to one campaign.

</details>


### [40] [Distributed Service Orchestration in Edge-Cloud Continuum for Digital Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28855)
*Johirul Islam,Hafiz Faheem Shahid,Ijaz Ahmad,Tanesh Kumar,Ayan Mondal,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出三层镜像注册架构（公有、私有远程MEC、私有本地LAN）以提升边缘服务部署的可靠性与可用性，实验表明近端注册表在抗网络中断和部署性能上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字医疗服务需持续可访问，云端访问带来高延迟、高带宽消耗和安全隐患；边缘计算虽可缓解，但服务部署通常依赖集中式注册服务器，一旦网络或注册服务器不可用，边缘节点部署即失败。因此需要提升部署可靠性与服务可用性。

Method: 提出三层注册架构：DockerHub作为远程公有注册表、基于MEC的离场远程私有注册表、基于LAN的本地私有注册表。通过测量估计部署时间、网络与计算负载及能耗，评估从不同层部署纳米服务的性能与效率。

Result: 实验显示，在提升对网络中断容忍度的同时，本地私有和远程私有注册表在部署性能上均优于远程公有注册表。

Conclusion: 邻近感知的服务分发能有效提升服务部署的韧性、性能和效率。

Abstract: Today's digital healthcare services rely on various applications and functions that must be continuously accessible. Cloud computing enables global access to these services through public networks, which often also introduce increased latency, higher bandwidth consumption, and additional security risks compared to local operation. Edge computing mitigates these challenges by deploying cloud services closer to the end users and data sources, thereby improving resilience to network disruptions and reducing latency, bandwidth usage, and exposure to security threats. However, service deployment typically relies on the availability of centralized registry servers. Consequently, if the network connection or the registry server itself becomes unavailable, service deployment at the target edge node may fail. To address this, we propose a three-tier registry architecture to enhance deployment reliability and service availability, considering DockerHub as a remote public registry, an MEC-based off-premises registry as a remote private registry, and a LAN-based on-premises registry as a local private registry. The performance and efficiency are analyzed through measurements related to the estimated deployment time, inflicted network and computational load, and energy consumption, while the required nanoservices are deployed from different tiers. The experimental results indicate that alongside the improved tolerance to network disruptions, the local private and remote private registries also outperform the remote public registry in the deployment performance. The findings highlight the effectiveness of proximity-aware service distribution in improving the resilience, performance and efficiency of service deployment.

</details>


### [41] [TempoKV: Timely Staging of LLM KV Caches for Memory-Semantic Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35065)
*Jay H. Park,Hyungjun Kim,Dong Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 TempoKV，一种时序感知的资源承诺层，将“KV 复用知识”与“暂存资源获取”解耦，显著降低受保护快速层字节-时间开销并保持服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务中可复用的前缀 KV 缓存会超出 GPU 显存。SSD 支持的内存语义分层提供容量但快速层有限；逻辑上的 KV 命中不等于可立即供 GPU 取用。按需暂存会暴露 SSD 延迟，而立即暂存又会过早占用快速层容量。

Method: TempoKV 记录可复用 KV 命中为仅元数据声明，直到“距离取用的运行时估计时间”降至“使 KV 驻留并受保护的存储估计时间”时才请求承诺。估计值随运行时进展与暂存状态自适应，且承诺受可用受保护容量约束。在 vLLM 与 LMCache 上实现，基于 SSD 支持的 CXL 内存设备，不改变请求调度。

Result: 在两个模型和三种前缀缓存比例下，相比立即暂存，每请求受保护快速层字节-时间降低 63–91%，同时保留提前暂存的大部分服务收益。快速层容量从 100 GiB 降至 25 GiB 时，输出吞吐与 p95 TTFT 几乎不变。相比未修改的 LMCache Device-DAX L1 配置，p95 TTFT 最多降低 48.0%，输出吞吐最多提升 27.8%。

Conclusion: 通过时序感知的资源承诺，TempoKV 在不改变调度的前提下减少了快速层容量占用，并提升吞吐、降低首 token 延迟，验证了将复用知识与暂存资源获取解耦的有效性。

Abstract: Reusable prefix key-value (KV) caches can outgrow GPU memory in large language model (LLM) serving. A memory-semantic flash hierarchy offers SSD-backed capacity with a limited fast tier, but a logical KV hit is not necessarily ready for GPU retrieval. Demand staging exposes SSD latency, whereas immediate staging can reserve fast-tier capacity long before retrieval begins. We present TempoKV, a timing-aware resource-commitment layer that separates early knowledge of reuse from the acquisition of staging resources. It records reusable-KV hits as metadata-only claims and requests commitment when the runtime-estimated time until retrieval falls to the storage-estimated time needed to make KV resident and protected against eviction. These estimates adapt to runtime progress and staging state, while commitment remains subject to available protected capacity. We implement TempoKV in vLLM and LMCache on an SSD-backed CXL memory device without changing request scheduling. Across two models and three prefix cache ratios, TempoKV reduces protected fast-tier byte-time per request by 63-91% versus immediate staging while retaining much of the serving benefit of advance staging. In a fast-tier capacity sweep, output throughput and p95 time to first token (TTFT) remain nearly unchanged as capacity decreases from 100 to 25 GiB. Compared with unmodified LMCache's Device-DAX L1 configuration, TempoKV reduces p95 TTFT by up to 48.0% and increases output throughput by up to 27.8%.

</details>


### [42] [AReaL-TIK: Stateful Agentic Optimization of Unified RL Kernels through an Optimization IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35140)
*Ran Yan,Youhe Jiang,Jiayi Nie,Wenshuang Li,Yingqi Peng,Taiyi Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelBraid：一个从位级一致手调实现出发的智能体框架，通过优化中间表示组织源码搜索，统一 rollout 与策略更新的 GPU 内核，在保数值一致的前提下提升 RL 后训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步 PPO/GRPO 中 rollout 与策略更新使用不同 GPU 内核，会造成数值不一致，从而扰动当前 token 概率与 rollout 时概率的比值；重新计算 rollout 对数概率虽可避免偏差，但引入额外前向计算，因此需要位级一致且可复用的统一内核。

Method: 基于手工位级一致实现构建 KernelBraid。其优化 IR 将实现与修改关联到数值要求、负载测量与推导历史以组织源码搜索；智能体协调改动并保留已验证中间结果，提升需通过正确性检查并在各负载限制内改善总体延迟。

Result: H20 上 12 个端到端训练配置相对带对数概率重算的 AReaL 平均吞吐 1.10x，平均训练奖励比约 1.00x；15 对模型-GPU 的隔离层分析平均加速 1.40x；A100/H20/H200 上 10 个算子全部通过位级检查；统一注意力搜索在 7M LLM token 下获得 2.52x 延迟加速；消融评估了保留证据与分支探索的贡献。

Conclusion: KernelBraid 表明通过智能体驱动的、以数值一致为约束的内核统一与优化，可在不损害训练正确性的情况下提升 RL 后训练吞吐；代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training often uses distinct GPU kernels for rollout and policy update. In synchronous PPO and GRPO, numerical disagreement can perturb ratios between current token probabilities and those assigned during rollout. Recomputing rollout log-probabilities with the policy-update backend avoids this discrepancy but adds a forward pass. Bitwise-consistent unified kernels permit reuse when the policy snapshot and probability processing match the objective. Their optimization must preserve agreement across distinct execution regimes. We present KernelBraid, an agentic framework starting from a hand-tuned, bitwise-consistent implementation. Its optimization intermediate representation (IR) organizes source-code search by linking implementations and modifications to numerical requirements, workload measurements, and derivation history. The agent coordinates changes and retains verified intermediates for further exploration; promotion requires passing correctness checks and improving aggregate latency within per-workload limits. Across 12 end-to-end training configurations on H20, KernelBraid achieves 1.10x average throughput relative to AReaL with log-probability recomputation, and the mean training-reward ratio rounds to 1.00x. Isolated-layer profiling yields 1.40x average speedup in summed phase time across 15 model-GPU pairs. Operator-level evaluation covers correctness and performance for 10 operators on A100, H20, and H200, all passing the prescribed bitwise checks. Unified-attention search achieves 2.52x speedup in summed workload latency over the starting implementation using 7M LLM tokens; ablations assess the contributions of retained evidence and branch exploration to search efficiency and attained performance. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/areal-project/AReaL-TIK.

</details>


### [43] [Analyzing Solana's Blocks and Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35171)
*Yaron Hay,Dvir David Biton,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了约一百万个Solana区块，统计了区块大小、交易执行时间单位与费用、被调用的Solana程序，并基于交易的读写集分析了区块内的冲突及冲突图，以评估网络的并行潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana是最流行的区块链之一，也是智能合约（dApp）使用最广泛的区块链之一。理解其执行的智能合约类型及其相互作用，对现代区块链设计者和智能合约开发者都极具价值，尤其是Solana主打的并行执行优势需要实证数据来验证。

Method: 收集并分析约一百万个近期Solana区块。统计每区块交易数、单笔交易的执行时间单位与手续费、以及被调用的Solana程序。同时依据Solana规定的每笔交易声明的读集（readset）与写集（writeset），分析区块内产生的冲突并构建冲突图。

Result: 给出了区块大小分布、交易执行时间单位、费用以及被调用程序的统计数据；并基于读写集得到了区块内冲突与冲突图的统计结果。数据与代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Solana上智能合约的执行特征及区块内冲突程度提供了实证依据，这些冲突统计对评估Solana所宣称的并行执行潜力至关重要，且数据与代码开源便于后续研究复现与扩展。

Abstract: Solana is one of the most popular blockchains, and is arguably the most widely used blockchain for smart contracts, also known as dApps. Understanding the types of smart contracts that are being executed by Solana and their interplay is therefore highly beneficial both for designers of modern blockchains and developers of smart contracts. To that end, in this paper we analyze a million recent Solana blocks. We report statistics about the size of blocks (number of transactions per block), execution time units for individual transactions and fees, and invoked Solana programs. Further, based on the declared readset and writeset of each transaction, as mandated by Solana, we analyze the conflicts and corresponding conflict graphs arising within each block. These latter statistics are important to understand the potential for parallelism in the network, which is one of the main claimed benefits of Solana. The data and code are available in open source.

</details>


### [44] [WavePP: High-Throughput Pipeline Parallel LLM Prefill under Prefix Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35263)
*Aaryam Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WavePP 是一个基于 TensorRT-LLM 的 prefill 运行时，通过将请求准入与流水线执行重叠，异步完成跨阶段前缀复用查找、缓存保护和空间预留，并动态规划分块大小，从而提升流水线并行下的 prefill 吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水线并行可提升 prefill 吞吐，但保持流水线满载需要高效调度和请求准备。在各阶段独立保存与驱逐缓存状态的系统中，本地缓存命中并不能保证前缀可在整个流水线复用，协调开销会拖慢请求准入节奏、降低整体吞吐。

Method: WavePP 将请求准入与流水线执行重叠：异步寻找可在所有阶段复用的前缀，保护已缓存状态，并为剩余输入预留空间，同时先前请求继续执行；随后动态规划每个请求的分块大小以最大化流水线填充；各阶段在执行请求前完成本地准备。

Result: 在相同系统与流水线拓扑下，WavePP 在 GLM 5.2 与 MiniMax M2.7 的 40 个测试设置中有 37 个提升 TensorRT-LLM 的 prefill 吞吐；在高缓存复用、并发 128 时吞吐分别提升 2.91 倍与 2.02 倍。在 Kimi K3 的 28 个设置中，并发 8 及以上的全部 18 个设置里，其吞吐均高于 TRT-LLM、SGLang 和 vLLM 的张量/专家并行与流水线并行基线。

Conclusion: 通过将请求准入与流水线执行重叠以及动态分块规划，WavePP 有效缓解了跨阶段缓存协调开销，在多种模型与设置下显著提升流水线并行 prefill 吞吐，并优于多个主流推理框架的并行基线。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism can improve prefill throughput by processing multiple request chunks concurrently across different stages of the model. However, keeping the pipeline fully utilized requires efficient scheduling and request preparation. In systems where stages retain and evict cache state independently, a local cache hit does not guarantee that the same prefix can be reused across the pipeline. Here, coordination overhead can impede request admission cadence and thus reduce overall throughput. In this paper, we present WavePP, a prefill runtime built on top of TensorRT-LLM that addresses these challenges by overlapping request admission with pipeline execution. WavePP asynchronously finds a prefix that can be reused across all stages, protects the cached state, and reserves space for the remaining input while earlier requests continue to execute. It subsequently plans the chunk sizes of each request dynamically to maximize pipeline fill. Each stage then completes the local preparation before executing the request. In the same system and pipeline topology, WavePP improves TensorRT-LLM's prefill throughput in 37 of 40 tested settings on GLM 5.2 and MiniMax M2.7. At concurrency 128 with high cache reuse, these changes increase throughput by factors of 2.91 and 2.02, respectively. Across 28 Kimi K3 settings, WavePP also has the highest measured throughput in all 18 settings at concurrency eight or higher, compared with tensor/expert-parallel and pipeline-parallel baselines from TRT-LLM, SGLang, and vLLM.

</details>


### [45] [Weaver: A System for AI-RAN Compute Sharing with Foundation Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35276)
*Leyang Xue,Tianxin Wang,Xin Zhe Khooi,Jiaxun Yang,Dheeraj Mahendiran,Yufeng Xia,Mun Choon Chan,Myungjin Lee,Mahesh K. Marina*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Weaver 系统，利用 AI-RAN 基站中的闲置 GPU 容量，在不影响 RAN 延迟性能的前提下，机会性地进行基础模型的去中心化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN 基础设施为基站配备 GPU 加速硬件，除主 RAN 处理外产生大量闲置算力。作者希望将这类可用于去中心化基础模型训练的算力加以利用，因为基础模型训练是最耗费算力的 AI 工作负载之一。

Method: 作者首次在微尺度（单站点内传输时隙间）和宏尺度（跨站点间）刻画 AI-RAN 系统的闲置 GPU 容量，发现 40-85% 的 GPU 容量未被使用，单站点呈时间突发性，但跨站点呈空间互补性。为此提出 Weaver：采用 RAN 优先设计，在 MAC 调度器中集成闲置算力控制器，通过计算感知调度平滑 RAN 的 GPU 需求并释放更多可用闲置算力；再用两级弹性训练框架适应站点内和跨站点的动态异构闲置容量。

Result: 在符合 O-RAN 的系统原型上实验表明，Weaver 可创造最多 4.9 倍可用闲置算力，并利用最多 83% 的可用闲置容量。在多站点测试平台上，相比基线方法，训练吞吐量提升 2.1-3.7 倍。

Conclusion: Weaver 验证了在 AI-RAN 系统中安全高效地复用闲置 GPU 算力进行基础模型训练的可行性，兼顾 RAN 性能与训练效率。

Abstract: The emergence of AI-RAN infrastructure, which equips cell sites with GPU-accelerated hardware, creates an opportunity to colocate non-RAN workloads with primary RAN processing. We explore using this spare capacity for decentralized training of foundation models (FMs), one of the most compute-intensive AI workloads. We present the first characterization of spare GPU capacity in AI-RAN systems at both micro-scale--across transmission slots within a cell site--and macro-scale--across sites. Our analysis finds that 40-85% of GPU capacity is unused; although this capacity is temporally bursty at individual sites, it is spatially complementary across sites. To safely and efficiently harness these resources, we present Weaver, a system that opportunistically trains FMs alongside latency-critical RAN workloads without degrading RAN performance. Weaver adopts a RAN-first design: a spare-compute controller integrated into the MAC scheduler uses compute-aware scheduling to smooth RAN GPU demand and exposes more usable spare GPU capacity. A two-level elastic training framework then adapts to dynamic, heterogeneous spare capacity within and across sites. Experiments on an O-RAN-aligned system prototype show that Weaver creates up to 4.9x more usable spare compute and utilizes up to 83% of the available spare capacity. On a multi-site testbed, Weaver improves training throughput by 2.1-3.7x over baseline approaches.

</details>


### [46] [TopoEP: Topology-Aware Load Balancing for Expert-Parallel MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35481)
*Jiacheng Zhu,Xie Zhao,Gongming Zhao,Hongli Xu,Yao Fei,Jin Fang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TopoEP：GPU原生、拓扑感知的MoE负载均衡系统，无需host同步，在32卡H800上提升端到端训练吞吐6.2%–11.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态路由导致专家并行MoE训练严重负载不均，热点专家所在GPU成为straggler，拖慢每层专家并行阶段。现有EPLB方案多在CPU上计算均衡计划，需device-host传输与跨rank同步，逐层逐microbatch调度开销大；其规划公式也忽略了现代scale-up/scale-out GPU集群的分层通信成本。

Method: 提出GPU原生的拓扑感知负载均衡系统TopoEP。在每个MoE层和训练microbatch中，将当前路由结果转化为热点专家复制与token重路由决策，并执行计划而不依赖数据相关的host同步，降低关键路径开销。使用确定性GPU求解器，先做跨节点放置再做节点内细化，使所有rank能独立产生按位一致的规划。

Result: 在32-GPU NVIDIA H800集群上，将TopoEP集成到Megatron-LM后，在三个代表性MoE模型上端到端训练吞吐提升6.2%–11.4%。

Conclusion: TopoEP通过GPU原生、拓扑感知、无host同步的每层每microbatch负载均衡规划，有效缓解MoE专家并行straggler问题，显著提升大规模训练效率。

Abstract: Dynamic routing creates severe load imbalance in large-scale expert-parallel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training, turning GPUs that host hot experts into stragglers. As each MoE layer waits for its slowest rank, these stragglers prolong the expert-parallel stage and reduce overall training efficiency. Existing expert-parallelism load-balancing (EPLB) systems commonly compute load-balancing plans on the CPU, incurring device--host data transfers and cross-rank synchronization that make scheduling at every layer and microbatch expensive. Their planning formulations also overlook the hierarchical communication costs of modern scale-up and scale-out GPU clusters.
  We present \textit{TopoEP}, a GPU-native, topology-aware load-balancing system for large-scale MoE training. At each MoE layer and training microbatch, \textit{TopoEP} converts the current routing result into hot-expert replication and token-rerouting decisions and executes the resulting plan without data-dependent host synchronization, reducing critical-path overhead. To generate these decisions, \textit{TopoEP} uses a deterministic GPU solver that performs inter-node placement followed by intra-node refinement, allowing all ranks to independently produce bitwise-identical plans. On a 32-GPU NVIDIA H800 cluster, integrating \textit{TopoEP} with Megatron-LM improves end-to-end training throughput by 6.2\%--11.4\% across three representative MoE models.

</details>


### [47] [GPUPhysBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Correct and Efficient GPU Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35639)
*Yuchen Sun,Jinjin He,Sinan Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GPUPhysBench，一个包含50项任务的基准，用于测试编码智能体能否在固定预算下编写、编译、测试并优化物理仿真的GPU代码，同时评估正确性与运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为物理仿真编写快速GPU代码非常困难：实现既要保持数值精度，又要处理不规则数据访问、同步和迭代求解器。现有编码智能体评估缺乏针对此类物理仿真工作负载的测试，因此需要专门的基准来同时考察正确实现数值方法与高效运行的能力。

Method: 构建GPUPhysBench基准，包含50项任务，覆盖流体、可变形固体和颗粒材料，从单个仿真算子到完整模拟器。智能体在固定时间预算内，借助NVIDIA GPU编写、编译、测试并优化GPU代码。评估报告通过率，以及相对于专家优化参考实现的运行时性能。

Result: 在六对前沿模型-框架组合的单次评估中，两个最强组合通过了全部50项任务，但即使最快者也只有22%的任务达到参考速度的0.9倍以上，且没有任何提交比参考实现快超过5%。最大差距出现在碰撞检测、约束求解和迭代求解器方面。

Conclusion: GPUPhysBench将物理仿真工作负载引入编码智能体评估，同时测试正确实现数值方法与使其高效运行的能力。

Abstract: Writing fast GPU code for physical simulation is difficult: implementations must preserve numerical accuracy while handling irregular data access, synchronization, and iterative solvers. We introduce GPUPhysBench, a benchmark of 50 tasks testing whether coding agents can meet these demands. Tasks cover fluids, deformable solids, and granular materials, from individual simulation operators to complete simulators. Agents write, compile, test, and optimize GPU code with access to a NVIDIA GPU under fixed time budgets. We report pass rates and runtime performance relative to expert-optimized reference implementations. In a single-attempt evaluation of six frontier model-harness pairs, the two strongest pass all 50 tasks, but even the fastest reaches at least 0.9 the reference speed on only 22% of them, and no submission is more than 5% faster than the reference. The largest gaps arise in collision detection, constraint solving, and iterative solvers. GPUPhysBench brings physical simulation workloads to coding-agent evaluation, testing both the ability to implement numerical methods correctly and the ability to make them run efficiently.

</details>


### [48] [Dynamic Wakeup under Costly Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35662)
*Umesh Biswas,Maxwell Young*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究动态唤醒问题，在时隙多址信道中，数据包在任意时刻激活，目标是快速获得一次成功传输。提出随机算法Lowball，在无冲突检测且未知包数n的条件下，同时优化延迟与碰撞代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有唤醒问题研究大多只关注首次成功前的时隙数（延迟），忽略了碰撞可能带来的额外显著延迟（每次碰撞代价C）。因此需要同时控制延迟和碰撞代价。

Method: 设计并分析随机算法Lowball，无需冲突检测、无需知道包数n。针对常数0<ε≤1/2，分析其在自适应、非预测性对手下的表现。

Result: 存在常数K>0：当C≥K lg^{1/ε} n时，Lowball的期望延迟为O(C^{1/2+ε} ln C)，期望碰撞代价为O(√C)；低于该阈值时，两者期望均为O(n log^{Θ(1/ε)} n)。算法成功概率为1。对于传输概率仅依赖C和本地年龄、且同时激活包使用相同概率调度的一类算法，证明期望延迟与期望碰撞代价的最大值为Ω(√C)。

Conclusion: Lowball在动态唤醒问题中实现了延迟与碰撞代价的良好权衡，并在受限算法类中达到接近最优的下界，表明其在无冲突检测、未知n的条件下具有理论最优性。

Abstract: The wakeup problem captures a fundamental symmetry-breaking challenge among devices sharing a communication channel. We study the dynamic setting, where packets become active at arbitrary times on a time-slotted multiple access channel. In each slot, a transmission succeeds if and only if exactly one packet transmits; two or more simultaneous transmissions cause a collision. The goal is to obtain a successful transmission quickly.
  Prior work on wakeup has largely focused on the number of slots until the first success, referred to as the latency. However, a collision may incur substantial additional delay, represented by a per-collision cost $C$. We therefore seek to control both latency and the collision cost of an execution, defined as $C$ times its number of collisions.
  We design and analyze a randomized algorithm for dynamic wakeup, Lowball, without collision detection or knowledge of the number of packets, $n$. Fix a constant $0<ε\le 1/2$. There is a constant $K>0$ such that, when $C\ge K\lg^{1/ε} n$, Lowball has expected latency $O(C^{1/2+ε}\ln C)$ and expected collision cost $O(\sqrt{C})$. Below this threshold, both expectations are $O(n\log^{Θ(1/ε)} n)$. These guarantees hold against an adaptive, non-anticipating adversary, and the algorithm succeeds with probability 1. For algorithms in which each packet's transmission probability depends only on $C$ and the packet's local age, with packets activated together using the same probability schedule, we prove that the maximum of expected latency and expected collision cost is $Ω(\sqrt{C})$.

</details>

### [49] [Data Processing for Offline Evaluation in Recommender Systems: a Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31696)
*Alberto Carlo Maria Mancino,Angela Di Fazio,Danilo Danese,Matteo Attimonelli,Daniele Malitesta,Antonio Ferrara,Claudio Pomo,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了推荐系统离线评估中的数据预处理实践，提出统一框架与分类法，并揭示现有实践在数据变换、辅助信息表示与数据划分协议上的同质化与不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估是推荐系统研究的主流实验范式，但已有研究多聚焦推荐模型与评估方法，对模型训练前的数据处理决策关注不足；这些决策直接影响算法可用信息及实验结果的可比性与可复现性。

Method: 采用跨领域系统性综述方法，考察从数据集选择、交互表示到数据准备、多模态特征提取及训练-验证-测试划分的全流程数据管线，覆盖协同、序列、会话、图、知识感知、情境感知、多模态、联邦、跨域、对比学习与LLM推荐等范式；并引入统一的数据变换与特征提取框架和分类法，区分数据准备与多模态辅助信息表示提取。

Result: 实证分析显示，当前实践以少数数据集层面的变换（尤其是基于支持度的过滤）为主导，依赖表示的变换较少；辅助信息准备与表示方式存在显著异质性；数据划分协议描述不一致，相似标签可能掩盖不同的实验条件。

Conclusion: 推荐系统离线评估的数据处理实践缺乏标准化，影响结果可比性与可复现性；该综述提供的框架与分类法有助于规范数据处理描述，提升实验透明度与研究可比性。

Abstract: Offline evaluation is the dominant experimental paradigm in recommender systems research, enabling reproducible and cost-effective comparisons on historical interaction data. Yet, while considerable attention has been devoted to recommendation models and evaluation methodologies, the data processing decisions that precede model training have received less scrutiny. These decisions determine the information available to recommendation algorithms and can affect the comparability and reproducibility of experimental results. This survey provides a systematic, cross-domain characterisation of data processing practices for the offline evaluation of recommender systems. We examine the data-centric pipeline, from dataset selection and interaction representation to data preparation, multimodal feature extraction, and train-validation-test splitting. Our analysis spans recommendation paradigms, including collaborative, sequential, session-based, graph-based, knowledge-aware, context-aware, multimodal, federated, cross-domain, contrastive-learning, and LLM-based recommendation. Beyond reviewing existing practices, we introduce a unified framework and taxonomy for describing data transformations and feature-extraction strategies, distinguishing data preparation from the extraction of representations from multimodal side information. Our empirical analysis reveals a landscape dominated by a narrow set of dataset-level transformations, particularly support-driven filtering, while representation-dependent transformations remain less common. We further identify substantial heterogeneity in how auxiliary information is prepared and represented, as well as inconsistencies in the specification of data splitting protocols, where similar labels may conceal different experimental conditions.

</details>


### [50] [MM-VeriRec: Failure-Guided Fusion for Verifiable Agentic Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31718)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-VeriRec，一个可验证的多模态推荐协议与失败引导融合方法，用于处理隐藏视觉约束、图文不匹配与不可能任务拒答，并将失败转化为可操作的诊断标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像常携带文本元数据无法表达的推荐约束（如电影海报需显暗调、产品需极简风格），且视觉上不可能满足的请求应被拒答。现有智能体多模态推荐器难以判断视觉证据何时具有决定性，也无法在无有效动作时拒答。

Method: MM-VeriRec 基于真实电影海报与产品图像数据集构建任务，用确定性视觉属性验证每条推荐，并把失败转化为三类可操作标签：文本陷阱跟随、视觉忽视、错误接受。方法强调融合不是简单拼接模态，而应诊断哪一模态失效并路由到相应修复。同时设置自适应属性门控与一个独立推导的留一法 CLIP 检测器（非对齐门控）进行迁移测试。

Result: 在 MM-ML 1M 与 Amazon Reviews 数据集上，更强的文本与视觉嵌入提升了检索效果但未消除上述失败模式，而失败引导融合能消除。非对齐门控下，独立推导的留一法 CLIP 检测器仍达到 0.7028 与 0.6111 的视觉接地成功率，高于 VBPR 基线与普通融合。文本—视觉差距在两个 LLM 家族中均可复现，且有效修复方式随领域不同而不同。

Conclusion: MM-VeriRec 既是一个基准，也是一个面向可信智能体多模态推荐的实用诊断闭环，为可验证的多模态推荐提供了可诊断、可迁移的评估与修复框架。

Abstract: Images often carry recommendation constraints that text metadata only hints at. A movie may need to look dark, a product may need a minimal style, and a visually impossible request should be rejected. Agentic multimodal recommenders must reason over text-image evidence, decide when visual evidence is decisive, and abstain when no valid action exists. We introduce MM-VeriRec, a verifiable multimodal recommendation protocol and failure-guided fusion method for hidden visual constraints, image-text mismatch, and impossible-task abstention. MM-VeriRec builds tasks from real movie-poster and product-image datasets, verifies each recommendation with deterministic visual attributes, and converts failures into actionable labels: text-trap following, visual ignorance, and false acceptance. Fusion should not merely concatenate modalities, but should diagnose which modality failed and route to the appropriate repair. Across MM-ML 1M and Amazon Reviews datasets, stronger text and vision embeddings improve retrieval but do not remove these failure modes, whereas failure-guided fusion does. The adaptive attribute gate reads the same tags the verifier checks and its scores are verifier-aligned upper bounds testing whether the taxonomy routes to the correct repair. More informative is transfer under a non-aligned gate: an independently derived leave-one-out CLIP detector still reaches 0.7028 and 0.6111 visual-grounded success, above both a VBPR baseline and plain fusion. The text-versus-visual gap reproduces across two LLM families, and the repair that helps differs by domain. MM-VeriRec is both a benchmark and a practical diagnostic loop for trustworthy agentic multimodal recommendation.

</details>


### [51] [Overview of the TREC 2025 Million Large Language Models track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31921)
*Evangelos Kanoulas,Panagiotis Eustratiadis,Jamie Callan,Mark Sanderson,Yongkang Li,Jingfen Qiao,Gabrielle Poerwawinata,Vaishali Pal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出以"检索"范式实现代理式AI中的专家LLM选择：由助手代理通过观察模型行为动态推断其专长，而非依赖静态元数据或人工描述，并通过TREC Million LLM Track构建了首个大规模专家检索基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI生态中，各代理拥有专门专长，专家选择直接影响系统整体性能。现有方法通常假设模型数量少且文档完备，依赖静态元数据或手写描述，但真实世界的专长高度多样，无法被这类方式充分刻画。作者预期未来将出现数以百万计的专用语言模型，因此需要一种可扩展、自适应的专家选择机制。

Method: 提出基于检索的范式：助手代理通过检查模型的可观测行为来动态推断其专长。收到用户查询后，助手根据候选LLM所展现的能力对其进行排序，从而实现高效、自适应的专家选择。TREC Million LLM Track将该范式落地：把检索目标从文档转向专家LLM，向参赛者提供由超过一千个LLM的查询、答案与对数概率构成的发现集，要求其为每个模型推断有意义的专长表示。

Result: 构建了包含一千多个LLM的发现集（查询、答案、对数概率），并定义了测试流程：给定未见过的测试查询，系统需按预期性能对LLM进行排序。这成为代理式AI中专家检索的首个大规模基准。

Conclusion: 随着专用语言模型数量不断增长，静态元数据不足以描述模型专长；以行为观察为基础的检索式专家选择更具适应性与可扩展性，TREC Million LLM Track为该方向提供了首个大规模评测基准。

Abstract: Agentic AI envisions ecosystems of intelligent agents collaboratively solving complex tasks with minimal human intervention. In such ecosystems, each agent possesses specialized expertise, making effective expert selection central to overall system performance. While most current approaches assume a small number of well-documented models, real-world expertise is far more diverse and cannot be adequately captured through static metadata or hand-written descriptions. We anticipate a future with millions of specialized language models (LLMs), each excelling in different domains or problem types. Rather than relying on predefined capability statements, we propose a retrieval-based paradigm in which an assistant agent infers expertise dynamically by examining models' observable behavior. Upon receiving a user query, the assistant ranks candidate LLMs based on demonstrated competence, enabling efficient and adaptive expert selection. The TREC Million LLM Track operationalizes this paradigm by shifting the retrieval target from documents to expert LLMs. Participants are given a discovery set consisting of queries, answers, and log-probabilities from more than one thousand LLMs and are challenged to infer meaningful expertise representations for each model. Given an unseen test query, systems must then rank the LLMs according to their expected performance, providing the first large-scale benchmark for expertise retrieval in agentic AI.

</details>


### [52] [Overview and Analysis of the RecSys Challenge 2026: Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33045)
*Seungheon Doh,Sergio Oramas,Bruno Sguerra,Abhinav Bohra,Claudio Pomo,Francesco Barile*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍RecSys Challenge 2026会话音乐推荐挑战赛，将其定义为物品推荐与回复生成联合任务，并综述数据集、评测协议与官方结果；进一步以"检索—重排—生成"框架分析16个参赛系统，归纳有效设计并指出基准与评测协议的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话音乐推荐需要同时完成大规模曲库检索与基于证据的自然语言回复生成，但缺乏统一的共享评测任务与对有效系统设计的系统认识，因此该挑战赛旨在提供数据、协议与跨系统分析。

Method: 提出"检索—重排—生成"统一框架，对16个被接收系统进行对比分析，并开展主办方的统计分析，考察推荐性能在用户、请求与对话上下文间的差异。

Result: 强系统通常融合异构候选来源，并在学习式重排中保留来源特有证据；稳健设计还包括以多轮对话与物品信号支撑冷启动检索、使用意图检测器、以及建模完整多轮上下文而非仅当前查询。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来会话推荐系统与共享评测提供实践指导，同时指出基准局限：单一标准答案的相关性标注与对合成对话的教师强制评测。

Abstract: The RecSys Challenge 2026 studies conversational music recommendation as a joint item recommendation and response generation problem: given a multi-turn dialogue, systems must retrieve relevant tracks from a large catalog and produce a grounded natural-language response. This paper presents the challenge task, dataset, evaluation protocol, and official results. Beyond the leaderboard, we analyze the 16 accepted systems through a common retrieve--rerank--generate framework and examine how recommendation performance varies across users, requests, and dialogue contexts. Strong systems commonly combine heterogeneous candidate sources and preserve source-specific evidence for learned reranking. Across the system papers and our organizer-side analysis, robust design also means 1) grounding cold-start retrieval in multi-turn conversation and item signals, 2) using intent detectors, and 3) modeling the full multi-turn context rather than the current query alone. We further identify limitations of the benchmark and evaluation protocol, including single-ground-truth relevance and teacher-forced evaluation of synthetic dialogues. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for future conversational recommender systems and shared evaluation efforts.

</details>


### [53] [Recipe-Matching, Not Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31927)
*Ali Habibullah,Mohammad Alshiekh,Yazan Alshoibi,Salman Khan,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文发现MathNet-Retrieve基准的可被LLM构建的"配方式"训练数据显著抬高，R@1的提升中约一半至三分之二来自数据由LLM撰写这一事实，而非真实检索能力，基准分数与真实保留能力出现倒挂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准本应测试检索模型匹配同一数学问题的能力，但训练数据若按基准发布提示由LLM生成，可能让分数虚高。作者想厘清"配方匹配"到底买了多少超出基准声称能力之外的分。

Method: 同一底座训练两个模型，唯一差别是训练文件：一个是另一厂商LLM与评判器按基准提示写的配对，另一个是用计算机代数验证、无任何LLM参与的配对。辅以非LLM复述控制（LLM改写、回译）和跨语言真实重复测试来分离效应。

Result: 配方模型在易层领先45个R@1点；非LLM复述控制显示其中一半到三分之二源于配对由LLM撰写。剩余15-25点仅在基准自身提示下出现，在跨语言真实重复上消失。难层奖励配方配对结构，单靠LLM改写得零分，加负例才解锁，且每个奏效负例牺牲跨语言分数。SABER-Math上攻击失败，MELD有迁移但无保留损失。

Conclusion: 只有在MathNet-Retrieve上能把"基准分上升、真实保留下降"的倒挂归因于训练文件的一处改动。作者发布无生成器的重复评估、近失配测试和三个训练模型。

Abstract: MathNet-Retrieve asks a retriever to find, for a math problem, a document stating the same problem. An LLM under one fixed prompt writes each gold document and its near-miss distractors; LLM judges filter them. We call this procedure the "recipe", training on pairs built the same way "recipe-matching", and ask how much score it buys beyond the ability the benchmark claims to test. Two models from one base, matched in rows and settings, differ only in the training file: pairs written under the benchmark's published prompt by another vendor's LLM and judge, or computer-algebra-verified pairs with no LLM anywhere. The first leads by 45 R@1 points on the easy tier. By a non-LLM paraphrase control, half to two thirds of that gap comes from the pairs being LLM-written at all: LLM rewrites under two unrelated prompts, with the verified model's negatives, recover 30 and 22 of the 45 points; back-translations with the same negatives recover almost none. The remaining 15 to 25 points appear only under the benchmark's own prompt and vanish on real duplicates no generator wrote, the same problem in two languages. The hard tier rewards the recipe's pair structure, a deep rewrite against a minimal-edit near-miss: LLM rewrites alone score zero on it, attaching negatives unlocks it, and every negative that does so costs cross-language points; the sets scoring highest on it separate near-misses no LLM wrote worse than LLM rewrites with verified negatives. MELD also moves when a model trains on pairs built its way, without losing retention; on SABER-Math the registered attack fails, and the one gain, from its LLM-written summaries, is small but holds at a matched budget. Only on MathNet-Retrieve could we pin an inversion, benchmark score up and real retention down, to one edit of a training file. We release the generator-free duplicate evaluations, the near-miss test and three trained models.

</details>


### [54] [When Does Dense Retrieval Need Asymmetric Geometry? A Bias-Variance Theory of Shared and Dual Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32488)
*Maojun Sun,Yancheng Yuan,Jian Huang,Ruijian Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为稠密检索中"共享 vs 双路"投影选择建立了偏差-方差理论，提出 CARS 选择器，并通过检索实验验证其能显著降低遗憾并提升几何选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索支撑 RAG、语义搜索与问答，但在共享投影与双路查询-文档投影之间如何选择缺乏理论依据，实践者只能凭经验决定。

Method: 针对低秩双线性打分提出偏差-方差理论：共享投影对应半正定算子，双路投影可实现任意低秩算子；推导两者的精确近似差距，并证明一个局部高斯边界——当方向性信号平方超过其额外自由度的估计代价时，双路风险更低。据此提出交叉拟合的非对称风险选择器 CARS，从训练对中估计可复现的方向性信号；其高斯版本给出精确的选择功效与遗憾公式。

Result: 在多个数据集与嵌入模型上：当查询旋转从 0° 增至 90°，Dual 相对 Shared 的平均 NDCG@10 优势翻倍以上；在秩-样本量网格中，n=32 时 Shared 在 16 格中胜 13 格，而 n=1024 与 n=2048 时 Dual 胜全部 32 格；168 条可比的算子风险曲线全部随数据增长向 Dual 移动。相比两个固定几何基线，CARS 将留出遗憾降低 49–96%，几何选择平均准确率达 90.1%。

Conclusion: 共享与双路投影的取舍由方向性信号强度与额外参数估计代价的权衡决定；随数据量增加最优选择系统性地偏向 Dual。CARS 能有效近似该边界，在低遗憾下自适应选择几何结构。

Abstract: Dense retrieval powers retrieval-augmented generation, semantic search, and question answering, yet the theoretical basis for choosing between shared and dual query-document projections remains unclear. We introduce a bias-variance theory for low-rank bilinear scoring. Shared projections induce positive-semidefinite operators, whereas dual projections realize arbitrary low-rank operators. We derive their exact approximation gap and prove a local Gaussian boundary: dual has lower risk exactly when squared directional signal exceeds the estimation cost of its additional degrees of freedom. This boundary motivates the Cross-fitted Asymmetry Risk Selector (CARS), which estimates reproducible directional signal from training pairs; its Gaussian counterpart admits exact selection-power and regret formulas. Guided by the theory, we run retrieval experiments across multiple datasets and embedding models. The mean Dual-minus-Shared NDCG@10 advantage more than doubles as query rotation increases from 0 degrees to 90 degrees. In the rank-sample-size grids, Shared wins 13 of 16 cells at n=32, whereas Dual wins all 32 cells at n=1024 and n=2048. Consistent with this shift, all 168 comparable operator-risk curves move toward Dual as training data grow. Compared to the two fixed-geometry baselines, CARS reduces held-out regret by 49-96% and achieves 90.1% mean geometry-selection accuracy.

</details>


### [55] [Concepts Complement Dense Semantics: Learning Compact Sparse Spaces for Text-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32671)
*Yoonseo Kim,Jungwoo Choi,Cheonyoung Park,Youngwook Kim,Yongho Song,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRASP提出一个紧凑且可归因的稀疏学习框架，从语料中挖掘视觉-文本概念，通过轻量稀疏头预测概念级证据，与稠密语义匹配互补，提升跨模态检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态检索中，稠密表示虽能捕捉整体语义相似性，但常掩盖细粒度视觉-文本信息，难以实现精确匹配。现有引入可学习稀疏分支的方法依赖冗余的语言模型词元空间，且稀疏维度缺乏显式归因。

Method: 提出GRASP框架，从语料中挖掘视觉-文本概念；训练一个轻量级稀疏头，为每张图像或文本预测相关概念，从而产生可解释的概念级证据，作为稠密语义匹配的补充。

Result: 大量实验表明，GRASP相比最先进的稠密-稀疏基线提升了检索精度，同时得到更紧凑、更具归因性的稀疏空间。

Conclusion: GRASP通过概念级稀疏学习，在提升跨模态检索精度的同时，实现了更紧凑、可归因的稀疏表示，为稠密-稀疏互补匹配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cross-modal retrieval has been advanced by vision-language pre-trained models that encode images and texts into a shared dense embedding space. While dense representations effectively capture overall semantic similarity, they often obscure fine-grained visual-textual information needed for precise cross-modal matching. Recent methods introduce a learned sparse branch to complement dense matching with lexical evidence, but they rely on a redundant language-model token space and lack explicit grounding for sparse dimensions. We propose GRASP, a compact and grounded sparse learning framework that mines visual-textual concepts from the corpus. A lightweight sparse head is trained to predict concepts relevant to each image or text, yielding interpretable concept-level evidence that complements dense semantic matching. Extensive experiments show that GRASP improves retrieval accuracy over the state-of-the-art dense-sparse baselines while yielding a more compact and grounded sparse space.

</details>


### [56] [RandSlot: Learning Compact Visual Document Representations with Random Soft Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32699)
*Dewen Guo,Shi Yu,Lingxiao Zhang,Yang Zhang,Tao XU,Dan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 RandSlot，通过在训练时向查询和文档序列追加独立采样的随机单位向量并反复重采样，学习紧凑的多向量视觉文档表示，在相同向量预算下提升检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索需要能匹配分布在文本、表格与页面布局中的证据，多向量表示虽能捕捉细粒度信息，但存储与比较大量向量会带来高昂的检索成本，因此需要在有限向量预算下提升表示质量。

Method: 训练时在查询和文档输入序列后追加独立采样的随机单位向量（不使用可学习的软 token 参数），并在每次使用时重新采样；编码器将这些辅助输入与原始内容一起上下文化，产出少量检索向量，并用标准 late-interaction 目标进行训练，使编码器在不同输入条件下提取相关信息。

Result: 在不同骨干模型上的实验表明，在相同向量预算下，RandSlot 相比其他读出策略提升了检索质量；进一步分析显示，即使推理时将随机软 token 替换为零，这种增益仍能保持。

Conclusion: 随机输入在训练中能够改善紧凑检索表示，且推理阶段无需再进行采样，说明该方法简单有效且易于部署。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval requires expressive representations to match queries with evidence distributed across text, tables, and page layouts. Multi-vector representations capture fine-grained information, but storing and comparing many vectors introduces substantial retrieval costs. In this paper, we introduce RandSlot, a simple approach to learn compact visual document representations with random soft tokens. During training, we append independently-sampled random unit vectors to query and document input sequences and resample them at every use, without introducing learnable soft-token parameters. The encoder contextualizes these auxiliary inputs with the original content to produce a small set of retrieval vectors. A standard late-interaction objective trains the encoder to extract relevant information under varying input conditions. Experiments with different backbone models show that RandSlot improves retrieval quality over alternative readout strategies under the same vector budget. Further analysis shows that these gains can persist when random soft tokens are replaced with zeros at inference, demonstrating that random inputs during training can improve compact retrieval representations even when inference no longer requires sampling.

</details>


### [57] [Mend the Measurement Gap: Latent User Preference Modeling for Short-Form Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32839)
*Shuo Chang,Yueqi Wang,Zihuan Diao,Ali Montazer,Jiangguo Zhang,Joyneel Misra,Dapeng Hong,Tomer Margolin,Sourabh Bansod,Ningren Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化潜在价值模型（FLVM），将异构行为反馈视为对低维因子化潜在价值状态的噪声测量，以更准确地度量短视频用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统依赖异构行为反馈推断用户偏好，但这些信号是不完美的测量：同样的行为可能源于真实喜爱、被动消费或注意力缺失。短视频场景尤为严重，基于观看时长的信号受视频时长等测量混杂因素影响（相同时长对不同长度视频意味不同偏好程度），而比率类指标会系统性偏向短视频。因此优化原始互动指标可能放大测量伪影而非提升用户价值。

Method: 提出因子化潜在价值模型（FLVM）。模型将观测行为视为对低维、因子化潜在价值状态的噪声测量，用结构化输出头建模异构反馈信号。包含两条路径：受限基线路径捕获视频时长、用户倾向、会话上下文等测量混杂特征带来的可预测变化；路由潜在路径估计与偏好相关的价值优势。所得潜在价值分数可作为排序特征或排序分数接入现有推荐系统。

Result: 在 YouTube Shorts 上，该模型提升了离线指标，并在在线 A/B 测试中将一项主要观看愉悦度指标提升 2.67%。

Conclusion: 通过将行为反馈建模为潜在价值状态的噪声测量并分离测量混杂因素，FLVM 能更准确地度量用户偏好，显著提升短视频平台的用户体验指标，具备在实际推荐系统中落地的可行性。

Abstract: Recommender systems rely heavily on heterogeneous behavioral feedback to infer user preference. Although abundant, these signals are imperfect measurements: the same observed behavior can arise from different underlying states, such as genuine enjoyment, passive consumption, or inattention. The challenge is especially acute in short-form video, where watch-based signals are strongly affected by measurement confounders such as video duration - the same watch time can imply different levels of preference for videos of different lengths, while ratio-based metrics can systematically favor short videos. As a result, optimizing raw engagement can amplify measurement artifacts rather than improving user value. We propose a Factorized Latent Value Model (FLVM) for measuring user preference from heterogeneous behavioral feedback. The model treats observed behaviors as noisy measurements of a low-dimensional, factorized latent value state and uses structured output heads to model heterogeneous feedback signals. A restricted baseline path captures predictable variation from measurement-confounding features such as video duration, user propensity, and session context, while a routed latent path estimates preference-relevant value advantage. The resulting latent value score can be integrated into an existing recommender system as a ranking feature or ranking score. On YouTube Shorts, a major short-form video platform, this model improves offline metrics and lifts a primary viewer enjoyment metric by 2.67% in online A/B tests.

</details>


### [58] [PILAR: A Page-Grounded Unified Evidence Representation via an Entity-Linked Assertion Graph for Open-Domain QA Agents over Multimodal Document Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32895)
*Joongmin Shin,Gyuho Shim,Jung-hun Lee,Jaehyung Seo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PILAR 是一种页面锚定的统一证据表示（实体链接断言图），将句子、表格、图注事实映射到共同断言空间，在多模态开放域问答中实现最佳端到端表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态开放域问答系统将文本、表格、图片证据分开存储或仅检索粗粒度页面，削弱了全局证据链接能力，难以应对组合型、跨文档和多模态问题。

Method: 提出 PILAR，以实体链接断言图作为统一证据表示，把句子、表格、图形派生的事实映射到共同断言空间，并将该图作为鲁棒页面检索之上的受控链接层。在共享读取器设置下，结合四个智能体框架、十四个检索后端和两个基准进行评估，并进行消融实验。

Result: PILAR 取得最佳端到端 EM/ANLS。相比扁平检索单次提升 +1.6 EM，组合型问题提升 +2.9，3 跳问题提升 +5.9。消融显示当前增益主要来自文本实例化部分，视觉断言需配合局部性感知过滤才有帮助。

Conclusion: PILAR 应被定位为面向多模态开放域问答的统一证据表示框架，而非独立的视觉推理模块。

Abstract: Open-domain question answering (ODQA) over multimodal document corpora requires linking evidence scattered across text, tables, and figures. Existing systems often store these sources separately or retrieve only coarse pages, which weakens global evidence linking. We present PILAR, a page-grounded unified evidence representation instantiated as an entity-linked assertion graph. PILAR maps sentence-, table-, and figure-derived facts into a common assertion space and uses the graph as a controlled linking layer over robust page retrieval. In a shared-reader evaluation with four agent frameworks, fourteen retrieval backends, and two benchmarks, PILAR achieves the best end-to-end EM/ANLS. Gains are largest on compositional, cross-document, and multimodal questions, with a single-shot improvement of +1.6 EM over flat retrieval, rising to +2.9 on compositional and +5.9 on 3-hop questions. Ablations show that current gains are driven mainly by the text-instantiated slice of the framework, while visual assertions help only after locality-aware filtering. We therefore position PILAR as a unified evidence representation for multimodal ODQA rather than a standalone visual-reasoning module.

</details>


### [59] [Inspire: Benchmarking Scientific Literature Search for Open Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33233)
*Jianrong Ding,Zhengyan Shi,Jianyuan Zhong,Kai Qiu,Qi Dai,Yifan Yang,Chong Luo,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: INSPIRE 是一个用于评估智能体在"问题公开、目标论文隐藏"情境下检索先验文献的基准，通过分阶段分析揭示当前智能体的主要瓶颈在资源暴露环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献检索常常始于一个开放的研究问题，而非已知的目标论文或固定的候选集合。现有评估多针对已知目标，难以刻画智能体自行构建相关性标准、在开放语料中寻找有用先验工作的真实场景，因此需要一个新的基准来评测并诊断这一过程。

Method: 每个实例将一个研究简报与目标特定的时间截点（晚近论文前三个月）配对，在开放语料上检索，目标论文身份及其被引先验保持隐藏；以该论文研究谱系中分级引用的先验文献作为排序评估标准。除端到端检索质量外，利用记录的搜索轨迹将过程分解为三个耦合阶段：资源暴露（有用先验是否被检索到）、选择（被暴露的先验是否被保留）和排序（保留文献的排序效果）。并构建可重放的事后演示作为学习信号。

Result: 在476个计算机科学目标、统一搜索接口与预算下，最强智能体仅取得0.284的nDCG@10。结果显示当前智能体更易恢复孤立的先验文献，而难以组装更广泛的相关先验组合；阶段分析指出资源暴露是最大的瓶颈，选择与排序阶段进一步造成损失。事后演示可在不改变测试时信息的前提下提升留出集搜索表现。

Conclusion: INSPIRE 支持端到端比较与阶段化诊断，适用于智能体必须自行构建相关性标准的检索场景，并提供了可操作的学习信号。

Abstract: Scientific literature search often begins with an open research problem rather than a known target paper or a fixed candidate set. We introduce INSPIRE, a benchmark for evaluating agents that search prior literature to make progress on solution-redacted research problems. Each instance pairs a research brief with a target-specific cutoff three months before a later paper and evaluates ranked outputs against graded cited antecedents from that paper's realized research lineage. Search proceeds over an open corpus, while the identity of the target paper and membership of its cited antecedents remain hidden from the agent. Beyond end-to-end retrieval quality, INSPIRE uses logged search trajectories to distinguish three coupled stages: resource exposure, whether useful antecedents are surfaced during search; selection, whether exposed antecedents are retained; and ranking, how effectively retained papers are ordered. Across 476 computer-science targets under a shared search interface and budget, the strongest evaluated agent achieves 0.284 nDCG@10. Results show that current agents more readily recover an isolated antecedent than assemble a broader portfolio of relevant prior work. The stagewise analysis identifies resource exposure as the largest observed bottleneck, with further losses in selection and ranking. We additionally construct replay-valid hindsight demonstrations and show that they improve held-out search without changing test-time information, establishing that the benchmark provides an actionable learning signal. INSPIRE therefore enables both end-to-end comparison and stage-resolved diagnosis in a setting where the agent must construct its own working criterion of relevance.

</details>


### [60] [What Gets Measured Gets Managed: Sign-aware Recommendation Needs Sign-aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33346)
*Minchan Kim,Jungmin Hwang,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示图神经网络符号感知推荐系统"价态盲"的悖论，提出 Signed Recall/HR/NDCG 系列指标，重新定义评价体系并给出可行的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号感知推荐系统引入负反馈以更深入理解用户偏好，但作者通过实证诊断发现，当前最先进的图神经网络符号感知推荐系统虽在训练中使用符号信息，却在排序阶段无法区分喜欢与不喜欢的物品，常在 Top-K 推荐中混入被讨厌的内容。同时，Recall、HR、NDCG 等传统指标对负反馈与未观测物品均赋予零效用，形成系统性评价盲区，使该问题长期未被察觉。

Method: 1) 通过线性探测证明价态信息确实存在于学习到的嵌入中，但内积打分函数无法获取该信息；2) 提出 Signed Recall、Signed HR、Signed NDCG 一组符号化指标，显式惩罚推荐被讨厌内容的行为；3) 在此新指标下对现有方法进行系统性重评估；4) 设计概念验证性的辅助损失函数，验证所提指标能否作为可操作的训练信号。

Result: 在新指标下重新评估后，原先在传统指标中排名靠前的方法往往无法保护用户免受厌恶内容侵扰，既定性能排名格局被根本性重塑；概念验证性的辅助损失表明，该指标能提供可操作的训练信号，引导模型具备价态感知行为，且不牺牲传统相关性表现。

Conclusion: 现有符号感知推荐系统存在"价态盲"问题，根源在于内积打分与评价指标的双重缺陷；文章提出的符号化指标既暴露了该盲区，又能作为有效训练信号，推动更符合用户真实偏好的推荐。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Sign-aware recommender systems have recently been developed to leverage negative feedback for a deeper understanding of user preferences. However, our empirical diagnosis reveals that state-of-the-art graph-based sign-aware recommender systems are paradoxically valence-blind. Even though they explicitly incorporate sign information during training, they consistently fail to differentiate liked items from disliked ones at the ranking stage, frequently infiltrating top-K recommendations with disliked content. Through linear probing, we show that while valence information exists in the learned embeddings, it remains inaccessible to the inner-product scoring function. This widespread failure remains entirely undetected because conventional evaluation metrics, such as Recall, HR, and NDCG, assign a uniform utility of zero to both negative and unobserved items, creating a systematic evaluation blind spot. To bridge this gap, we propose a family of signed metrics, Signed Recall, Signed HR, and Signed NDCG, that explicitly penalize the recommendation of disliked content. Systematic re-evaluation under our proposed metrics fundamentally reshapes the established performance landscape, revealing that methods ranked highly under conventional metrics often fail to protect users from disliked content. Finally, through a proof-of-concept auxiliary loss, we confirm that the proposed metrics provide actionable training signals, guiding models toward valence-aware behavior without sacrificing conventional relevance. For transparency, our source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/signed-rec-benchmark-07E4

</details>


### [61] [From PDF to Evidence: Structure-Aware Retrieval for Clinical Practice Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33447)
*Xingyu Lin,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文将指南文档中的证据获取视为文档图像分析问题，通过解析页面图像为结构化元素并检索结构感知的证据单元，在26部临床实践指南上显著优于OCR文本切块与视觉RAG基线，且上下文减少3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指南文档以非结构化PDF发布，其证据锁定在表格、流程图和分级推荐等视觉结构中，而标准检索流程会将其压平成固定大小的文本块，导致检索结果只能指向页面而无法指向具体证据元素，丢失了文档自身的布局与结构上下文。

Method: 将证据获取建模为文档图像分析任务：先把每页图像解析为带类型的结构元素，再检索遵循文档自身布局（章节、表格行、流程图路径、分级推荐）的结构感知证据单元，每个单元保留其结构上下文，使检索结果可定位到具体元素而非整页。

Result: 在来自9个来源的26部临床实践指南（3,619页，中英文）与199个证据查询上，结构感知单元在BM25、稠密和混合检索下均将金标准元素排在第一（混合检索Element Hit@1为0.382），相较逐元素OCR文本有显著的元素级排序提升（MRR_e +0.107, p=0.002；Hit@5提升为方向性, p=0.17），同时达到与页面级相当的召回率（Page Hit@5 0.879 vs. 0.889, p=0.75），但上下文用量减少3.8倍，并明显优于ColPali视觉RAG基线（PH@5 0.497）。

Conclusion: 结构感知的检索方法能够在保持页面级召回的同时，实现更精确的元素级证据定位，并大幅降低上下文开销，为指南等结构化视觉文档的证据获取提供了优于传统文本切块与视觉RAG的方案。

Abstract: Guideline documents are published as unstructured PDFs whose evidence is locked in visual structures---tables, flowcharts, and graded recommendations---that standard retrieval pipelines flatten into fixed-size text chunks. We cast evidence access as a document image analysis problem: parse each page image into typed structural elements, then retrieve structure-aware evidence units that follow the document's own layout (sections, table rows, flowchart paths, graded recommendations), each keeping its structural context so a result points to a specific element rather than a page. On 26 clinical practice guidelines from 9 sources (3,619 pages, Chinese and English) with 199 evidence queries, structure-aware units rank the gold element first under BM25, dense, and hybrid retrieval (hybrid Element Hit@1 of 0.382), with a significant element-level ranking gain over per-element OCR text (MRR_e +0.107, p=0.002; the Hit@5 gain is directional, p=0.17), while matching page-level recall (Page Hit@5 0.879 vs. 0.889, p=0.75) at 3.8x less context and clearly outperforming a ColPali visual-RAG baseline (PH@5 0.497).

</details>


### [62] [Beyond the Beam: Constructive Repair and Candidate Completion for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33745)
*Zijun Zhao,Peng Zhang,Gang Zhang,Yuanchi Ma,Hui He,Zhendong Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究生成式推荐中标识符分配与检索的失效问题，提出"Beyond the Beam (BB)"框架，通过输出不变性证书、最小替换修复及统一评分机制提升新物品检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器通过生成标识符检索物品，但目录扩展后有效标识符可能落在beam之外，导致检索失败。核心问题在于：标识符分配能修复哪些失败，以及超出初始beam后检索应如何继续进行。

Method: 以固定生成器和保留旧标识符为前提刻画分配修复，用输出不变性证书识别所有可接受分配共有的失败；在有效前缀下，通过耦合支持与排序约束给出目标恢复的新物品数量可行区间。BB采用积分流公式获得最小替换修复，选择共享映射并适配生成器；推理时用生成似然与协同证据定义统一分数用于排序、候选优先级和停止判定。

Result: 穷举有限目录评估确认每种可行情形均可构造。在三个Amazon Reviews类别和三个随机种子上，完整T5流程相比最佳生成基线的平均Recall@10提升15.5–46.3%，NDCG@10提升15.2–44.4%。匹配对照显示共享构造与适配在Beauty和Toys上提升新目标排序与认证效率；联合评分与候选补全在三个数据集、T5及解码器式LC-Rec上均提升NDCG@10。

Conclusion: 标识符分配修复可被精确刻画，BB框架通过最小替换修复与统一评分有效突破初始beam限制，在多项数据集与基线对比中显著提升检索指标。

Abstract: Generative recommenders retrieve items by generating identifiers, but a valid identifier can remain outside the beam after catalog expansion. This raises two connected questions: which failures can identifier assignment repair, and how should retrieval proceed beyond the initial beam? We characterize assignment repair with a fixed generator and retained old identifiers. Output-invariance certificates identify failures shared by all admissible assignments. Under a common effective prefix, coupled support and ranking constraints give the exact feasible interval of new-item counts for target recovery. Building on this characterization, Beyond the Beam (BB) obtains minimum-replacement repairs through an integral flow formulation, selects a shared map and adapts the generator. At inference, generative likelihood and collaborative evidence define one score for ranking, candidate priority and stopping. Retained prefix bounds guide candidate completion and certify its global Top-$K$ when the stopping condition is met. Exhaustive finite-catalog evaluation confirms construction in every feasible case. Across three Amazon Reviews categories and three random seeds, the full T5 procedure improves mean Recall@10 by 15.5--46.3% and NDCG@10 by 15.2--44.4% over the best-performing evaluated generative baseline for each dataset and metric. Matched controls show that shared construction and adaptation improve new-target ranking and certification efficiency on Beauty and Toys. Combined scoring and candidate completion improve NDCG@10 across all three datasets with both T5 and decoder-only LC-Rec.

</details>


### [63] [STITCH-RAG: Spatio-Temporal Influence Tracing over Topic Hypergraphs for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34127)
*Haodong Yang,Mengzhu Chen,Jia Cai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 STITCH-RAG 超图框架，通过半合并主题超图、时空影响桥接传播与连续 PPR 打分，改善多跳检索的证据连接与生成上下文保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳 RAG 需在分布式证据间建立连接，同时保持简洁忠实的生成上下文。现有索引存在两类互补缺陷：基于分块的 RAG 易打断跨段落证据链；而未标注的成对投影缺少生成主题来源，无法同时保留主题级共参与和单次出现的实体描述。

Method: STITCH-RAG 含三个耦合组件：(1) 半合并主题超图，将多实体共参与编码为主题摘要超边，同时保留按规范名等价链接的每块实体状态；(2) 时空影响桥接传播 STIBP，结合主题空间传播与确定性块索引链接，在频率自适应衰减下跨名等价状态传播；(3) 以连续 STIBP 分数替代二值实体匹配种子，用于局部化 Personalized PageRank（PPR），并刻画该先验比二值先验赋予真实证据更多 PPR 质量的条件。

Result: 在所报告协议下，STITCH-RAG 在 HotpotQA 与 2WikiMultiHopQA 上取得所比较方法中最高的 Contain-Acc 与 LLM-Acc 点估计，并在标准化检索比较中取得高于所纳入方法的 Recall@8。混合领域基准结果仅作为辅助的偏好型证据，因为仅有 LLM 评判的准确率可用。

Conclusion: STITCH-RAG 通过主题超边与连续影响力传播统一了主题级共参与与实体级描述保留，缓解分块索引的证据链断裂问题，在多项多跳问答基准上取得领先的检索与生成指标。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation requires a retriever to connect evidence distributed across documents while preserving a concise, faithful generation context. Existing indexes leave two complementary gaps: chunk-based RAG can break cross-passage evidence chains, whereas an unlabeled pairwise projection without generating-topic provenance cannot jointly preserve topic-level co-participation and per-occurrence entity descriptions. We propose STITCH-RAG, a hypergraph-based framework with three coupled components. First, a semi-merged topic hypergraph encodes multi-entity co-participation as topic-summary hyperedges while retaining per-chunk entity states linked by canonical-name equivalence. Second, spatio-temporal influence bridging propagation (STIBP) combines topic-space propagation with deterministic chunk-index linkage across name-equivalent states under frequency-adaptive decay. Third, continuous STIBP scores replace binary entity-match seeds in localized Personalized PageRank (PPR). We characterize the condition under which this prior assigns more PPR mass to ground-truth evidence than a binary prior. Under the reported protocol, STITCH-RAG attains the highest reported Contain-Acc and LLM-Acc point estimates among the compared methods on HotpotQA and 2WikiMultiHopQA, and higher Recall@8 than the methods included in the standardized retrieval comparison. Results on the mixed-domain benchmark remain auxiliary preference-based evidence because only LLM-judged accuracy is available.

</details>


### [64] [RidgeRank: Efficient Visual Document Reranking via Score Fusion and a Shallow Linear Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34192)
*Shubing Yang,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RidgeRank通过闭式融合重排器分数与检索器分数，并对中间隐状态施加单向量校正，在无需相关性标签的情况下实现高效视觉文档重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型重排视觉文档检索结果准确但全量打分慢；现有压缩重排器方法需要相关性标签才能恢复精度，且仅依赖重排器分数排序，忽略了可恢复的相关性信号。

Method: RidgeRank衡量重排器分数缺失的相关性信号，通过闭式融合规则从检索器分数中恢复；最大化相关目标得到最优融合权重及重排器分数无法单独达到最优的条件。再用单向量校正重排器，该向量通过一次中心化岭回归在未压缩页面的同模型全深度分数上获得，应用于中间隐状态。

Result: 在ViDoRe 2和ViDoRe 3的12个数据集上，使用两种检索器和两种语言模型骨干评估，RidgeRank使NDCG@5与完整交叉编码器相差在1.2个百分点以内，加速比最高达48倍，推进了视觉文档重排的精度-延迟帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: RidgeRank无需相关性标签即可有效压缩多模态重排器，通过融合检索器信号与隐状态校正，在大幅提升速度的同时接近完整交叉编码器精度。

Abstract: Multimodal language models rerank visual document retrieval results accurately, but scoring every candidate page at full cost makes them slow. Some methods that compress these rerankers need relevance labels to regain accuracy, and they rank by the reranker score alone. RidgeRank measures how much relevance signal the reranker score lacks and recovers it from the retriever score through a closed-form fusion rule. Maximizing a correlation objective gives the optimal fusion weight, along with the exact condition under which the reranker score by itself cannot reach that optimum. The reranker is further corrected by a single vector applied to an intermediate hidden state, obtained through one centered ridge regression onto the same model's full-depth scores on uncompressed pages. On 12 datasets drawn from ViDoRe 2 and ViDoRe 3, evaluated with two retrievers and two language model backbones, RidgeRank brings NDCG@5 to within 1.2 pp of a full cross encoder with speedups of up to 48 times, advancing the accuracy and latency Pareto frontier for visual document reranking.

</details>


### [65] [Measuring and Mitigating Identity-Cue Preference Drift in LLM-based Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34229)
*Zhuoxiong Gan,Qiang Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PromptShift，一个免训练、可解释的框架，用于量化并缓解大模型推荐系统中身份线索导致的偏好漂移，实验显示可大幅降低漂移并提升难度加权命中指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型推荐系统中，提示词里的身份线索会在行为证据不变的情况下把推荐引向群体层面模式，产生身份线索偏好漂移，需要一种无需训练即可量化并缓解该问题的可解释方法。

Method: 定义Drift衡量身份线索提示推荐与仅基于用户历史参考推荐在物品成员与排序上的分歧；用SliceShift度量被线索列表相对历史参考向该身份切片内热门物品的偏移；提出难度加权的DifHitRate指标；基于正向交互构建身份切片×物品表，并设计自适应事后重排，在原始LLM排序与逆切片流行度之间以个性化权重插值。

Result: 在两个数据集、三种LLM上，身份线索提示的Drift高于无身份改述对照，且六个数据集-模型设置中SliceShift均为正；PromptShift持续降低Drift与SliceShift，宏平均SliceShift下降62.42%，同时提升DifHitRate、HitRate与MRR。

Conclusion: 身份线索偏好漂移可在不训练模型的情况下被测量与缓解，代价是存在一定且依赖指标的效用损失。

Abstract: In large language model-based recommender systems, identity cues embedded in prompts can steer recommendations toward group-level patterns even when the underlying behavioral evidence remains unchanged. We introduce PromptShift, an interpretable, training-free framework for quantifying and mitigating such identity-cue preference drift. We define Drift as the divergence, in both item membership and ranking order, between a recommendation list generated under an identity-cued prompt and the reference list produced from the same user's interaction history alone. SliceShift then measures the extent to which a cued list gravitates, relative to the history-only reference, toward items that are more popular within the cued slice than among the global user population. Beyond conventional accuracy, we propose DifHitRate, a difficulty-weighted hit metric that credits only relevant items, assigning higher credit to hits that are less popular within the cued slice and ranked higher in the list. All components are supported by an identity-slice-by-item table constructed from positive interactions, which further enables an adaptive post-hoc reranking strategy: the reranker interpolates between the original LLM ranking and inverse slice-popularity, with personalized interpolation weight. Experiments on two datasets with three LLMs show that identity-cued prompts incur higher mean Drift than identity-free paraphrase controls, an effect beyond generic wording sensitivity, and that SliceShift is positive across all six dataset-model settings. PromptShift consistently reduces both Drift and SliceShift, lowering macro-mean SliceShift by 62.42%, while improving DifHitRate, HitRate and MRR. These results demonstrate that identity-cue preference drift can be measured and mitigated without any model training, albeit with a modest, metric-dependent utility cost.

</details>


### [66] [SPRINT: Single-Step Generative Recommendation via Average Probability Velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34306)
*Zhuo Cai,Shoujin Wang,Peilin Zhou,Min Xu,Julian McAuley,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"平均概率速度"视角，用双向Transformer在单次前向传播中生成SID全部token概率，并设计双层级流对比目标恢复token连贯性，实现高效且更准确的生成式推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SID的生成式推荐中，自回归模型逐token解码、非自回归模型虽并行但仍需多轮细化，二者每条物品都需多次前向传播，延迟敏感场景下开销过高。作者追问：能否在单次前向传播中生成一个物品的SID。

Method: 将SID生成视为token生成概率的"流"，用其在整个生成过程中的平均速度（平均概率速度）刻画，并证明该平均速度完全由每个token的平均生成概率决定。因此用双向Transformer在单次前向中直接参数化并学习所有token概率；针对位置间独立生成导致token连贯性丢失的问题，设计双层级流对比目标，在token级和SID级同时对比目标SID与负样本SID。

Result: 实验表明，相比次快的AR/NAR方法提速8.39–10.04倍，推荐准确率相对次优方法平均提升7.77%。

Conclusion: 通过平均概率速度视角，单次前向传播生成SID是可行的，配合双层级流对比目标可兼顾token连贯性，从而在效率与准确率上同时超越现有AR/NAR范式。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) based generative recommendation represents each item as a sequence of discrete tokens, and recommends by generating the SID of the item a user would like to interact with. Both dominant paradigms in this domain generally pay for generation token by token: autoregressive models decode the tokens left-to-right, while non-autoregressive models decode in parallel yet still need multiple rounds of refinement to stay competitive. Therefore, both generally spend multiple forward passes per item, a cost that is prohibitive in latency-sensitive recommender systems. We ask whether an item can be generated in a single forward pass, and answer it through a new perspective which we call average probability velocity. We view SID generation as a flow of token generation probabilities and characterize it by its average velocity over the whole generation process. We prove that this average velocity is fully determined by the average generation probability of each token. Therefore, we directly parameterize and learn the probabilities of all tokens in a single forward pass with a bidirectional Transformer. As these probabilities are generated independently across positions and the coherence among tokens is lost, we further design a dual-level flow contrastive objective to restore the coherence among an item's tokens. It contrasts the target SID against negative SIDs at both the token and SID levels. The token level ranks the generation probabilities of the target tokens above those of negative SIDs, while the SID level scores the tokens of each SID as a whole item for capturing token coherence of each item. Extensive experiments show that our model not only generates recommendations far more efficiently ($8.39-10.04\times$ speedup over the second-fastest AR/NAR method) but also attains superior recommendation accuracy ($7.77\%$ average improvement over the second-best.

</details>


### [67] [Correcting to Predict: Pseudo-Value Correction for Multimodal Attribute Value Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34383)
*Junhao Zhang,Feiran Hu,Xiao Hu,Baoliang Cui,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Correcting to Predict (C2P)框架,将商品属性值抽取视为纠正过程,通过多样化伪值训练与自一致性精炼,在离线基准和AliExpress在线A/B测试中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在商品属性值抽取(AVE)中面临隐式属性抽取困难的问题,需要联合视觉与文本线索进行推理,且容易混淆语义相似的属性值,因为正确值往往依赖易被忽略的细微多模态线索。

Method: C2P将属性抽取建模为纠正过程:给定初始伪值(检索候选或占位符),模型学习利用多模态证据对其进行纠正;训练时使用多样化伪值学习基于证据的纠正行为,并引入自一致性精炼阶段降低对伪值扰动的敏感度;推理时用固定占位符触发纠正行为,实现高效单次前向预测,无需在线检索或迭代精炼。

Result: 在公开基准和大规模工业数据集上,C2P优于强基线,在歧义属性上提升尤为明显;AliExpress在线A/B测试显示卖家采纳率、属性完整度和用户参与度均有持续提升。

Conclusion: C2P通过将AVE重构为纠正任务,验证了其在真实场景部署中的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Product attribute value extraction (AVE) is a fundamental task in e-commerce, aiming to identify specific values of predefined attributes from multimodal product profiles such as text and images. While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown promise for AVE, they face challenges in extracting implicit attributes that require joint reasoning over visual and textual cues, often confusing semantically similar values. However, existing methods often fail to resolve such ambiguities because the correct value often depends on subtle multimodal cues that are easy to miss or override. To address this challenge, we propose Correcting to Predict (C2P), a framework that treats attribute extraction as a correction process. Given an initial pseudo-value such as a retrieved candidate or placeholder, the model learns to correct it using multimodal evidence. During training, diverse pseudo-values help the model learn evidence-based correction behavior, and a self-consistency refinement stage further reduces sensitivity to pseudo-value perturbations. At inference, a fixed placeholder triggers the learned correction behavior, enabling efficient single-pass prediction without online retrieval or iterative refinement. We evaluate C2P on a public benchmark and a large-scale industrial dataset. Offline results show that C2P outperforms strong baselines, with notable gains on ambiguous attributes. Online A/B tests on AliExpress further show consistent improvements in seller adoption, attribute completeness, and user engagement, validating C2P's effectiveness and efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [68] [Relevance-Resolution Transfer via Scale-Decomposable Fractional Diffusion for Multi-Length Cross-Modal Hash Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34393)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiangyang Jia,Ting Gan,Xu Zhang,Shuquan Wei,Sitong Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MultiBit 方法，通过将相关性分辨率从多标签结构转移到多长度汉明空间，解决跨模态哈希中的相关性分辨率瓶颈（RRB）问题，并在多个基准上提升了检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态哈希可高效检索异构数据，现有方法虽利用多标签训练结构中的细粒度关系，但未约束这些关系在有限、多长度汉明空间中如何保持为一致的候选排序，即作者提出的相关性分辨率瓶颈（RRB）。

Method: MultiBit 首先从数据集级标签共现与标签特异性构建尺度可分解的分数关系教师，并在连续扩散尺度上建模从局部到长程的依赖；随后将离散化扩散尺度及其求积权重映射到最大长度码的尺度感知比特子块，把目标码长组织为嵌套前缀，并使其汉明候选排序与教师关系对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明检索精度有所提升，代码见补充材料。

Conclusion: MultiBit 通过将相关性分辨率从多标签结构迁移至多长度汉明空间，有效缓解 RRB，并提升跨模态检索性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal hashing enables efficient retrieval by encoding heterogeneous data into compact binary codes. Recent methods exploit fine-grained relations encoded in multi-label training structure, yet none of them constrains how those relations survive as consistent candidate rankings in finite, multi-length Hamming spaces, which we term the relevance resolution bottleneck (RRB).
