To address the RRB, we propose MultiBit, which transfers relevance resolution from multi-label structure to multi-length Hamming spaces. MultiBit first constructs a scale-decomposable fractional relation teacher from dataset-level label co-occurrence and label specificity, and models dependencies from local to long-range over continuous diffusion scales. It then maps the discretized diffusion scales and their quadrature weights to scale-aware bit subblocks of the maximum-length code, organizes the target code lengths as nested prefixes, and aligns their Hamming candidate rankings with the teacher relations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate improved retrieval accuracy. Code is available in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [69] [Eval4DiRec: A Unified and Systematic Evaluation Framework for Diffusion-based Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34404)
*Cong Wang,Shoujin Wang,Yishuo Li,Qi Zhang,Liang Hu,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Eval4DiRec，首个面向扩散推荐系统的统一开源评测框架，支持14个代表性模型、5类推荐场景，通过一致可复现的实验设置进行系统评测，并揭示影响性能的关键因素与实践挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散推荐系统发展迅速，但缺乏统一、系统的定量评测基准，导致数据处理、训练配置、推理流程和评测协议不一致，实验结果难以复现、比较不公平。因此需要构建统一评测框架。

Method: 提出Eval4DiRec统一开源评测框架，覆盖14个代表性扩散推荐模型和5种推荐场景，采用一致且可复现的实验设置；基于该框架开展大规模实证研究，对模型进行统一协议下的基准测试。

Result: 实验结果显示扩散模型在推荐中具有较强潜力，同时揭示了显著影响性能的关键因素和实际挑战（具体指标见原文）。

Conclusion: Eval4DiRec为扩散推荐系统提供了公平评测的基础，有助于指导未来研究，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Leveraging the strong generative capabilities and stable training dynamics of diffusion models, diffusion-based recommender systems (RSs) have recently emerged as a novel recommendation paradigm, attracting increasing attention from both academia and industry. However, despite the rapid growth of diffusion-based RSs, a critical issue has emerged: the lack of a unified and systematic quantitative evaluation benchmark, which often results in irreproducible experimental results and unfair comparisons across studies due to inconsistent data processing, training configurations, inference procedures, and evaluation protocols. To address this challenge, we propose Eval4DiRec, the first unified and open-source evaluation framework specifically designed for diffusion-based RSs. Eval4DiRec supports 14 representative diffusion-based RS models across five different recommendation scenarios, providing consistent and reproducible experimental settings to systematically assess their performance. Built upon this framework, we conduct extensive empirical studies to benchmark these models under unified protocols. The results highlight the strong potential of diffusion models for recommendation while also revealing key factors and practical challenges that substantially affect their performance, thereby establishing a solid foundation to facilitate fair evaluation and guide future research in this promising field. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wangcong2001/Eval4DiRec.

</details>


### [70] [EvoSkillRec: Skill-Genome Evolution for Recommender Architecture Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34552)
*Xiaopeng Li,Kuo Cai,Bo Chen,Wenlin Zhang,Mengyang Ma,Yingyi Zhang,Zichuan Fu,Yu Yang,Qidong Liu,Yiyu Wang,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Jiang Wu,Zhanbo Xu,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EvoSkillRec 提出一种"晋升-复用"框架，将推荐模型分解为原子化可执行技能并以类型化技能基因组表示，通过受限技能空间与开放式代码空间协同演化，实现推荐架构的积累式进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统的进步不仅依赖数据与参数规模，还依赖架构中编码的任务特定归纳偏置（如CTR预测的稀疏特征交互、序列推荐的时序注意力、多任务学习的专家路由）。然而这些偏置通常由人类专家设计或仅在预定义算子空间内搜索；近期LLM驱动的代码演化虽扩展了空间，但无约束编辑常产生无效或低效架构，未充分利用既有架构设计知识，也无法保留并复用成功创新。

Method: 框架首先将推荐模型分解为原子可执行技能，把架构表示为带类型的技能基因组，每个技能含输入-输出类型、语义标注与实现代码。随后在两个耦合空间中对不同任务演化模型：受限技能空间负责变异、重组、特化与复用已验证技能；开放式代码空间由LLM规划器与合成器利用历史演化轨迹和积累经验发明新技能模块。自动研究控制器负责评估候选、诊断失败、检索相关技能、将验证过的创新晋升入技能库，并在两个空间之间自适应分配提案预算。

Result: 在CTR预测、多任务学习和多领域学习上的大量实验，包括生成式排序模型中预测质量与模型FLOPs利用率在资源受限下的协同优化，均一致证明了EvoSkillRec的有效性。

Conclusion: EvoSkillRec 通过将技能验证与晋升机制同开放式代码生成结合，使推荐架构演化具备可复用性与累积性，为跨任务推荐模型的自动化架构设计提供了一条兼顾约束与创新的有效路径。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems advance not only by scaling data and parameters, but also by encoding task-specific inductive biases through architecture, including sparse feature interactions for click-through rate (CTR) prediction, temporal attention for sequential recommendation, and expert routing for multi-task learning. However, these biases are typically human expert designed or searched within predefined operator spaces. Although Recent LLM-driven code evolution expands this space, unconstrained edits often produce invalid or ineffective architectures, underuse established architecture design knowledge, and fail to preserve successful innovations for reuse. We introduce EvoSkillRec, a promotion-and-reuse framework for cumulative recommender architecture evolution. It first decomposes recommenders into atomic executable skills and represents architectures as typed skill genomes, with each skill equipped with input--output types, semantic annotations, and implementation code. We then evolve models with different tasks through two coupled spaces: a constrained skill--space that mutates, recombines, specializes, and reuses validated skills, and an open-ended code--space in which LLM planners and synthesizers invent new skill modules using prior evolution traces and accumulated experience. An autoresearch controller evaluates candidates, diagnoses failures, retrieves relevant skills, promotes validated innovations into the skill library, and adaptively allocates the proposal budget between the two spaces. Extensive experiments on CTR prediction, multi-task learning, and multi-domain learning, including resource-constrained co-optimization of predictive quality and model FLOPs utilization in generative ranking models, consistently demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed EvoSkillRec.

</details>


### [71] [No Attention, No Problem: Rethinking Session-based Recommendation with Pure Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34802)
*Tao Huang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出纯卷积框架NextConvRec用于会话推荐，兼顾效率与性能，在4个基准数据集上平均超越SOTA约1.73%，会话平均推理时间减少16.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型虽能通过自注意力捕获长程依赖而被广泛用于会话推荐，但传统卷积模型虽更高效，却因全局建模能力弱而在该任务中逐渐失去优势。作者希望探索一种既能保持卷积效率、又能实现长程偏好建模的纯卷积方案。

Method: NextConvRec包含两部分：一是结构与位置卷积编码器（SPCE）用于预处理，将可学习的卷积位置偏置与通过GCN层提取的会话级结构信号相结合；二是骨干卷积模块，通过深度卷积与逐点卷积扩大有效感受野，从而在无需注意力机制的情况下实现稳健的长程偏好建模。

Result: 在4个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，NextConvRec平均超越多个当前最优基线约1.73%，并将每个会话的平均推理时间降低16.7%。

Conclusion: 卷积架构仍是实现高效且准确的会话推荐的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Session-based recommendation (SBR) predicts the next choice in a session by analyzing recent interactions. Transformer-based models are widely used because of their ability to capture long-range dependencies through self-attention mechanisms. In contrast, traditional convolutional models, although more efficient, are often limited by their weak global modeling capabilities and are losing ground in SBR tasks. In this work, we propose a Next-generation Pure Convolutional Framework (NextConvRec) for SBR tasks, aiming to balance efficiency and performance. NextConvRec uses a Structural and Positional Convolutional Encoder (SPCE) for preprocessing, combining learnable convolutional positional biases with session-level structural signals extracted through GCN layers. Its backbone convolutional module effectively expands the effective receptive field through depthwise convolutions and pointwise convolutions, enabling robust long-range preference modeling without attention mechanisms. Extensive experiments on 4 benchmark datasets show that NextConvRec outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines by around 1.73% on average, and reduces the average inference time per session by 16.7%. The convolutional architectures remain a promising direction for efficient and accurate session-based recommendations.

</details>


### [72] [ColNanoVDR: Document-Free Query Distillation for Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrieval via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34899)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Ziyi Wang,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 ColNanoVDR 框架，首次实现无文档（document-free）的多向量视觉文档检索蒸馏，用 OTW 目标在无需页面数据的情况下对齐学生与教师查询表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量 VDR 依赖数十亿参数查询编码器，每次检索开销大；蒸馏到小模型可去除瓶颈，但标准方法需匹配 MaxSim 分数，需编码缓存所有训练页面（可达 TB 级）。已有的无页面方法 NanoVDR 仅适用于单向量检索。

Method: OTW（Optimal Transport with Learned Weights）：用带熵正则的最优传输对齐学生查询 token 与教师查询 token，为每个学生 token 学习权重，无需两种分词间的对应关系。并证明该对齐代价可上界任意页面上的 MaxSim 分数差。

Result: 从五个 SOTA 教师蒸馏出的 149M 纯文本学生模型，在 ViDoRe v1-v3 上保留约 95% 教师的 NDCG@5，查询编码速度最高提升 26 倍；相同训练下，OTW 达到分数蒸馏的效果，同时不编码页面、读取的缓存教师数据少 12.6 倍。

Conclusion: ColNanoVDR 将无文档蒸馏扩展到多向量 VDR，OTW 在无需页面数据的前提下保持检索性能并大幅提升效率，验证了方法的有效性与理论保证。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrievers built on vision-language models lead visual document retrieval (VDR), but they run a multi-billion-parameter query encoder on every search. Distilling this encoder into a small student that queries the teacher's existing index would remove the bottleneck. The standard recipe, however, matches the teacher's MaxSim scores and so requires encoding and caching every training page, which can reach terabytes of page tokens. NanoVDR avoids pages entirely by training on the teacher's query embeddings alone, but only for single-vector retrievers. We present ColNanoVDR, to our knowledge the first framework to bring this document-free distillation to multi-vector VDR. Its objective, OTW (Optimal Transport with Learned Weights), aligns the student's query tokens with the teacher's by entropic optimal transport, with a learned weight for each student token, and needs no correspondence between the two tokenizations. We prove that the resulting alignment cost bounds the MaxSim score difference on every page. Distilled from five state-of-the-art teachers, the 149M text-only students retain about 95% of their teachers' NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1-v3 while encoding queries up to 26x faster. Under identical training, OTW matches score distillation while encoding no page and reading 12.6x less cached teacher data.

</details>


### [73] [PEAR: Progressive Evidence-Based AutoResearch for Industrial Search Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35031)
*Yifan Wang,Shipeng Zhu,Fei Xiong,Yuqin Yang,Yonghui Huang,Kunyao Wu,Yue Wang,Weichao Meng,Yu Gong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PEAR 框架，通过证据驱动的研究状态维护与置信度门控的多级验证阶梯，解决工业搜索中 AutoResearch 的知识累积可靠性不足与多保真度证据难以统一利用的问题，并在真实系统中显著提升核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 AutoResearch 范式迁移到工业搜索面临两个挑战：一是常见的“更好则保留”规则在非平稳流量下会把瞬时收益误判为持久改进，损害搜索知识的可靠累积；二是候选修改可在多种保真度层级（低成本代理到线上验证）上评估，这些层级在成本、目标一致性与统计可靠性上各不相同，现有方法依赖单一信号或任务特定流程，缺乏统一依据来跨层级利用证据。

Method: 提出 Progressive Evidence-Based AutoResearch（PEAR），包含两个互补组件：(1) 证据驱动 AutoResearch：在预设目标与干预范围内，为每个策略任务维护独立的、假设引导的研究状态，通过 Plan-Execute-Evaluate-Update 转换进行演进，将实验与上下文感知的证据解释及假设修正相链接；(2) Confidence-Gated Verifier Ladder：将评估组织为四个递增保真度层级——Offline Replay、Shadow-Traffic Evaluation、Rapid Online Evaluation、Decision-Grade Online Evaluation，并以统一的置信度门控仅在证据支持统计显著正向效应时提升候选，从而支持广泛低成本探索、把昂贵线上实验留给被提升的候选。

Result: 在真实工业搜索系统中，两个 A/B 实验显示，用 PEAR 优化后的策略相较各自基线，Main Order/DAU 分别显著提升 2.7336% 和 3.2957%。

Conclusion: PEAR 通过假设引导的持续性研究状态与统一置信度门控的多保真度验证阶梯，缓解了非平稳流量下的误保留问题并统一了跨层级证据的利用，在真实工业搜索中验证了其有效性与指标收益。

Abstract: AutoResearch improves systems through iterative experimentation: agents propose candidate modifications, evaluate them, and use the results to guide subsequent exploration. Applying this paradigm to industrial search presents two challenges. (1) Common AutoResearch approaches follow a keep-if-better rule, retaining the highest-scoring candidate for subsequent experiments. Under non-stationary traffic, transient gains may be mistaken for persistent improvements, impairing reliable accumulation of search knowledge. (2) Candidate modifications can be evaluated at multiple fidelity levels, from low-cost proxies to online validation, differing in cost, objective alignment, and statistical reliability. Existing methods rely on individual signals or task-specific procedures, lacking a unified basis for using evidence across levels to guide search. We introduce Progressive Evidence-Based AutoResearch (PEAR) with two complementary components. Evidence-driven AutoResearch maintains an independent, hypothesis-guided research state for each strategy task within a predefined objective and intervention scope. Each state evolves through a Plan-Execute-Evaluate-Update transition that links experimentation to context-aware evidence interpretation and hypothesis revision. Confidence-Gated Verifier Ladder organizes evaluation into four levels of increasing fidelity: Offline Replay, Shadow-Traffic Evaluation, Rapid Online Evaluation, and Decision-Grade Online Evaluation. A unified confidence-based gate promotes candidates only when evidence supports a statistically significant positive effect, enabling broad low-cost exploration while reserving costly online experiments for promoted candidates. In a real-world industrial search system, strategies optimized with PEAR significantly increased Main Order/DAU by 2.7336% and 3.2957% relative to their respective baselines in two A/B experiments.

</details>


### [74] [Recommendation Ranking Off-Policy Evaluation under Ranking-Dependent Examination via Examination-Relevance Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35034)
*Riki Okamura,Toshiharu Sugawara*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对日志点击无法区分"未检查项"与"检查但未点击项"导致的评估偏差，提出两种基于点击分解（检查+相关性）的离策略评估估计器 LE-IIPS 与 ED-DR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐排序策略的离策略评估依赖日志数据，但日志点击无法区分未检查项与检查未点击项，当假设的检查结构不成立时现有估计器会产生偏差。

Method: 将点击分解为检查与相关性两部分：先提出基于潜变量检查的独立逆倾向得分估计器 LE-IIPS，利用策略检查概率比修正 IIPS 偏差；再将其扩展为双稳健框架，得到检查分解双稳健估计器 ED-DR。

Result: ED-DR 在检查概率正确（无论相关性估计是否准确）时无偏；在排序独立检查假设下，即使两个模型估计都不准确也无偏。实验表明大样本下 ED-DR 的 MSE 低于现有方法，尤其当检查依赖排序时。

Conclusion: ED-DR 在大样本、检查依赖排序的场景下表现优于现有方法，但在小样本或级联用户行为条件下存在局限。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation, which estimates evaluation policy performance from logged data, is key for recommender ranking policies. However, logged clicks cannot distinguish unexamined items from examined non-clicks, causing bias in existing estimators when the assumed examination structures fail. We propose two estimators based on the decomposition of clicks into examination and relevance. First, the latent-examination independent inverse propensity score (LE-IIPS) estimator corrects the IIPS bias using policy examination probability ratios. Second, the examination-decomposed doubly robust (ED-DR) estimator extends LE-IIPS to a doubly robust framework. ED-DR is unbiased if the examination probabilities are correct regardless of relevance accuracy, or under ranking-independent examination, even if both model estimates are inaccurate. Experiments show that ED-DR achieves a lower MSE than existing methods with large sample sizes, especially when the examination depends on ranking. We also highlight its limitations under small samples or cascade user behavior conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Mitigating Popularity Bias in Recommendation with Global Listwise Learning and Progressive Bi-Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35041)
*Tianyu Zhu,Jiandong Ding,Yansong Shi,Guoqing Chen,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Mult-BiW，将多项似然与 IPS 结合并引入双重加权与渐进策略，缓解推荐中的流行度偏差，实验优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中用户反馈呈长尾分布，算法偏好热门物品，加剧流行度偏差。现有基于逆倾向评分(IPS)的重加权方法受限于局部无偏目标和倾向估计不准确，效果受限。

Method: 1) 提出 Mult-IPS 去偏框架，将多项似然与 IPS 结合，捕捉对整个物品集的全局无偏用户偏好；2) 提出 Bi-Weighting 策略，联合利用倾向分数与收集模型，并加入平滑机制提升倾向估计鲁棒性；同时给出经验偏差上界与收集模型最优形式的理论分析；3) 设计渐进式 Bi-Weighting 策略，从判别式表示学习逐步过渡到流行度去偏，缓解激进重加权对表示学习的负面影响。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，Mult-BiW 持续优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: Mult-BiW 通过全局无偏目标、双重加权与渐进策略有效缓解流行度偏差，并在实验中取得一致优势。

Abstract: In recommender systems, user feedback typically follows a long-tail distribution, which leads many recommendation algorithms to exacerbate popularity bias by disproportionately favoring popular items. To mitigate this issue, recent studies have employed Inverse Propensity Scoring (IPS) to rebalance training data via reweighting user-item interactions. However, the effectiveness of IPS-based approaches is often constrained by locally unbiased objectives and inaccurate propensity estimation. In this paper, we propose Multinomial Likelihood with Bi-Weighting (Mult-BiW) to address these limitations. First, we introduce a debiasing framework, termed Mult-IPS, which integrates multinomial likelihood with IPS to capture global and unbiased user preferences over the entire item set. Second, we develop a Bi-Weighting (BiW) strategy that jointly leverages propensity scores and a collection model, incorporating a smoothing mechanism to enhance the robustness of propensity estimation. We further provide theoretical analyses that establish an upper bound on the empirical bias and characterize the optimal form of the collection model. Third, to mitigate the adverse effects of aggressive reweighting on representation learning, we design a Progressive Bi-Weighting strategy that gradually transitions from discriminative representation learning to popularity debiasing. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that Mult-BiW consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [76] [RenderRank: Learning to Rerank Text with Compressed Visual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35069)
*Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Jungseob Lee,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RenderRank，利用压缩视觉文档表示进行重排序，在显著减少输入token的同时达到优于文本基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本重排序器逐候选文档处理文本token，输入序列长、计算开销大。将文档渲染为图像可用视觉token压缩表示，从而降低输入长度，尤其适合需要对每个查询评估多个候选文档的重排序任务。

Method: RenderRank从压缩的视觉文档表示中学习查询相关的相关性打分，而非使用文本token序列。训练分两步：首先将视觉输入的相关性分数与文本教师模型对齐，然后针对同一查询优化正负文档之间的相对分数。

Result: 在BEIR的11个数据集上，RenderRank减少16.5–35.5%输入token，平均NDCG@10达55.96，优于所有4B参数以下的文本基线及部分更大模型。在4个长文档数据集上，平均NDCG@10为88.27，输入token约为文本基线的一半，平均吞吐量达最高基线的1.70倍。

Conclusion: 压缩的视觉表示能够支持准确的相关性打分，为文档重排序提供了替代文本token表示的可行方案。

Abstract: Rendering document text as images allows vision-language models to encode documents as visual tokens, which can reduce input sequence length compared with text input. This reduction in input length is particularly useful for reranking, where each query involves scoring multiple candidate documents and token savings apply to each candidate evaluation. We introduce RenderRank, a reranker that learns query-dependent relevance scoring from compressed visual document representations instead of the text token sequences used by conventional text-based rerankers. Training first aligns relevance scores from visual inputs with those of a text-based teacher, then refines the relative scores of positive and negative documents for the same query. Across 11 datasets from BEIR, RenderRank uses 16.5-35.5% fewer input tokens while achieving an average NDCG@10 of 55.96, outperforming all evaluated text-based baselines below 4B parameters and some larger models. Across four long-document datasets, it achieves an average NDCG@10 of 88.27 with approximately half the average input token count of the evaluated text-based rerankers. In this setting, RenderRank delivers 1.70x the highest average throughput of the evaluated baselines. These results demonstrate that compressed visual representations can support accurate document relevance scoring, providing an alternative to text token representations for reranking.

</details>


### [77] [Signal or Noise? Modality Contribution and Cooperation in Multimodal GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35304)
*Antonios Georgakopoulos,Paul Groth,Lise Stork*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态GraphRAG中模态感知检索对下游推理的影响，以DocVQA为测试平台。发现表格和文本贡献最大，模态组合常产生冗余而非协同，主张按任务选择性检索模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态知识图谱常被默认认为检索更多模态证据能提升性能，但冗余或重叠的多模态证据可能干扰语言模型，且各模态在不同问题、模型和任务中的贡献是否均等仍不清晰。本文旨在厘清模态感知检索对下游推理的实际作用。

Method: 扩展已有基于知识图谱的QA框架使其具备模态感知能力，利用图结构追踪哪些模态支持哪些事实，并在边级别选择性过滤证据。以文档视觉问答（DocVQA）为测试平台，在先进的多模态GraphRAG流程中，对五个多模态LLM和两个DocVQA基准进行受控分析。

Result: 表格和文本提供最强贡献；模态组合经常产生冗余而非协同，尤其涉及文本信息的模态对。正向协同主要出现在非文本模态之间，且依赖问题意图和任务类型。在推理时提供全部可用多模态证据并不总是有益。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在GraphRAG系统设计中采用选择性、模态感知的检索策略，即根据下游任务过滤模态，而非统一检索所有模态，以构建更有效的系统。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge graphs (KGs) integrate information from text, figures, tables, and other modalities into a unified structured representation, with the promise that richer evidence enables better inference. In GraphRAG systems built over such graphs, it is commonly assumed that retrieving evidence from more modalities at inference time improves downstream performance. Yet, redundant or overlapping multimodal evidence may distract language models in question answering (QA), and whether each modality contributes equally across questions, models, and tasks remains poorly understood. In this work, we study how modality-aware retrieval affects downstream inference in a multimodal GraphRAG pipeline, using document visual question answering (DocVQA) as a testbed. We extend an existing KG-based QA framework to be modality-aware, leveraging the graph structure to track which modality supports which facts and to selectively filter evidence at the edge level. This enables us to investigate whether providing all available multimodal evidence at inference time benefits QA, and to evaluate the contribution and cooperation of modalities across question, task, and model characteristics. Through a controlled analysis within a state-of-the-art multimodal GraphRAG pipeline, five multimodal LLMs and two DocVQA benchmarks, we find that tables and text provide the strongest contributions, and that combining modalities frequently produces redundancy rather than synergy, particularly for pairs involving textual information. Positive cooperation appears mainly between non-text modalities and depends on question intent and task type. Our findings argue for selective, modality-aware retrieval in the design of more effective GraphRAG systems, where modalities are filtered according to the downstream task rather than retrieved uniformly.

</details>


### [78] [Can Generative Retrievers Learn Semantic IDs Without Forgetting How to Speak?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35430)
*Junchen Fu,Kleomenis Katevas,Vandana Rajan,Sofía Celi,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SpeakGR: 在生成式检索中同时学习语义标识符(SID)并保持语言生成能力的双目标框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索(GR)通过生成文档语义标识符实现端到端检索，但仅针对检索的微调会使预训练语言模型过度特化于SID预测，严重扭曲其自然语言分布，从而限制其在既需检索文档又需生成自然语言回复的交互系统中的适用性。

Method: 提出SpeakGR双目标框架，联合监督式SID学习与说话保持正则化：采用在策略(on-policy)蒸馏目标，在模型自生成前缀上，通过原始文本词表上的前向KL对齐当前模型与冻结的原始模型。进一步提出自适应SpeakGR，根据观测到的语言漂移动态调整保持强度。

Result: 相比仅SFT，SpeakGR在MS MARCO上将WikiText-2前向KL降低81.3-93.8%，在Natural Questions上降低81.2-85.2%，并在三种不同LLM上保持有效检索性能。自适应SpeakGR在多数设置下进一步提升检索效果，同时语言漂移显著低于仅SFT。

Conclusion: SpeakGR通过说话保持正则化有效缓解检索微调造成的语言能力退化，实现了检索与语言生成双目标的兼顾。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) enables end-to-end retrieval by generating document semantic identifiers (SIDs). However, retrieval-only fine-tuning can over-specialize pretrained language models to SID prediction, substantially distorting their natural-language distribution and limiting their suitability for interactive systems that must both retrieve documents and generate natural-language responses. We introduce SpeakGR, a dual-objective framework that learns SIDs while preserving language generation. It combines supervised SID learning with speak-preserving regularization: an on-policy distillation objective that aligns the current model with a frozen copy of the original model on student-generated prefixes using forward KL over the original text vocabulary. We further propose Adaptive SpeakGR, which dynamically adjusts the preservation strength based on observed language drift. Compared with SFT-only, SpeakGR reduces WikiText-2 forward KL by 81.3-93.8% on MS MARCO and 81.2-85.2% on Natural Questions (NQ) while retaining effective retrieval across three different LLMs. Adaptive SpeakGR further improves retrieval over SpeakGR in most settings while maintaining substantially lower language drift than SFT-only.

</details>


### [79] [Rubric-Calibrated Preferences: Cross-Query Calibration of LLM Judgments via Item Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35739)
*Fabian David Schmidt,Donato Crisostomi,Carlos Lassance,Nils Reimers*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RCP（Rubric-Calibrated Preferences），将逐查询的相对LLM判断（Bradley-Terry锦标赛）与跨查询共享的评分标准（rubric）结合，用IRT将相对排序分数校准到统一尺度，得到稠密的相关性概率，并提出新指标RCP-nDCG，显著优于传统nDCG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排器质量提升后，依赖人工相关性标签的nDCG指标因标签昂贵、稀疏、含噪且离散而难以区分接近的候选。LLM判断可提供稠密标签：查询内相对判断能区分接近候选但跨查询不可比；绝对评分跨查询可比但过于粗糙，无法区分相关性相似的文档。因此需要一种兼具两者优点的方法。

Method: RCP结合两类LLM判断：一是listwise Bradley-Terry锦标赛对每个查询内的文档排序，二是由严格程度递增的yes/no标准构成的rubric提供绝对标准。再借助项目反应理论（IRT），利用共享标准将所有查询的锦标赛分数校准到同一尺度，从而将相对判断转化为跨查询可比的稠密相关性概率。基于此提出检索指标RCP-nDCG，替代nDCG的离散标签。

Result: 与46名外部标注者的盲评对比：校准将查询平均分与平均人工评分的相关性从0.538提升到0.795；概率将有用文档排在无用文档之上的AUC为0.910（基准标签为0.651）。当标注者偏好某一重排器且仅一个指标一致时，该指标为RCP-nDCG的比例达72.4%（185次比较，随机约53%）。在TREC-DL上，RCP-nDCG与NIST评估者在所有被显著区分开的重排器对上一致；在NanoBEIR上解决了nDCG的许多并列，并区分出1.9倍的重排器对。

Conclusion: Rubric校准将相对LLM判断转化为跨查询可比且与人类判断一致的稠密相关性标签，RCP-nDCG在多个基准上优于传统nDCG，为LLM时代的重排器评估提供了更灵敏可靠的指标。

Abstract: Rerankers decide which documents users and LLMs see, yet their standard metric, nDCG, relies on human relevance labels that are costly, sparse, noisy, and discretely graded. As rerankers approach each other in quality, nDCG on these labels therefore increasingly fails to separate them. LLM judges could supply dense labels. Relative judgments within one query tell even close candidates apart, yet their scores share no scale across queries. Absolute grades share one scale but are too coarse to distinguish documents of similar relevance. We propose Rubric-Calibrated Preferences (RCP), which combine both kinds of judgment. A listwise Bradley-Terry tournament orders each query's documents, and a rubric of yes/no criteria of increasing stringency provides an absolute standard. Item Response Theory (IRT), which scores test-takers based on their answers to common questions, then uses the shared criteria to put all queries' tournament scores on one scale. RCP's retrieval metric, RCP-nDCG, replaces nDCG's discrete labels with the resulting calibrated relevance probabilities. Against blind grades from 46 external annotators, calibration raises the correlation between a query's mean score and its mean human grade from 0.538 to 0.795. The probabilities rank a useful document above a non-useful one with probability 0.910 (AUC, chance 0.5), versus 0.651 for the benchmark labels. When the annotators' grades prefer one of two rerankers and exactly one metric agrees, that metric is RCP-nDCG in 72.4% of 185 comparisons (chance about 53%). On TREC-DL, RCP-nDCG sides with NIST assessors' grades on every reranker pair that these grades separate significantly. RCP-nDCG also resolves many of nDCG's ties and separates 1.9 times as many reranker pairs on NanoBEIR. Rubric calibration thus turns relative LLM judgments into dense relevance labels that are comparable across queries and agree with human judgment.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Process Mining of Patient Flow in an Emergency Department: Bottlenecks, Variant Fragmentation, and Data-Driven Redesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32010)
*Iliano Fasolino*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该文用过程挖掘全流程分析1,820例急诊留观事件日志，发现流程高度非标准化（884种变体、主流才占4.3%）、平均滞留6.58小时且长尾严重，并出现危重患者反而滞留更久的优先度倒置现象；模型完全拟合但精度仅0.71，说明缺乏规范路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科流程标准化程度最低，长滞留常被归因于"需求大"而非基于数据测量；作者希望用真实事件日志量化急诊流程的实际运行状态，识别结构性缺陷而非主观归因。

Method: 对匿名化事件日志（1,820例就诊、25,115个事件）执行完整过程挖掘流水线：预处理解决爆发式日志记录与缺失值；绩效分析在案例层与转移层量化流转；使用Inductive Miner建模，并用基于token的一致性检验评估流程结构；最后基于结果推导两个再设计场景并设定量化目标。

Result: 过滤后日志含1,754例、16,376事件；平均吞吐时间6.58小时且长尾明显；存在884种控制流变体，最常见变体仅覆盖4.3%案例；出现临床优先度倒置——紧急患者（分诊2级，8.03小时）滞留时间比危重患者（1级，5.83小时）长38%；模型适应度完美（1.0）但精度低（0.71）。

Conclusion: 完美适应度与低精度的组合表明急诊缺乏规范性路径，问题在于流程本身的无序而非个体偏离；据此提出基于分诊等级的诊疗路径与前瞻性出院两个再设计场景，并给出量化改进目标。

Abstract: Emergency departments (EDs) run some of the least standardized processes in healthcare, and long stays are often attributed to demand rather than measured on data. We analyze an anonymised event log of 1{,}820 ED stays (25{,}115 events) through a full process mining pipeline: preprocessing resolves burst logging and missing values, performance analysis quantifies flow at the case and transition level, and Inductive Miner with token-based conformance checking assesses process structure. The filtered log (1{,}754 cases, 16{,}376 events) exhibits a mean throughput of 6.58 hours with a heavy tail, 884 distinct control-flow variants whose most frequent one covers only 4.3\% of cases, and an inversion of clinical priority in which urgent patients (acuity 2, 8.03 h) stay 38\% longer than critical ones (acuity 1, 5.83 h). Perfect fitness (1.0) combined with low precision (0.71) reveals absence of normative pathways rather than deviance. Two redesign scenarios, acuity-based care pathways and anticipatory discharge, are derived with quantified targets.

</details>


### [81] [Robust Hierarchical Structures for Agentic Document Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33322)
*Ruiying Ma,Yiming Lin,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 SHED，一种两阶段工作流，用于从层次化文档中推断“鲁棒且紧凑”的结构，提升 LLM agent 处理文档的效率和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 及 agent 通常把 PDF、Word 等文档当作纯文本处理，忽略了文档按章节、小节层次化组织的事实。抽取该结构虽困难，但可让 agent（和人类）只处理相关章节，从而提升效率与效果。然而已有结构抽取工作无法保证推断结构与真实结构的匹配程度。

Method: 提出 SHED，一个两阶段工作流，用于推断鲁棒且紧凑的结构。第一阶段可插拔，兼容无限多种方法，每种方法针对特定文档类别保证鲁棒性；理论上用这些类别及其层次关系刻画文档空间。

Result: 在衡量鲁棒性—紧凑性权衡的 F-1 指标上，SHED 相比非 LLM 基线提升 13%–68%，相比昂贵的 LLM 方法提升 9%–15%。在 agentic 文档分析中，使用 SHED 的 agent 准确率高出 3%–23%，且成本最多降低 10 倍。

Conclusion: SHED 能有效推断鲁棒且紧凑的文档层次结构，既在结构抽取指标上优于现有方法，又能实际提升 agent 文档分析的准确率并显著降低成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) enable us to better understand text documents, including PDFs and Word documents. However, LLMs, as well as more modern LLM agents, i.e., those with tool-calling abilities, typically treat such documents as plain text, ignoring the fact that they are often organized hierarchically into sections and subsections. Extracting this structure, while difficult, can improve efficiency and effectiveness for agents (and humans)---since only sections relevant to a given task need to be processed. Unfortunately, prior work on structure extraction provides no formal guarantees on how well the inferred structure matches the true one. Instead, we target a robust and compact variant that is feasible to infer and useful in practice. Robustness ensures that the text under each subsection header is a superset of the text under the same header in the true structure. Compactness seeks to minimize this superset, reducing agentic cost (or human cognitive load). We propose SHED, a two-stage workflow for inferring a robust and compact structure. The first stage is pluggable with an infinite family of approaches, each guaranteeing robustness for a specific document class. We theoretically characterize the document space using these classes and their hierarchical relationships. Empirically, SHED improves F-1 scores (measuring the robustness--compactness trade-off) by 13%--68% over non-LLM baselines and 9%--15% over expensive LLM-based approaches. Finally, we show how SHED-inferred structures are valuable for agentic document analysis: agents using SHED outperform baselines, achieving 3%--23% higher accuracy while being up to 10x cheaper.

</details>


### [82] [WeaveData: A Multimodal Data Analysis System with Self-Critiquing and Self-Evolving LLM Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34764)
*Min Jia,Shihao Zhou,Jun-Peng Zhu,Peng Cai,Kai Xu,Chao Zhang,Li Li,Aoying Zhou,Heng Long,Qiu Cui,Liu Tang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: WeaveData 是一个面向多模态数据分析的自批判、自进化 LLM 规划系统，通过逻辑计划生成与逐步批判、失败诊断与经验积累、元数据知识图谱与交互式笔记本，提升 LLM 生成计划的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据分析（关系表、文本、图像）日益受到数据管理领域关注，LLM 能以自然语言生成分析计划，但生成的计划容易出错：可能静默地计算了与问题不符的内容、执行中失败，或返回的结果未能回答问题。

Method: 1）为每个问题生成带类型的逻辑计划，执行前逐步批判，执行后校验结果与问题是否一致；2）对失败或答非所问的计划进行进化：结合实际数据诊断失败原因、复用仍有效的结果、并积累规划经验供后续问题使用；3）将规划建立在覆盖所有模态的元数据知识图谱之上，对模糊问题进行用户澄清，并在交互式笔记本中为每个模型判断提供证据支撑。

Result: 论文在两个公开多模态数据集上对 WeaveData 进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: WeaveData 通过自批判与自进化的 LLM 规划机制，结合元数据知识图谱、用户澄清和证据溯源，能够提升多模态数据分析中计划生成的准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal data analysis, which answers questions over relational tables, text, and images, has attracted growing attention in the data management community. Large language models (LLMs) enable such analysis in natural language by generating analysis plans over relational and semantic operators. However, LLM-generated plans are error-prone: a plan may silently compute something other than what was asked, fail during execution, or return a result that misses the question. This paper presents WeaveData, a multimodal data analysis system with self-critiquing and self-evolving LLM plans. First, WeaveData generates a typed logical plan for each question and critiques it step by step before execution, and it checks the executed result against the question afterwards. Second, WeaveData evolves a plan that fails or misses the question: it diagnoses the failure with the actual data, reuses the results that remain valid, and accumulates planning experience for later questions. Third, WeaveData grounds planning in a metadata knowledge graph of all modalities, clarifies ambiguous questions with the user, and backs every model judgment with evidence in an interactive notebook. We demonstrate WeaveData on two public multimodal datasets.

</details>


### [83] [Transformations for Evolving Property Graph Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34789)
*Gauvain Devillez,Stefania Dumbrava,Angela Bonifati*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT，一个基于逻辑的框架，将属性图模式演化建模为可复用、受顺序约束的元变换，并通过相似度引导的搜索与剪枝在有限元图中高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图数据库广泛用于表示复杂且不断变化的数据，但系统性的模式演化支持仍然有限。实践中模式变换通常被手动定义、与特定应用场景耦合，难以在不同模式或演化场景间复用。因此需要一种系统化、可复用的模式演化方法。

Method: GRAFT将属性图模式演化形式化为对有限元图的探索：图中节点为模式，边为由原子编辑派生的、已接地的元变换。元变换被设计为可复用且受顺序约束。为保证可处理性，框架结合了相似度引导的搜索与剪枝，并保证无重复、可终止和正确性。

Result: 在四个基准及真实世界属性图模式演化场景上的实验表明，GRAFT能高效计算高质量的模式变换序列。使用贪心探索时，GRAFT在多数数据集上能到达精确目标模式，生成稳定的变换序列且运行时间较低。在真实世界及合成大规模数据集上的定性研究进一步验证了所获可复用元变换的质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRAFT为属性图模式演化提供了系统化、可复用的逻辑框架，能够高效生成高质量的变换序列，并通过实验证明其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Property graph databases are widely used to represent complex and evolving data; yet, systematic support for property graph schema evolution remains limited. In practice, schema transformations are typically defined manually, coupled to specific application contexts, and are difficult to reuse across schemas or evolution scenarios. We present GRAFT, a logic-based framework that models prop- erty graph schema evolution as reusable, order-constrained meta- transformations derived from atomic edits. Schema evolution is formulated as exploration of a finite meta-graph with schemas as nodes and grounded meta-transformations as edges. To ensure tractability, GRAFT combines similarity-guided search and pruning, guaranteeing duplication-freeness, termination and correctness. An experimental evaluation on four benchmark and real-world property graph schema evolution scenarios shows that GRAFT effi- ciently computes high-quality schema transformation sequences. Using greedy exploration, GRAFT reaches the exact target schema on most datasets, producing stable transformation sequences while keeping runtimes low. A qualitative study on both real-world and a synthetic large-scale dataset further shows the quality and robust- ness of the obtained reusable meta-transformations.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [ASSEMBLE: Atomic Skills for Evidence-Grounded Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32466)
*Xiyang Wu,Zongxia Li,Shengxin Zhang,Zhichao Liu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出ASSEMBLE框架，将支持证据显式化以增强长视频推理的可追溯性，在准确率与接地精度上均优于Gemini-2.5-Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂视频推理依赖分散在远距离时刻、实体和事件中的证据，但仅凭正确答案无法判断模型是否依赖了正确的视频片段。因此需要一个能让支持证据在长视频推理全程显式化的框架。

Method: ASSEMBLE将局部观察与跨片段叙述组织成带时间戳、可追溯到源视频的证据目录；接地感知阅读器组合问题特定的原子技能，其结构化输出包含显式证据引用与支持度评估。采用正确性门控的引用对齐作为直接接地信号，经教师监督微调后用GRPO联合优化答案正确性与引用对齐。使用9B阅读器（由235B教师监督）与共享预计算证据目录。

Result: 在三个长视频推理基准上，ASSEMBLE达到59.2%宏平均答案准确率（Gemini-2.5-Pro为58.3%），宏平均基于重叠的接地准确率提升6.7%，且在三个基准上均有增益。消融实验表明，在相同后训练阅读器与推理预算下，结构化技能推理比自由形式推理的接地准确率更高。

Conclusion: 显式证据接地可以直接整合进长视频推理，同时不牺牲答案准确率。

Abstract: Complex video reasoning often depends on evidence scattered across distant moments, entities, and events, yet a correct answer alone does not reveal whether a model relied on the right parts of the video. We introduce ASSEMBLE, a framework that makes supporting evidence explicit throughout long-video reasoning. ASSEMBLE organizes local observations and cross-clip narratives into timestamped evidence catalogs traceable to the source video. A grounding-aware reader then composes question-specific atomic skills whose structured outputs contain explicit evidence references and support assessments. We use correctness-gated citation alignment as a direct grounding signal: after teacher-supervised fine-tuning, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) jointly optimizes answer correctness and citation alignment. This produces inspectable intermediate traces while keeping final predictions linked to explicit supporting evidence. Using a 9B reader supervised by a 235B teacher and shared precomputed evidence catalogs, ASSEMBLE achieves 59.2% macro-averaged answer accuracy across three long-video reasoning benchmarks, compared with 58.3% for Gemini-2.5-Pro, while improving macro-averaged overlap-based Grounded accuracy by 6.7%, with gains on all three benchmarks. Ablations further show that, with the same post-trained reader and inference budget, structured skill inference improves Grounded accuracy over free-form reasoning. Together, these results show that explicit evidence grounding can be integrated directly into long-video reasoning without sacrificing answer accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [ReVR: Dual-Path Concept Reasoning for Multimodal Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33195)
*Zhikai Tan,Yuzhou Yang,Qichao Ying,Pinjie Xu,Sheng Li,Zhenxing Qian,Xinpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出ReVR，一个双路径推理框架，通过构建并应用可复用的验证概念来提升多模态假新闻检测的性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽借助显式概念提升可解释性，但仍存在两个问题：如何提高验证概念的可靠性与适用性，以及如何有效将可复用概念应用于未见新闻的验证。

Method: 采用智能体工作流对候选概念进行grounding与整合，并用统计画像刻画其历史行为；推理阶段包含两条路径：面向覆盖的路径用可训练编码器从完整概念库聚合证据，面向查询的路径提示冻结VLM对选定概念及其观察进行推理；两路径分歧时由学习到的冲突解决器进行选择。

Result: 在假新闻基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检测性能和泛化能力方面均有效。

Conclusion: ReVR通过构建与应用可复用验证概念的双路径推理，有效提升了多模态假新闻检测的性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) support multimodal fake news detection (FND) by producing explicit analyses. Recent methods further improve interpretability by organizing verification knowledge into explicit concepts. However, two questions remain: how to improve the reliability and applicability of verification concepts, and how to effectively apply reusable concepts to verify unseen news. We propose \textbf{ReVR}, a dual-path reasoning framework that constructs and applies reusable verification concepts for multimodal fake news detection. An agentic workflow grounds and consolidates candidate concepts, while statistical profiles characterize their historical behavior. During inference, a coverage-oriented path aggregates evidence from the complete concept library using a trainable encoder, while a query-focused path prompts a frozen VLM to reason over selected concepts and their observations. A learned conflict resolver selects between the two predictions when they disagree. Experiments on fake news benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the method regarding detection performance and generalizability.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Hypothesis-awkward: Property-Based Testing Strategies for Awkward Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31820)
*Tai Sakuma,Ianna Osborne,Peter Elmer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Hypothesis-awkward，一套为 Awkward Array 设计的 Hypothesis 策略集合，可生成几乎各种嵌套变长混合类型数组，用于基于属性的测试，已在 Awkward Array 的持续集成中应用并发现 38 个 bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Awkward Array 能表示大量嵌套、变长、混合类型数据，许多处理它的工具被广泛使用且活跃开发，但这些工具的单元测试多用预定义固定样本，仅能覆盖庞大组合空间中很小的一部分，难以发现边界情况。Hypothesis 作为 Python 基于属性的测试库，能自动生成测试数据并探索边界，因此需要为 Awkward Array 提供专门的生成策略。

Method: 提出 Hypothesis-awkward，包含一系列针对 Awkward Array 的 Hypothesis 策略。其主要策略可生成几乎所有的 Awkward Array，并提供控制布局、数据类型、缺失值、掩码及其他数组属性的选项。

Result: 用这些策略编写的属性测试已开始运行于 Awkward Array 的持续集成中，目前覆盖了 Awkward Array 的一小部分，并在其中发现 37 个 bug，以及在 Hypothesis 本身发现 1 个 bug。

Conclusion: 这些策略使 Awkward Array 及其使用工具的测试更加可靠，展示了属性基测试在复杂数组数据结构中的实用价值。

Abstract: Hypothesis-awkward is a collection of Hypothesis strategies for Awkward Array. Awkward Array can represent a wide variety of nested, variable-length, mixed-type data. Many tools that process Awkward Arrays are widely used and actively developed. The unit test cases of many of these tools list predefined input samples. In practice, such samples can cover only a small portion of the vast combinatorial space of Awkward Array instances. Hypothesis, a Python property-based testing library, generates test data that can make test cases fail and automatically explores edge cases. The main strategy of Hypothesis-awkward generates nearly all possible Awkward Arrays, with options to control the layout, data types, missing values, masks, and other array attributes. Property-based tests written with these strategies have begun to run in the continuous integration of Awkward Array; to date, they cover a small part of Awkward Array and have found 37 bugs in it, as well as one in Hypothesis itself. These strategies make the tests of Awkward Array and of the tools that use it more reliable.

</details>


### [87] [Verification as an Architectural Layer for LLM Agents: A V-Model Design, and a Pilot Study of Its Deterministic Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31937)
*Ali Afoud,Jie JW Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出将验证作为架构层引入LLM智能体，借鉴软件工程的V模型，为每个抽象层级配备专用验证器与确定性控制器。在MuSiQue四跳子集上的试验表明，无验证配置全部失败，而带验证配置显著改善终止与作答行为，且零成本确定性门控贡献了大部分修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ReAct模式的LLM智能体把策略选择、动作选择、格式化与结果判断四类职责集中在单一模型中，生成循环之外缺乏拒绝机制，导致无法推进的智能体不会报告失败，只能被外部预算强制终止。作者因此希望用架构化的验证层来替代这种靠耗尽预算的终止方式。

Method: 将验证作为架构层，把软件工程的V模型迁移到智能体：规格层级从需求逐级下降到单步，每级对应一个专用验证器；由确定性控制器执行每个判定；仅验证结果可写入记忆，使拒绝能定位引入故障的层级。每个验证器区分零成本的确定性门控与可选的LLM评判器，以独立测量各自贡献与成本。文中实现验收级与单元级验证器对，在MuSiQue四跳子集、8B参数骨干上比较五种共享执行器、工具集与评分器的配置，仅验证方式不同。

Result: 47次执行中，两种无验证配置在十个问题上全部未答对，每次运行都终止于步数上限或提供方token限制；带验证但无规划器的配置答对八个、其余选择弃答。九个观察到的修正中有八个由确定性门控以零边际成本产生，而一旦引入验证，规划反而降低性能。

Conclusion: 结果刻画的是终止行为而非大规模准确率。作者提出为期十二个月的计划以完成并评估完整架构，包括本试点尚未涵盖的集成级验证器对。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents built on the ReAct pattern concentrate four responsibilities in one model: selecting a strategy, choosing each action, formatting it, and judging whether the result is adequate. Nothing outside the generative loop can reject its output, so an agent that cannot make progress does not report failure; it runs until an external budget stops it. We propose treating verification as an architectural layer by adapting the V-model from software engineering: specification levels descend from requirements to individual steps, each level is paired with a dedicated verifier, a deterministic controller enforces every verdict, and only verification outcomes write to memory, so a rejection localizes the level that introduced the fault and an agent halts by declining rather than by exhaustion. Each verifier separates a zero-cost deterministic \emph{gate} from an optional LLM \emph{judge}, so the contribution and cost of each can be measured independently. We report a pilot implementing the acceptance- and unit-level verifier pairs, comparing five configurations that share one executor, tool set, and scorer and differ only in verification, on the four-hop stratum of MuSiQue with an 8B-parameter backbone. Across 47 executions, the two unverified configurations answered none of ten questions, every run ending at a step cap or provider token limit; the verified configuration without a planner answered eight and abstained on the rest. Deterministic gates produced eight of the nine observed corrections at zero marginal cost, and planning degraded performance once verification was present. These results characterize termination behavior, not accuracy at scale; we outline a twelve-month plan to complete and evaluate the full architecture, including the integration-level pair the pilot omits.

</details>


### [88] [Methodological Harness in Agentic Software Engineering: An Empirical Study on Mining Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32014)
*Jessica Díaz,Jorge Pérez,Sergio Gil-Borrás*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于 AIDev 数据集的 116,211 个 GitHub 仓库（分析 5,435 个），检验智能体软件工程治理框架的可观测性。结果显示：至少一个机制的人口流行率为 21.7%，高可见仓库达 65.3%，但多机制组合罕见；规则文件主要指导智能体并陈述规范，而持久共享知识、可执行规范、结构化协商与渐进式自主仅少数出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体软件工程需要协调与治理机制协同工作，既有框架提出了包含八种机制的方法学工具集（如上下文工程、持久共享知识、可执行规范等），并通过规则文件、规范、架构决策记录等制品实现，但该框架此前尚未在仓库规模上得到实证验证。

Method: 从 AIDev 数据集的 116,211 个 GitHub 仓库中，按可见度（以 stars 作为流行度/声誉指标）分层设计，选取 5,435 个仓库进行分析。RQ1 检测并量化七种可观测机制的制品及其引入时间；RQ2 对来自 150 个仓库的规则文件进行定性编码。

Result: 至少一种机制的人口流行率为 21.7%，而在最高可见度仓库中为 65.3%。多机制配置较为罕见。在存在规则文件的仓库中，规则文件几乎总是用于指导智能体并陈述规范；而持久共享知识、可执行规范、结构化协商与渐进式自主仅在少数仓库中出现。

Conclusion: 该工具集在实证上可被观测，但主要表现为孤立机制的存在，而非框架所提出的集成化系统，说明框架的落地程度与理想设计之间仍存在明显差距。

Abstract: Context. Agentic software engineering requires mechanisms to coordinate and govern agents' work. The framework motivating this study proposes a methodological harness with eight mechanisms: context engineering, persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, N-version mindset and parallel agents, normative specifications, structured consultation, evidence-based acceptance, and graduated autonomy. These are realized through artifacts such as rule or context files, specifications, and architectural decision records, but the framework had not been empirically validated at repository scale. Objective. We analyze to what extent and how this harness is observable in repositories with agentic activity. RQ1 characterizes adoption through artifact prevalence, breadth, co-occurrence, and temporal evolution; RQ2 examines rule files - a key observable artifact and persistent source of agent instructions - to assess how their content reflects the proposed mechanisms. Method. From the AIDev dataset of 116,211 GitHub repositories, we analyzed 5,435 using a design stratified by visibility, measured through stars as an indicator of popularity and/or reputation. For RQ1, we detected and quantified artifacts and introduction dates for the seven observable mechanisms. For RQ2, we qualitatively coded rule files from 150 repositories. Results. Population prevalence of at least one mechanism is 21.7%, versus 65.3% among the most visible repositories. Multi-mechanism configurations are rare. Where rule files exist, they almost always guide the agent and state norms, while persistent shared knowledge, executable specifications, structured consultation, and graduated autonomy appear only in a minority. Conclusions. The harness is empirically observable, but mainly through isolated mechanisms rather than the integrated system proposed by the framework.

</details>


### [89] [CG-Diff: Organizing Code Changes Around Call Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32057)
*Bimal Raj Gyawali,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Nadia Polikarpova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于调用图的代码审查工具 CG-Diffs，将 PR 变更按调用关系重组为可导航的子图，并通过实验验证其优于 GitHub 原生视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码审查工具以逐文件方式展示变更，但变更往往围绕调用关系组织；实证发现约 40% 的 PR 中过半变更函数由调用图连接，且必需的被调用者常与调用者位于不同文件，因此逐文件视图不利于理解与导航。

Method: 首先对 GitHub 真实 PR 进行实证研究以量化调用图连接情况；据此提出 CG-Diffs 概念，将变更代码的调用图分解为更小、更易导航的有向子图；并实现基于网页的 PR 查看界面，围绕 CG-Diffs 重构 PR 展示；最后通过被试内研究将新界面与 GitHub PR 视图对比。

Result: 被试内研究表明，CG-Diffs 有助于参与者定位方向，并提供更有意义的导航结构；同时发现局限：函数节点在多个 CG-Diffs 中重复出现会造成困惑，而函数之外（如全局变量、导入）的变更不够直观。

Conclusion: 研究显示利用调用图为不熟悉代码库的上下文理解与导航提供帮助具有前景，可惠及开源新贡献者及审阅不熟悉的 LLM 生成 PR 的审查者。

Abstract: Tools for code review present code changes file-by-file. We argue that, oftentimes, changes can be better organized around a call graph of the changed code. From an empirical study of GitHub pull requests (PRs) in-the-wild, we find that (1) in around 40% of the PRs, more than half of the changed functions (and methods) are connected by call graphs, and (2) necessary callees are often located in different files from their callers. Based on these findings, we develop the notion of CG-Diffs, subgraphs derived by decomposing a call graph of the changed code into smaller and more navigable directed graphs. We then implement a web-based interface for viewing PRs that restructures the PR around these CG-Diffs. Through a within-subject study comparing our interface against GitHub's PR view, we find that CG-Diffs help orient participants and provide them with more meaningful structures to navigate the PR. We also found several limitations: function nodes repeated across multiple CG-Diffs can be disorienting, and changes not contained in a function (e.g. globals, imports) are not as immediately apparent. Our study shows promise in using call graphs to help contextualize and navigate unfamiliar codebases, which may benefit new contributors to open source, and reviewers of unfamiliar LLM-generated PRs.

</details>


### [90] [IChart2Code: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Interactive Chart Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32413)
*Xu Zhang,Hongzhang Zheng,Zhili Huang,Yaoyi Wang,Ling Xu,Sheng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出IChart2Code交互式图表代码生成基准及浏览器评估协议，并设计TRAIL双智能体框架提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表到代码基准只关注静态输出，缺乏对交互规范的任务表示、浏览器执行与交互后验证的评估协议，无法满足交互式图表代码生成的需求。

Method: 构建含377个任务、20种图表形式、13类数据、6族1209项交互需求的IChart2Code基准；设计基于浏览器的评测协议（可执行性门控＋数据保真、静态视觉正确、交互正确三维度），用MLLM评审逐项打分；提出TRAIL轨迹引导双智能体框架，由Inspector生成检查、浏览器执行并产出结构化修复反馈。

Result: 评审协议对人工标注的条目级F1达0.8844；TRAIL在四个评估维度上相较直接提示平均分别提升7.89、4.98、3.45、4.47个百分点。

Conclusion: IChart2Code为交互式图表代码生成提供可执行、可验证的评测基准，TRAIL框架有效提升多模型在数据、视觉与交互维度的表现。

Abstract: Interactive chart code generation requires models to reproduce a reference chart's appearance and underlying data and correctly implement the state changes triggered by specified user interactions. Existing chart-to-code benchmarks focus on static outputs and lack task representations or evaluation protocols for interaction specification, browser execution, and post-interaction verification. We introduce IChart2Code, a benchmark comprising 377 tasks across 20 chart forms and 13 data families, with 1209 interaction requirements in six families. Each task provides a reference screenshot, task-local data, and natural-language interaction requirements, with executable HTML/JavaScript code as the target output. We further develop a browser-based evaluation protocol with an Executability gate and three rubric-guided dimensions: Data Fidelity, Static Visual Correctness, and Interaction Correctness. The protocol tests runtime viability, consistency with task-local data, fidelity of the initial rendering to the reference screenshot, and interaction-induced state changes in a sandboxed browser. A rubric-guided MLLM judge evaluates task-specific items for the three scored dimensions using the collected browser observations and achieves an overall item-level F1 score of 0.8844 against adjudicated human labels. We also propose TRAIL, a trajectory-guided dual-agent framework for interactive chart code generation. An Inspector derives task-specific inspection checks, executes them in the browser, and uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures and produce structured repair feedback. Averaged across four MLLMs, TRAIL improves the four evaluation dimensions over direct prompting by 7.89, 4.98, 3.45, and 4.47 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [91] [How AI Changes DevOps Performance: A Mechanism-Based Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32414)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过审计证据、构建双构念模型并进行离散事件仿真，区分AI生成代码（AGC）与代理式AI（AGT）对DORA绩效指标的差异化影响，并给出测量规则、治理护栏与样本量指导的实证验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI工具对软件交付绩效（DORA指标）影响的证据混杂、多为横截面研究，且常将AI生成代码与代理式AI混为一谈。研究动机是厘清这两类AI能力各自及联合的作用机制，弥补因果与纵向证据的不足。

Method: 审计现有证据，形式化一个含四个能力调节变量的双构念模型；实现交付流水线的离散事件仿真；开展六项实验：析因设计、Shapley分解、600次参数抽取的敏感性分析、固定需求稳健性检验，以及含反事实的5,000个模拟团队交错采纳面板。

Result: AGC提高失败率、返工与恢复时间；当评审能力有余时缩短前置时间（-13%），评审饱和后则延长；低能力团队的部署频率提升完全来自计划外返工。AGT改善部署频率（+31%~+34%）、前置时间（-8%~-22%）与恢复时间（-29%~-38%），但其稳定性效应依赖自主CI修复掩盖缺陷，高能力团队逃逸缺陷增加。能力降低了AGC的绝对失败率惩罚（7.9 vs 1.5个百分点），但未降低相对惩罚（+34% vs +84%）。双向固定效应低估采纳效应8-16%；检测稳定性效应约需50个团队，检测其能力调节约需400个。

Conclusion: 在模型内，DORA指标对AI的响应不仅源于代码质量，还通过排队、批处理与监督机制发生作用。论文据此提出测量规则、代理式修复的治理护栏，以及基于样本量的现场验证协议。

Abstract: Context. AI tools for software development have progressed from code completion to autonomous agents, yet evidence on their effects on DORA performance is mixed, mostly cross-sectional, and often conflates AI-generated code (AGC) with agentic AI (AGT). Objective. We examine how AGC and AGT, separately and jointly, affect deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, recovery time, and deployment rework rate. Method. We audit the evidence, formalise a two-construct model with four capability moderators, and implement a discrete-event simulation of a delivery pipeline. Six experiments include a factorial design, Shapley decompositions, sensitivity analysis over 600 parameter draws, a fixed-demand robustness test, and a staggered-adoption panel of 5,000 simulated teams with counterfactuals. Results. AGC increased failure rate, rework, and recovery time in both capability profiles. When AI increased change volume, AGC shortened lead time only where review capacity was spare (-13%) and lengthened it once review saturated; in the low-capability team, its deployment-frequency gain came entirely from unplanned rework. AGT improved deployment frequency (+31% to +34%), lead time (-8% to -22%), and recovery time (-29% to -38%), but its stability effect depended on autonomous CI repair masking defects, and escaped defects increased in the high-capability team. Capability reduced AGC's absolute failure-rate penalty (7.9 vs. 1.5 points) but not its relative penalty (+34% vs. +84%). Two-way fixed effects underestimated adoption effects by 8-16%; detecting the modelled stability effect required about 50 teams, and its capability moderation about 400. Conclusions. Within the model, DORA metrics respond to AI through queueing, batching, and oversight, not only code quality. We derive measurement rules, guardrails for agentic remediation, and a sample-size-informed field-validation protocol.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond the Model: Demystifying Harness Effects in Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32459)
*Haichuan Hu,Quanjun Zhang,Shengcheng Yu,Zhifei Chen,Tianyu Luo,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了软件工程（SE）智能体中"harness"（外壳框架）设计对性能的影响，发现其效果取决于模型能力与任务类型的共同作用，并据此构建了轻量模块化框架 NanoHarness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的SE智能体性能不仅取决于基础模型，还受agent harness的显著影响。但harness设计的作用尚未被充分理解，尤其在跨模型、跨任务及不同harness组件层面。

Method: 先评测两种代表性harness（mini-SWE-agent与OpenCode），使用Qwen与DeepSeek两大家族共十个模型，在SWE-bench Pro、ProgramBench、GitTaskBench三个基准上测试；再基于mini-SWE-agent构建轻量模块化框架NanoHarness，分析工具注册表、上下文压缩、显式规划、子智能体、惰性技能五个组件的作用。

Result: harness效果与模型能力和任务类型联合相关：复杂harness在SWE式问题修复上随模型能力提升边际收益递减，但在更复杂的开放式仓库级任务上对强模型有益。组件分析显示，结构化工具使用与任务专用子智能体改进最稳定，而上下文压缩与通用子智能体会损害仓库生成性能。组合后NanoHarness相较mini-SWE-agent在Qwen3.7-Max和DeepSeek-V4-Pro上分别提升7.37和6.21个百分点，恢复产品级harness的大部分增益。

Conclusion: harness设计是SE智能体性能的一等因素，本研究为构建更有效、更高效的编码智能体提供了洞见。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, yet their performance is not determined by the base model alone. The agent harness substantially shapes how SE agents interact with repositories, execute actions, and validate solutions. However, the role of harness design remains insufficiently understood, especially across different models, tasks, and harness components. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of harness effects in SE agents. We first evaluate two representative harnesses, mini-SWE-agent and OpenCode, with ten models from two prominent open-weight model families, Qwen and DeepSeek, on three benchmarks: SWE-bench Pro, ProgramBench, and GitTaskBench. We then construct NanoHarness, a lightweight modular harness built on top of mini-SWE-agent, and use it to analyze five representative harness components: tool registry, context compression, explicit planning, subagents, and lazy skills. Experimental results show that harness effectiveness depends jointly on model capability and task type. Complex harnesses provide diminishing marginal gains on SWE-style issue repair as model capability improves, but can benefit stronger models on more complex and open-ended repository-level tasks. Component-level analysis on ProgramBench further shows that structured tool use and task-specific subagents provide the most stable improvements, while context compression and general subagents can hurt repository-generation performance. When combined, NanoHarness improves over mini-SWE-agent by 7.37 and 6.21 percentage points on Qwen3.7-Max and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, respectively, recovering most of the gains of product-level harnesses. These findings highlight harness design as a first-class factor in SE-agent performance and provide insights for building more effective and efficient coding agents.

</details>


### [93] [Evolution of Deprecated APIs and Their Replacements in Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32618)
*Gangqiang He,Guanping Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过830条维护者指定的弃用API与替代API映射，系统考察了替代API的模块位置、参数差异、版本间排名变化及弃用后生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别探讨API弃用、替代API识别和客户端迁移，但跨版本和生命周期状态下的"弃用API—替代API"关系尚不清晰，尤其缺乏对替代局部性、参数接口差异、排名变化及弃用后生命周期的综合理解。

Method: 从33个Python库中构建830条维护者指定的映射（涵盖类、函数、方法）；比较定义、在两种迁移场景下追踪基于相似度的排名，并通过源码检查与逐版本执行识别生命周期事件。

Result: 同类替换在类、函数、方法中分别占39.1%、55.6%、79.9%；多数映射无参数变化，但函数映射变化更频繁多样；排名随版本和代码表示变化，各方法仅在约60%情况下对总体趋势一致；49.0%关键版本中其他候选效应占主导或混合；弃用后56.4%保留源定义、68.6%保留可执行原调用；753条映射中25.2%为源先或调用先结束，其中77.4%为源先。

Conclusion: 弃用API与替代API的关系取决于API粒度、发布上下文、代码表示、候选竞争和生命周期状态，可支持演化感知的替代API推荐与自动化API迁移。

Abstract: Context: API deprecation is common in library evolution, but migration requires finding replacements and adapting calls. Prior studies examine deprecation, replacement identification, and client migration separately, leaving deprecated API-replacement API relationships across releases and lifecycle states insufficiently understood. Objective: We examine replacement locality, parameter-interface differences, changes in replacement rankings across versions, and post-deprecation lifecycles from source-definition and original-invocation perspectives. Method: We construct 830 maintainer-specified mappings from 33 Python libraries, covering classes, functions, and methods. We compare definitions, track similarity-based rankings under two migration scenarios, and identify lifecycle events through source inspection and release-by-release execution. Results: Same-module replacements account for 39.1%, 55.6%, and 79.9% of class, function, and method mappings, respectively. Most mappings have no parameter changes, but function mappings show more frequent and diverse changes. Rankings vary across releases and code representations, and the applicable methods agree on the overall trend in only about 60% of cases. Other-candidate effects are dominant or mixed in 49.0% of key versions. After deprecation, 56.4% of mappings retain the source definition and 68.6% retain an executable original invocation. Among 753 mappings with at least one observed ending event, 25.2% are source-first or invocation-first rather than same-release. Of these cases, 77.4% are source-first. Conclusions: Deprecated API-replacement API relationships depend on API granularity, release context, code representation, candidate competition, and lifecycle state. These results support evolution-aware replacement API recommendation and automated API migration.

</details>


### [94] [AsynCodeBench: Benchmarking Collaboration of Asynchronous Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32662)
*Kaituo Zhang,Zhen Xiong,Zhimeng Jiang,Mingyu Zhong,Zhouyuan Yuan,Zhecheng Li,Bowen Lin,Chia-Yuan Chang,Mingzhi Hu,Huazheng Wang,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AsynCodeBench，一个以依赖图为核心的多智能体软件工程基准，用可执行依赖检查器量化跨智能体协作，并提出 ADPR 与 DRS 两个指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编码已从单智能体问题求解转向分布式协作，但仍缺乏对协作的直接度量，现有评测多沿用单智能体编码的任务级结果，混淆了个体编码能力与跨智能体协调能力。

Method: 构建 AsynCodeBench，以显式依赖图表示每个任务并提供可执行 Dependency Checkers；据此提出两个互补指标：ADPR 衡量最终被满足的跨智能体依赖数量，DRS 衡量每个依赖首次被满足的执行步位置。基准包含来自真实仓库的 19 个任务，暴露 52 条有向依赖作为协作评测单元。

Result: 跨模型家族、规模和代际的实验显示编码能力与协作能力存在明显差距：编码性能提升不一定带来更强的协作，任务级指标与依赖级协作度量可能显著背离。依赖轨迹分析发现成功协调往往非渐进出现，而是在执行轨迹的短区间内集中爆发，称为 hopping window。

Conclusion: 需要以依赖为中心的细粒度指标来独立评估多智能体协作，而非仅依赖任务级结果；协作能力与编码能力应被区分对待。

Abstract: Multi-agent coding has emerged as an increasingly active direction in software engineering, where complex development tasks are decomposed across multiple specialized agents working on different parts of the problem. Despite the shift from individual problem solving to distributed collaboration, multi-agent systems still lack a direct measure of collaboration and are largely evaluated through task-level outcomes inherited from single-agent coding, conflating individual coding capability with cross-agent coordination. We introduce AsynCodeBench, a dependency-centric benchmark for asynchronous multi-agent software engineering that represents each task with an explicit dependency graph and executable Dependency Checkers. Through this dependency-tracking process, we propose two complementary measures: Asynchronous Dependency Pass Rate (ADPR), which measures how many cross-agent dependencies are ultimately satisfied, and Dependency Resolution Step (DRS), which measures when each dependency first becomes satisfied during execution. AsynCodeBench comprises 19 tasks from real-world repositories, exposing 52 directed dependencies as explicit units for evaluating cross-agent collaboration. Experiments across model families, scales, and generations reveal a clear gap between coding and collaboration capability: improvements in coding performance do not necessarily translate into stronger collaboration, and task-level metrics can diverge substantially from dependency-level collaboration measures. Dependency-trajectory analysis further reveals that successful coordination often emerges not gradually, but through concentrated bursts in which many dependencies become resolved over a short portion of the execution trajectory, a pattern we term a hopping window.

</details>


### [95] [Social3Source: Connecting Social Provenance, Societal Qualities, and Social Purpose on an Open-Source Software Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32823)
*Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"Social3Source"概念，主张软件开发不应止于开源，而应进一步将社会价值纳入开发过程，使其真正造福人类与社会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当今软件生产与部署速度空前加快，但作者质疑：如果软件无益于社会，则其意义何在。作者认为构建软件的终极目标应是改善人类生活与社会，而仅靠"开源"并不足够。

Method: 本文属概念性/立场性论文，提出了Social3Source的理念、原则，并对其实现路径进行了初步讨论，说明如何将社会层面的考量整合进开源软件开发之中。

Result: 论文给出了Social3Source的概念界定、核心原则及实施层面的初步探讨，阐述了社会因素如何融入开源开发流程。

Conclusion: 作者强调，我们从未生产过如此多的软件，因此更应确保这些软件"真正有价值"（make it count），即在开源基础上进一步追求社会效益。

Abstract: The speed at which software can be created and deployed today has increased dramatically. But what is the point of all this software if it does not contribute to society? The ultimate goal of building software should be bettering human lives and society. And this goes beyond publishing the software as open source; it requires considering how it is developed, the societal qualities it embodies, and the purposes it serves. This is what I call Social3Source. This paper presents the concept, principles, and initial discussion for the implementation of Social3Source, illustrating how it integrates social considerations into open-source software development. We have never generated as much software as we do today. Let's make sure it counts.

</details>


### [96] [What Does a Skill Actually Do? Estimands and Evaluation Validity for Tool and Skill Use in LLM Agents: A Critical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33153)
*Shuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性综述，从"估计目标（estimand）"视角系统梳理大语言模型智能体中工具与技能库设计的评价问题，提出六个分析轴，并指出配对增益、触发条件对比、预算约束收益等常见报告之间的关键区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越依赖从数千条目的库中运行时选取的外部工具与可复用技能，但相关研究声称检索器、路由器或技能库"改进"了智能体时，所指可能是检索召回率、启用技能库带来的成功率变化、仅限被触发任务的配对对比，或近似预算约束下的增益。这些说法含义不同却常被混用，因此有必要厘清每种设计究竟在比较什么、依赖哪些假设。

Method: 沿用基于估计目标的智能体评估思路，沿六个轴刻画工具与技能设计：处理对比、目标人群、结果指标、预算约束、汇总测度与识别假设。以13项核心实证研究为证据综合的支柱，辅以相关方法学工作及对更广泛文献在design层面的解读。通过"触发条件配对"的分解、分析性反例与跨研究比较来显化区分；同时对比"精选技能供给 vs 检索器替换""触发子集 vs 全任务结果""观测成本下降 vs 预算约束比较"。文末给出报告清单与实例。

Result: 本综述未开展新实验，实证结果均来自所引研究，数值玩具示例仅为分析性说明。主要论证发现：仅按任务配对并不能识别调用效应；配对增益与回退计数测度的是特定协议下的不一致，而非期望结果变差的任务比例；模块部署的总效应与预算约束下的效率回答的是不同问题。触发条件配对、反例与跨研究比较共同表明，许多"改进"声称在估计目标不清时不可直接比较。

Conclusion: 提出的六轴框架、报告清单与工作示例把原文作者已部分意识到的区分明确化，帮助研究者在报告工具/技能库效果时说明处理对比、目标人群、结果与识别假设。核心结论是：配对增益、总效应与预算约束效率互不等价，报告时应明确其所回答的具体问题并据此选择匹配的测量与假设。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly draw on external tools and reusable skills selected at run time from libraries that hold thousands of entries. Reports that a retriever, router, or skill library "improves" an agent may refer to retrieval recall, the success change from enabling a library, a paired contrast restricted to triggered tasks, or a gain under an approximate budget constraint. This critical review asks what each design compares and under which assumptions. Building on estimand-based approaches to agent evaluation, we describe tool and skill designs along six axes: treatment contrast, target population, outcome, budget constraint, summary measure, and identification assumptions. Thirteen core empirical studies anchor the evidence synthesis, supplemented by related methodological work and design-level reading of the wider literature. Our contribution is to make explicit distinctions that some source authors already acknowledge through a decomposition of trigger-conditioned pairing, analytic counterexamples, and comparisons across studies. Pairing on the task does not by itself identify an invocation effect; paired gain and regression counts measure protocol-specific discordance rather than the share of tasks whose expected outcomes worsen; and total effects of module deployment answer a different question from budget-constrained efficiency. We compare curated skill provision with retriever replacement, triggered subsets with all-task outcomes, and observed cost reductions with budget-constrained comparisons. A reporting checklist and worked examples connect these distinctions to information that studies can report. The review runs no new experiments; empirical results come from the cited studies, and numerical toy examples are analytical illustrations.

</details>


### [97] [WideSWE: Can Coding Agents Coordinate Changes Across Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33382)
*Baoyi Wang,Xingliang Wang,Jinyang Wu,Keming Wu,Chen Zhi,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WideSWE 基准，用于评估编码智能体在跨多个仓库的协同修改任务上的能力，任务成功率最高仅 42.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评测虽已从解决单个问题发展到长周期开发，但任务完成度大多在单一代码库内评估；而软件生态中许多功能与缺陷修复需要跨多个仓库协同修改，缺乏相应评测标准。

Method: 从 103 个软件生态中挖掘并人工审核跨仓库变更，构建 120 个真实任务（60 个缺陷修复 + 60 个功能开发）；提示词来源于相关 issue 和 pull request；系统性地审查与改编隐藏测试，以支持多样化的正确实现，同时保留必需行为与回归检查；在七种智能体配置上评测，并对比逐仓库独立执行与联合执行的差异。

Result: 七种配置的完整任务成功率在 10.83% 到 42.50% 之间，Codex CLI 搭配 GPT-5.6-sol 的配置成功率最高。轨迹分析显示失败模式包括：未能识别所需变更、识别到变更但未完成、修改了相关仓库却未完全满足请求。独立执行主要能弥补遗漏的工作，但对已尝试但失败实现的纠正效果较差；联合执行可利用相关仓库信息指导实现与验证。

Conclusion: 跨仓库协同修改对当前编码智能体仍具挑战，联合执行优于逐仓库独立执行；WideSWE 为跨仓库编码智能体评测提供了基准与开源代码支持。

Abstract: Coding-agent evaluation has progressed from resolving individual issues to carrying out long-horizon development, yet task completion is still largely assessed within a single codebase. In software ecosystems, many features and bug fixes require coordinated changes across multiple repositories. We introduce WideSWE to evaluate coding agents on such cross-repository tasks. Mining and reviewing changes across 103 software ecosystems yields 120 real-world tasks, balanced between 60 bug fixes and 60 features. We derive prompts from related issues and pull requests. We systematically review and adapt hidden tests to support diverse correct implementations while preserving required behavior and regression checks. Across seven agent configurations, full task success ranges from 10.83% to 42.50%, with the configuration pairing Codex CLI with GPT-5.6-sol achieving the highest rate. Trajectories show agents failing to identify necessary changes, recognizing changes but leaving them unfinished, or modifying the required repositories without fully satisfying the request. To examine whether working on one repository at a time can alleviate these difficulties, we compare it with joint execution under identical prompts. Independent execution mainly recovers omitted work and is less effective at correcting previously attempted but unsuccessful implementations. Joint execution can use information from related repositories to guide implementation and verification. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-ACES-ISE/WideSWE.

</details>


### [98] [Graph-Guided Repository Environment Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33429)
*Jianying Pan,John Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graph2Env，一种以 DepGraph（带类型的依赖图）为中心的 agent 方法，显式表示仓库环境需求、依赖关系及其状态，并结合执行反馈持续精化，最终在 200 个 Python 仓库上取得 81.0% EBSR 与 59.3% ESSR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体日益依赖执行来验证解决方案，因此构建可靠的执行环境成为关键能力。然而仓库环境构建困难：执行需求分散在各类仓库工件中，且往往只有在执行时才暴露。已有的基于智能体的方法通过迭代交互来解决，但关于当前构建状态的信息（已发现的需求、已满足与未解决的前置条件及其依赖）容易散落在交互历史中，缺乏结构化的显式表示。

Method: 提出 Graph2Env：以 DepGraph 为核心，DepGraph 是一种带类型的依赖图，显式刻画仓库执行所需的环境需求、需求间的依赖关系及各自状态。Graph2Env 用 DepGraph 指导环境构建，并借助执行反馈持续精化该图，同时将成功的修复持久化为可重放的构建流程；随后在全新环境中应用这些产物，验证所构建环境是否可复现。

Result: 在取自 RATBench 与 EnvBench 的 200 个 Python 仓库基准上，与静态依赖推断基线 pipreqs、三个专用环境构建系统（Repo2Run、RAT、SetupX）以及两个通用编码智能体（SWE-agent、Claude Code）对比，Graph2Env 达到 81.0% 的环境构建成功率（EBSR）与 59.3% 的环境搭建成功率（ESSR），分别比最强基线高出 9.5 与 9.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将分散的环境需求与构建状态结构化为显式依赖图并配合执行反馈与可重放修复流程，Graph2Env 显著提升了仓库环境构建与搭建的成功率及可复现性，优于静态推断、专用系统与通用编码智能体等基线方法。

Abstract: Coding agents now increasingly rely on execution to validate their solutions, making the construction of reliable execution environments a critical enabling capability. However, repository environment construction is challenging because execution requirements are fragmented across repository artifacts and may only become apparent during execution. Existing agent-based approaches address this problem through iterative interaction, but information about the current construction state, including discovered requirements, satisfied and unresolved prerequisites, and their dependencies, can remain distributed across the interaction history. We present Graph2Env, an agent-based approach centered on DepGraph, a typed dependency graph that explicitly represents the environment requirements needed for repository execution, their dependency relations, and their states. Graph2Env uses DepGraph to guide environment construction and continuously refines it with execution feedback, while persisting successful repairs into a replayable construction procedure. The resulting artifacts are then applied in a fresh environment to verify that the constructed environment can be reproduced. We evaluate Graph2Env on a benchmark of 200 Python repositories drawn from RATBench and EnvBench, against a static dependency-inference baseline (pipreqs), three specialized environment-construction systems (Repo2Run, RAT, and SetupX), and two general-purpose coding agents (SWE-agent and Claude Code). Graph2Env achieves an 81.0% Environment Build Success Rate (EBSR) and a 59.3% Environment Setup Success Rate (ESSR), outperforming the strongest baseline by 9.5 and 9.0 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [99] [Path2Spec: Path-Aware Specification Generation via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33438)
*Dan Huang,Zhensu Sun,Huihui Huang,Jinfeng Jiang,Xiaofei Xie,Yintong Huo,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Path2Spec，一种基于分治与路径推理的LLM形式化规约生成框架，通过提取执行路径生成路径级规约并合并，解决现有方法规约过于宽泛的质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规约对程序验证、理解和维护至关重要，但人工编写成本高、难以规模化。现有LLM自动生成规约方法存在质量缺陷：作者分析发现至少34.6%通过验证的规约未能真正刻画程序的独特行为，而仅衡量验证成功率的指标无法捕捉这一质量问题。其根本原因在于现有方法将程序视为单一整体，产生过于笼统、粗粒度的约束。

Method: 提出Path2Spec分治框架，进行系统化的基于路径的推理：利用LLM从输入程序中提取所有执行路径，为每条路径生成特定规约，再合并为完整规约；对于路径生成困难的复杂程序，采用"先分解再重试"策略，依据逻辑分支递归拆分为子程序，分别生成规约后合并。

Result: 在SG-Bench（120个程序）和SV-COMP（265个程序）两个公开基准上评估，Path2Spec优于最先进基线SpecGen：SG-Bench上87.5%对66.7%，SV-COMP上83.0%对44.2%。人工评估进一步验证其生成的规约质量更高，与代码的语义对齐更精确。

Conclusion: Path2Spec通过路径级分治推理显著提升了LLM生成形式化规约的质量和语义精确性，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Formal specifications are critical for program verification, comprehension, and maintenance. However, manually writing them is costly and difficult to scale. Recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) are promising for automated specification generation, but existing methods suffer from quality issues. We analyze a state-of-the-art approach and find that at least 34.6% of successfully verified specifications actually fail to meaningfully capture the program's distinct behavior, which is a quality issue not captured by metrics that only measure verification success. We further found that a major factor contributing to such hidden quality issues stems from the design of existing methods: these methods treat a program as a single unit, resulting in overly general, coarse-grained constraints. To this end, we introduce Path2Spec, a divide-and-conquer framework that addresses these limitations through systematic path-based reasoning. Path2Spec leverages LLMs to extract all execution paths from an input program, generates path-specific specifications for each, and merges them into a comprehensive overall specification. For complex programs where path-based generation struggles, Path2Spec employs a decompose-then-retry strategy that recursively breaks a program into smaller subprograms based on logical branches, generates specifications for each, and merges them back. We evaluate Path2Spec on two public benchmarks: SG-Bench (120 programs) and SV-COMP (265 programs). Results show that Path2Spec can outperform the state-of-the-art baseline SpecGen: 87.5% versus 66.7% on SG-Bench, and 83.0% versus 44.2% on SV-COMP. Human evaluation further validates that Path2Spec generates higher-quality specifications with precise semantic alignment to the code.

</details>


### [100] [Neuro-Symbolic Indirect-Call Analysis under Opaque Pointers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33547)
*Kaixuan Li,Bozhi Wu,Jian Zhang,Peixin Wang,Ting Su,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Facet 是一种针对不透明指针 LLVM IR 的间接调用分析，通过分别识别结构体字段与恢复其被赋值的函数，再按字段身份关联，重建调度关系，同时产出保持召回率的调用图与精化调用图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接调用解析是 C 调用图构建的核心。MLTA 等可扩展的类型分析依赖 LLVM IR 中的类型信息，将间接调用与结构体字段对应的函数关联。但单一 pointee 类型常无法准确表示指针所指内存，且 LLVM 17 以不透明指针取代 pointee 类型，使字段敏感分析失去匹配键。即便恢复被擦除的类型，仍缺失类型所编码的关键关系：程序到底将哪些函数赋给了该字段。

Method: Facet 识别间接调用加载函数指针时所来自的结构体字段，并单独通过初始化器、存储和聚合拷贝恢复被赋值到该字段的函数，然后依据字段身份将两者关联，无需端到端的值流路径。它针对边添加与边删除采用不同的证据规则对候选调用图变更进行分类，并记录每次精化背后的假设；仅对符号化有界候选中的残余情况交由 LLM 判定。

Result: 在 14 个 C 程序上，Facet 将平均目标集大小从 25.9 降至 5.2，并将观测召回率从 0.79 提升至 0.99。其恢复的字段身份在共同解析位点上与带类型 IR 的一致率为 98.1%。应用于缺陷检测时，精化后的调用图在从 nginx 到 Linux 内核的 C 软件中发现 17 个深层缺陷，其中 3 个潜伏超过十年，12 个已确认。

Conclusion: Facet 是首个在不透明 IR 上重建调度关系的分析，一次分析即可同时得到保持召回率的调用图与精化调用图，并在调用图精度、召回率及实际缺陷检测中展现出显著效果。

Abstract: Resolving indirect calls is central to call-graph construction for C. Scalable type-based analyses such as MLTA use type information in LLVM IR to associate indirect calls with functions assigned to the corresponding structure fields. However, a single pointee type often misrepresents the memory a pointer addresses, and LLVM 17 removed pointee types in favor of opaque pointers. Therefore, field-sensitive analyses lose their matching key. Recovering the erased types restores the matching key but still misses the relation that the type encoded: which functions the program assigns to the field. We present Facet, to our knowledge the first analysis that reconstructs this dispatch relation over opaque IR. Facet identifies the structure field from which an indirect call loads its function pointer. It separately recovers the functions assigned to that field through initializers, stores, and aggregate copies. It then joins the two by field identity, without requiring an end-to-end value-flow path. Facet classifies proposed call-graph changes under distinct evidence rules for edge addition and removal and records the assumption behind each refinement. An LLM decides only the residual cases among symbolically bounded candidates. One analysis yields both a recall-preserving call graph and a refined call graph. On 14 C programs, Facet reduces the mean target-set size from 25.9 to 5.2 and raises observed recall from 0.79 to 0.99. Its recovered field identities agree with typed IR at 98.1% of jointly resolved sites. Applied to bug detection, the refined call graph found 17 deep bugs in C software from nginx to the Linux kernel, three of them latent for over a decade; 12 are confirmed.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond the Prompt: Linking What Developers Ask, Do, and Understand with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33712)
*Yunhan Qiao,Summit Haque,Christopher Hundhausen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一套端到端的 "Say, Do, Understand" 工作流，用于分析开发者与编码智能体交互时"说什么、做什么、事后能解释什么"，并在10名经验开发者使用 GitHub Copilot 的观察性研究中验证，通过任务表现与理解程度的交叉分析得出四种用户画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已能替开发者修改代码，但仅凭提示词、屏幕活动或任务成功率都不足以完整刻画这一协作过程。作者认为需要同时捕捉开发者向智能体输入的内容、协作期间的行为以及事后能否解释过程与解决方案，从而更全面地理解人机协作中的理解与沟通问题。

Method: 提出包含五个阶段（Capture、Prepare、Analyze、Integrate、Interpret）的工作流，并配备三种工具：提示词编码手册、对屏幕录制活动与事件进行编码的方案，以及分别评估"过程解释"和"解决方案解释"的评分量表。随后开展观察性研究，让十名经验丰富的开发者在不熟悉的代码库上使用 GitHub Copilot。

Result: 将任务表现与理解程度交叉后得到四种用户画像。两项指标在八名开发者上一致，但在两人上出现分离：一人通过多数测试却无法解释解决方案，另一人通过很少测试却能很好地解释。在该样本中，最常要求智能体检查其工作的人，自己独立测试所花时间最少。作者强调这些模式仅为描述性，不能推广到样本之外。

Conclusion: 该工作流可用于分析人机协作中的沟通与理解，作者建议计算教育者、行业从业者和研究者采纳并评估该流程，以改进软件工程中的人机交流研究与实践。

Abstract: Coding agents can now change code for developers, who describe goals, supply context, and respond to the agent's work. Yet prompts, screen activity, and task success each tell only part of this story. We present Say, Do, Understand, an end-to-end workflow for analyzing what developers write to an agent, what they do while it works, and what they can explain afterwards. The workflow has five stages (Capture, Prepare, Analyze, Integrate, and Interpret) and three instruments: a prompt codebook, a scheme for coding screen-recorded activities and events, and separate rubrics for explaining the process and the solution. We applied it in an observational study of ten experienced developers who used GitHub Copilot on an unfamiliar codebase. Crossing task performance with understanding produced four personas. The two measures agreed for eight developers but split for two: one passed most tests but could not explain the solution, and another passed few tests but explained it well.
