In this sample, the personas that most often asked the agent to check its work spent the least time testing on their own. These patterns are descriptive and do not generalize beyond the sample. We recommend the workflow to computing educators, industry practitioners, and researchers to adapt and evaluate human--AI communication in software engineering.

</details>


### [102] [RAISE: Reinforcing Access Control Policy Synthesis in LLMs via Symbolic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33796)
*Yingming Zhou,Adarsh Vatsa,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CedarInstruct数据集（首个支持可形式化验证的Cedar策略合成训练与语义评测的数据集）与RAISE两阶段训练方法，其中RAISE-OC通过将验证器信号转化为引导式探索，使Qwen3.5-9B在语义成功率上显著超越前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言访问控制需求翻译为策略需要复杂的权限、约束与例外推理，即便是前沿大模型也常生成违反授权语义的策略。该领域缺乏同时支持训练与语义评测、且带可执行形式化验证的数据资源与训练方法。

Method: 1) 构建CedarInstruct数据集：5,800个场景、44个领域，其中1,408个来自单一合成组织，每个场景配有经验证的目标策略与可执行验证计划。2) 提出RAISE两阶段训练：先做经验证的监督微调（SFT），再进行从验证器信号中学习的强化学习阶段。3) 对比六种消耗逐步丰富验证器信号的RL实例，其中RAISE-OC将失败检查与符号反例转化为引导式探索，并用off-context GRPO学习。4) 使用约5.4K验证场景与LoRA微调。

Result: SFT的成功主要源于让模型表达其已具备的授权逻辑：未训练模型很少写出合法Cedar，但一旦写出往往推理正确。SFT之后，如何使用验证器信息比使用多少信息更重要。六种RL实例中仅RAISE-OC相比SFT有实质性提升。RAISE-OC训练的Qwen3.5-9B在留出场景的语义成功率上分别超过零样本GPT-6 Astra与Claude Opus 5达13.33与16.26个百分点，且训练可迁移至独立构建的CedarBench。

Conclusion: 形式化验证信号的有效利用方式（而非信号数量）是提升策略合成语义正确性的关键；RAISE-OC通过off-context GRPO将验证反馈转化为引导探索，可在小规模数据与LoRA微调下取得超越前沿大模型的效果，并具备跨基准迁移能力。

Abstract: Translating natural-language access-control requirements into policies requires careful reasoning about permissions, constraints, and exceptions, and even frontier LLMs often produce policies that violate the intended authorization semantics. We construct CedarInstruct, to our knowledge the first dataset that supports both training and semantic evaluation for formally verifiable Cedar policy synthesis. It contains 5,800 scenarios across 44 domains and 1,408 representing a single synthetic organization, each with a verified target policy and an executable verification plan. On this data we introduce RAISE, which trains policy synthesizers from formal verification in two stages, verified supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by a reinforcement learning (RL) stage that learns from verifier signal. We find that SFT succeeds largely by letting models express authorization logic they already have, since untrained models rarely write valid Cedar but often reason correctly when they do. After SFT, how the verifier's information is used matters more than how much of it is used. Of six RL instantiations that consume progressively richer verifier signal, only RAISE-OC improves meaningfully on SFT; it turns failed checks and symbolic counterexamples into guided exploration and learns from the result with off-context GRPO. With about 5.4K verified scenarios and LoRA fine-tuning, RAISE-OC trains Qwen3.5-9B to surpass zero-shot GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5 by 13.33 and 16.26 percentage points in semantic success on held-out scenarios, and training transfers to the independently constructed CedarBench.

</details>


### [103] [Identical Runs, Different Results: Benchmarking AI Coding Agents on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33812)
*Eduardo Ariño de la Rubia,Szilard Pafka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过在单一机器学习任务上进行大规模复制实验（584次运行、6个智能体×6个模型端点），揭示编码智能体的运行间方差常超过智能体间差异，主张应以"智能体-模型配对"为单位、多次尝试、同时报告合规性与质量进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一编码智能体重复运行同一任务会得到不同的基准分数，但这一方差对实际在自己任务上使用智能体的团队意味着什么尚不清楚。作者希望量化这种运行间变异对智能体比较与交付结果的影响。

Method: 在一个航空延误XGBoost分类器训练代码改进任务上做密集复制：共584次运行，比较6个智能体与6个开放权重模型端点；对6个智能体-模型配对在固定设置下各重复52次，并对其中3个用同家族更大模型重复；用从未见过的留出集评分结果。

Result: 同一配对的相同运行之间的差异大于不同配对之间的差异，少量运行无法可靠排序；要区分观察到的智能体差异需每个配对数十到上百次运行。更大模型得分明显更高，但幅度小于一个运行间标准差，且某智能体上的差距比其他智能体大一倍以上。不到二十分之一的运行违反任务数据规则，但这些运行分数最高。先剔除违规运行并在少量尝试中保留最佳合规结果能可靠提升交付模型。在较晚年份航班上，交付模型只保留了相对起始代码增益的三分之一。按标价，同一模型上两个智能体的成本相差二十倍以上，主要来自提示缓存。

Conclusion: 应把智能体和模型作为"配对"来评估、跨多次尝试评估，并在报告质量的同时报告合规性。

Abstract: Repeated runs of the same coding agent are known to give different benchmark scores. We ask what that variation means for a team running an agent on its own task, by intensive replication on one machine-learning task: an agent improves the training code of an XGBoost classifier for airline delays, and a holdout it never sees scores the result. Across 584 runs, we compare six agents on six open-weight model endpoints, run six agent-model pairings 52 times each under fixed settings, and repeat three of them on a larger model from the same family. Identical runs of one pairing varied more than the pairings differed from one another, so comparisons of a few runs ranked them unreliably; resolving the agent differences we observed would take tens to more than a hundred runs of each. Runs on the larger model scored clearly higher, but by less than one run-to-run standard deviation, and the gap was more than twice as large with one agent as with the others. Fewer than one run in twenty broke the task's data rules, but those runs held the highest scores. Rejecting those runs first and keeping the best compliant result among a few attempts reliably improved the delivered model, even though a few runs could not rank the agents. On flights from a later year, the delivered models kept only a third of their gain over the starting code. At list prices, cost differed more than twentyfold between two agents on the same model, mostly through the prompt cache. Agents and models should be evaluated as pairings, over repeated attempts, with compliance reported beside quality. Data, code and every delivered program: https://github.com/earino/identical-runs-different-results

</details>


### [104] [Counterfactual Rollout Replay: Forkable Environments as Free Process Rewards for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33875)
*Yuanhao Li,Hongbo Wang,Xuhong Chen,Yiming Cao,Xunzhu Tang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出反事实回放重放（CRR）：利用可分叉环境获取步级回报对比，替代结果型强化学习中的优势估计，无需人工过程标签或过程奖励模型，在SWE-bench系列上提升14B策略的pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果型强化学习只给SWE智能体一个终端成败信号，缺少对中间决策的直接指导。为获得步级信用分配，通常需要人工过程标注或训练过程奖励模型，成本高。作者希望在不增加这类监督成本的前提下，利用可恢复状态的可执行环境得到步级优势信号。

Method: CRR在训练时进行：选取少量决策点，恢复该状态，采样一个替代动作，并在当前策略下将分支前向回放；保留真实训练轨迹，仅把选中步骤的优势替换为“真实终端回报与采样反事实回报之差”。该方法不需要人工过程标签，也不训练过程奖励模型；所谓“free”仅指监督成本，不含回放计算开销。

Result: 使用14B策略，CRR在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Live和SWE-rebench上提升pass@1，并可与过程奖励方法与轨迹搜索方法结合。在SWE-bench Verified上，同一硬件等墙钟时间对比为41.7%对扩展版outcome-only GRPO的36.7%，提升5.0个百分点（已计入分叉开销）。

Conclusion: CRR为结果型RL提供了一种廉价的过程监督替代方案，在状态可可靠恢复的环境中有效。局限性在于随机性延续以及回放代价高昂或不完美时效果受限。

Abstract: Outcome-only reinforcement learning gives software engineering (SWE) agents a terminal success signal but little direct guidance about intermediate decisions. We introduce Counterfactual Rollout Replay (CRR), a training-time procedure that uses forkable executable environments to obtain step-level return contrasts. CRR selects a small set of decision points, restores each state, samples an alternative action, and rolls the branch forward under the policy. It retains the realised training trajectory and replaces the advantage at selected steps with the difference between its terminal return and the sampled counterfactual return. The method needs no human process labels or learned process reward model; free refers to those supervision costs, not replay compute. With a 14B policy, CRR improves pass@1 on SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Live, and SWE-rebench, and combines with process-reward and trajectory-search methods. On SWE-bench Verified, an equal-wall-clock comparison on the same hardware yields 41.7% versus 36.7% for extended outcome-only GRPO, a 5.0-point gain with fork overhead included. These results apply to environments with affordable, reliable state restoration; stochastic continuations and expensive or imperfect replay remain limitations.

</details>


### [105] [Green AI: Cost of LLM-Based Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33918)
*Negar Alizadeh,Nishant Saurabh,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM代码补全中准确率与能耗的权衡，评估25个开源权重模型在RepoBench（仓库级下一行补全，变化上下文长度）与McEval（Python/Java/Rust的FIM补全）上的表现，发现能耗主因取决于任务结构：RepoBench由输入上下文长度及其与模型规模的交互主导，McEval由输出生成及其与激活参数量的交互主导；输出生成每token能耗高于提示处理。小模型与量化模型常达到帕累托最优，能以显著更低能耗提供接近大FP16模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重LLM因隐私问题被越来越多用于本地部署的代码补全系统，但推理能耗在大上下文负载与多编程语言场景下研究不足，准确率与能耗的权衡缺乏系统分析。

Method: 在25个开源权重LLM上评估两类负载：RepoBench的仓库级下一行补全（不同上下文长度）与McEval的Python/Java/Rust的FIM补全；用相关分析与聚类稳健线性回归，分析输入token、输出token、模型规模及其交互对能耗的影响。

Result: 能耗主导因素强依赖任务结构：RepoBench中主要是输入上下文长度及其与模型规模的交互；McEval中主要是输出生成及与激活参数量的交互。输出生成每token比提示处理更耗能。小模型与高度量化模型常达到帕累托最优，准确率可比肩大FP16模型而能耗大幅降低；增大模型或上下文并不必然带来成比例的补全质量提升，量化可在有限精度损失下显著提升能效。

Conclusion: 研究支持面向可持续AI辅助软件开发的能耗感知部署策略，例如优先采用小规模或量化模型、按任务结构优化上下文与输出策略。

Abstract: Code completion is one of the most widely used applications of large language models (LLMs) in software development. Open-weight LLMs are increasingly adopted for locally deployed code completion systems, partly due to privacy concerns. Despite advances in LLM accuracy, the energy cost of inference remains underexplored, particularly under large-context workloads and across programming languages. This study investigates the trade-off between accuracy and energy consumption in LLM-based code completion and how workload characteristics, context size, and model scale influence inference energy usage. We evaluate 25 open-weight LLMs on two workloads: repository-level next-line completion with varying context sizes on RepoBench, and fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code completion across Python, Java, and Rust on McEval. We analyze the influence of input tokens, output tokens, model size, and their interactions on energy consumption using correlation analysis and cluster-robust linear regression. Our findings show that the dominant drivers of energy consumption depend strongly on task structure. In RepoBench, energy consumption is primarily influenced by input context size and its interaction with model scale, whereas in McEval, output generation and its interaction with active parameter count dominate. Output generation is more energy-intensive per token than prompt processing. Across both benchmarks, smaller and heavily quantized models frequently achieve Pareto-optimal trade-offs, often providing accuracy comparable to larger FP16 models while consuming substantially less energy. Increasing model size or context length does not necessarily lead to proportionally better completion quality, while quantization can substantially improve energy efficiency with limited accuracy degradation. These findings support more energy-aware deployment strategies for sustainable AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [106] [Maat: Independent Deterministic Contract-Based Governance for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34017)
*Uliana Elina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Maat——一个不含语言模型、基于版本化工作流契约（anchor）的确定性多智能体交接验证层，并通过六域受控实验与后续审计重新评估其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统中，单个智能体的错误会被下游当作上下文接受并沿工作流传播，形成特有的可靠性问题。现有的防护措施多依赖学习型或 LLM 评判器，其判决本身具有概率性；作者因此追问：能否用确定性层来阻断"契约可检出"的交接缺陷。

Method: 提出 Maat 运行时治理层，按版本化工作流契约（anchor）校验智能体间的交接，验证与打分路径中不含任何语言模型。在六个受控领域工作流（6–15 个智能体、522 次试验）中评估，注入数据级缺陷，并使用确定性的七项检查评分表。后续进行发表后审计：核对成对试验中"归因于已验证缺陷"的完成或中止，并人工复核全部 94 次治理组中止。

Result: 版本 1 声称六个工作流全部提升（2.9%–26.5%）。发表后审计发现三个基准评分器把任何"提前中止"都算作已阻止缺陷。在治理运行完成或中止可归因于已验证缺陷的成对试验中，评分表变化为 +7.7% 至 +29.1%（五个工作流），软件开发域持平；当可归因中止发生较早时，模型调用成本下降 17%–53%。人工复核 94 次治理组中止发现 35 次误报（37%），根源是验证器缺陷而非模型行为；若把这些中止计为失败工作，治理组在六个工作流中的四个低于未治理组。

Conclusion: 结果支持对"契约可表达"的缺陷采用确定性交接验证，并表明验证器配置与中止归因本身必须被测试；但该研究不构成对普遍正确性、幻觉检测或模型无关有效性的证明。

Abstract: Large-language-model multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) introduce a characteristic reliability problem: an error produced by one agent can be accepted as context by downstream agents and propagate across the workflow. Many proposed safeguards rely on learned or LLM-based judges whose verdicts are themselves probabilistic; we ask whether a deterministic layer can instead stop contract-detectable handoff defects. We present Maat, a runtime governance layer that validates agent-to-agent handoffs against a versioned workflow contract, or anchor, with no language model in the validation or scoring path. We evaluate it in six controlled domain workflows (6-15 agents, 522 trials) with injected data-level defects and a deterministic seven-check rubric. Version 1 reported gains in all six workflows (2.9-26.5%). A post-publication audit found that three benchmark scorers credited any early halt as a prevented defect. On paired trials where the governed run completed or halted on a finding attributable to a verified defect, the rubric score changes by +7.7% to +29.1% in five workflows and is flat in software development; model-call cost falls 17-53% where attributable halts occur early. A hand review of all 94 governed-arm halts found 35 false alarms (37%), caused by validator defects rather than model behaviour; counting those halts as failed work, the governed arm scores below the ungoverned arm in four of six workflows. The results support deterministic handoff validation for contract-expressible defects and show that validator configuration and halt attribution must themselves be tested; they do not establish universal correctness, hallucination detection, or model-independent effectiveness.

</details>


### [107] [JET: Judge-Guided Evolution at Test Time for Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34126)
*Yao Long Teng,Jiayi Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 JET（Judge-Guided Evolution at Test Time），在源域轨迹上进化出可执行的 judge，然后冻结并迁移到目标域，用于指导智能体程序在测试时的进化，无需访问目标评测器或更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的可执行程序决定其工具使用、观测处理与失败应对方式，在测试时进化该程序有助于适应新任务。但真实奖励不可用时，难以判断哪些程序改动应被保留；执行轨迹虽能反映行为，却需要一个在任务与候选程序变化时仍保持有效的评判者。

Method: JET 先在带标注的源域轨迹上进化一个可执行的 judge，随后将其冻结并迁移到目标域来引导程序进化。该 judge 提供分数与诊断性反馈，不依赖目标域评测器，也不更新模型权重。作者还设计了 PushT 上的精确 judge 对照实验，由 judge 从观测中重建评分规则。

Result: 在未见过的 WebShop 任务上，冷启动（从未进化程序出发）时 JET 的平均奖励比固定评分标准引导高约 13%，热启动时高 4%，冷启动精确成功率相对提升 36%。PushT 精确 judge 对照显示，在无 judge 误差时，程序搜索本身成为瓶颈。分析还发现进化代码中存在有用的奖励预测逻辑，且仅靠更好的最终选择无法解释性能提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持在保持评测器不变的迁移场景下，采用可执行 judge 迁移来支持智能体程序的自适应进化。

Abstract: An agent's executable program governs how it uses tools, processes observations, and responds to failures. Evolving this program at test time can help adaptation, but deciding which changes to retain is difficult when true rewards are unavailable. Execution traces provide evidence of agent behavior, yet interpreting that evidence requires a judge that remains useful as tasks and candidate programs change. We introduce Judge-Guided Evolution at Test Time (JET), which evolves an executable judge on labeled source trajectories, then freezes and transfers it to guide target-side program evolution. The judge supplies scores and diagnostic feedback without target evaluator access or model-weight updates. On unseen WebShop tasks, JET achieves approximately 13% higher mean reward than fixed-rubric guidance when evolution begins from an unevolved program (cold start) and 4% higher when it begins from one already optimized on source tasks (warm start), with a 36% relative improvement in cold-start exact success. An exact-judge control on PushT, where the judge reconstructs the scoring rule from observations, shows that without judge error, program search becomes the bottleneck. Analyses identify useful reward-prediction logic in the evolved code and show that better final selection alone cannot explain the gains. These results support executable judge transfer for program adaptation under evaluator-preserving task shifts.

</details>


### [108] [The Construction of an Empirical Dataset of Incomplete Software Changes from Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34317)
*Savira Ramadhanty,Profir-Petru Pârţachi,Yoshiya Ishida,Takashi Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于开源软件真实不完整变更（由缺陷及其修复挖掘而来）的数据集，用以更真实地评估共变更规则提取方法，并据此重新评估了LCExtractor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发中修改一个组件可能影响多个相关组件，45.7%的开发者会遗漏相关变更。已有共变更规则提取方法多基于人工构造的不完整变更进行评估，可能无法代表真实世界数据。

Method: 通过问题跟踪平台中诱导缺陷及其修复信息，从开源软件集合中挖掘不完整变更，构建真实数据集；分析不完整变更的特征；在该数据集上重新评估现有共变更规则提取方法LCExtractor。

Result: 89.4%的遗漏变更涉及五个或更少的文件；重新评估确定了LCExtractor的最优排序准则以及所使用提交数量的影响。

Conclusion: 基于真实不完整变更的数据集能够更代表性地评估共变更规则提取方法，并为LCExtractor的配置（排序准则与提交数量）提供指导。

Abstract: During software development, a modification to a software component may propagate across the system, requiring precise identification and correct revision of all affected components. This is a complex task, and developers often (45.7%) miss related changes. To address this, several methods have been developed to extract co-change rules from files that are frequently changed together in the revision history. However, previous research evaluated the methods using artificially created incomplete changes, which may not be representative of real-world data. To solve this problem, we construct a dataset by mining incomplete changes from a collection of open-source software, using information about induced bugs and their respective fixes from an issue tracking platform. We also analyze the characteristics of incomplete changes using this constructed dataset and found that 89.4% of missed changes involved five or fewer files. Finally, we re-evaluate LCExtractor, an existing co-change rule extraction method, on our constructed dataset, and we identify the optimal sorting criterion and the impact of the number of used commits.

</details>


### [109] [The Last Mile Is the File: OfficeEditBench for Preservation-Aware Office Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34469)
*Zhiwen Wu,Chengxu Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出OfficeEditBench基准，用于评估Office文件"变更范围维护"能力，揭示高交付率但零完整合约满足的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型Office编辑同时带来两项义务：传播所有必要更新，且不触碰受保护状态。更新过少导致依赖不一致，更新过多则改动用户未授权内容，现有评估难以刻画这一矛盾。

Method: 构建OfficeEditBench，含170项任务，覆盖电子表格、演示文稿与文档。任务合约规定必需更新、受保护状态、原生结构与交互要求；并分析来自WorkBuddy、Doubao、Codex的510个归档任务-系统结果，区分文件交付、目标完成与验证器定义的接受度。

Result: 硬性包有效交付率达92%—100%，但无一选定输出满足完整合约。案例分析显示局部正确不足：更新后的值丢失生成公式、修订规则未传导至相关结论、新截止日期遗漏保留的前置条件。

Conclusion: 将工件级检查与Office文件的可维护性相联系，分析维护失败并区分冻结的自动判定与人类可接受性，为"完成必要修改同时保留既有逻辑与范围"提供测试平台。

Abstract: A small Office edit creates two obligations: propagate every required update and leave protected state untouched. Updating too little leaves dependencies inconsistent; updating too much changes content the user did not authorize. We introduce OfficeEditBench, a 170-task benchmark for change-scoped maintenance of spreadsheets, presentations, and documents. Task contracts specify required updates, protected state, native structures, and applicable interaction requirements. Across 510 archived task-system outcomes from WorkBuddy, Doubao, and Codex, we distinguish file delivery, target completion, and verifier-defined acceptance. Hard package-valid delivery ranges from 92% to 100%, yet no selected output satisfies the complete contract. Case analysis highlights why local correctness is insufficient: an updated value can lose its generating formula, a revised rule can fail to reach related conclusions, and a new deadline can omit a retained prerequisite. These mechanisms connect artifact-level checks to the continued maintainability of Office files. We analyze maintenance failures while distinguishing frozen automatic verdicts from human acceptability. OfficeEditBench provides a testbed for completing required changes while preserving the logic and scope of existing work.

</details>


### [110] [From Noisy Telemetry to Actionable Warnings: GPU Failure Prediction in Industrial Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34473)
*Yongqian Sun,Run Zhu,Wenwei Gu,Mengyao Li,Shenglin Zhang,Guanjin Wang,Yang Zhang,Xin Wu,Linlin Han,Feng Wang,Xiaozhou Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Falcon：面向生产GPU集群的故障特异性预警框架，通过缺失感知与同行相对特征、故障特异学习器选择及事件策略，实现最高F1与17-35小时中位提前预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群是AI服务的关键基础设施，但生产环境中准确且可操作的GPU故障预测仍是难题。基于字节跳动GPU集群的工单关联遥测数据，识别出三大障碍：工作负载混杂的遥测、异构的故障前兆，以及窗口级预测与可操作告警之间的差距。

Method: 提出Falcon故障特异性预警框架，包含三部分：缺失感知的时序特征与同行相对特征、故障特异的学习器选择，以及基于阈值、持续性和冷却时间的事件告警策略。

Result: 在测试集上，Falcon在四个基线中取得最高F1，最佳故障类型F1达70.6%；检出案例的中位提前时间为17.34-35.57小时。生产部署中，Falcon被校准为高精度告警以反映误报成本。

Conclusion: 故障特异性建模能够从嘈杂的生产GPU遥测中提升早期预警效果。

Abstract: GPU clusters are critical infrastructure for AI services, but accurate and actionable GPU failure prediction remains a problem in production settings. We study ticket-linked telemetry from a ByteDance GPU cluster and identify three obstacles: workload-confounded telemetry, heterogeneous fault precursors, and the gap between window-level predictions and actionable alerts. These findings motivate Falcon, a fault-specific warning framework combining missingness-aware temporal and peer-relative features, fault-specific learner selection, and an event policy based on thresholding, persistence, and cooldown. On the test set, Falcon achieves the highest F1 among four baselines and reaches 70.6% F1 on the best-performing fault type. Detected cases provide median lead times of 17.34-35.57 hours. We further report a production deployment, where Falcon is calibrated toward high-precision alerts to reflect false-positive costs. Together, these results show that fault-specific modeling improves early warning from noisy production GPU telemetry.

</details>


### [111] [CLAD: Constrained Abstract Domain for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34628)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出约束拉格朗日抽象域CLAD，用于在凸约束组合定义的输入区域上对神经网络进行紧致验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NNV抽象域（如Lp范数球）对实际输入区域描述过于受限，导致过近似松弛，产生验证失败或虚假反例。

Method: CLAD将各约束以拉格朗日乘子松弛进目标函数，用投影原始-对偶方法求解max-min问题，交替执行输入上的投影梯度步与乘子更新，支持任意带次梯度的凸约束。

Result: 在1,944个实例上评估：标准无约束Linf属性下与GCPCROWN表现相当且运行时间相近；带约束属性下，L2-ball属性多验证60%实例，总计多22%。

Conclusion: CLAD能在凸约束组合输入区域上计算更紧致的可靠过近似，显著提升约束属性验证效率。

Abstract: Neural network verification (NNV) formally verifies that a network satisfies a specified property for all inputs within a defined region. Modern NNV tools employ abstract domains to compute a sound over-approximation of the network's behavior from the given input region, thus the tightness of these abstractions essentially determines efficiency. A long line of increasingly precise domains has been developed, but they all describe the valid input region in the same restrictive way, e.g., an Lp-norm ball. A practical input region is rarely a simple Lp ball, but rather a combination Lp ball with additional constraints. Verifying a network over such a region with existing abstraction produces a loose over-approximation, which results in either failing to verify a property or spurious counterexamples. We introduce Constrained Lagrangian Abstract Domain (CLAD), a new abstract domain that computes a sound over-approximation of neural networks over input regions defined by a combination of convex constraints. CLAD propagates these constraints and tightens bounds over the true feasible region. However, bounding a neuron over the intersection of these constraints has no closed-form solution, so CLAD relaxes each constraint into the objective with a Lagrange multiplier and solves the resulting max-min problem with a projected primal-dual method, alternating a projected gradient step on the input with a multiplier update. CLAD supports any convex constraint with a subgradient, e.g., from automatic differentiation. We evaluate CLAD on 1,944 instances across four convolutional networks with motion-blur structured perturbations with halfspace or L2-ball constraints. On standard unconstrained Linf property, CLAD verifies as many instances as GCPCROWN at a similar runtime. On constrained properties, CLAD verifies 60\% more instances than GCPCROWN on L2-ball properties, and 22% more in total.

</details>


### [112] [Codoku: Renewable Program-Reasoning Challenges for Frontier Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34661)
*Cong Li,Hao Sun,Zenan Li,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Codoku 是一个可再生的程序推理基准：求解者在部分程序中填写带类型的空格，以满足全局静态与动态约束，从而抵抗代码执行与数据污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序推理基准假设模型只能通过推理预测程序行为，但编码智能体可直接执行程序得到答案，且固定题目集易被污染且更新成本高，无法跟上智能体能力提升的速度。

Method: 提出 Codoku，通过"语义具化"从零合成谜题：部分程序无法执行，有效填法在指数级相互依赖的选择空间中极为稀疏，迫使求解者进行真正的程序推理；每题附带可解性见证，可按需生成复杂度可控的新题。用固定预算、可自由使用工具的编码智能体评测五个前沿模型、共 300 道题。

Result: 小型谜题已足以挑战开放权重模型，而即便是专有模型在大型谜题上也只能解出约一半。

Conclusion: Codoku 为程序推理提供了一个可再生测试平台，能够持续跟上快速进步的编码智能体。

Abstract: Existing program-reasoning benchmarks ask large language models to predict a program's behavior on a given input. Coding agents break two assumptions on which these benchmarks rest: an agent can recover the answer by executing the program instead of reasoning about it, and fixed task sets drawn from existing programs are increasingly exposed to contamination, yet costly to renew. We introduce Codoku (code sudoku), a renewable benchmark in which a solver fills typed cells in a partial program to satisfy global static and dynamic constraints, such as a prescribed control-flow graph and execution path. Because a partial program cannot be executed and valid fillings are sparse in an exponentially large space of interdependent choices, neither tool use nor enumeration can substitute for program reasoning. Puzzles are synthesized from scratch via semantic reification, so fresh puzzles of controllable complexity can be generated on demand, each with a witness that guarantees solvability. We evaluate five frontier models on 300 puzzles through a coding agent free to use any tool within a fixed budget. Small puzzles already challenge open-weight models, whereas even proprietary models solve only about half of the large ones. Codoku thus offers a renewable testbed for program reasoning that can keep pace with rapidly improving coding agents. GitHub: https://github.com/connglli/Codoku.

</details>


### [113] [When Ambiguity Meets Atypicality: Dual-Perspective Test Input Prioritization for DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34703)
*Haoran Li,Shihai Wang,Bin Liu,Jialuo Chen,Wenjing Zhu,Yu Liu,Tengfei Shi,Shudi Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DuFP，一种基于KNN密度的DNN测试输入优先级排序方法，联合考虑类间（决策模糊性）与类内（分布非典型性）双视角，构建混合不确定性得分，在图像与文本、干净/损坏/对抗场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN存在脆弱性，其测试中的测试输入优先级排序对于早期故障检测和降低标注成本至关重要。但准确识别导致失败的输入仍有挑战：决策模糊性（类间竞争）与分布非典型性（类内典型性）是两种常用视角，单独使用任一视角都会带来盲区。

Method: DuFP基于类条件密度估计构建优先级排序框架。依据估计结果，用模糊性得分刻画预测正确性中的决策模糊性，用非典型性得分量化分布非典型性，再将两者整合为混合不确定性得分以指导最终排序。该方法为KNN密度驱动。

Result: 在图像和文本数据集上，于干净、损坏与对抗三种场景下开展优先级排序与选择任务评估，DuFP均有效且高效地优先选出引发故障的输入，并超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 联合类间与类内双视角可弥补单一视角的盲区，DuFP通过密度估计融合模糊性与非典型性，提升DNN测试输入优先级排序的准确性与效率。

Abstract: While Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable progress in cutting-edge domains, their inherent brittleness has become a growing concern. To ensure the reliability and safety of DNN-enabled software, DNN testing has emerged as an indispensable practice. Within this context, test input prioritization is essential for early fault detection and reducing labeling costs. However, it remains challenging to accurately identify failure-inducing inputs. Although decision ambiguity and distributional atypicality are two widely adopted perspectives for characterizing inter-class competition and intra-class typicality respectively, relying on either perspective in isolation inevitably introduces blind spots. In this paper, we propose DuFP (Dual perspective Feature space Prioritization), a KNN density-based test input prioritization approach for DNNs that jointly incorporates both inter-class and intra-class perspectives. The prioritization framework of DuFP is built upon class-conditional density estimation. Based on the estimation results, prediction correctness is characterized by an ambiguity score and an atypicality score, with the former reflecting decision ambiguity and the latter quantifying distributional atypicality. A hybrid uncertainty score is then constructed by integrating both scores to guide the final prioritization. We evaluate DuFP on prioritization and selection tasks across image and text datasets under clean, corrupted, and adversarial scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that DuFP effectively and efficiently prioritizes fault-inducing inputs and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [114] [Multi-SWT-Bench: A Multilingual Benchmark for Reproduction Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34752)
*Kazuki Kusama,Sota Nakashima,Haruka Tokumasu,Masanari Kondo,Lingming Zhang,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出多语言基准 MULTI-SWT-BENCH（1963个实例、8种语言），对4种代表性方法进行实证研究，揭示复现测试生成存在系统性的语言差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复现测试生成基准仅针对单一编程语言构建，无法在不同编程生态间进行统一评估，缺乏跨语言泛化能力的认识。

Method: 构建涵盖 Python、Java、TypeScript、JavaScript、Go、Rust、C、C++ 八种语言的基准 MULTI-SWT-BENCH（1963 个实例）；使用四种代表性方法（MSWE-agent、MOpenHands、Codex、Claude Code）对当前最先进 LLM 进行实证研究，并开展跨语言失败分析。

Result: 发现系统性语言差距：所有方法与 LLM 在 Python 上的成功率均高于全语言总体成功率，C++ 成功率尤其偏低；失败分析识别出语言特定挑战（如仓库测试惯例）与跨语言挑战（如推断隐式环境配置、迭代修订中保持目标行为）。

Conclusion: 多语言评估至关重要；研究结果为开发可跨软件生态泛化、并可靠捕捉问题特定行为的复现测试生成方法提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Reproduction test generation translates a natural-language issue description into executable tests that fail on the original code and pass after the issue is resolved, providing executable evidence for verifying candidate patches. Existing benchmarks are constructed for individual programming languages, preventing a unified evaluation across diverse programming ecosystems. To address this limitation, we introduce MULTI-SWT-BENCH, a multilingual benchmark for reproduction test generation consisting of 1,963 instances across eight programming languages: Python, Java, TypeScript, JavaScript, Go, Rust, C, and C++. Using this benchmark, we conduct an empirical study of state-of-the-art LLMs with four representative methods (MSWE-agent, MOpenHands, Codex, and Claude Code) and perform a failure analysis across programming languages. Our evaluation reveals a systematic language gap. Across every evaluated method and LLM, the success rate on Python exceeds the aggregate success rate across all languages, while C++ exhibits particularly low success rates. Our failure analysis identifies both language-specific challenges arising from repository testing conventions and cross-language challenges in inferring implicit setup requirements and preserving the target behavior through iterative revisions. These findings demonstrate the importance of multilingual evaluation and provide actionable directions for developing reproduction test generation methods that generalize across software ecosystems and reliably capture issue-specific behavior.

</details>


### [115] [TraceLib: System-Call Bitmap Feedback Mechanism for Language-Agnostic Web Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34778)
*I Putu Arya Dharmaadi,Elias Athanasopoulos,Fatih Turkmen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TraceLib，一种基于系统调用的语言无关反馈机制，用于 Web 模糊测试。它观察系统调用转移并生成 65,536 位 AFL 风格位图，无需应用级插桩。集成到 WebFuzz 后在 16 个 Web 应用上评估，投影版 TraceLib 在全部 8 个 PHP 应用上超过黑盒，在 4 个上超过 Native，追踪开销约每请求 1 毫秒延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖反馈是模糊测试的重要指导来源，但传统上需要依赖特定语言和运行时的应用级插桩。由于现代 Web 应用跨多语言、多运行时，这种应用级插桩实现和维护成本高昂。因此作者希望提出一种与语言无关、运行时独立的系统调用反馈机制，使任意 Web 模糊器无需依赖应用代码覆盖即可获得有效反馈。

Method: 提出 TraceLib：观察系统调用转移，选取部分转移并用有界参数哈希进行增强，转换为 65,536 位的 AFL 风格位图。Web 模糊器据此判断是否保留能增加未见位图位置的请求。将 TraceLib 集成到 WebFuzz，并在五种反馈模式下评估：两个 TraceLib 变体（覆盖所有被追踪系统调用、以及投影到受监控文件路径和识别出的 SQL 缓冲区）、源自 Xiao 等人的 N-gram 比较器、WebFuzz 原生灰盒反馈、无反馈的黑盒模糊测试。

Result: 在 16 个被测 Web 应用上评估（8 个 PHP 应用，以及覆盖 Node.js、Ruby、Java、Go、Python 的 8 个其他应用以验证平台可移植性）。结果显示，投影版 TraceLib 在全部 8 个 PHP 应用上超过黑盒，并在 Joomla、Drupal、PrestaShop 和 Bagisto 四个应用上超过 Native。在相同重放请求负载下，追踪器每请求约增加 1 毫秒的服务器端延迟。

Conclusion: 紧凑的系统调用反馈可以作为覆盖指导的有效、运行时无关的替代方案，验证了语言无关 Web 模糊测试反馈机制的可行性。

Abstract: Coverage feedback is an important source of guidance for fuzzing. However, obtaining such feedback normally requires application-level instrumentation that is specific to the language and runtime of the application. Given that modern web applications span multiple languages and runtimes, this application-level instrumentation is costly to implement and maintain. Therefore, we present TraceLib, a system-call feedback mechanism for enabling language-agnostic web fuzzing. Our proposed approach observes the transitions of system calls, enriches selected transitions with bounded argument hashes, and converts them into a 65,536-position AFL-like bitmap. By using the generated bitmap, any web fuzzer can decide whether to retain requests that add previously unseen bitmap positions without consulting application code coverage.
  We integrate TraceLib into WebFuzz and evaluate it under five WebFuzz feedback modes: the two proposed TraceLib variants (one over every traced system call and one projected onto monitored file paths and recognized SQL buffers), the N-gram comparator adapted from Xiao et al. representing the most recent work to our knowledge, WebFuzz's Native grey-box feedback, and black-box fuzzing without feedback. We evaluate TraceLib on sixteen web applications under test (WUTs): eight PHP applications and eight further applications spanning Node.js, Ruby, Java, Go, and Python to demonstrate platform portability. The results show that our proposed TraceLib projected exceeds black-box on all eight PHP WUTs while exceeding Native on four: Joomla, Drupal, PrestaShop, and Bagisto. In addition, measured on an identical replayed request workload, the tracer costs approximately one millisecond of server-side latency per request. These results indicate that compact system-call feedback is a useful runtime-independent proxy for coverage guidance.

</details>


### [116] [Certified Compilation in the TELEPERM XS Nuclear Safety I&C Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34871)
*Alexandre Berard,Richard B. Kreckel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析民用核电站安全仪控系统(TXS平台)软件生产工具链中的验证作用，重点讨论形式化编译流程及CompCert认证编译器对产品安全案例的贡献，并指出其生成代码在某类简单重复模式下运行时性能欠佳，通过形式化方法在改进编译器性能的同时缩小可信计算基(TCB)，从而强化而非仅维持安全案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民用核电站的大型安全仪控系统对软件可靠性与安全性要求极高，其安全案例需要严格的验证支撑。Framatome的TELEPERM XS数字I&C平台需覆盖安全停堆与限制控制等应用。在实际使用CompCert认证编译器时发现其在某类简单但反复出现的代码生成模式下运行时性能不理想，因而需要在不削弱安全性的前提下解决该问题。

Method: 阐述验证在软件生产工具链各阶段中的作用，聚焦形式化编译流程；分析CompCert认证编译器对产品安全案例的贡献；对编译器生成的目标代码进行审查，定位性能欠佳的简单重复代码模式；运用形式化方法在编译器层面处理该问题，并同步缩小可信计算基(TCB)。

Result: 发现Certified编译器生成的某类简单且反复出现的代码模式运行时性能不佳；通过形式化方法可在改进编译器性能的同时缩小TCB。

Conclusion: 形式化方法不仅能够修复编译器性能问题，还能通过缩小可信计算基来强化核安全仪控产品的安全案例，实现安全性与性能的双重收益，而非仅仅维持原有安全水平。

Abstract: The large safety instrumentation & control (I&C) systems in civil nuclear power plants (NPPs) are mainly safe-shutdown systems (reactor protection) or limitation and control systems. Framatome's established TELEPERM XS (TXS Core) product family is a digital I&C system platform to cover all these applications. We illustrate the role of verification in the different stages of the software production toolchain, focus on the formal compilation process, and discuss the contribution of the CompCert certified compiler to the safety case of the product. Scrutinizing the object code produced by this compiler has exhibited suboptimal run-time performance in a certain simple but recurring generated code pattern. We explain how formal methods allow us to address this issue in the compiler while simultaneously reducing its trusted computing base (TCB), thereby strengthening the safety case rather than merely preserving it.

</details>


### [117] [Verifying Graceful Degradation in a Distributed Malware-Detection System with SPIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34873)
*Andrei Aldea,Dumitru-Bogdan Prelipcean*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文用Promela对Bitdefender端点恶意软件检测流水线建模，经SPIN穷举验证六个LTL安全与活性性质，证明优雅降级机制不会产生误报、违约，且无死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端点恶意软件检测是分布式架构：端点代理采集特征、发送到远程服务器分析、再本地执行判决。由于端点会直接执行远程返回的判决，其周边机制绝不能在服务器瞬时故障时转化为错误动作。然而这类失败处理逻辑很少受到直接的形式化关注，缺乏强正确性保证。

Method: 从Bitdefender生产架构抽象出端点决策流水线的Promela形式模型，刻画其优雅降级回退链：主分析服务器超时后回退到旧协议服务器，再失败则回退到缩减签名的本地扫描，之后才执行判决。在假设检测签名可靠的前提下，用线性时序逻辑（LTL）规定六个安全与活性性质，并用SPIN模型检测器进行穷举验证，同时检查性质非空成立，并报告状态空间随并发扫描与端点数量的增长情况。

Result: 验证证明：回退机制从不导致误报（不对良性文件执行处置动作）；即使在超时响应迟到的情况下，每次扫描也恰好只确定一个判决；检测强度仅按显式且有序的方式被削弱；且总能终止于某个执行决策，因此流水线无死锁。所有性质均非空成立，并给出了随并发扫描和端点规模的状态空间增长情况。

Conclusion: 该工作表明模型检测可为生产安全系统的失败处理逻辑提供强正确性保证，而这一层此前很少获得直接的形式化关注。

Abstract: Modern endpoint malware detection is distributed: a lightweight agent on each endpoint collects features from a scanned file or process, sends them to a remote server for analysis, and then enforces the returned verdict locally by blocking, quarantining, or disinfecting. Because the endpoint acts on the verdict, the distributed machinery surrounding detection must never turn a transient server failure into a wrong action. We present a formal model, in Promela, of the endpoint decision pipeline of such a system, abstracted from a production architecture at Bitdefender. The model captures the system's graceful-degradation fallback chain: when the primary analysis server times out, the endpoint falls back to an older legacy-protocol server, and failing that to a reduced-signature local scan, before enforcing a verdict. Assuming detection signatures are sound, we specify six safety and liveness properties in linear temporal logic (LTL) and verify them exhaustively with the SPIN model checker. We prove that the fallback machinery never causes a false positive (an enforcement action against a benign file), commits to exactly one verdict per scan even when timed-out responses arrive late, weakens detection strength only in an explicit and ordered way, and always terminates in an enforcement decision, so the pipeline is deadlock-free. Each property is checked to hold non-vacuously, and we report how the state space grows with concurrent scans and endpoints. The work shows how model checking can give strong correctness guarantees for the failure-handling logic of a production security system, a layer that has received little direct formal attention.

</details>


### [118] [SmartMemory: Detecting On-chain-off-chain Communication Inconsistency for Smart Contract via Memory-based Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34983)
*Zeqin Liao,Yuhong Nan,Henglong Liang,Zixu Gao,Lianyu Hu,Yuqiang Sun,Zhijie Zhong,Xiaoyu Ma,Zibin Zheng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SmartMemory，首个基于记忆代理的 OFC 不一致（OFCI）检测框架，通过业务语义规范化、记忆代理知识复用和污点分析，在自建数据集上达到 80.68% 精度与 87.65% 召回率，并从 325 个真实应用中检测出 36 个未知 OFCI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约支撑去中心化金融，链上/链下通信（OFC）需求增长带来跨链桥、现实资产代币化、法币稳定币等多样化应用，相关安全事件频发。但既有研究只针对 OFC 应用内分散的漏洞类别，缺乏统一视角，导致已知模式之外的漏洞被遗漏。本文识别 OFC 不一致（OFCI）为 OFC 漏洞的根本原因，它源于业务逻辑缺陷，最终破坏链上与链下资产表示的等价性而引发不一致。自动检测 OFCI 面临两大挑战：(1) 定位异构业务逻辑；(2) 将已有漏洞知识迁移以识别未见过的 OFCI 实例。

Method: SmartMemory 首先将多样化的 OFC 合约实现映射为规范化的业务语义表示，以定位用于 OFCI 检查的业务逻辑，从而应对异构性。其次集成基于记忆的代理，从特征中提炼漏洞知识为模式和检测规则，实现跨案例知识迁移以识别未见过的 OFCI。最后执行污点分析，验证每个候选 OFCI 的可达性、类型与影响。

Result: 构建了首个真实世界 OFCI 数据集，包含 48 个 DApp、81 个 OFCI。SmartMemory 在该数据集上取得 80.68% 精度与 87.65% 召回率。此外，在对 325 个真实 OFC 应用的分析中，检测出 36 个此前未知的 OFCI，均已被相关方确认并修复。

Conclusion: SmartMemory 首次以记忆代理框架统一检测 OFC 不一致问题，验证了以业务语义规范化与知识迁移为核心的检测思路的有效性，并在真实应用中发现了大量未知漏洞，表明其对 OFC 安全具有实际价值。

Abstract: Smart contracts underpin decentralized finance, where growing demand for on-chain/off-chain communication(OFC) has driven diverse applications such as cross-chain bridges, real-world asset tokenization, and fiat-backed stablecoins. TheOFC-related security incidents in these applications are increasingly frequent, but prior studies address separate vulnerability categories within OFC applications rather than providing a unified view, causing vulnerabilities outside known patterns to be missed.In this paper, we identify OFC inconsistency (OFCI) as a root cause of OFC vulnerabilities, which arises from business-logic flaw and ultimately breaks the equivalence between the on-chain and off-chain asset representations to induce inconsistency.Automatically detecting OFCIs faces two challenges including (1)locating heterogeneous business logic, and (2) transferring existing vulnerability knowledge to identify unseen OFCI instances.
  To this end, we propose SmartMemory, the first framework to leverage a memory-based agent for OFCI detection. To address heterogeneity, SmartMemory maps diverse implementations ofOFC contracts into a canonical business-semantic representation to locate the business logic for OFCI inspection. For knowledge reuse, SmartMemory integrates a memory-based agent to distill vulnerability knowledge from features into patterns and detection rules, enabling knowledge transfer across cases to identify unseenOFCIs. Lastly, SmartMemory performs taint analysis to verify the reachability, type, and impact of each candidate OFCI. We construct the first real-world OFCI dataset comprising 48 DApps with 81 OFCIs for evaluation, on which SmartMemory achieves80.68% precision and 87.65% recall. In addition, through an analysis of 325 real-world OFC applications, SmartMemory detects 36 previously unknown OFCIs, all of which have been confirmed and fixed by corresponding parties.

</details>


### [119] [Making the Invisible Visible: A Framework for Reflective AI Use in Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34997)
*Ali Shakiba,Thomas Chaffey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI Journal"结构化反思框架，通过执行追踪与认知审计使学生在软件工程教育中与生成式AI的交互过程可见，从而支持对评估性判断和负责任AI实践的考察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已广泛嵌入软件工程教育，支持需求开发、设计探索、文档撰写与原型制作等活动。然而教育者通常只能看到最终产物，难以了解学生在学习过程中如何评估、验证和优化AI生成的输出，这给评估学生的评估性判断能力与负责任AI辅助实践带来了挑战。

Method: 提出AI Journal框架，包含两个组成部分：(1) 执行追踪（execution tracking），记录提示词、输出、意图与交互情境；(2) 认知审计（cognitive auditing），捕捉验证策略、干预决策、信心判断、关键学习时刻以及对AI辅助工作的反思。该框架在软件工程专业第一学期课程中部署实施。

Result: AI Journal使教育者得以观察到仅靠产物评估无法发现的学习侧面。初步观察显示，学生在验证实践、干预策略以及对AI辅助工作的认知方面存在差异。关键学习时刻往往出现在学生评估情境适用性、可行性和需求一致性时，而非识别明显错误时。

Conclusion: AI Journal是一种实用、轻量且与模型无关的方法，可使AI辅助学习过程可见。通过突出验证、干预与反思，它将关注点从以产物为中心的评估转向评估性判断与负责任的AI辅助实践。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is increasingly embedded in software engineering education, supporting activities such as requirements development, design exploration, documentation, and prototyping. However, educators often have visibility only into final artefacts, with limited insight into how students evaluate, verify, and refine AI-generated outputs during the learning process. This creates challenges for assessing evaluative judgement and responsible AI-assisted practice. This paper introduces the AI Journal, a structured reflection framework designed to make student-GenAI interaction visible in first-year software engineering education. The framework combines execution tracking, which records prompts, outputs, intent, and interaction context, with cognitive auditing, which captures verification strategies, intervention decisions, confidence judgements, critical learning moments, and reflections on AI-supported work. Deployed in a first-semester software engineering course, the AI Journal enabled visibility into aspects of student learning not observable through artefact-based assessments alone. Preliminary observations suggested variation in verification practices, intervention strategies, and perceptions of AI-supported work. Critical learning moments frequently occurred when students evaluated contextual suitability, feasibility, and requirements alignment rather than identifying obvious errors. The AI Journal demonstrates a practical, lightweight, and model-agnostic approach for making AI-assisted learning processes visible. By foregrounding verification, intervention, and reflection, it shifts attention from product-focused assessment toward evaluative judgement and responsible AI-assisted practice.

</details>


### [120] [Graph-Based Learning for Multi-Horizon Martian Atmospheric Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35042)
*Gary Myler,James Holmes,Manish Patel,Amel Bennaceur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MaGMA，一个基于图的数据工程框架，将OpenMARS再分析场转化为结构化学习对象，用于火星大气多时间尺度预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星大气行为融合了空间、时间、垂直及沙尘驱动过程，现有建模与预报方法难以应对，而火星天气预报对未来探测很重要。

Method: 将局部大气斑块作为图节点，通过空间邻域、时间连续性、较长时依赖和动态相似大气状态进行连接；模型整合近期大气历史、工程化物理描述符和垂直大气信息，实现多时间尺度预报。

Result: 在五个未见火星年（含一个全球沙尘暴年）上评估。常规年份整体R^2约0.73-0.85；沙尘柱预报在多数年份-时间尺度对比中优于经典与深度时序基线。沙尘暴年短时间尺度沙尘柱预报R^2前两个时间尺度高于0.8，但更广的多变量性能下降。

Conclusion: 基于图的数据工程可为行星大气预报创建可复用且有诊断价值的表示，同时表明需要更好地学习稀有极端情形并改进垂直大气结构的利用。

Abstract: Martian weather forecasting is important for future exploration, but atmospheric behaviour on Mars combines spatial, temporal, vertical, and dust-driven processes in ways that challenge current modelling and forecasting approaches. This paper introduces MaGMA (Martian Graph-based Multi-horizon Atmospheric Forecasting), a graph-based data engineering framework that transforms OpenMARS reanalysis fields into structured learning objects for Martian atmospheric forecasting. Local atmospheric patches are represented as graph nodes and linked through spatial neighbourhoods, temporal continuity, longer temporal dependencies, and dynamically similar atmospheric states. The model integrates recent atmospheric history, engineered physical descriptors, and vertical atmospheric information to support forecasting across multiple horizons. We evaluate MaGMA across five unseen Martian years, including regular years and a global dust storm year. In regular years, the model achieves overall R^2 values of approximately 0.73-0.85. For dust-column forecasting, it outperforms classical and deep temporal baselines in most year-horizon comparisons. During the global dust storm year, dust-column prediction remains strong at shorter horizons, with R^2 above 0.8 for the first two horizons, while broader multivariate performance declines. The results show that graph-based data engineering can create reusable and diagnostically useful representations for planetary atmospheric forecasting, while highlighting the need for better learning under rare extreme regimes and improved use of vertical atmospheric structure.

</details>


### [121] [Research-Native by Construction: Minimal Nodes, Re-verifiable Workflows, and Compounding Memory for Long-Horizon Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35182)
*Di Wang,Yu Liu,Bing Cui,Chaoqun Ji,Dongyuan Ni,Jingyu Lu,Kunlei Cui,Pu Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍 AfS（Agent for Science），一个面向长时程科研工作的平台，通过将科研纪律编码为机械强制执行的"法律"来提升机器产出研究的可信度，而非依赖模型自觉遵守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科研智能体多为附加技能文件夹的通用编码智能体，继承了其在完工压力下造假、跳过或掩盖的失败模式，亟需从机制层面而非模型自觉层面解决可信度问题。

Method: 核心主张是"将可信度从要求模型表现良好转移到使不合规状态不可表达"。系统将科研纪律编码为机械强制执行的法则（测量前承诺、不可伪造冻结、报告非事实、证据持久但裁决不持久、负面结果为一等公民、机制问题交给框架而语义判断交给模型），并围绕三个时间尺度组织：单次运行内的研究节点、项目内的冻结闭包条件与哈希链制品账本、跨项目的两级知识库。此外包含节点契约、写路径门控、两级记忆和运行时基座。

Result: 通过三条真实失败尝试的跟踪展示机制如何捕获这些失败，并附两个已闭合的科研活动作为示例；未报告基准测试结果，仅测量当前机器装置的运行成本，定量对比套件仍在建设中。

Conclusion: AfS 以"使不合规状态不可表达"为原则，将科研纪律机械化嵌入平台各层，为长时程机器科研提供过程完整性保障，但缺乏当前可用的定量评估。

Abstract: We describe AfS (Agent for Science), a platform built for long-horizon scientific work, where a project runs for tens of hours across dozens of agent runs with a human present only occasionally. Most agents for science are general coding agents with a skills folder attached, and they inherit that lineage's failure mode: under pressure to finish, they fabricate, skip, or smooth over. Our design rests on one claim: most of the credibility of machine-made research can be moved from asking the model to behave to making the non-compliant state unrepresentable. We encode research discipline as mechanically enforced laws (commitment before measurement; unforgeable freezing; reports are not facts; evidence persists but verdicts do not; negative results are first-class; mechanical questions to the framework and semantic judgment to the model), organized around three time horizons: a minimal set of research nodes within a run, an inquiry contract with frozen closure conditions and a hash-chained artifact ledger within a project, and a two-tier knowledge base with promotion by rewriting across projects. This is a system description written under one rule: each mechanism appears in exactly one place, with the invariant it enforces, the failure it prevents, the way it is realized, and the cost it imposes. It covers the node contract, the write-path gates, the two-tier memory, and the runtime substrate. Three traces walk real failure attempts through the mechanisms that catch them, and two closed campaigns are included as worked illustrations rather than as an evaluation. We report no benchmark: a process-integrity suite that would support quantitative comparison is under construction, and what we can measure today is only the operating cost of the machinery.

</details>


### [122] [Is there a future for models in the LLM era?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35281)
*Marco Calamo,Massimo Mecella,Monique Snoeck*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARTHUR框架，将LLM多智能体方法与MDE结合，用于重构由传统MDE代码生成器从UML图产生的遗留Java代码，结果表明MDE远未过时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件开发与LLM深度结合的时代，MDE是否仍有意义？作者希望探究MDE能否与LLM方法成功结合，以弥补各自单独使用时的不足。

Method: 提出ARTHUR（Architecture Refactoring Through Hybrid UML Reasoning），一种由LLM驱动的多智能体框架，结合MDE的可靠性与LLM的易用性，重构传统规则式MDE代码生成器从UML图产生的遗留Java代码，并支持Spring Boot等现代框架，同时保持与基于模型测试的一致性。作者还对比了直接从概念模型生成代码的效果。

Result: 通过时间、成本、通过测试率，以及compile@k、pass@k、pass^k等指标评估了多个项目的重构结果，并观察了直接生成代码与重构方式的差异。

Conclusion: 初步测试结果表明，MDE远未到被淘汰的地步，LLM与MDE的结合具有实际价值。

Abstract: In an era where software development is deeply tied with Large Language Models, does Model Driven Engineering (MDE) still make sense? This raises the question of the extent to which MDE can be successfully combined with an LLM approach to address the downsides of each approach separately. In this paper, we try to answer that question by investigating a central Research Question: Can Agents powered by Large Language Models improve code generated from UML diagrams and text specifications by Model Driven Engineering (MDE) tools? To investigate this problem, we developed a novel LLM-powered Multi-Agentic approach called ARTHUR (Architecture Refactoring Through Hybrid UML Reasoning), a framework that aims at combining the reliability of MDE and the ease of use of LLMs. ARTHUR is designed to refactor legacy Java code produced by traditional, rule-based, MDE code generators from UML diagrams. To asses the answer to our question, we have refactored the legacy code of several projects from our custom dataset crafted for this purpose. \name{} which made it possible to add support for modern frameworks like Spring Boot, while ensuring compliance with Model-Based Testing techniques to verify that the code still corresponds to the initial model's specifications. We then measured the results obtained in terms of time and cost, passing test rate, and \texttt{compile@k}, \texttt{pass@k} and \texttt{pass$^k$} metrics. We also observed the effect of generating code directly from the conceptual model without the refactoring. Our preliminary test results show that MDE could not be more far from retirement, after all.

</details>


### [123] [Do Coding Agents Reuse Existing Code or Reinvent the Wheel?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35357)
*Dongsheng Ma,Sizhe Wang,Xinyi Huang,Zhengren Wang,Yuhan Wang,Luyang Si,Xincheng Wei,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 RepoReuse，一个面向真实代码仓库的多轮评测基准，用于审计编码智能体的代码复用行为，并揭示其随轮次增加而忽视已有代码、积累冗余的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只关注通过率，无法回答智能体在真实仓库迭代开发中是否复用已有代码这一基本问题。重复实现意味着同一修复要做两次，而智能体生成速度远超人工审计速度，冗余会无监督地累积。

Method: 构建 RepoReuse 多轮基准：需求逐轮揭示，工作区跨轮累积。采用全自动流水线，结合基于 AST 的依赖图、引导式证据收集与执行验证的任务合成，可便捷扩展到新仓库。除通过率外，还测量复用率、复用召回率与跨轮结构性冗余。

Result: 对 3,000 轮的审计显示：智能体逐步停止探索相关仓库代码；即使历史代码就在工作区中，其复用自身历史的程度也在下降；到第 5 轮时，50.8% 的任务链中残留重复逻辑——而通过率几乎不变。

Conclusion: 这些缺陷对通过率不可见，说明代码生成评测不能只看功能正确性，还需评估代码复用等行为。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed for iterative development on real repositories, yet existing evaluation barely answers a basic question: \emph{do coding agents reuse existing code or reinvent the wheel?} The question matters: every duplicated implementation is a fix applied twice and agents produce code far faster than humans can audit, so redundancy accumulates unsupervised. Thus, we present \textbf{RepoReuse}, a multi-turn benchmark for auditing code reuse in real repositories, where requirements are revealed turn by turn and the workspace accumulates across turns. It is built by a fully automated pipeline combining AST-based dependency graphs, guided evidence collection, and execution-verified task synthesis, and scales readily to new repositories. Beyond pass rates, we measure the reuse rate together with recall and cross-turn structural redundancy. An audit over 3{,}000 turns shows that agents progressively stop exploring relevant repository code, reuse their own history less even when it is fully in the workspace, and leave duplicated logic in 50.8\% of task chains by turn~5---all while pass rates barely move. Such deficiencies are invisible to pass rates, underscoring the need to evaluate code generation beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [124] [MCP Error Messages Written for Developers Hurt the Most Capable Agents Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35381)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析150个常用MCP服务器中3001条错误消息,发现近三分之一(949条)在告诉调用者下一步该做什么,而其中约一半步骤依赖服务器无法看见的调用方条件;仅通过工具行动的智能体常按步骤照做却无法完成任务,通过"点名服务器工具"或"删除该步骤"可显著提升恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多MCP服务器封装的是为人类开发者构建的Web API,其错误消息面向人类读者,指示其运行命令、修改配置、打开网页或等待。然而读取这些消息的智能体只能调用服务器提供的工具,无法执行这些人类动作,导致错误处理失效。作者希望量化这一问题,并寻找可行的补救办法。

Method: 对150个广泛使用的MCP服务器的3001条错误消息进行统计,识别其中指示"下一步"的消息及其依赖服务器不可见信息的比例,并按凭证错误、限流错误分类统计。随后在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard任务上测试五个仅通过工具行动的OpenAI模型,测量诸如"过期凭证"和"限流"场景下的任务恢复率,并对比两种补救措施:MCP开发者侧在步骤中点名具体服务器工具,以及智能体开发者侧用一句提示词在模型读取前删除该步骤。

Result: 3001条错误消息中有949条给出下一步指令,其中约一半依赖服务器无法感知的调用方信息。凭证错误中67条步骤里62条要求终端命令、配置修改或网页操作;限流错误中30条里20条只说要等待重试而未指明需重复的调用。测试显示:过期凭证下步骤中的终端命令仅恢复45%任务,且造成的性能损失从GPT-5.5的18分增至GPT-6 Astra的69分;GitHub的"等待后重试"仅恢复6%。补救后,在步骤中命名服务器工具可将过期凭证恢复率提升至84%(用登录工具替代命令)、限流恢复率提升至88%(用需重复的调用替代裸等待);用一句提示词删除该步骤则可将过期凭证恢复率提升至82%。

Conclusion: 面向人类的错误消息在智能体场景下适得其反,会导致错误的跟随行为和严重的性能下降,且模型越强损失越大。解决方法明确:面向MCP开发者应让步骤指向具体服务器工具,面向智能体开发者应在模型读取前剥离面向人类的指令步骤。这提示错误消息设计需以调用方(智能体)的能力边界为前提。

Abstract: Many Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers wrap web APIs built for human developers, and their error messages tell the reader to run a command, edit a configuration, open a web page or wait. Many agents that read them can only call the server's tools. In 150 widely used MCP servers, 949 of 3,001 error messages tell the caller what to do next, and half of these steps depend on something the server cannot see about the caller. On credential errors, 62 of 67 steps ask for a terminal command, a configuration change or a web page; on rate limits, 20 of 30 say to wait and retry without naming the call to repeat. We tested five OpenAI models that act only through the tools of Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard tasks, and the agents did what the step said. On expired credentials, a terminal command in the step left 45% of tasks recovered, and the loss it caused grew from 18 points for GPT-5.5 to 69 for GPT-6 Astra. On a rate limit, GitHub's "Wait before retrying." left 6%. We tested two remedies. For MCP developers, naming a server tool in the step raised recovery on expired credentials to 84%, with the login tool in place of the command, and on a rate limit to 88%, with the call to repeat in place of the bare wait. For agent developers, deleting the step with a one-sentence prompt before the model reads it raised recovery on expired credentials to 82%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Replay in the Silent Degrees of Freedom: Continual Learning Without an Offline Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31630)
*Zhang Yanhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需离线阶段、基于局部生物约束规则的持续学习方法，通过隔离规则、不应期轮换规则、稳态压力与相对新颖性门控，在k-WTA动力学下实现回放巩固，并在split-MNIST与split-CIFAR-10上取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回放的持续学习通常依赖专门的离线阶段，或将回放样本与输入流交错进行；而大脑在清醒时也能通过局部睡眠、依赖使用的短暂关闭期进行巩固。作者因此追问：一个由局部、生物约束规则训练的网络，能否完全无需离线阶段实现巩固。

Method: 提出三项机制：1）隔离规则，将回放更新限制在当前输入下不可见的隐藏突触上（k-winner-take-all动力学），优化器状态仅在掩码内推进；2）不应期轮换规则，使刚发放的单元在下一轮竞争中退出，扩大可巩固集合；3）稳态压力与相对新颖性门控，决定回放爆发何时触发、轮换何时运行。该方法反转了非干扰持续学习的常规方向：保持当前输入的隐藏计算不变，同时将过去记忆写入当前批次未使用的自由度。

Result: 在class-incremental split-MNIST上无需离线阶段达到91.6±0.3%，在两个留出划分上达到或超过最佳离线夜间调度，与DER++持平并优于experience replay、ER-ACE、A-GEM及未掩码局部回放；单次遍历中领先DER++（91.8%对90.1%），而夜间调度降至76.9%。优势在小缓冲区时最大，大缓冲区时让位于反向传播参考方法；在split CIFAR-10上领先离线排练与experience replay，但落后于ER-ACE和DER++。轮换贡献了大部分增益，隔离提供不变性保证。

Conclusion: 该机制不依赖于局部规则：在相同调度下，带k-WTA隐藏层的反向传播网络也能从轮换中获益，且隔离在其上同样无额外代价。说明所提方法具有通用性，可用于不同训练范式。

Abstract: Replay-based continual learning almost always consolidates in a dedicated offline phase or by interleaving replayed samples with the input stream, whereas brains also consolidate during wakefulness through local sleep, brief use-dependent off-periods of individual circuits. We ask whether a network trained by local, biologically constrained rules can consolidate with no offline phase at all. An isolation rule confines replay updates to hidden synapses invisible to the current input under k-winner-take-all dynamics, with optimiser state advanced only inside the mask; a refractory rotation rule makes units that have just fired sit out the next competition, widening the consolidable set; a homeostatic pressure and a relative-novelty gate decide when replay bursts fire and when rotation runs. This inverts the usual direction of non-interfering continual learning: the hidden computation on the current input is held invariant (exactly on the proven channels, and for all but 0.3% of waking samples per update elsewhere) while past memories are written into the degrees of freedom the current batch leaves unused. On class-incremental split-MNIST the system reaches 91.6+-0.3% with no offline phase, at or above the best offline-night schedule on two held-out splits, tied with DER++ and above experience replay, ER-ACE, A-GEM and unmasked local replay; in a single pass it leads DER++ (91.8% against 90.1%) while the night falls to 76.9%. The advantage is largest at small buffers and gives way to the backpropagation references at large ones; on split CIFAR-10 the system leads offline rehearsal and experience replay but trails ER-ACE and DER++. Rotation carries most of the gain; isolation adds the invariance guarantee. The mechanism is not tied to the local rule: under the same schedule a backpropagation network with k-WTA hidden layers gains from rotation, and isolation is again free on top of it.

</details>


### [126] [OMP-MoE: Efficient Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts LLMs via Orthogonal Matching Pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31631)
*Dezhi Li,Lujun Li,Qiyuan Zhu,Hao Gu,Bei Liu,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMP-MoE，一个免训练的MoE大模型压缩框架，通过正交匹配追踪将专家剪枝重构为稀疏信号重建问题，并结合注水策略与自适应推理，在25-50%剪枝率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽能高效扩展大语言模型，但因内存需求巨大而面临部署挑战。现有剪枝方法要么搜索成本过高，要么忽视专家之间动态的相互依赖关系，因此需要一种更高效、更准确的压缩方法。

Method: 提出免训练框架OMP-MoE：1）将单个专家贡献视为字典原子，把剪枝问题重构为稀疏信号重建任务，用正交匹配追踪以线性复杂度贪心最小化重建误差来选择专家；2）通过注水策略优化跨层专家分配，兼顾重建质量与路由稳定性；3）引入OMP-MoE†自适应推理机制，基于能量预测动态调整专家激活。

Result: 在Qwen、DeepSeek-V2、GPT-OSS、Mixtral等MoE模型上，25-50%剪枝率下均持续优于现有方法。Qwen3-30B-A3B在50%压缩下保留93.3%原始性能，搜索速度提升33倍，推理加速1.55倍。

Conclusion: OMP-MoE在免训练条件下实现了高效且高质量的MoE专家剪枝，显著降低搜索与推理开销，为MoE大模型部署提供了有效方案，代码将在录用后开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable efficient scaling of large language models but face critical deployment challenges due to massive memory requirements. Existing pruning methods either incur prohibitive search costs or neglect the dynamic interdependencies between experts. To address these challenges, we present OMP-MoE, a novel training-free compression framework for reducing expert redundancy in MoE-based LLMs. Based on observations of expert contribution patterns, we reformulate the pruning problem as a sparse signal reconstruction task solved through Orthogonal Matching Pursuit. Specifically, our method first treats individual expert contributions as dictionary atoms and selects experts that greedily minimize reconstruction error with linear computational complexity. Then, we optimize cross-layer expert allocation through a water-filling strategy that accounts for both reconstruction quality and routing stability. Finally, we introduce OMP-MoE†, an adaptive inference mechanism that dynamically adjusts expert activation based on energy prediction. Comprehensive experiments on Qwen, DeepSeek-V2, GPT-OSS, and Mixtral MoE demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods at 25-50% pruning ratios. For Qwen3-30B-A3B at 50% compression, we retain 93.3% of original performance, achieving 33$\times$ faster search and 1.55$\times$ inference speedup. Codes will be available after acceptance.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Through Game: Skewed Transfer of Tabular Knowledge to Strengthen Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32272)
*Longfei Huang,Shangdong Yang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出偏斜知识迁移（SKT），通过在多模态共享参数空间中自适应整合模态梯度，将表格知识非对称地迁移到图像模型，解决测试时表格数据不可用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表格-图像学习日益受关注，但测试时表格数据不可用。实用方案是训练时把表格知识迁移到图像模型，但存在被忽视的关键挑战：图像与表格之间的模态不平衡，以及跨模态迁移中两者的非对称模态关系，限制了表格数据的辅助作用。

Method: 提出 SKT：首先引入多模态共享头，在不增加额外参数的情况下利用跨模态结构；然后设计两步纳什议价策略以有效利用表格梯度——第一步寻找模态平衡点，第二步用偏好感知将组合梯度导向对图像有利的方向。并理论分析 SKT 的帕累托改进与收敛性。

Result: 在常用表格-图像数据集上的实验表明，SKT 以表格数据为辅助信息，能够持续提升图像单模态性能。

Conclusion: SKT 通过非对称梯度整合显式实现了表格知识向图像模型的迁移，为测试时表格缺失场景下的多模态学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal tabular-image learning is gaining growing attention, yet it faces challenges due to tabular data unavailable at test time. A practical solution involves transferring tabular knowledge to images during training to enhance the performance of image models at inference. However, the overlooked yet important challenges lie in the modality imbalance between images and tables, as well as their asymmetric modality relationship in cross-modal transfer, which limits the auxiliary role of tabular data. To address these issues, we propose Skewed Knowledge Transfer (SKT), which asymmetrically transfers tabular knowledge to improve the image model by adaptive integration of modality gradients in a shared parameter space. Specifically, we first introduce a multimodal shared head, which allows the model to benefit from cross-modal structure without adding additional parameters. We then design a two-step Nash Bargaining strategy to effectively leverage tabular gradients. In the first step, SKT seeks a point of modality balance and uses preference awareness in the second step to steer combined gradients toward image-beneficial directions. Furthermore, we theoretically analyze the Pareto improvement and convergence of SKT. To this end, tabular knowledge is explicitly transferred to enhance image models. Empirical experiments on widely used tabular-image datasets reveal that SKT consistently improves image unimodal performance by using tabular data as auxiliary information.

</details>


### [128] [EEGAgentBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Short- and Long-Horizon EEG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31632)
*Huyu Wu,Weining Weng,Yuchen Liu,Yiqiang Chen,Yang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEGAgentBench，一个统一评测LLM智能体在短程与长程EEG分析能力的基准，覆盖六类应用、2秒至近23小时信号，含10个确定性工具，并评测了15个模型家族共29个前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG分析正从短片段分类转向长程解读，需要迭代证据积累、多步推理与专用工具协同。现有EEG智能体评测碎片化：任务有限、时间跨度窄、协议不一致，且未全面评估推理、工具使用与工作流构建能力。

Method: 构建EEGAgentBench统一基准：涵盖从知识问答到睡眠分期的六类代表性EEG应用；信号时长2秒至近23小时；预测目标包括类标签、事件区间与epoch级序列；支持知识推理、短程解读、长程事件检测与序列理解四类统一评测。提供10个确定性EEG分析工具，仅暴露任务相关信号测量值，智能体需自主选工具、迭代积累证据并构建多步工作流。

Result: 评测15个模型家族共29个前沿LLM。结果表明EEGAgentBench能有效区分超越模型规模与推理成本的智能体能力，同时揭示当前LLM智能体在长程EEG分析中的显著局限，尤其在持续证据积累与多步推理方面。

Conclusion: EEGAgentBench为短程与长程EEG分析提供了统一、系统的智能体评测框架，暴露出长程分析的关键瓶颈，为后续LLM智能体能力提升指明方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis is evolving from short-segment classification toward long-horizon interpretation that demands iterative evidence accumulation, multi-step reasoning, and coordinated use of specialized signal-processing tools. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise as autonomous agents for EEG analysis, existing EEG agentic evaluations remain fragmented, covering limited tasks over narrow temporal horizons with inconsistent protocols, and providing no comprehensive assessment of agents' reasoning, tool-use, and workflow construction capabilities. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{EEGAgentBench}, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM agents on short- and long-horizon EEG analysis. EEGAgentBench spans six representative EEG applications ranging from knowledge question answering to sleep staging. It encompasses signal durations from 2 seconds to nearly 23 hours, with prediction targets ranging from class labels to event intervals and epoch-level sequences. This design supports unified evaluation across knowledge reasoning, short-horizon interpretation, long-horizon event detection, and sequential understanding. The benchmark further provides 10 deterministic EEG analysis tools that expose only task-relevant signal measurements. Agents must therefore select tools autonomously, accumulate evidence iteratively, and construct multi-step workflows. For evaluation, we benchmark 29 frontier LLMs from 15 model families. Results demonstrate that EEGAgentBench effectively distinguishes agent capabilities beyond model scale and inference cost, while revealing substantial limitations of current LLM agents in long-horizon EEG analysis, particularly in sustained evidence accumulation and multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [129] [Getting Motif-ated: Controllable AI Compositions from Injected Motif Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32788)
*Chao Peter Yang,Cynthia Rudin,Yue Jiang,Simon Mak,Stephen Ni-Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotiGen 提出一种对预训练符号音乐生成模型进行"改造"的方案，通过将音乐动机作为结构化提示并加入注意力偏置与两阶段课程学习，使模型能够按指定动机进行创作，生成作品中超过 92.3% 成功运用了所提示的动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号音乐生成系统（如 NotaGen）虽能根据简短提示生成风格可信的古典乐谱，但几乎不提供对音乐本身的可控接口。理论上可在从零训练的基础模型中内置控制能力，但这需要海量高质量标注数据与算力，实际难以实现。因此作者希望找到一种可行的替代路径，让预训练模型获得新的指令控制能力。

Method: MotiGen 采用"改装"预训练符号音乐模型的思路：将音乐动机作为一条结构化提示行注入，对动机对应的 token 施加标量注意力偏置以强化其影响，并通过两阶段课程学习建立关联——第一阶段从训练数据中围绕动机出现位置裁剪出的聚焦片段学习，第二阶段再学习包含动机的完整乐谱。

Result: 实验表明，模型在超过 92.3% 的生成作品中成功运用了所提示的音乐动机，并在示例网站上展示了使用多种不同动机生成的乐曲。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需从零训练即可为预训练符号音乐模型增加动机控制能力的可行方案，验证了提示注入、注意力偏置与课程学习相结合的有效性，为音乐生成的可控性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep learning has transformed symbolic music generation by borrowing the training paradigms of large language models, with systems such as NotaGen now producing complete, stylistically convincing classical scores from a short prompt. These systems could become powerful creative partners, helping musicians generate endless possibilities. However, current systems expose almost no control handles on the music itself. In principle, control handles could be built into a foundation model trained from scratch, but this is rarely practical without massive amounts of quality annotated data and compute resources. We therefore present MotiGen, a recipe for retrofitting pretrained symbolic music models to use new instruction prompts. MotiGen injects a musical motif as a structured prompt line, reinforces it with a scalar attention bias toward the motif tokens, and learns the association with a two-phase curriculum. First, it learns from focused excerpts cropped around motif occurrences in the training data, then full scores including the motifs. Our experiments show that our model composes with the prompted motif in over 92.3\% of generated pieces. Generated pieces using a variety of motifs are included in our sample site: https://motigen-site.github.io/.

</details>


### [130] [Enhancing generalization in endwall film cooling prediction: Incorporating the superposition principle into transformer-based neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31633)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu,Zhendong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理增强神经算子框架SDNO，通过两阶段预测涡轮端壁气膜冷却温度场，具备对训练中未见孔数（10-20孔）的泛化预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局随气膜孔数量变化，传统模型难以泛化到训练样本中未出现的孔数与位置，需要提升变孔数条件下的预测泛化能力。

Method: 受气膜冷却叠加原理启发，将端壁温度场预测分为两阶段：第一阶段将冷却布局划分为若干随机分配气膜孔的子区域，用基于Transformer的Calculate Net预测各子区域温度场；第二阶段用Super Net将各子区域温度场叠加为整体温度场。同时采用对孔位置敏感的符号距离函数（SDF）编码孔位置信息，并以1-5孔变位置样本训练。

Result: 训练后的模型展现出优异的泛化能力，可准确预测训练样本中未见的10-20孔冷却布局的气膜冷却效率；相比全监督基线，SDNO提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: 该物理增强神经算子框架有效验证了所提预测模型在变孔数涡轮端壁气膜冷却预测中的有效性与泛化优势。

Abstract: In this study, a physics-enhanced neural operator framework is proposed to enhance the generalization prediction ability of the cooling layout of a turbine endwall with variable number of film holes. Specifically, inspired by the film cooling superposition principle, we propose a film cooling prediction model, namely superposition-based deep neural operator (SDNO), that divides the endwall temperature field prediction into two stages. In the first stage, the cooling layout of a turbine endwall is divided into several sub-parts with randomly assigned film holes, and a Transformer-based neural operator network, namely Calculate Net, is designed to predict the temperature field of each sub-part. Then, in the second stage, another neural operator network, i.e., Super Net, is trained to combine the temperature fields predicted by Calculate Net for each sub-part and obtain the superposed temperature field of the full cooling layout. Additionally, instead of directly taking the film cooling contours as pixel plots, a signed distance function (SDF) which is sensitive to the variable locations of cooling holes, is designed to encode the location information of cooling holes. Furthermore, the proposed endwall film cooling prediction model is trained with the samples that changing the number of film holes from 1-5 with variable locations. Then, the trained prediction shows excellent generalization prediction ability, which can accurately predict the film effectiveness of the cooling layout with 10-20 film cooling holes that are unseen in the training samples. The proposed SDNO also improves prediction accuracy relative to the fully supervised baseline. With the above, the effectiveness of our proposed prediction model has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [131] [Symmetry-quotient Flatness and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31634)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在对称商空间框架下建立定理级链条：商线性稳定性⇒商平坦性⇒输入平滑性⇒泛化，并给出商平坦度与批大小、学习率的显式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦性常被认为是泛化的关联指标，SGD 也被视为隐式偏向平坦解；但标准平坦度定义在原始参数空间，对正缩放等保函数对称性不具备不变性，因此需要发展对称感知的商空间理论。

Method: 针对平方损失和具保函数群作用的模型，在正则商流形上以经验损失 Hessian 的迹定义商平坦度；通过商空间版的平坦度到平滑性论证，证明商平坦度控制输入平滑性；对线性化 SGD 动力学证明一步均方商线性稳定性蕴含显式商平坦度上界，并推广到高阶张量矩；在局部覆盖与有界性假设下推导总体泛化界。

Result: 建立了商线性稳定性、商平坦性、输入平滑性与泛化之间的逐级蕴含关系；商平坦度可由批大小和学习率显式上界；并由此将泛化界表示 为商平坦度及商线性稳定性的函数。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用对称不变的商空间平坦度刻画，能更合理地连接 SGD 稳定性与泛化，为理解隐式偏置与泛化提供对称感知的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a theorem-level pipeline in symmetry-quotient settings: quotient linear stability implies quotient flatness, quotient flatness implies input smoothness, and input smoothness yields generalization under local covering assumptions. Flatness is often associated with generalization, and Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) is frequently viewed as implicitly biased toward flat solutions. However, standard flatness measures are typically defined in the raw parameter space and are therefore not invariant under function-preserving symmetries such as positive rescaling. We develop a symmetry-aware theory of quotient flatness, quotient linear stability, input smoothness, and generalization on quotient spaces of neural-network parameters. For square loss and models equipped with function-preserving group actions, we define quotient flatness as the trace of the Hessian of the empirical loss on the regular quotient manifold. We show that quotient flatness controls input smoothness through a quotient-space analogue of the flatness-to-smoothness argument. We also prove that one-step mean-square quotient linear stability of the linearized SGD dynamics implies an explicit quotient-flatness bound in terms of the batch size and learning rate, and extend this analysis to higher-order tensor moments. Finally, under local covering and boundedness assumptions, we derive population generalization bounds in terms of quotient flatness and, consequently, in terms of quotient linear stability.

</details>


### [132] [What Next-Event Accuracy Cannot See: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Emergency Department Trajectory Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31635)
*Zhen Xuen Brandon Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 EDSim-Bench 基准，系统量化临床轨迹模拟器在闭环自回归 rollout 下的误差累积问题，发现 next-event 准确率不足以评估模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床轨迹模型通常用观测历史上的下一事件准确率评估，但模拟需要模型在自己的生成历史上条件化，导致误差累积。该问题在序列建模中众所周知，却未被系统量化于临床轨迹模拟器。

Method: 构建 EDSim-Bench，使用 425,028 条 MIMIC-IV-ED 就诊记录并在 MC-MED 上外部复现。从留出的就诊前缀出发，模型生成整段就诊剩余部分，从终止、事件构成、时序、条件保真度、占用预测等维度评估，并以仅用训练集的三阶 n-gram 作为参考基线。对比 Transformer、GRU、LSTM 等神经架构及不同种子、模型缩放、时间漂移和外部站点。

Result: 尽管下一事件准确率差距在 0.001 内，三种神经架构在 rollout 下表现迥异。同一 Transformer 配方在不同种子下终止分数从 0.43 到 0.96，散度达 n-gram 的 4.2 至 137 倍；无前缀训练的神经模型在终止和事件构成上接近 n-gram。推理时干预改善终止但无法同时恢复构成与时序。监督所有可序列位置比仅监督前缀末位位置，使 Transformer、GRU、LSTM 散度降低一到两个数量级。但最佳模型生成的就诊长度约为观测的一半，模型排名在占用预测上发生反转。

Conclusion: 下一事件准确率不足以评估临床轨迹模拟器，需要跨种子、rollout 标准和下游任务的闭环评估。

Abstract: Clinical trajectory models are usually evaluated by next-event accuracy on observed histories. Simulation is different: models must condition on their own generated events, allowing errors to compound. Although this problem is well known in sequence modelling, it has not been systematically quantified for clinical trajectory simulators. We developed EDSim-Bench to evaluate this failure mode using 425,028 MIMIC-IV-ED stays, with external replication on MC-MED, and release the evaluation protocol and scoring code. Starting from held-out visit prefixes, models generate the remainder of each visit and are evaluated on termination, event composition, timing, conditional fidelity, and occupancy forecasting, with a train-only order-3 n-gram as a reference baseline. Despite next-event accuracies within 0.001, three neural architectures behaved very differently under rollout. Across seeds, one Transformer recipe ranged from 0.43 to 0.96 in termination score and from 4.2- to 137-fold the divergence of the n-gram; no prefix-trained neural model approached the n-gram on termination or event composition. Inference-time interventions improved termination but did not jointly recover composition and timing. Supervising every eligible sequence position rather than only the final prefix position was associated with one to two orders of magnitude lower divergence across Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models, with the pattern persisting under model scaling, temporal shift, and external-site evaluation. Nevertheless, even the best model generated visits approximately half as long as observed, and model rankings reversed on occupancy forecasting, a downstream quantity relevant to bed management. These results show that next-event accuracy is insufficient to evaluate clinical trajectory simulators and motivate closed-loop evaluation across seeds, rollout criteria, and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [133] [Grounding Vision-Language Models in Driving Semantics: A Multi-Dataset Predicate Framework for Explainable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31636)
*Mohamed Chouai,Fazli Faruk Okumus,Stefan Kugele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性的多数据集谓词框架，从可测量的几何、运动学、时序、地图与交通管制证据中推导驾驶场景语义，并在nuPlan与nuScenes上构建统一谓词知识图谱（Predicate KG），实现跨数据集一致的语义表示并可提升驾驶场景推理任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在驾驶场景理解中应用日益广泛，但其输出所表达的语义关系难以与底层交通情境进行验证，缺乏可追溯、可核对的语义表示手段。

Method: 提出确定性多数据集谓词框架，从几何、运动学、时序、地图和交通控制等可测量证据中推导驾驶场景语义；仅通过数据集特定接口恢复所需场景信息，谓词定义在nuPlan与nuScenes间保持一致，并统一物化为Predicate Knowledge Graph。在每数据集200个场景上用手工标注的谓词关系进行定量语义验证，并使用冻结的LLaVA-OneVision-7B模型在九个NuPlanQA子任务上评估Predicate KG。

Result: 语义验证宏F1达nuPlan 0.94、nuScenes 0.93，共享谓词的平均跨数据集差异为0.02。在九个NuPlanQA子任务中，谓词接地在七个子任务上取得最高准确率，包括Traffic Light（53.2%→71.5%）、Situation Assessment（76.2%→86.1%）和Action Recommendation（82.9%→89.0%）；Weather/Lighting基本不变（89.4% vs. 88.8%），与缺乏相应谓词一致；仅输入Predicate KG在九个中的八个子任务上优于仅输入metadata。

Conclusion: 确定性谓词提供一致且可追溯的语义表示，在oracle grounding条件下可降低谓词词表所覆盖推理任务对视觉输入的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for driving-scene understanding, yet the semantic relations expressed in their outputs are often difficult to verify against the underlying traffic situation. This paper introduces a deterministic multi-dataset predicate framework that derives driving-scene semantics from measurable geometric, kinematic, temporal, map, and traffic-control evidence. Dataset-specific interfaces are used only to recover the required scene information, while predicate definitions remain unchanged across nuPlan and nuScenes and are materialised in a common Predicate Knowledge Graph. Quantitative semantic validation against manually annotated predicate relations on 200 scenarios from each dataset yields macro F1 scores of 0.94 on nuPlan and 0.93 on nuScenes, with an average cross-dataset difference of 0.02 across the shared predicates. The Predicate KG is further evaluated using a frozen LLaVA-OneVision-7B model on the nine NuPlanQA subtasks. Predicate grounding achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated visual-input conditions in seven of nine NuPlanQA subtasks, including Traffic Light (53.2% to 71.5%), Situation Assessment (76.2% to 86.1%), and Action Recommendation (82.9% to 89.0%). Weather/Lighting remains essentially unchanged (89.4% vs. 88.8%), consistent with the absence of corresponding predicates, while Predicate KG only input outperforms metadata-only input in eight of nine subtasks. The results show that deterministic predicates provide a consistent and traceable semantic representation and, under oracle grounding, can reduce visual dependence for reasoning tasks covered by the predicate vocabulary.

</details>


### [134] [FIDAL: Diversity-Aware Federated Active Learning Under Real-World Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31637)
*David Dueñas Gaviria,Shadi Albarqouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FIDAL是一个面向医学影像的开集联邦主动学习框架，整合标定式全局-局部证据不确定性、支持集多样性加权与自适应OOD拒识，能在无需人工调阈值的情况下查询高信息量ID样本并排除无关离群样本。在三个多中心基准上性能与标注效率均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可跨机构协作训练而无需集中数据，但高标注成本、域偏移和类别不平衡仍是主要障碍，尤其当无关的分布外（OOD）样本稀释标注数据时更为严重。现有主动学习方法只关注分布内（ID）数据的不确定性或多样性，忽视了联邦临床场景中的未知样本，因此在开集场景下失效。

Method: 提出FIDAL开集联邦主动学习框架，包含三个核心组件：标定式全局-局部证据不确定性、支持集多样性加权、自适应OOD拒识。拒识门控基于基础模型的高斯覆盖信号，按客户与每轮使用Otsu准则自动确定阈值，从而在无人工调参下查询高信息量ID样本并排除无关离群点。

Result: 在三个多中心医学影像基准（皮肤科、组织病理学、含自然伪影的乳腺钼靶）的真实开集场景下评估：相比基于检测器的开集方法，平衡准确率最高提升约12个百分点；是唯一同时位于三个基准准确率-ID纯度帕累托前沿的方法。相同查询预算下，对OOD样本的标注量至少比所有精度匹配的基线少1.3倍，在乳腺钼靶基准上估计节省7-29小时专家阅片时间。仅标注数据池一小部分即可匹配或超过全监督性能。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了在医学开集联邦主动学习中整合不确定性、多样性与OOD拒识的价值，为降低标注成本、提升跨模态与跨中心鲁棒性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across institutions without centralizing data, yet high annotation costs, domain shifts, and class imbalance remain major obstacles, especially when irrelevant out-of-distribution (OOD) samples dilute the labeled data. Existing active learning methods target uncertainty or diversity within in-distribution (ID) data and overlook unknown samples in federated clinical settings. We propose FIDAL, an open-set federated active learning framework that combines calibrated global-local evidential uncertainty, support-set diversity weighting, and adaptive OOD rejection.
