The rejection gate thresholds a foundation-model Gaussian-coverage signal per client and per round with Otsu's criterion, so that highly informative ID samples are queried while irrelevant outliers are excluded without any hand-tuned threshold. Evaluated on three multi-center medical imaging benchmarks (dermatology, histopathology, and mammography with organically occurring artifacts) in realistic open-set scenarios, FIDAL outperforms detector-based open-set methods by up to about 12 percentage points of balanced accuracy and is the only method on the accuracy-ID purity Pareto front of all three benchmarks. At an equal query budget it spends at least 1.3 times fewer annotations on OOD samples than every accuracy-matched baseline, saving an estimated 7-29 hours of expert reading on the mammography benchmark. By labeling only a fraction of the data pool, it matches or exceeds fully supervised performance across modalities. These results highlight the value of integrating uncertainty, diversity, and OOD rejection in open-set federated active learning for medicine.

</details>


### [135] [Energy-aware frugal Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31638)
*Gaston Plat,Paul Saves,Nathalie Bartoli,Thierry Lefebvre,Joseph Morlier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将计算能耗足迹量化指标引入贝叶斯优化框架，用以在模型性能与节约性之间取得平衡，实验显示优化收敛与能耗间存在权衡，有时可同时获得更优解与更低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设计优化框架以预测精度为首要目标，忽视计算开销，导致规模化架构与多学科设计优化问题即便使用样本高效的贝叶斯优化器也难以处理，因此需要一种同时兼顾性能与计算节约的优化引导机制。

Method: 在贝叶斯优化框架中引入一个量化计算能耗足迹的指标，并以其指导模型的参数设置，使模型配置在性能与节约性之间取得平衡。

Result: 计算机实验揭示了最优收敛与底层能耗足迹之间存在的权衡关系，部分情况下同时获得了更优的寻优结果和更低的能耗。

Conclusion: 将能耗足迹纳入贝叶斯优化目标可有效引导模型参数配置，实现性能与计算成本的平衡，存在打破精度与能耗单纯对立关系的可能。

Abstract: Modern design optimization frameworks aim first and foremost for models with the most accurate predictions without balancing computational overhead. It remains a reason why scaled architecture and multidisciplinary design optimization problems are difficult to address, even with sample-efficient Bayesian optimizers. In this paper, a metric quantifying the computational energy footprint is introduced within a Bayesian optimization framework to guide the parameter setting of a model towards configurations that balance both performance and frugality. The computer experiments highlighted existing tradeoffs between optimum convergence and the underlying energy footprint, and sometimes resulted in both a better-found optimum and lower energy consumption.

</details>


### [136] [When Does Domain Adaptation Help on Physical Vibration Sensors? A Held-Out-Bearing Study of Neural-Operator and Convolutional Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31639)
*Kumbha Nagaswetha,Rabi Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在留出轴承（held-out-bearing）协议下重新评估滚动轴承故障诊断的域适应，发现仅源域迁移远弱于常见99%报告值，并证明输入表示（阶次域 vs 频率域）比对齐方法更决定适应成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有滚动轴承振动故障诊断基准常报告超过99%的准确率，但其评测划分把同一物理轴承同时放入训练与测试集，存在数据泄漏，导致迁移难度被严重低估；作者希望揭示真实跨轴承条件下的迁移能力，并厘清是什么因素主导迁移效果。

Method: 提出留出轴承协议，将每个轴承单元完整划入训练或测试集；在转速变化等工况偏移下评测仅源域迁移，并以计算阶次跟踪（computed order tracking，按轴角重采样使故障频率固定于轴阶次）作为可控表示变换；对比傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与特征维度匹配的卷积网络，并在频率域与阶次域中使用相同归一化RBF-MMD损失进行无监督对齐，同时考察小样本标签预算的作用；另用第二个数据集（留出单元为故障直径而非轴承）验证协议。

Result: 仅源域迁移在转速变化下仅达0.36，而目标域有监督上限为0.97；FNO将源域迁移从0.36提升至0.61，匹配维度的卷积网络在两种表示下均接近随机水平；相同RBF-MMD无监督对齐在频率域为0.71，在阶次域达0.95，距目标有监督上限仅0.02，并在每个留出轴承折上均高于0.86；表示正确后，小的标签预算增益有限；第二个以故障直径为留出单元的数据集暴露出即使目标有监督模型也无法避免的失败。

Conclusion: 对该任务而言，决定适应是否有效的关键在于输入表示而非对齐方法：阶次域表示使无监督对齐接近有监督上限，而频率域表示下对齐收益有限；留出轴承协议能更真实地暴露跨工况迁移的困难，且该协议在故障直径留出设置下揭示了更根本的失败模式。

Abstract: Diagnosing rolling-element bearing faults from vibration is a canonical physical-sensing task and a widely used benchmark for domain adaptation under operating-condition shift. Accuracies above 99 percent are commonly reported, but under evaluation splits that place the same physical bearing in both training and test. We revisit the task under a held-out-bearing protocol, assigning every bearing unit entirely to either the training or the test set, and find that source-only transfer is far weaker than such numbers suggest: on a change of shaft speed it reaches only $0.36$, against a target-supervised ceiling of 0.97. We then study what governs transfer. Treating computed order tracking, a shaft-angle resampling that places fault frequencies at fixed shaft orders independent of running speed, as a controlled change of representation, we find that a Fourier Neural Operator raises source-only transfer from $0.36$ to $0.61$ on the speed shift, where the fault peaks move, while a convolutional network of matched feature dimension stays near chance in both representations. The representation also decides whether unsupervised alignment can work: with the same normalized RBF-MMD loss and no target labels, the operator reaches 0.71 in the frequency domain but 0.95 in the order domain, within 0.02 of the target-supervised ceiling and above $0.86$ on every held-out bearing fold. Once the representation is right, a small label budget adds little. These results indicate that, for this task, the input representation rather than the alignment method decides whether adaptation helps. A second dataset, whose held-out units are fault diameters rather than bearings, shows that the same protocol exposes failures that even a target-supervised model cannot avoid.

</details>


### [137] [Measure Learning at Steady State: A BIRD-SQL Formula 1 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31640)
*Manoj Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将朴素全上下文 ICL 视为一个学习系统，在更长的共享世界日程上评分，发现无界 ICL 是较差的学习机制候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Continual Learning Bench 以短时域增益对比重置基线来评分，并发现朴素全上下文 ICL 在所测记忆中表现最强。作者质疑这一结论，认为短时域评分可能忽略长期代价。

Method: 在预设的晚期窗口（174 道 BIRD-SQL F1 题目的最后 40 道）上测量相对基线的稳态学习差距。将分数拆解为探索效率（SQL 试探次数）、任务奖励（命中数）和交付成本（API 费用与上下文规模）。

Result: 在 gpt-5.6-luna 上，晚期试探次数从 4.6–5.6 降至 0.95，而命中数仅小幅上升；ICL 上下文增长至约 95k tokens，成本大约翻倍。

Conclusion: 短时域增益低估了晚期试探节省、也遗漏了成本反转，因此无界 ICL 并非合适的学习机制候选。

Abstract: Continual Learning Bench scores learning as short-horizon gain versus a reset baseline and finds naive full-context ICL strongest among the memories it tested. We treat ICL as one learning system and score it on a longer shared-world schedule. Steady-state learning is the gap versus baseline on a pre-set late window (last 40 of 174 BIRD-SQL formula-1 questions). We split the score into exploration efficiency (SQL probes), task reward (hits), and delivery cost (API dollars and context size). On gpt-5.6-luna, late probes fall from 4.6-5.6 to 0.95 while hits rise only modestly and ICL context grows to about 95k tokens with cost roughly doubling. Short-horizon gain understates the late probe saving and misses the cost inversion, so we find that unbounded ICL is a poor candidate for the learning mechanism.

</details>


### [138] [Product-Aware Deterministic Rounding for Quantized Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31641)
*Piyush Sao,Narasinga Miniskar,Pedro Valero-Lara,Keita Teranishi,Sudip Seal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量化中确定性、乘积感知的舍入决策问题，提出通过零空间约简与条件期望补全的多项式时间算法来最小化矩阵乘法的乘积误差，并给出动态激活与静态权重两种场景下的理论界限与实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量舍入决策会通过矩阵乘法相互耦合，传统逐元素舍入（如 round-to-nearest）忽略这种耦合，导致乘积误差较大。作者希望在尺度、裁剪边界与网格固定后，研究确定性的乘积感知舍入，以更优地最小化整体乘积误差。

Method: 对动态激活舍入，采用零空间约简在保持松弛乘积的同时将分数决策数降至至多 r（活跃间隙加权权重块的秩）；再用条件期望补全构造确定性多项式时间算法。对可复用的静态权重，将精确期望乘积损失度量化为固定输出偏置下的非中心输入二阶矩，自由偏置重校准则得到中心化协方差；并证明精确优化即使在秩为一时也是 NP 难的。此外还提出裁剪感知初始化。

Result: 条件期望补全的平方乘积误差至多 OPT_dyn + rν_max²/4（ν_max 为该块最大行范数）。在 K=1024、r=16 的均衡块中，该方法达到抖动归一化中位误差 0.010，而 round-to-nearest 为 0.899。裁剪感知初始化在 10% 裁剪下将中位归一化误差降低 43.4 倍。留出 Digits 实验表明，保留输入均值或校正输出偏置在全部四种位宽与校准规模设置下都优于 round-to-nearest。

Conclusion: 乘积感知的确定性舍入能显著降低矩阵乘法的量化乘积误差，条件期望补全在多项式时间内逼近最优，且配合裁剪感知初始化与偏置校正可稳定优于传统舍入方法。

Abstract: Scalar rounding decisions interact through matrix multiplication. We study deterministic
  product-aware rounding after scales, clipping bounds, and grids are fixed, with each active
  scalar choosing between adjacent levels. For dynamic activation rounding, null-space
  reduction preserves the relaxed product while leaving at most $r$ fractional decisions, where
  $r$ is the rank of the active gap-weighted weight block. Conditional-expectation completion
  gives a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm with squared product error at most $
  \mathrm{OPT}_{\mathrm{dyn}}+rν_{\max}^2/4$, where $\mathrm{OPT}_{\mathrm{dyn}}$ is the best
  admissible error and $ν_{\max}$ is the largest row norm of that block. For reusable static
  weights, the exact expected product-loss metric is the uncentered input second moment with
  fixed output bias; free bias recalibration yields the centered covariance. Exact optimization
  is NP-hard even at rank one. In balanced blocks with $K=1024$ and $r=16$, conditional-
  expectation completion attains a dither-normalized median error of $0.010$, compared with
  $0.899$ for round-to-nearest. Clipping-aware initialization reduces median normalized error
  by a factor of $43.4$ at ten-percent clipping. Held-out Digits experiments show that
  retaining the input mean or correcting the output bias improves median product error over
  round-to-nearest in all four tested bit-width and calibration-size settings.

</details>


### [139] [Information Design Against Gaming and Learning Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31643)
*Madhava Gaikwad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了带弃权选项的二分类器在面对博弈型与学习型对手时的弃权策略张力：边界局部化弃权对博弈对手最优但对学习对手最差，两种自然防御（固定比率弃权、边界附近弃权）在Blackwell意义下不可比，并刻画了二者之间的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署带弃权选项的二分类器时，弃权查询的选择取决于对手类型：博弈型对手已知分类器并试图操纵特征跨越边界，而学习型对手需通过弃权反馈推断边界。二者需求冲突，因此需要分析这一张力并找到合理的防御权衡。

Method: 作者从理论层面分析两种防御：(1) 固定比率弃权；(2) 边界附近弃权。通过Blackwell可比性框架比较二者，并推导在错误eps下重构边界所需的查询复杂度，其中d为分类器族的VC维。同时在七个二分类任务（表格、图像、语言模型特征输入）上验证理论速率，采用标签加反事实访问的查询方式。

Result: 两种防御在Blackwell意义下不可比，任一方的响应都无法通过后处理另一方来模拟。重构边界到误差eps所需查询数：固定比率防御下为tildeΘ(d/eps)，边界附近防御下为Θ(d log(1/eps))。前者是查询分布上的最坏情况，任何重构算法都无法在该分布上缩小差距。实验在七个任务上确认了两种速率，标签加反事实访问比已发表的仅标签基线少用最多200倍查询即可提取边界。

Conclusion: 弃权策略的选择本质上取决于对手模型，固定比率与边界附近弃权不可相互模拟，且存在明确的查询复杂度权衡与帕累托前沿；实验验证了理论并显示反事实访问能大幅降低边界重构查询成本。

Abstract: A principal who deploys a binary classifier with an abstention option must decide which queries the mechanism abstains on. The right choice depends on the adversary. A gaming adversary already knows the classifier and tries to manipulate features across the boundary, so the principal does best by abstaining on queries close to that boundary. The same boundary-localizing rule is the worst possible choice against a learning adversary who does not know the classifier: each abstention now tells the adversary that the boundary is nearby, which is enough to drive a binary search. We analyze this tension. The two natural defenses, abstaining at a fixed rate and abstaining near the boundary, are Blackwell-incomparable: neither can be simulated by post-processing the other's responses. The number of queries needed to reconstruct the boundary to error $\eps$ is $\tildeΘ(d/\eps)$ under the first defense and $Θ(d \log(1/\eps))$ under the second, where $d$ is the VC dimension of the classifier family and $\tildeΘ$ suppresses factors polylogarithmic in $d$ and $1/\eps$. The first rate is a worst case over query distributions; no reconstruction algorithm can close the gap at the distributions that attain it. We characterize the Pareto frontier between the two defense objectives, and confirm both rates on seven binary-classification tasks spanning tabular, image, and language-model-feature inputs: label-plus-counterfactual access extracts the boundary with up to $200\times$ fewer queries than a published label-only baseline.

</details>


### [140] [MaD-RL: Matching Distributions for Calibrating LLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31644)
*Sourabh Kulkarni,Ksheeraj Sai Vepuri,Basar Demir,Jason Bohrer,Emily Shen,Jianfa Chen,Nan Jiang,Ankit Jain,Harihar Subramanyam,Mannat Singh,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于强化学习的通用"分布匹配"框架，用于控制语言模型输出中潜在类别属性的分布，而非仅仅最大化期望奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法（如GRPO）主要为最大化奖励，但合成数据生成、公平性约束、策略探索等任务需要控制输出分布。主流方法会降低输出多样性，而熵正则和采样温度效果有限且仅适用于token空间和均匀分布。

Method: 提出通用的RL分布匹配框架，可将模型输出的潜在类别属性分布匹配到指定目标分布；指出先前工作是L2散度的特例，并针对KL和JS散度设计奖励函数并提供理论论证。

Result: 通过数学推理和编程任务的实验验证了方法的有效性；实证表明GRPO会通过将策略概率集中于单一模式来降低输出多样性。

Conclusion: 分布匹配框架能有效控制模型输出分布，优于仅依赖熵正则或采样温度等受限方法，为多样化后训练目标提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used in language-model post-training to maximize rewards assigned to individual model outputs, such as scores from binary verifiers or reward models trained on human feedback. However, applications such as synthetic-data generation, fairness-related constraint satisfaction, and policy exploration require controlling the distribution of outputs across model generations rather than only maximizing expected reward. We propose a general RL-based framework for \textit{Distribution Matching} allowing matching the distribution of a latent categorical attribute of model outputs to a specified target distribution. Empirically, we demonstrate that dominant post-training recipes such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) reduce output diversity by concentrating policy probability towards a single mode. Entropy regularization and sampling temperature can improve the spread of the distribution but have constrained effectiveness, limited to apply only in token space and toward uniform distributions. We show that prior work in this area is a specific case of Distribution Matching involving the $L_2$ divergence. We then propose reward functions for other divergences such as KL and Jensen-Shannon and motivate them with theoretical justification. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a set of experiments involving mathematical reasoning and programming.

</details>


### [141] [STAR: Adaptive Spatial-Temporal Normalization for Unified Microservice Incident Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31645)
*Xinhua Miao,Linyu Zhu,Bowei Yang,Zhengong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR，一个空间-时间自适应表示学习框架，通过时间自适应归一化（TAN）和空间自适应归一化（SAN）应对非平稳时序动态与异构服务依赖结构，统一支持异常检测、故障分类与根因定位三类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微服务系统的自动化事件管理依赖多模态可观测数据（指标、日志、追踪）的鲁棒表示学习。现有自监督框架虽能统一建模异常检测（AD）、故障分类（FT）与根因定位（RCL），但在非平稳时序动态和异构服务依赖结构方面表现不佳。

Method: STAR框架包含两个紧密耦合的机制：时间自适应归一化（TAN），利用多尺度时间上下文对多变量时间序列进行动态归一化；空间自适应归一化（SAN），在服务依赖图上执行结构感知归一化。与将归一化视为静态或任务无关的先前方法不同，STAR将其形式化为可学习、上下文条件的变换，并与微服务系统内在属性对齐。所得自适应表示被整合进统一的自监督框架，实现端到端无监督支持AD、FT、RCL三类任务。

Result: 在两个真实世界微服务基准上的大量实验表明，STAR持续优于所有最先进基线，在全部三项任务上均取得显著且稳定的提升。

Conclusion: 结果凸显自适应归一化是一种原则性且有效的机制，可用于复杂软件系统中鲁棒的多模态表示学习。

Abstract: Automated incident management in large-scale microservice systems relies on learning robust representations from multimodal observability data, including metrics, logs, and traces. Although recent self-supervised frameworks enable unified modeling for anomaly detection (AD), failure triage (FT), and root cause localization (RCL), they often struggle with non-stationary temporal dynamics and heterogeneous service dependency structures. In this paper, we propose STAR, a Spatial-Temporal Adaptive Representation learning framework that explicitly addresses these challenges through adaptive normalizations. STAR introduces two tightly coupled mechanisms: Temporal Adaptive Normalization (TAN), which dynamically normalizes multivariate time series using multi-scale temporal context, and Spatial Adaptive Normalization (SAN), which performs structure-aware normalization over service dependency graphs. Unlike prior methods that treat normalization as static or task-agnostic, STAR formulates it as a learnable, context-conditioned transformation aligned with the intrinsic properties of microservice systems. The resulting adaptive representations are integrated into a unified self-supervised framework, enabling end-to-end unsupervised support for AD, FT, and RCL tasks. Extensive experiments on two real-world microservice benchmarks demonstrate that STAR consistently outperforms all state-of-the-art baselines, yielding significant and stable improvements across all three tasks. Our results highlight adaptive normalization as a principled and effective mechanism for robust multimodal representation learning in complex software systems.

</details>


### [142] [Typed Temporal Interaction Features for Simulation-Backed Forecasting of Open-Source Game Release Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31647)
*Shayma Alkobaisi,Anas Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot，一个基于类型化时序特征、带校准风险估计与工作量感知排序的质量事件预测流水线，用于开源游戏发布级 30 天内质量事件预测。但评估为仿真驱动而非真实经验结论，结果仅支持协议可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视频游戏的质量依赖代码、资产、配置、测试、贡献者与问题工作流之间的交互关系，而传统缺陷预测器通常将这些关系扁平化或直接忽略，因此需要能刻画此类类型化交互的预测方法。

Method: 构建 GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot：类型化时序特征流水线，包含校准风险估计与工作量感知排序。由于缺乏人工审核的发布时间与爆发标签，采用仿真评估：5 个随机种子世界，每个含 120 个项目与 24 个版本，使用项目不相交验证与未来跨项目测试，并开展消融及缺失性、漂移、引擎、项目规模、告警阈值、归因等分析。

Result: AUPRC 0.520、AUROC 0.673、Brier 0.207，20% 测试预算下工作量感知召回 29.68%；最接近的本地对比方法 Static-Hetero-Reimpl 达 0.522 AUPRC，-0.002 差异经 Holm 校正后不显著；推理每版本 0.023 毫秒。

Conclusion: 结果支持所提协议的可复现性，而非部署有效性。真实 OSSGameBench 版本重建、分层标签审核与官方图模型对比在期刊投稿或实际应用前仍属必要。

Abstract: Open-source video-game quality depends on inter-actions among code, assets, configuration, tests, contributors, and issue workflows, yet conventional defect predictors usually flatten or omit these relations. We investigate release-level forecasting of a quality incident within thirty days using GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot, a typed temporal feature pipeline with calibrated risk estimates and effort-aware ranking. Because the accessible OS-SGameBench materials do not provide manually audited release dates and outbreak labels, the executed evaluation is explicitly simulation-backed rather than an empirical claim about real games. Five seeded worlds each contain 120 projects and 24 releases, with project-disjoint validation and future cross-project testing. The pilot obtains an AUPRC of 0.520, AUROC of 0.673, Brier score of 0.207, and 29.68% effort-aware recall at a twenty-percent testing budget. Its closest local comparator, Static-Hetero-Reimpl, reaches 0.522 AUPRC; the -0.002 difference is not statistically significant after Holm correction. Inference requires 0.023 milliseconds per release in the measured environment. Ablations and controlled missingness, drift, engine, project-size, alert-threshold, and attribution analyses expose where typed interactions help and where they fail. Results support the reproducibility of the proposed protocol, not deployment effectiveness. Real OSSGameBench release reconstruction, stratified label audits, and official graph-model comparisons remain mandatory before journal submission or operational use in practice. This boundary protects research integrity and supports credible evaluation.

</details>


### [143] [Energy Vision--Language--Action: A Controlled Multimodal Benchmark for Intent-Conditioned Residential Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31648)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Oualid Doukhi,Ehsan Reihani,Saeed Sepasi,Deok Jin Lee,Moussa Ayyash,Reza Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVLA，一个面向住宅能源管理的受控多模态基准，将电池调度建模为"视觉+数值状态+语言目标→16步电池动作轨迹"的轨迹预测问题，包含约658万个多模态实例，实验发现语言通路对预测质量贡献显著，而RGB视觉通路未带来聚合误差优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要研究于机器人领域，即把视觉观测与语言指令映射为物理动作。本文希望将VLA范式拓展到住宅能源管理场景，并构建一个可控的多模态基准，用以研究语义意图与隐式上下文如何影响住宅能源-动作预测。为此需要解决现有数据缺乏受控多模态条件的问题，尤其是如何在不改变显式数值状态的情况下，仅通过视觉纹理编码隐藏运行模式。

Method: 提出 EVLA 基准：将电池调度表述为多模态轨迹预测，输入为 RGB 能量场表示、数值运行状态和自然语言目标，输出为由有限时域采样型参考生成器产生的16步电池动作轨迹。源窗口来自公开住宅电力负荷数据，电价、电池荷电状态、室内温度、时刻为基准生成的元数据。隐藏运行模式仅通过能量场纹理编码，实现视觉变化配对而显式数值状态固定。将439,203个保留基础窗口与3种隐藏模式、5种语言目标交叉，得到6,588,045个多模态实例。初步研究在固定子集（5,000训练/500验证/500测试）上评估36种配置、3个训练种子。

Result: 在 MobileNet 系列对比中，移除处理后的语言使轨迹均方误差从 0.3856 ± 0.0039 升至 0.8628 ± 0.0001；而移除视觉得到 0.3843 ± 0.0013，与完整模型相当。结果显示出强烈的模态使用不对称性：处理后的语言通路与预测质量强相关，而当前 RGB 通路未提供聚合误差优势。这些结果刻画的是固定试点子集与所执行协议，而非全基准训练。

Conclusion: EVLA 为研究语义意图与隐式上下文如何影响住宅能源-动作预测提供了受控实验环境，并揭示语言通路的显著贡献与视觉通路的局限。当前结论限于试点子集和特定协议，全基准训练有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are studied mainly in robotics, where visual observations and language instructions are mapped to physical actions. This paper introduces Energy Vision-Language-Action (EVLA), a controlled multimodal benchmark for intent-conditioned residential energy management. EVLA frames battery scheduling as a multimodal trajectory-prediction problem in which an RGB energy-field representation, a numerical operating state, and a natural-language objective are mapped to a 16-step battery-action trajectory generated by a finite-horizon sampling-based reference generator. Source windows are derived from public residential electrical-load data, while electricity price, battery state of charge, indoor temperature, and time of day are generated benchmark metadata. A hidden operating regime is encoded only through energy-field texture, enabling paired visual changes while the explicit numerical state is fixed. Crossing 439,203 retained base windows with three hidden regimes and five language objectives yields 6,588,045 multimodal instances. An initial study evaluates 36 configurations over three training seeds using fixed subsets of 5,000 training, 500 validation, and 500 test instances. In the MobileNet-family comparison, removing processed language increases trajectory mean-squared error from 0.3856 +/- 0.0039 to 0.8628 +/- 0.0001, whereas removing vision yields 0.3843 +/- 0.0013, comparable to the full model. The results show strong asymmetry in modality use: the processed-language pathway is strongly associated with prediction quality, while the current RGB pathway provides no aggregate error advantage. These results characterize the fixed pilot subset and executed protocol rather than full-benchmark training. EVLA provides a controlled setting for studying how semantic intent and latent context influence residential energy-action prediction.

</details>


### [144] [From Phase Transition to Systemic Failure: A Decoupled Analytics Framework for GNN Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31656)
*Shuai Yan,Dan Peng,Jie Li,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过构建可控合成同质图回归基准，分离研究标签噪声与特征分布偏移对GNN的影响；结果显示GNN对中等标签噪声相对鲁棒，而对严重分布偏移极为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量是GNN在实际图挖掘任务中可靠部署的主要瓶颈，其中标签噪声与特征分布偏移是两类常见但本质不同的退化来源，需在可控条件下分别研究其影响。

Method: 构建合成同质图回归基准，使标签噪声与分布偏移可独立操控；共设置41种配置、开展410次运行，评估代表性GNN模型在不同噪声与偏移条件下的表现。

Result: 1）在加性标签污染下，性能在较大噪声范围内保持相对稳定，仅在约50%噪声比例附近的过渡区后才急剧恶化；2）在极端特征分布偏移下，所有测试模型均严重退化，测试MSE增加48至316倍，相关性下降73%至89%。

Conclusion: 在该可控设定下，GNN对中等标签扰动远比严重分布失配更耐受；研究为理解数据质量如何影响基于GNN的图挖掘系统提供了受控经验基线，并为面向部署的监控与模型维护提供实践启示。

Abstract: Data quality is a major bottleneck for the reliable deployment of graph neural networks (GNNs) in real-world graph mining tasks. Among various sources of degradation, label noise and feature distribution shift (hereafter referred to as distribution shift) are two common yet fundamentally different challenges. To study their effects under controlled conditions, this paper constructs a synthetic homophilic graph regression benchmark in which the two factors can be manipulated separately. A total of 41 configurations and 410 runs are conducted to evaluate the behavior of representative GNN models under varying noise and shift conditions. The results show two distinct patterns. First, under additive label corruption, performance remains relatively stable over a broad range of noise settings and begins to deteriorate sharply only after an observed transition region around the 50 percent noise ratio. Second, under extreme feature distribution shift, all tested models suffer substantial degradation, with test MSE increasing by 48 times to 316 times and correlation dropping by 73 percent to 89 percent. These findings suggest that, in the present controlled setting, GNNs are considerably more tolerant to moderate label perturbation than to severe distribution mismatch. The study provides a controlled empirical baseline for understanding how data quality affects GNN-based graph mining systems and offers practical implications for deployment-oriented monitoring and model maintenance.

</details>


### [145] [Cross-Material Support Transfer for Core-Loss Prediction Under Waveform Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31659)
*Cong Yao,Chunye Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，功率磁材料损耗模型在训练数据稀薄的梯形/PWM波形上误差大，其根源是协变量偏移下的信息缺失而非类别不平衡；作者提出MIST方法，借助同类材料迁移支持信息，将材料D的p95误差从20.39%降至12.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率磁材料通常用正弦和三角波形进行表征，但实际变换器中的磁芯承受梯形和PWM型磁通轨迹，导致损耗模型必须在训练数据最稀薄的区域做预测。在MagNet Challenge最终测试中，材料D的梯形样本占测试集16.4%，却仅占训练集1.4%，形成极端的表征-部署失配，最佳提交方案的p95误差卡在15.9%，为五种材料中最差。论文的动机是揭示这一障碍的本质并找到解决方案。

Method: 论文首先通过受控实验反驳“类别不平衡”的解释：四种标准补救措施均失败，且将梯形样本比例提高到测试集水平反而进一步降低精度。随后提出“材料身份支持迁移”（MIST）方法：在所有五种挑战材料上联合训练一个仅2784参数的预测器；材料身份通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）引入；对稀缺材料的真实标签损失进行重加权；材料D不进行微调，以避免其无梯形训练集的偏差被重新引入。

Result: MIST将材料D的五种子p95误差从20.39±2.03%降至12.38±0.92%，将梯形类别的p95误差从37.4±8.8%降至15.16±1.69%，以最佳提交方案六分之一的参数量且无微调阶段即超越其性能；在匹配容量下移除材料身份会使误差膨胀一个数量级。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，稀缺材料的表征应与同类材料联合进行；缺失的信息支持可以从同类材料中“借用”，而无需在训练数据稀疏区域进行外推。

Abstract: Power magnetic materials are characterized on the sinusoidal and triangular waveforms that excitation hardware conveniently produces, whereas deployed converters expose cores to trapezoidal, PWM-shaped flux trajectories, so loss models must predict exactly where their training data are thinnest. The final test of the MagNet Challenge embeds a deliberately extreme instance of this characterization-deployment mismatch: for material D, trapezoids form 16.4% of the test set but only 1.4% of the training set. The 95th-percentile relative error, hereafter p95, of the best submission, built on sequential transfer learning, stalled at 15.9%, the worst among the five materials. This paper shows that the obstacle is missing information under covariate shift rather than class imbalance, and that the missing support can be borrowed from sibling materials instead of being extrapolated. Controlled experiments first refute the imbalance reading: four standard remedies fail, and raising the trapezoidal share to the test-set level degrades accuracy further. The proposed material-identity support transfer, MIST, then trains one 2784-parameter predictor jointly on all five challenge materials. Material identity enters through feature-wise linear modulation, or FiLM, the scarce material's true-label loss is reweighted, and material D receives no fine-tuning, so that the bias of its trapezoid-free training set is never re-installed. MIST lowers the five-seed material-D p95 from 20.39+/-2.03% to 12.38+/-0.92% and the trapezoidal-class p95 from 37.4+/-8.8% to 15.16+/-1.69%, surpassing the best submission with one-sixth of its parameters and no fine-tuning stage; removing material identity at matched capacity inflates the error by an order of magnitude. These results argue that scarce materials should be characterized jointly with their siblings.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond the Graph: An Adaptive Meta-Learner Fuses Explainability, Weather, and Dynamics for Robust Bus ETA Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31667)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYB(nm)自适应混合集成框架，动态融合五种互补模型（MST-AV、GDRN-DFT、KOOP-NET、FENN、MGCN），通过元学习器实现公交到站时间（ETA）预测，在加尔各答三条线路、4000余次行程数据上取得优于主流图神经网络的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的公交ETA预测对城市出行、乘客满意度和公交效率至关重要，但现有模型难以应对非线性时空动态、数据稀疏及天气等外部因素。作者希望借助多模型互补优势，克服单一模型的局限。

Method: 提出HYB(nm)自适应混合集成框架，动态融合五种互补模型：历史基线MST-AV、周期性时间模式分析GDRN-DFT、Koopman神经算子KOOP-NET、天气特征工程神经网络FENN、实时图卷积网络MGCN；并由一个感知实时上下文的元学习器进行动态加权融合。基于加尔各答三条公交线路的GPS与天气数据（超4000次行程）进行评估。

Result: HYB(2)展现出优越的鲁棒性，精度达到可与领先图神经网络相媲美的最先进水平，并在不同预测时长与运行条件下实现稳定性与效率的平衡。各组件发挥所长：MST-AV低延迟可解释、FENN抗天气干扰、MGCN捕捉网络动态。

Conclusion: 可扩展的HYB(k)架构为公交机构提供从经济型单模型到定制化高保真混合模型的灵活工具，推动预测性与公平的城市交通发展。

Abstract: Accurate bus Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is vital for urban mobility, passenger satisfaction, and transit efficiency, yet existing models falter against nonlinear spatiotemporal dynamics, data sparsity, and factors such as weather. This paper proposes HYB(nm), an adaptive hybrid ensemble framework that dynamically fuses five complementary models - a historical baseline (MST-AV), periodical temporal pattern analysis (GDRN-DFT), Koopman Neural Operators for nonlinear dynamics (KOOP-NET), weather-integrated feature-engineered neural networks (FENN), and real-time graph convolutional networks (MGCN) - via a meta-learner attuned to real-time context. Evaluated on GPS and weather data from three Kolkata bus routes comprising more than 4,000 trips, the framework leverages the individual strengths of its components (for example, the low-latency explainability of MST-AV, the weather resilience of FENN, and the network-dynamics capture of MGCN) to deliver the superior robustness of HYB(2), state-of-the-art accuracy rivalling leading graph neural networks, and balanced trade-offs in stability and efficiency across prediction horizons and operating conditions. The extensible HYB(k) architecture equips transit agencies with flexible tools, ranging from economical single models to tailored high-fidelity hybrids, advancing predictive, equitable urban transport.

</details>


### [147] [NanoForecast v0.5: Competitive Time Series Forecasting Through Training Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31669)
*Gautam Kishore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NanoForecast v0.5是一个6.5M参数的预测模型，在仅修复训练流程、不改架构的前提下，性能与31倍规模（200M）的TimesFM相当，且训练约12小时、推理无需GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望验证：通过修复训练流程中的缺陷（而非扩大模型规模或改进架构），小模型能否达到远超其规模的模型性能，从而降低时间序列预测的算力门槛。

Method: 保持v0.3架构不变，仅修复训练流程：修正损失范围（loss-scope）处理、张量形状对齐、扩大数据增强覆盖范围，并在相同数据和算力预算下重新训练，最终得到6.5M参数的v0.5模型。采用统一的MASE评估协议，与TimesFM（200M）、PatchTST（15M+）对比。

Result: MASE整体由3.030降至1.704，降低43.8%。在三个ETT数据集上全部超过TimesFM（MASE 0.676/1.110/0.287 vs. 0.705/1.360/0.545），汇率数据集也领先（4.317 vs. 4.383）；但在高基数电力与交通数据集上TimesFM明显领先。对比PatchTST则三个ETT数据集全胜。训练约12小时（单张T4，Google Colab），推理在Apple M4 CPU上完成、无需GPU。

Conclusion: 仅靠训练流程修复即可让小模型性能大幅提升并逼近/局部超越大模型，说明训练正确性对性能的影响可能被低估。代码、预训练权重与评估框架均以Apache 2.0开源。

Abstract: We present NanoForecast v0.5, a 6.5M-parameter forecaster that competes with models 31x its size (TimesFM, 200M parameters) after training pipeline fixes and no architecture change. Retraining the v0.3 architecture with corrected loss-scope handling, tensor shape alignment, and wider augmentation coverage cuts overall Mean Absolute Scaled Error by 43.8% under one fixed protocol (MASE 3.030 to 1.704) on the same data and compute budget. NanoForecast v0.5 beats TimesFM on all three ETT datasets (MASE 0.676/1.110/0.287 vs. 0.705/1.360/0.545) and on exchange rate (4.317 vs. 4.383); TimesFM keeps a clear lead on the high-cardinality electricity and traffic sets. Against PatchTST (15M+ parameters, official configuration), v0.5 wins all three ETT sets. Training takes about 12 hours on a single cloud GPU (NVIDIA T4, Google Colab) and inference needs no GPU (measurements in this paper are on an Apple M4 CPU). We release all code, pretrained checkpoints, and evaluation framework under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/eulogik/NanoForecast

</details>


### [148] [Active Causal Discovery Benchmark: Evaluating LLM Agents Under Budgeted Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31675)
*Sagar Deb,Devam Shah,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ACDB 基准，用 SC​​M 环境评测 LLM 智能体在预算受限干预下恢复因果图的能力，并给出三层评分与校准审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏统一、可复现的评测环境来判断 LLM 智能体能否在主动干预（active intervention）场景下真正恢复因果结构，因此需要一个基于结构因果模型（SCM）、带固定 API 与分层评分的基准来系统审计这一能力。

Method: ACDB 由线性高斯世界生成器 + 固定的 observe-intervene-submit 接口 + 三层评分契约组成，三层分别衡量骨架恢复、DAG 恢复与干预效率；在六级难度阶梯上对比 PC（贪婪主动定向启发式）、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4 以及结构盲随机 DAG 基线，并通过密度探针校准地板值。

Result: PC 加贪婪主动定向是最强非 oracle 方法（directed F1 42.7%，SHD 4.79），优于 Claude Sonnet 4.6（31.7%，7.25）与 GPT-5.4（22.9%，9.27）；结构盲随机基线达 23.6% directed F1，密度探针将地板降至 16.9%；最有信息量的诊断是精确率-召回率分解：PC 高精确率但欠干预，LLM 过度干预且精确率较低，统计工具访问常导致更多弃权而非有效干预。

Conclusion: 当前结果应被理解为基准审计与校准报告，而非 LLM 已解决主动因果发现的证据；密度探针结果推动 v1 校准，且工具访问未必带来增益。

Abstract: We introduce the Active Causal Discovery Benchmark (ACDB), an SCM-grounded environment for evaluating whether LLM agents recover causal graph structure from observations and budget-constrained hard interventions. ACDB pairs a linear-Gaussian world generator with a fixed observe-intervene-submit API and a three-layer scoring contract that separates skeleton recovery, DAG recovery, and intervention efficiency. On the current six-level ladder, PC with a greedy active orientation heuristic is the strongest non-oracle method (directed F1 42.7%, SHD 4.79), ahead of Claude Sonnet 4.6 raw active (31.7%, 7.25) and GPT-5.4 raw active (22.9%, 9.27). The most informative diagnostic is the precision-recall decomposition: PC under-commits with high precision, LLMs over-commit with lower precision, and statistical-tool access often increases abstention rather than useful intervention. A structure-blind random DAG baseline reaches 23.6% directed F1 on this dense v0 ladder; a density probe lowers this floor to 16.9%, motivating the v1 calibration pass. The current results should therefore be read as a benchmark audit and calibration report, not as evidence that current LLMs solve active causal discovery.

</details>


### [149] [When Keywords Drop but Classifiers Hold: Soft Refusals under KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31678)
*Kang Chen,Xiuze Zhou,Hong Chen,Yuanguo Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究KV cache压缩在保持任务精度时，是否仍能保持轻量关键词监控器与强refusal分类器之间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受内存限制，常采用KV cache压缩，而部署系统通常在生成后用关键词过滤或学习分类器来判定拒答。但是否“匹配压缩”在保持任务精度的同时，也保持关键词监控器与更强拒答分类器的一致性，尚不明确。

Method: 采用配对协议，在n=200条有害提示和长填充上下文中，每条提示在共享prefill后分别以完整保留和匹配eviction各回答一次；同一回复由关键词启发式、HarmBench Llama-2-13B分类器、辅助LLM评判及人工（针对分歧）共同打分。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B上，关键词拒答率从98.0%降至80.5%（McNemar p~1e-8），而分类器拒答率接近上限（99.0%-99.5%），MMLU精度不变（50.0%）；人类标签主要跟随分类器，符合软拒答。该差距并不普遍，在短填充和配对SnapKV下减弱。

Conclusion: 压缩下的安全审计应依赖多个与服务上下文匹配的评判器，而不能仅依赖关键词拒答率。

Abstract: KV cache compression is widely used for long context LLM inference under memory constraints, while deployed systems typically score refusals after generation with keyword filters or learned classifiers. Such monitors are intended to indicate whether a model declined a harmful request under the serving regime actually used. However, it remains unclear whether matched compression that preserves task accuracy also preserves agreement between lightweight lexical monitors and stronger refusal classifiers. We study this with a paired protocol on n=200 harmful prompts with a long filler context: each prompt is answered once under full retention and once under matched eviction after a shared prefill, and the same replies are scored by keyword heuristics, the HarmBench Llama-2-13B classifier, an auxiliary LLM judge, and humans on disagreements. On Qwen2.5-3B, keyword refusal falls from 98.0% to 80.5% (McNemar p~1e-8) while classifier refusal stays near ceiling (99.0%-99.5%) and MMLU accuracy is unchanged (50.0%); human labels predominantly follow the classifier, consistent with soft refusals. The gap is not universal and weakens under short fillers and paired SnapKV, so safety auditing under compression should rely on several judges matched to the serving context rather than on keyword rates alone.

</details>


### [150] [Does Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture Pretraining Help Time Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31680)
*Yutong Feng,Bowen Liao,See Kiong Ng,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对一种JEPA自监督预训练实例进行了迄今最大规模的跨架构评估，发现其收益高度依赖下游骨干网络，并非普遍可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA作为时间序列自监督预训练范式前景可观，但其效果证据混杂；现有研究多只测试单一骨干或少量架构，无法判断JEPA预训练到底是稳定提升还是高度依赖下游模型。

Method: 在九个骨干网络和十一个基准上，对该JEPA实例进行大规模评估，覆盖时序预测与时空预测两类任务，是迄今最广泛的JEPA跨架构评测。

Result: 该实例的收益在不同骨干间差异显著：在部分架构上稳定提升，在另一些架构上稳定退化，且在同一数据集上亦如此；该模式在两类任务家族中均成立。

Conclusion: 这种变异是该JEPA实例的普遍性质，而非数据集特定现象，实际选择骨干网络时应予以考虑。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA) have emerged as a promising self-supervised pretraining paradigm for time series, learning representations by predicting target embeddings in latent space rather than reconstructing raw signals. Yet evidence on their benefits remains mixed, and most studies test only a single backbone or a narrow set of architectures, leaving unclear whether JEPA pretraining is a reliable improvement or one that depends heavily on the downstream model. We address this gap through a large scale evaluation of one JEPA instantiation across nine backbones and eleven benchmarks spanning temporal and spatio-temporal forecasting, the most extensive cross architecture assessment of JEPA for time series to date. We find that the benefit of this instantiation varies sharply across backbones, producing consistent gains for some architectures and consistent degradation for others, even on the same dataset. This pattern holds across both task families, indicating the variability is a general property of this instantiation rather than a dataset specific artifact worth accounting for when choosing a backbone in practice.

</details>


### [151] [Autonomous Research Project Management as an Agent Skill: A Case Study in Exact Spectral Spatial Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31683)
*Alexander Chen,Jeffrey Meng,Bram Hoex,Tong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作展示了一个智能体技能在消费级硬件上端到端自主完成机器学习研究：对36×72网格上的NOAA Kaplan SST v2月异常场（2005年）评估FFT-based KRR求解器，由DeepSeek V4 Flash在DeepSeek Harness中编排，仅CPU（Apple M2 Pro）运行，并采用文件化epic/issue追踪支撑长周期状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验智能体能否自主开展完整科研闭环，并探讨自主研究的可信治理问题，主张科学可信度需要可审查状态、可证伪评审门与负面结果透明报告，呼吁以智能体可访问的结构化格式取代静态PDF稿件。

Method: 由DeepSeek V4 Flash在DeepSeek Harness中、通过智能体技能套件自主编排；将长周期状态解耦为基于文件的epic与issue追踪基底；在36×72规则空间网格上，用2005年逐月NOAA Kaplan SST v2异常场评估基于FFT的Kernel Ridge Regression求解器。

Result: 纯CPU硬件（Apple M2 Pro）上完成，求解器耗时78.7秒、峰值常驻内存1.57 GB；跨74个子智能体会话，出现两次假设评审门失败并回退至文献检索，修复了bootstrap索引缺陷，全程仅需四次离散人工干预。

Conclusion: 智能体技能可在消费级硬件上实现长周期、闭环的自主机器学习研究并具备一定科学韧性；研究治理应强调可审查状态、可证伪评审门与负面结果透明披露，并推动以结构化格式替代静态论文。

Abstract: This work presents an end-to-end demonstration of autonomous machine learning research conducted by an agent skill on consumer hardware. The demonstration evaluates an FFT-based Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR) solver for regular spatial grids using 2005 monthly NOAA Kaplan SST v2 anomaly fields on a $36 \times 72$ grid. This was autonomously executed by DeepSeek V4 Flash, orchestrated by our agent skill suite within DeepSeek Harness (DSH). Experiments were executed on CPU-only hardware (Apple M2 Pro; 78.7 s solver time, 1.57 GB peak RSS). Long-horizon state was decoupled into a file-based epic- and issue-tracking substrate. Across 74 sub-agent sessions, the agent demonstrated closed-loop scientific resilience: routing two failed hypothesis review gates back to literature retrieval, patching bootstrap indexing bugs, and executing with only four discrete human steering events. Finally, we reflect on autonomous research governance, arguing that scientific credibility requires inspectable state, falsifiable review gates, and transparent reporting of negative results, urging the machine learning community to favour agent-accessible structured formats over static PDF manuscripts.

</details>


### [152] [What does FFN compression change downstream? Same-state causal restoration in diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31685)
*Shaurya Omar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SSR方法，通过在压缩模型当前输入上恢复原始FFN并测量轨迹变化，直接衡量被移除FFN计算对扩散语言模型去噪轨迹的因果影响，并据此挑选恢复窗口以低成本挽回精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）迭代去噪计算开销大，需要激进压缩；但现有压缩目标多衡量压缩计算对原计算局部近似的好坏，局部误差无法反映哪些被移除的计算真正影响下游去噪轨迹。作者希望直接测量"同当前输入的闭环效应"，即恢复被移除的FFN后轨迹如何变化。

Method: 提出Same-State Causal Restoration（SSR）：在压缩模型到达的精确当前输入上恢复原始FFN，测量由此产生的轨迹变化，从而直接评估被移除FFN计算在去噪闭环中的作用。进一步用任务标签无关的校准，为留出推理冻结单一恢复窗口，并对比同预算的局部误差基线。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct与Dream-v0-Instruct-7B上，压缩侧状态在固定去噪阶段对下游效应的排序能力显著优于局部NMSE；受控干预显示修正结构比幅度更重要。激进的LLaDA压缩下，仅恢复四个transition即可挽回89.9%的损失精度，同时保留约36.8%的全模型MAC节省，并优于同等预算的局部误差基线。Dream实验表明恢复稠密行为与修复最终任务是两个不同的结果。

Conclusion: 局部近似误差不足以代表被移除计算的重要性，SSR通过同输入闭环测量能更准确识别关键计算；低预算恢复少数关键transition即可大幅挽回压缩损失，为DLM高效压缩提供了新视角，同时也提示"恢复原行为"与"修复任务表现"并非等价目标。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable flexible, parallel generation, but their iterative denoising remains computationally expensive, motivating increasingly aggressive compression. Existing compression objectives largely measure how well compressed computation approximates the original locally, but local error does not reveal which removed computations actually matter to the downstream denoising trajectory. We introduce Same-State Causal Restoration (SSR), which restores the original FFN on the exact current input reached by the compressed model and measures how the resulting trajectory changes. To our knowledge, this is the first direct measurement of the same-current-input closed-loop effect of removed FFN computation in DLM compression. Across LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-v0-Instruct-7B, compressed-side state ranks this downstream effect substantially better than local NMSE at fixed denoising phase, while controlled interventions show that correction structure matters beyond magnitude. Using task-label-free calibration, SSR freezes a single restoration window for held-out inference. Under aggressive LLaDA compression, restoring only four transitions recovers 89.9% of the lost accuracy while retaining an estimated 36.8% whole-model MAC saving and outperforming an equal-budget local-error baseline. Dream further shows that restoring dense behavior and repairing the final task are distinct outcomes.

</details>


### [153] [3-D Emissions Mapping and Social Cost Estimation for US Domestic Aviation at West Coast Hubs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31686)
*Hesam Shafiei Nia,Don MacKenzie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用物理信息自编码器重建美国西海岸枢纽航班轨迹，构建三维航空排放地图，并量化社会环境与机场周边健康影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空排放清单缺乏精确轨迹数据，难以支撑高分辨率的社会成本与健康影响评估。

Method: 基于2025年1月ADS-B轨迹记录，提出结合CNN、Bi-GRU与带跳跃连接3D CNN的编码器和TCN解码器的物理信息自编码器，与基线AE及三次样条插值对比；通过BADA性能表、ICAO EEDB排放指数及BFFM2高度修正映射排放，并量化各飞行阶段社会成本及枢纽50 km内LTO循环健康影响。

Result: 所提AE模型在5%至50%缺失率下均优于TCN-AE和三次样条插值；货币化显示NOx在直接气候强迫中呈小幅净冷却效应，但占货币化空气质量与健康成本的99.7%，而其质量不足CO2的0.4%，是健康成本的主导因素。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将AE轨迹重建、时空特征分离编码与基于高度的排放建模结合，生成区域航空排放清单，为美国国内航空环境影响评估与机场周边健康政策提供量化依据。

Abstract: Existing aviation emissions inventories lack accurate trajectory data for high-resolution social cost and health impact assessment. This paper develops a 3-D emissions map by reconstructing flight trajectories for US west coast hubs to estimate regional environmental and near-airport health impacts. A physics-informed autoencoder (AE) is applied to ADS-B trajectory records for January 2025 covering US west coast hubs. The encoder combines a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), a Bi-GRU, and a 3-D CNN with skip connection; the decoder is a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN). It is benchmarked against a baseline-AE and cubic spline interpolation. Emissions are mapped via EUROCONTROL Base of Aircraft Data (BADA) performance tables and ICAO Engine Emissions Databank (EEDB) emission indices, with altitude corrections via Boeing Fuel Flow Method 2 (BFFM2). Social costs are quantified for all flight phases, with health impacts assessed for Landing and Takeoff cycles within 50 km of each hub. The proposed AE model outperforms both a TCN-AE and cubic spline interpolation across 5% to 50% missing rates. Monetizing the emissions inventory shows NOx produces a small net cooling effect in direct climate forcing, while accounting for 99.7% of monetized air-quality and health cost despite being under 0.4% of CO2 by mass, making it the dominant health-cost driver. To our knowledge, this is among the first studies combining AE-based trajectory reconstruction with separate spatial-temporal feature encoding and altitude-based emissions modeling to produce a regional aviation emissions inventory for air quality, climate impact and population exposure. The resulting emissions map and social cost estimates provide quantitative context for environmental impact assessment and near-airport health policy evaluation for US domestic aviation.

</details>


### [154] [Same Probe, Different Numbers: Are Activation Probes Robust to Inference-Time Numerical Non-Determinism?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31796)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测量了推理配置（批大小与数值精度）变化对激活探针的影响，发现探针整体稳定，但聚合准确率会低估判定变化程度，建议监控评估应报告逐样本一致率并注明服务配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活探针常在一个推理配置下训练，却在部署时使用的任意批大小与数值精度下运行。由于GPU内核非批不变、浮点格式舍入不同，部署时看到的激活与训练时不同，需要量化这种差异带来的代价。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-3-4B上，针对批大小4/8/16与float32/bfloat16/float16组合，训练768个探针并做跨配置迁移评估，逐样本比较判定结果；同时考察解码阶段、激活扰动（相对L2）以及Cohen's kappa与AUROC等指标。

Result: 探针较为稳定：1,392次迁移中prompt准确率变化不超过0.47个百分点，仅0.076%判定改变；float32下仅变批大小，201,960个判定无一改变。解码阶段翻转率升至2.8%，但真实token一致的样本仅0.12-0.15%翻转，token不一致的样本翻转率达12.9%，说明主因是文本而非算术。bfloat16变批大小使2.1%样本的首个生成token翻转，到第20个token时25%落在不同token上。翻转对称，Cohen's kappa高于0.94，AUROC变化至多0.05。激活扰动的中位相对L2约1e-2（bfloat16），约为float16的8倍。探针能吸收这种扰动，而模型自身的下一token argmax不能。

Conclusion: 激活监控的稳健性评估应报告逐样本一致率而非聚合准确率，区分表征噪声与输入变化，并明确说明服务配置。

Abstract: Activation probes are increasingly used to monitor LLMs in deployment. A probe is typically trained under one inference configuration, then used under whatever batch size and numerical precision the serving stack uses. Because common GPU kernels are not batch-invariant and floating-point formats round differently, the activations seen at deployment are not the ones the probe was trained on. We measure what that costs for Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B and Gemma-3-4B across batch sizes 4, 8 and 16 and float32, bfloat16 and float16, training 768 probes on one configuration, evaluating each on every other, and comparing verdicts example by example. Probes are stable, but aggregate accuracy is the wrong instrument for showing it: it understates how many verdicts change by a factor of two to nine. At the prompt, accuracy never moves by more than 0.47 percentage points across 1,392 transfers and only 0.076% of verdicts change; under float32 with only the batch size varied, none of 201,960 verdicts change. During decoding the flip rate rises to 2.8%, but rows whose realised tokens matched flip in only 0.12-0.15% of cases, while rows whose tokens diverged flip in 12.9%: the cause is the text, not the arithmetic. A bfloat16 batch-size change flips the first generated token for 2.1% of rows and leaves 25% on different tokens by token 20. Flips are symmetric, Cohen's kappa stays above 0.94, and AUROC moves by at most 0.05 points. Underneath, activations move about as much as the format's rounding: a bfloat16 batch-size change perturbs them by a median relative L2 of 1e-2, roughly 8x the float16 figure. Probes absorb this; the model's own next-token argmax does not. Robustness evaluations of activation monitors should report per-example agreement rather than aggregate accuracy, separate representational noise from input change, and state the serving configuration.

</details>


### [155] [seq2cause: One Autoregressive Backbone, Four Causal Discovery Tasks in Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31801)
*Hugo Math*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架Seq2Cause，用预训练自回归模型作为摊销的条件独立性检验引擎，无需任务特定重训练即可覆盖事件序列因果推断的四个不同regime。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统（车辆、患者、基因组）产生离散事件序列，核心问题不是预测而是因果：哪些事件导致哪些事件、哪些导致更高层结果（故障、疾病）。该问题沿两个轴分解为依赖类型（事件→事件 vs 事件→结果）与因果范围（单序列 vs 群体），形成四个结构不同、可识别条件各异的regime。现有方法最多只处理其中一个，均假设多流结构与低词表，且无法扩展到数百以上事件类型。

Method: 提出Seq2Cause统一框架，以单一共享原语解决全部四个regime：将预训练自回归模型改造为摊销的条件独立性检验引擎，无需任务特定重训练。建立"预测—因果"对偶性：模型的超额交叉熵同时约束四个regime下的因果识别误差，使下一词预测的每次改进都免费收紧因果保证。

Result: 在非线性SCM（词表达8,000种类型）与真实车辆诊断日志（29K事件类型、474个故障结果）上，Seq2Cause是首个以单一冻结骨干在规模上填充全部四个regime的方法；现有方法在此设定下或不适用、或不准确、或计算不可行。

Conclusion: 用一个冻结的预训练自回归模型即可在规模化事件序列上统一实现四类因果推断任务，并通过预测—因果对偶性把预测性能直接转化为因果识别保证，突破现有方法在词表规模与任务覆盖上的限制。

Abstract: Complex systems such as vehicles, patients, or genomes emit discrete event sequences whose operative question is causal, not predictive: which events cause which other events, and which cause higher-level outcomes such as failures or diseases? This question decomposes along two axes -- dependency type (event $\to$ event vs.\ event $\to$ outcome) and causal scope (single sequence vs.\ population) -- yielding four structurally distinct regimes with different identifiability conditions. No existing method addresses more than one, because all assume multi-stream structure with low vocabulary, and none scales beyond a few hundred event types.
  We present \textsc{Seq2Cause}, a unified framework that resolves all four regimes through a single shared primitive: a pretrained autoregressive model repurposed as an amortized conditional independence testing engine requiring no task-specific retraining. We establish a prediction--causality duality: the model's excess cross-entropy simultaneously bounds causal identification error across all four regimes, so that every improvement in next-token prediction tightens causal guarantees for free.
  On nonlinear SCMs (vocabularies up to $8{,}000$ types) and real-world vehicle diagnostic logs ($29$K event types, $474$ failure outcomes), \textsc{Seq2Cause} is the first method to populate all four regimes at scale with a single frozen backbone. Existing methods are either inapplicable, inaccurate, or computationally intractable in this setting.

</details>


### [156] [Medium-Term Multi-Resolution Electric Load Forecasting using Economic Data and Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31806)
*Lindas Eloi,Goude Yannig,Ciais Philippe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究社会经济数据对中期电力负荷预测（1–48个月、月度和日度分辨率）的影响，采用表格基础模型与集成特征选择流程，在法国20年数据集上验证：精选经济协变量可使预测精度提升约20%，MAPE分别限制在4%（月）和5%（日）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期负荷预测（MTLF，数月到数年）介于长期（LTLF，依赖经济预测与电器发展情景）与短期（STLF，受天气、日历、自回归驱动）之间，既需要外推能力又需要变异性建模。但目前尚不清楚MTLF能否从经济指标中获益，尤其在何种预测期与分辨率下获益。

Method: 构建覆盖法国20年电力负荷、气象变量及经济特征（消费者价格与生产指数、电动汽车数量、就业等）的数据集；采用新的特征选择流程，生成具有多样特征子集的专家模型集成；使用表格基础模型（FM）在1个月至48个月的预测期、月度与日度分辨率下进行预测；并通过特征重要性与上下文重要性开展可解释性分析。

Result: 在2015–2025年间，精选经济协变量使预测技能相比基线提升约20%，且该提升在各类预测期与分辨率下保持稳定；月度粒度MAPE限制在4%，日度粒度限制在5%。可解释性分析显示：基础模型所利用的上下文有限，暗示可缩减上下文以节省计算；经济预测因子的特征重要性随预测期延长而增加。

Conclusion: 在MTLF中纳入经济数据有望弥合与LTLF之间的差距，从而实现无缝衔接的负荷预测。

Abstract: Accurate medium-term, from a few months to a few years, electricity load forecasts are crucial for informed decision-making in power plant maintenance scheduling, load dispatch and price settlement. Being comprised between Long-Term Load Forecasting (LTLF) which uses mostly economic projections and appliances development scenarios, and Short-Term Load Forecasting (STLF) driven by weather, calendar and autoregressive patterns, Medium-Term Load Forecasting (MTLF) requires both extrapolation capabilities and variability modeling. Yet, it remains unclear if MTLF can benefit from economic indicators, and especially at which forecast horizon and resolution. To address these challenges we investigated the impact of socioeconomic data on predictions issued 1 month and up to 48 months in advance for France at monthly and daily resolution using a tabular Foundation Model (FM). A dataset covering 20 years of observations of electricity load, weather variables and economic features such as consumer price and production indices, electric vehicle counts or employment is created for the study. To avoid noisy data, we used a new feature selection pipeline, creating ensemble of expert models with diverse feature subsets, to demonstrate that selected economic covariates improve forecast skill by 20% over 2015-2025. This enhancement is steady across lead times and resolutions limiting the Mean Absolute Percentage Error to 4% for monthly granularity and 5% for daily granularity. Explainability of the models is investigated through feature and context importance. Results showed that the FM is limited in the context it leverages pointing towards potential computational savings with a reduced context, while feature importance of economic predictors grows with the forecast horizon. This suggests that including economic data in MTLF could bridge the gap with LTLF leading to seamless forecasts.

</details>


### [157] [Relational Compression: A Framework for Relational Fidelity in Constrained Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31816)
*Yaniv Shulman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"关系压缩"形式化为源-描述-重建框架下以关系结构本身作为保真内容的问题，通过有限码字碰撞给出具体实现，并揭示其与聚类重建、图割等目标的精确对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当所关注的信息存在于元素之间的关系而非元素本身时，传统压缩的保真目标不再适用。作者希望为图摘要、谱稀疏化、相似性保持表示、关系蒸馏等分散方法提供一个统一的关系压缩框架。

Method: 在经典源-描述-重建意义下形式化关系压缩，每个实例指定源关系、保留描述、重建/评估关系、保真准则与受限资源；在此接口下统一梳理图摘要、谱稀疏化、相似性保持表示与关系蒸馏，并显式保留各自的重建与资源假设。进一步提出有限码字碰撞作为具体实现，用同码字概率构建关系几何，将成对对齐与分离关联到聚合Rényi-2占用率与类别分配的球面几何。

Result: 同码字概率导出的关系几何与平方欧氏质心重建及归一化图关联/割目标存在精确对应；图与图像研究展示了有限码字族内的互补路径：图与教师定义的关系要求直接作用于相等或碰撞，而重建通过联合解码器起作用。

Conclusion: 不同关系要求可在统一的受限表示框架下被形式化与检验，有限码字碰撞为关系压缩提供了可行的具体实现与目标对应关系。

Abstract: What should a compressed representation preserve when the information of interest lies in relationships among elements rather than in the elements themselves? We formulate relational compression in the classical source-description-reconstruction sense, but with relational structure itself as the fidelity-bearing content. Each instance specifies the source relation, retained description, reconstructed or evaluated relation, fidelity criterion, and constrained resource. We use this interface to situate selected methods from graph summarization, spectral sparsification, similarity-preserving representation, and relational distillation within a common formulation while keeping their different reconstruction and resource assumptions explicit. We develop finite-codeword collision as one concrete realization. Same-codeword probability yields a relational geometry linking pair-specific alignment and separation to aggregate Rényi-2 occupancy and the spherical geometry of categorical assignments, with exact objective correspondences to squared-Euclidean centroid reconstruction and normalized graph association and cut. Graph and image studies illustrate complementary routes within the finite-codeword family: graph- and teacher-defined relational requirements act directly on equality or collision, while reconstruction acts through a joint decoder. Together, these results illustrate how distinct relational requirements can be formulated and tested within a common constrained-representation framework.

</details>


### [158] [Averaged Mirror Descent and Dual Gradient Methods: Convergent Algorithms for Entropic Gromov-Wasserstein Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31848)
*Joanna Mark,Gabriel Rioux,Riccardo Passegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD) 方法，通过对连续镜像下降步骤取平均，证明了其在任意代价下的收敛性，并分析了固定步长对偶梯度法的收敛性，解决了 EGW 求解器收敛性受限于特定代价类的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) 距离用于度量度量测度空间之间的差异并识别最优对齐，但计算开销大。实践中常用熵正则化得到 Entropic GW (EGW) 问题，最流行的求解器是镜像下降 (MD)，但其收敛性仅在受限代价类下得到证明；而近期提出的对偶梯度法虽适用于一般代价，却需要依赖正则化参数的步长选择。因此需要解决这两个问题。

Method: 论文引入 Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD)，对连续的 MD 步骤进行平均，并证明其对任意代价收敛。同时，论文证明固定步长的对偶梯度法在更复杂迭代下也能对任意代价收敛。两种方法均考虑了实践中不可避免的非精确迭代。

Result: 论文在不同设置下比较了这些方法的经验性能，特别表明 AMD 和对偶梯度法在经典 MD 失败的例子上均能收敛。

Conclusion: AMD 与固定步长对偶梯度法在任意代价下具有收敛保证，并在经典 MD 失效的情形下仍能收敛，为 EGW 问题提供了更稳健的求解方案。

Abstract: The Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distance measures the discrepancy between metric measure (mm) spaces and identifies optimal alignments between them based solely on their intrinsic structure. Since it identifies isomorphic mm spaces, it provides a natural notion of distance for heterogeneous datasets which may admit isomorphic representations. In order to accelerate computation of GW distances, many practitioners employ entropic regularization to obtain an Entropic GW (EGW) problem. The most popular EGW solver is the Mirror Descent (MD) algorithm, which reduces EGW computations to an iterative process where an entropic optimal transport (EOT) problem is solved at each iteration. Despite its widespread use, the convergence of MD for this problem has only been established for restricted classes of costs. On the other hand, a recently proposed dual gradient method is available for general costs, but requires a choice of step size which depends on the regularization parameter. To address these two issues, we introduce Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD), which averages consecutive MD steps, and prove its convergence for arbitrary costs. Then, we establish that the dual gradient method with a fixed step size also converges for arbitrary costs at the cost of a more complicated iteration. In both cases, we also account for inexact iterations which are inescapable in practice. We compare the empirical performance of these methods across various settings and, in particular, show that AMD and the dual gradient method both converge on an example where classical MD fails.

</details>


### [159] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Equity Trading: Benchmarking Actor-Critic Methods with Forward Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31870)
*Bicheng Wang,Xinyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了五种深度强化学习actor-critic方法（A2C、PPO、DDPG、TD3、SAC）与监督式价格预测基线在股票交易中的表现，发现DDPG收益最高但风险也最大，TD3和SAC在风险收益间更均衡，前瞻性再训练对部分方法有改善作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票市场噪声大、非平稳且仅部分可预测，持续盈利的交易非常困难，因此需要系统评估不同深度强化学习方法在端到端学习交易动作上的实际效果，并与传统监督预测方法进行权衡比较。

Method: 使用2000-2020年20只标普500大盘股日度数据，加入趋势跟踪技术指标和对数min-max缩放；训练期为2000-2018，回测期为2019-2020。对比A2C、PPO、DDPG、TD3、SAC五种DRL actor-critic方法与监督式价格预测基线，并设置两种评估模式：一次性训练和前瞻性再训练（在每个连续测试窗口前用所有可用数据重新训练）。

Result: DDPG年化收益率最高（55.5%）、夏普比率1.38、alpha 0.22，但市场beta也最高（1.24）。TD3和SAC风险收益更均衡，夏普比率分别为1.37和1.33，最大回撤约25%。前瞻性再训练改善A2C、PPO和SAC，TD3基本不变，DDPG年化收益从55.5%降至29.8%，说明TD3对超参数更稳健。预测基线最大回撤最小（9.6%），beta最低（0.31）。

Conclusion: 端到端深度强化学习能带来更高收益，但伴随更高风险；而预测驱动策略风险更低。二者之间存在明显的收益与风险权衡，TD3和SAC在稳健性和风险收益平衡上表现更优。

Abstract: Consistently profitable trading is difficult because equity markets are noisy, non-stationary, and only partially predictable from historical data. We benchmark five deep reinforcement learning (DRL) actor-critic methods: A2C, PPO, DDPG, TD3, and SAC, that learn trading actions end-to-end from market states, and compare them with a supervised price-forecasting baseline. Using daily data for 20 large-capitalization S&P 500 stocks from 2000 to 2020, enriched with trend-following technical indicators and log min-max scaling, we train on 2000-2018 and backtest on 2019-2020. Each agent is evaluated both when trained once and under forward retraining, in which it is retrained on all data available before each successive test window. DDPG achieves the highest annual return (55.5%), Sharpe ratio (1.38), and alpha (0.22), but also the highest market beta (1.24). TD3 and SAC offer a better risk-return balance, with Sharpe ratios of 1.37 and 1.33 and maximum drawdowns of about 25%. Forward retraining improves A2C, PPO, and SAC, leaves TD3 essentially unchanged, and reduces DDPG's annual return from 55.5% to 29.8%, consistent with TD3's greater robustness to hyperparameters. The forecasting baseline has the smallest maximum drawdown (9.6%) and the lowest beta (0.31), underscoring a trade-off between the higher returns of end-to-end DRL and the lower risk of forecast-driven strategies.

</details>


### [160] [TemporalGraphLLM: Temporal Graph Neural Networks with Large Language Models for Dynamic Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31881)
*Moran Beladev,Or Eitan,Gilad Katz,Lior Rokach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TemporalGraphLLM框架，将时序图神经网络与LLM结合以处理动态文本属性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态文本属性图（DTAGs）中图结构与文本语义随时间演化。现有TGNN擅长建模动态节点关系但缺乏文本上下文推理能力，LLM擅长文本理解却难以进行时序下的结构化图推理，二者难以联合建模。

Method: 提出TemporalGraphLLM框架，可集成任意时序GNN与LLM。通过图-时间感知的指令微调，并采用新的时序GNN注入方式，用图嵌入替代专门的附加token。

Result: 在边分类、链接预测和基于边的文本生成任务上取得最先进性能，并在真实动态图数据集上得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了LLM与TGNN的协同潜力，为演化图上的学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Dynamic text-attributed graphs (DTAGs), where nodes, edges, and textual attributes evolve over time, are crucial in applications such as social networks, citation graphs, and knowledge graphs. However, existing approaches struggle to jointly model the temporal evolution of graph structures and the semantic richness of textual attributes. While Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) capture evolving node relationships, they often lack contextual text reasoning. Conversely, Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in textual understanding but struggle with structured graph reasoning in temporal settings. To bridge this gap, we propose TemporalGraphLLM, a novel framework that can integrate any temporal GNN with an LLM for enhanced reasoning in DTAGs. Our approach fine-tunes LLMs using graph-time-aware instruction tuning and novel temporal GNNs injection to replace dedicated added tokens with graph embeddings. TemporalGraphLLM effectively leverages pretrained TGNNs within an LLM framework to achieve state-of-the-art performance on edge classification, link prediction, and edge-based text generation tasks. Extensive evaluation on real-world dynamic graph datasets demonstrates state-of-the-art performance. Our findings highlight the synergistic potential of LLMs and TGNNs, opening new directions for learning on evolving graphs.

</details>


### [161] [FARE: Deep Reinforcement Learning For Fair Exposure Constrained Uncertainty Aware Financial Content Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31890)
*Arundeep Chinta,Lucas Vinh Tran,Jay Katukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FARE模块，将SOV约束排序建模为深度强化学习问题，在金融内容分发中兼顾公平曝光与点击率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务内容个性化需保证多样化产品的公平曝光，避免高CTR内容主导的"富者愈富"现象。SOV约束要求各内容类别在头部位置达到目标曝光比例，但现有CTR模型重排序层未考虑CTR预测不确定性。

Method: 将SOV约束排序类比算法金融中的约束交易执行，建模为深度强化学习问题；将CTR预测不确定性显式纳入智能体状态空间与策略设计，使高不确定性预测可做更大排序调整。提出FARE模块化不确定性感知执行层，含不确定性加权比例控制策略(FARE-PC)与学习式神经策略(FARE-ES、FARE-PPO)。

Result: 不确定性感知方法可显著降低SOV相对公平目标的偏差，同时最小化参与度损失。在合成数据上无梯度进化策略优于策略梯度方法，而在KuaiRand-Pure上结论反转。

Conclusion: FARE无需重训底层CTR模型即可将任意黑盒CTR预测转化为SOV公平排序，验证了不确定性感知在公平排序中的有效性，并揭示了不同策略方法的场景依赖性。

Abstract: Content personalization systems in financial services must ensure fair exposure across diverse offerings-a requirement driven by contractual obligations and the need to prevent "rich-get-richer" dynamics where content with high click-through rate (CTR) dominates while other relevant products receive minimal visibility. Share of Voice (SOV) constraints, which guarantee each content category a target fraction of top-position exposure, address this by promoting product diversity and balanced user discovery. While re-ranking layers atop CTR models are common in practice, we propose two key novelties: (1) framing SOV-constrained ranking as a deep reinforcement learning problem analogous to constrained trade execution in algorithmic finance, and (2) explicitly incorporating CTR prediction uncertainty into the agent's state space and policy design-enabling larger ranking adjustments for high-uncertainty predictions where deviation from CTR-optimal ordering is less costly. We introduce FARE (Fair Ranking Executor), a modular uncertainty-aware execution layer that translates any black-box CTR model's predictions into SOV-fair rankings without retraining the underlying model. Our uncertainty-weighted proportional control policy (FARE-PC) and learned neural policies (FARE-ES, FARE-PPO) demonstrate that uncertainty-aware approaches can substantially reduce SOV deviation from fairness targets while minimizing engagement loss, with gradient-free evolution strategies outperforming policy gradient methods on synthetic data and the ordering reversing on KuaiRand-Pure.

</details>


### [162] [DOHF: Online Diffusion Fine-tuning with Doob's $h$-transform Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31882)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Diffusion Online h-guidance Fine-tuning（DOHF），将 Doob's h-transform 转化为可实践的在线训练算法，用于基于奖励的扩散模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励的扩散微调在理想结果稀少或条件修正估计成本高昂时面临实际挑战，需要一种无需额外网络评估、能适应黑盒与非可微奖励的方法。

Method: DOHF 为生成样本分配最优性权重，在当前的 rollout 策略下估计归一化的局部修正 ∇log h，并将其直接蒸馏进生成模型；同时从统一的 h-transform 视角刻画群体最优的 DiffusionNFT 更新以及各类无分类器引导方法。

Result: 框架可兼容黑盒和非可微奖励且无需额外网络评估，并在三种实验场景下展现出改进的对齐效果。

Conclusion: 通过低成本估计与迭代蒸馏来适配概率条件化，能够改善统计采样与视觉生成等任务中的生成式学习。

Abstract: Reward-based diffusion fine-tuning faces practical challenges when desirable outcomes are rare or conditioning corrections are costly to estimate. In this work, we propose Diffusion Online $h$-guidance Fine-tuning (DOHF), which turns Doob's $h$-transform into a practical online training algorithm. DOHF assigns optimality weights to generated samples, estimates the normalized local correction $\nabla\log h$ under the current rollout policy, and distills it directly into the generative model. Theoretically, we characterize the population-optimal DiffusionNFT update as well as the various classfier free guidance methods through a unified $h$-transform perspective. Methodologically, our framework accommodates black-box and non-differentiable rewards without additional network evaluations. We further show improved alignments under three empirical scenarios. Our work demonstrates how adapting probabilistic conditioning through inexpensive estimation and iterative distillation can improve generative learning across statistical sampling and visual generation.

</details>


### [163] [Rethinking Cross-Channel Importance in Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32187)
*Yong-Hoon Choi,Kwang-Hyun Park,Youngjin Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示跨通道重要性存在"相关≠有用≠被使用"的层级差异，并证明这种重要性随预测步长（future offset）显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测中常把"统计相关""预测有用""被模型实际使用"视为同一重要性概念，但作者认为三者未必一致，需要实证检验并以机制与问题视角重新定义跨通道重要性。

Method: 通过受控Ridge预测器进行步长自适应的源选择实验；将选定集信号迁移到匹配的非线性预测器；对iTransformer施加同样的步长特定源逻辑；用功能干预（functional interventions）比较iTransformer、TimesNet与跨通道TimeMixer的源依赖排序；并做有界的后验支持实验。

Result: 步长自适应源选择在32个数据集-预测长度条件中21个提升Ridge，平均增益5.16%，信号可迁移至非线性预测器；但施加于iTransformer仅20个中11个获胜，平均增益0.208%，且与受控增益对齐度低；功能干预显示强预测器确实使用跨通道信息，但其源依赖排序与受控效用及模型间彼此一致性都较低；有界后验支持在16个条件中12个改善冻结的通道独立预测器，聚合bootstrap区间为正。

Conclusion: 跨通道重要性必须相对于预测机制与所定义的问题来解释：相关、有用与实际被使用是三个不同概念。

Abstract: Cross-channel modeling is central to multivariate time-series forecasting, yet channels that are statistically related, predictively useful, and actually used by a trained forecaster are often treated as if they defined the same notion of importance. We show that they need not coincide. Cross-channel dependency structures change substantially across future offsets, and horizon-adaptive source selection improves a controlled Ridge predictor in 21 of 32 dataset--prediction-length conditions, with a mean gain of $5.16\%$. This selected-set signal also transfers to a matched nonlinear predictor. Yet imposing the same horizon-specific source logic on iTransformer yields only 11 of 20 wins and a mean gain of $0.208\%$, with little alignment between controlled and neural gains. Functional interventions further show that strong forecasters use cross-channel information, while their source-reliance rankings agree little with controlled utility or with one another across iTransformer, TimesNet, and a cross-channel TimeMixer. As a constructive consequence, bounded post-hoc support improves a frozen channel-independent forecaster in 12 of 16 dataset--horizon conditions, with a positive aggregate bootstrap interval. Cross-channel importance should therefore be interpreted relative to the forecasting mechanism and question that define it: related $\neq$ useful $\neq$ used.

</details>


### [164] [DP-Rec: Towards Dynamic Patching for Efficient Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32215)
*Dwipam Katariya,Thomas Caputo,Akshat Shreemali,Juan Manuel Origgi,Nikita Seleznev,Pranab Mohanty,Kalanand Mishra,Nam Nguyen,James Montgomery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-Rec，一种面向序列推荐的动态潜在分块架构。它借鉴Byte Latent Transformer思想，用对比熵惊讶度将交互序列切分为信息量不同的片段，再由轻量编码器压缩为动态潜在行为向量，交给更大的潜在Transformer处理并解码做下一项预测，从而在受限算力下实现长序列的高效高精度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer推荐模型以固定速率运算，对用户历史中每个物品分配相近计算量，忽略其信息含量差异。这导致长序列计算开销过高、且对行为噪声更敏感。实践中常用的有损序列压缩、分阶段建模或截断虽降低开销，却限制了模型在推理时利用完整长历史上下文的能力。

Method: DP-Rec从物品级建模转向分块级建模：使用对比熵惊讶度（contrastive entropy surprise）分割交互序列，识别信息量高的行为边界；由轻量分块编码器将这些带时序上下文的片段压缩为数量更少的动态潜在行为向量；再由更大的潜在Transformer处理，并解码用于下一项预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，在受限计算预算下，DP-Rec能够有效扩展到长序列，并在效率—精度权衡上优于非压缩基线和固定大小压缩基线。

Conclusion: DP-Rec通过动态潜在分块建模，在保持长历史完整上下文的同时降低计算开销，为序列推荐提供了更优的效率与精度平衡方案。

Abstract: Transformers have redefined sequential recommendation by effectively modeling dynamic user behaviors and long-range dependencies. However, they remain inherently inefficient: standard architectures operate at a fixed rate, allocating comparable computation to every item in a user's history regardless of its information content. This leads to prohibitive computational overhead on long sequences and increased sensitivity to behavioral noise. To address this, practitioners often resort to lossy sequence compression, staged modeling, or truncation. This limits the model's ability to leverage the full context of long histories during inference. Inspired by the recent success of Byte Latent Transformer, we propose DP-Rec, a dynamic latent patching architecture for recommendation. DP-Rec shifts from item-level modeling to patch-level modeling by segmenting interaction sequences using contrastive entropy surprise to identify informative behavioral boundaries. A lightweight patch encoder compresses these temporally contextualized segments into a reduced set of dynamic latent behavior vectors, which are then processed by a larger latent transformer and decoded for next-item prediction. Extensive experiments show that, under constrained computational budgets, DP-Rec scales effectively to long sequences and achieves a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off over both non-compressed and fixed-size compression baselines.

</details>


### [165] [CyberWorld: World Models for Sample-Efficient Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31893)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Raheeb Hassan,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CyberWorld，一个 Dreamer 风格的世界模型框架，用于自主网络防御，通过从向量、图、文本和多模态表示中学习潜在网络动态，实现了比无模型 PPO 高得多的样本效率和良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络防御方法多为无模型强化学习，依赖大量环境交互，样本效率低。世界模型在其他领域已展现样本效率优势，但将其扩展到网络安全领域存在一个根本问题：在网络世界模型中，"世界"应由什么构成。因此本文旨在探索网络动态世界建模的可行性及合适的表示形式。

Method: 提出 CyberWorld，一种 Dreamer 风格的世界建模框架，从被防御网络的向量、图、文本和多模态四种表示中学习潜在网络动态，并基于想象轨迹优化策略。系统比较了不同表示选择下的性能与鲁棒性。

Result: 在 CyberWheel 全部四种可评分攻击策略上，基于图的 CyberWorld 变体在 3.6k–15.8k 环境步后即超过策略无关的对照组，而无模型 PPO 需要数百万步。图结构在依赖拓扑的攻击下鲁棒性更强，更简单表示在整体性能上仍有竞争力。在成功运行中，所需回合数随网络规模从 15 增至 100 台主机基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 学习到的网络动态可作为自主防御的样本高效且可扩展的基础，世界表示形式是影响鲁棒性与可扩展性的核心设计维度。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has become a prominent approach to autonomous cyber defense. Existing methods are predominantly model-free and consequently require extensive environment interaction. World models provide an alternative by learning predictive dynamics and optimizing policies through imagined trajectories, yielding substantial gains in sample efficiency in robotics and embodied control. Extending this paradigm to cybersecurity raises a fundamental question: what should constitute the "world" in a cyber world model? We introduce CyberWorld, a Dreamer-style world modeling framework that learns latent cyber dynamics from vector, graph, textual, and multimodal representations of the defended network. Across all four scoreable CyberWheel attack strategies, the graph-based CyberWorld variant exceeds a strategy-agnostic control after 3.6k-15.8k environment steps, compared with millions of steps required by model-free PPO. Across representation choices, graph structure provides greater robustness under topology-dependent attacks, while simpler representations remain competitive in overall performance. Among successful runs, the number of episodes required to reach the control remains approximately constant as network size increases from 15 to 100 hosts. These results establish learned cyber dynamics as a sample-efficient and scalable basis for autonomous defense, and identify world representation as a central design axis for robustness and scalability.

</details>


### [166] [Graph Memory: Spectral Associative Memory via Dirichlet Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32365)
*Zhaoyang Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出谱稠密联想记忆（spectral dense associative memory），将经典向量联想记忆推广到图结构数据，通过狄利克雷能量与谱范数距离诱导的log-sum-exp能量实现检索，并证明指数存储容量与指数衰减检索误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密联想记忆主要针对向量值模式，而许多现代机器学习问题本质上是图结构的，需要针对关系模式、图扩散几何、社区结构和图归纳偏置的记忆机制，因此需要将联想记忆扩展到图数据。

Method: 提出谱稠密联想记忆框架。检索通过狄利克雷能量结合谱范数距离诱导的log-sum-exp能量实现，对存储的拉普拉斯矩阵进行softmax加权平均，且结果仍是有效的图拉普拉斯矩阵。

Result: 证明了指数级存储容量和指数衰减的检索误差；建立了对图学习关键谱量（特征值、特征空间、扩散算子）的理论保证。在合成图数据、真实航空网络、蛋白质构象数据和可穿戴传感器数据上验证了稳健的图检索并保持图几何结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为图结构数据提供了新的联想记忆范式，将稠密联想记忆与现代图学习和生成式AI相连接。

Abstract: Dense associative memories have traditionally focused on storing and retrieving vector-valued patterns. Many modern machine learning problems, however, are naturally graph-structured, requiring memory mechanisms for relational patterns, graph diffusion geometries, community structures, and graph-based inductive biases. We propose a spectral dense associative memory for storage and retrieval of graph data, extending the classical vector-valued memories. Retrieval is performed through a log-sum-exp energy induced by Dirichlet energy with spectral norm distances, producing a softmax-weighted average of the stored Laplacians that remains a valid graph Laplacian. We prove exponential storage capacity and exponentially decaying retrieval error. Beyond graph retrieval, we establish theoretical guarantees for spectral quantities central to graph learning, including eigenvalues, eigenspaces, and diffusion operators. Experiments on synthetic graph data, real-world airline network, protein conformation data and wearable sensor data demonstrate robust graph retrieval while preserving the graph geometry of the data. Our framework provides a new associative memory paradigm for graph-structured data and bridges dense associative memory with modern graph learning and generative AI.

</details>


### [167] [Understanding the Synergy between SFT, RLVR, and OPD in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31900)
*Emre Can Acikgoz,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Srijan Bansal,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大模型后训练中SFT、RLVR与在线策略蒸馏（OPD）组成的多阶段流水线，指出各阶段并非独立——能提升当前模型的阶段可能削弱下一阶段效果。作者通过Qwen3在数学与科学推理上的受控实验，揭示了OPD有效性取决于学生-教师兼容性而非教师规模，并提出教师适配与学生预热可显著提升OPD精度，同时发现OPD为下游RLVR提供比SFT更好的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM后训练常将SFT、RLVR和OPD组合成多阶段流水线，但这些阶段通常在设计和评估时被孤立看待，忽视了阶段间的相互作用。作者希望揭示这种组合的后果，即某个提升当前模型的阶段可能降低下一阶段的训练效果。

Method: 使用Qwen3模型在数学和科学推理任务上进行受控实验，覆盖九组学生-教师配对、参数比例从2倍到53倍。通过分别考察OPD、SFT预热、RLVR强化、教师适配等组合，系统分析各阶段对OPD兼容性的影响，并比较OPD与SFT对下游RLVR初始化的差异。

Result: OPD有效性取决于学生-教师兼容性而非教师规模；简短SFT预热可提升后续OPD，而RLVR强化过的学生在同一教师蒸馏下反而退化；用RLVR适配教师能按能力增量提升下游OPD精度；结合教师适配与学生预热后，相同蒸馏步数下平均OPD精度从29.2%提升至43.8%（相对提升50%）；在相同精度下，OPD为下游RLVR留下的初始化优于SFT，且该差距随RL算力增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 每个后训练阶段的选择不应只看它能带来什么能力，还应考虑它为下一阶段创造的学习接口。多阶段流水线需要整体协同设计，而非各阶段孤立优化。

Abstract: Modern LLM post-training composes supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and on-policy distillation (OPD) into multi-stage pipelines, yet these stages are typically designed and evaluated in isolation. We show that this composition is consequential: a stage that improves the current model can make the next stage less effective. Through controlled experiments with Qwen3 models on math and science reasoning, we first characterize OPD across nine student-teacher pairs spanning 2x to 53x parameter ratios and show that OPD effectiveness depends on student-teacher compatibility rather than teacher scale alone. The surrounding stages of OPD reshape this compatibility in three ways: (1) A brief SFT warm-up improves subsequent OPD, while an RLVR-strengthened student regresses under distillation from the same teacher. (2) Adapting the teacher with RLVR raises downstream OPD accuracy in proportion to the capability it adds. Following these two interventions, we find that combining teacher adaptation and student warm-up alone raise average OPD accuracy from 29.2\% to 43.8\% (50\% relative improvement) after the same number of distillation steps, with additional preparatory training. (3) At comparable accuracy, OPD leaves a stronger initialization for downstream RLVR than SFT, with a gap that widens as RL compute scales. Our results suggest that each post-training stage should be chosen not only for the capability it adds, but for the learning interface it creates for the next stage.

</details>


### [168] [Algorithmic Harms Associated with Generative Model-Augmented Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33073)
*Christine Herlihy,Xumei Xi,Shloka Desai,Kevin Bannerman Hutchful,Pedro Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成模型融入机器学习平台后产生的算法危害，扩展了危害分类体系并提出因果分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有危害分类与威胁模型无法充分应对生成模型带来的新问题：一方面需要处理代表性危害与服务质量危害的新型因果驱动因素，另一方面需要预见并缓解内生性危害（如输入与设计目标或模型归纳先验不一致时的"净化"现象）。

Method: 作者提出一个扩展的算法危害分类体系，聚焦于在非对话式推荐系统中使用生成模型的情形；并对（输入，输出）联合分布中问题子集的形成方式进行因果分析。

Result: 构建了扩展的危害分类法，并揭示了问题性（输入，输出）联合分布子集的因果来源。

Conclusion: 扩展的危害分类法与因果分析可为生成模型推荐场景下的危害检测与缓解工作提供指导与依据。

Abstract: In this work, we consider algorithmic harms that may arise as generative models are incorporated into machine learning platforms. We argue that existing harm taxonomies and threat models require extension to (1) address novel causal drivers of well-studied representational and quality-of-service harms; and (2) anticipate and mitigate endogenous harms, such as sanitization, which may arise when system inputs are misaligned with the system designer's objectives, or the generative model's inductive priors. To this end, we introduce an expanded taxonomy of algorithmic harms associated with the use of generative models in non-conversational recommendation systems. In addition, we offer a causal analysis of how problematic subsets of the (input, output) joint distribution can arise, in an effort to inform harms detection and mitigation efforts.

</details>


### [169] [ROTE: Benchmarking Neural Memorization on Complexity-Controlled Symbolic Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31918)
*Xinye Chen,Stefan Güttel,Mohammad Mozaffari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROTE基准协议，用于评估神经架构的符号记忆能力，通过LZW复杂度调控序列，比较多种序列模型在记忆质量、展开稳定性与计算开销间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对神经序列模型符号记忆能力及其规则外推的系统评估手段，需要一个可控复杂度的基准来揭示记忆容量与算法序列复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 提出ROTE协议，使用LZW压缩调控序列复杂度，所有架构统一训练为有限上下文条件预测器，采用teacher-forced单步预测与基于留存符号的闭环rollout评估；覆盖门控循环、最小循环、注意力及循环-注意力混合模型，并保留各自原生计算特性；报告归一化字符串距离、训练时间、内存占用与参数量。

Result: 在LZW复杂度扫描下对多类模型进行系统比较，揭示算法序列复杂度与神经架构记忆容量之间的联系，并展示记忆质量、rollout稳定性与计算代价之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: ROTE为符号记忆与外推能力提供了可复现的评估框架，帮助理解不同神经架构在复杂度变化下的记忆与计算权衡，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce ROTE (RollOut Testing of Exact memorization), a benchmarking protocol for evaluating symbolic memorization of neural architectures. We study memorization and the extension of symbolic rules in neural sequence models by using sequences whose complexity is regulated by Lempel--Ziv--Welch (LZW) compression. Under ROTE, each architecture is trained as the same finite-context conditional predictor and is evaluated using teacher-forced one-step prediction as well as closed-loop rollout on the withheld symbols.
