Following a shared prediction-and-rollout evaluation routine, the benchmark evaluates gated recurrent, minimal recurrent, attention-based, and hybrid recurrent-attention models with their native computational characteristics preserved. Beyond standard predictive metrics, the benchmark reports normalized string distances, training time, memory usage, and parameter count across an LZW-complexity sweep. The study establishes a connection between the complexity of algorithmic sequences and the memorization capacity of neural architectures, revealing the trade-offs involving memorization quality, rollout stability, and computational expense. Our software and reproducible experimental code can be obtained from https://github.com/nla-group/rote.

</details>


### [170] [Resource-Aware Federated Mixture-of-Experts with Adaptive Pruning for Onboard Learning in LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31932)
*Mohamed Shaaban,Mohamed Elmahallawy,Marius Bernahrndt,Tobias Hecking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COSMIC-FL 是一个面向低轨卫星星座的资源感知联邦学习框架，通过 MoE 架构与结构化剪枝实现高效在轨学习，在保持精度的同时最多降低 80% 的通信、计算与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星需在灾害响应、环境监测等任务中在轨学习，但传统联邦学习假设的计算、存储和通信资源超出资源受限卫星平台的能力，且常需将原始影像传回地面站，因此亟需资源感知的联邦学习方案。

Method: 提出 COSMIC-FL：引入两种互补的 MoE 架构——共享主干、激活任务特定通道子集的 Sliced 设计，以及用轻量门控将输入路由到物理分离专家网络的 Modular 设计；通过语义类别到专家的映射，使卫星仅训练、更新和通信与其本地数据相关的专家路径；并结合分阶段优化与三种结构化剪枝策略（服务端剪枝、客户端固定比例剪枝配合均值投票聚合、基于聚合重要性和 MAD 差距准则的自适应客户端逐层剪枝）。

Result: 在六个图像分类基准上于高度非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）设置下实验表明，COSMIC-FL 保持有竞争力的精度，相较 SOTA 联邦学习方法将通信、计算和能耗最多降低 80%；并在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上验证了真实嵌入式部署约束下的效率提升。

Conclusion: COSMIC-FL 通过语义专家路由与结构化剪枝联合适配计算量与模型稀疏性，在异构空间平台上取得良好的精度—效率权衡，适用于资源受限的低轨卫星在轨学习。

Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellites are increasingly expected to perform onboard learning for applications such as disaster response and environmental monitoring. However, conventional federated learning (FL) is ill-suited to onboard satellite learning, as it assumes computational, memory, and communication resources beyond the capabilities of resource-constrained LEO platforms, often necessitating the transmission of raw imagery to ground stations. We present COSMIC-FL, a resource-aware FL framework for efficient onboard learning in LEO satellite constellations. COSMIC-FL introduces two complementary Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures: a Sliced design that shares backbone representations while activating task-specific channel subsets, and a Modular design that employs lightweight gating to route inputs to physically separated expert networks. A semantic class-to-expert mapping enables each satellite to train, update, and communicate only the expert paths relevant to its local data. To further improve efficiency, COSMIC-FL integrates staged optimization with three structured pruning strategies: server-side pruning, client-side fixed-ratio pruning with mean-vote aggregation, and adaptive client-side per-layer pruning based on aggregated importance and a MAD-based gap criterion. Combined with semantic expert routing, these techniques jointly adapt computation and model sparsity to both data semantics and layer importance, yielding a favourable accuracy--efficiency trade-off for heterogeneous space platforms. Experiments on six image classification benchmarks under highly non-i.i.d. settings show that COSMIC-FL maintains competitive accuracy while reducing communication, computation, and energy consumption by up to 80% over SOTA FL methods. We further validate COSMIC-FL on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, confirming its efficiency gains under realistic embedded deployment constraints.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Fixed Features: Architecture-Dependent Sensitivity to Node Representations under Heterophily](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33764)
*Priyanath Maji,Sidharth Gaur,Rajavinoth Paul Durai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过为两个大规模异质图基准构建并行特征变体，评估七种GNN架构在从静态fastText到上下文Transformer嵌入等不同表征下的表现，发现表征对性能的影响因架构而异，但架构排序保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在异质图上的评估通常固定节点特征表征，仅比较架构，导致无法确定异质性鲁棒性结论是否会随输入表征变化而改变。作者希望厘清架构与节点表征是否为可独立对待的评估因素。

Method: 为Roman-Empire和Amazon-Ratings两个大规模异质图基准构建并行特征变体，将每个图与从静态fastText向量到上下文Transformer嵌入的多种表征配对，并在这些表征下评估七种GNN架构；同时进行排序相关性分析。

Result: 表征效应因架构而异：在Roman-Empire上，上下文增益从GCN-sep的2.38个百分点到GAT的13.67个百分点不等，H2GCN提升8.77个百分点；在Amazon-Ratings上（节点文本仅为简短商品标题），GAT从fastText到MPNet提升6.78个百分点，而GCN-sep仅变化0.20个百分点。排序相关性分析显示，架构的相对排序在不同表征下高度稳定。

Conclusion: 架构性能依赖于节点表征，同一表征变化在不同架构上产生不同幅度的增益，因此架构与表征不能作为独立评估因素对待；主要效应是差异化敏感性，而非排序不稳定。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) perform well on homophilic graphs but struggle in heterophilic settings, where connected nodes often carry dissimilar labels. Existing evaluations typically compare architectures under a fixed node-feature representation, leaving unclear whether conclusions about heterophily robustness remain stable as the input representation changes. We address this question by constructing parallel feature variants of two large-scale heterophilic benchmarks, Roman-Empire and Amazon-Ratings, pairing each graph with representations ranging from static fastText vectors to contextual Transformer embeddings and evaluating seven GNN architectures across these representations. We find that the effect of representation varies across architectures: on Roman-Empire, the contextual gain ranges from 2.38 percentage points for GCN-sep to 13.67 points for GAT, with H2GCN gaining 8.77 points. On Amazon-Ratings, where node text is limited to short product titles, GAT improves by 6.78 points from fastText to MPNet, while GCN-sep changes by only 0.20 points. These results show that architectural performance is conditional on node representation: the same representation change can produce different magnitudes of performance gain across architectures, so architecture and representation cannot be treated as independent evaluation factors. A rank-correlation analysis on these two benchmarks further shows that the relative ordering of architectures remains highly stable across representations, isolating differential sensitivity, rather than ranking instability, as the primary effect.

</details>


### [172] [Measurement Boundaries in LLM Financial Agent Evaluation: Fixed-Tape Execution and Multi-Defect Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32379)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在金融智能体测试框架下考察LLM智能体评测中执行性能与审计分数的可解释性边界：执行比较估计的内容受条件限制，目标召回率无法单独刻画审计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评测常直接用执行性能差异和审计分数下结论，但缺乏控制条件约束，容易把执行规则、模型响应与反馈混淆，也不清楚单一指标（如目标违规召回率）能否代表审计质量。

Method: 研究A在三个共享24天上升阶段的合成设置中，对比理想化与压力执行下的独立运行，并将存储的响应轨迹分别重放到两种执行目标；研究B修正不完整答案键，用显式多标签提示替换遗留任务为匹配的零/一/二缺陷任务，比较不同审计器与来源组合下的目标违规召回率，并进行Holm校正。

Result: 研究A中解析出的决策路径仅在450对中的19.8%一致；在固定响应条件下，压力执行使总收益变化-0.0170（95%区间[-0.0230,-0.0117]），为理想基线的10.4%，且十个种子聚类无法确定模型排名。研究B中目标违规召回率从一缺陷到二缺陷的下降在六种组合中五种为正（中位数0.267），三种通过Holm校正；但同时包含两个目标标签的审计器微精度仅0.149，在98/100个零缺陷任务上仍给出发现，仅在21/100例中返回精确双缺陷集合。

Conclusion: 两项研究揭示不同限制：执行比较所能估计的对象，以及目标召回率所捕捉的内容。固定条件与诊断性控制共同界定了分数可支持的论断范围。

Abstract: What controls are needed to interpret execution performance and audit scores in LLM agent evaluations? We study two limits on these interpretations in a financial agent harness. In Study~A, comparing independent runs under idealized and stressed execution on three synthetic settings that share one 24-day upward phase mixes the execution rule with fresh model responses and portfolio feedback: the parsed decision paths agree in only $19.8\%$ of $450$ pairs. Replaying each stored response tape through both execution destinations gives a narrower result. Conditional on those responses, stressed execution changes total return by $-0.0170$ (95\% interval $[-0.0230,-0.0117]$), or $10.4\%$ of the idealized baseline, and ten seed clusters do not resolve the model ranking. Study~B corrects an incomplete answer key and replaces legacy tasks with matched zero-, one-, and two-defect tasks under an explicit multi-label prompt. The drop in target violation recall from one to two defects is positive in five of six combinations of auditor and source (median $0.267$), with three surviving Holm correction. Yet the auditor that includes both target labels most often has micro-precision $0.149$, emits findings on $98/100$ zero-defect tasks, and returns the exact dual-defect set in only $21/100$ cases. Target recall by itself therefore gives a poor account of audit quality on this construction. The studies address different limits: what an execution comparison estimates, and what target recall captures. Together, they show how fixed conditions and diagnostic controls bound the claims a score can support.

</details>


### [173] [Cache-Aware Conv3D Lowering Across Embedded World-Model Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31938)
*Jiaming Zhang,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种缓存感知的算子降级方法，将因果Conv3D表示为批处理空间Conv2D，在边缘设备上显著加速生成式世界模型的VAE解码与整体推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型可为具身规划提供视觉推演，但其在边缘设备上的可行性不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于执行运行时如何表示其运算。作者希望在修改学习模型的前提下，通过运行时优化提升解码效率。

Method: 提出缓存感知降级（cache-aware lowering），将受支持的因果Conv3D调用表达为批处理空间Conv2D操作，同时保留预训练权重、时间缓存语义、卷积参数、偏置位置和输出布局；并与TensorRT全特化方案及BF16/FP32精度评估进行对比。

Result: 在64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上运行完整Cosmos3-Edge图像到视频流程，VAE解码加速约7倍，完整生成延迟降低超过2倍，且重复解码评估保持完整快速路径覆盖无回退；同样降级也改进Cosmos3-Nano并迁移至架构不同的Wan2.1 VAE；TensorRT可再获得1.36倍稳态提升但需更多特化。

Conclusion: 缓存感知降级是一种轻量级运行时优化，在不修改学习模型的情况下即可恢复大部分可用的解码器加速，并可跨模型与架构迁移。

Abstract: Generative world models can provide visual rollouts for embodied planning, yet their feasibility on edge devices depends not only on the learned model but also on how the execution runtime represents its operations. We introduce a cache-aware lowering that expresses supported causal Conv3D calls as batched spatial Conv2D operations while preserving pretrained weights, temporal-cache semantics, convolution parameters, bias placement, and output layout. Across the complete Cosmos3-Edge image-to-video pipeline on a 64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the proposed route accelerates VAE decoding by approximately $7\times$ and reduces complete-generation latency by more than $2\times$, while repeated decoder evaluations maintain complete fast-path coverage without fallbacks. The unchanged lowering also improves Cosmos3-Nano and transfers to LingBot-World's architecturally distinct Wan2.1 VAE. A clean-device comparison against fully specialized TensorRT shows that TensorRT provides a further $1.36\times$ steady-state improvement, but requires substantially greater per-module and per-runtime-state AOT specialization. Same-latent BF16 and FP32 evaluations characterize the finite-precision differences introduced by the alternative execution order. Together, these results position cache-aware lowering as a lightweight runtime optimization that recovers most of the available decoder acceleration without modifying the learned models themselves.

</details>


### [174] [VEX-Bench: Benchmarking Verification Complexity of LLM-Generated Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35028)
*Hanxun Huang,Yutao Wu,Qizhou Wang,Silvia Montaña-Niño,Yige Li,Xiang Zheng,Elif Buse Doyuran,Phoebe Matich,Xiao Liu,Xingjun Ma,Sarah Erfani,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VEX-Bench基准，用于评估LLM生成虚假信息在筛选阶段的验证复杂度，并定义VEX分数衡量生成内容对有限验证资源的消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM使虚假信息生产成本极低但验证成本高昂，形成信息生态的不对称。媒体、平台和事实核查机构在时间、人力和预算受限下依赖筛选机制，因此需要评估不同模型与生成方法所产虚假信息的验证复杂度。

Method: 构建VEX-Bench基准，从新闻与事实核查实践提炼多维验证复杂度（可核查性、潜在危害、来源可信度信号、冒充合法性、预期验证成本）；定义整合诱导产出与验证复杂度的VEX分数；覆盖2类虚假信息、6个高风险领域、60个真实话题，评估7个前沿LLM和7种生成方法共5,880篇文章；采用LLM-as-judge并结合内容分析方法验证，包括有序Krippendorff α及事实核查智能体。

Result: 没有任何单一生成方法在所有维度上占优，凸显多维度评估的必要性。LLM能以比基于智能体的验证低3倍至169倍的成本生成高VEX虚假信息。此类内容在筛选中常被优先处理，消耗稀缺验证资源，导致资源受限验证系统中的系统性错配风险。

Conclusion: VEX-Bench为评估LLM生成虚假信息的验证复杂度提供了统一框架，揭示了验证资源错配风险，代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made misinformation inexpensive to produce but not to verify, creating a growing asymmetry in the information ecosystem. Under tight time, labor, and budget constraints, media organizations, platforms, and fact-checkers rely on screening to prioritize which content to verify. We introduce VEX-Bench, a unified benchmark for evaluating the verification complexity of LLM-generated misinformation, as perceived during screening, across models and generation methods. Verification complexity is assessed along multiple dimensions derived from journalistic and fact-checking practices, capturing checkability, harm potential, source credibility signals, imposter legitimacy, and expected verification effort. We define the VEX score as an integrated measure combining elicitation yield and verification complexity to quantify how generated content consumes limited verification capacity. We construct a benchmark spanning two misinformation categories, 6 high-stakes domains, and 60 real-world topics, and evaluate 7 frontier LLMs and 7 generation methods, yielding 5{,}880 articles. We employ an LLM-as-judge for scalable evaluation and validate it using content-analysis methodology, including ordinal Krippendorff $α$ for inter-annotator reliability, complemented by fact-checking agents for verification. Our findings show that no single method dominates all dimensions, underscoring the need for multi-dimensional evaluation. LLMs can generate high-VEX misinformation at 3$\times$ to 169$\times$ lower cost than agent-based verification. Such content is often prioritized during screening, consuming scarce verification resources and introducing a systematic risk of misallocation in resource-constrained verification systems. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/VEX-Bench}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [175] [Simple Extensions of Single-Objective Acquisition Functions and Hedge Strategies for Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31940)
*Haris Moazam Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于超体积变换的框架，将单目标采集函数与对冲策略直接扩展到多目标贝叶斯优化，无需专门设计多目标采集函数，即可匹配或超越复杂的最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标贝叶斯优化通常依赖专门设计的采集函数或标量化方案来显式处理目标间的权衡，实现复杂、需大量定制。作者质疑这种复杂性是否必要，希望用更简单的方式复用成熟的单目标优化组件。

Method: 提出通过超体积（hypervolume）变换，将标准单目标采集函数直接扩展到多目标场景；并将原本仅用于单目标优化的对冲（hedge）策略推广到多目标情形。方法对现有贝叶斯优化流程改动极小，无需定制的多目标公式，且可原则上适配一大类常用单目标采集函数与对冲策略，同时保留其直观解释与计算效率。

Result: 在多种合成与真实多目标基准上评估，尽管方法简单，其在优化性能和样本效率上一致地达到或超过更复杂的最先进MOBO方法。

Conclusion: 有效的多目标贝叶斯优化可通过复用并谨慎扩展成熟的单目标采集策略来实现，为现有方法提供了更简单、更灵活的替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is commonly approached through specialized acquisition functions or scalarization schemes designed to explicitly account for trade-offs among non-preferential objectives. In this work, we show that such complexity might be unnecessary. We propose a framework that extends standard single-objective acquisition functions directly to the multi-objective setting through a hypervolume-based transformation. We further extend hedge strategies for acquisition functions, which are typically used only in single-objective optimization, to the multi-objective regime. Our approach requires minimal modification to existing Bayesian optimization pipelines and avoids the need for bespoke multi-objective formulations. We demonstrate how a broad class of commonly used single-objective acquisition functions and hedge strategies can be adapted in a principled manner to handle multiple objectives, while preserving their intuitive interpretation and computational efficiency. Empirically, we evaluate the proposed methods across a range of synthetic and real-world multi-objective benchmarks. Despite their simplicity, our extensions consistently match or outperform more complex state-of-the-art MOBO methods in terms of optimization performance and sample efficiency. These results suggest that effective multi-objective Bayesian optimization can be achieved by reusing and carefully extending well-established single-objective acquisition strategies, offering a simpler and more flexible alternative to existing approaches.

</details>


### [176] [KernelZero: Co-Evolving Proposer and Coder for Continuously Improved GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33074)
*Changxin Ke,Rui Zhang,Zixiang Fang,Zhenghong Li,Yuanbo Wen,Jiashuo Shen,Shuo Wang,Jiaming Guo,Ling Li,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KernelZero共进化框架，通过Proposer与Coder交替优化自动生成高质量GPU内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的GPU内核生成方法面临两个主要限制：一是缺乏与模型当前能力相匹配的高质量训练数据；二是内核正确性与性能之间存在固有权衡。

Method: KernelZero包含两个专门模型：Proposer从API集合生成Torch模块，Coder将其翻译为CUDA或Triton内核。采用前沿驱动的模块生成机制，根据Coder当前弱点持续生成能力对齐的训练模块；并引入正确性感知组相对策略优化（CA-GRPO），仅在正确性足够可靠后才优化性能。通过交替优化Proposer与Coder形成自动课程学习。

Result: KernelZero-7B在CUDA上超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet，在Triton上超越DeepSeek-V4-Pro。在KernelBench Level 1和2上，CUDA的pass@1分别为75.8%和69.6%，pass@10达100%和97%；Triton的pass@1分别为77.2%和72.5%。

Conclusion: KernelZero通过共进化机制实现了针对性且训练高效的能力提升，验证了自动课程学习在GPU内核生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential to modern machine learning systems, yet automatically generating kernels that are both correct and efficient remains challenging. Existing LLM-based approaches face two major limitations: the scarcity of high-quality training data aligned with the model's current capabilities, and the inherent trade-off between kernel correctness and performance. To address these challenges, we propose KernelZero, a co-evolution framework that continuously improves GPU kernel generation through two specialized models: a Proposer that generates Torch modules from API sets and a Coder that translates them into CUDA or Triton kernels. KernelZero uses a frontier-driven module generation mechanism to continuously produce capability-aligned training modules based on the Coder's current weaknesses. It further introduces Correctness-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (CA-GRPO), which optimizes performance only after correctness becomes sufficiently reliable. By alternating the optimization of the Proposer and Coder, KernelZero forms an automatic curriculum that enables targeted and training-efficient capability improvement. Empirically, KernelZero-7B surpasses Claude-4.5-Sonnet on CUDA and DeepSeek-V4-Pro on Triton. On KernelBench Level 1 and 2, it achieves CUDA pass@1 scores of 75.8% and 69.6%, respectively, with pass@10 reaching 100% and 97%. On Triton, it achieves pass@1 scores of 77.2% and 72.5%, respectively.

</details>


### [177] [On-Policy Attention Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31947)
*Arian Raje,Anupam Nayak,Anthony Fei,Akaash Parthasarathy,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPAL（On-Policy Attention Linearization），通过让学生模型在自身长上下文轨迹上采样、接受冻结全注意力教师的密集监督，解决混合线性注意力模型蒸馏中误差累积与长上下文崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合Transformer用线性注意力替代大部分softmax注意力，可在低内存下达到接近Transformer的质量。现有工作多从已训练的全注意力Transformer蒸馏得到此类模型，但蒸馏模型在长上下文检索与推理（尤其thinking模式）下常崩溃：线性注意力层须将上下文压缩为固定大小状态，误差在长序列上累积；而off-policy蒸馏从未教学生如何从这种漂移中恢复，因此长序列任务尤为困难。

Method: 提出On-Policy Attention Linearization（OPAL）：让混合注意力学生模型采样自己的长上下文轨迹，并从冻结的全注意力教师处获得密集监督，从而在线修正误差累积。无需SFT或RLVR。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B与MiMo-7B-RL-0530上应用OPAL，仅用3B训练token：常识推理恢复全注意力性能的87–94%，NIAH检索100%，数学推理83–93%。相比最强先前线性化方法（检索仅恢复68%、数学推理绝对平均准确率提升21.6%），OPAL完全恢复检索并在数学推理上达到67.6–72.2%。

Conclusion: OPAL通过on-policy长上下文采样与教师密集监督，有效缓解线性注意力状态压缩导致的误差累积，在不依赖SFT或RLVR的情况下大幅缩小混合注意力学生与全注意力教师之间的差距，尤其在检索与数学推理等长上下文任务上。

Abstract: Hybrid transformer architectures that replace most softmax attention layers with linear attention offer transformer-level quality at a fraction of the memory cost. Rather than pretraining such models, a growing body of work distills them from already trained full-attention transformers. However, these distilled models often collapse on long-context retrieval and reasoning tasks, particularly when operating in thinking mode, where the efficiency gains of hybrid architectures matter most. Since linear attention layers must compress context into a fixed-size state, their errors compound over long sequences. As off-policy distillation never teaches the student model to recover from this drift, tasks that necessitate longer sequence lengths become especially challenging. We introduce On-Policy Attention Linearization (OPAL) in which the hybrid attention student samples its own long-context trajectories and receives dense supervision from the frozen full-attention teacher. Applying OPAL to Qwen3-4B and MiMo-7B-RL-0530, we recover $87$--$94\%$ of full-attention performance on commonsense reasoning, $100\%$ on needle-in-a-haystack (NIAH) retrieval, and $83$--$93\%$ on mathematical reasoning with only 3B training tokens. We achieve these results without supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Compared with the strongest prior linearization method, which recovers $68\%$ of its teacher's retrieval performance and $21.6\%$ absolute average mathematical reasoning accuracy, OPAL fully recovers retrieval and achieves $67.6$--$72.2\%$ on math reasoning.

</details>


### [178] [Model-Agnostic Online Certificate-Driven Calibration for Time Series Forecasting Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31960)
*Chenfeng Huang,Zixuan Ma,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个模型无关的在线鞅PAC-Bayes框架，在时间依赖和分布漂移下为时间序列OOD泛化提供有限样本证书，并将其用作在线校准的正则化器，在多种预测器上提升了漂移场景下的稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列的分布外泛化要求预测器在部署动态与训练条件不同（协变量漂移、概念漂移、时间依赖）时仍然可靠。虽然PAC-Bayes域适应提供了可计算的证书，将目标风险分解为源风险项、源到目标失配项和复杂度项，但标准分析依赖于独立采样和分布稳定性假设，而这些假设在时间序列中因序列相关性和非平稳漂移而被违反。因此需要一种能在时间依赖与分布漂移下仍给出有限样本保证的方法。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的在线鞅PAC-Bayes框架，在时间依赖和分布漂移下给出有限样本证书：用鞅集中不等式替代独立样本集中，能够适应损失尺度和可预测变差。将该证书作为在线校准的代理正则化器，在固定的预测骨干之上训练一个门控残差贝叶斯头，生成纠正性更新；当门关闭时退回到骨干预测。在线校准结合源风险锚、后验漂移惩罚，以及由预测前观测到的目标窗口计算的时间自适应失配项，遵循"先预测后更新"协议：结果仅在预测后可得，并用于更新后续预测。

Result: 在卷积、基于注意力和基于大语言模型的预测器上的实验表明，在协变量漂移和概念漂移下，方法的稳定性和准确性均得到提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将PAC-Bayes域适应扩展到时间序列的非平稳、序列相关场景，提供了可计算且适应时间依赖的在线泛化证书，并通过门控残差贝叶斯头的在线校准机制有效提升了多种预测骨干在分布漂移下的性能。

Abstract: Time series out-of-distribution generalization requires forecasters to remain reliable when deployment dynamics differ from training conditions due to covariate shift, concept shift, and temporal dependence. Probably Approximately Correct Bayesian domain adaptation provides computable certificates by decomposing target risk into a source risk term, a source-to-target mismatch term, and a complexity term, but standard analyses rely on independent sampling and distributional stability, assumptions that are violated in time series by serial dependence and nonstationary shift. We propose a model-agnostic online martingale Probably Approximately Correct Bayesian framework that yields finite-sample certificates under temporal dependence and distribution shift. The certificate replaces independent-sample concentration with martingale concentration that adapts to loss scale and predictable variation. We use the certificate as a surrogate regularizer for online calibration by training a gated residual Bayesian head on top of a fixed forecasting backbone, producing a corrective update that reverts to the backbone prediction when the gate is closed. Online calibration combines a source risk anchor, a posterior-shift penalty, and a time-adaptive mismatch term computed from target windows observed before forecasting. It follows a predict-then-update protocol in which outcomes become available only after forecasting and are used to update subsequent predictions. Experiments across convolutional, attention-based, and large language model-based forecasters show improved stability and accuracy under covariate and concept shift.

</details>


### [179] [ZonoGPT: Towards An Abstract Domain for Verifying Large GPT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34457)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZonoGPT 是一种用于验证大型 Transformer 的抽象域，其空间复杂度与网络深度无关，首次实现了对标准架构（如 GPT-2 Medium）的可扩展验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证可为 Transformer 的鲁棒性、安全性和公平性提供可审计的形式化保证，但现有工作局限于小型或受限的 Transformer，且难以在深层模型中保持精度。

Method: 提出 ZonoGPT 抽象域，采用结构化 zonotope 与生成元缩减机制高效保留相关性；针对 Attention 和 LayerNorm 引入块级融合变换以保留特征关系，并设计保持生成元关系的 GELU 仿射变换以维持精度。

Result: 成为首个可验证标准架构的方法，可扩展至官方 HuggingFace 模型（最大至 GPT-2 Medium，24 个 block、3 亿+参数），并成功验证了文本与视觉任务中的 1,339 个实例。

Conclusion: ZonoGPT 通过深度无关的空间复杂度与精度保持机制，突破了 Transformer 验证的可扩展性瓶颈，为大型标准模型提供了实用且精确的形式化验证途径。

Abstract: Transformer-based models are widely used for reasoning, coding, and multimodal agentic tasks. To provide formal assurance of desirable behaviors, such as robustness, safety, and fairness, neural network verification techniques prove required properties and provide auditable guarantees before deployment. However, prior work remains limited to small or restricted Transformers, and maintaining precision across deep models remains challenging. In this work, we introduce ZonoGPT, an abstract domain for verifying large transformers that maintains a space complexity independent of network depth. ZonoGPT uses a structured zonotope and a generator reduction mechanism to efficiently preserve correlations. To maintain precision, it introduces block-specific fused transformations for Attention and LayerNorm that retain feature relations, along with an affine transform for GELU that preserves generator relations. These mechanisms enable \tool{} to be the first approach to verify standard architectures, scaling to official HuggingFace models up to GPT-2 Medium (24 blocks, 300M+ parameters) and successfully verifying 1,339 instances across text and vision tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Model Casting and Low-Parameter Gating: Towards More Sparsely Activated FFNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31975)
*Maria Lomeli,Antoine Groudiev,Matthijs Douze,Loïc Cabannes,Pierre Emmanuel Mazaré,François Fleuret,Maximilian Beck,Gergely Szilvasy,Naila Murray,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 model casting 与 LoPA Gating,通过稀疏化 FFN 激活并降低门控矩阵计算量,将推理前向计算量最多降低约 3 倍以上,并在 CPU/GPU 上实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 FFN 的门控机制在大稀疏度下仍受限于门控矩阵计算,存在约 3 倍的加速上限,现有剪枝与 RELU 化方法难以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出 model casting:先计算门控矩阵输出,利用其高稀疏性跳过后续两个矩阵的计算;进一步提出 LoPA Gating,一种低 FLOPs 的门控矩阵参数化方式,为门控矩阵分配更少的算力与参数。方法覆盖两种场景:对预训练模型进行稀疏化诱导激活的 casting,以及从头训练 LoPA。

Result: 在同等质量下,LoPA Casting 实现 3.2 倍 FLOPs 加速,而 top-p 与 TEAL 等竞争方法最多约 1.6 倍;使用专用 kernel 时,在 90% 稀疏度下 GPU 实测加速达 3.31 倍,超过标准门控的 3 倍上限;RELU 化方法在稀疏度上于 80% 以下即趋于饱和。

Conclusion: model casting 与 LoPA Gating 能显著提升 FFN 稀疏激活的推理效率,突破传统门控的加速天花板,在多种设置下优于现有剪枝方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces model casting, a mid-training recipe that drastically sparsifies the activations within the Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layer. With this strategy, at inference time, we first compute the output of the gating matrix and, thanks to its high sparsity, we avoid computations with the two other matrices, reducing the FLOP count by up to 3x. While this theoretical speedup is an upper bound, model casting translates into significant speedups both on CPU and GPU.
  We then introduce LoPA Gating, a new FFN design that increases the maximum theoretical speedup. It is a low-FLOPs parameterization of the gating matrix that overcomes the 3x cap by allocating fewer FLOPs and parameters to the gating matrix, compared to the two other FFN matrices that are sparsely activated.
  We consider two cases: (i) we cast a pre-trained model with a sparsity inducing activation; (ii) we train with LoPA from scratch. In all settings, we significantly outperform existing pruning solutions and regular RELU-fication. For instance, at matched quality, we achieve a 3.2x FLOP speedup with LoPA Casting, against 1.6x at best for competing methods top-p and TEAL. Using dedicated kernels, we achieve an actual 3.31x speed-up on GPU at 90% sparsity, past the 3x ceiling of standard gating; RELU-fication, meanwhile, plateaus below 80% sparsity.

</details>


### [181] [Verifying Neural Networks with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34553)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSB，一个强化学习框架，用于改进 DNN 形式化验证中的分支启发式，通过 actor-critic 学习最大化长期收益，在 600 个实例上多解 11% 且分支探索减少 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN 形式化验证对安全关键系统至关重要，但现有验证器的分支启发式基于静态评分函数做贪心决策，无法预见长期效率，也未利用日益增长的验证数据来提升性能。

Method: RSB 在分支定界框架上训练 actor-critic 架构以最大化累积未来奖励而非即时得分；actor 根据原始神经元特征和学习到的图嵌入生成注意力权重，用其对基线启发式分数进行重缩放，从而指导神经元分支选择。

Result: 在 600 个具挑战性的实例上，RSB 持续优于最先进的分支启发式，多求解 11% 的实例，同时减少 50% 的分支探索。

Conclusion: 通过学习式、面向长期效率的分支策略，RSB 显著提升了 DNN 验证的效率与成功率，表明强化学习可有效精化传统分支启发式。

Abstract: Formal verification can play a key role in ensuring the reliability of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) deployed in safety-critical systems. Modern DNN verifiers employ a branch-and-bound framework, which alternates between branching (splitting into smaller subproblems) and bounding (pruning subproblems) to efficiently explore the verification space. However, existing branching heuristics make greedy decisions based on static scoring functions. They do not anticipate long-term efficiency or leverage the growing availability of verification data to improve performance. This work introduces RSB, a reinforcement learning framework that learns to refine baseline branching heuristics. It trains an actor-critic architecture to maximize cumulative future rewards rather than immediate scores. The actor generates attention weights from observations of raw neuron features and learned graph embeddings, which rescale baseline heuristic scores to guide neuron branching. Evaluation on 600 challenging instances demonstrates that RSB consistently outperforms state-of-the-art branching heuristics, solving 11% more instances while reducing branch exploration by 50%.

</details>


### [182] [Understanding the Subspace Stabilization of the Hessian and Gradient Covariance Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31983)
*Fangshuo Liao,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了Hessian子空间演化的不稳定度指标，发现Hessian与梯度协方差矩阵的顶部子空间都会稳定，并通过类间/类内分解给出了顶部(C-1)子空间的近似显式形式，用outlier与bulk特征值的分离解释了稳定现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian矩阵顶部子空间稳定是神经网络二阶信息研究中的重要且令人惊讶的现象。已有工作通过逐步Hessian顶部子空间的重叠度来衡量稳定性，并将其归因于训练后期参数变化减小。但该解释依赖于参数变化幅度，可能掩盖与参数变化无关的真实稳定性，因此需要一个不依赖参数变化幅度的新指标来重新审视该现象。

Method: 1) 定义新的子空间演化不稳定度指标，用于检测独立于参数变化幅度的子空间稳定；2) 观察梯度协方差矩阵的顶部子空间具有与Hessian类似的性质；3) 对梯度协方差矩阵进行类间与类内分解，导出顶部(C-1)子空间近似显式形式；4) 在梯度流设定下分析该近似的缓慢演化与Hessian外点/主体特征值分离的关系。

Result: 新指标能够检测到与参数变化幅度无关的子空间稳定现象；所给出的显式形式可以近乎完美地近似Hessian及梯度协方差矩阵的顶部(C-1)子空间；在梯度流框架下证明该近似的缓慢演化源于Hessian外点特征值与主体特征值之间的分离。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对Hessian顶部子空间稳定现象的新的、不依赖参数变化幅度的解释：稳定性来自外点与主体特征值分离所导致的近似量缓慢演化，而非仅由训练后期参数变化减小所致；同时揭示了Hessian与梯度协方差矩阵顶部子空间之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: The phenomenon of the top subspace stabilization of the Hessian matrix is an surprising and critical aspect in study of the second-order information of neural network training. Prior work argues that the top subspace of the Hessian stabilizes by measuring the overlap between the top subspaces of the step-wise Hessian, and explains this stabilization with diminishing parameter change in the late phase of training. In this paper, we define a new instability metric for the subspace evolution, and use it to detect subspace stabilization that is independent of the magnitude of parameter change. In the meantime, we observe that the gradient covariance matrix has a similar property of its top subspace to the Hessian. By using a between-class and within-class decomposition of the gradient covariance matrix, we identify an explicit form that gives a near-perfect approximation of the top-$(C-1)$ subspace of the Hessian and the gradient covariance matrix. In the gradient flow set-up, we show that the slow evolution of the idenfied approximation is due to the separation between the outlier and the bulk eigenvalues of the Hessian matrix, thus providing an explanation to the phenomenon of the top subspace stabilization of the Hessian matrix.

</details>


### [183] [Audit the Scaffold, Not the Checkpoint: A Stationarity Dichotomy for Recursive Self-Improvement in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34924)
*Sebastian Bobadilla-Suarez,Bob Suh,Ryan Fortin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"平稳性二分法"：当智能体可达的编辑集合固定时，迭代自我修改必然陷入严格递减收益；只有该集合扩张才能突破天花板。冻结权重只能保证最终上限，却无法保证过程中的平稳性。核心结论是"审计脚手架，而非检查点"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计往往检查系统权重是否冻结，但这检查的是错误的对象。作者希望厘清迭代自我修改何时会遇到收益递减，并区分通常被混为一谈的三种机制：固定类内的搜索、提升上限本身的测试时训练，以及介于两者之间的脚手架重写。

Method: 将"细化"过程解读为对草稿与目标之间残差误差的梯度提升（一个补丁或 git diff），然后测量该解读在何处失效：补丁是组合叠加而非并列投票，且失败会重叠。以饱和度为测量对象，在 SWE-bench 与 401 个生产会话上观察每轮改进与 churn 的衰减。

Result: 每轮改进在 SWE-bench 上衰减趋近于零，churn 在 401 个生产会话上呈几何衰减，其形状与一个前 AI 时代的人类基线相同（后者确立了该机制但未识别其成因）。Best-of-k 编排恰好实现最佳工作者的上限，宽度买的是速率而非预算；对固定池的重复咨询有可预先计算的地平线，由池本身决定；在 30 个同族工作者上失败重叠达到最大值，多数投票在 23/55（42%）的任务上失败。

Conclusion: 天花板在横向同样起作用。两种失效源于工程选择而非关于代码的定律，因此它们共同指明了一个值得构建的测试框架（harness）。核心主张是：审计脚手架，而非检查点。

Abstract: An auditor who checks whether a system's weights are frozen is checking the wrong thing. Our stationarity dichotomy says that iterative self-modification hits strict diminishing returns whenever the agent's reachable set of edits stays fixed, and can escape only if that set expands. Rewriting scaffolding (tools, verifiers, decomposition) expands what an agent reaches without touching a weight, so frozen weights buy an eventual ceiling but no stationarity along the way. The criterion also separates three regimes usually merged: search within a fixed class, test-time training that raises the ceiling itself, and scaffold rewriting between them. Audit the scaffold, not the checkpoint.
  The same ceiling binds sideways. Best-of-$k$ orchestration realizes the best worker's ceiling exactly: width buys rate, not budget. Re-consulting a fixed pool has a horizon computable in advance, decided by the pool alone, and the one arrangement that would beat it, a weighted vote, needs diversity real workers lack: on 30 same-family workers the failure overlap sits at its maximum, and a majority fails 23/55 (42%) of tasks.
  We obtain the criterion by reading refinement as gradient boosting on the residual error between draft and target, a patch or git diff, and then measuring where that reading breaks: patches compose instead of standing beside each other to be voted on, and failures overlap. What we measure is saturation. Per-round improvement decays toward zero on SWE-bench, and churn decays geometrically across 401 production sessions, a shape shared with a pre-AI human baseline that establishes the regime without identifying its cause. Both breaks are engineering choices rather than laws about code, so together they specify a harness worth building.

</details>


### [184] [Lagrangian and Hamiltonian Neural Networks With a Dissipative System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31988)
*V. Rayamajhi,J. Singal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拉格朗日神经网络与哈密顿神经网络对显含时间的耗散系统的适用性，以阻尼谐振子为例，发现两类模型均能预测其经验物理行为并有效学习底层的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明拉格朗日神经网络和哈密顿神经网络能有效学习无阻尼系统的底层拉格朗日量与哈密顿量，但对于拉格朗日量、哈密顿量及总能量均显含时间的耗散系统，其适用性尚不明确，值得进一步探究。

Method: 构建一维单分量谐振子的仿真系统，分别考虑带阻尼与不带阻尼两种情形作为对照，并分别用拉格朗日神经网络和哈密顿神经网络对这两类系统进行建模与训练。

Result: 拉格朗日方法与哈密顿方法均能预测阻尼振子系统的经验物理行为，并能在不同程度上有效“学习”其底层的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量，这与此前在无阻尼振子系统中的结果一致。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了拉格朗日力学与哈密顿力学的一些重要性质，尤其是那些在非显含时间系统中不显现的性质。

Abstract: We investigate the applicability of Lagrangian and Hamiltonian Neural Network models to a dissipative system that has explicit time dependence in its Lagrangian, Hamiltonian, and total energy. To do so we consider these neural network models for simulated systems of a harmonic one-dimensional, one-component oscillator with damping, as well as without damping for comparison. We find that both the Lagrangian and Hamiltonian approaches are able to predict the empirical physical behavior of the damped oscillator systems and to effectively ``learn'' to varying degrees the underlying Lagrangians and Hamiltonians, as has previously been shown to be the case with undamped oscillator systems. These investigations elucidate important properties of Lagrangian and Hamiltonian mechanics, including properties that are not manifest when considering systems without explicit time dependence.

</details>


### [185] [TokenCast: Forecasting Token Consumption During LLM Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35760)
*Chaoqian Ouyang,Ling Yue,Libin Zheng,Huanghui Guo,Shengxiang Xu,YiShu Wang,Ran Li,Jian Yin,Shaowu Pan,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TokenCast，通过学习每个执行片段的可组合成本表示，实现对 LLM 智能体任务 token 消耗的实时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一任务在多次运行中 token 消耗可能相差一个数量级以上。智能体基于工具反馈和中间结果选择下一步，同时不断增长的上下文会推高后续每次调用的输入规模。因此任务总消耗在执行前难以预测，且预测需随运行推进不断修正。

Method: TokenCast 为每个执行片段学习可组合的成本表示，记录该片段自身消耗及引入的上下文增长量。通过组合相邻片段得到累积估计，捕捉早期片段上下文被后续每次调用重读所带来的额外输入成本。随着执行推进，新观测证据刷新预测，且无需额外 LLM 调用。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，平均每次运行的累积预测时间为 32.8 ms。在 4 个任务套件和 6 个智能体模型的 96 个评估组合中，相对最强基线平均降低 14.5% 的平均绝对误差。在离线预算控制回放中，相同轨迹完成度下平均比固定预算策略少用 21.3% 的 token。

Conclusion: TokenCast 以低成本、无需额外 LLM 调用的方式实现可随执行动态修正的 token 消耗预测，并在预测精度和预算控制上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) agent executes the same task, token consumption can vary by over an order of magnitude across runs. The agent chooses its next steps based on tool feedback and intermediate results, while the growing context steadily inflates the input size of every subsequent call. The total consumption of a task is therefore hard to predict before execution and the prediction must be revised as the run unfolds. In this paper, we propose TokenCast, which learns a composable cost representation for each execution segment, recording its own consumption and the context growth it introduces. Composing adjacent segments yields a cumulative estimate that captures the extra input cost incurred when context from earlier segments is re-read by every later call. As execution unfolds, newly observed evidence refreshes the forecast, requiring no additional LLM calls and incurring a mean cumulative prediction time of 32.8 ms per run on SWE-bench Verified. Across 4 task suites and 6 agent models, TokenCast's mean absolute error reduction against the strongest comparator averages 14.5% over 96 evaluated combinations. In offline budget-control replay, TokenCast uses 21.3% fewer tokens on average than a fixed-budget policy at matched trace completion. The code is available at https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/TokenCast.

</details>


### [186] [Trapped by Their Own Rollouts: Understanding Aggregation--Rollout Feedback in Federated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32573)
*Jinqian Chen,Jihua Zhu,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究联邦同策略蒸馏（FedOPD），揭示FedAvg的学习率敏感性现象，并提出FedTOPS方法实现显著协作增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦同策略蒸馏中，当适配提示分布在客户端时，能否通过联邦协作获益尚不明确。FedAvg在小学习率下可能不如独立本地训练，其协作增益被学习率敏感性所掩盖，需要解释并解决这一问题。

Method: 将学生模型的双重角色（学习者与未来训练数据生成者）建模为聚合-rollout反馈机制，在具有共同最优解和稳定更新的可解模型中建立理论。据此提出FedTOPS（联邦教师引导的同策略缩放），复用当前轨迹上的教师反馈，在客户端预测变化约束下调整FedAvg的更新幅度。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，FedTOPS相较于FedAvg将宏平均Avg@8提升4.56至14.57个百分点，覆盖所评估的多个学生模型与本地学习率设置。

Conclusion: 学习率在联邦同策略蒸馏中具有耦合双重作用，聚合诱导的优化滞后会延缓学生获取有效教师监督。通过教师引导的更新幅度缩放（FedTOPS）可有效缓解该问题并释放联邦协作增益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach to language-model adaptation, aligning teacher supervision with the student's own generated trajectories. When adaptation prompts are distributed across clients, can this process benefit from federated collaboration? We study federated OPD and find that substantial collaboration gains can be obscured by learning-rate sensitivity: FedAvg can perform no better than independent local training at a small learning rate, yet recover a clear advantage at a larger rate. We explain this phenomenon through the student's dual role as learner and generator of future training data. An aggregation-induced optimization lag can delay access to useful teacher supervision, which in turn slows subsequent learning. Our theory establishes this aggregation--rollout feedback in a solvable model with a common optimum and stable updates, and identifies two coupled roles of learning rate: learning from current supervision and reaching future supervision. Guided by this analysis, we propose FedTOPS (Federated Teacher-guided On-Policy Scaling), which reuses teacher feedback on current trajectories to adapt the FedAvg update magnitude under clientwise predictive-change constraints. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, FedTOPS improves macro Avg@8 over FedAvg by 4.56--14.57 percentage points across the evaluated student models and local learning rates.

</details>


### [187] [Mechanistic Interpretability Reveals Shared Causal Subspaces in Brain-to-Speech Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31992)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Ayushi Mishra,Sanghamitra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机制可解释性中的激活修补方法，探究语音解码器内部哪些神经元承载跨模态信息，发现跨模态收益来自小群神经元（而非单一神经元），且这些神经元在解码器早期阶段具有条件特异性、在后期阶段则相互重叠，主要来源为发声语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑活动解码隐蔽语音（如默念、想象）比解码发声语音更难。跨模态迁移是一种有前景的补救手段，但解码器内部如何表征与处理不同语音形式的脑活动仍不清楚，因此作者希望明确哪些内部神经元承载跨模态信息、这些神经元是否在不同语音形式间共享。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，利用同一句子在发声、默念和想象三种条件下的配对记录进行激活修补（activation patching）：将一个条件下句子的解码器内部活动插入到另一条件下同一句子的处理过程中，并测量解码准确率的变化。

Result: 没有任何单一神经元能单独带来这种收益；收益来自小群神经元，其中发声语音是最有用的来源。这些小群神经元在解码器早期阶段大体上是条件特异的，但在后期阶段出现重叠。

Conclusion: 这些发现指向通过鼓励共享的后期表征（主要从发声数据中学习）的训练目标，来构建更数据高效的隐蔽语音解码器。

Abstract: Decoding covert speech, such as mimed or imagined, from brain activity is harder than decoding vocalized speech. Cross-modal transfer, where information from one speech form helps decode another, is a promising remedy; yet how a decoder internally represents and processes brain activity from different speech forms remains unclear. In this work, we ask: which internal neurons of a decoder carry cross-modal information, and are these neurons shared across different speech forms? To answer these questions, we leverage mechanistic interpretability, using recordings of the same sentences in vocalized, mimed, and imagined input pairs for activation patching. We insert the decoder's internal activity for a sentence in one condition into its processing of the same sentence in another and measure the change in decoding accuracy. We find that no single neuron drives this benefit; instead, it arises from small groups of neurons, with vocalized speech as the most useful source. These groups are largely condition-specific in the early stage of the decoder but overlap in the later stage. These findings point toward more data-efficient covert speech decoders through training objectives that encourage shared later-stage representations learned mainly from vocalized data.

</details>


### [188] [Over-the-Air Federated Learning in Heterogeneous Mobile Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32832)
*Ming Xiang,Nicolò Michelusi,Yonina C. Eldar,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedOAG，通过梯度归一化和隐式gossiping自动满足能量约束并均匀混合设备更新，无需全设备传输，也不依赖特定衰落模型，在动态无线条件下收敛到无偏非凸目标的稳定点，达到最优速率O(1/√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中计算（Over-the-air computation）可借助波形叠加实现联邦学习中的同步模型聚合，是无线网络中可扩展且高效的方案。但现有方法难以应对异构衰落信道：要么强制所有设备进行无偏更新，导致因最弱信道而显著放大接收端噪声；要么允许部分设备贡献，但对衰落模型失配敏感，且只能收敛到有偏目标。

Method: 提出FedOAG方法，包含两个算法组件：利用梯度归一化自动满足能量约束；通过隐式gossiping机制均匀混合各设备的更新。该方法不要求所有设备都参与传输，也不依赖特定衰落模型或时变统计信道分布的先验知识。

Result: 理论分析表明，FedOAG可收敛到无偏非凸目标的稳定点，收敛速率为O(1/√T)，这是任意随机一阶方法所能达到的最优速率；并在真实数据集上、动态无线条件下的数值实验中验证了分析结论。

Conclusion: FedOAG通过算法层面的设计有效缓解了异构衰落信道下的噪声放大与模型偏置问题，在无需特定信道模型假设的前提下实现了无偏、最优速率的收敛，为无线网络中的联邦学习部署提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Over-the-air computation has emerged as a scalable and efficient solution for deploying federated learning algorithms in wireless networks by exploiting waveform superposition for simultaneous model aggregation. Most existing work struggles with heterogeneous fading channels. These approaches either enforce unbiased updates from all devices or allow partial device contributions, requiring careful tuning of the convergence bound to mitigate bias under specific fading models. However, the former significantly amplifies receiver noise due to the weakest channel, whereas the latter is sensitive to fading model mismatch and converges only to a biased objective. To tackle these challenges, we propose FedOAG, which employs algorithmic components to automatically satisfy energy constraints via gradient normalization and evenly mix devices' updates through implicit gossiping. Importantly, FedOAG does not require transmission from all devices, nor does it rely on a specific fading model or knowledge of time-varying statistical channel distributions. We show that FedOAG converges to a stationary point of an unbiased non-convex objective at the best possible rate $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ for any stochastic first-order method. We corroborate our analysis with numerical experiments over dynamic wireless conditions on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [189] [Can Circuit Alignment Predict OOD Generalization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31996)
*Ayan Banerjee,Abhra Chaudhuri,Josep Llados,Umapada Pal,Anjan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Circuit Alignment Score (CAS)，仅用训练模型权重即可预测分布外 (OOD) 泛化，并具有理论一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征相似性度量 (CKA、SVCCA、RSA) 比较的是激活而非预测泛化能力，且对分布偏移所引发的计算图结构重路由不敏感。作者希望无需目标域数据即可预测 OOD 泛化。

Method: CAS 通过图核比较跨域类别特定电路 (circuits)，分解为同类一致性与跨类混淆；并将其建模为域分布上的 Lebesgue 积分，用蒙特卡洛估计恢复学习器的 OOD 准确率排序。

Result: 理论上证明成对逆序误差以 O(1/M) 速率消失 (M 为采样域数)；在 PACS 的 48 个学习器上，CAS 与 OOD 准确率的秩相关达 0.88，而 CKA 为 0.58、SVCCA 为 0.23、RSA 为 0.14，其他基准也呈类似趋势，甚至优于依赖数据的方法。

Conclusion: CAS 是首个可证明一致的分布鲁棒性预测器，且无需目标域数据或标签。代码已开源。

Abstract: Can out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization be predicted from a trained model's weights alone, without any target-domain data? Existing representational similarity metrics (CKA, SVCCA, RSA) compare activations rather than forecast generalization. We show they are provably insensitive to structural rerouting in the computational graph, the very change distribution shift induces. We close this gap with the Circuit Alignment Score (CAS), which compares class-specific circuits across domains via graph kernels, decomposed into same-class coherence and cross-class confusion. Casting CAS as a Lebesgue integral over the domain distribution, we prove its Monte Carlo estimate recovers the ground-truth ranking of learners by OOD accuracy, with pairwise inversion error vanishing at rate $O(1/M)$, where $M$ is the number of sampled domains. Across $48$ learners on PACS, CAS attains $0.88$ rank correlation with OOD accuracy, versus $0.58$ (CKA), $0.23$ (SVCCA), and $0.14$ (RSA), with similar trends on other benchmarks and even against data-dependent methods, making it the first provably consistent predictor of distributional robustness requiring neither target-domain data nor labels. The code is available at: https://github.com/ayanban011/ACE

</details>


### [190] [SketchSSM: Write to the Full State, Read from a Compact Sketch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33051)
*Omin Kwon,JoongWon Shin,Minseo Kim,Kurt Keutzer,Sehoon Kim,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SketchSSM，一种针对混合注意力模型的线性注意力状态读取优化方法：利用固定离线基向量对查询进行低秩近似，在每次状态更新时一次性读取完整状态并预计算基向量输出存入紧凑 sketch，后续解码步骤通过查询相关系数重建输出，避免全状态读取。在四个基于 Mamba-2、GDN、KDA 的模型上，状态访问流量降低约 10 倍，平均精度基本保持，单张 NVIDIA B300 上内核加速最高达 7.78x，解码吞吐提升最高 2.64x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力模型用线性注意力替代大部分 softmax 注意力层，可抑制 KV-cache 增长并支持更大解码批次，但此时递归状态访问成为主要瓶颈。ReplaySSM 虽通过缓存键值摊销状态更新，然而在两次状态更新之间状态并未改变，每个新查询仍须执行一次全状态读取，造成冗余开销。

Method: 作者观察到低秩状态加权查询近似能准确保持状态读取输出，且尽管未来查询未知，用于近似查询的基向量可以离线固定。据此提出 SketchSSM：保留完整的全状态更新，仅对读取进行近似。每次状态更新时读取一次完整状态，为这些基向量预计算输出并存入紧凑 sketch；之后每个解码步骤将 sketch 向量与查询相关系数组合，重建输出而无需全状态读取。

Result: 在四个基于 Mamba-2、GDN 和 KDA 的模型上，SketchSSM 将状态访问流量降低约 10 倍，同时在四个解码基准上基本保持平均精度，并在四个 RULER 检索任务上保持召回率。在单张 NVIDIA B300 上，相对标准 vLLM 基线，线性注意力内核加速分别达到 7.78x（Mamba-2）、5.22x（GDN）和 5.20x（KDA），在 Nemotron 3 Super 上解码吞吐最高提升 2.64x。

Conclusion: 通过离线固定基向量对查询做低秩近似、仅在状态更新时进行一次全状态读取并预计算 sketch，SketchSSM 在不改变状态更新的前提下大幅削减状态读取开销，实现了精度与效率的良好折中，验证了读取路径近似这一思路的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid-attention models replace most softmax attention layers with linear attention, reducing KV-cache growth and enabling larger decode batches where recurrent state access becomes a major bottleneck. ReplaySSM amortizes state updates by buffering keys and values, but each new query still requires a full-state read even though the state remains unchanged between state updates. We observe that low-rank state-weighted query approximation accurately preserves state-read outputs. Although future queries are unknown, the basis vectors used to approximate them can be fixed offline. Based on this observation, we introduce SketchSSM, which preserves full-state updates while approximating reads. At each state update, SketchSSM reads the full state once to precompute outputs for these basis vectors, storing them in a compact sketch. Each subsequent decode step combines the sketch vectors with query-dependent coefficients to reconstruct the output without a full-state read. Across four Mamba-2-, GDN-, and KDA-based models, SketchSSM reduces state-access traffic by approximately 10x while largely preserving average accuracy across four decode benchmarks and recall on four RULER retrieval tasks. On one NVIDIA B300, linear-attention kernel speedups over the standard vLLM baseline reach 7.78x, 5.22x, and 5.20x for Mamba-2, GDN, and KDA, respectively, with up to 2.64x higher decode throughput on Nemotron 3 Super.

</details>


### [191] [Integrating Language Models into Listened and Imagined Speech Decoding from MEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31997)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Sai Samrat Kankanala,Shihab A. Shamma,Sriram Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用配对的听-想象MEG数据集，将语言模型信息在训练和解码两个阶段引入神经解码，发现想象言语解码比听言语解码更受益于语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象言语解码是脑机接口的重要目标，但神经响应弱、信噪比低、数据集有限使其困难。语言模型能提供上下文线索，但其对神经解码的帮助程度，以及在感知与想象言语解码中作用是否不同尚不明确。

Method: 使用配对的听-想象MEG数据集，在两阶段引入语言模型信息：一是训练对比式神经解码器，将MEG表征与声学及上下文语言表征对齐；二是在推理时提出神经约束束搜索框架，结合神经证据与语言模型下一词概率。

Result: 该方法提升了听和想象言语的跨被试词解码。想象言语解码从语言模型中获益更多：神经与语言模型证据的最优平衡向语言模型偏移，相对仅神经解码的增益更大。

Conclusion: 当神经证据较弱时，语言先验最有用，因此对想象言语脑机接口尤为有价值。

Abstract: Decoding imagined speech is an important goal for brain-computer interfaces but remains challenging due to weak neural responses, low signal-to-noise ratio, and limited imagined-speech datasets. Language models provide strong contextual cues for text prediction, but how much they can help neural decoding and whether their contribution differs for decoding perceived and imagined speech remains unclear. To investigate this, we use a paired listened-imagined MEG dataset and incorporate language-model information at two stages. First, we train a contrastive neural decoder that aligns MEG representations with acoustic and contextual language representations, improving cross-subject word decoding for both listened and imagined speech. Second, at inference, we introduce a neural-constrained beam-search framework that combines neural evidence with language-model next-word probabilities. We find that imagined-speech decoding benefits more from the language model than listened-speech decoding. For Imagined speech, the best-performing balance between neural and language-model evidence shifts toward the language model, and the gain over neural-only decoding is larger. Together, these results suggest that language priors are most useful when neural evidence is weaker, making them particularly valuable for imagined-speech BCIs.

</details>


### [192] [FoldAttention: Declared-Reference Softmax for Fast Decode and Deterministic Backward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33410)
*Sriman Achanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoldAttention 提出一种加性 softmax 注意力公式，在扫描 KV 缓存前固定有限参考值 $Z_i$，使每个权重一计算即确定，从而支持无重缩放的加法组合，并在 Hopper 解码与反向传播中显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码需反复读取不断增长的 KV 缓存，注意力成为长上下文下的主要开销。现有高性能核使用 online softmax，在扫描键时才确定某行的归一化参考值，导致先前贡献只是临时的、可能需要重缩放，影响效率与确定性。

Method: 作者利用 softmax 对公共平移的不变性，提出 FoldAttention：一种加性 softmax 注意力形式，在扫描 KV 缓存前固定有限参考 $Z_i$。每个权重 $2^{s_{ij}-Z_i}$ 计算后即为最终值，因此不同键区间的贡献可直接相加，其商等于实数算术下的 softmax 注意力。基于此性质开发两项 Hopper 解码技术：(1) 最终权重在读取键值字节前进行门控，并用每次调用的深度 $T$ 裁剪小于 $2^{-T}$ 的键同时保留其质量；(2) 加性部分可直接组合 split KV 与共享前缀级联而无需重缩放。相同原理也使反向传播确定化：CTA 在归约前将有限的部分梯度舍入到整数网格并按任意顺序相加。

Result: 在 H100 上 $T=16$ 时，FoldAttention 对七个真实模型生成解码速度为最快 BF16 基线的 1.36-2.30 倍，在 MHA 与 GQA 形状上最高达 3.09 倍；七个中的六个误差在最低 BF16 误差的 1.5% 以内；在读取所有键时，匹配误差下快 1.14-1.30 倍。在 Qwen3-8B 上，整个解码步骤最高快 1.46 倍，似然与长上下文准确率与 BF16 核相当。其确定性反向传播比确定性 FlashAttention-3/4 最高快 1.84 倍，比最快非确定性核快 1.05 倍。

Conclusion: 通过固定参考值的加性 softmax 公式，FoldAttention 在保持精度与匹配 BF16 误差的同时，显著加速 Hopper 解码，并消除了确定性反向传播的性能代价。

Abstract: Autoregressive decode repeatedly streams a growing KV cache, making attention a major cost at long context. Existing high-performance kernels use online softmax, which discovers a row's normalization reference as it scans keys. Earlier contributions therefore remain provisional and may require rescaling. We argue that the reference need not be discovered: softmax is invariant to a common shift, so the reference only has to keep the weights in range. We present FoldAttention, an additive formulation of softmax attention that fixes a finite reference $Z_i$ before scanning the KV cache. Each weight $2^{s_{ij}-Z_i}$ is then final when computed, so contributions add across disjoint key ranges and their quotient equals softmax attention in real arithmetic. We use this property to develop two techniques for Hopper decode: (1) final weights gate key and value reads before the bytes are fetched, and a per-call depth $T$ cuts keys below $2^{-T}$ while keeping their mass, and (2) additive partials compose split KV and shared-prefix cascades without rescaling. On H100 at $T=16$, FoldAttention decodes seven real-model generations 1.36-2.30$\times$ faster than the fastest BF16 baseline, and up to 3.09$\times$ faster across MHA and GQA shapes, at an error within 1.5% of the lowest BF16 error on six of the seven; reading every key, it is 1.14-1.30$\times$ faster at matched error. We validate on Qwen3-8B that a whole decode step is up to 1.46$\times$ faster while likelihood and long-context accuracy match those under BF16 kernels. The same principle makes the backward deterministic: CTAs round bounded partial gradients onto an integer grid declared before the reduction and add them in any order. FoldAttention thereby removes the determinism tax: its deterministic backward is up to 1.84$\times$ faster than deterministic FlashAttention-3/4 and 1.05$\times$ faster than the fastest nondeterministic kernel.

</details>


### [193] [VC Dimension and Expressivity of Real-Valued Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31999)
*Gavin Dooley,Andy Yang,Yijia Jessica Zhu,David Chiang,Peter Cholak,Anand Pillay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究softmax注意力多层实数transformer的表达能力，给出其VC维与split VC维的上界（$O(n^4)$、$O(n^6)$），并构造下界（$\Omega(n)$），进而刻画对称函数及实数比特访问的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于transformer能力与局限的结果往往对transformer的定义施加了诸多限制。本文希望在最少的额外假设下，研究作用于实数值的softmax注意力多层transformer的表达能力。

Method: 借助实几何（real geometry）中的结果，对softmax注意力、多层、实数域上的transformer进行分析，推导其VC维与split VC维的上界，并构造具体transformer以给出下界。

Result: 得到VC维上界为$O(n^4)$、split VC维上界为$O(n^6)$（$n$为输入长度），并构造出相应下界$\Omega(n)$。在对称（置换不变）函数类中，transformer能一致表达单符号字母表上的所有函数，非一致表达双符号字母表上的所有函数，但即便非一致也无法表达六符号字母表上的某些函数。还证明了关于transformer能访问实数多少比特的局限。

Conclusion: 在极弱假设下，softmax注意力多层实数transformer的表达能力存在明确的VC维上界与下界，并在对称函数与实数比特访问方面表现出具体的能力边界。

Abstract: Whereas previous results on abilities and limitations of transformers have restricted the definition of transformers in various ways, here we study softmax-attention, multi-layer transformers operating on real values, with very few additional assumptions. Applying results from real geometry, we obtain upper bounds on the VC dimension and split VC dimension of such transformers ($O(n^4)$ and $O(n^6)$, respectively, where $n$ is the input length). Conversely, we also construct specific transformers witnessing lower bounds on these quantities ($Ω(n)$ in each case). These results have some notable consequences. For example, within the class of symmetric (permutation-invariant) functions, we show that transformers can uniformly express all functions over an alphabet of one symbol and non-uniformly express all functions over an alphabet of two symbols, but cannot (even non-uniformly) express some functions over an alphabet of six symbols. We also prove limitations on how many bits of a real number a transformer can access.

</details>


### [194] [QwenGyre: An Elastic Reinforcement Learning Framework for Training xLong-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33848)
*Weiqi Wang,Yuxin Zhou,Mouxiang Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Yi Zhang,Yuyan Luo,Zhiyu Yin,Chencan Wu,Jiemin Jiang,Wentao Yao,Chujie Zheng,JianWei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QwenGyre，一个面向超长时域（xlong-horizon）在线强化学习的端到端框架，通过弹性GPU重分配与轨迹处理，缓解执行方差与轨迹冗余问题，在NL2RepoBench上取得6.0%绝对提升，并实现最高1.85倍与1.78倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体越来越多地执行超长时域任务，单次执行可跨数小时、数百次模型-环境交互，每次rollout接近1M token。对此类执行应用在线强化学习面临两大挑战：一是严重的执行方差与过长的rollout延迟导致大量GPU闲置；二是复杂的非线性分支产生大量轨迹冗余，严重拖累训练效率。

Method: 提出QwenGyre端到端框架。其能够在rollout与训练之间弹性重分配GPU而不中断正在进行的执行；同时其轨迹处理器可重建分支历史、对部分进展进行评分，并对冗余路径去重，以约束训练成本。方法在旗舰模型Qwen 3.8 2.4T上进行扩展，每次rollout使用700K token。

Result: 在NL2RepoBench上仅用48步即取得6.0%的绝对提升（52.5%提升至58.5%）。在多个不同领域训练数据集上的评估中，QwenGyre相较Colocate与Async分别实现最高1.85倍与1.78倍的加速。

Conclusion: QwenGyre有效应对超长时域在线强化学习中的GPU闲置与轨迹冗余问题，在大规模模型与长rollout场景下同时提升了训练效率与下游任务性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly undertake extreme-long (xlong) horizon tasks, where a single execution can span hours, hundreds of model--environment interactions, and nearly 1M tokens per rollout. Applying online reinforcement learning (RL) to such executions poses two fundamental challenges: (1) severe execution variance and prolonged rollout delays cause massive GPU idling; and (2) complex non-linear branching generates massive trajectory redundancy, crippling training efficiency. To address these, we presents QwenGyre, an end-to-end framework for xlong-horizon online RL. QwenGyre elastically reallocates GPUs between rollout and training without interrupting live executions, while its trajectory processor reconstructs branching histories, scores partial progress, and deduplicates redundant paths to bound training costs. Scaled to our flagship model, Qwen~3.8 2.4T, with 700K tokens per rollout, QwenGyre yields a 6.0% absolute gain on NL2RepoBench (52.5% $\to$ 58.5%) in 48 steps. Across our evaluations on diverse domains of training datasets, QwenGyre delivers up to $1.85\times$ and $1.78\times$ speedups over Colocate and Async, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [Is invariance all you need for algorithmic fairness? Removing demographic information can create new bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32004)
*Aditya Parikh,Eike Petersen,Stella Frank,Enzo Ferrante,Melanie Ganz,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"人口统计表示不变性"作为公平性理想目标，指出强制不变性会妨碍偏见缓解甚至制造新偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部模型中编码的人口统计信息常被视为算法偏见的风险因素，因此人口统计表示不变性被推崇为理想状态。但作者认为，虽然人口统计捷径学习确实是威胁，但当人口统计特征与目标标签相关时，一定程度的编码是必要的，因此需要重新审视不变性的合理性。

Method: 作者从数学和实证两方面展开分析，区分了边际表示不变性与类条件表示不变性，并证明它们分别对应标准群体公平概念：人口统计均等与机会均等。随后在五个表格数据集和两个胸部X光影像数据集上，对强制两种不变性类型在预测性能和公平性上的影响进行了理论与实证评估。

Result: 研究表明，强制人口统计不变性可能妨碍偏见缓解，甚至产生新的偏见；边际不变性与类条件不变性分别对应人口统计均等和机会均等，且不变性并非公平性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 人口统计表示不变性既不可取，也不足以保证公平性，需要重新思考将其作为公平性理想目标的普遍假设。

Abstract: Encoded demographic information in internal model representations is a commonly assumed risk factor for algorithmic bias, with demographic representation invariance often being touted as the ideal state. However, while demographic shortcut learning is a genuine threat, some degree of encoding is necessary when demographics correlate with target labels. Here, we show, mathematically and empirically, that enforcing demographic invariance can actually hamper bias mitigation and even create new biases. We distinguish marginal from class-conditional representation invariance, and show that they imply the standard group fairness notions of demographic parity and equalized odds, respectively. We evaluate the effects on predictive performance and fairness of enforcing both invariance types, both theoretically and empirically across five tabular and two chest X-ray imaging datasets. Our findings support our mathematical argument that demographic representation invariance is neither desirable nor sufficient for fairness.

</details>


### [196] [Broken Symmetry in BF16 Attention: Why FlashAttention Gradients Blow Up Late in Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34272)
*Junlin Chen,Daize Dong,Huanwei Di,Haolong Jia,Jiawei Wu,Haotian Xie,Mingkai Zheng,Yang Li,Leshang Chen,Huishu Wang,Eric P. Xing,Hongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现BF16 FlashAttention-3在大规模预训练中会因软最大乘加错误及softmax梯度守恒律被BF16取整破坏而导致梯度爆炸，提出GProj投影修正恢复到FP32级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BF16及FlashAttention等融合注意力内核在大规模预训练中被广泛信任，但作者用FlashAttention-3预训练时发现训练在25B token后梯度范数暴增千倍、loss高出FP32注意力0.2 nats且无NaN，说明现有内核存在未修复的数值稳定问题。

Method: 将两层注意力的反向传播改为FP32验证误差来源；修复前向softmax中的融合乘加问题；针对softmax分数梯度沿每行求和为零的守恒律被BF16取整破坏问题，提出GProj（gauge projection），在cast后每行用两个秩一修正恢复零和。

Result: 修复FMA可阻止梯度爆炸，但query梯度仍错误超过自身大小；GProj将剩余的中位query/key梯度误差从219%/13%降至0.34%/0.37%，与FP32注意力相当，每步训练仅增加4.7%时间。

Conclusion: 在匹配的从头训练中，GProj能达到与FP32注意力相同的loss，而FlashAttention-3和key smoothing均不稳定，证明恢复softmax梯度守恒律对BF16注意力训练稳定性至关重要。

Abstract: BF16 is now standard in large-scale pretraining, including in fused attention kernels such as FlashAttention, and these kernels are widely trusted. When we used FlashAttention-3 to pretrain a 450M-parameter transformer on 50B tokens, however, we ran into a problem: training was healthy for 25B tokens, then the gradient norm grew a thousandfold and the loss ended 0.2 nats above FP32 attention, without a single NaN. Recomputing the attention backward of just two layers in FP32 removes almost all of the excess gradient. Part of the cause is known: a fused multiply-add in the forward softmax, so far treated as an extreme-input NaN case and never fixed in FlashAttention-3. Repairing it stops the blow-up, but the query gradient is still wrong by more than its own size, and training still drives attention logits to thousands of times their size under accurate gradients. The remaining error comes from a broken conservation law. The softmax score gradient sums to zero along every row, which makes the query gradient blind to where the keys sit as a group; rounding it to BF16 leaves a small nonzero sum that leaks the mean key into the gradient, and the leak grows exactly as late training makes keys large and attention sharp. We introduce GProj (gauge projection), which restores the zero sum after the cast with two rank-one corrections per row. It cuts the remaining median query/key gradient errors from 219%/13% to 0.34%/0.37%, on par with FP32 attention, for 4.7% more time per training step. In matched from-scratch runs it trains to the same loss as FP32 attention, while FlashAttention-3 and key smoothing both destabilize.

</details>


### [197] [Human Activity Recognition via Ultra-Wideband Data: A Framework for Dimensionality Reduction, Pattern Discovery, and Predictive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32008)
*Nahid Sahel Gozin,Reza Sedaghat,Prathap Siddavaatam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出融合聚类、多项式扩展与Incremental PCA的CPE-IPCA及PPSA两种降维方法，结合频繁模式挖掘与分类器，实现UWB雷达六类人体活动100%高精度识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB雷达数据存在高维、噪声大、复杂及非线性等问题，在计算资源受限的实时系统中难以高效处理。研究旨在高效压缩数据、挖掘显著模式，并高精度分类六种活动类型，服务于医疗、辅助生活及智能环境。

Method: 提出两种降维技术：CPE-IPCA（聚类+多项式特征扩展+增量PCA）和PPSA（PCA后标准化）。使用Apriori与FP-Growth挖掘频繁模式，再用随机森林和向量空间模型（VSM）进行分类。

Result: CPE-IPCA仅用50个成分即保留100%方差；PPSA用80个成分保留99.1%方差，压缩与计算效率优于传统非线性方法。随机森林在CPE-IPCA上达100%准确率、PPSA上99%；VSM在PPSA上精确率、召回率、F1均达100%，在CPE-IPCA上接近完美（精确率1.00，召回率0.98-1.00，F1 0.99-1.00）。

Conclusion: 该框架构建了快速、可解释且鲁棒的人体活动识别系统，适用于医疗、辅助生活与智能环境应用。

Abstract: Recent advances in sensor technology have enabled more effective human activity recognition (HAR), particularly in real-time systems with limited computational resources. However, Ultra-Wideband (UWB) radar data remain challenging due to high dimensionality, noise, complexity, and nonlinear characteristics. This research proposes a framework to efficiently reduce data size, uncover significant patterns, and classify six activity types (Standff, Liedown, Noactivity, Sit, Stand, and Walk) from UWB signals with high accuracy. Two novel dimensionality reduction techniques are introduced in this paper. The first, Clustered Polynomial Expansion with Incremental PCA (CPE-IPCA), combines clustering and polynomial feature expansion with Incremental PCA, preserving 100% of the variance in only 50 components. The second, Post-PCA Standardization Approach (PPSA), standardizes data after PCA and retains 99.1% of the variance in 80 components, achieving superior compression and computational efficiency compared to conventional nonlinear methods. Frequent patterns are identified using Apriori and FP-Growth, which are then classified with Random Forest and a Vector Space Model (VSM). The framework achieves 100% accuracy with Random Forest on CPE-IPCA and 99% on PPSA, while VSM attains 100% precision, recall, and F1 on PPSA and near-perfect performance on CPE-IPCA (precision 1.00, recall 0.98-1.00, F1 0.99-1.00), demonstrating a fast, interpretable, and robust HAR system suitable for healthcare, assisted living, and smart environments.

</details>


### [198] [Spexis: Speculative Lookahead Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34370)
*Hyungyu Jung,Jaehyeok Yu,Hoonseo Choi,Sungkyun Kim,Jinho Lee,Jiwon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spexis是一个多GPU LLM推理框架，通过将推测执行与正常执行并行运行引入新的并行轴，在不增加KV-cache内存的情况下提升流水线和张量并行效率，在多种GPU配置下最高取得34%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU LLM推理中流水线并行与张量并行存在瓶颈。传统推测解码仅用于加速token生成，未挖掘其在并行调度和内存效率方面的潜力。

Method: Spexis将推测执行与正常执行并行运行，引入新的并行轴且不增加KV-cache内存占用，从而提高内存效率并缓解多GPU推理瓶颈；同时采用lookahead调度预测推测质量与未来内存压力，减少无效推测、KV-cache驱逐和重计算。基于vLLM实现。

Result: 在多种GPU配置下大幅提升服务性能，相比采用流水线与张量并行最优组合的基线最高加速34%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将推测执行作为独立并行轴并与前瞻调度结合，可有效提升多GPU LLM推理的内存与服务效率，为并行策略设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Spexis is a multi-GPU LLM inference framework that improves the efficiency of pipeline and tensor parallelism through speculative parallelism. Rather than using speculative decoding only to accelerate token generation, Spexis runs speculation in parallel with normal execution, introducing a new parallelism axis without increasing KV-cache memory usage. This improves memory efficiency and helps mitigate the bottlenecks of multi-GPU inference.
  Spexis further uses lookahead scheduling to predict speculation quality and future memory pressure, allowing it to reduce wasted speculation, KV-cache eviction, and recomputation. Built on top of vLLM, Spexis largely improves serving performance across a range of GPU configurations, achieving speedups of up to 34% over a baseline that uses the optimal combination of pipeline and tensor parallelism. Spexis's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mlsys-seo/spexis.

</details>


### [199] [Representation Learning for Exact Preimages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32018)
*Konstantin Hess,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIO 提出一种"原像因子化"框架，使前向模型保持高表达能力，同时让原像具备显式几何表示，从而支持可追踪的全局逆向优化（线性目标下存在闭式解），并证明其通用逼近能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程任务需要反向推理：给定性能或安全水平，刻画所有满足该水平的输入集合（原像），并在其上优化。但表达力强的神经预测器通常没有显式原像表示，恢复与优化代价高昂，形成"前向表达力—原像近似精度—下游优化可追踪性"的三难问题。

Method: 提出 TRIO 框架，核心为原像因子化：前向模型通过非线性径向变换（含神经网络）保持表达力；在求逆时每个变换退化为单个标量半径，从而得到简单的几何水平集，形成可复用的显式几何表示。对线性目标给出闭式全局解。

Result: 原像具有显式几何表示，可复用于下游优化；线性目标下可闭式求解全局最优；并证明通用逼近定理：TRIO 能以任意精度逼近任意连续前向映射及其全部可能不连通、非凸的原像族。

Conclusion: TRIO 在设计层面同时实现了表达性强的前向建模、精确的原像恢复以及可追踪的全局原像优化，三者兼顾而非相互妥协。

Abstract: Modern neural predictors can model highly nonlinear maps, but many scientific and engineering tasks require reasoning in the opposite direction: given a performance or safety level, the goal is to characterize the preimage, that is, the complete set of inputs which meet the desired target level and optimize over that set. For expressive neural predictors, however, such preimages typically have no explicit representation and are expensive to recover or optimize over. This creates a fundamental three-way challenge between expressive forward prediction, accurate preimage approximation, and tractable optimization over the preimage for downstream tasks. We introduce TRIO (tractable representations for preimage learning and inverse optimization), a framework for learning representations that make these objectives compatible by construction. Our key contribution is a preimage factorization: the forward model remains expressive through nonlinear radial transformations (including neural networks), while, under inversion, each transformation reduces to a single scalar radius, which yields simple geometric level sets. This yields an explicit geometric representation that is reusable for downstream optimization over the preimage, and, for linear objectives, we show that this admits a closed-form global solution. We finally prove a universal approximation theorem which shows that TRIO can approximate any continuous forward map and its entire family of potentially disconnected, nonconvex preimages arbitrarily well. Hence, TRIO combines expressive forward modeling, exact preimage recovery, and tractable global downstream optimization over preimages by design.

</details>


### [200] [E3J: An Efficient and Open-Source Backend for Euclidean Equivariant Operations on GPU and TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35099)
*Olivier Peltre,Armand Picard,Adrien Pichard,Miguel Bragança,Luca Giacomoni,Valentin Heyraud,Zachary Weller-Davies,Christoph Brunken,Jules Tilly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: e3j 是一个面向几何深度学习的快速欧几里得等变性后端，提供 GPU/TPU 的 JAX 绑定，在张量积与消息传递卷积中显著提速，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变性深度学习后端在吞吐与运行效率上存在瓶颈，且难以在 TPU 架构上实现大规模等变负载，需要一个高效、开源、跨硬件的解决方案。

Method: 结合优化的 CUDA 与 Pallas 内核及算法改进，构建支持 GPU 和 TPU 的 JAX 后端；在 MLIP 用例（MACE 水盒子 NPT 模拟）上进行基准测试，并对比 cuEquivariance、e3nn-jax 等后端。

Result: MLIP 用例中相较 cuEquivariance 最高提速 34%；张量积运算达到 H100 最大内存带宽 80% 以上；消息传递卷积前向吞吐常翻倍；TPUv6e 上达到 80% 以上内存带宽，较 e3nn-jax 提升最多一个数量级。

Conclusion: e3j 在 GPU 与 TPU 上均实现领先的吞吐与运行效率，为大规模等变深度学习工作负载提供了可行路径，并以 Apache 2.0 开源许可证发布。

Abstract: We present e3j, a fast Euclid-equivariance backend for geometric deep learning applications with JAX bindings for GPU and TPU. Leveraging both optimized CUDA and Pallas kernels and algorithmic improvements, the library achieves state-of-the-art throughput and runtime on both forward and backward paths. On a machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) use case, it outperforms established backends, measuring up to 34% speed-up over cuEquivariance on water box NPT simulation using MACE, while remaining fully open source. E3j achieves over 80% efficiency over the H100 maximum memory bandwidth on tensor product operations, and in many cases more than doubles throughput of message passing convolutions forward compared to previously available backends. In addition, with the release of dedicated Pallas TPU kernel, e3j opens the possibility of large scale equivariant deep learning workloads on TPU architectures, which has so far been difficult to achieve. Our benchmarks show that e3j also achieves over 80% of a TPUv6e memory bandwidth, up to one order of magnitude more than e3nn-jax. The library is available on GitHub, PyPI and is released under an open source Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [201] [Interactive Distributionally Robust Multi-Agent Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32048)
*Debamita Ghosh,George K. Atia,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一般和分布式鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（DRMGs）的在线学习问题，提出无模型框架RoMEX-φ，通过函数对偶表示与均衡探索实现可扩展的鲁棒多智能体强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型误设定是多智能体强化学习的核心挑战，智能体间的策略交互会放大转移不确定性。分布式鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（DRMGs）提供了应对此类不确定性的原则性框架，但现有方法往往依赖限制性假设，且难以扩展到大状态空间和联合动作空间。

Method: 针对带一般函数逼近和φ-散度不确定性集的一般和DRMGs，提出RoMEX-φ框架，融合基于均衡的探索与对偶拟合学习。通过鲁棒多智能体贝尔曼算子的函数对偶表示，利用中心化经验鲁棒差异实现从名义交互数据中进行可处理的最坏情况价值估计。并引入鲁棒多智能体解耦系数（robust MADC）刻画由策略交互和对抗性转移不确定性引起的内在探索复杂度。

Result: 建立了由robust MADC控制的次线性鲁棒遗憾保证，而非显式依赖状态与联合动作空间规模，用内在函数类复杂度替代表格依赖性。在可扩展的一般和DRMG（全变差不确定性）上的数值实验表明，RoMEX-φ对转移偏移的鲁棒性显著优于非鲁棒对应方法，同时与精确表格鲁棒基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 本研究为具有一般函数逼近的分布式鲁棒多智能体强化学习提供了一个可扩展框架。

Abstract: Model misspecification poses a fundamental challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where transition uncertainty can be amplified by strategic interactions among agents. Distributionally robust Markov games (DRMGs) provide a principled framework for addressing such uncertainty, yet existing methods often rely on restrictive assumptions or scale poorly to large state and joint action spaces. We study online learning in general-sum DRMGs with general function approximation and $φ$-divergence uncertainty sets. We propose RoMEX-$φ$, a model-free framework that integrates equilibrium-based exploration with dual fitted learning. Through a functional dual representation of the robust multi-agent Bellman operator, RoMEX-$φ$ enables tractable worst-case value estimation from nominal interaction data using a centered empirical robust discrepancy. We introduce the robust Multi-Agent Decoupling Coefficient (robust MADC) to characterize the intrinsic exploration complexity arising from strategic interactions and adversarial transition uncertainty. We establish sublinear robust regret guarantees governed by the robust MADC rather than explicitly by the state and joint action space sizes, replacing tabular dependence with intrinsic function-class complexity. Numerical experiments on a scalable general-sum DRMG under total variation uncertainty show that RoMEX-$φ$ is substantially more resilient to transition shifts than its non-robust counterpart while remaining competitive with an exact tabular robust baseline. Our results provide a scalable framework for distributionally robust multi-agent reinforcement learning with general function approximation.

</details>


### [202] [EdgeCraft: Automated Model Crafting for Edge IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35167)
*Genglin Wang,Kaiwei Liu,Liekang Zeng,Wangsong Yin,Shangcheng Jin,Guoliang Xing,Zhenyu Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeCraft 是一个 LLM 驱动的系统，可将高层意图自动转化为可部署的边缘机器学习产物，通过约束感知合成树和多保真验证器应对动态 SLO 与低成本设备验证挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘 IoT 场景中部署 ML 需在数据表示、模型设计、领域数据训练与运行时定制等巨大搜索空间中导航，流程碎片化且难以扩展。

Method: 提出 EdgeCraft：(1) 约束感知合成树，探索候选方案并依据实测 SLO 差距引导改进；(2) 多保真验证器，逐步结合低成本检查与完整目标设备验证，并复用已验证失败；(3) 多租户运行时，并行执行云端训练与设备验证并隔离请求。

Result: 在 50 个公开任务中，40 个任务的最佳观测质量超过任务特定参考，45 个任务找到 SLO 可行产物，两者在 38 个任务上重叠；在自采集 SEN 数据集上表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: EdgeCraft 能高效生成满足任务质量、时延与能耗 SLO 的可部署边缘 ML 产物，并具备向真实 IoT 感知任务推广的潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) increasingly powers Internet of Things (IoT) applications at the edge. Yet producing a deployable edge ML artifact for a specific scenario requires navigating a huge search space spanning data representation, model design, training on domain-specific data, and runtime customization. This workflow is fragmented and difficult to scale across diverse edge applications.
  We present EdgeCraft, an LLM-driven system that turns high-level intent into deployable edge ML artifacts. Building such a system raises two challenges: (1) How can an LLM be guided to find high-quality solutions that meet dynamic SLOs for task quality, latency, and energy? (2) How can trustworthy target-device verification be obtained at low cost? EdgeCraft addresses these challenges with two designs. (1) A constraint-aware synthesis tree explores alternative candidates and uses measured SLO gaps to guide each improvement. (2) A multi-fidelity verifier progressively combines low-cost checks with full target-device verification to reduce verification cost while preserving reliable verification results. It also records verified failures for reuse, avoiding repeated device work. To support concurrency, EdgeCraft provides a multi-tenant runtime that runs cloud training and target-device verification in parallel while isolating requests. Across 50 public tasks, EdgeCraft exceeds the task-specific Reference in best-observed quality on 40 tasks and finds an SLO-feasible artifact on 45, with the two outcomes overlapping on 38 tasks. Moreover, EdgeCraft achieves competitive performance on our self-collected SEN dataset, suggesting its generalizability to real-world IoT sensing tasks.

</details>


### [203] [Graph Forward Distribution Matching for Molecular Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32056)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Brett Savoie,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphFDM 提出一种基于前向过程的在线强化学习范式，用于图扩散模型的分子逆向设计，实现多属性精确控制且保持化学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法将图扩散的反向采样视为序列策略，用单一终端奖励优化数百个耦合决策，导致训练不稳定、有效性崩溃且属性提升有限。论文旨在在不牺牲化学有效性的前提下实现对多个属性的精确控制。

Method: GraphFDM（图前向分布匹配）通过前向过程进行优化，利用有效生成样本定义奖励倾斜的目标分布，并针对每个属性条件在图规模和分子结构上联合优化，将强化信号融入监督学习而不存储反向轨迹。论文推导了唯一最优目标，证明了条件式改进保证，并指出标准图扩散的固定图规模先验会带来不可消除的匹配差距。

Result: 在多条件聚合物和小分子生成任务中，GraphFDM 在每个目标属性上取得最低 MAE，相对最强基线最多降低 53.0%，化学有效性高于 0.99，并能泛化到分布外属性组合。

Conclusion: GraphFDM 通过前向分布匹配的在线强化学习范式，克服了反向策略优化的不稳定性与有效性崩溃问题，实现了多属性分子生成的高精度控制与良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Achieving precise control over multiple properties without sacrificing chemical validity remains a central challenge in molecular inverse design. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods fine-tune graph diffusion models by treating **reverse** sampling as a sequential policy, using a single terminal reward to optimize hundreds of coupled decisions. They often suffer from instability, validity collapse, and limited property gains. We introduce GraphFDM (Graph Forward Distribution Matching), a new online RL paradigm for graph diffusion that performs optimization through the **forward** process. GraphFDM uses valid generations to define a reward-tilted target distribution jointly optimized over graph size and molecular structure for each property condition, incorporating reinforcement signals into supervised learning without storing reverse trajectories. We derive the unique optimal target, prove a condition-wise improvement guarantee, and show that the fixed graph-size prior of standard graph diffusion leaves an irreducible matching gap. In multi-conditional polymer and small-molecule generation, GraphFDM achieves the lowest MAE on every target property, with reductions of up to 53.0\% relative to the strongest baselines and chemical validity above 0.99. It further generalizes to out-of-distribution property combinations.

</details>


### [204] [SOLO: Pretraining Billion-Parameter Language Models with Shared-Output Local Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35440)
*Bojian Yin,Shurong Wang,Yuqi Pan,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLO是一种共享输出的局部学习方法，用最终模块读头的只读共享副本替代各模块私有读头，在3.4亿至20亿参数Transformer预训练上接近反向传播精度，并显著降低激活内存与提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播虽能全局协调各层梯度，但要求每层持有激活并等待梯度回传，造成更新锁定；传统局部学习消除了这种锁定，却因每个模块使用私有读头、无法获得深层模块信息而难以扩展到十亿参数级预训练。

Method: 提出Shared-Output LOcal learning（SOLO）：将各模块私有的读头替换为最终模块读头的共享只读副本（取自上一步），该读头是唯一在全网络输出上训练的。副本传递来自最终模块的信息，但模块间不传梯度，也不重新引入更新锁定；同时不含更新锁定使每个流水阶段只需为O(1)个微批次保留激活，从而允许更大微批次。

Result: 在3.4亿至20亿参数、150亿token预训练的Transformer上，SOLO平均零样本准确率与反向传播相差不到1个百分点，困惑度差距随规模增大而缩小；读头消融表明性能提升来自共享机制；无更新锁定使每阶段激活内存从O(p)降至O(1)个微批次，吞吐最高达同分区流水反向传播最优实测值的1.44倍。

Conclusion: SOLO是首个在十亿参数级语言模型预训练中展示内存与吞吐收益的局部学习方法，使局部学习成为大规模预训练中反向传播的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models are trained with backpropagation, whose global gradient coordinates all layers but forces each to hold its activations and wait for the gradient to pass back through every deeper layer. Conventional local learning removes this update locking by training each module to predict the target through its own readout, but has not scaled to billion-parameter pretraining.
