We identify these private readouts as a key weakness, since they leave each module without information from deeper modules. We propose Shared-Output LOcal learning (SOLO), which replaces them with a shared, read-only copy of the final module's readout, the only one trained on the output of the whole network. Taken from the previous step, the copy transmits information from the final module without passing gradients between modules or reintroducing update locking. SOLO approaches backpropagation on Transformers of 340M to 2B parameters pretrained on 15B tokens, staying within one point in average zero-shot accuracy with a perplexity gap that narrows with scale. Readout ablations attribute SOLO's improvement over private readouts to sharing. Without update locking, each of p pipeline stages holds activations for O(1) micro-batches instead of O(p). The freed memory permits larger micro-batches, which reach up to 1.44x the best measured throughput of pipeline backpropagation on the same partition. To our knowledge, SOLO is the first local learning method to show such memory and throughput gains in billion-parameter language-model pretraining. Local learning thus becomes a practical alternative to backpropagation for large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [205] [Depth Laws for the Precision Floor of Trained Neural Networks: Amplification, Residual Scaling, and a Quantization-Aware Training Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32060)
*Ahmad S. Tarawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究神经网络在精度崩溃前所需的比特数（精度下限），提出以预测放大 $G$ 为核心的一阶理论 η_c=Λ/G，并验证其随深度增长的规律及量化训练悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化条件下网络精度何时崩溃、该临界值如何随深度变化尚缺乏统一理论，作者希望用一个简洁的物理量预测这一精度下限。

Method: 在 MLP、CNN、ViT 及九个预训练语言模型上，分别采用训练后量化（PTQ）与量化/噪声感知训练（QAT），以一阶理论刻画精度下限 η_c=Λ/G，并分析深度指数关系及残差分支缩放与预归一化的影响。

Result: 深度指数等式 α_PTQ=ρ 在 13 个训练架构中的 12 个及 12–48 层的 GPT-2 上以 95% 区间成立，Λ=1.45±14%；1/√D 缩放残差与预归一化可消除深度惩罚；QAT 悖论显示噪声感知训练增益随深度衰减，α_QAT/α_PTQ=1.45–1.47。

Conclusion: 精度下限由预测放大 G 决定，且随深度按幂律增加；残差缩放与预归一化可消除深度代价，而噪声感知训练会随深度加深反而使深度律变陡。

Abstract: How many bits does a network need before its accuracy collapses, and how does this grow with depth? We study the precision floor, the perturbation level or bit-width at which accuracy falls halfway to chance, in MLPs, CNNs, Vision Transformers and nine pretrained language models, under post-training quantization (PTQ) and quantization- or noise-aware training (QAT). (i) A first-order theory sets the floor through one full-precision quantity, the predictive amplification $G$: $η_c=Λ/G$, and $G^2$ grows linearly in depth at a rate proportional to the squared residual branch scale. (ii) The predicted equality $α_{PTQ}=ρ$ of depth exponents holds within 95% intervals in twelve of thirteen trained architectures and in GPT-2 from 12 to 48 layers, with $Λ=1.45\pm14\%$ across trained architectures. (iii) Residual branches scaled by $1/\sqrt{D}$ and pre-normalisation remove the depth penalty, and each quantizer turns noise into bits at a rate fixed by its step rule, giving $b_c=(α/γ)\log_2 D+C$. (iv) A QAT paradox: noise-aware training roughly doubles the tolerable noise of shallow networks, but the gain decays with depth, so the depth law steepens ($α_{QAT}/α_{PTQ}=1.45$-$1.47$ on two datasets, ten seeds each). Decision margins, cross-layer error cancellation and heavy tails do not set the floor.

</details>


### [206] [When Should a Human Take Back Control? Optimal Delegation under Turbulent AI Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32083)
*Haoze Yan,Julien Roze,Ved Upadhyay,Unal Tatar,Thibaut Mastrolia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个连续时间框架，用于在AI风险事件自激发（聚集）的情形下学习自适应的人类监督与委托策略，并提出Hawkes-PPO算法，在合成环境中优于固定策略并接近全信息oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署AI系统时需要决定何时委托任务、何时由人类介入监控与缓解风险。当失败聚集时（一次幻觉或有害输出可触发后续错误，形成高风险期），这些决策变得困难。现有监督与委托方法虽以历史为条件，但未建模事件聚集及监督努力对其的抑制，也未将委托决策联合建模，本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用自激（Hawkes）过程刻画风险事件如何提高后续事件的发生概率，使事件时机与历史成为决策核心。将问题表述为随机控制问题，联合考虑人类动作、监控努力，以及在“人机协作运行”与“完全AI委托”之间的切换，在运营收益与监督成本、级联AI失败及诱发不确定性之间权衡。人类参与被视为风险管理的内生组成部分：策略同时决定何时需要监督以及分配多少努力。研究了一种松弛的切换形式，并提出Hawkes-PPO——一种策略梯度方法，使用观测事件时间的指数滤波器组。

Result: 在合成环境中，该方法取得了比两种固定机制更高的风险调整目标值，并接近一个近似全信息oracle。数值仿真展示了级联风险如何影响干预与委托决策，说明切换策略与Hawkes-PPO算法在时间上高效监控、减少湍流风险发生与成本方面的优势。

Conclusion: 本文将强化学习与AI系统的自适应人类监督相连接，表明在自激风险动态下联合优化委托决策与监督努力具有价值，所提切换策略和Hawkes-PPO可有效降低风险事件与成本。

Abstract: Deploying AI systems requires deciding when to delegate tasks and when humans should intervene to monitor and mitigate risk induced by AI operations. These decisions are challenging when failures cluster: a hallucination or harmful output can trigger further errors, creating periods of elevated risk. We introduce a continuous-time framework for learning adaptive human oversight under such turbulent AI risk. Existing oversight and delegation formulations condition on history but do not model incident clustering, or its suppression by supervision effort, jointly with the delegation decision and this study fixes this gap. The self-exciting dynamics capture how risk events increase the likelihood of subsequent events, making their timing and history central to decision-making. We formulate a stochastic control problem combining human actions, monitoring effort, and switching between human-AI-assisted operation and full AI delegation, balancing operational rewards against oversight costs, and cascading AI-failures and induced uncertainty. Human participation is an endogenous component of risk management: the policy determines both when oversight is needed and how much effort to allocate. We study a relaxed switching formulation and propose Hawkes-PPO, a policy-gradient method that uses a bank of exponential filters of observed incident times. In a synthetic environment it attains a higher risk-adjusted objective than either fixed regime and approaches an approximate full-information oracle. We illustrate our results with numerical simulations by examining how cascade risks influence intervention and delegation, connecting reinforcement learning with adaptive human oversight of AI systems. In particular, we illustrate the benefit of our switching strategy and Hawkes-PPO algorithm to monitor the project efficiently along time, reducing turbulent risks occurrences and costs.

</details>


### [207] [Emergent One-Third Scaling Law as Attention Tries to Concentrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32100)
*Yizhou Liu,Sara Kangaslahti,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过玩具模型证明：任何学习尖锐分布的softmax都会产生以1/3指数增长的logit幅度，成为训练瓶颈并使损失按1/3幂律衰减；实验证实LLM损失缩放符合该预测，且瓶颈来自注意力头而非语言建模头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律（训练越久性能越好，呈幂律关系）是当今大语言模型的核心，但其起源仍存争议。近期有研究提出幂律可源于单个softmax头学习尖锐分布时的强非线性，但当模型包含多个softmax函数（如LLM）时情况尚不清楚，本文旨在解答这一问题。

Method: 作者使用玩具模型进行分析，考察任意位置的softmax在学 习尖锐分布时的logit动态；随后在真实LLM中检验多个softmax是否学习尖锐分布，并将LLM的损失缩放曲线与1/3幂律预测进行对比，同时分析logit增长动态以定位瓶颈来源。

Result: 玩具模型显示，任何学习尖锐分布的softmax都会使logit幅度按指数1/3幂律增长，其损失贡献也以相同指数1/3幂律衰减，因此只要至少一个softmax学到尖锐分布，总损失就遵循1/3缩放。实验确认LLM中许多softmax确实学习尖锐分布，且其损失缩放与该1/3预测相符。logit增长动态进一步表明，注意力头（而非语言建模头）才是驱动LLM中1/3损失缩放的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 注意力机制努力集中于特定信息——这正是Transformer的核心——可能同时也是训练神经缩放定律的核心。

Abstract: The neural scaling law relating longer training to better performance through a power law is central to today's large language models (LLMs), yet its origin remains debated. One recent proposal is that power laws can emerge from the strong non-linearity of a single softmax head learning peaked distributions. What happens with multiple softmax functions, as in LLMs, is unclear. Here, we show through toy models that any softmax learning peaked distributions, regardless of its position in the model, can develop logit magnitudes that grow in a power law with exponent $1/3$, becoming a training bottleneck whose loss contribution decays as a power law with the same exponent $1/3$. The overall loss therefore obeys $1/3$ scaling whenever at least one softmax learns peaked distributions. We confirm that many softmax functions in LLMs learn peaked distributions and that LLM loss scaling matches this $1/3$ prediction. Moreover, logit growth dynamics reveal that attention heads, rather than the language modeling head, are the bottleneck likely driving the $1/3$ loss scaling in LLMs. Attention trying to concentrate on specific information, which is the heart of Transformers, may therefore also be the heart of the neural scaling law of training.

</details>


### [208] [LLM Unlearning Evaluation with TRIAGE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32103)
*Danial Ataee,Peter Triantafillou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TRIAGE 框架，从内部表征视角评估机器遗忘，通过 Fisher 信息与 Hessian 的近似分析参数敏感性与局部曲率变化，并利用遗忘/邻近保留/通用保留划分量化"邻接差距"，将遗忘算法更新分类为四类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能记住隐私或有害信息，机器遗忘方法旨在移除目标知识同时保留其他能力。但现有评估主要依赖行为基准，只能判断模型"是否看起来遗忘了"，难以揭示遗忘如何改变模型本身、以及对相关知识的影响。

Method: TRIAGE 是一个与基准无关的评估框架：使用 Fisher 信息与 Hessian 的对角近似来测量参数敏感性和局部曲率的变化；采用遗忘 / 邻近保留 / 通用保留三分区量化语义相关知识中的"邻接差距"；并根据变化幅度与分布将算法更新分类为无操作、部分局部化、附带损害主导、全局破坏四类。实验覆盖 12 种遗忘方法、4 个语言模型及 WMDP、TOFU、MUSE 三个基准。

Result: 实验发现，行为上遗忘效果相似的方法，其内部变化和附带损害模式可能显著不同；这些内部特征在不同模型和基准间也存在差异，说明遗忘效果并非仅由遗忘算法本身决定。

Conclusion: TRIAGE 可与现有遗忘基准配合使用，用模型内部视角补充行为评估，刻画遗忘如何重塑参数空间并影响保留知识。

Abstract: Large language models can memorize private or harmful information, motivating machine unlearning methods that remove targeted knowledge while preserving other capabilities. However, existing evaluations rely primarily on behavioral benchmarks, which assess \emph{whether} a model appears to forget but provide limited insight into \emph{how} unlearning changes the model or affects related knowledge. We introduce \textit{TRIAGE} (\textit{Tripartite Representation-internal Introspection for Adjacency Gap Evaluation}), a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework for characterizing these changes. TRIAGE uses diagonal approximations of the Fisher information and Hessian to measure changes in parameter sensitivity and local curvature, and utilizes a Forget / \emph{Adjacent-Retain} / \emph{Generic-Retain} partition to quantify an \emph{adjacency gap} in semantically related knowledge. Based on the magnitude and distribution of these changes, TRIAGE further classifies each algorithm's update as \emph{no-op}, \emph{partially localized}, \emph{collateral dominant}, or \emph{globally destructive}. Across 12 unlearning methods, four language models, and the WMDP, TOFU, and MUSE benchmarks, we find that methods with similar behavioral forgetting can produce substantially different internal changes and patterns of collateral damage. These signatures also vary across models and benchmarks, indicating that the effects of unlearning are not determined solely by the unlearning algorithm. TRIAGE can be applied alongside existing unlearning benchmarks to complement behavioral evaluation with a model-internal view of how unlearning reshapes the model's parameter space and affects retained knowledge.

</details>


### [209] [An Attention-Driven Heterogeneous GNN Model for Credit Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32106)
*Kathiresan Jayabalan,Sethuraman Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出结合SMOTE-Tomek数据平衡技术与异构图神经网络（HGNN）的信用卡欺诈检测框架，在Kaggle数据集上达到99.97%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球向无现金经济转型使信用卡成为数字交易核心，但依赖度上升导致信用卡欺诈风险加剧。欺诈检测面临三大难点：欺诈模式不断变化、数据极度不平衡、需同时识别正常与欺诈交易。

Method: 采用Kaggle信用卡欺诈检测数据集，先用SMOTE-Tomek技术平衡高度不平衡的数据集，再用异构图神经网络（HGNN）进行分类。HGNN以异构图结构表示各类交易，通过基于注意力的消息传递机制捕捉复杂关系、时间因素和用户行为。

Result: HGNN模型取得99.97%准确率、99.48% F1分数、99.15%精确率和98.97%召回率。SMOTE-Tomek的整合提升了识别欺诈交易的能力并降低了假阳性率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明该模型有效，可应用于真实世界的信用卡欺诈检测场景。

Abstract: The global transition to a cashless economy has placed credit cards as the key element of digital transactions, acclaimed for their easy use, speed, and acceptance in most places. However, the growing dependence on this payment method has led to an escalation of the risks associated with credit card (CC) fraud. Detecting this type of fraud is a difficult task because the patterns are constantly changing, there is a data imbalance, and it is necessary to identify the legitimate transactions and the fraud ones at the same time. This study addresses this challenge by proposing a credit card fraud detection (CCFD) framework using a data balancing technique and a deep learning (DL) model. The proposed fraud detection model is trained and evaluated by collecting the dataset called Credit Card Fraud Detection from the Kaggle repository. As the dataset is highly imbalanced, we utilized the Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE)-Tomek technique to balance the dataset. Further, the balanced dataset is classified using the Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (HGNN) model. The HGNN model represent various transactions using a heterogeneous graph architecture and by using an attention based message passing technique, it managed to consider the complex relationships, time factors, and user behavior. The integration of SMOTE-Tomek in the model further boosted its capacity to identify fraudulent transactions, while lowering the rate of false positives. The HGNN model attained a 99.97% accuracy, a 99.48% F1-score, a 99.15% precision, and a 98.97% recall. The findings indicates that this model is effective and can be applied to real-world CCFD scenarios.

</details>


### [210] [SAMBAR: Selective Anchoring via Method of Multipliers for Balanced Knowledge Acquisition and Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32108)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Xunlan Zhou,Hongrui Zhao,Ziyu Chen,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAMBAR 是一种无需回放旧任务演示的持续学习算法，通过将持续学习建模为约束优化问题并用乘子法求解，结合对关键参数的选择性锚定，在 VLA 微调中防止灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型部署后需持续学习新任务，但对下游任务直接微调会导致灾难性遗忘，破坏早期任务与通用能力。现有持续学习方法多依赖回放旧任务演示数据，而旧数据往往不可得，因此需要一种无回放的持续学习方案。

Method: 将持续学习建模为约束优化问题，采用乘子法求解：利用对偶变量在约束违反累积时提高惩罚，驱动策略学习新任务同时避免模型参数偏离旧值过远；并选择性地锚定对先前任务关键的参数，保留旧知识，其余参数自由用于新任务学习。对偶变量与选择性锚定共同平衡知识获取与知识保持。

Result: 在 LIBERO 仿真基准和真实硬件上评估。顺序微调 VLA 时，所对比的每一个无回放基线都会完全遗忘其学习的第一个任务，而 SAMBAR 能保留其学过的所有任务。

Conclusion: SAMBAR 无需任何旧任务演示即可在 VLA 持续微调中有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在仿真与真实硬件上均能保留所有已学任务，优于现有无回放基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale pretraining to ultimately achieve generalist manipulation. Deployed VLA policies must support continual learning to acquire new tasks over time. Teaching a VLA a new task generally requires finetuning it on demonstrations of that task. However, naively finetuning on downstream tasks causes the policy to forget earlier tasks and degrades generalist capabilities. This failure is known as catastrophic forgetting. Most continual learning methods counter it by replaying data from earlier tasks. However, the old task demonstrations are not always readily available. In this paper, we introduce SAMBAR, a continual learning algorithm that prevents catastrophic forgetting during VLA finetuning without requiring access to the demonstrations of any previously learned task. We propose to cast continual learning as a constrained optimization problem and solve it with the method of multipliers. In our approach, the method of multipliers drives the policy to learn the new task without the model parameters drifting far away from their previous values. In contrast to a standard regularization penalty, the method of multipliers raises the penalty as the constraint violation accumulates by using a dual variable. We also selectively anchor the parameters critical to previous tasks to preserve past knowledge, leaving other parameters free for new task acquisition. The combination of dual variable and selective anchoring, therefore, balances knowledge acquisition with knowledge retention. We evaluate our method, SAMBAR, on the LIBERO simulation benchmark and on hardware. When sequentially finetuning on a VLA, every replay-free baseline we compare against completely forgets the first task it learned, whereas SAMBAR retains every task it has learned.

</details>


### [211] [How Reusable Are Benchmarks with Richer Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32109)
*Youssef Allouah,John Duchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基准测试能否在多准则自适应反馈下可靠指导模型选择，发现所需测试集规模随准则数指数增长，且攻击多任务大模型基准可制造大量虚假优胜者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者会根据评测反馈在多个准则上反复调整模型，因此需要检验基准测试在选择模型时是否仍然可靠。已有研究曾观察到基准复用较为可靠，并将其归因于开发者主要响应相对当前最优的显著改进，但这一解释在丰富反馈场景下是否成立尚不清楚。

Method: 作者从理论分析出发，考察在任意准则凸组合下、从k个自适应选择的模型中估计最佳分数所需的最坏情况测试集规模；并对具有五到十个准则的多任务大语言模型基准开展攻击实验，将反馈限制在非支配任务画像上。

Result: 理论上，所需测试集规模随准则数指数增长；在固定精度和置信度下，仅需O(log k)个准则即可达到与回答k个自适应统计查询相同的Θ(√k)代价。实验中出现了较大的复用集与留出集分数差距，以及频繁的虚假优胜者。

Conclusion: 这些结果在丰富反馈场景下对既有解释构成挑战，即开发者主要响应超过当前最优的显著改进这一说法难以充分说明基准复用的可靠性；同时，普通模型开发过程多久会遭遇此类脆弱性仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: We study whether benchmarks reliably guide model selection as developers adapt to evaluation feedback across multiple criteria. We find that the worst-case test-set size needed to estimate the best score among $k$ adaptively chosen models, under any convex combination of the criteria, grows exponentially with the number of criteria, reaching the $Θ(\sqrt{k})$ cost of answering $k$ adaptive statistical queries with only $O(\log k)$ criteria, at fixed accuracy and confidence. In attacks on multi-task large language model benchmarks with five to ten criteria, feedback restricted to nondominated task profiles produces large reused-to-held-out score gaps and frequent false winners. These results challenge a prominent explanation for prior observed reliable benchmark reuse---that developers mainly respond to convincing improvements over the current best---in rich-feedback settings, while leaving open how often ordinary model development encounters this vulnerability.

</details>


### [212] [Handwritten Digit Leakage from Smartphone Motion Sensors Across Unseen Users and Phone Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32117)
*Constantino Álvarez Casado,Erkka Rantahalvari,Matteo Pedone,Matti Matilainen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Le Nguyen,Simo Hosio,Olli Silvén,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究智能手机运动传感器读数是否能在跨用户、跨设备条件下预测手写数字（10分类任务），基于HuMIdb数据集（481名参与者、19,628条记录），比较了手工特征+经典机器学习、MiniRocket核方法以及紧凑的传感器patch transformer，发现数字在假设已知绘制区间的前提下仍具可预测性，但存在采集顺序捷径，限制了关于实际隐私泄露的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机运动传感器原本用于支持交互应用，但其读数可能超出预期用途而泄露触摸屏输入信息。作者希望在跨用户和跨设备的真实条件下，验证手写数字是否仍可被预测，从而评估这种侧信道隐私风险。

Method: 在假定已知绘制区间（segmentation）的前提下，将任务建模为10分类问题，使用HuMIdb数据集中481名参与者的19,628条记录。在加速度计、线性加速度、陀螺仪和重力信号上，比较了手工特征结合经典机器学习算法、MiniRocket核方法以及一种紧凑的传感器patch transformer。评估设置包括75名未见参与者、9种未见手机型号，并进行了3个随机种子的实验。

Result: 该transformer在75名未见参与者上取得57.74%准确率和82.64%的top-3准确率；在9种未见手机型号的未见参与者上，3个种子平均准确率为58.77±0.95%。低运动强度的记录仍具信息量，准确率与运动水平并非单调关系；所测试的对比预训练、数据增强及派生信号均未带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 在假设已知绘制区间的情况下，手写数字在陌生用户和陌生手机型号上仍可被预测，说明该传感器侧信道风险具有跨用户与跨设备的泛化性。然而，采集顺序等捷径限制了关于实际隐私暴露程度的结论。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Arritmic/motion-digit-leakage。

Abstract: Smartphone motion sensors support interactive applications, but their readings may also reveal touchscreen input beyond their intended use. Assuming known drawing intervals, we study whether handwritten digits remain predictable across users and devices, as a 10-class problem on 19,628 HuMIdb recordings from 481 participants. We compare handcrafted features with classical machine learning algorithms, MiniRocket kernels, and a compact sensor patch transformer on accelerometer, linear acceleration, gyroscope, and gravity signals. The transformer achieves 57.74\% accuracy and 82.64\% top-3 accuracy on 75 unseen participants, and 58.77$\pm$0.95\% over 3 seeds for unseen participants on 9 unseen phone models. Low motion recordings remain informative, accuracy is not monotonic in motion level, and the tested contrastive pretraining, augmentation, and derived signals give no consistent gains. Digits are thus predictable beyond familiar users and phone models under assumed segmentation, while acquisition-order shortcuts limit conclusions about practical privacy exposure. Code available at: https://github.com/Arritmic/motion-digit-leakage.

</details>


### [213] [What Should We Freeze? Guarded Freezing: Connectivity Shapes the Fine-Tuning of Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32124)
*Leonel Aguilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出移除价值（removal-value）与漂移价值（drift-value）两种评分来指导预训练模型微调中的冻结选择，并据此构建 Guarded Freezing 策略，在 VGG-8、语言模型和 DINOv3 视觉-点云适配中均带来更好的旧任务保持效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调预训练模型时，仅冻结权重可能无法保持性能，因为其他权重的更新会改变冻结核心的输入，从而影响整体表现。因此需要依据"输入路径是否被切断"来更合理地选择冻结哪些权重。

Method: 首先分析冻结核心可被孤立的情形，提出基于容量的 removal-value 评分，近似 HOPE 的移除代价在不同移除顺序下的平均值；针对 transformer 中共享残差流导致进入冻结神经元的路径保持开放的情况，推导出 drift-value，即在局部更新模型下衡量各权重更新对输出扰动的仅前向代理指标。最终形成 Guarded Freezing：当输入路径被切断时用 removal-value 选择，当路径保留时用 drift-value 选择。

Result: VGG-8 中切断进入冻结核心的路径后，该评分使 70% 冻结比 DEFT 在相近新任务精度下多保留 5.22±0.51 个百分点的旧任务精度；语言模型中 40 epochs 时该策略在保留损失较大时优于 Wanda 和 RIA 的冻结评分，160 epochs 后在 Qwen2.5-1.5B 上比静态 Fisher 多保留 0.0433±0.0102；DINOv3 视觉-点云适配中 drift-value 保留 0.440 图像精度，而相同数量的随机掩码仅为 0.187。

Conclusion: 冻结选择应结合网络的路径结构：输入路径被切断时用 removal-value，路径保留时用 drift-value，即 Guarded Freezing，可在多类任务中提升旧任务保持能力。

Abstract: When adapting pre-trained models through fine-tuning, freezing weights alone might not preserve performance, as updates elsewhere can change the inputs to the frozen core, ultimately affecting overall performance. We first analyse the case where a selected frozen core can be isolated and propose removal-value, a capacity-based score that approximates HOPE's removal cost averaged over removal orders. We show that in VGG-8, cutting paths from trainable neurons into a frozen core makes selection using this score useful: $70\%$ frozen preserves $5.22\pm0.51$ percentage points more old-task accuracy than DEFT at similar new-task accuracy. In transformers, shared residual streams leave paths into frozen neurons open. For this case, we derive drift-value, a forward-only proxy for the output disturbance from updating each weight entry under a local update model. In language models, at 40 epochs, this policy exceeds adapted Wanda and RIA freezing scores in settings with substantial retention loss, while its differences from Fisher remain unresolved. After 160 epochs on Qwen2.5-1.5B, it retains $0.0433\pm0.0102$ more than static Fisher. In DINOv3 vision-transformer adaptation to point clouds, drift-value retains $0.440$ image accuracy versus $0.187$ for a random mask of the same count. These results motivate Guarded Freezing: select by removal-value when incoming paths are cut, and by drift-value when they remain.

</details>


### [214] [Latency-Aware Client Assignment for Parallel Split Learning With Global Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32132)
*Mohammad Kohankhaki,Valentin Rentschler,Anke Schmeink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出延迟预算并行拆分学习（LB-PSL），通过将池化批次的类别目标与提供样本的客户端选择解耦，实现延迟感知的客户端分配以降低建模训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨孤岛拆分学习中，Parallel Split Learning with Global Sampling 虽能在类别分布异构时形成代表性的池化批次，但在多个客户端可提供同一类别时忽略了客户端延迟。这促使作者引入延迟感知的提供者分配机制。

Method: 作者提出 Latency Budgeted Parallel Split Learning with Global Sampling，将每个池化批次的整数类别目标与客户端选择分离。flow 变体将分配建模为整数网络流问题，最小化当前目标下建模的客户端完成时间；fast 变体采用贪心下一完成规则降低调度构建成本。两者均保持目标流不变，且每个本地样本每轮仅使用一次。规划规则权衡两种变体的调度构建开销进行选择。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上，flow 变体减少建模训练时间 6.75%，最终精度下降 0.30 个百分点。在每客户端 20 个候选类别的 Tiny ImageNet 上，fast 变体减少建模时间 16.87%，并比延迟不敏感基线更早达到全部四个验证目标。在 405 次调度比较中，规划规则在 96.54% 的情况下与较低实际成本的差距在 2% 以内。

Conclusion: 延迟感知的提供者分配可在不改变规定类别目标的前提下减少建模训练时间，而更优变体取决于分配收益是否超过调度构建开销。

Abstract: In cross-silo split learning, Parallel Split Learning with Global Sampling forms representative pooled batches when class distributions differ across clients, but ignores client delay when several clients can supply the same class. We introduce Latency Budgeted Parallel Split Learning with Global Sampling, which separates each pooled batch's integer class target from the choice of clients that supply its examples. The flow variant formulates this assignment as an integral network-flow problem and minimizes modeled client-side completion time for the current target. The fast variant uses a greedy next-completion rule to reduce schedule-construction cost. Both preserve the target stream and use every local example once per epoch. A planning rule selects between the variants while accounting for the cost of constructing both candidate schedules. On CIFAR-10, the flow variant reduces modeled training time by 6.75%, with a 0.30 percentage-point decrease in final accuracy. On Tiny ImageNet with 20 candidate classes per client, the fast variant reduces modeled time by 16.87% and reaches all four validation targets earlier than the latency-unaware baseline. Across 405 schedule comparisons, the planning rule stays within 2% of the lower realized cost in 96.54% of cases. In our evaluation, latency-aware provider assignment reduces modeled training time without changing the prescribed class targets, while the preferred variant depends on whether assignment savings outweigh schedule-construction overhead.

</details>


### [215] [REALM: Regime-Switching, Explainable, and Activation-Induced Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32141)
*Xiaoran Cheng,Sen Na,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REALM是一种由神经激活模式诱导区制的线性模型混合体，通过对宽浅学生网络激活做二值化聚类来定义区制并拟合线性模型，再用可解释门控复现区制分配，从而在保持竞争性预测性能的同时提供稳定的两级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度ReLU网络是分段仿射映射，将输入空间划分为具有不同激活模式的单元。研究者希望在每个单元内拟合局部线性模型，以在保持预测精度的同时提升可解释性。核心挑战在于识别稳定、数据自适应且易于解释的区制（regimes）。

Method: 提出REALM：区制由神经激活模式诱导。先将深层教师网络蒸馏为宽浅学生网络（WSSN），再对其隐藏层激活进行二值化和聚类以定义区制，并在每个区制内拟合一个线性模型。由于区制来自内部结构，路由器不承担预测负担。为增强区制分配的可解释性，训练多分类逻辑回归作为"解释性门控"来复现区制分配。两级结构（门控+线性模型）均可从原始表格特征或学习到的卷积特征层面进行解释。此外还分析了理想化设定下分区复杂度与稳定性之间的权衡。

Result: 在表格和图像数据集上的实验表明，REALM相对于其他DNN引导的混合代理模型和内在可解释模型取得了具有竞争力的预测性能，同时能产生稳定的区制级解释。理论分析显示：随着区制数量增加，更细的分区可改善逼近效果，但可能降低区制分配的稳定性。

Conclusion: REALM通过神经激活模式诱导区制、门控复现区制分配的两级设计，实现了在两级上均可解释的混合线性模型，兼顾了预测性能与稳定、可解释的区制级说明。

Abstract: Deep ReLU networks are piecewise-affine mappings that partition the input space into cells, each characterized by a distinct activation pattern. This structure motivates fitting a local linear model within each cell to preserve predictive accuracy while improving interpretability. The challenge is to identify regimes that are stable, data-adaptive, and easy to explain. We propose REALM, a mixture of linear models whose regimes are induced by neural activation patterns. Because the number of activation cells in a deep neural network (DNN) can grow rapidly with depth, we first distill a deep teacher into a wide, shallow student network (WSSN), then binarize and cluster its hidden-layer activations to define the regimes and fit a linear model within each regime. Since the regimes are discovered from internal structure, the router does not carry the predictive burden. To make regime assignment interpretable, we train a multiclass logistic regression, the explanatory gate, to reproduce the regime assignments. The two-level structure is interpretable at both stages in terms of raw tabular or learned convolutional features: the gate identifies features that determine regime assignments, while the linear models identify features that drive predictions within each regime. We analyze an idealized setting that illustrates a trade-off between partition complexity and stability: as the number of regimes grows, finer partitions can improve approximation but may reduce regime-assignment stability. Experiments on tabular and image datasets show that REALM achieves competitive predictive performance relative to other DNN-guided mixture surrogates and inherently interpretable models while producing stable regime-level explanations.

</details>


### [216] [Spectral Reversal: Counteracting Singular Value Bias for Graph Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32143)
*Hanxu Yang,Yuhuan Zhao,Xiaodong He,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱偏置"现象，即预训练GNN优化过程中表征过度对齐大奇异值方向而忽略低能量方向；据此提出谱反向提示SRP，通过谱域软阈值掩码重平衡贡献，实现参数高效图适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练GNN经自监督学习后，冻结编码器的高效适配仍是难题。图提示法虽参数高效，但现有方法将预训练模型视为不透明的特征提取器，忽视了其内部谱结构信息，导致低能量方向中蕴含的互补信息未被利用，尤其在分布偏移下影响适配效果。

Method: 提出谱反向提示（SRP）框架：在冻结GNN编码器的谱域上施加可学习的软阈值掩码，降低主导（大奇异值）方向的权重并放大较弱方向；同时引入零空间增强模块，捕捉在冻结编码器下激活极小的方向上的变化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SRP以极少的额外参数取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: 重新加权谱成分是一种有原则且有效的参数高效图适配策略，为图提示与预训练GNN适配提供了新的谱视角。

Abstract: Pre-training Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) via self-supervised learning has become a dominant paradigm, yet efficiently adapting frozen encoders remains a challenge. Graph prompting offers a parameter-efficient alternative to fine-tuning, but existing methods largely treat pre-trained models as opaque feature extractors, ignoring their internal spectral structure. In this work, we identify a systematic phenomenon in pre-trained GNNs, which we term spectral bias: optimization during pre-training disproportionately aligns representations with directions associated with large singular values, leaving low-energy directions under-explored. We show that these underutilized directions can encode complementary information that is beneficial for downstream adaptation, especially under distribution shift. To leverage this insight, we propose Spectral Reverse Prompt (SRP), a prompting framework that rebalances the spectral contributions of frozen GNN encoders. SRP applies a learnable soft-thresholding mask in the spectral domain to down-weight dominant directions while amplifying weaker ones. In addition, SRP incorporates a null-space augmentation module that captures variation in directions with minimal activation under the frozen encoder. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SRP achieves state-of-the-art performance with minimal additional parameters, highlighting that reweighting spectral components is a principled and effective strategy for parameter-efficient graph adaptation.

</details>


### [217] [Playing to Par: Reinforcement Learning for Provably Optimal Quadrilateral Block Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32146)
*Arjun Narayanan,Per-Olof Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用强化学习智能体构建平面区域的全四边形网格分解，使其顶点不规则度达到离散Gauss-Bonnet定理给出的理论下界（par）。智能体直接在网格半边数据结构上进行局部编辑，并通过行为克隆+PPO克服稀疏奖励问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四边形网格的质量可由是否完整、单元形状好坏、以及不规则顶点数量来评判。其中不规则顶点总数存在一个理论下界，由离散Gauss-Bonnet恒等式根据区域拓扑和角点角度决定。论文旨在训练智能体构建能够达到该下界（par）的最优分解，并克服随机探索无法达到稀疏目标奖励的困难。

Method: 智能体通过局部编辑直接作用于网格的半边数据结构，策略网络的卷积遵循网格自身连通性，因此可泛化到比训练中见过的更大的区域。奖励直接以理论下界为目标，且非常稀疏（随机动作在多于八条边的区域上无法达到）。方法采用行为克隆：先在易于构造的最优网格上进行反向行走生成示范，再进行PPO训练。

Result: 在96个留出区域上，智能体在所有区域都生成全四边形网格，平均95.7个可用，90个可证明最优；Gmsh最强配置在相同单元数下仅51个完成、38个可用、0个最优，即使单元数增加三到十四倍也从未产生更规则的网格。在64个两倍训练规模区域上，智能体全部完成、62个可用，超出par的中位数小于1，而Gmsh在相同单元数下为39。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成达到理论最优下界的高质量全四边形网格分解，并显著优于现有最强工具Gmsh，同时具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: A quadrilateral block decomposition of a planar domain is judged by whether it is complete, whether its elements are well shaped, and how many of its vertices are irregular. The last has a provable floor: the discrete Gauss-Bonnet identity enforces a lower bound on the total vertex irregularity of any all-quadrilateral mesh of a given domain purely based on its topology and corner angles. We train a reinforcement learning agent to build decompositions that reach this bound, which we call par. It acts directly on the mesh's half-edge data structure through local edits, with a policy network whose convolutions follow the mesh's own connectivity, so it applies unchanged to domains larger than any seen in training. The reward targets the floor directly, and it is sparse: random play reaches it on no domain with more than eight sides. We overcome this exploration barrier via behaviour cloning on optimal meshes that are trivial to construct, walked backward into demonstrations, before training it with PPO. On 96 held-out domains the agent produces an all-quadrilateral mesh on every one, a usable one on 95.7 on average, and a provably optimal one on 90; Gmsh's strongest configuration at the same element count completes 51, is usable on 38 and optimal on none, and even at three to fourteen times the elements never produces a more regular mesh. On 64 domains twice the training size the agent completes all, is usable on 62, and keeps a median excess over par below one against Gmsh's 39 at the same element count.

</details>


### [218] [CAFE: Counterfactual Prediction via Fast Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32167)
*Xinyan Han,Xiaoyu Lin,Hao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang,Bo Li,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAFE框架，用摊销推断近似贝叶斯反事实后验预测分布，单次前向传播即可预测个体反事实结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实预测通常无法仅从观测数据中识别，即使在完全观测的加性噪声模型（ANM）中，不同的因果图可能产生相同观测分布却隐含不同的个体反事实结果；基于单一估计图的预测忽略了这种结构不确定性。

Method: CAFE是一种摊销推断框架，直接近似贝叶斯反事实后验预测分布。在覆盖多样ANM先验的合成反事实任务上预训练基于Transformer的模型；给定观测数据集、个体事实观测和干预，CAFE通过一次前向传播近似对应的后验预测分布。

Result: 实验表明，CAFE在可识别设定中能准确预测个体反事实结果，并在存在因观测上不可区分的因果图导致的结构不确定性时，能近似后验预测分布；在制造业和葡萄栽培等现实场景中表现出较强性能，证明其超出训练先验假设的经验稳健性。

Conclusion: CAFE通过整合多个合理SCM的预测来处理结构不确定性，实现了快速且稳健的贝叶斯反事实预测，且在现实应用中具备良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Counterfactual prediction estimates an individual's outcome under an alternative intervention given their factual observations. Such outcomes are generally not identifiable from observational data without additional assumptions. Even within the class of fully observed additive noise models (ANMs), different causal graphs can generate the same observational distribution yet imply different individual counterfactual outcomes. Predictions based on a single estimated graph ignore this structural uncertainty. We therefore target a Bayesian counterfactual posterior predictive distribution that combines predictions from plausible SCMs. We introduce CAFE (\textbf{C}ounterf\textbf{A}ctual Prediction via \textbf{F}ast Posterior \textbf{E}stimation), an amortized inference framework that directly approximates the Bayesian counterfactual posterior predictive distribution. We pretrain a transformer-based model on synthetic counterfactual tasks generated from a diverse prior over ANMs. Given an observational dataset, an individual's factual observations, and an intervention, CAFE approximates the corresponding posterior predictive distribution in a single forward pass. Experiments show that CAFE accurately predicts individual counterfactual outcomes in identifiable settings and approximates the posterior predictive distribution when structural uncertainty induced by observationally indistinguishable causal graphs exists. Strong performance in realistic manufacturing and viticulture settings further demonstrates its empirical robustness beyond the assumptions of the training prior.

</details>


### [219] [Uncertainty-Aware Selection of Online Algorithms with Simulator Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32170)
*Yongyi Guo,Zifan Xu,Ziping Xu,Kelly W. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线强化学习中算法选择问题，提出并分析 Uncertainty-Aware 选择规则，通过模拟器集成提升有限离线数据下的选择可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习性能高度依赖设计选择（尤其是影响探索的选择）。常用做法是用离线数据拟合模拟器，再在模拟器中评估候选算法并部署最优者。但最简单的 Plug-In 选择规则只依据拟合模拟器上的最优表现选择算法，当用于拟合模拟器的离线数据有限时，评估会变得不可靠。

Method: 提出 Uncertainty-Aware 选择方法：构建模拟器集成（例如通过 bootstrap 重采样获得），并选择在集成上平均表现最好的在线算法。作者在多臂赌博机设定下对该方法进行理论分析，并在涉及奖励塑形超参数选择的深度 RL 机器人控制任务上开展实证研究。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法能缓解拟合模拟器时数据有限的影响，并在多臂赌博机中相比 Plug-In 选择具有显著的 regret 优势。实证上，在深度 RL 机器人控制任务的奖励塑形超参数选择中，该方法带来更可靠的选择和更好的在线性能。

Conclusion: Uncertainty-Aware 选择通过模拟器集成有效应对离线数据有限的问题，在多臂赌博机理论分析与深度 RL 实证中均优于 Plug-In 选择，能提升算法选择的可靠性与在线表现。

Abstract: The performance of online reinforcement learning depends critically on design choices, especially those that affect exploration. These choices are often selected by fitting a simulator to offline data, evaluating candidate algorithms in that simulator, and deploying the best-performing one. The simplest Plug-In selection rule simply selects the best performing algorithm on the fitted simulator, making evaluations unreliable when the offline data used to fit the simulator are limited. We investigate Uncertainty-Aware selection, which forms an ensemble of simulators---for example, obtained by bootstrap resampling---and selects the online algorithm with the best average performance across the ensemble. While ensemble-based approaches have been used to mitigate distribution shift and facilitate sim-to-real transfer, we formally show that this approach can mitigate the effects of limited data when fitting the simulator and theoretically has significant regret gains compared to Plug-In selection in multi-armed bandits. We also empirically investigate the Uncertainty-Aware selection approach in deep RL experiments on robotic control tasks that involve selecting reward-shaping hyperparameters, and show that it leads to more reliable selection and improved online performance.

</details>


### [220] [Efficient Support Recovery of Mixtures of Sparse Linear Classifiers with Less Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32176)
*Xiaxin Li,Arya Mazumdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性分类器混合模型中的支撑恢复问题，提出自适应与非自适应测量方案，在减少测量次数的同时实现亚线性解码时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由多个线性决策规则混合生成的数据中，识别真正重要的特征（即恢复l个k-稀疏向量的非零坐标）具有重要意义。已有方法所需测量次数多、解码复杂度高（超二次），需要更高效的方案。

Method: 提出自适应和非自适应两类测量与解码方案。每次测量随机均匀选取l个向量之一，返回其与测量向量的内积符号，据此恢复各稀疏向量的支撑。

Result: 自适应方案相比已有方法显著减少测量次数，并将解码复杂度从超二次降低到关于环境维度的亚线性；非自适应方案在保持高效解码的同时改进了此前的测量上界。

Conclusion: 所提方法在混合模型支撑恢复中实现了样本复杂度与解码时间之间更优的权衡，整体效率优于已知方法。

Abstract: The support recovery problem in mixture of linear classifiers intends to identify which features actually matter when data is generated by a mixture of several linear decision rules. In particular, the aim is to recover the support (nonzero coordinates) of $l$ unknown $k$-sparse vectors from sign measurements. Each measurement is generated by selecting one of the $l$ vectors uniformly at random, and returning the sign of its inner product with a chosen measurement vector.
  In this paper, we propose adaptive and non-adaptive schemes that significantly improve upon prior results by reducing the number of measurements and achieving sublinear decoding time simultaneously. In particular, our adaptive constructions substantially reduce measurements compared to existing approaches, while also lowering decoding complexity from super-quadratic to sublinear in the ambient dimension. We further provide a non-adaptive scheme that improves previous measurement bounds while maintaining efficient decoding.
  Overall, our approach yields a more efficient trade-off between sample complexity and decoding time for support recovery in mixture models than previously known methods.

</details>


### [221] [Analytic-Walk Rotary Positional Encodings for Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32178)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AW-RoPE，将旋转位置编码放在边上并对所有游走路径求和，使图注意力能区分连接两节点的不同路径，在多个合成与真实基准上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图上的旋转位置编码从谱坐标给每个节点分配旋转，导致两节点间的旋转因子只依赖端点，无法区分连接它们的不同路径，限制了图结构信息的表达。

Method: AW-RoPE 将旋转放在边上，并对所有游走路径的传输特征进行求和，使不同路径贡献可相互增强或抵消；提供精确变体（通过可微线性求解计算完整求和）与稀疏变体（在有限深度截断）。两者作用于投影后的 query 和 key，稀疏递推还可增强消息传递网络。

Result: 在五个合成任务上，两种变体相对最强基线将 nRMSE 降低 15%–58%；在真实 superpixel、peptide 与 OGB 基准上，稀疏递推配合 Performer 核在每个数据集均取得最佳均值，配合 GIN 在 13 个数据集中的 12 个上最佳。分析表明 AW-RoPE 能区分仅由端点连接方式决定的路径，而节点级旋转编码无法做到。

Conclusion: AW-RoPE 通过在边上定义旋转并对游走求和，突破了节点级旋转编码的表达瓶颈，能以精确或稀疏形式高效实现，并在合成与真实任务上稳定优于基线。

Abstract: Rotary position encodings make attention sensitive to relative position, but extending them to graphs requires choosing how graph structure enters the rotation. Previous works assign each node a rotation from spectral coordinates, so the rotary factor between two nodes depends only on their endpoints and cannot distinguish the routes connecting them. We introduce \textit{Analytic-Walk Rotary Positional Encodings} (AW-RoPE), which place the rotations on edges and sum the transported features over all walks, so contributions along different routes can reinforce or cancel. An exact variant evaluates the complete sum by a differentiable linear solve, and a sparse variant truncates it at a finite depth. We prove forward and parameter-derivative truncation bounds at fixed inputs and parameters. Both variants act on projected queries and keys, and the sparse recurrence also augments message-passing networks. Across five synthetic tasks both variants reduce nRMSE by $15$--$58\%$ relative to the strongest baseline, and on real superpixel, peptide and OGB benchmarks the sparse recurrence attains the best mean on every dataset with Performer kernels and on twelve of thirteen datasets with GIN. Analysis shows that AW-RoPE can distinguish routes whose only cue is how two endpoints are connected, while node-wise rotary encodings cannot.

</details>


### [222] [HM-ROUTER: Joint Model and Harness Routing for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32213)
*Hao Mark Chen,Royson Lee,Yasuyuki Okoshi,Dimitris Anastasiou,Wayne Luk,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HM-Router 提出一种联合路由方法，为每个查询同时选择合适的模型与harness，通过共享表示与CP张量分解的交互项建模二者兼容性，从而在少量训练样本下泛化到未观测组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent性能同时取决于底层模型和管理工具使用与执行的harness，选择合适的一对需要权衡二者兼容性，但训练样本往往只覆盖不断增长的组合空间中的一部分，导致未观测组合难以预测。

Method: HM-Router 学习跨路由共享的独立模型表示与harness表示，并引入受CP张量分解启发的交互项，用以刻画模型与harness的兼容性如何随查询变化；这种共享机制使已观测pair的训练样本能推断未观测组合。作者从12个公开agent基准构建了含293条路由、73个模型、25个harness的基准。

Result: HM-Router 在平均路由准确率上超过最强已评估学习基线7.3个百分点，并在六个基准子集的全部七个成本预算上领先。当90%路由的训练结果被隐藏时，允许未观测组合相比限制在已观测路由使归一化准确率提升15.8点，并展现出对新路由与新组件的训练样本效率及对未见基准的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联合选择模型与harness并利用共享表示与交互项建模兼容性，可有效应对组合空间稀疏问题，实现更优的路由准确率、成本预算表现与泛化能力，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Agent performance depends on both the underlying model and the harness that manages its tool use and execution. Selecting a suitable pair requires accounting for their compatibility, yet training samples may cover only a subset of the growing combination space. We introduce HM-Router, a routing method that jointly selects a model and harness for each query. It learns separate model and harness representations shared across routes, with an interaction term inspired by canonical polyadic (CP) tensor decomposition to capture how their compatibility varies with the query. This sharing allows training samples from observed pairs to inform predictions for unobserved combinations. We curate a benchmark from 12 public agent benchmarks, covering 293 routes, 73 models, and 25 harnesses. HM-Router exceeds the strongest evaluated learned baseline by 7.3 percentage points in mean routing accuracy and leads at all seven evaluated cost budgets on the six-benchmark subset. When 90% of routes have their training outcomes withheld, allowing unobserved combinations improves normalized accuracy by 15.8 points over restricting the same router to observed routes. HM-Router has also demonstrated training sample efficiency for new routes and components and generalization to unseen benchmarks. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/hmarkc/HM-Router.

</details>


### [223] [CompassPlay: Rewarding the Proposer for Where It Moves the Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32228)
*Sophia Xiao Pu,Ximeng Sun,Jiang Liu,Jialian Wu,Emad Barsoum,Zicheng Liu,William Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CompassPlay 提出一种通过梯度对齐奖励出题者的自博弈方法，使任务奖励与目标能力的参考任务梯度对齐，从而提升训练价值与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自博弈中，出题者生成可验证任务来训练解题者。传统上出题者的奖励常依赖解题者的成功率（难度），但同等难度的任务在训练价值上可能存在差异，仅用难度作为奖励并不充分。

Method: CompassPlay 通过梯度对齐来奖励出题者：奖励偏好那些其解题者损失梯度与代表目标能力的参考任务梯度相对齐的任务。该方法基于学习进度的一阶近似，能在无需额外解题者训练的情况下为每个合格任务打分。以 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 进行代码自博弈时，使用小型外部参考集来引导任务生成。

Result: 在代码自博弈中，CompassPlay 相比 AZR 的难度奖励，域内代码平均准确率提升 1.5 个百分点，域外数学提升 2.7 个百分点。在 Lean4 定理证明中，CompassPlay 以少 40% 的 GPU 时间达到难度基线 150 次迭代的累计覆盖率。

Conclusion: 以梯度对齐替代纯难度作为出题者奖励，可同时提升自博弈的性能与训练效率，并在不同领域（代码、数学、定理证明）中验证有效。

Abstract: In self-play, a proposer generates verifiable tasks to train a solver. Proposer rewards often depend on the solver's success rate, but equally difficult tasks can differ in their training value. We introduce CompassPlay, a self-play method that rewards the proposer through gradient alignment. The reward favors tasks whose solver loss gradients align with those of reference tasks representing the target capabilities. It draws on a first-order approximation to learning progress and scores each eligible task without additional solver training. Our experiments show gains in performance and training efficiency. In coding self-play with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B, a small external reference set guides task generation. CompassPlay improves average accuracy over AZR's difficulty reward by 1.5 percentage points on in-domain coding and 2.7 on out-of-domain mathematics. In Lean4 theorem proving, CompassPlay matches the difficulty baseline's 150-iteration cumulative coverage with 40\% fewer GPU-hours.

</details>


### [224] ["Where Can I Trust You?": Boundary-Aware Evaluation of Surrogate Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32230)
*Jackson Eshbaugh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 代理模型保真度不仅取决于与教师模型一致的总频率，还取决于一致发生的区域（尤其在决策边界附近的稳定性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理模型通常只用全局一致率评估，但局部一致性差异的结构与后果不清。作者想探究分歧是否系统性地集中在教师决策边界附近，以及保留这一结构是否比单一全局分数提供更多信息。

Method: 在多个数据集和代理模型类别上，用两种识别近边界样本的方法测量教师边界附近的保真度；将一致性按置信度定义区域进行条件化；并在评估集构成变化时比较预测教师—代理一致性的能力；还比较了独立训练的深度教师模型所识别的决策边界。

Result: 教师决策边界附近的保真度显著更低；按置信度区域条件化的一致性在评估集构成变化时比全局分数更能预测教师—代理一致性；全局和边界附近一致性相同的代理，在基于代理自身选择的变化下反应差异很大；独立训练的深度教师可在多数预测上一致，却把不同样本判为近边界。

Conclusion: 代理保真度取决于一致发生在何处，而非仅一致频率；对深度模型还取决于教师决策边界在多次训练中的稳定性，这对以边界作为稳定参考区域评估代理提出挑战。

Abstract: Surrogate models are commonly evaluated by how often they agree with their teacher model over an evaluation set. Local variation in this agreement is well known, but its structure and consequences are less clear. We ask whether disagreement is systematically concentrated near the teacher's decision boundary and whether retaining that structure provides information beyond a single global score. Across several datasets and surrogate model classes, we find substantially lower fidelity near teacher decision boundaries under two different methods of identifying near-boundary examples. Moreover, conditioning agreement on confidence-defined regions improves prediction of teacher--surrogate agreement when evaluation-set composition changes, relative to the global score alone. Yet surrogates that agree equally well with the teacher both globally and near the decision boundary can respond very differently to changes selected using the surrogate itself. Finally, we show that independently trained deep teachers can agree on most predictions while identifying different examples as lying near their decision boundaries, complicating the use of those boundaries as stable reference regions for evaluating surrogates. Together, these results show that surrogate fidelity depends not only on how often a surrogate agrees with its teacher, but also on where that agreement holds and, for deep models, how stable the teacher's decision boundary is across training runs.

</details>


### [225] [Arithmetic Simplicity in Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32240)
*Bin Fu,Pengfei Gu,Jose Nunez,Fabian Vazquez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出将AdaGrad、Adam与AdamW改造成算术简单（仅含加、减、乘及除以2的整数次幂）的梯度下降方法，便于芯片实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始AdaGrad、Adam、AdamW使用非算术简单运算（如开方、任意除法），不利于硬件/芯片设计实现，需要简化运算以易于芯片实现。

Method: 利用AdamW递推式x_{t+1}=(1-λη)x_t-(η/s)m_t（Adam为λ=0特例），将其转化为静态情形s=S(T)，其中T为迭代次数，S(T)为固定函数；并给出静态Adam的收敛性分析。

Result: 成功将AdaGrad、Adam、AdamW转化为算术简单形式，并给出静态Adam的收敛性分析，该静态Adam同样是算术简单的。

Conclusion: AdaGrad、Adam、AdamW可被改造为算术简单的梯度方法，静态Adam具备收敛性保证，适合芯片设计实现。

Abstract: A gradient descent method is arithmetically simple if the operations are limited to $+,-, \times$, and division $x/2^t$ with integer $t$. An arthmetically simple gradient method is easy to implement in chip design. We show how to transform AdaGrad, Adam, and AdamW into arithmetically simple.
  AdamW is based on the recursion $x_{t+1}=(1-λη)x_t-\frac{η}{s}m_t$ and Adam is the special case of AdamW with $λ=0$. We transform them into a static case with $s=S(T)$, where $T$ is the number of iterations, and $S(T)$ is a fixed function. The convergence analysis is given for a static Adam, which is also arithmetically simple.

</details>


### [226] [Anytime-Valid LLM Leaderboards via Benchmark-weighted and Block-Factorized e-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32248)
*Hongfu Gao,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Bingyi Jing,Zhou Wang,Yiming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BB-EDGE 框架，将大模型排行榜表示为有向图，通过基准加权与块因子化的 e-process 在任意时刻控制 FWER，认证模型间的有向性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 排行榜按固定基准上的平均性能排名模型，但评估结果的运行间变异可能产生无根据的模型优越性结论，排行榜更新会进一步加剧这一风险。

Method: BB-EDGE 将排行榜建模为有向图，边表示成对的平均性能优势认证。对每个方向，按协议定义的块因子化证据，并按块权重分配赌注，构造经验 Bernstein e-process，再用 direct e-Holm 认证有向优势；理论分析权重比例的线性赌注与异构基准平均原假设。

Result: 理论上证明在块内与跨对任意依赖下实现 anytime FWER 控制，并支持任何时候有效的 Top-k 认证与同步排名区间。实验表明 BB-EDGE 在保持 anytime FWER 控制的同时具有高效率。

Conclusion: BB-EDGE 为 LLM 排行榜提供了一种原则性、具备 anytime 有效 FWER 控制的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) leaderboards compare model capabilities by ranking models according to their mean performance on fixed benchmarks. However, variability in evaluation outcomes across runs may produce unsupported claims of model superiority on the benchmark, a risk compounded by leaderboard updates. In this paper, we propose BB-EDGE (Benchmark-Weighted and Block-Factorized e-processes for Directed Graph Evaluation), a principled framework that represents an LLM leaderboard as a directed graph whose edges certify pairwise mean-performance advantages, with anytime-valid family-wise error rate (FWER) control. Concretely, for each direction, BB-EDGE constructs an empirical-Bernstein e-process by factorizing evidence over protocol-defined blocks and assigning stakes proportional to the corresponding block weights, then applies direct e-Holm across these $e$-processes to certify directional advantages as edges. Theoretically, we characterize weight-proportional linear stakes under heterogeneous benchmark-average nulls and prove anytime FWER control under arbitrary within-block and cross-pair dependence. BB-EDGE further supports anytime-valid Top-$k$ certification and simultaneous rank intervals. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and four real-world benchmarks demonstrate that BB-EDGE maintains anytime FWER control while achieving high efficiency.

</details>


### [227] [Representation Editing for Multimodal Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32263)
*Longfei Huang,Xiangyu Wu,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FIRE方法，从表征工程视角直接编辑多模态TTA中的中间表征，通过频域混合构建低秩编辑子空间并联合多级目标优化，在多模态基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态测试时自适应（TTA）方法主要调整融合特征以弥合源-目标域差距，缺乏对中间表征错位的显式控制，而中间表征错位是分布偏移下性能下降的关键原因。

Method: 从表征工程视角出发，在每个单模态编码器的中间层引入表征编辑器，实现逐层校准；编辑器通过快速傅里叶变换进行频域混合以构建结构化基，提升低秩编辑子空间的多样性与稳定性；并引入多级自适应目标联合优化编辑器，促进跨模态语义对齐、源-目标统计对齐及非对称预测一致性。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的多模态基准上、多种损坏类型下的实验表明，FIRE优于现有多模态TTA方法。

Conclusion: FIRE通过直接编辑中间表征并逐层校准单模态表示，产生对齐的单模态表征用于融合，从而提升预测可靠性，为多模态TTA提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to adapt a pretrained multimodal model online to distribution shift across modalities using unlabeled test data, showing broad potential in real-world applications. However, existing methods primarily focus on adjusting fused features to bridge the source-target gap, lacking explicit control over intermediate representation misalignment, which is a key driver of performance drop under distribution shift. In this work, we tackle this challenge from the perspective of representation engineering. Unlike previous TTA methods that update fusion weights in place, we propose FourIer Representation Editor (FIRE), a novel multimodal TTA approach that directly edits semantically rich intermediate representations. Specifically, we first adopt representation editors into each intermediate layer of the unimodal encoders, enabling layer-wise calibration of unimodal representations. To further enhance the diversity and stability of the low-rank editing subspaces, each representation editor performs frequency domain mixing via the fast Fourier transform to construct structured bases. Moreover, we introduce multi-level adaptation objectives to optimize these editors, jointly promoting cross-modal semantic alignment, source-target statistical alignment, and asymmetric prediction consistency. In this way, FIRE yields aligned unimodal representations for fusion and further improves prediction reliability. Extensive experiments on two widely used multimodal benchmarks under various corruption types demonstrate the superiority of FIRE over existing multimodal TTA methods.

</details>


### [228] [When Can Old Evaluations Certify a New Model? Label-Efficient Release Decisions under Evaluator Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32267)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Mridul Banik,Md. Shifatul Ahsan Apurba,Md Masud Al Mahmud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"portfolio vigilance"序贯认证器，将历史信息仅用于指导下注、不进入有效性证明，从而在无标签或脏历史下安全认证模型风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型更新需要认证当前人群风险低于阈值，但可信标签昂贵，只能用廉价评估器（如LLM评判）打分。复用历史审计中的评估器误差很诱人，但历史误差可能不可见地变化；若无变化界定则无标签检验无效，必须继续购买标签。因此需要区分"可进入有效性"的历史与"仅可指导标签收集"的历史。

Method: 分析三种历史状态：误差不可见变化时无标签检验不可能、给出必须购标率；有变化上界时无标签认证有效但代价明确；中间地带历史有信息但不可信，则提出portfolio vigilance——一个序贯认证器，混合由历史引导的betting expert与仅从当前标签学习的expert，历史只影响下注方式，故对任意历史都保持有效性。

Result: 在规范模型中，准确历史缩短决策但从不提高证据增长率，陈旧历史会破坏它。CIFAR-10N与DICES-990上需匹配预测驱动监控器标签量的0.465（CI[0.327,0.575]）和0.740（CI[0.618,0.877]），无假认证，六个外部块全部更少。历史被污染时保持在其较优组件8.0%以内，而仅信任历史最多贵1.66倍。DICES-990与ToxicChat重复裁判实验中改rubric使分数超出run-to-run波动，组合需0.790（[0.667,0.909]）和0.631（[0.520,0.770]）倍标签，且少于仅信任历史。

Conclusion: 核心贡献不是先验引导下注或专家混合，而是分离"可进入有效性的历史"与"只能指导标签收集的历史"，实现既安全又省标签的模型风险认证。

Abstract: Releasing a model update requires certifying that its current-population risk stays below a threshold. Trusted labels are expensive, while a cheap evaluator, such as an LLM judge, scores every example. Reusing evaluator errors from earlier audits is tempting, but when may such evidence replace current labels? It depends on the status of history. If the errors can change invisibly, no label-free test detects the change, and every valid, useful certifier must keep buying labels at a rate we characterize; if a bound on the change is assumed, label-free certification is valid at an explicit error cost. For the middle ground, where history is informative but untrusted, we propose \emph{portfolio vigilance}, a sequential certifier mixing a betting expert guided by history with one that learns only from current labels; history affects only how it bets, so validity holds for any history. The contribution is not prior-informed betting or expert mixtures, but separating history that may enter validity from history that may only guide label collection. In a canonical model, accurate history shortens decisions but never raises the evidence growth rate; stale history can destroy it. On held-out CIFAR-10N and DICES-990 data, portfolio vigilance needs 0.465 (95\% CI $[0.327,0.575]$) and 0.740 ($[0.618,0.877]$) times the labels of a matched prediction-powered monitor, with no observed false certification, and fewer labels on all six external blocks. Under corrupted advice it stays within 8.0\% of its better component, while trusting history alone costs up to 1.66 times as much. In post-confirmatory repeated-judge experiments on DICES-990 and ToxicChat, changing a fixed LLM judge's rubric moves its scores beyond run-to-run variation; the portfolio then needs 0.790 ($[0.667,0.909]$) and 0.631 ($[0.520,0.770]$) times the labels of the matched monitor, and fewer than trusting history alone.

</details>


### [229] [Self-Reconstruction Dynamics for Autoencoder Reconstruction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32268)
*Hitoshi Iyatomi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用冻结自编码器重复重建产生的"自重建动力学"（SRD）作为样本特异信号，实现重建精化（SRD-RR），在多个数据集上恢复约38.6%–45.3%的可恢复MSE差距，并揭示像素保真与感知质量之间的失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准AE推理只做单次编码-解码，固定解码器下潜变量未必对每个样本最优。作者想探究：一个已训练好的AE能否为改进自身重建提供信息，即AE对自身重建结果的重复应用是否蕴含样本特异的修正线索。

Method: 将冻结AE反复作用于其重建结果，得到图像与潜空间中的短程轨迹，称为Self-Reconstruction Dynamics (SRD)。基于此提出SRD-RR：在AE冻结、无逐样本测试时优化的条件下，用短程SRD预测潜变量修正量。并提出MSE-recov指标，以经验解码器优化参考为基准衡量MSE恢复比例。还设计了使用SRD派生伪目标、不直接访问原始图像的变体。

Result: 在六个数据集上，SRD-RR单步转换恢复38.6%可恢复MSE差距，两步恢复45.3%；无原始图像访问的两步变体恢复40.7%，平均PSNR提升1.74 dB。移除轨迹信息会降低增益，跨样本轨迹分配导致严重退化，证实强样本特异性；非线性SRD条件精化优于固定与训练的线性潜变量修正。在DINOv2-based RAE上同样有效。但像素MSE潜变量精化暴露像素保真与感知质量的强失配，而SRD伪目标可缓解该退化。

Conclusion: SRD是一种有用的样本特异精化信号，AE自身动力学中蕴含可用于自我改进的信息；但最终目标函数决定其如何转化为像素与感知质量，像素保真与感知质量之间存在明显失配。

Abstract: Standard autoencoder (AE) inference uses a single encoder-decoder pass, though the latent may not be optimal for each sample under a fixed decoder. We ask whether a trained AE can reveal information for improving its own reconstruction. Repeated application of a frozen AE to its reconstruction produces transient image- and latent-space trajectories, termed Self-Reconstruction Dynamics (SRD). Although this degrades fidelity in the AEs studied here, SRD contains sample-specific information for correcting the reconstruction. We propose SRD-guided Reconstruction Refinement (SRD-RR), which predicts a latent correction from a short SRD with the AE frozen and no per-sample test-time optimization. We also introduce MSE-recov, an MSE recovery ratio relative to an empirical decoder-optimized reference. Across six datasets, SRD-RR recovers 38.6% of the empirically recoverable MSE gap with one transition and 45.3% with two. A two-transition variant trained without direct access to original images, using an SRD-derived pseudo-target, achieves 40.7% recovery and a 1.74 dB average PSNR gain. Removing trajectory information reduces the gain, while cross-sample trajectory assignment causes severe degradation, confirming strong sample specificity. Nonlinear SRD-conditioned refinement consistently outperforms fixed and trained linear latent correction. On a pretrained DINOv2-based representation autoencoder (RAE) with substantially different latent dynamics, SRD conditioning again improves a matched trajectory-free predictor. However, pixel-MSE latent refinement reveals a strong mismatch between pixel fidelity and perceptual quality, while the SRD-derived pseudo-target mitigates this degradation. Overall, SRD is a useful sample-specific refinement signal, while the objective determines how it translates into pixel and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [230] [Certification Frontiers for Gaussian LoRA: Independent Priors, Posterior Risk, and Prediction-Preserving Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32271)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了高斯LoRA后验的PAC-Bayes认证前沿，区分改变先验、改变随机预测器和改变复杂度计数三类干预，揭示了仅降低复杂度无法认证后验的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准的后验本身并不能给出有用的泛化认证；只有当采样预测器的损失和相对可容许先验的KL散度都足够小时，后验才能给出有信息量的PAC-Bayes认证。因此需要刻画高斯LoRA后验的认证前沿。

Method: 1) 精确各向同性KL包络消除先验尺度，给出在采样前排除后验宽度的宽度阈值，并用零KL下限识别无法达到的目标；2) 在低秩因子的完整GL(r)对称性上闭式最小化KL且不改变采样适配器乘积，并推导独立划分训练先验中心时的非中心目标。

Result: 在RoBERTa的567种配置审计中，64次采样摘要即使零KL和Chernoff核算也给出0.7298的认证下限，说明仅降复杂度无法在该预算下认证；在受控高斯因子任务中，Chernoff在20/20数据集上以1024次采样认证风险低于0.1，Hoeffding则一个都不能认证；在合成秩四因子上，矩阵平衡比标量平衡平均多降29.3%的KL且不改变预测。

Conclusion: 降低复杂度不足以认证这些后验；矩阵平衡可显著降低KL而不影响预测，数值分裂先验场景明确了剩余的风险与复杂度预算。

Abstract: Post-hoc Bayesian fine-tuning places Gaussians around trained low-rank adapters, yet a calibrated posterior does not by itself yield a useful generalization certificate. Such a posterior admits an informative PAC-Bayes certificate only when both the loss of its sampled predictors and its KL divergence from an admissible prior are small. In this research, we characterize this certification frontier for Gaussian LoRA posteriors and separate three interventions: changing the prior, changing the stochastic predictor, and changing only how its complexity is counted. First, an exact isotropic KL envelope eliminates the prior scale and yields a width threshold that excludes posterior widths before sampling, while a zero-KL floor identifies targets that no complexity reduction can reach at a measured risk bound. Second, we minimize KL in closed form over the full $GL(r)$ symmetry of the low-rank factors, leaving every sampled adapter product unchanged, and derive the noncentral objective required when the prior center is trained on an independent split. On a small-pool RoBERTa audit of 567 configurations, the recorded 64-draw summaries imply a certificate floor of $0.7298$ even with zero KL and Chernoff accounting, so reducing complexity alone cannot certify these posteriors at the recorded budgets. For a stable posterior in a controlled Gaussian-factor task, Chernoff accounting certifies risk below $0.1$ on 20 of 20 datasets with 1024 draws, whereas Hoeffding certifies none. On deliberately deformed synthetic rank-four factors, matrix balancing reduces KL by $29.3\%$ on average beyond scalar balancing without changing any prediction. Numerical split-prior scenarios make the remaining risk and complexity budgets explicit.

</details>


### [231] [Topology-Adaptive Hyperbolic Graph Attention Networks Guided by the Hyperbolic Sombor Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32275)
*Haifang Cao,Boan Tao,Xiyuan Gao,Timing Li,Yu Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出超曲Sombor指数（HSO）作为轻量结构先验，并构建HSO-GAT实现拓扑自适应的超曲图注意力网络，在八个基准数据集上取得节点分类与链接预测的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超曲图神经网络通常依赖共享曲率配置与特征驱动注意力，未能显式利用局部层次拓扑模式。为弥补这一缺口，需要引入能捕捉层次指示性度分层的轻量结构先验。

Method: 引入Hyperbolic Sombor Index（HSO）作为结构先验；提出HSO-GAT，包含两个互补模块：HSO引导的局部曲率自适应（基于聚合的节点级HSO信号进行节点级几何缩放），以及HSO门控超曲图注意力（通过特征条件门控实现结构感知的消息传递）。理论上证明边级HSO对度不平衡的单调敏感性，并分析节点自适应超曲映射的有效性与径向缩放性质。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HSO-GAT在节点分类与链接预测任务上均一致达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: HSO-GAT通过将拓扑结构先验与几何自适应及消息传播统一，验证了显式利用局部层次拓扑模式的有效性，为超曲图表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry has emerged as a principled space for representing hierarchical graphs. However, existing hyperbolic graph neural networks typically rely on shared curvature configurations and feature-driven attention, failing to explicitly exploit local hierarchical topological patterns. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Hyperbolic Sombor Index (HSO) as a lightweight structural prior for capturing hierarchy-indicative degree stratification. Building on this, we propose \textbf{HSO-GAT}, a topology-adaptive hyperbolic graph attention network that unifies geometric adaptation and message propagation. Specifically, it comprises two complementary modules: HSO-Guided Local Curvature Adaptation, which performs adaptive node-wise geometric scaling from aggregated node-level HSO signals, and HSO-Gated Hyperbolic Graph Attention, which enables structure-aware message passing through feature-conditioned gating. Theoretically, we establish the monotonic sensitivity of edge-level HSO to degree imbalance and analyze the validity and radial scaling properties of node-adaptive hyperbolic mappings. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSO-GAT consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both node classification and link prediction tasks.

</details>


### [232] [HyperLabel: Multi-Label Classification via Hypergraph-Based Label Correlation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32276)
*Peiyu Zhang,Heng Ping,Nikos Kanakaris,Yucheng Zhao,Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Xiongye Xiao,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 HyperLabel，一个基于超图神经网络的编码器-解码器框架，显式建模多标签分类中的高阶标签依赖，在七个基准数据集上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签分类的核心挑战在于建模由共现模式产生的复杂标签依赖，而现有方法主要通过对比目标或成对注意力隐式学习，缺乏结构性指导，难以捕捉高阶标签相关性。

Method: 构建标签超图，用样本定义超边编码多路共现模式作为显式结构先验；采用跨模态学习框架，HGNN+ 进行双向消息传递融合特征信息与标签结构，并用共享交叉注意力解码器通过互补学习目标处理两种模态。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上取得 SOTA 性能，macro-F1 提升显著（Delicious +10.3%，Bibtex +8.2%），验证显式超图结构能有效捕捉复杂标签关系。

Conclusion: 显式超图结构能有效建模超越成对交互的高阶标签依赖，为多标签分类提供了新的结构化解决思路，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-label classification (MLC) requires predicting multiple relevant labels for each instance, where a central challenge is modeling complex label dependencies arising from co-occurrence patterns. Existing approaches are limited in capturing high-order label correlations, relying on implicit learning through contrastive objectives or pairwise attention mechanisms without structural guidance. We propose HyperLabel, an encoder-decoder framework that explicitly models label dependencies through hypergraph neural networks. Our contributions are twofold: (i) We construct a label hypergraph where sample-defined hyperedges naturally encode multi-way co-occurrence patterns, providing explicit structural prior knowledge that captures relationships beyond pairwise interactions. (ii) We propose a unified cross-modal learning approach where HGNN+ performs bidirectional message passing to integrate feature information with label structure, and a shared cross-attention decoder processes both modalities through complementary learning objectives. Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyperLabel achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly significant improvements on macro-F1 scores (+10.3% on Delicious, +8.2% on Bibtex), validating that explicit hypergraph structure effectively captures complex label relationships. The code is available at https://github.com/iZHpy/Multi-label_hypergraph .

</details>


### [233] [SIMANF: Sample Free Learning of Unnormalized Distributions via Simulated Annealing in Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32279)
*Vikas Kanaujia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIMANF：将模拟退火与归一化流结合的无样本框架，逐步从平滑分布过渡到目标分布，缓解模式崩溃并提升高维多模态分布的学习与采样效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效学习与采样高维、多模态、未归一化分布仍是难题。归一化流虽能高效采样，但基于反向KL散度、仅用未归一化目标密度训练时容易发生模式崩溃，导致模态覆盖不足。

Method: 提出SIMANF框架，将模拟退火与归一化流融合：逐步把目标分布从平滑初始形式变换为原始目标分布，并在各阶段顺序训练流；通过阶段间迁移已学表示，促进模态覆盖并逐步捕获更细特征。退火后进行最终精修阶段，结合反向KL散度与基于流生成样本的重要性加权前向KL目标。训练全程无需目标样本，仅使用未归一化密度。

Result: 在Many-Well分布以及高维标量Phi4格点场论分布上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SIMANF通过模拟退火式逐步训练与阶段间表示迁移，在无目标样本条件下改善了归一化流的模态覆盖与细粒度建模能力，适用于高维多模态未归一化分布。

Abstract: Efficiently learning and sampling from high dimensional, multimodal unnormalized distributions without target samples remains a challenging problem. Although normalizing flows can generate samples efficiently, training based on the reverse KL divergence using only the unnormalized target density may suffer from mode collapse. We introduce SIMANF, a sample free framework that integrates simulated annealing with normalizing flows. SIMANF progressively transforms the target distribution from a smooth initial form to the original target distribution and trains the flow sequentially across these stages. By transferring the learned representation between stages, the method promotes mode coverage while progressively capturing finer features of the target distribution. Following annealing, a final refinement stage combines the reverse KL divergence with an importance weighted forward KL objective using samples generated by the flow. SIMANF requires no target samples during training and uses only the unnormalized density. We demonstrate its effectiveness on Many-Well distributions and high dimensional Scalar Phi4 lattice field theory distribution.

</details>


### [234] [A Solvable Theory of Pre-training Data Poisoning: Regime-Dependent Scaling Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32288)
*Indranil Halder,Rastri Dey,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练数据投毒率ε增大时模型在干净数据上性能的退化规律。作者发现实验上Δ近似满足幂律Δ≈Cε^a（非整数指数），并从理论上证明这一现象需要目标函数具有真正奇异性；在重尾协变量截断岭回归中给出了依赖极限次序的指数ε^{q*/(q*+2)}或ε^{2-2/q*}，并用数值实验和LLM预训练的有限训练时间假说加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究多关注定向后门及其在安全后训练后的存活性，忽略了更基本的问题：随着投毒率ε上升，模型在干净数据上的性能如何退化。作者在OLMo风格模型的受控预训练中发现相对验证困惑度增幅Δ符合非整数指数幂律，因此希望从理论上说明这种幂律成立需要什么条件。

Method: 首先证明一个解析性障碍：若污染目标在干净最优解附近对ε解析，则Δ一般为ε的二次函数，非整数指数意味着真正的奇异结构。随后在带重尾协变量的可解截断岭回归中引入标签偏移投毒，用q*控制重尾程度，分析超额风险随ε的缩放指数及其对极限次序的依赖，并通过数值模拟验证预测；最后将有限训练时间解释为对曲率谱的截断，在重尾逆曲率谱假设下推导观察到的缩放律。

Result: 理论中心结果是超额风险的缩放指数取决于取极限的次序：在高维比例极限下为ε^{q*/(q*+2)}，而先取充足数据极限则得到ε^{2-2/q*}，两者不可交换。数值模拟支持该预测。作者进一步论证在局部LLM预训练中有限训练时间相当于对曲率谱的截断，并据此在重尾逆曲率谱假设下导出了观察到的幂律缩放。

Conclusion: 非整数幂律的干净数据性能退化不能由解析性污染目标解释，而需要奇异结构；在截断岭回归模型中，重尾性使缩放指数依赖极限次序且不可交换，为理解预训练数据投毒下干净性能退化提供了理论机制，并将LLM预训练中的有限训练时间与曲率谱截断联系起来。

Abstract: Pre-training data poisoning of large language models is usually studied using targeted backdoors and their survival through safety post-training, which leaves open a more basic question: how does a model's clean data performance degrade as the poison rate $\varepsilon$ grows? Motivated by our controlled pre-training runs of OLMo-style models, in which the relative clean data validation perplexity increase $Δ$ between poisoned and clean models matched in architecture, token budget, and optimization schedule is well fit by a power law $Δ\approx C\varepsilon^{a}$ with a non-integer exponent, we ask what such a law requires theoretically. We first prove an analyticity barrier: whenever the contaminated objective depends analytically on $\varepsilon$ around a nondegenerate clean data optimum, $Δ$ is generically quadratic in $\varepsilon$, so a generic non-integer exponent is a signature of genuinely singular structure. We then supply that structure in solvable truncated ridge regression with heavy-tailed covariates, controlled by $q_\star$, and a label-shift poisoning. Our central result is that the excess risk scaling exponent depending on the order of limits: in the higher dimensional proportional regime it is $ε^{q_\star/(q_\star+2)}$, whereas taking the ample-data limit first gives $ε^{2-2/q_\star}$, and the limits do not commute. We confirm this prediction through several numerical simulations. Finally, we argue that finite training time plays the role of truncation on the curvature spectrum in local LLM pre-training, deriving the observed scaling law under heavy tailed inverse curvature spectrum as a modeling hypothesis.

</details>


### [235] [A Journey to the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32290)
*Jaerin Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过密集学习率扫描实验发现，不同一阶优化器在按直流增益(dc gain)归一化学习率后，其损失、锐度(sharpness)与连续梯度对齐度的轨迹几乎重叠，并据此划分出低、中、高学习率三个不同机制区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明深度学习常发生在"稳定边缘(EoS)"，即Hessian最大特征值稳定在学习率的倒数附近。但到达该机制之前发生了什么、优化器与学习率如何共同影响训练轨迹，尚不清楚，因此作者希望厘清优化器、学习率与模型在塑造学习进程中的各自角色。

Method: 固定一个深度学习问题，改变一阶优化方法并进行密集的学习率扫描；追踪学习轨迹的三个特征量——损失(loss)、锐度(sharpness)以及相邻梯度之间的对齐度(alignment)。进一步将学习率按优化器的直流增益(dc gain)进行缩放，再比较不同优化器在不同学习率下的轨迹。

Result: 按dc gain缩放学习率后，不同优化器扫描得到的损失、锐度和对齐度轨迹在很大学习率范围内几乎完美重叠。dc归一化优化器还决定了锐度何时脱离该普适曲线并进入稳定边缘。据此可区分三个区域：低学习率区域(轨迹几乎对优化器不敏感)、高学习率区域(优化器按EoS倒数规则支配锐度)、以及中间的过渡区域(渐进锐化与优化器无关地自发产生)。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了优化器、学习率与模型在塑造学习进程中的不同作用：优化器通过dc增益归一化后呈现普适行为，学习率决定所处机制区域，而中期渐进锐化则独立于优化器。这为理解稳定边缘之前的训练动力学提供了统一视角。

Abstract: It has recently been found that deep learning often occurs at the "edge of stability (EoS)," where the maximum Hessian eigenvalue of the model is stabilized at a value reciprocal to the learning rate. However, what happens before we reach that regime? We fix a deep learning problem and vary first order optimization methods with dense learning rate sweeps. We then track the characterizing quantities of a learning trajectory: the loss, the sharpness, and the alignment between consecutive gradients. To our surprise, if we scale the learning rate by the dc gain of the optimizer, these traces from the sweeps from different optimizers almost perfectly overlap across a large range of learning rates. The dc-normalized optimizers have another role that only becomes apparent in high learning rates: they select when the sharpness value detaches from this universal curve and enters the edge of stability. Upon this discovery, we specify three distinct regimes with respect to the dc-adjusted learning rate: the low-LR regime where the trajectory is nearly insensitive to the optimizer, the high-LR regime, where the optimizer governs the sharpness according to the EoS reciprocal rule, and the in-between mid-LR regime where so-called progressive sharpening originates independently of the optimizer. This distinguishes the role of the optimizer, the learning rate, and the model in shaping the learning progress.

</details>


### [236] [FUND: Density Flow for Sampling Unnormalised Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32296)
*Vikas Kanaujia,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需真实样本训练的学习玻尔兹曼分布的算法，将flow matching从样本轨迹匹配改为分布轨迹匹配，迭代重塑目标分布，在多个基准上优于MCMC与流基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从玻尔兹曼分布高效采样对复杂物理系统建模至关重要。MCMC存在临界慢化、高自相关和模式混合差的问题；Boltzmann Generators虽是有希望的替代方案，但仍受限于昂贵的基于MCMC的训练、低效采样和遍历性差。因此需要一种无需真实样本即可学习玻尔兹曼分布的方法。

Method: 算法受flow matching启发，但根本性地从样本-轨迹匹配转向分布-轨迹匹配。该方法迭代地重塑目标分布，使用模型生成的样本来指导学习，从而确保全面的模式覆盖，并且训练过程不需要任何真实样本。

Result: 在标准基准上验证，包括2D高斯混合、Many-Well分布以及高维标量φ^4理论。所提方法在采样性能和精度上均优于传统MCMC和基于流的基线。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了无样本学习复杂物理分布的新途径，为玻尔兹曼分布的高效采样提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Efficient sampling from Boltzmann distributions is central to modelling complex physical systems. Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods suffer from critical slowing down, high autocorrelation, and poor mode-mixing, limiting their scalability. Recent advances, like Boltzmann Generators, offer a promising alternative but remain constrained by costly MCMC-based training, inefficient sampling, and poor ergodicity. We introduce an algorithm for learning Boltzmann distributions that does not require any true samples for training. Our approach draws inspiration from flow matching but departs fundamentally from sample-trajectory matching to distribution-trajectory matching. The algorithm iteratively reshapes the target distribution, using model generated samples to guide learning and ensure comprehensive mode coverage. We validate our method on standard benchmarks, including a 2D Gaussian mixture, Many-Well distributions, and high-dimensional scalar $φ^4$ theory. The proposed approach not only improves sampling performance and accuracy over traditional MCMC and flow-based baselines but also establishes a new method for sample-free learning of complex physical distributions.

</details>


### [237] [Refresh or Realize? Compute Allocation in Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32298)
*Sipeng Chen,Xu Zheng,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上研究 Drifting 模型训练中算力分配问题：应更深入地拟合当前漂移场，还是更频繁地重算漂移场。结果显示，算力有限时，重算新漂移场比深入拟合更划算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drifting 模型通过每次迭代重算有限样本漂移场并朝漂移目标做优化器更新，但更新发生在所有样本共享的参数空间，网络实际产生的位移未必匹配给定位移。因此存在一个基本训练问题：额外算力应投入更贴近拟合当前目标，还是投入重算漂移场。

Method: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，将目标固定 k 个优化器步并测量实际位移；比较不同 k 下网络逼近冻结目标的程度、取得净进展所需步数，以及 k=2 时的 FID；再在近似匹配实测墙钟时间下，比较“更深拟合”与“更新漂移场”两种算力分配；并分析有限支撑重抽后漂移场方向旋转程度与参数更新的差异。

Result: 固定目标时，更深拟合确实使网络更接近冻结目标；取得净进展所需步数从训练早期约 16 步降到后期 1 步。额外步数免费时，k=2 也能降低 FID；但一旦计入成本，在近似匹配墙钟时间下，把预算用于重算新漂移场比更深拟合获得更低 FID，且在两个训练种子上一致。重抽有限支撑使方向旋转远大于参数更新（余弦约 0.3–0.6 对约 0.95），且在漂移场空间最优的校正并不比无参数校正更可靠地改善 FID。

Conclusion: 对 Drifting 而言，把每个目标拟合好与把算力花好是两个不同目标。算力受限时，更频繁重算漂移场优于更深入拟合当前漂移场。

Abstract: Drifting Models train a one-step generator by recomputing a finite-sample drift field at every iteration and taking an optimizer step toward the drifted target. The field says how generated samples should move, but the step is taken in parameters shared by all samples, so the motion the network actually makes need not match the motion it was given. This leaves a basic training question open: should extra compute go into fitting the current target more closely, or into recomputing the field? We study it on ImageNet 256x256. Holding the target fixed for k optimizer steps and measuring the realized displacement, we find that deeper fitting does bring the network closer to the frozen target, and that the number of steps needed before it makes any net progress drops from about sixteen early in training to one later on. When the extra steps come for free, k=2 also lowers FID. Once they are paid for, the result flips: at approximately matched measured wall-clock, spending the budget on fresh fields gives lower FID than deeper fitting, on both training seeds. The target itself shows why a fresh field is worth so much. Redrawing the finite support rotates its direction far more than a parameter update does (cosine ~0.3-0.6 against ~0.95), and a correction that is optimal in field space is not reliably better in FID than a parameter-free one. For Drifting, fitting each target well and spending compute well are different goals.

</details>


### [238] [When Does Synergy Help Active Feature Acquisition? A PID-Based Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32301)
*Jie Li,Maruf A. Dhali,Hjalmar R. Bouma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynAFA，一种依赖状态的主动特征获取策略，结合成对联合信息与条件信息，并用部分信息分解（PID）刻画其信息结构；实验显示其收益高度依赖预算与数据集，暴露了将局部信息转化为有效获取目标的持续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动特征获取（AFA）策略很少区分交互特征所贡献的信息是冗余的、独有的还是协同的，作者希望明确成对协同信息何时能带来收益，并探究将局部信息结构转化为有效获取目标的可能性。

Method: 提出 SynAFA：一种依赖状态的 AFA 策略，结合成对联合信息与条件信息，并利用部分信息分解（PID）刻画其信息结构；在五个表格数据集与 MNIST-loop 上评估，并辅以置换检验与受控合成实验（包括在总联合信息近似不变时调节协同占比），以及对非贪心局部搜索的诊断分析。

Result: 性能异质：SynAFA 在 PhysioNet 低预算下增益最强（协同与冗余特征对获置换检验支持），但随预算增加优势减弱，且不依赖成对提议；在 MiniBooNE 上显著差于几乎所有基线，在 MNIST-loop 上差于 CAE。合成实验表明在预算内，其优势随联合信息更偏协同而上升。非贪心局部搜索虽改善诊断性的集合级目标，但预测性能未提升甚至常显著变差，且该目标的改善与固定分类器的预测效用仅弱相关。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了成对协同可惠及 AFA 的条件，同时揭示了一个持续存在的挑战：难以将局部信息结构有效转化为获取目标，预算相关的性能衰减无法通过非贪心局部搜索解决。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects informative features under budget constraints. However, existing policies rarely distinguish whether information contributed by interacting features is redundant, unique, or synergistic. We introduce SynAFA, a state-dependent AFA policy that combines pairwise joint information and conditional information, with Partial Information Decomposition (PID) characterizing its information structure. Across five tabular datasets and MNIST-loop, performance is heterogeneous. SynAFA shows its strongest gains at low budgets on PhysioNet, where synergistic and redundant feature pairs are supported by permutation tests, but its advantage diminishes as budgets increase and does not depend on pair proposals. SynAFA performs significantly worse than nearly all baselines on MiniBooNE, and than CAE on MNIST-loop. Controlled synthetic experiments further show that, within budget, SynAFA's advantage rises as joint information becomes more synergy-dominated, including when total joint information is held approximately constant. Further analyses show that the budget-dependent erosion is not resolved by non-greedy local search, which improves a diagnostic set-level objective but leaves predictive performance no better, often significantly worse, and that improvements in this objective are weakly aligned with the fixed classifier's predictive utility. These findings characterize when pairwise synergy can benefit AFA while exposing a persistent challenge in translating local information into effective acquisition objectives.

</details>


### [239] [On the Capability and Limitation of Hard Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32302)
*Lijia Yu,Shuaitong Liu,Gaojie Jin,Xinyu Li,Xiao-Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究硬提示（离散提示）的理论基础，回答了其计算复杂性、本质局限性与泛化能力三个核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程能让大语言模型无需改权重即成为特定任务专家，但现有理论进展多集中于软（连续）提示，更实用的硬（离散）提示理论基本空白，本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 通过理论分析（复杂性证明、构造性/不可能性论证与泛化界推导）研究硬提示；并对比软提示、短硬提示、长硬提示与线性硬提示的表现差异。

Result: 证明判断硬提示是否存在为NP完全、求最优硬提示为NP难；揭示硬提示不完备、短硬提示增强有限、长硬提示出现“提示主导答案现象”，而线性硬提示可避免短/长提示的局限；给出针对有限任务泛化到全分布的提示长度紧界及可泛化的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文提供了关于硬提示的首批理论洞见，并为真实场景下大语言模型的可靠使用给出可证明的实践指导。

Abstract: Prompt engineering has become an indispensable tool for using large language models (LLMs), turning LLMs into task-specific experts without changing their weights. Despite notable theoretical advances in prompt engineering, the theory for the more practical hard or discrete prompts is largely open. In this paper, we try to fill this gap either by providing a complete solution or by making substantial progress on the three core theoretical questions regarding hard prompts.
