First, we show that determining the existence of a hard prompt for a transformer to solve a downstream task is NP-complete and that finding an optimal hard prompt is NP-hard, which is the first computational complexity result for hard prompting, as far as we know. Second, we show that, unlike soft or continuous prompts, hard prompts have essential limitations: hard prompts are not complete; short hard prompts do not significantly enhance the ability of transformers; and long hard prompts exhibit the "prompt dominating answer phenomenon," meaning that, with high probability, the same answer is given for all queries of the same length. On the other hand, linear hard prompts do not have the limitations of short or long prompts. Third, we provide a tight bound on the size of the task in terms of the prompt length for the performance of prompts on the finite task to generalize to the entire data distribution, leading to a necessary and sufficient condition for generalizability. This is the first result on generalization for prompting, as far as we know. Our findings not only offer the first theoretical insights into hard prompts but also provide provably reliable practical guidance for real-world LLM usage.

</details>


### [240] [What Can a Leaderboard Certify? Compositional Controllability for Fair Evaluation and Training of Biomedical Literature-Review Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32318)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Lingxiao Guan,Danqi Hu,Kai Liu,Kevin Chang,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"组合可控性"框架，用于在比较长程智能体时进行可接受性检验，并构建BioLitBench基准，验证了传统排行榜结论的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排行榜仅依据最终输出对长程智能体排名，但更高分数并不能说明两个系统是否具有可比性，也无法定位差异来源于哪个阶段。证据、输入或预算的不对等会影响分数，而统计校正无法消除这种错配。

Method: 引入"组合可控性"概念，比较窗口可覆盖单阶段、多阶段或整个智能体。核心结果用窗口外部的干扰项来界定观测分差与受控分差之间的差距，从而形成一种在查看分数之前即可执行的可接受性检验。不可接受的比较被拒绝；可接受的配对只有在分差超过采样与干扰半径之和时才被认证排序，否则保持未定。据此为每个系统给出排名区间。同时构建BioLitBench基准，包含2,042篇生物医学文献，表示为结构化论断图。

Result: 在七个已发表流程中，传统统计分析在21组两两比较中有14组宣布了胜者，但排名第一的系统是唯一获得了目标综述参考文献的系统。该检验要求匹配输入并固定主干模型，结果拒绝了21组中的11组比较，包括所有涉及排名第一系统的比较；传统结论中有7组落入被拒绝的配对。相同比较窗口还可支持阶段级训练：在Qwen3.8-27B上训练SCRIBE，使用各阶段出口处测得的奖励。在匹配证据下，SCRIBE获得认证排名区间[1,2]，对所有评估的已发表流程以及Claude和OpenAI智能体都具有认证优势；在同一池中，SCRIBE与最强的已发表检索器持平，并被认证高于三个已发表流程。

Conclusion: 仅凭排行榜最终分数进行排名可能产生误导，组合可控性检验能够在查看分数前筛除不可比的比较，并将排序结论转化为带认证的排名区间，从而提高长程智能体评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Leaderboards rank long-horizon agents by their final outputs. Yet a higher score alone does not establish whether two systems are comparable or which stage accounts for the difference. Unequal evidence, inputs, or budgets can affect scores, and statistical corrections do not remove this mismatch. We introduce compositional controllability to address these questions. A comparison window covers one stage, several stages, or the whole agent. Our central result bounds the gap between observed and controlled score differences using only nuisance outside the window. This yields an admissibility test applied before scores are inspected. Inadmissible comparisons are refused. For admissible pairs, an ordering is certified only when the score gap exceeds the combined sampling and nuisance radii; otherwise, it remains undecided. These decisions give each system a rank interval. We introduce BioLitBench, a benchmark of 2,042 biomedical articles represented as structured claim graphs. Among seven published pipelines, a conventional statistical analysis declares a winner in 14 of 21 pairwise comparisons. Yet the top-ranked system alone received the target review's bibliography. To isolate pipeline performance, our test requires matched inputs and a fixed backbone model. It refuses 11 of the 21 comparisons, including every comparison involving the top-ranked system. Seven of the 14 conventional conclusions fall within these refused pairs. The same comparison windows support stage-level training. We train SCRIBE on Qwen3.8-27B using rewards measured at each stage's exit. Under matched evidence, SCRIBE achieves a certified rank interval of [1,2], with certified advantages over all evaluated published pipelines and the evaluated Claude and OpenAI agents. Under same pool, SCRIBE matches the strongest published retriever and is certified above three published pipelines.

</details>


### [241] [Phase Space Attention:A Hairer Lift Circumvents the Single-Layer Induction Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32319)
*Kingsuk Maitra,Shagun Sood Morteza Hosseini,Suman Gunnala,Vikram Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在post-RoPE基底上，通过将注意力提升到辛相空间（辛上剪切变换），绕开SHT单层归纳障碍，构建线性、单步、因果、双线性且辛一致的注意力机制，并给出部署可行性与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHT（Sanford-Hsu-Telgarsky）单层归纳障碍限制了单层注意力实现归纳（induction）的能力。作者希望在保持线性、单步、因果、双线性的设计类内绕过该障碍，而非突破其界本身。

Method: 在post-RoPE基底上将注意力提升到辛相空间（类比Hairer对Stormer-Verlet的提升），把障碍重新表述为滤波阶次缺口：单层双线性得分实现联合阶次(0,0)的z变换，而归纳判别器需要key侧阶次≥1。对post-RoPE的query和key流施加辛上剪切M_γ:(q,p)↦(q+γp,p)来闭合该缺口。证明该提升在因子化子类内唯一、算子层面精确辛、且必须post-RoPE放置。在仅T4的高斯约化下推导闭式双支归纳相变，r=0.9876无拟合参数。部署方面给出精确推导：KV cache不变、前缀复用与投机解码保留、每token每层开销6d FLOPs、INT8余量增加至多log2(1+2γ)比特、融合核不变、不增加参数。

Result: 在91.3M参数下，超临界扫描定位到涌现带：γ=0.80时3/3种子形成归纳，平均717步；γ=0时2/3种子、2700步。作者如实报告不利结果：key-only半提升在归纳准确率上达到0.949，优于对称算子的0.811，若以归纳准确率为目标，半提升是更好的构造。恢复T3通道会使d_k=64时的γ_c从1.030移到0.569并消除交叉。附带41个notebook和结果文件。

Conclusion: 辛相空间提升可在指定设计类内绕开SHT归纳障碍，且部署代价极低（缓存、解码、内核均不受影响）。但该相变律仅为解析可解的极限情形而非稳健预测，T3通道会改变临界点；且对称算子并非归纳准确率最优，半提升更佳。

Abstract: We circumvent the Sanford-Hsu-Telgarsky (SHT) single-layer induction obstruction within a linear, one-step, causal, bilinear, symplectically consistent design class on the post-RoPE substrate, by lifting attention onto a symplectic phase space, mirroring Hairer's lift of Stormer-Verlet. The lift exits the premise of the SHT counting argument rather than the bound itself. The reframing exhibits the obstruction as a filter-order gap: a one-layer bilinear score realises a $z$-transform of joint order $(0,0)$, whereas the induction discriminator requires key-side order $\geq 1$. Applying the symplectic upper shear $M_γ:(q,p)\mapsto(q+γp,p)$ to the post-RoPE query and key streams closes it. We prove this lift is unique within the factorised subclass, exactly symplectic at operator level, and requires post-RoPE placement; and in an explicit $T_4$-only Gaussian reduction we derive a closed-form two-branch induction phase transition, held out at $r=0.9876$ with zero fitted parameters. That law is an analytically solvable limit, not a robust prediction: restoring the $T_3$ channel moves $γ_c$ at $d_k=64$ from 1.030 to 0.569 and removes the crossover.
  Deployability follows by exact derivation: the KV cache is unchanged, prefix reuse and speculative decoding are preserved, overhead is $6d$ FLOPs per token per layer, INT8 headroom grows by at most $\log_2(1+2γ)$ bits, fused kernels are unmodified, and no parameters are added.
  At 91.3M parameters a supercritical sweep locates an emergence band: induction forms 3/3 seeds at $γ=0.80$ in a mean of 717 steps, against 2/3 seeds and 2700 steps at $γ=0$. Adverse results are reported as directly: a key-only half-lift reaches 0.949 against 0.811 for the symmetric operator, so if induction accuracy is the objective, the half-lift is the better construction. Forty-one notebooks and result files ship as ancillary material.

</details>


### [242] [Superposed Inference for Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32320)
*Quanling Zhao,Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Ye Tian,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SupHDC 提出一种共享编码的超维计算推理范式：通过随机槽键叠加多个查询后统一编码，再用槽特定分类器恢复各自预测，实现推理加速且精度损失小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超维计算（HDC）推理对每个查询独立编码，需重复承担高维投影的开销，效率受限，作者希望利用高维冗余性实现多个查询的共享推理。

Method: SupHDC 为每个查询分配轻量随机槽键（slot keys），将加键后的多个查询叠加（superpose）后再统一编码，并使用槽特定的分类器恢复各查询的单独预测；作者用随机特征核视角说明为何无需精确恢复每个超向量，只需保留决定预测的类别证据。

Result: 在十个数据集上，SupHDC 实现 1.39 倍解析加速且平均精度无损，最高 2.08 倍加速仅损失 2.67 个百分点平均精度；在 Raspberry Pi 5 上实现 2.01 倍实测墙钟加速，平均预测精度损失 2.26 个百分点。

Conclusion: SupHDC 表明超维冗余不仅可用于提升鲁棒性，还可作为共享推理的容量资源，从而在几乎不损失精度的情况下提升推理效率。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) is attractive for efficient and robust learning, but conventional inference still encodes every query independently, repeatedly paying the cost of high-dimensional projection. We introduce SupHDC, a new inference paradigm that processes multiple queries through a shared encoding computation. SupHDC assigns lightweight random slot keys, superposes the keyed queries before encoding, and uses slot-specific classifiers to recover their individual predictions. A random-feature kernel view explains why exact recovery of each hypervector is unnecessary: inference only needs to preserve the class evidence that determines the prediction. Across ten datasets, SupHDC achieves 1.39x analytical speedup with no average accuracy loss, and up to 2.08x speedup with only a 2.67 percentage-point mean accuracy loss. On a Raspberry Pi~5, it delivers 2.01x measured wall-clock speedup with a 2.26 percentage-point loss in mean prediction accuracy. SupHDC shows that high-dimensional redundancy can be used not only for robustness, but also as capacity for shared inference.

</details>


### [243] [Not All Errors Matter: Decision-Relevant Prediction Error Predicts Planning Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32322)
*Linhao Wang,Yiyan Fan,Dongjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出总预测误差不能可靠反映世界模型的规划性能，提出决策相关预测误差（DRPE）及等误差评估协议，证明DRPE与规划成功率强相关，而总误差关联弱，并给出DRPE正确排序的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常以预测误差为训练和评估目标，隐含假设"预测越准，决策越好"。作者质疑这一假设：总误差相近的模型可能因误差分布在不同的状态维度上而在规划性能上差异巨大，因此需要一种关注决策相关维度的误差度量与评估方法。

Method: 提出决策相关预测误差（DRPE），仅度量影响决策的状态维度上的预测误差；设计等误差评估协议，在保持总误差不变的前提下改变误差分配方式；在具有已知状态相关性且使用标准化规划器的因子化网格世界中，对55个受控模型与学习模型在不同误差水平和分配下进行评估，并给出DRPE正确排序模型的充分条件的形式化。

Result: 总预测误差与规划成功率仅弱相关（Spearman ρ=-0.25），而DRPE强相关（ρ=-0.84，受控模型族内达-0.98）。总误差仅相差1%的模型，规划成功率可相差60个百分点（97% vs 37%）。相关误差依赖任务，同一总误差下模型排名会随任务反转。更深的想象会放大决策相关误差，学习模型在稀有但决策关键事件上表现出系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 总预测误差不足以评估世界模型的决策价值，评估应聚焦决策相关误差；DRPE与等误差评估协议能更真实地反映模型的规划能力，且作者形式化了DRPE优于总误差的充分条件。

Abstract: World models are typically trained and evaluated by prediction error, assuming that more accurate predictions lead to better decisions. We show that this assumption can fail because models with similar total error can differ substantially in planning performance when their errors occur on different state dimensions. We introduce Decision-Relevant Prediction Error (DRPE), which measures prediction error on the state dimensions that affect decisions. We also develop an iso-error evaluation protocol that varies error allocation while keeping total error fixed. In a factored gridworld with known state relevance and a standardized planner, we evaluate 55 controlled and learned models across different error levels and allocations. Total prediction error is weakly related to planning success (Spearman $ρ=-0.25$), whereas DRPE is strongly predictive ($ρ=-0.84$; $-0.98$ within the controlled family). Models with only a 1\% difference in total error can differ by 60 percentage points in planning success (97\% vs 37\%). The relevant error also depends on the task, with model rankings reversing across tasks at the same total error. Deeper imagination further amplifies decision-relevant errors, while learned models exhibit systematic bias on rare but decision-critical events. We formalize sufficient conditions under which DRPE correctly ranks models and total prediction error cannot.

</details>


### [244] [Active Feature Acquisition With Incomplete Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32325)
*Reza Rezvan,Valter Schütz,Han Wu,Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练数据不完整情形下的主动特征获取（AFA-ITD），分析缺失数据对一步与多步获取价值的影响，并比较三种学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中获取全部特征往往成本过高甚至不可能，且静态特征子集不足以应对不同实例；同时已有AFA方法大多假设训练数据完整，这一假设在实践中常被违背，因此需要研究训练数据缺失下的AFA问题。

Method: 作者形式化定义AFA-ITD问题；在MCAR假设下理论分析一步获取值与多步获取值的变化；并考察三种从不完整数据中学习的方法：别名化（aliasing）、过滤（filtering）和生成式恢复（generative restoration），给出各自的样本复杂度结论。

Result: 理论上，MCAR下一步获取值保持不变，而多步获取值可能下降；过滤方法所需训练实例数随维度呈指数增长，生成式恢复则随获取预算呈指数增长。实验上，在受控实验及常见AFA数据集上发现，缺失性主要损害利用多步获取的方法，生成式恢复在许多实验中能挽回损失的性能。

Conclusion: 训练数据缺失会显著影响依赖多步获取的AFA方法，而生成式恢复是一种在多种情形下有效缓解性能下降的策略。代码已公开。

Abstract: In many prediction tasks, acquiring all features can be a prohibitively expensive or outright impossible task. Further, in many cases a static subset of features may not be enough to solve the problem sufficiently across various instances. Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) addresses these problems by formalizing the trade-off between feature cost and predictive performance during sequential feature selection. However, prior AFA work largely assumes access to complete training data, an assumption that is often violated in practice. Here we study AFA with Incomplete Training Data (AFA-ITD), showing that under missing completely at random (MCAR) data, one-step acquisition values remain unchanged, whereas multi-step values can decrease. We analyze three approaches to learning from incomplete data: aliasing, filtering, and generative restoration. We show that filtering can require a number of training instances scaling exponentially with the dimension, whereas generative restoration scales exponentially with the acquisition budget. We empirically test our theory on a controlled experiment and across common AFA datasets and find that missingness mainly damages methods that exploit multi-step acquisitions and that generative restoration is able to recover lost performance in many experiments. Code is available at https://github.com/Linusaronsson/AFA-Benchmark/tree/missing-data.

</details>


### [245] [Continual Data Unlearning in Diffusion Models via Transition-based Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32328)
*Sunbeom Jeong,Sehwan Kim,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种持续数据遗忘框架，利用已完成的删除转变作为方向性参考来正则化后续更新，在请求累积时更好地平衡删除持久性与生成效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的数据遗忘旨在移除特定训练样本的影响而不抑制其代表的概念。但当删除请求顺序到达时，新请求的更新会损害生成效用并破坏此前的删除效果，现有方法难以同时兼顾二者。

Method: 记录每个请求在相同固定噪声输入上去噪器响应在遗忘前后的变化，将其作为方向性参考。不匹配完整的删除后响应，而是施加单侧惩罚，抑制沿记录方向的逆转，同时不惩罚正交响应变化和超越参考的进展。为保持存储与请求数量无关，维护固定容量的代表性转变记录库，依据进展方向对参数更新的局部敏感性进行筛选保留。

Result: 实验表明，随着请求累积，该框架在删除持久性与生成效用之间取得了优于现有遗忘基线的平衡，且仅需少量转变记忆。

Conclusion: 以已完成删除转变作为方向性参考并结合固定容量记录库，可有效实现持续数据遗忘，兼顾删除持久性与生成效用，并保持存储开销恒定。

Abstract: Data unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove the influence of specific training examples without suppressing the broader concepts they represent. However, when deletion requests arrive sequentially, updates for new requests can degrade generative utility and undermine earlier deletions. We propose a continual data unlearning framework that uses completed deletion transitions as directional references to regularize future updates. For each request, we record changes in denoiser responses on the same fixed noisy inputs before and after unlearning. Rather than matching full post-deletion responses, we apply a one-sided penalty that discourages reversal along the recorded directions relative to the post-deletion references, while leaving orthogonal response changes and progress beyond these references unpenalized. To keep storage independent of the number of requests, we maintain a fixed-capacity bank of representative transition records. Records are selected based on the local sensitivity of progress along their recorded directions to parameter updates, allowing them to be retained even when their penalties are inactive. Empirical evaluations show that the proposed framework achieves a better balance between deletion persistence and generative utility than existing unlearning baselines as requests accumulate, using only a small transition memory.

</details>


### [246] [When the Merge Coefficient Stops Mattering: Proximity Regularized Merging for Continual LoRA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32332)
*Yixuan Liu,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song,Jin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出邻近正则化合并（PRM），在顺序LoRA合并中于任务向量训练阶段加入邻近惩罚，不改变后续写回规则，从而提升免回放持续学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免回放持续学习中使用参数高效适配器时，常将每个新任务学到的低秩适配器合并进运行模型。现有方法主要关注"写回多少"任务向量，但忽略了任务向量本身是否被训练成"可合并"的，这可能是提升效果的关键。

Method: PRM是一种对顺序LoRA合并的最小改动：在任务向量训练期间加入邻近（proximal）惩罚，而保持后续写回规则不变。文中还使用匹配前缀范数控制和邻近强度扫描等诊断手段进行分析。

Result: 在Base→+Prox诊断中，PRM在多种写回规则、骨干网络和类增量设置下提升了AAA；其固定系数变体与强系数基线相比仍具竞争力。机制上，邻近训练缩小任务向量半径、降低Fisher加权干扰、拓宽系数平台，并显现稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Conclusion: 顺序LoRA合并的有效性不仅取决于任务向量写回的程度，还取决于任务向量本身是否被训练得易于合并。PRM以最小改动实现了鲁棒的任务向量正则化。

Abstract: Rehearsal-free continual learning with parameter-efficient adapters can be cast as a sequence of task-vector write-in operations: for each new task, a low-rank adapter is learned and merged into a running model. We propose Proximity Regularized Merging (PRM), a minimal modification to sequential LoRA merging that adds a proximal penalty during task-vector training without changing the subsequent write-in rule. PRM acts as a robust task-vector regularizer: in the reported Base->+Prox diagnostics, it improves AAA across multiple write-in rules, backbones, and class-incremental settings, while its fixed-coefficient variant remains competitive with strong coefficient-based baselines. Mechanistically, matched-prefix norm controls and proximal-strength sweeps show that proximal training shrinks the task-vector radius, lowers Fisher-weighted interference, broadens the coefficient plateau, and exposes a stability-plasticity trade-off. Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of sequential LoRA merging depends not only on how much of a task vector is written in, but also on whether the task vector itself has been trained to be mergeable.

</details>


### [247] [A Comparative Analysis of Attention versus State-Space Models for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32341)
*Enes Arda,Semih Cayci,Atilla Eryilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"信念几何"统一框架，从证据组装、信念维持与寻址三种能力出发，理论比较注意力机制与状态空间模型（SSM）的表征能力，并用实验验证其结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer与SSM是两类重要的序列学习架构，但二者的比较多停留在经验层面，已有的理论分析通常局限于特定任务或特定架构，缺乏统一、通用的分析框架。

Method: 作者提出"信念几何"（belief geometry）统一分析框架：以广义的上下文线性回归为起点，采用累积贝叶斯遗憾（cumulative Bayes regret）作为度量，抽象出序列学习问题所需的三种能力——证据组装、信念维持与寻址，并设计三个可隔离这些能力的案例进行分析。

Result: 在信念维持方面，SSM在平稳聚合核上达到最优遗憾；在位置组装方面，SSM具有记忆优势；在内容寻址方面，softmax注意力相对sigmoid选择性SSM具有指数级宽度优势。基于LLaMA型Transformer与Mamba-2的实验表明，这些架构性结论可推广到解析可处理类与线性回归测试床之外。

Conclusion: 该工作为注意力与SSM的表征能力比较提供了统一的理论工具，揭示了二者在不同能力维度上各自的优势，并通过实验验证了理论洞见的普适性。

Abstract: Transformers and state-space models (SSMs) are two prominent sequential learning architectures, yet their comparison remains largely empirical and existing theoretical analyses are typically task-specific or architecturally restricted. In this paper, we develop belief geometry, a unified analytical framework for comparing the representational capabilities of broad classes of attention and SSMs. Starting from a generalized formulation of in-context linear regression and using cumulative Bayes regret as our measure, we abstract three capabilities required by many sequential learning problems in our belief geometry: evidence assembly, belief maintenance, and addressing. We then study three cases of our formulation that isolate these capabilities and yield sharp architectural lessons: For belief maintenance, SSMs attain the optimal regret over stationary aggregation kernels; for positional assembly, SSMs have a memory advantage; and for content addressing, softmax attention has an exponential width advantage over sigmoid-selective SSMs. Experiments with LLaMA-type Transformers and Mamba-2 show that these architectural insights extend beyond our analytically tractable classes and linear-regression testbed.

</details>


### [248] [Analog-Friendly Predictive Coding without Activation Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32350)
*Francesco Innocenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 activation-matched Bregman PC，用与激活函数匹配的 Bregman 散度替代标准平方误差能量，消除对激活函数导数的需求，并结合 mirror descent 实现仅依赖加权和、局部预测误差、状态积分和激活函数的局部推理与学习规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）作为反向传播（BP）的局部、基于能量的替代方案，其迭代推理动力学适合模拟硬件实现。但标准非线性 PC 需要在推理和学习阶段都计算激活函数的导数，这在物理实现上非常困难。

Method: 提出 activation-matched Bregman PC：将标准平方误差能量替换为与激活函数相匹配的 Bregman 散度，从而消除激活导数；再结合通过 mirror descent 进行的推理，得到仅需加权和、局部预测误差、状态积分和激活函数的局部推理与学习规则。

Result: 在数字实验中，Bregman PC 在分类和生成任务上与标准 PC 和 BP 性能相当，同时保留了 PC 的特征学习动力学，并在稳定的大模型参数化下收敛到 BP。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性 PC 提供了更利于模拟硬件实现的表述，同时保留了其关键计算特性。

Abstract: Predictive coding (PC) is a local, energy-based alternative to backpropagation (BP) whose iterative inference dynamics make it attractive for implementation on analog hardware. However, standard nonlinear PC requires evaluating the derivative of the activation function during both inference and learning, which can be difficult to realise physically. Here, we introduce \textit{activation-matched Bregman PC}, replacing standard squared-error energies with Bregman divergences matched to the activation function. This formulation eliminates activation derivatives and, when combined with inference via mirror descent, yields local inference and learning rules requiring only weighted sums, local prediction errors, state integration, and the activation function. In digital experiments, Bregman PC performs comparably to standard PC and BP on classification and generative tasks, while preserving characteristic learning dynamics of PC and its convergence to BP under stable large-model parameterisations. These results provide a more analog-friendly formulation of nonlinear PC while retaining its key computational properties.

</details>


### [249] [Editable Map-Conditioned Trajectory Generation for Human Mobility Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32360)
*Takayuki Mizuno,Shouji Fujimoto,Mikito Hiruki,Atushi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将路网栅格作为可编辑条件，用自回归模型生成人类移动轨迹，支持不重训练即可响应地图编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间模拟基础设施干预需要能直接响应编辑后地图的移动生成器，但许多数据驱动生成器未将地图作为可编辑条件暴露出来。

Method: 把任务建模为地图条件下的自回归人类移动生成：以道路栅格为条件，解码器输出名义31.25米网格单元、每分钟一令牌的序列；词汇按网格局部化，支持留出与局部编辑地图而不需重训练。实例化ResNet-50视觉前缀配置与ViT交叉注意力配置，在日本石川县874个网格、87,400条手机轨迹上从零训练，其中219个网格留出。

Result: 在110网格测试集上，ResNet-50配置下正确地图生成在60%网格上比打乱地图更接近留出真实轨迹（基于Hausdorff能量距离，p=0.021）；DTW有方向性但不确凿（57%，p=0.074）。与真实密度相关性：正确地图0.38，打乱地图0.01。ViT配置轨迹级敏感性与密度增益更弱。示例性桥梁移除编辑可在不重训练下改变生成续段。

Conclusion: 结果支持可编辑地图人类移动模拟的可行性。

Abstract: Geospatial simulation of infrastructure interventions requires mobility generators that respond directly to edited maps, yet many data-driven generators do not expose the map as an editable condition. We formulate this task as map-conditioned autoregressive generation of human mobility: a road raster conditions a decoder that emits nominal 31.25 m mesh-cell tokens at one-minute intervals. The mesh-local vocabulary supports held-out and locally edited maps without retraining or vocabulary changes. We instantiate a ResNet-50 visual-prefix configuration and a Vision Transformer (ViT) cross-attention configuration, trained from scratch on 87,400 smartphone-derived trajectories from 874 meshes in Ishikawa Prefecture, Japan; 219 meshes are held out. We evaluate map sensitivity by comparing correct-map and within-split shuffled-map generations with held-out real trajectories. On the 110-mesh test split, for the ResNet-50 configuration, correct-map generations are closer than shuffled-map generations on 60% of meshes under Hausdorff-based energy distance (p = 0.021), while DTW is directional but inconclusive (57%, p = 0.074); correlation with real density is 0.38 with the correct map versus 0.01 with shuffled maps. The ViT configuration shows weaker trajectory-level sensitivity and smaller density gains. An illustrative bridge-removal edit changes generated continuations without retraining. Together, these results support the feasibility of editable-map human-mobility simulation.

</details>


### [250] [Black-Box Auditing of Epistemic Reliability in Multi-Agent Debate Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32361)
*Derui Wang,Zewei Shi,Rayne Holland,Ruoxi Sun,Xingliang Yuan,Jason Xue,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究辩论蒸馏中出现的"认识可靠性退化"问题，提出ER-Audit黑盒审计框架，通过对比适应前后冻结验证器检查点，检测被聚合性能提升掩盖的隐藏任务退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论蒸馏能让较弱验证器通过多智能体辩论记录提升判断力，但在受监控任务上的收益并不代表其在相关未监控任务上同样可靠。存在一种风险：适应过程保留了受监控任务表现，却降低了对隐藏任务正确答案的支持。作者想探究这种退化是否仅由灾难性遗忘导致，以及标准评估能否检测到它。

Method: 提出ER-Audit两阶段黑盒审计框架，对比适应前后的冻结验证器检查点。设计两个评估基准，将受监控任务与隐藏任务提示基于共享上下文配对。框架通过评估语义有效的复述寻找"非退化"的反例；若未找到，则使用独立复述进行序贯假设检验。推导了非退化概率的随时有效下置信界，支持有限预算内数据依赖的停止规则。还建立了跨固定复述分布的共同下界，并扩展到与其混合分布总变差距离有界的分布。设置对抗性辩论者，在维护正确受监控回答的同时操纵辩论论点。

Result: 实验显示，更高的隐藏任务准确率可能与更多非退化反例及更低的非退化界同时存在。这种背离挑战了仅用广泛灾难性遗忘来解释的观点，表明审计能揭示被聚合性能提升所掩盖的选择性隐藏任务退化。

Conclusion: 适应会保留受监控性能却降低隐藏任务支持，且这种退化不能仅归因于灾难性遗忘，标准评估无法检测。ER-Audit审计框架可有效发现被聚合指标掩盖的选择性退化。代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Debate distillation adapts weaker verifiers using multi-agent debate transcripts to improve their judgement in subsequent debates, but gains on monitored tasks do not establish reliability on related unmonitored tasks. We study epistemic reliability degradation, in which adaptation preserves monitored performance while reducing support for correct responses on hidden tasks. We consider an adversarial debater that manipulates debate arguments while defending the correct monitored response, and ask whether the resulting degradation merely reflects catastrophic forgetting and whether standard evaluation can detect it. To address these questions, we propose ER-Audit, a two-stage black-box auditing framework that compares frozen verifier checkpoints before and after adaptation, and introduce two evaluation benchmarks pairing monitored and hidden task prompts grounded in shared contexts. ER-Audit searches for counterexamples to non-degradation by evaluating semantically valid paraphrases and, if none is found, uses independent paraphrases for sequential hypothesis testing. We derive anytime-valid lower confidence bounds on the non-degradation probability, allowing data-dependent stopping within a finite budget. We further establish a common lower bound across fixed paraphrase distributions and extend it to distributions within a bounded total variation distance of their mixtures. Our experiments show that higher hidden-task accuracy can coexist with more counterexamples to non-degradation and lower non-degradation bounds. This divergence challenges explanations based solely on broad catastrophic forgetting and shows that auditing can uncover selective hidden-task degradation concealed by aggregate performance gains. Our code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/CSIRO-CQS-AI-alignment-Team/Epistemic-Reliability-Auditor.

</details>


### [251] [DiffPTS: Rethinking Diffusion ELBO for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32363)
*Weiwei Ye,Dongyuan Li,Hangchen Liu,Haotong Jiang,Yoshihide Sekimoto,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视Location-Scale噪声模型下的ELBO，提出统一端到端优化的概率时间序列预测框架DiffPTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DDPM的概率时间序列预测方法遵循标准框架，仅考虑ELBO的部分组件，且将估计器训练视为独立的回归任务，与变分推断框架分离，缺乏统一性。

Method: 作者在Location-Scale噪声模型（LSNM）下重新推导ELBO，发现其自然诱导出估计器的高斯负对数似然目标，并定义了统一近期扩散预测范式的联合训练目标，据此提出可端到端优化ELBO所有组件的通用框架DiffPTS。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，DiffPTS持续优于近期模型，达到SOTA性能，相比现有扩散方法平均CRPS降低超过14.53%、MSE降低超过16.55%。

Conclusion: 通过基于ELBO原则性重构，DiffPTS实现了扩散概率预测中所有组件的端到端优化，验证了统一变分推断框架的有效性。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting requires modeling and predicting complex and time-varying distributions. Recently, Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based approaches have shown promise by equipping the dif- fusion process with pretrained mean and variance estimators to accommodate distributional shift. However, these methods typically follow the standard DDPM framework and consider only partial components of the evidence lower bound (ELBO), treating the training of estimators as designed regression tasks separate from the variational inference framework. To address this, we rethink the ELBO under the Location-Scale Noise Model (LSNM) and find that it naturally induces a Gaussian negative log likelihood objective for the estimators and inherently defines a joint training objective that unifies recent diffusion paradigms for probabilistic forecasting. Building on this principled ELBO reformulation, we propose Diff- PTS, a general framework that enables end-to-end optimization of all components within the ELBO. Across multiple benchmarks, DiffPTS consistently outperforms recent models, achieving state-of-the-art performance with an average CRPS/MSE reduction of over 14.53%/16.55% compared to existing diffusion-based methods. The code is available at https://github.com/wwy155/DiffPTS.

</details>


### [252] [STRIDE: State-Transition Representation via Increment Dynamics and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32367)
*Yuchen Xiong,Siming Huang,Jianfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE框架，将连续预测重构为状态转移预测：用导数指纹定义状态、学习局部转移函数，结合POD与稀疏动态实现连续概率预测，在多个基准上显著优于14个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预测（continuous forecasting）传统上直接预测数值轨迹，本文试图将问题重构为状态转移预测，通过导数指纹定义状态并学习局部转移函数（qpairs），以更好地刻画长时程动态并提升概率预测性能。

Method: STRIDE（State-Transition Representation via Increment Dynamics and Evolution）：以导数指纹定义状态，学习局部状态转移函数（qpairs）；用尾部卷积窗口估计局部联合转移频率，其滞后差分构成高维增量轨迹；通过POD获得坐标路径，用带记忆的稀疏动态联合预测；重组预测并结合历史锚定反演恢复未来转移分布，采样状态路径选择局部函数生成连续预测。实验采用三种子设置，与14个基线在9个基准族上比较，另含5个独立Markov与隐状态基线覆盖230个任务，以及64条独立初始化的Aizawa轨迹匹配对照实验。

Result: 在190个多步配对任务上，系统加权后期Energy获胜比例达73.6%–78.5%；对DLinear在72个配对多步任务上为77.3%。在64条Aizawa轨迹上，相对四个匹配对照的后期Energy降低约24%–56%，四个预设对比均通过Holm校正。

Conclusion: 结果表明转移统计量预测可有效连接到连续概率预测，并在长时程场景下（尤其Aizawa匹配研究）取得显著提升。

Abstract: We introduce STRIDE (State-Transition Representation via Increment Dynamics and Evolution), which defines states through derivative fingerprints and learns local functions for state transitions (qpairs), recasting continuous forecasting as transition prediction. Trailing convolution windows estimate local joint-transition frequencies, whose lagged differences form a high-dimensional increment trajectory. Proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) gives coordinate paths, jointly forecast by sparse dynamics with memory. Recombining their forecasts and applying history-anchored inversion recovers future transition distributions; sampled state paths select local functions to generate continuous forecasts. Three-seed experiments compare STRIDE against fourteen baselines across nine benchmark families. Five independent Markov and hidden-state baselines cover all 230 evaluated tasks, with 220 complete whole-horizon pairs. Against these comparators, system-weighted late-Energy win fractions range from 73.6% to 78.5% on 190 multi-step pairs; against DLinear, the fraction is 77.3% on 72 paired multi-step tasks. Matched controls examine the intermediate representation. On 64 independently initialized Aizawa trajectories, late-Energy reductions against four matched controls range from approximately 24% to 56%, with all four prespecified contrasts passing Holm correction. These results connect transition-statistic prediction to continuous probabilistic forecasting, with substantial long-horizon gains in the matched Aizawa study.

</details>


### [253] [Attribution Without a Second Pass: Inline Per-Sample Gradient Provenance at ~1% Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32380)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Traceprop 通过在训练反向传播中内联记录投影后的逐样本梯度，避免事后归因方法的二次遍历，用 Kronecker 分解草图实现全层扩展，成本低于 1% 墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用的数据归因方法（TRAK、LoGRA、EK-FAC）都是事后的：训练完成后需对训练集再次前向/反向遍历以重算逐样本梯度，集成模型时还要对每个检查点重复该过程，开销巨大。因此需要一种能在训练过程中内联记录梯度、避免额外遍历的方法。

Method: Traceprop 在训练的反向传播中内联记录投影后的逐样本梯度；使用 Kronecker 分解草图（Kronecker-factored sketch），可将追踪从单个层扩展到所有层而无需物化稠密投影矩阵，并支持从最后一块（last-block）到全层不同追踪范围。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA L4 上对 GPT-2 与 Pythia（最大 2.8B）做 LoRA 微调时，内联记录在 last-block 范围仅占墙钟时间的 0.30–1.08%，追踪 Pythia-1B 全部层时仍低于 1%（0.79%）。相比 LogIX（最接近的内联竞品），在相同存储下分解草图便宜 2.0–4.1 倍，且差距随追踪范围增大而扩大，在所有测试范围上均显著，同时归因质量持平或更优。内联构建归因存储比一次事后遍历便宜 60–242 倍，比五检查点 TRAK 集成便宜 301–1211 倍，记录的梯度与 autograd 精确一致。

Conclusion: Traceprop 证明了内联、低开销、可扩展到全层的数据归因记录是可行的，能大幅降低归因成本并保持质量；由于每个存储的梯度都带有源文件血缘信息，同一次训练过程还可同时产出符合 EU AI Act 第 26 条的审计追踪。

Abstract: Data attribution methods used in practice (TRAK, LoGRA, EK-FAC) are post-hoc: after training they make a second pass over the training set to recompute per-sample gradients, repeated per checkpoint when ensembled. Traceprop avoids that pass by recording projected per-sample gradients inline on the training backward pass. A Kronecker-factored sketch scales from a single tracked layer to every layer without materializing a dense projection matrix. On LoRA fine-tunes of GPT-2 and Pythia models up to 2.8B on one NVIDIA L4, inline logging costs 0.30-1.08% of wall-clock time at last-block scope and stays under 1% (0.79%) even when tracking every layer of Pythia-1B. Against LogIX, the closest inline-capable competitor, the factored sketch is 2.0-4.1x cheaper at equal storage, a gap that grows with tracked scope and is significant at every scope tested, while matching or exceeding LogIX's attribution quality at matched storage. Building the attribution-ready store inline is 60-242x cheaper than one post-hoc pass and 301-1211x cheaper than a five-checkpoint TRAK ensemble, with recorded gradients matching autograd exactly. Because each stored gradient carries source-file lineage, the same pass also produces EU AI Act Article 26 audit trails.

</details>


### [254] [TimeES: Probabilistic and Deterministic Time Series Forecasting via Evolutionary Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32384)
*Weiwei Ye,Renhe Jiang,Hangchen Liu,Dongyuan Li,Yoshihide Sekimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TimeES，一种基于演化谱理论（Evolutionary Spectra）的通用框架，可同时支持概率性与确定性时间序列预测，通过可参数化的演化谱建模非平稳随机过程，并将谱估计复杂度从 O(NM) 降至 O(NK)，在多个预测任务上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列本质上是非平稳的，趋势、周期模式与不确定性随时间演化。傅里叶域是建模时间序列的自然视角，但现有深度学习方法未显式建模傅里叶谱的演化性与随机性，因而难以同时准确预测非平稳序列的期望轨迹及其不确定性。

Method: 受演化谱（ES）理论启发，推导可参数化的演化谱表达，将非平稳随机过程建模转化为由随机变量调制的、随时间演化的表示学习。同时利用厄米对称性与谱能量稀疏性进行频率选择，将谱估计复杂度由 O(NM) 降至 O(NK)（K << M/2）。模型采用简单的线性主干网络。

Result: 在确定性与概率性预测任务上均取得一致的最先进性能，并具备高效率与良好的可解释性。

Conclusion: TimeES 将演化谱理论引入深度学习预测框架，用简单线性主干实现高效、可解释且领先的确定性/概率性预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Real-world time series are inherently non-stationary, with trends, periodic patterns, and uncertainty evolving over time. While the Fourier domain offers a natural lens to model time series, current deep learning approaches do not explicitly model evolution and randomness in the Fourier spectra, which limits their ability to accurately predict both the expected trajectory and its uncertainty in non-stationary time series. Motivated by Evolutionary Spectra (ES) theory, we propose TimeES, a general framework that enables probabilistic and deterministic forecasting via the evolutionary spectra theory. Specifically, we derive a parameterizable evolutionary spectra formulation, recasting non-stationary random process modeling as learning an evolving representation modulated by random variables. Furthermore, we reduce the complexity of the estimated spectra from O(NM) to O(NK), where K << M/2, by exploiting Hermitian symmetry and spectral energy sparsity for frequency selection. Based on a simple linear backbone, our proposed TimeES achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across both deterministic and probabilistic forecasting tasks, with high efficiency and interpretability. Code is available at: https://github.com/wwy155/TimeES.

</details>


### [255] [Using Machine Learning to Investigate Predictors of Fasting Blood Glucose: Insights into Circadian Timing and Age Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32386)
*Viktoriya Bu-Dager,Silvia Cirstea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于NHANES 2017–2020数据，构建可解释机器学习框架预测对数空腹血糖，最终用10个预测变量达到MAE 0.0804、R² 0.7761。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖调节受损是代谢功能障碍和2型糖尿病的重要诱因，需要可解释的预测工具来识别关键风险因素并支持代谢健康研究。

Method: 整合NHANES多个子数据集，采用防泄漏流程（先划分数据、再在训练折内进行插补、标准化和独热编码），使用94个候选预测变量及构建的昼夜节律交互项，评估Elastic Net、LASSO和XGBoost模型，并通过MAE、RMSE、R²、校准和SHAP进行性能评估。

Result: 交互增强的XGBoost模型在独立测试集上表现最佳，MAE为0.0804，RMSE为0.1148，R²为0.7761，仅使用10个预测变量。糖化血红蛋白是最主要预测因子，其次是胰岛素、糖尿病诊断、γ-谷氨酰转移酶、年龄、种族和性别。睡眠中点与年龄的交互项在重复随机划分中稳定保留，但贡献相对较小。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明昼夜节律与年龄的交互作用值得在代谢健康领域进一步研究，同时验证了可解释机器学习框架在血糖预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Impaired glucose regulation is a major contributor to metabolic dysfunction and type 2 diabetes. This study developed an interpretable machine-learning framework to predict log-transformed fasting blood glucose using metabolic, hormonal, lifestyle, demographic, nutritional, and circadian variables from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey 2017--2020 pre-pandemic dataset. After merging multiple NHANES sub-datasets, data processing used a leakage-resistant pipeline in which imputation, scaling, and one-hot encoding were performed only after dataset splitting and within training folds. Elastic Net, LASSO, and XGBoost models were evaluated using 94 candidate predictors and engineered circadian interaction terms. Performance was assessed using mean absolute error, root mean squared error, coefficient of determination, calibration, and Shapley Additive Explanations. The final interaction-augmented XGBoost model achieved strong performance on the independent test set, with a mean absolute error of 0.0804, a root mean squared error of 0.1148, and a coefficient of determination of 0.7761, using 10 predictors. Glycohemoglobin was the dominant predictor, followed by insulin, diabetes diagnosis, gamma-glutamyl transferase, age, race, and gender. Among the engineered interaction terms, sleep midpoint multiplied by age was consistently retained in repeated random-split analyses, although its contribution remained modest relative to dominant glycaemic predictors. These findings support further investigation of circadian-age interactions in metabolic health.

</details>


### [256] [Recovery-Directed Symbolic Distillation of Neural Likelihoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32409)
*Kianté Fernandez,Xinwei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种符号蒸馏流水线，将训练好的神经似然转化为可解释的显式表达式，用于高效参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销神经似然可用于解析不可解或未指定似然的模型推断，但其黑箱特性限制了可解释性，因此需要将其转化为可解释且高效的显式表达式。

Method: 采用以参数恢复为导向的目标函数引导符号回归，使其保留参数恢复精度而非仅拟合似然函数；在留出数据集上评估候选表达式，并联合考虑表达式复杂度、参数恢复性能和与学习似然的分布距离进行选择。在扩散决策模型上进行评估。

Result: 恢复导向目标优于标准符号回归目标；所得符号似然在参数评估上比神经似然及精确似然快100倍以上，同时精度损失可控，并在经验数据的贝叶斯层次推断中展示计算优势。

Conclusion: 该流水线为将符号蒸馏与现有神经似然估计方法集成提供了轻量级接口，可适配多种基于模拟的推断场景。

Abstract: Amortized neural likelihoods enable computationally expensive inference for models with analytically intractable or unspecified likelihoods, but their black-box nature limits interpretability. We introduce a symbolic distillation pipeline that converts trained neural likelihoods into explicit, interpretable expressions optimized for efficient parameter estimation. Our approach uses a recovery-directed objective to guide symbolic regression toward expressions that preserve parameter-recovery accuracy rather than merely approximating the likelihood function. Candidate expressions are evaluated on held-out datasets and selected using a criterion that jointly accounts for expression complexity, parameter-recovery performance, and distributional distance from the learned likelihood. We evaluate the pipeline on the diffusion decision model, a classical cognitive model, whose analytically tractable likelihood provides ground truth for controlled evaluation. The proposed recovery-directed objective improves parameter recovery over standard symbolic-regression objectives. The resulting symbolic likelihoods enable over 100 times faster parameter evaluation than both neural likelihoods and, when available, the exact likelihood, while maintaining a manageable loss in precision. We further demonstrate these computational benefits in Bayesian hierarchical inference on empirical data. Our pipeline provides a lightweight interface for integrating symbolic distillation with existing neural-likelihood estimation methods and can be adapted to a range of simulation-based inference settings.

</details>


### [257] [HoTS: Homophily-Aware Temperature Scaling for Graph Neural Network Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32426)
*Inwoo Tae,Yoontae Hwang,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究图节点分类中的置信度校准问题，提出基于局部同配性的温度缩放方法HoTS，在多项基准上取得最佳校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图节点分类中，置信度（通常是最大预测类概率）被用于排序预测、将不确定节点交由人工审核或控制风险，因此需要校准良好的类别概率。现有的事后校准器要么使用单一全局温度，要么使用缺乏原则性结构形式的图感知模块，未能合理刻画局部图结构如何影响节点级校准。

Method: 作者先论证仅依赖logit的温度规则不足：logit相同但局部同配性不同的节点需要不同最优温度。随后在带高斯特征与单层线性GCN的population-concentration contextual stochastic block model下分析，证明在等距类均值条件下，类模板得分的Bayes后验为温度缩放softmax，其逆温度由同配性相关信号强度决定。在同配性正信号区，温度近似与归一化局部同配性成反比。据此提出Homophily-aware Temperature Scaling (HoTS)：根据基于熵的logit集中度与估计的局部同配性为每个节点分配正标量温度。HoTS含三个温度参数，保持预测类别不变，并从校准数据中学习结构校正强度。

Result: 在18个节点分类基准、2个GNN骨干网络和8个校准基线上，HoTS取得最佳平均期望校准误差（ECE）4.79%、最佳平均排名，并在选择性分类中提供最可靠的置信度排序。

Conclusion: 局部同配性应作为节点级校准的结构性因素，HoTS以简单且理论支撑的节点自适应温度缩放实现了优于现有方法的校准性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: For graph node classification, calibrated class probabilities are needed when confidence scores, usually the maximum predicted class probability, are used to rank predictions, defer uncertain nodes to human review, or control risk. Existing post-hoc calibrators either apply one global temperature or use graph-aware modules without a principled structural form. We study how local graph structure should enter node-level calibration. Our first results show that a logit-only temperature rule is insufficient when nodes with identical logits but different local homophily require different optimal temperatures. We then analyze a population-concentration contextual stochastic block model with Gaussian features and a one-layer linear GCN. Under equidistant class means, the Bayes posterior over class-template scores is a temperature-scaled softmax whose inverse-temperature is governed by a homophily-dependent signal strength. In the positive-signal homophilic regime, the resulting temperature decreases approximately inversely with normalized local homophily. This law motivates Homophily-aware Temperature Scaling (HoTS), a simple post-hoc calibrator that assigns each node a positive scalar temperature from entropy-based logit concentration and estimated local homophily. HoTS has three temperature parameters, preserves the predicted class, and learns the strength of the structural correction from calibration data. Across 18 node-classification benchmarks, two GNN backbones, and eight calibration baselines, HoTS achieves the best mean Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of 4.79%, the best average rank, and the most reliable confidence ranking in selective classification. Code is available at https://github.com/inu0104/HoTS.

</details>


### [258] [Beyond the Manifold Hypothesis: Hybrid Spectral Parameterizations for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32432)
*Ségolène Martin,Anne Gagneux,Quentin Bertrand,Rémi Emonet,Mathurin Massias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了流匹配与扩散模型在不同参数化（预测$x_1$、$x_0$或$v$）下性能差异的成因，提出"谱混合"参数化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配与扩散模型可预测不同量（数据$x_1$、源噪声$x_0$或速度$v$），理论上等价但实际性能差异显著，需要阐明其驱动因素并据此设计更优参数化。

Method: 识别两个主要驱动因素：源-数据信噪比，以及神经架构引入的信息瓶颈。分析表明，除内在数据维度外，影响最优参数化最关键的因素是各数据协方差方向上的特定信噪比。据此提出随时间与数据协方差方向自适应调整的"谱混合"参数化；并分析架构诱导的压缩如何改变哪种参数化更易学习。

Result: 所提谱参数化对高斯数据最优；$v$-预测对丢弃方向的敏感性强于$x_1$-预测；跨架构与源尺度的实验表明，该方法在各机制下均稳健，可显著加速优化，且相比标准参数化几乎不增加训练成本。

Conclusion: 参数化选择受信噪比与架构瓶颈共同支配，谱混合参数化能自适应各时间与协方差方向，实现稳健、高效的训练，几乎无额外成本。

Abstract: Flow matching and diffusion can be trained to predict different quantities, most commonly the data $x_1$, the source noise $x_0$, or the velocity~$v$. Although theoretically equivalent, these can lead to substantially different performances. We identify two main drivers for these differences: the source--data signal-to-noise ratio, and the information bottleneck induced by the neural architecture. We show that, beyond intrinsic data dimension, the factor affecting the optimal parametrization the most is a certain signal-to-noise ratio in each data covariance direction. From this analysis, we introduce new \emph{spectral hybrid} parameterizations that adapt across time and data covariance directions; we show that these are optimal for Gaussian data. We also show that architecture-induced compression changes which parameterization is easier to learn, with $v$-prediction being more sensitive to discarded directions than $x_1$-prediction. Experiments across architectures and source scales show that our spectral parameterizations are robust across regimes, can substantially accelerate optimization, while incurring essentially no additional training cost compared with standard parameterizations.

</details>


### [259] [Rethinking Training-Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning: Where It Arises and How to Correct It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32444)
*Tianrun Yu,Kaixiang Zhao,Shangzhe Li,Yuxiao Yang,Porter Jenkins,Weitong Zhang,Taylor W. Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RLVR中训练引擎与推理引擎之间的概率不一致（训练-推理失配），提出校准重要性采样（CIS）：基于logit位移的加性刻画，用置信度感知的截断策略替代无界重要性比率，理论上把误差第二矩项变为常数界，在三个MoE模型和五个数学推理基准上取得最高平均成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中rollout由推理引擎采样、梯度由训练引擎计算，二者对同一token赋予不同概率，导致策略更新存在偏差。精确重要性采样虽可校正，但其误差由重要性比率的无界二阶矩控制，方差大、训练不稳定，因此需要一种既能校正失配又能控制方差的方案。

Method: 提出校准重要性采样（CIS）。首先由实证支持的logit位移刻画：失配表现为log-odds上的加性位移ε_t，由softmax前的逐logit扰动决定，且其分布近似与token置信度无关。基于此设计置信度感知截断——对大的正位移用单一常数阈值截断，映射回重要性比率上限，该上限随token置信度升高而收紧（即高置信token截断更严）。

Result: 理论证明CIS将精确重要性采样中控制误差的无界二阶矩替换为被常数界定的项，代价是由截断超额部分控制的偏差。在三个混合专家模型、五个数学推理基准上的评测中，CIS在三个模型上均取得五个基准的最高平均分。诊断分析显示：相比截断重要性采样，CIS对低置信度token施加的截断偏差更小；而对小重要性权重做向上裁剪会降低留出准确率。

Conclusion: 训练-推理失配可由logit位移的加性结构与置信度无关分布刻画，据此设计的CIS在理论上把无界二阶矩转为常数界，在实证上跨模型跨基准稳定最优，且偏差更集中于高置信token而非低置信token，说明置信度感知的截断方向是有效的。

Abstract: We study training-inference mismatch in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for large language models, where rollouts are sampled by an inference engine while gradients are computed by a training engine, and the two engines assign different probabilities to the same tokens. To account for this discrepancy in policy updates, we introduce calibrated importance sampling (CIS). CIS is motivated by an empirically supported logit-displacement characterization that expresses the mismatch as an additive displacement $\varepsilon_t$ in log-odds, determined by the per-logit perturbation before the softmax, whose distribution is approximately invariant to token confidence. This characterization motivates a confidence-aware truncation: large positive displacements are truncated at a single constant threshold, which maps back to an importance-ratio cap that tightens as token confidence increases. Theoretically, we show that CIS replaces the unbounded second moment that governs the error of exact importance sampling with a term bounded by a constant, at the cost of a bias controlled by the truncated excess. In evaluation across three mixture-of-experts models and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CIS achieves the highest five-benchmark average on all three models among the evaluated baselines. Diagnostic analyses show that CIS places less truncation bias on low-confidence tokens than truncated importance sampling, while upward clipping of small importance weights reduces held-out accuracy.

</details>


### [260] [Length-Independent State Tracking Under a Parallel Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32447)
*Julien Brandoit,Arthur Fyon,Thomas Braipson,Tom Clara,Florent De Geeter,Pierre Sacré,Damien Ernst,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文形式化了有限精度下的有限状态跟踪问题，提出长度无关跟踪需要同时具备收缩与分离两种动态，证明仿射递归在有限精度下最多只能实现确定型自动机，并据此提出非仿射但可并行扫描的神经有限状态机（NFSM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RNN、线性注意力与状态空间模型依赖仿射递归实现可扩展并行训练，但其表达能力保证基于理想算术，在有限精度下不成立——使其高效的并行扫描本身成为扰动来源。作者希望刻画并解决有限精度下的长度无关状态跟踪问题。

Method: 作者首先形式化有限精度下的有限状态跟踪，定义长度无关性（在任意序列长度上保持正确，且精度代价不随长度增长）。指出长度无关跟踪要求单一映射内同时存在两种竞争动态：收缩以抑制数值扰动，分离以保持不同状态可区分。继而证明仿射递归每步只有单一速率兼顾二者，因此在有限精度下最多实现确定型（definite）自动机。为此将扫描兼容性重新解释为计算预算，提出NFSM——一种非仿射、扫描兼容的RNN，专为长度无关有限状态跟踪设计。

Result: 在覆盖阿贝尔群、非阿贝尔群、不可逆幺半群及文本状态跟踪任务的合成基准上，仿射基线在所有非确定型任务上均失败，多数在数百步内失效；而单个NFSM层能学出所有代数任务的精确转移表（从而在测试长度之外也保证正确），NFSM堆叠在文本任务上于所有测试长度均保持完美准确率。

Conclusion: 仿射递归的扫描兼容性并非无代价，它构成有限精度下表达能力的内在预算限制；通过引入非仿射的NFSM，可在保持并行训练效率的同时实现真正的长度无关有限状态跟踪，为长序列处理提供理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Learning robust and scalable finite-state tracking is fundamental to sequence processing. While linear recurrent neural networks (RNNs), linear attention, and state space models enable scalable parallel training through affine recurrences, their theoretical expressivity guarantees assume idealized arithmetic and do not extend to finite precision, where the parallel scan that makes them fast is itself a source of perturbation. We formalize finite-state tracking at finite precision and characterize length independence: tracking that stays correct at every sequence length, at a precision cost that does not grow with the length. We show that length-independent state tracking requires two competing dynamics within a single map: contraction to suppress numerical perturbations and separation to keep distinct states apart. We prove that affine recurrences, which offer a single rate at each step to serve both roles, realize at most definite automata at finite precision. Instead of treating scan compatibility as a restriction on the update map, we reinterpret it as a computational budget and introduce the Neural Finite-State Machine (NFSM): a nonaffine, scan-compatible RNN built for length-independent finite-state tracking. On synthetic benchmarks spanning abelian and nonabelian groups, noninvertible monoids, and textual state-tracking tasks, affine baselines fail on every nondefinite task, most of them within a few hundred steps. A single NFSM layer instead learns the exact transition tables of every algebraic task, which certifies correctness beyond the tested lengths, and a stack of NFSMs keeps perfect accuracy on the textual tasks at every tested length.

</details>


### [261] [Write Back the $Δ$: Revisiting the Same Tokens with Fresh Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32457)
*Wencheng Ye,Anning Hu,Xiangdong Zhang,Tianyi Wang,Yikang Li,Hengyu Jin,Bing Li,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReFlux，一种可学习的反馈图机制，通过将深度增量 Δ 而非完整状态反馈到早期层，让 Transformer 在不显著增加计算的前提下复用深层计算，提升语言建模与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 只能严格前向计算，深层无法回头修正早期表示。现有方法要么重复执行深度（增加计算），要么用预定义方向修改残差流（缺乏实例级适应性）。推理时反馈虽可直接复用深层状态，但"该反馈什么"尚不明确，因此需要寻找更优的反馈信号。

Method: 作者认为层间累积的新增计算量——深度增量 Δ——比完整状态更适合作为反馈信号，具备更有效、可组合、可扩展的特点。基于此提出 ReFlux：一个可学习的反馈图，动态选择并组合携带增量的路径，支持对同一 token 的同步反馈和对后续 token 的流式反馈。

Result: 在多种模型、语料和基准上，同步 ReFlux 在十个语言建模语料上持续降低困惑度，准确率提升 2.1–2.3 点，多跳推理上增益达 4.7 点。流式 ReFlux 保留大部分增益，同时维持基础模型 1x 的理论主干 FLOPs。

Conclusion: ReFlux 是一种高效范式，能够释放 LLM 的潜在计算能力，使模型以更新的表示重新访问同一 token。代码已开源。

Abstract: Transformers process information strictly forward through depth, preventing deeper computation from revisiting and refining earlier representations. To augment the standard forward pass, existing approaches either re-execute depth, incurring additional computation, or modify the residual stream using predefined directions, limiting their instance-level adaptation. Recently, inference-time feedback offers a direct mechanism for recycling endogenously produced computation by writing deeper residual states back to earlier layers, yet what should be fed back remains unclear. We argue that the depth increment Delta, capturing newly accumulated computation between two layers, provides a more effective, composable, and scalable feedback signal than the full state. Building on this observation, we introduce ReFlux, a learnable feedback graph that dynamically selects and composes increment-carrying routes. ReFlux supports synchronous feedback to the same token and streaming feedback to subsequent tokens. Extensive experiments across various models, corpora, and benchmarks show that synchronous ReFlux consistently reduces perplexity across ten language-modeling corpora, and improves accuracy by 2.1-2.3 points, with gains reaching 4.7 points on multi-hop reasoning. Streaming ReFlux further retains most of these gains while preserving the base model's 1x theoretical backbone FLOPs. These results establish ReFlux as an efficient paradigm for unlocking the latent computational potential of LLMs, allowing them to revisit the same tokens with fresh representations. Code implementation can be found at https://github.com/gooogleshanghai/reflux.

</details>


### [262] [Adapting Nonstationary Multi-output Gaussian Processes to Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32464)
*Zikai Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究多目标贝叶斯优化中非平稳多输出GP表达力与决策可靠性不匹配的问题，提出MOLRN-BO并取得最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOBO常用独立GP加平稳核，难以表达非平稳结构且无法在目标间共享信息。但作者发现表达力强的非平稳GP未必带来可靠BO决策：多输出低秩非平稳（MO-LRN）GP在训练拟合好时可能伴随较大离设计误差和过于乐观的采集预测，存在匹配问题。

Method: MOLRN-BO结合正则化共享谱代理与目标特异残差、预序均值校正与温度化协方差缩放，并采用Pareto局部qLogEHVI优化加周期性全局搜索。

Result: 在12个确定性双目标基准上，MOLRN-BO显著优于原MO-LRN，在9种算法的最终归一化超体积和归一化反世代距离上取得最佳平均问题排名，且在不利尾部表现最强，随时优化方面与领先基线相当。

Conclusion: 消融表明共享谱结构改善离设计预测，局部—全局决策策略提升优化性能，分层校准降低系统性候选偏差。说明非平稳多输出代理在结构与使用方式针对序贯优化需求显式调整时，可实现强而稳健的MOBO性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) commonly relies on independent Gaussian processes (GPs) with stationary kernels, limiting its ability to represent nonstationary structure and share information between objectives. However, expressive nonstationary GPs do not necessarily make reliable BO decisions. We study this mismatch for the multi-output low-rank nonstationary (MO-LRN) GP: strong training fit can coexist with large off-design errors and optimistic acquisition predictions. We introduce MOLRN-BO, which combines a regularized shared-spectral surrogate with objective-specific residuals, prequential mean correction and tempered covariance scaling, and Pareto-local qLogEHVI optimization with periodic global search. Experiments on 12 deterministic bi-objective benchmarks show that MOLRN-BO substantially improves upon the original MO-LRN and achieves the best average problem ranks for final normalized hypervolume and normalized inverted generational distance among nine evaluated algorithms. It also achieves the strongest adverse-tail performance while remaining competitive with the leading baselines in anytime optimization. Ablation studies further show that the shared spectral construction improves off-design prediction, the local--global decision policy improves optimization performance, and hierarchical calibration reduces systematic candidate bias. These results demonstrate that nonstationary multi-output surrogates can deliver strong and robust MOBO performance when their structure and use are explicitly adapted to the demands of sequential optimization.

</details>


### [263] [PolyStepOR: Learning to Decide Without Optimal Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32465)
*Viet The Nguyen,Gunther Gust,An Thai Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyStepOR提出一种无需最优参考决策与梯度的决策聚焦学习方法，直接利用实际决策成本训练预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策聚焦学习（DFL）旨在让预测器服务于下游决策质量，但通常依赖昂贵的最优参考决策，且面临分段常数损失难以求导的问题。

Method: PolyStepOR直接从已实现的决策成本训练，无需预先计算最优解；通过修复或不可行惩罚扩展到约束内预测；针对分段常数损失，扰动预测器参数、评估产生的决策，并用最优传输引导朝更低成本方向优化，不依赖导数。

Result: 无需任务特定调参，PolyStepOR在经典优化基准上表现强劲，在预测约束问题与真实世界问题上具有竞争力；理论上刻画了保持决策的扰动与边界检测，界定了对成本误差的敏感性，并为平滑目标建立了平稳性保证。

Conclusion: PolyStepOR用前向评估替代最优参考决策与导数，为决策聚焦学习提供了更实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictors for downstream decision quality, but often relies on optimal reference decisions that are expensive to obtain. We present PolyStepOR, which trains directly from realized decision costs without pre-computed optima and extends to in-constraint predictions through repair or infeasibility penalties. To handle piecewise-constant losses, PolyStepOR perturbs predictor parameters, evaluates the resulting decisions, and uses optimal transport to favor lower-cost directions, requiring no derivatives. Without task-specific tuning, PolyStepOR performs strongly on classical optimization benchmarks and competitively on predicted-constraint and real-world problems. Theoretically, we characterize decision-preserving perturbations and boundary detection, bound sensitivity to cost errors, and establish stationarity guarantees for a smoothed objective. PolyStepOR thus replaces optimal reference decisions and derivatives with forward evaluations.

</details>


### [264] [Bison: Cross-Dataset Learning for Unseen-Compound Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32467)
*Yunfan Liu,Kasra Ghorbani,Yufei Huang,Zicheng Liu,Jiangbin Zheng,Jingbo Zhou,Shaorong Chen,Chang Yu,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨数据集基准Chem-PerturBridge，并开发Bison模型，通过共享基因表示与离散扩散机制整合八个数据集，实现未见化合物转录响应的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未见化合物的转录响应受限于化学覆盖碎片化、实验平台与基因面板异质性，需要评估跨条件下的分子泛化能力。

Method: 基于Chem-PerturBridge构建包含8个数据集、16,771个化合物的基准，测试化合物从所有训练集中剔除；提出Bison模型：共享基因表示连接各原生面板，两个离散扩散模型结合上下文依赖响应与分子偏差，并通过匹配的药物对比监督学习。

Result: 整体响应一致性高，但对药物特异性差异预测较弱；Bison在完整基准上取得最高平均整体响应与药物对比Pearson相关（对比11种独立训练方法）；联合训练较单数据集训练将药物对比平均相关提升27.4%，八个数据集均有增益。

Conclusion: 匹配的药物对比能将互补筛选转化为共享分子监督，用于未见药物响应预测，同时保留原生基因测量。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptional responses to unseen compounds is limited by fragmented chemical coverage and heterogeneous experimental platforms and gene panels. To assess molecular generalization across these settings, we build on Chem-PerturBridge to benchmark eight datasets with 16,771 compounds, withholding test compounds from every training dataset. This comparison reveals that high overall response agreement can coexist with weak prediction of drug-specific differences, despite reproducible signals across repeated measurements. To exploit complementary chemical supervision while targeting these differences, we introduce Bison: a shared gene representation connects native panels, while two discrete diffusion models compose context-dependent responses with molecular deviations learned through matched drug-contrast supervision. A single Bison model jointly trained across all eight datasets achieves the highest mean overall-response and drug-contrast Pearson correlations on the full benchmark in comparison with 11 methods trained independently per dataset. Compared with dataset-specific training of the same architecture, joint training increases mean drug-contrast correlation by 27.4\%, with gains across all eight datasets and improvements in overall response prediction. These results demonstrate how matched drug contrasts turn complementary screens into shared molecular supervision for unseen-drug response prediction while preserving native gene measurements.

</details>


### [265] [On the Pitfalls of Verbalized Confidence Priors for Calibrating Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32470)
*Shuoyuan Wang,Beier Luo,Hao Zeng,Chengyao Yu,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Bingyi Jing,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CalibSFT，一个即插即用的监督微调阶段，通过塑造校准且广泛支持的置信度先验来突破置信度感知强化学习的探索瓶颈，从而减少大推理模型的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）在表达不确定性时常过度自信。现有的置信度感知强化学习虽能优化校准，但依赖on-policy rollout，受限于模型RL前的置信度分布（即置信度先验）。论文揭示该先验高度集中于少数高值并在RL中持续存在，理论上会抑制罕见置信度值的策略梯度更新并抬高预期Brier风险下界，形成探索瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出CalibSFT，一个即插即用的监督微调阶段，在RL前塑造具有广泛支持的校准置信度先验。针对每个问题，CalibSFT构造结合成功率与响应级正确性的置信度目标（可证明保持proper-scoring最优性），并在置信度谱上平衡训练响应以支持多样化探索；同时引入正确性条件监督，在全部响应上引导置信度，仅在正确响应上监督推理过程，从而避免模仿错误推理。

Result: 在16个数学与通用推理基准上，将CalibSFT与五种代表性RL算法结合，均降低了校准误差并提升了判别能力，同时保持可比的准确率。此外，CalibSFT在下游选择性预测与模型路由任务上具有实际收益。

Conclusion: CalibSFT通过塑造校准且支持广泛的置信度先验，有效缓解了置信度感知RL的探索瓶颈，改善了LRM的不确定性校准，具有即插即用、跨算法普适及下游实用等优点。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) often suffer from overconfidence when expressing their uncertainty. Confidence-aware reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising way to optimize calibration. However, it relies on on-policy rollouts and is thus constrained by the model's pre-RL confidence distribution, which we term confidence prior. In this work, we reveal that off-the-shelf LRMs exhibit a confidence prior heavily concentrated on a few high values, which persists throughout RL. Theoretically, we prove that this concentration suppresses policy gradient updates for rarely sampled confidence values and inflates the lower bound on expected Brier risk. To overcome this exploration bottleneck, we propose CalibSFT, a plug-and-play supervised fine-tuning stage that shapes a calibrated confidence prior with broad support before RL. For each question, CalibSFT constructs confidence targets combining its success rate with response-level correctness, which provably preserves proper-scoring optimality, and then balances training responses across the confidence spectrum to enable diverse confidence exploration during RL. To learn from incorrect responses without imitating their reasoning, CalibSFT introduces correctness-conditional supervision, guiding confidence across all responses while supervising reasoning only on correct ones. Across 16 mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks, incorporating CalibSFT reduces calibration errors and improves discrimination across five representative RL algorithms while preserving comparable accuracy. Furthermore, CalibSFT delivers practical benefits for downstream selective prediction and model routing. Our code is available at https://github.com/ml-stat-Sustech/verbalized-confidence-training.

</details>


### [266] [Fast and Precise Learned Charged-Particle Trajectory Regression at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32479)
*Jonathan Renusch,Benjamin Huth,Daniel Murnane,Eleni Xochelli,Doğa Elitez,Paul Gessinger-Befurt,Andreas Stefl,Jeremy Couthures,Andreas Salzburger,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan,Markus Elsing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将高能物理探测器数据中的带电粒子轨迹参数回归视为序列建模任务，提出使用双向门控线性循环编码器，首次实现达到经典轨迹拟合完整精度的端到端学习拟合，并通过自定义内核在GPU推理中获得更高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨迹拟合方法（卡尔曼滤波、线性化最小二乘）虽为稀疏采样线性高斯数据的最优估计器，且通过精细建模可达10^5分之一的精度，但计算成本高。大型强子对撞机的模式识别需要大量计算，降低成本成为重要需求。

Method: 将带电粒子轨迹参数回归建模为序列建模任务，采用双向门控线性循环编码器（bidirectional gated linear recurrent encoder）进行端到端学习，并设计自定义内核以提升GPU推理吞吐量。

Result: 该方法能够复现经典轨迹拟合技术的完整精度；借助自定义内核，GPU推理吞吐量显著高于在同等价位多核CPU服务器上运行的经典拟合软件。

Conclusion: 这是首个达到完整精度并同时具备降低计算成本潜力的端到端学习轨迹拟合方法，其加速效果可为大型强子对撞机的模式识别带来可观成本节约。

Abstract: We propose a training recipe that treats charged-particle trajectory parameter regression on high-energy physics detector data as a sequence-modeling task. Kalman filters and linearized least-squares fits have been the classical standard approach for this task: they are optimal estimators for sparsely sampled linear-Gaussian data and are commonly used for trajectory parameter regression (fitting). The classical fitting techniques implemented for this domain reach a final precision of one part in $10^5$ through detailed modeling of detector geometry, material, detection effects and precise numerical integration of the equations of motion through the detector's inhomogeneous magnetic field. With this study, we demonstrate that using a bidirectional gated linear recurrent encoder, one is able to reproduce the full precision of classical track fitting techniques. Using a custom kernel, we also achieve significantly higher throughput during GPU inference, compared to classical fitting software running on similarly priced multi-core CPU servers representing typically employed hardware. Such a speedup would lead to considerable cost savings for the pattern recognition at the Large Hadron Collider. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end learned track fit to reach the full precision and, at the same time, offer the opportunity to reduce the computing costs.

</details>


### [267] [What Should Federated LoRA Share? FedSAIL via Input-aware Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32485)
*Junye Du,Shuaida He,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSAIL通过引入输入感知的动作矩阵，发现其主右奇异方向在客户端间稳定对齐，据此估计共享动作子空间以正则化本地训练，在多个基准上提升性能并显著降低通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA方法依赖参数相似性来识别跨异构客户端的共享更新结构，但这种相似性可能主要由共同初始化导致，在独立初始化下会退化为随机重叠；且仅依赖参数相似性忽略了本地输入分布的影响。

Method: 论文提出输入感知的动作矩阵（action matrix），用本地层输入的二阶矩统计对适配器更新进行加权。实验发现即使参数相似性消失，该动作矩阵的主右奇异方向仍在客户端间强对齐，且该共享几何保留任务条件差异并随网络深度变化。基于此提出FedSAIL：不直接平均权重，而是估计共享动作子空间来正则化本地训练，同时保留客户端特定的系数。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，FedSAIL相较竞争的联邦LoRA方法持续提升预测性能，同时显著降低通信成本。

Conclusion: 输入感知的动作矩阵比原始参数提供更稳健的跨客户端共享结构，基于共享动作子空间的正则化是联邦LoRA中兼顾性能与通信效率的有效方向。

Abstract: Federated low-rank adaptation (LoRA) requires identifying an update structure that is shared across heterogeneous clients. Prior work reports strong similarity among trained LoRA projection matrices across clients; however, such agreement may be largely induced by common initialization and collapses toward random overlap under independent initialization. More crucially, relying solely on parameter similarity inherently ignores the influence of local input regime. To uncover a more robust shared structure, we introduce an input-aware action matrix that weights the adapter update by the second-moment statistics of local layer inputs. Empirically, while parameter similarity vanishes, the leading right singular directions of this action matrix remain strongly aligned across clients. This shared geometry preserves task-conditioned differences and naturally varies across network depths. Motivated by these findings, we propose Federated Subspace-Guided Action-Informed Learning (FedSAIL). Instead of averaging weights, FedSAIL estimates a shared action subspace to regularize local training while preserving client-specific coefficients. Across several benchmarks, our approach consistently improves predictive performance over competing federated LoRA methods while reducing communication cost significantly.

</details>


### [268] [Elastic Selective Spectral Hybrids for Train-Once, Export-Many Budgeted Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32486)
*Dachuan Song,Chuchu Chen,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出弹性选择性谱混合模型（ESSH），通过输入依赖的衰减和读写门控改造谱状态空间通道，结合滑窗注意力与双速率容量映射，实现单一模型支持多质量-成本档位，并保持高效解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不同计算与延迟预算下部署语言模型需要具备不同质量-成本权衡的紧凑模型。弹性谱状态空间模型虽提供可截断的有序通道，但其线性时不变滤波器无法随上下文演化选择性地保留相关信息或遗忘无关信息。

Method: 将每个Hankel谱通道实现为独立的循环单元，使用拟合的阻尼旋转模式，并引入输入依赖的衰减与写/读门控，使时间保留和状态更新依赖输入，同时保持通道截断与结构化循环。结合选择性谱混合器与滑动窗口注意力，通过双速率容量映射（以不同速率缩减谱通道数与前馈宽度）及全模型蒸馏联合训练多种容量，支持分块并行训练与融合循环解码。

Result: 满容量时ESSH的语言建模质量与同等规模的独立训练模型相当，较小导出模型呈现平滑的质量-成本权衡。在语言理解、检索、跨域文本和DNA实验上验证了质量保留及相对独立训练和弹性基线的权衡优势。1.53B配置下，融合单批解码在B300上为每token 1.37 ms，相比Mamba-2和Mamba-3实现提速2.14-2.80倍，相比同参数量的Transformer++提速3.03倍。

Conclusion: ESSH框架在保持高质量的同时实现了灵活的质量-成本权衡与高效解码，验证了选择性谱混合与容量弹性化结合的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying a language model under different computing and latency budgets calls for compact models with different quality-cost trade-offs. Towards this end, elastic spectral state space models provide ordered and temporally decomposed channels that can be truncated, but they are associated with linear time-invariant filters that cannot selectively preserve relevant past information or forget irrelevant information as the context evolves. To address this, we introduce the Elastic Selective Spectral Hybrid (ESSH), which realizes each Hankel spectral channel as an independent recurrent unit using fitted damped rotation modes. It also features an input-dependent decay and write/read gates that make temporal retention and state update input-dependent while preserving channel-wise truncation and a structured recurrence for efficient execution. ESSH combines these selective spectral mixers with sliding-window attention and jointly trains multiple capacities by reducing spectral-channel count and feed-forward width at different rates through a two-rate capacity map with full-model distillation. The resulting models support chunked parallel training and fused recurrent decoding while avoiding computation for discarded channels. At full capacity, ESSH achieves language-modeling quality comparable to similarly sized independently trained models, while smaller exports exhibit a smooth quality-cost trade-off. We validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework using language understanding, retrieval, cross-domain text, and DNA experiments, by assessing quality retention and the trade-off against independently trained and elastic baselines. At the 1.53B model configuration, fused batch-one decoding takes 1.37 ms per token on a B300, providing a 2.14-2.80x speedup over the tested Mamba-2 and Mamba-3 implementations and 3.03x over Transformer++ at matched parameter counts.

</details>


### [269] [Neural Dynamics as the Composition of Quantized Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32487)
*Jacopo Minniti,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Richard Sproat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"量子(quanta)"这一中间抽象，将深度网络训练描述为可复用计算单元的按序、突发且二值激活式的获得过程，并推导其获得动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习常在两个层面被解读：宏观层面（由缩放律总结的损失聚合趋势）与微观层面（神经元、特征与电路）。核心挑战在于理解这两个层面如何连接，从而解释基本计算如何组合并共同塑造宏观行为。因此需要一个介于两者之间的中间抽象。

Method: 将训练视为"量子"（可复用计算，突发获得、在各样本上二值激活以降低损失）的按序获得；通过近似群体梯度更新推导量子的获得动力学，得到由需求（计算在样本中被需要的频率）与条件复杂度（在已有计算基础上获得该计算的难度）共同支配的获得优先级。在布尔组合任务上推导获得顺序预测；再训练一个 Transformer 完成数字到英文数名的映射，并从其检查点轨迹中恢复候选量子，构建保留大部分行为且暴露可解释潜在计算与获得动力学的模型。此外，量子结构可作为训练目标以提升 Transformer 泛化。

Result: 在布尔组合任务上得到获得顺序的预测，并展示错落的离散获得可产生平滑的聚合损失，且在特定量子组合几何下产生缩放律。在数字到英文数名的 Transformer 上成功恢复候选量子，构建的模型保持大部分原始行为，同时暴露可解释的潜在计算与符合理论的获得动力学；量子结构作为训练目标还能提升泛化。

Conclusion: 量子抽象可为研究多种宏观现象提供有用的"计算原子"。

Abstract: Deep learning is commonly interpreted at two levels: the macroscopic, through aggregate trends in loss summarized by scaling laws, and the microscopic, through neurons, features, and circuits. A central challenge is understanding how these levels connect, so that we can explain how elementary computations compose and collectively shape macroscopic behavior. To this end, we study an intermediate abstraction in which training is described as the ordered acquisition of quanta: reusable computations acquired suddenly and binary-activated across examples to reduce loss. By approximating population-gradient updates, we derive quanta's acquisition dynamics. This yields an acquisition priority governed by demand, how frequently a computation is required across examples, and conditional complexity, how difficult that computation is to acquire given those already available. In a Boolean compositional task, we derive predictions for acquisition order and show how staggered discrete acquisitions can produce smooth aggregate loss and, under certain geometries of quanta composition, give rise to scaling laws. We then train a Transformer to map numerals to English number names and recover candidate quanta from its checkpoint trajectory. From these units, we construct a model that preserves much of the Transformer's behavior while exposing interpretable latent computations and acquisition dynamics consistent with the theory. Separately, the quanta structure can serve as training targets to improve transformer generalization. Together, these results suggest the quanta abstraction can provide useful computational atoms for studying a variety of macroscopic phenomena.

</details>


### [270] [SoFT: Soft Targets for Generalizable LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32493)
*Huihao Jing,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Zhongwei Xie,Guijia Zhang,Yuxuan Liu,Haoyu Huang,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SoFT（软目标微调），通过为示范 token 设定最低目标概率并对 Base 分布做最小 KL 改动，实现多教师、多领域蒸馏中分布内学习与分布外泛化的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师、多领域示范蒸馏到单一学生模型时，学生既要获得多样能力，又要保持对训练任务之外的泛化。实验显示不同 SFT 方法在分布内学习与分布外泛化之间存在不同权衡，因此需要对该平衡进行更显式的控制。

Method: 提出软目标微调（SoFT）：为每个被示范的 token 设定最低目标概率，同时相对 Base 分布做最小的 KL 改动；由此得到的目标函数将示范学习与自适应的、向 Base 模型加权的正则项耦合起来。进一步使用领域特定的梯度预算来控制该平衡，并为每条轨迹确定一个概率阈值。

Result: 在混合领域推理与智能体任务上，SoFT 在对比方法中取得最佳整体性能，分布内能力获取与分布外泛化均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式控制示范学习与保留 Base 能力之间的平衡，SoFT 能在多教师、多领域蒸馏中同时提升分布内能力和分布外泛化。

Abstract: Distillation enables student language models to acquire new capabilities from expert teachers. However, integrating knowledge from multi-teacher, multi-domain demonstrations into a single student remains challenging. We study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) in this setting, where students must acquire diverse capabilities while maintaining generalization beyond the training tasks. Our experiments reveal varying trade-offs between in-distribution learning and out-of-distribution generalization across SFT methods, motivating more explicit control over this balance. To this end, we propose soft-target fine-tuning (SoFT) to balance learning from teacher demonstrations with retaining the Base model's existing capabilities. SoFT sets a minimum target probability for each demonstrated token while making the smallest KL change to the Base distribution. The resulting objective couples learning from demonstrations with adaptively weighted regularization toward the Base model. We further use domain-specific gradient budgets to control this balance and determine a probability threshold for each trajectory. Experiments on mixed-domain reasoning and agentic tasks show that SoFT achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods, with improvements in both in-distribution capability acquisition and out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [271] [Rondo: Unsupervised Discovery of Recurring Temporal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32500)
*Yingtian Shi,Ankith Chandra,Thomas Plötz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rondo 是一种无监督方法，用于在连续时间流中发现多尺度的重复层级结构，通过构建可复用单元及其重复组合的词表，并在流演化过程中不断精炼和扩展发现，在有限数据和持续流场景下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列常具有多尺度结构特性，从短小的重复单元到由这些单元组成的复杂序列。无监督地发现这些组件及结构有助于构建智能系统解读时序数据，减少昂贵的人工标注。然而现有方法通常忽视时间序列的层级结构，将不同时间尺度的重复模式视为相互独立，并且大多假设可访问完整数据序列，把发现视为静态过程，无法随新观测到达而演化。

Method: 提出 Rondo，一种用于在连续时间流中建模重复层级结构的无监督方法。通过显式构建可复用单元及其重复组合的词表，Rondo 捕捉跨复杂时间模式共享的结构，并在流演化过程中不断精炼和扩展其发现。

Result: 在跨多个领域和数据模态的时间序列评估中，Rondo 优于现有的无监督重复发现基线，在有限数据和持续流设置下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: Rondo 为重复模式发现、可扩展的行为理解以及在长时无标签时间流上运行的自适应智能系统提供了更强的基础。

Abstract: Many real-world time series data exhibit structural properties at multiple scales, from short, recurring units to complex sequences composed of these units. Unsupervised discovery of both these components and structure enables the design of intelligent systems that help interpret temporal data, thereby limiting the amount of costly human annotations required. Existing modeling approaches typically overlook the hierarchical structure inherent to many time series, treating recurring patterns at different temporal scales as independent structures. Moreover, most assume access to the complete data sequence and treat discovery as a static process, limiting their ability to evolve as new observations arrive. We introduce Rondo, an unsupervised approach for modeling recurring hierarchical structure in continuous temporal streams. By explicitly constructing vocabularies of reusable units and their recurring compositions, Rondo captures structure shared across complex temporal patterns while refining and expanding its discoveries as the stream evolves. Evaluations on temporal sequences spanning diverse domains and data modalities show that Rondo outperforms existing unsupervised recurrence-discovery baselines, with particularly pronounced advantages in limited-data and continual-stream settings. These capabilities provide a stronger foundation for recurring-pattern discovery, scalable behavior understanding, and adaptive intelligent systems operating on long, unlabeled temporal streams.

</details>


### [272] [Fisher Simplicity in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks and Multilayer Perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32503)
*Ami Tavory,Meir Feder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨KAN中"系数为零/很小"与"Fisher意义下不显著"是否等价，发现两者在固定基KAN中并不一致，系数大小不能作为Fisher剪枝标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN被寄予可解释性期望：边函数可被检视、剪枝并约化为符号结构，因此小或零的基系数常被当作"简单性证书"。但这一直觉缺乏精确的统计依据，需要厘清架构层面的"死单元"概念与Fisher统计层面的"不显著方向"何时一致。

Method: 引入Fisher零空间（Fisher nullity）作为精确统计定义：若扰动某参数方向在任务分布下不可见，则该方向是Fisher简单的。分别分析死ReLU单元与固定基KAN。单层高斯情形下，系数Fisher矩阵是输入分布下的基Gram矩阵，与拟合系数无关。多层KAN中，Fisher简单性基于图路径：数据须到达某基原子，且其扰动须经下游网络传播。将这两个条件编码为有效边测度，并在局部字典独立性与有效测度非退化假设下推导结论。辅以受控诊断实验验证。

Result: 死ReLU单元中两种概念一致：闭合激活区域使相关得分方向消失。固定基KAN中则不一致：单层高斯下Fisher矩阵独立于系数；多层下Fisher简单性依赖图路径条件。受控诊断证实零系数可保持秩，而有效路径断开会移除所预测的方向。

Conclusion: 在局部字典独立性与有效测度非退化条件下，零有效暴露恰好识别边内的Fisher零方向。因此系数幅度本身并非KAN的Fisher剪枝准则。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) are motivated in part by interpretability: their learned edge functions can be inspected, pruned, and reduced to symbolic structure. In a fixed-basis KAN, this makes a small or zero basis coefficient look like a certificate of simplicity, much as a dead rectified linear unit (ReLU) marks unused computation in a multilayer perceptron (MLP). Fisher nullity gives a precise statistical notion: a parameter direction is Fisher-simple exactly when perturbing it is invisible under the task distribution. We study when these architectural and Fisher notions agree.
  For a dead ReLU unit, they agree: the closed activation region makes the associated score directions vanish. For a fixed-basis KAN, they do not. In the single-layer Gaussian case, the coefficient Fisher matrix is a basis Gram matrix under the input distribution and is independent of the fitted coefficients. In a multilayer KAN, Fisher simplicity is graph-path based: the data must reach a basis atom and its perturbation must propagate through the downstream network. We encode these two conditions in an effective edge measure and, under local dictionary independence and effective-measure nondegeneracy, show that zero effective exposure exactly identifies Fisher-null directions within an edge. Controlled diagnostics confirm that zero coefficients can preserve rank while effective path disconnections remove the predicted directions. Coefficient magnitude alone is therefore not a Fisher-based pruning criterion for KANs.

</details>


### [273] [What Do Latent Predictive Vehicle Representations Retain? Measuring State, Geometry, and Local Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32512)
*Enzo Nicolás Spotorno,Josafat Leal Filho,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套针对动作条件潜在预测器的测量协议，通过物理读出分别测试保留性、物理邻域组织、预测与局部响应，并以IPG CarMaker数据的时序联合嵌入预测模型为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆控制器通常以物理量（位置、偏航角、速度、偏航率）定义代价、约束与参考，并要求模型能随命令变化而改变预测。现有仅以潜在目标上的预测误差衡量模型，无法检验这些物理量是否可恢复、是否随命令正确响应，因此需要一种能将物理读出与动作响应分离评估的协议。

Method: 提出一个面向动作条件潜在预测器的测量协议，配合物理读出，分别测试四项能力：保留性、物理邻域组织、预测能力、对命令扰动的局部响应。协议使用未训练编码器作为参照，并设置三条匹配的响应路径以定位误差来源（表示或预测器）。

Result: 在基于IPG CarMaker信号训练的时序联合嵌入预测模型案例中：表示保留了所测平面输出，但同架构的未训练编码器保留得略好；未来命令输入改善了一秒预测且保留性几乎不变；对小命令脉冲的响应在潜在坐标中就已偏离模拟器，导致在所有比较中于邻近命令间选择时后悔值升高；在响应上更新预测器可在局部纠正响应，但以预测精度为代价。

Conclusion: 分别测量保留性、预测与局部响应，是判定潜在预测模型能否作为控制候选模型的关键；该协议为后续闭环评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Models of vehicle dynamics learned from logged states and commands complement physics-based models, and latent world models, which predict in a learned representation, are used to plan and train controllers in other domains. Vehicle controllers are usually specified in physical terms: costs, limits, and references depend on position, yaw angle, speed, and yaw rate, and the optimizer compares or differentiates predicted outcomes across nearby commands. A latent model placed in such a controller must therefore let these quantities be recovered and must change its predictions with commands as the vehicle does, and prediction error on its own latent targets measures neither. We contribute a measurement protocol for action-conditioned latent predictors with a physical readout that separately tests retention, physical-neighborhood organization, forecasting, and local response to command perturbations, using an untrained-encoder reference and three matched response paths that locate errors in the representation or the predictor.
