In a case study of a temporal joint-embedding predictive model trained on signals logged in IPG CarMaker, the representations retain the measured planar outputs, though an untrained encoder of the same architecture retains them slightly better; future-command input improves one-second forecasts with retention nearly unchanged; and responses to small command pulses diverge from the simulator already in latent coordinates, raising regret when choosing among nearby commands in all comparisons. Updating the predictor on responses corrects them locally at a cost in forecast accuracy. Measuring retention, forecasting, and local response separately is thus what qualifies a predictive latent as a candidate model for control, and the protocol provides the basis for its closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [274] [Does Transolver really need a Transformer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32525)
*Shizheng Wen,Siddhartha Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transolver 的核心不在 Transformer 注意力，而在于全局的 slicing/deslicing 混合机制；作者提出 FlashSlice 高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Transolver 类神经算子性能来源：为什么 physics-attention 有效？其中哪些组件真正关键？

Method: 在九个 3D 流体动力学基准上做系统消融；用 averaging neural operators 理论进行分析；提出 FlashAttention 风格的 flashslice 核。

Result: 将 token attention 替换为常数线性映射不影响精度；去除全局混合或仅做一次会导致性能崩溃；slicing/deslicing 配合逐点 MLP 已可实现连续算子的通用逼近；flashslice 与最优实现误差达浮点级别，并显著节省内存与计算。

Conclusion: Transolver 不需要 Transformer 注意力，注意力在此语境下冗余；关键在于全局 slicing/deslicing 混合；flashslice 提供高效可扩展实现。

Abstract: The widely used Transolver family of neural operators is based on physics-attention, which softly assigns the points of an unstructured mesh to a small number of slices, applies self-attention among the resulting tokens, and broadcasts the result back to the points. We provide a comprehensive empirical and theoretical analysis to elucidate the mechanisms which are responsible for model performance. To this end, we perform careful ablations on a challenging suite of nine 3D fluid dynamics benchmarks to find that replacing token attention with a constant linear map does not affect the accuracy. Thus, Transolver does not need a Transformer at all. However, removing the global mixing (slicing/deslicing) or doing it only once leads to performance collapse. We leverage the theory of averaging neural operators to explain and corroborate our findings by showing that just slicing/deslicing, in conjunction with pointwise MLPs, already suffices for universal approximation of continuous operators and attention is redundant in this context. Finally, we provide a novel FlashAttention-style efficient implementation of the key slicing/deslicing module of Transolver. This flashslice kernel streams slice and deslice over the points without materializing the heavy slice-weight tensor, while reproducing the best available implementation to floating point error. At the same time, it leads to very significant memory and compute savings, particularly at large slice counts.

</details>


### [275] [Activation Flow: Manufacturing Activations for Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32530)
*Hong Kiat Tan,Linh Le,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ActFlow方法：无需微调，仅用k个正确标签即可制造激活，破解模型的"蓄意表现不佳"（sandbagging）锁，恢复其在ARC-Easy上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于均值差的激活引导（difference-in-means steering）需要模型表现出目标行为时记录的激活。然而sandbagging模型会故意降低表现，不产生这类激活，导致该引导方法失效，因此需要一种无需微调即可制造目标激活的手段。

Method: ActFlow设定目标logits使每个标注样本的正确答案排第一，并通过在某层向全部k个残差流加入同一个向量x，使logits朝目标移动。ActFlow是一族关于x的常微分方程，每个规则将所需logit变化映射为x的速度。最小范数规则恰好落在目标上，其他规则仅保留Jacobian的顶部奇异方向。

Result: 在三个指令微调模型上测试，每个模型被sandbagging提示词和密码锁定LoRA锁定。当k=40时，保留五个奇异方向的ActFlow将六个锁定模型的平均留出ARC-Easy准确率从0.05提升到0.85，对比微调为0.88、诚实模型为0.92。在18个"锁定模型×k"组合中，有16个优于最小范数规则；其引导方向与诚实均值差方向近乎正交，并在诚实方向失效的两个LoRA锁上成功解锁。

Conclusion: ActFlow提供了一种无需微调、仅凭少量正确标签即可破解sandbagging锁的有效激活引导方法，其保留奇异方向的规则在多数情况下优于最小范数规则，且独立于诚实均值差方向。

Abstract: Difference-in-means steering requires activations recorded while a model shows the desired behavior, which a sandbagging model withholds by deliberately underperforming. We introduce Activation Flow (ActFlow), which manufactures these activations from $k$ correct labels without fine-tuning. ActFlow sets target logits that rank each labeled item's correct answer first, and moves the logits toward them by adding one vector $x$ to all $k$ residual streams at one layer. ActFlow is a family of ordinary differential equations for $x$, one for each rule that maps the required logit change to the velocity of $x$. The smallest-norm rule lands exactly on the targets, while the others keep only the top singular directions of the Jacobian. We test ActFlow on three instruction-tuned models, each locked by a sandbagging prompt and by a password-locked LoRA. At $k=40$, ActFlow keeping five singular directions raises the mean held-out ARC-Easy accuracy over the six locked models from $0.05$ to $0.85$, against $0.88$ for fine-tuning and $0.92$ for the honest models. Furthermore, it scores higher than the smallest-norm rule in 16 of the 18 combinations of locked model and $k$, and its steering direction is nearly orthogonal to the honest difference-in-means direction. It also unlocks two LoRA locks where the honest direction fails.

</details>


### [276] [What Does a ProcGen Generalization Gap Measure? Action Rules, Convergence, and the Missing Random Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32532)
*Abhisek Keshari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张强化学习中的泛化差距需要一个参考基准——随机策略在同一测试关卡上的回报（随机地板），并以 ProcGen 为例说明该基准如何改变对若干标准指标的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的泛化差距（训练关卡回报减留出关卡回报）通常被直接报告而缺乏参考点。作者认为缺失的参考应是一个可测量的随机地板：统一随机策略在相同关卡、相同评测框架下的回报。缺少该基准会导致对策略性能、熵收敛性和泛化结论的误读。

Method: 在 ProcGen 的八个环境中，对相同检查点和相同关卡，比较采样动作与贪心（argmax）测试动作对留出回报的影响；用原始策略熵评估收敛性，并合并效果相同的动作后重新计算；审计十二个先前 ProcGen 代码库的留出评估协议（是否采样测试动作、是否为默认、是否报告循环平均）；并将这些检查应用于作者自己的案例研究。

Result: 采样式与贪心式测试动作会使留出回报双向变化，贪心评估在三个环境中达到或低于随机地板：miner 中采样策略在留出关卡上达到地板的 4.9 倍，而 argmax 低于地板。原始熵显示八分之七环境未收敛，但其中 35-65% 熵分布在效果相同的动作上；合并后仅剩一至三个环境未收敛，对照地板只有 heist 未学到可迁移内容。审计发现十一个使用留出评估的代码库均对策略梯度智能体采样测试动作，九个为默认而非显式选择，六个报告循环内平均而非固定检查点评估。同样的检查使作者案例中一个统计显著的编码器效应被降级，并排除了编码器内训练-测试 CKA 统计量。

Conclusion: 建议每篇报告泛化差距的工作都须说明其动作规则、使用匹配且固定种子的评估协议，并在两个关卡集合上都报告随机地板。

Abstract: A generalization gap in reinforcement learning (return on training levels minus return on held-out levels) is usually reported without a reference point. We argue that the missing reference is a measured random floor: the return of a uniform-random policy on the same levels under the same harness. On ProcGen, the floor changes what several standard numbers mean. On identical checkpoints and levels across eight environments, switching between sampled and greedy (argmax) test-time actions moves held-out return in both directions, and greedy evaluation takes three environments to or below the floor: in miner, the sampled policy scores 4.9x the floor on held-out levels while its argmax scores below it. Raw policy entropy places seven of eight environments short of convergence, but 35-65% of that entropy is spread across actions with identical effects; after merging them, one to three remain short, and against the floor only heist has learned nothing that transfers. An audit of twelve prior ProcGen codebases finds that all eleven with held-out evaluation sample test-time actions for their policy-gradient agents, nine by default rather than explicit choice, and six report running in-loop averages rather than evaluating a fixed checkpoint. Applied to our own case study, the same checks grade down a statistically significant encoder effect and rule out a within-encoder train-vs-test CKA statistic. We recommend that every reported gap state its action rule, use a matched and seeded protocol, and report the random floor on both level sets.

</details>


### [277] [Shared Autoregressive Context Can Distort Relationships in Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32546)
*Thomas S. Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在单次自回归补全中批量生成多条记录时，共享上下文会扭曲合成数据中变量间的关系，且这种扭曲主要由"答案历史"这一因果渠道造成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为降低生成成本，人们常在一次请求中让大模型连续生成多条合成记录（共享补全批处理）。但前序答案会成为后续记录的上下文，可能改变变量之间的关系结构。作者希望检验这种请求构造方式是否损害合成数据的有效性。

Method: 在2,000个欧洲社会调查（ESS）受访者档案上做匹配实验：固定档案、示例、问题与解码参数，仅改变每次请求生成的受访者数量（10条 vs 1条），并在三个随机种子上比较国内相关性误差；使用Qwen3.8-27B与Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct。进一步设计受控干预（重排前序答案数值而保持档案与边际分布不变、隐藏前序答案）来识别因果渠道，并做探索性校正检验。

Result: 每次生成10条相比1条，国内相关系数的平均绝对误差在Qwen3.8-27B上增加48-58%，在Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct上增加114-127%（三个种子一致）。扭曲主要表现为关系强度被夸大，但仍与人类相关性排序保持较高一致性。重排前序数值会显著改变后续答案的相关性，证明答案历史是因果渠道；隐藏前序答案可降低相关性误差，但损害边际精度。探索性校正显示，更低的相关性误差可能与更差的边际分布和回归估计并存。

Conclusion: 请求构造本身就是数据生成过程的一部分；合成数据的有效性必须针对其预期支撑的具体分析来评估，不能仅以边际分布或单一指标判断。

Abstract: Large language models can generate several records within one autoregressive completion, making earlier answers available as context for later records. This paper shows that such shared-completion batching can distort relationships among variables in the resulting synthetic data, using controlled tests on synthetic survey respondents. In a matched experiment on 2,000 European Social Survey profiles, generating ten rather than one respondent per request increases mean absolute error in within-country correlations by 48-58% for Qwen3.8-27B and 114-127% for Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct across three seeds, holding profiles, examples, questions and decoding parameters fixed. The distortion primarily reflects exaggerated relationship strength, while retaining substantial agreement with the human ordering of correlations. Controlled interventions establish answer history as a causal channel: re-pairing the same preceding values, with profiles and marginal distributions fixed, changes correlations among subsequently generated responses. Hiding preceding answers reduces correlation error in the tested settings but worsens marginal accuracy. Exploratory corrections across social-attitude, health and economic data likewise show that lower correlation error can coexist with worse marginal distributions and regression estimates. Request construction is therefore part of the data-generating process, and synthetic-data validity must be evaluated against the analyses the generated data are intended to support.

</details>


### [278] [Prioritizing Repeated LLM Evaluation for Hidden Failure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32547)
*Keita Broadwater,Akin Broadwater*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将大模型可靠性评测建模为预算受限的"隐藏失败发现"问题，通过浅层评测+基于特征排序，优先深挖零失败提示，显著提升隐藏失败发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测通常对每个提示只生成少量随机响应，受推理预算限制，这种浅层评测难以观察到低概率但实际重要的失败。一个在小样本中无失败的提示，可能仍具有非零的潜在失败概率，却被误判为可靠。

Method: 将LLM可靠性评测形式化为预算受限的发现问题：每个提示关联一个未知的逐次生成失败概率。提出预算化发现框架，先对提示集做浅层评测，再利用试验级失败结果与提示派生表示，学习基于特征的失败倾向排序，从而对浅层零失败提示打分，把深层评测预算集中到更可能隐藏失败的提示上。

Result: 在AIRBench和StrongREJECT上跨多种模型与系统提示条件评测。核心检验：不用深层评测结果训练的模型，能否对零浅层失败提示按其深层失败可能性排序。AIRBench上，排序最高的10%未解决提示实现隐藏失败提升：Qwen 2.5 7B为2.54倍，Gemma 3n E4B为1.87倍，分别找回后续观察到的隐藏失败的25.4%和18.7%（随机分配仅10%）。语义邻域与特征消融分析表明，该预测信号可从提示内容与关系的多种表示中恢复。

Conclusion: 将评测视为预算受限的发现问题，并用可迁移的特征排序优先深挖零浅层失败提示，能在有限预算下更高效地暴露隐藏失败；该信号不依赖特定表示，具有稳健性。

Abstract: Large language models are commonly evaluated by generating a small number of stochastic responses for each prompt in a benchmark. Because inference budgets are limited, this shallow evaluation may fail to observe low-probability but operationally important failures. A prompt that produces no failures in a small sample may therefore appear reliable despite having a nonzero latent probability of failure under repeated inference.
  We formulate LLM reliability evaluation as a budget-constrained discovery problem in which each prompt is associated with an unknown per-generation failure probability. We propose a budgeted discovery framework that first performs shallow evaluation across the prompt set and then uses trial-level failure outcomes together with prompt-derived representations to learn a feature-based ranking of failure propensity. The resulting scores prioritize prompts with zero observed shallow failures for deeper evaluation, concentrating the deep-evaluation budget where hidden failures are more likely to be discovered.
  We evaluate this approach on AIRBench and StrongREJECT across multiple model and system-prompt conditions. The central empirical test asks whether models fit without access to deep-evaluation outcomes can rank prompts with zero observed shallow failures according to their likelihood of producing failures under deeper evaluation. On AIRBench, the highest-ranked 10\% of unresolved prompts achieves 2.54x hidden-failure lift for Qwen 2.5 7B and 1.87x for Gemma 3n E4B, recovering 25.4\% and 18.7\% of subsequently observed hidden failures, respectively, compared with 10\% expected under random allocation. Semantic-neighborhood and feature-ablation analyses further show that this predictive signal can be recovered from multiple representations of prompt content and relationships.

</details>


### [279] [Compositional Objectives: Learning Structure in Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32566)
*Pranavchandra Vivekananda,Sumukh Bettadapura,Ajan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究epiplexity（可学习结构）的谱近似计算，提出固定迹约束下的谱目标及组合目标，发现在ImageNet等基准上，观测者"看到什么"比增加关系/交互token更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能可被定义为对可学习新颖性的追求，但缺乏将所学结构组合以采取行动的能力时，可学习新颖性可能无意义。epiplexity通过有界观测者衡量数据中的可学习结构，而其谱近似计算及何种关系应被观测者纳入目标尚不明确。

Method: 提出epiplexity的闭式谱近似，采用固定迹约束；发现epiplexity目标偏好更均匀的谱质量分布而非集中于少数方向。为弥补"分散但未组织成任务特征"的缺口，提出组合目标，让观测者测量图像各部分与交互之间的关系，并与仅给定掩码部分的原始谱目标进行对比。

Result: 在ImageNet训练中，带掩码部分的谱目标产生近乎最大程度分散的表示，并在多数评估中取得最强的冻结特征分类性能，线性探测精度是整图基线的两倍以上。在多个图像基准上，改变观测者所见比添加关系与交互token更重要。面向预测的组合目标显著提升留出观测者预测但分类较弱，表明谱多样性、可预测性与下游效用是不同性质。

Conclusion: 谱分散的有用性不仅取决于保留了多少结构，还取决于观测者向目标提供了哪些关系。

Abstract: Intelligence is defined in many ways. One of these definitions defines intelligence as the pursuit of learnable novelty. However, learnable novelty can be meaningless without the ability to compose the learned structures to take action and achieve goals. Learnable novelty builds on epiplexity, which is a way to measure learnable structure in data through a bounded observer. In this paper, we investigate a closed-form spectral approximation to compute epiplexity. We use a fixed-trace constraint and find that the epiplexity objective prefers a more uniform distribution of spectral mass rather than concentrating it in a small number of directions. However, a representation may spread information across many directions without organizing that information into features useful for a particular task. To address this gap, we propose a compositional objective whose observer measures the relationships between the parts and interactions of an image. We compare it with the original spectral objective given only the masked parts. In our ImageNet training runs, the spectral objective with masked parts produces an almost maximally spread representation while achieving the strongest frozen-feature classification performance on most evaluations, more than doubling the linear-probe accuracy of the whole-image baseline. Across multiple image benchmarks, changing what the observer sees matters more than adding relation and interaction tokens. At the same time, our prediction-oriented compositional objective produces substantially better held-out observer prediction but relatively weaker classification, revealing that spectral diversity, predictability, and downstream utility are distinct properties. These results suggest that the usefulness of spectral spreading depends not only on how much structure is preserved, but on which relationships the observer makes available to the objective.

</details>


### [280] [CAESAR: Clustering via Autonomous Embedding-Space Agglomerative Reorganization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32570)
*Ilan Bacry,Rémi Devaux,Antoine Jardin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAESAR方法，通过重组织预训练嵌入空间以提升聚类效果，且无需预先知道簇数K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最近邻图的聚类算法（如FINCH）高度依赖嵌入空间质量，而预训练视觉与语言模型的嵌入并未针对聚类优化；同时多数强聚类方法需要簇数K作为输入，但真实无监督场景中K通常未知，发现K本身也是问题的一部分。

Method: 训练一个重组织网络，将互为最近邻的样本拉近、将非近邻样本推开，从而得到更适合聚类的重组织嵌入空间；整个CAESAR流程被设计为推断K而非假设K已知。

Result: 在文本和图像数据集上，重组织嵌入相比原始空间持续提升聚类效果；与推断K的同类方法及被给予真实K（具oracle优势）的强深度聚类方法相比，CAESAR在文本上全面胜出，在最难的图像基准上取得最佳结果，在其他基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 重组织预训练嵌入是应对类重叠、簇数未知等现实数据聚类问题的简单而有效的途径。

Abstract: Clustering algorithms that operate on nearest-neighbor graphs, such as FINCH (First Integer Neighbor Clustering Hierarchy), depend heavily on the quality of the embedding space they are given. However, pretrained vision and language model embeddings are not optimized for this purpose. We propose CAESAR, a method that reorganizes a pretrained embedding space: a reorganization network is trained to pull mutual nearest neighbors together and push non-neighbors apart, yielding a reorganized embedding space substantially better suited to clustering. CAESAR offers a second major advantage: it never requires the number of clusters $K$. This matters because in realistic unsupervised settings, $K$ is typically unknown and discovering it is often part of the problem, yet most strong clustering methods take it as an input. We therefore design the entire CAESAR pipeline to infer $K$ rather than assume it is known. Empirically, reorganizing the embeddings consistently improves clustering over the raw space on both text and image datasets. Since the few deep clustering methods that also infer $K$ do not release their code, we complement controlled comparisons with methods that infer $K$ on the same embedding space by comparisons with strong deep clustering methods that are given the true $K$, giving them a substantial oracle advantage. Even so, CAESAR outperforms all of them on text, achieves the best results on the most challenging image benchmark and remains competitive on the others. Reorganizing pretrained embeddings thus emerges as a simple and powerful route to clustering realistic data, where classes overlap and the number of clusters is unknown.

</details>


### [281] [DimPO: Dimensionality Reduction for Attention using Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32579)
*Vojtěch Lanz,Yufei Cui,Prasanna Parthasarathi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 DimPO，将 listwise 偏好优化与 top-k 交叉熵项结合，用于在不更新预训练模型的前提下通过线性投影降低 query/key 维度，并在多种模型和任务上比较其与 KL 基线在长上下文场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性投影可在不更新预训练模型的情况下降低 query 和 key 向量的维度，但何种训练目标最能保持模型行为尚不明确。论文关注是否对 key 的偏好以及最高权重 key 上的注意力质量，比用 KL 散度匹配完整注意力分布提供更好的信号，尤其在长上下文设置中。

Method: DimPO 结合 listwise 偏好优化与轻量 top-k 交叉熵项以保持 head fidelity。它从冻结语言模型的注意力模式进行离线训练，每层一个映射，query 与 key 共享该映射，并与其他层分开训练。

Result: 在 LLaMA3.2-3B、LLaMA3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B 和 Qwen3-4B Instruct 上，pairwise 偏好目标优于 triplet 基线；在最后 40% 层投影到一半维度时，短上下文任务保留 98% 原始分数。投影更多层或在长上下文 RULER 上，pairwise 方法快速退化。KL 和 DimPO 因训练时使用所有 key，在 8B 模型上投影至多 50% 层时，RULER 4k 分数约保留 95%。KL 投影更接近原始注意力分布和注意力输出，但 DimPO 下游性能更好；超过 50% 投影层后，DimPO 在 SQuAD、common-word extraction、frequent-word extraction 和 variable tracking 等任务上逐渐优于 KL。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在降维条件下，保持任务相关注意力的排序与集中度可能比复现完整注意力分布更重要。

Abstract: A linear projection can reduce the dimension of query and key vectors without updating the pretrained model, but it remains unclear which training objective best preserves model behavior. We ask whether preferences over keys and attention mass on the highest-weighted keys provide a better signal than matching the full attention distribution with KL divergence, especially in long-context settings. We introduce DimPO, which combines listwise preference optimization with a lightweight top-k cross-entropy term for head-fidelity. DimPO is trained offline from the attention patterns of a frozen language model, with one map per layer, shared by the query and the keys and trained separately from the other layers. Across LLaMA3.2-3B, LLaMA3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, and Qwen3-4B Instruct models, pairwise preference objectives outperform the triplet baseline and retain 98% of the original score on short-context tasks when projecting to half the dimension on the last 40% of the layers. With more projected layers or on long-context RULER, they degrade rapidly. In contrast, KL and DimPO, which use every key during training, retain about 95% of the original RULER 4k score on the 8B model when projecting up to 50% of the layers. KL-based projections remain closer to the original attention distribution and attention output, yet DimPO achieves better downstream performance. Beyond 50% of projected layers, DimPO increasingly outperforms KL on tasks including SQuAD, common-word extraction, frequent-word extraction, and variable tracking. These results suggest that under dimensionality reduction, preserving the ordering and concentration of task-relevant attention can matter more than reproducing the full attention distribution.

</details>


### [282] [Age of Learning: Temporal Persistence of Prediction Errors as a Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32593)
*Chenyang Wang,Stefan Forsström,Roger Olsson,Di Yuan,Qing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"学习年龄"(Age of Learning, AoL)这一学习状态变量,通过度量预测错误在时间上的持续程度来刻画学习动态,并设计了离线和流式两种训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习算法主要依赖损失、间隔和预测置信度等瞬时信号来刻画模型行为,这些信号只反映当前优化步骤的预测难度,无法捕捉模型"持续错误了多久"这一时间维度。作者希望引入时间持续性信号来区分长期未解决的学习不足与短暂性错误。

Method: 提出AoL变量:错误未解决时AoL持续增加,预测正确时重置。离线设置下,累计样本级AoL并聚合为类别级状态,指导自适应重加权与重采样;流式设置下,因无法访问完整历史,利用当前观测与缓存观测维护轻量级类别级AoL状态。

Result: 在长尾分类任务中,AoL可提升或持平标准训练基线,当学习困难随时间持续时收益更大;多种子流式实验显示在时间稳定的不平衡条件下增益可复现;对类别频率、损失和间隔的分析表明,AoL与常规难度度量相关,但额外捕捉了错误持续时间的信息。

Conclusion: 时间持续性为刻画和控制不平衡及非平稳环境下的学习动态提供了一种有用的补充信号。

Abstract: Current machine learning algorithms primarily rely on instantaneous signals such as loss, margin, and prediction confidence to characterize model behavior. These signals indicate how difficult a prediction is at the current optimization step, but they do not capture how long the model has remained incorrect. We study this temporal dimension of learning and introduce Age of Learning (AoL), a learning-state variable that measures the persistence of prediction errors over time. AoL increases while an error remains unresolved and resets when a correct prediction is achieved, thereby distinguishing persistent under-learning from transient mistakes. We develop AoL-based training strategies for both offline and streaming settings. In offline learning, sample-level AoL is accumulated over training and aggregated into class-level states that guide adaptive reweighting and resampling. In streaming learning, where full historical access is unavailable, we maintain lightweight class-level AoL states using current and buffered observations. Across long-tailed classification settings, AoL improves or matches standard training baselines, with larger benefits when learning difficulty persists over time. Multi-seed streaming experiments further show reproducible gains under temporally stable imbalance. Analysis of class frequency, loss, and margin shows that AoL is related to conventional difficulty measures but captures additional information about error duration. These results suggest that temporal persistence provides a useful complementary signal for characterizing and controlling learning dynamics in imbalanced and non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [283] [Not Every Term Adds New Structure: Sobolev Novelty for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32597)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Yuheng Jing,Tianyi Liu,Chen Li,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sobolev Novelty，一种针对符号回归的项级结构独立性度量，并给出无需调参的理论校准阈值，可即插即用地提升多种符号回归范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归需在庞大的符号结构组合空间中搜索紧凑且有意义的数学方程。现有方法多用表达式级目标（如拟合误差）引导搜索，只能整体评估候选方程，难以判断单个项是否带来真正的新结构、还是与其余项高度冗余。

Method: 提出 Sobolev Novelty：对每个项，基于其在观测输入上的函数值与精确导数构造经验 Sobolev 签名，量化该行为中无法被其余项重建的部分。进一步推导理论校准阈值，形成有原则且无需调参的结构新颖性判定准则。

Result: 在基准真值方程中，92.6% 的项表现出充分的结构新颖性；而 15 种符号回归方法产生的表达式中该比例平均仅为 38.3%，揭示了科学方程与当前符号回归解之间的显著差距。作为轻量插件，Sobolev Novelty 可用于项剪枝、搜索引导、LLM 反馈和数据选择，在多个符号回归范式中均带来一致性性能提升。

Conclusion: Sobolev Novelty 提供了原理清晰、无需调参的项级结构新颖性度量，适用于多种符号回归范式，具有广泛适用性并可持续提升性能。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover compact and meaningful mathematical equations from data, but searching the vast combinatorial space of symbolic structures remains challenging. Existing methods typically guide this process using expression-level objectives, such as fitting error, which assess a candidate equation as a whole but provide little information about whether an individual term contributes genuinely new structure or is largely redundant with the rest of the expression. We introduce \textbf{Sobolev Novelty}, a term-level measure of structural independence for symbolic equations. For each term, we construct an empirical Sobolev signature from its function values and exact derivatives over the observed inputs, and quantify how much of this behavior cannot be reconstructed by the remaining terms. We further derive a theory-calibrated threshold, yielding a principled and tuning-free criterion for identifying structurally novel terms. Using this threshold, 92.6\% of terms in benchmark ground-truth equations exhibit sufficient structural novelty, compared with only 38.3\% on average for expressions produced by 15 SR methods, revealing a substantial gap between scientific equations and current SR solutions. As a lightweight plug-in, Sobolev Novelty can be incorporated into diverse SR paradigms to support term pruning, search guidance, LLM feedback, and data selection, yielding consistent performance gains and demonstrating broad applicability.

</details>


### [284] [AnchorRep: Defending LLMs Against Cross-Model Adversarial Transfer via Representation Repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32602)
*Gal Wertheizer,Rom Himelstein,Tomer Peretz,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AnchorRep，一种通过塑造内部表示几何来防御跨模型对抗迁移攻击的轻量 LoRA 适配器方法，将跨模型攻击成功率降至 ≤1.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对单一开源权重 LLM 优化的对抗攻击可迁移至架构不同的模型，使拥有一个模型白盒访问权限的攻击者能够攻破独立部署的其他系统，形成跨模型共享漏洞。然而现有防御并非针对这种跨模型威胁设计。作者发现跨模型迁移与模型间共享的内部表示几何结构一致，因此将其作为防御切入点。

Method: AnchorRep 直接作用于表示几何：使用轻量 LoRA 适配器，将受防御模型对有害提示的内部表示推离冻结锚模型对相同提示的表示。训练仅使用一小组有害提示，不需要对抗样本。

Result: 在五个模型、四个架构家族上，对 2,000 次迁移攻击，AnchorRep 将跨模型攻击成功率降至 ≤1.1%（其中两个模型为 0%），在 Mistral 上降幅最大（36% → 1.1%）。现有防御虽能降低迁移，但代价高昂：或导致高达 77% 的退化良性输出，或将过度拒绝提高至多 18%。由于标准基于拒绝的指标无法捕捉这类退化良性输出，作者提出 Benign Garble Rate 进行量化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨模型鲁棒性可通过塑造表示几何来实现，而无需针对特定攻击进行训练。同时也说明需要新的指标（如 Benign Garble Rate）来评估防御带来的附带有害影响。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks optimized on a single open-weight LLM can transfer to and jailbreak architecturally different models, allowing an attacker with white-box access to one model to compromise independently deployed systems. This creates a shared vulnerability across models, yet existing defenses are not designed for this cross-model threat. We find that cross-model transfer aligns with shared internal representation geometry, making it a natural defense target. AnchorRep targets this geometry directly with a lightweight LoRA adapter that pushes the defended model's internal representations of harmful prompts away from those of a frozen anchor model on the same prompts. Training uses a small set of harmful prompts and no adversarial examples. Across five models and four architectural families, AnchorRep reduces cross-model attack success rate to <=1.1% on 2,000 transferred attacks (0% on two), including the largest drop on Mistral (36% -> 1.1%). Existing defenses can reduce transfer, but only at high cost either inducing up to 77% degenerate benign output or increasing over-refusal by up to 18%. Because such degenerate benign outputs are not captured by standard refusal-based metrics, we introduce the Benign Garble Rate to quantify them. Our results suggest that cross-model robustness can be achieved by shaping representation geometry, without requiring attack-specific training

</details>


### [285] [Learning the Graph and the Embedding Together: Classifier-Independent Rewiring for Heterophilic Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32613)
*Harshit Kumar,Sujan Chakraborty,Priyanka Saha,Pritam Kar,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出亲和力引导的图重连方法，联合估计图结构与节点表示，输出与分类器无关的重连图和节点嵌入，在异质图上平均提升5.8个点并显著缩小分类器间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在异质图（connected nodes often carry different labels）上优势大幅下降。图重连是常用补救手段，但现有重连方法通常只搭配单一分类器评估，难以区分增益来自新拓扑还是特定分类器配对，因此需要分类器无关的评估与输出。

Method: 提出亲和力引导的重连方法，同时估计图与节点表示。以期望最大化（EM）思路交替进行：在当前图上训练轻量GNN；在模块度目标下结合伪标签同质性项对候选边重新加权。候选边来自紧凑候选池，由对比学习得到的节点相似度与邻域分布亲和力打分。方法输出两个与分类器无关的结果：重连图与在其上学习的节点嵌入。另有完全无监督变体（重连时不使用标签）。

Result: 在六个异质图基准和五个下游分类器上，相比使用标准特征的原始图，在30个分类器–数据集组合中的23个提升准确率，平均增益5.8个点，并将分类器间准确率差距缩小约四倍。消融实验显示两个输出互补：嵌入贡献大部分精度增益，重连图使不同分类器更一致；无监督变体保留大部分改进。重连图同质性更高，并改善标签传播与社区检测。

Conclusion: 亲和力引导重连通过联合学习图结构与表示，提供分类器无关的重连图与嵌入，有效缓解异质图上的性能退化，提升精度、缩小分类器差异并增强图的可分性（同质性、标签传播与社区检测）。

Abstract: Graph neural networks lose much of their advantage on heterophilic graphs, where connected nodes often carry different labels. Graph rewiring is a popular remedy, but rewiring methods are usually evaluated with a single classifier, which makes it hard to tell whether the gains come from the new topology or from that particular pairing. We propose an affinity-guided rewiring method that estimates the graph and the node representation together. It alternates, in the spirit of expectation maximisation, between training a lightweight graph neural network on the current graph and re-weighting candidate edges under a modularity objective with a pseudo-label homophily term. Candidate edges come from a compact pool scored by a contrastively learned node similarity and a neighbourhood-distribution affinity. The method returns two classifier-independent outputs: a rewired graph and a node embedding learned on it. Across six heterophilic benchmarks and five downstream classifiers, it improves accuracy over the original graph with normalised features in 23 of 30 classifier-dataset combinations, with a mean gain of 5.8 points, and reduces the accuracy spread between classifiers about fourfold. A controlled ablation shows that the two outputs are each useful and play complementary roles: the embedding contributes most of the accuracy gain, while the rewired graph makes different classifiers agree. A fully unsupervised variant, which uses no labels during rewiring, retains most of the improvement. The rewired graphs are also more homophilic and improve label propagation and community detection.

</details>


### [286] [Stabilizing the Dynamic Low-Rank Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32615)
*Zhonghan Xu,Ling Wang,Junhao Chen,Jianwei Zhao,Jinwei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析动态低秩训练(DLRT)在高压缩下失败的原因，提出稳定方法SDLRT，通过补偿缓冲和负反馈秩控制，成功找到可训练子网络，并在DeBERTa-v3上以极低参数开销取得SuperGLUE最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低秩参数化下直接训练神经网络可同时减少内存、计算和存储。动态低秩训练(DLRT)因能在线识别高效子网络且无需特殊初始化而具吸引力，但在高压缩率下无法找到可训练网络。

Method: 推导最佳秩-r逼近的梯度流，指出DLRT的偏差源于曲率耦合项(高压缩下不可忽略)。据此提出SDLRT：维护轻量补偿缓冲，重新注入被忽略的顶部奇异方向；并对截断容差引入负反馈以稳定各层秩。

Result: SDLRT在DLRT崩溃的情形下仍能可靠找到可训练子网络；作为DeBERTa-v3上的PEFT适配器，在仅比LoRA多2.8%参数开销下，取得SuperGLUE最佳平均分。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析DLRT失败原因并引入补偿缓冲与负反馈秩稳定机制，SDLRT有效解决了高压缩下的训练不稳定问题，实现高效且性能优异的低秩训练。

Abstract: Training neural networks directly in a low-rank parameterization is an appealing route to reducing memory, compute, and storage simultaneously during both training and inference. Dynamic low-rank training (DLRT), which confines weights to a rank-$r$ manifold via the Galerkin projection of the gradient flow, is particularly attractive because it identifies efficient subnetworks on the fly without specialized initialization or post-factorization. However, DLRT fails to find trainable networks under high compression. In this paper, we derive the gradient flow of the best rank-$r$ approximation and point out that the offset of DLRT comes from a curvature-coupling term which is large and thus non-negligible under aggressive compression. Guided by this analysis, we propose a stable dynamic low-rank training method, named SDLRT, which maintains a lightweight compensation buffer that reinjects the top neglected singular directions. Additionally, we introduce a negative feedback on the truncation tolerance to stabilize each layer's rank. Experimentally, SDLRT reliably finds trainable subnetworks where DLRT collapses and as a PEFT adapter on DeBERTa-v3, it achieves the best average score on SuperGLUE at only $2.8\%$ parameter overhead over LoRA.

</details>


### [287] [Intuition vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32619)
*Shahar Haim,Daniel C. McNamee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结的自监督视觉模型表示支持通过潜在向量算术进行隐式规则推理，无需任务特定微调或生成模型组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型自监督视觉模型的表示几何是否能迁移到视觉推理任务，假设关系性表示可通过编码输入间的相似性与变换，以潜在向量算术实现隐式推理。

Method: 在Bongard问题、ARC-AGI-1/2及新ARC-GEN实例上考察DINOv3、MAE和随机像素投影；使用冻结嵌入的最近质心读出，以及总结演示输入-输出变换的潜在差分向量（直觉向量）。

Result: Bongard基准上简单最近质心读出与任务特定基线差距在4个百分点内；ARC中直觉向量与同任务测试向量对齐更好，无关任务近正交；沿直觉向量迁移查询改善精确输出检索，ARC-AGI-2达70.7；794任务的397,000个ARC-GEN实例中单对直觉向量识别生成任务约87%留一准确率。

Conclusion: 冻结视觉表示上的潜在向量算术支持跨多问题域的隐式规则推断，无需生成模型组件，表明推断抽象变换与生成其特定后果可能是可分离的能力。

Abstract: Large self-supervised vision models learn representations that support scene segmentation and the semantic decomposition of physical objects. We ask whether their representational geometry supports transfer to visual reasoning problems without any task-specific fine-tuning. We hypothesized that relational representations may bridge perception and abstract reasoning by encoding similarities and transformations among visual inputs such that an intuitive, implicit form of reasoning may be performed via latent vector arithmetic. Specifically, we examine DINOv3, MAE, and random pixel projections on abstract and naturalistic Bongard problems, ARC-AGI-1 and ARC-AGI-2, and novel ARC-GEN instances. On both Bongard benchmarks, the accuracy of a simple nearest-centroid readout of frozen visual embeddings is within four percentage points of the task-specific baselines reported with the original benchmarks. In ARC, latent difference vectors summarizing demonstration input-output transformations, which we refer to as intuition vectors, show greater alignment with test vectors from the same task, whereas those from unrelated tasks are near orthogonal. This latent geometry is operational: transporting a query along its intuition vector consistently improves exact-output retrieval, reaching 70.7 on ARC-AGI-2 evaluation. Across 397,000 ARC-GEN instances from 794 tasks, single-pair intuition vectors identify the generating task with approximately 87\% leave-one-out accuracy. These findings suggest that latent vector arithmetic over frozen visual representations supports implicit rule inference across varied problem domains without a generative model component, indicating that inferring an abstract transformation and generating its instance-specific consequence may be separable capacities.

</details>


### [288] [Extremely Fast and Compact Binary Graph Representations via Randomized Operator Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32641)
*Srajan Agarwal,Megha P,Bikas C Das,Zakaria Laskar,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超快、纯代数的图二值哈希方法，直接从图结构构建二值节点表示，无需节点特征或梯度训练，在十个数据集上提升分类精度且生成耗时低于一秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图二值哈希方法面临两难：要么为计算效率牺牲全局拓扑信息，要么生成成本高昂；同时密集浮点节点表示带来显著内存与计算开销。

Method: 借助 Nyström 思想的随机列采样近似高阶结构转移矩阵，并结合高效的标签安全语义传播机制；将所得连续表示按列阈值离散化为紧凑二值码，全程无特征、无梯度训练。

Result: 在十个节点分类数据集上，分类精度持续优于现有无特征二值基线，且多数数据集上生成时间在亚秒级。

Conclusion: 二值表示天然适配事件驱动计算，可兼容神经形态脉冲神经网络与无梯度学习规则；简单的代数近似可为离散图表示学习提供高效替代学习式流程的方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks typically rely on dense, floating-point node representations, which can impose substantial memory and computational costs. Binary graph hashing offers an alternative by encoding node information as compact bit strings. However, existing approaches either sacrifice global topological information for computational efficiency or incur substantial generation costs. We introduce an ultra-fast, entirely algebraic hashing method that constructs binary node representations directly from graph structure, without requiring node features or gradient-based training. Our method approximates a high-order structural transition matrix using randomized column sampling inspired by the Nyström method and combines it with an efficient label-safe semantic propagation mechanism. The resulting continuous representations are discretized through column-wise thresholding to obtain compact binary codes. Experiments on ten node classification datasets show that the proposed method consistently improves classification accuracy over existing feature-free binary baselines while requiring sub-second code generation on many datasets. The resulting binary representations are also naturally suited to event-driven computation, making them compatible with neuromorphic spiking neural networks and gradient-free learning rules. These results demonstrate that simple algebraic approximations can provide an efficient alternative to learned pipelines for discrete graph representation learning.

</details>


### [289] [Quantization-Aware Pre-Training with Constrained Empirical Weight Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32659)
*Ningfeng Yang,Tor M. Aamodt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CEWT方法，一种无超参数、无内存开销的振荡抑制优化器后更新步骤，通过将权重的经验分布约束为零均值高斯来提升量化感知预训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知预训练（QAPT）能提升DNN推理效率，但存在"舍入边界权重振荡"问题，会引入有害噪声并显著降低收敛速度。现有方法或引入额外超参数/内存开销，或无法稳定提升模型精度。

Method: 提出Constrained Empirical Weight disTribution（CEWT）优化方法：作为优化器的事后更新步骤，将权重投影到权重空间中距离最近的点，使其经验分布（直方图）匹配零均值高斯分布。核心洞见是许多量化器隐含假设待量化数据是零均值高斯样本的排列，而该假设在QAPT期间不成立，因此以硬约束方式强制零均值高斯先验来抑制有害噪声。

Result: 在多种SOTA量化器与超球面优化器组合上，训练时间几何平均仅增加4%，即可稳定降低低精度（低至1-bit激活与权重、最大610M参数）LLaMA/GPT模型的预训练困惑度，平均降低2.5、最高降低21个点，且不引入任何超参数或存储开销。

Conclusion: CEWT是首个无超参数、无内存开销的振荡抑制方法，能一致地提升QAPT性能，为低精度大模型预训练提供了高效且易用的优化方案。

Abstract: Quantization-Aware Pre-Training (QAPT) can increase the inference efficiency of DNNs, but a problematic behaviour known as rounding boundary weight oscillation can introduce detrimental noise into the training process and significantly reduce convergence speed. While existing methods can reduce this detrimental noise, they either introduce additional hyperparameters or memory overhead, or cannot consistently improve model accuracy. In this work, we propose optimization with $\textbf{C}$onstrained $\textbf{E}$mpirical $\textbf{W}$eight dis$\textbf{T}$ribution (CEWT), the first hyperparameter-free memory-overhead-free oscillation suppression method that consistently improves QAPT performance: an optimizer post-update step that projects weights to the nearest point in weight space whose empirical distribution (histogram) matches a zero-mean Gaussian. Our key insight is many quantizers are designed with the implicit assumption that the to-be-quantized data are permutations of samples from a zero-mean Gaussian, and this assumption is not true during QAPT. By enforcing the zero-mean Gaussian prior as a hard constraint, CEWT can suppress this detrimental noise. Empirical results on various combinations of SOTA quantizers and hypersphere optimizers suggest, that with a geomean increase of 4% in training time, CEWT can consistently reduce the pre-training perplexity (by an average of 2.5 and up to 21 points) of low-precision (down to 1-bit activations and weights and up to 610M parameters) LLaMA/GPT models without introducing any hyperparameters or storage overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/1733116199/cewt

</details>


### [290] [Equivariant Neural Primal-Dual Assignment for Maximum Common Edge Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32661)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yanchao Li,Zhuo Yang,Yuxin Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ENPDA学习共享的等变神经原始-对偶匹配策略，用于最大公共边子图匹配，可在无需重新训练的情况下快速解新图对，约快三个数量级，并提供每对图的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子相似性搜索需要匹配大量图对，重复查询成本重要；最强基线虽准确但每对图都要训练单独网络，代价高。

Method: 提出等变神经原始-对偶分配（ENPDA）：学习共享匹配策略，重算候选匹配的精确目标边缘并学习修正与步长更新分数；目标价格响应多源竞争同一目标。四轮更新加匈牙利投影得到部分一对一匹配。

Result: 在精确算术下证明每对图保证：重排图会置换分配和价格状态，投影匹配为一对一，修复价格给出有效MCES上界；减去保留边数界定最优性差距，结合结构上限，为291对原生测试中的60对证明全局最优。三个分子基准上比同预算解析方法提升7.4-8.6准确率点，精化搜索一秒后仍保留2.5-3点增益；迁移到社会与蛋白质图边删除任务提升9.1-17.6点；需保留芳香环时所有数据集恢复更多参考键。

Conclusion: ENPDA通过共享神经策略显著加速MCES查询并提供理论保证，在分子及跨域图上优于解析基线。

Abstract: Maximum common edge subgraph (MCES) matching finds a partial vertex correspondence between two labeled graphs that preserves as many labeled edges as possible. Molecular similarity search requires matching many graph pairs, making the cost of repeated queries important. The strongest baseline attains accurate MCES solutions but trains a separate network for each pair. We introduce Equivariant Neural Primal-Dual Assignment (ENPDA), which learns a shared matching policy and applies it to new pairs without further training, answering queries roughly three orders of magnitude faster and recovering its training cost after a few dozen queries. The policy recomputes exact objective marginals for candidate matches and learns corrections and step sizes that update their scores. Target prices respond to competition when several source vertices favor the same target. Four update rounds and a Hungarian projection produce a partial one-to-one matching. We prove per-pair guarantees that hold for any network parameters. In exact arithmetic, reordering either graph permutes the assignment and price states, the projected matching is one-to-one, and repaired prices give a valid MCES upper bound. Subtracting the preserved-edge count bounds the optimality gap; combined with structural caps, these certificates prove global optimality for 60 of 291 native test pairs. On three molecular benchmarks with disjoint train/validation/test splits, ENPDA improves over an analytic counterpart with the same update and projection budget by 7.4-8.6 accuracy points; after one second of refinement search, 2.5-3 points of the gain remain. Transferred without fine-tuning to edge-deletion tasks from social and protein graphs, the policy gains 9.1-17.6 points over the analytic counterpart. When output matchings must keep aromatic rings intact, ENPDA recovers more reference bonds than the baselines on all three datasets.

</details>


### [291] [Distance-KV: Exploiting Relative Distance for Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32663)
*Xianpeng Shang,Canbin Huang,Jiang Li,Tian Lan,Qianyi Cai,Xiaojun Quan,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Distance-KV，根据层、注意力头、相对距离的联合空间学习静态 KV 保留模式，无需在线打分即可压缩 KV 缓存，在长文本任务中性能最佳并显著降低显存与解码延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理的显存占用与解码延迟随上下文长度快速增大。现有 KV 缓存压缩方法多依据 token 重要性或跨头的注意力模式差异来保留缓存，但论文发现检索能力随相对距离显著变化，即使在同一注意力头内也是如此，这一结构尚未被利用。

Method: Distance-KV 在层、注意力头与相对距离的联合空间中学习一个静态 KV 保留模式。该模式在离线阶段以冻结的语言模型学习，并可跨输入复用，据此对 KV 缓存进行剪枝与紧凑化，无需在线重要性打分。

Result: 在三个骨干模型与四个长文本基准上，Distance-KV 总体性能一致优于同类 KV 缓存压缩方法，在 RULER 128K 上比最强压缩基线高出最多 9.3 分。在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 的 128K 场景下，KV 显存减少 65.4%，解码相比 Dense 提速 1.66 倍。

Conclusion: 相对距离是理解 LLM 长上下文信息检索的重要结构维度，也是设计更高效推理方法的关键依据。

Abstract: The memory usage and decoding latency of LLM inference grow rapidly with context length. To reduce these costs, key-value (KV) cache compression methods selectively retain cached states based on token importance or differences in attention patterns across heads. However, we discover that retrieval capability varies substantially with relative distance, even within the same attention head. To exploit this structure, we introduce Distance-KV, which learns a static KV retention pattern over the joint space of layers, attention heads, and relative distances. The pattern is learned offline with the language model frozen and reused across inputs to prune and compact the KV cache without online importance scoring. Across three backbone models and four long-context benchmarks, Distance-KV consistently achieves the best overall performance among competing KV cache compression methods, exceeding the strongest compression baseline by up to 9.3 points on RULER at 128K. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct at 128K, Distance-KV reduces KV cache memory by 65.4% and achieves a $1.66\times$ decoding speedup relative to Dense. Together, these results identify relative distance as an important structural dimension for understanding how LLMs retrieve information over long contexts and for designing more efficient inference methods.

</details>


### [292] [Timestep Weighting: A Hidden Key to Effective ELBO-Based Flow-Matching RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32665)
*Qinwei Ma,Jingzhe Shi,Simin Fan,Ling Li,Mengdi Wang,Alex Lamb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于ELBO的强化学习中时间步加权的影响与动态特性，发现有效加权取决于奖励景观与学习阶段，并提出简单静态加权、预算化剖面选择与动态调度以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ELBO的RL为flow matching模型的奖励反馈微调提供了与采样器无关的方法，其中时间步加权对性能影响巨大，还能统一理解先前工作中启发式选择的预测损失，但该问题研究不足，通常直接沿用预训练配置。作者希望系统探究时间步加权的影响与动态规律。

Method: (1) 在受控的CIFAR图像生成实验及机器人任务上，研究加权如何影响不同噪声水平下的奖励驱动更新；(2) 通过梯度分析揭示跨噪声协调的模式及其在训练中的演化；(3) 基于分析，研究简单静态加权、预算化剖面选择与动态调度。

Result: 有效加权同时依赖奖励景观和学习阶段；梯度分析揭示了跨噪声协调的不同模式及训练演化；所提出的静态加权、预算化剖面选择与动态调度均能超越常规目标选择，提升性能。

Conclusion: 时间步加权是flow-matching RL中的重要设计选择；有效加权可能取决于策略当前行为与奖励偏好行为之间的差距，需进一步研究在学习过程中选择与自适应调整加权的方法。

Abstract: ELBO-based reinforcement learning offers a sampler-agnostic approach to fine-tuning flow matching models with reward feedback. Timestep weighting in ELBO-based RL has large impact on performance, and it also provides a unified view (as we show in this work) to understand prediction losses heuristically chosen in prior work, yet it remains under-researched and is often chosen to inherit pretrain configs. We investigate impacts and dynamics of timestep weighting in ELBO-based RL. We show that effective weighting depends on both the reward landscape and stage of learning. (1) Through experiments on controlled CIFAR image generation, complemented by robotics, we investigate how weighting impacts reward-driven updates across noise levels. (2) Through gradient analysis, we reveal distinct patterns of cross-noise coordination across tasks and their evolution during training. These findings motivate the hypothesis that useful weighting depends on the gap between the policy's current behavior and the behavior favored by the reward. (3) Guided by this analysis, we study simple static weighting, budgeted profile selection, and dynamic schedules that improve performance beyond conventional target choices. Our results establish timestep weighting as an important design choice for flow-matching RL and motivate further research into methods that choose and adapt it throughout learning.

</details>


### [293] [STAMP: Predicting Out-of-Distribution Generalization without Target Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32672)
*Md Kawsher Mahbub,Milon Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP是一种仅用源域数据、无需目标域标签的OOD性能预测准则，通过对比语义稳定图像对的输出相关性来估计模型泛化能力，在医学影像和ImageNet多种分布偏移上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测训练模型在分布偏移下能否泛化仍然困难，尤其在无法获得目标域数据时。现有方法受限，因此需要一个仅依赖源域、无需目标标签的评估准则。

Method: STAMP（语义时序增强模型预测）从成对的源域图像出发，计算输出空间的相关比η²=S_B/S_T，通过对比语义稳定图像对与随机图像对来度量：η²越高，说明模型输出随语义身份变化而非噪声变化。还提出了类别匹配变体和温度缩放的STAMPTS版本。

Result: 在44个胸部X光模型（涵盖CNN、ViT、MetaFormer、基础模型及SSL/VLM探针）上，temporal STAMP与VinDr-CXR、CheXpert、MIMIC-CXR的macro AUROC达到0.844–0.855的Spearman相关；类别匹配变体将单类RSNA从0.311提升至0.663。在27个ImageNet模型上，STAMPTS在ObjectNet达到ρ=0.984，在四种额外分布偏移上ρ≥0.905，控制ImageNet准确率后偏相关为0.662–0.949。单GPU约12秒/模型。

Conclusion: STAMP取得了最佳的平均源域医学排名，在无目标数据的情况下优于目标域的ATC和AoTL估计器，是一种实用的部署前模型选择与审计工具。

Abstract: Predicting whether a trained model will generalize under distribution shift remains difficult, especially when target-domain data are unavailable. We introduce STAMP (Semantic Temporal Augmented Model Prediction), a source-only, target-label-free criterion that estimates out-of-distribution (OOD) performance from paired source-domain images. STAMP computes the output-space correlation ratio $η^2=S_B/S_T$ by contrasting semantically stable pairs with random pairs: higher $η^2$ indicates that model outputs vary with semantic identity rather than nuisance variation. On 44 chest X-ray models spanning CNNs, ViTs, MetaFormers, foundation models, and SSL/VLM probes, temporal STAMP attains Spearman correlations of $0.844$--$0.855$ with macro AUROC on VinDr-CXR, CheXpert, and MIMIC-CXR; a class-matched variant improves single-class RSNA from $0.311$ to $0.663$. STAMP attains the best average source-only medical ranking and outperforms the target-domain ATC and AoTL estimators without any target data. On 27 ImageNet models, temperature-scaled STAMPTS attains $ρ{=}0.984$ on ObjectNet and $ρ\geq0.905$ on four additional distribution shifts, with partial correlations of $0.662$--$0.949$ after controlling for ImageNet accuracy. Requiring approximately 12s per model on one GPU, STAMP is a practical pre-deployment model-selection and auditing tool.

</details>


### [294] [SIFT: Enhancing Time Series Foundation Models via Semantic Invariance and Structural Fidelity Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32676)
*Yi Tang,Tengxue Zhang,Yang Shu,Chenjuan Guo,Chenchen Sun,Yisheng An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SIFT方法，通过语义不变对抗增强与结构保真增强，提升时间序列基础模型微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型虽具备强大零样本能力，但因其低维特性和结构多样性，朴素微调易导致过拟合和均值预测陷阱。

Method: SIFT在微调中保持语义不变性与结构保真：采用语义不变对抗增强，通过语义谱分解划分语义空间，在非核心语义子空间生成扰动以增强鲁棒性；并实施基于组件的结构保真增强，进行组件级mixup并施加重构目标以保持结构保真。

Result: 在10个真实数据集上对代表性TSFM进行实验，SIFT显著提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: SIFT能有效缓解过拟合与均值预测陷阱，显著增强时间序列基础模型的适应能力。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have achieved remarkable zero-shot performance through extensive pre-training on massive time series datasets. Nevertheless, due to the low-dimensional properties and diverse structural patterns of time series data, performing naive fine-tuning on TSFMs often leads to overfitting and falling into the mean-prediction trap. To address these challenges, we propose SIFT, a robust adaptation method that enhances time series foundation models by preserving Semantic Invariance and structural Fidelity throughout the fine-Tuning process. We employ semantic-invariant adversarial augmentation, which utilizes semantic spectrum decomposition to partition the semantic space and then generates perturbations within the non-core semantic subspace to bolster the model's robustness against these perturbations, mitigating overfitting. We implement a component-based structural fidelity enhancement, which facilitates component-wise mixup and imposes a reconstruction objective to improve the model's ability to preserve structural fidelity, alleviating the mean-prediction trap. Extensive experiments on representative TSFMs covering 10 real-world datasets demonstrate that SIFT can significantly enhance the performance of TSFMs.

</details>


### [295] [The GUI Is Not the State: Diagnosing State Aliasing in GUI World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32679)
*Dongsheng Liu,Chao Jin,Wenkui Yang,Hejin Wang,Junwei Yang,Zeren Zhang,Ziwei Chen,Huaibo Huang,Jie Cao,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出GUI世界模型仅依赖当前观测和动作会产生"状态别名"问题，并提出诊断基准StateAliasBench与轻量预测状态恢复方法，通过状态增强显著改善基于状态敏感的预测与下游智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI世界模型（GUI-WMs）在预测未来状态时大多只以当前GUI观测和动作为条件，忽略了决定状态转移的隐藏环境状态。这导致"状态别名"：可见界面遗漏了转移相关状态，使相同的可观测条件可能对应不同的合法未来，从而影响智能体的规划与仿真可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出诊断基准StateAliasBench，通过严格配对显式分离这类歧义；2) 提出轻量级"预测状态恢复"方法，从历史中推断结构化状态，并以确定性的状态接口增强原本冻结的GUI-WMs；3) 采用家族特定专家完成异质状态类型的恢复，并通过多教师蒸馏整合为统一的估计器。

Result: 实验显示：现有GUI-WMs在仅观测条件下出现系统性失败；引入预测状态增强后，各评测世界模型的状态敏感预测大幅恢复，生成保真度得以保持，AndroidWorld上GUI智能体的下游性能也获得提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的GUI世界建模不仅应关注"可见的内容"，还应建模决定后续演化的隐藏转移状态。

Abstract: GUI World Models (GUI-WMs) are increasingly used to predict future states for agent planning and simulation, yet most existing formulations condition only on the current GUI observation and action. We identify state aliasing, where the vis- ible interface omits transition-relevant environment state, so identical observable conditions can correspond to different valid futures. To diagnose this failure mode, we introduce StateAliasBench, a diagnostic benchmark that explicitly isolates such ambiguities via strict pairing. We further propose lightweight predictive- state recovery that infers structured state from history and augments otherwise frozen GUI-WMs through a deterministic state interface. Family-specific special- ists provide state recovery across heterogeneous state types, and multi-teacher dis- tillation consolidates them into a single unified estimator. Experiments show that existing GUI-WMs exhibit systematic failures under observation-only condition- ing, while predictive-state augmentation substantially restores state-sensitive pre- diction across evaluated WMs, preserves generative fidelity, and improves down- stream performance of GUI agents on AndroidWorld. These results suggest that reliable GUI world modeling should account not only for what is visible, but also for the hidden transition state that determines what happens next.

</details>


### [296] [Self-Evolving Time-Series Forecasting Agents with Episodic Memory and Online Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32689)
*Junyi Wang,Yilin Wang,Wen Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FASE——一种反馈感知的自进化时序预测智能体，利用已完成后预测实例的真实值作为反馈，通过情景记忆与在线策略学习提升后续预测，无需更新 LLM 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的时序预测智能体大多只关注当前预测实例，不保留或利用早期实例反馈；而现实在线预测中，随预测原点推进，先前实例的真实目标会逐步可得，这些反馈本可用于调整后续动作，但现有方法未能利用。

Method: FASE 将反馈转化为任务特定经验，结合两个组件：(1) 情景记忆，检索相关的已完成后实例；(2) 在线策略学习，把跨实例累积的反馈总结为排序指导（ranking guidance）。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 基准选取的 29 组数据集配置上评估，FASE 在评估方法中取得最强的总体点预测性能，相较最佳单一基础模型基线，归一化 MAE 降低 9.1%；随着延迟反馈累积，FASE 的累积优势增大。

Conclusion: FASE 能通过已完成预测实例的反馈持续自进化，且在无需更新 LLM 参数的情况下实现性能提升，体现了在线反馈驱动经验积累的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used for time-series forecasting because they can organise contextual information, perform multi-step analysis, and guide the sequence of actions required to complete forecasting tasks. Most existing agents focus only on the current forecasting instance. However, in real-world deployments, forecasting commonly operates online, with new forecasts issued from the currently available history as the forecast origin advances and the ground-truth targets of earlier instances progressively become available. These targets provide feedback on the actions taken in earlier instances, yet existing agents generally do not preserve or utilise this information to adapt their subsequent actions. To address this limitation, we introduce FASE, a Feedback-Aware Self-Evolving forecasting agent that converts such feedback into task-specific experience for subsequent forecasting instances. FASE combines episodic memory, which retrieves relevant completed instances, with online policy learning, which summarises the feedback accumulated across instances into ranking guidance. The proposed framework is evaluated on 29 dataset configurations selected from the GIFT-Eval benchmark. Across these 29 configurations, FASE attains the strongest aggregate point forecasting performance among the evaluated methods and reduces the normalised MAE by 9.1% relative to the best individual foundation model baseline. The results further indicate that the cumulative advantage of FASE increases as delayed feedback accumulates. Together, these findings demonstrate that FASE can continually self-evolve through feedback from completed forecasting instances without updating the parameters of the LLM.

</details>


### [297] [BiasReducer: Adaptive Bias Mitigation for Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32720)
*Shuang Liu,Yongliang Miao,Yanguang Liu,Haoyi Xiong,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量框架BiasReducer，通过编辑奖励模型的线性奖励头并按数据集选择相关属性编辑，缓解奖励模型对长度、自信度等表层属性的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型会偏好长度或自信度等表层属性，导致LLM产出评分更高但并非更正确的回答。现有缓解方法要么需重训练（耗数据与算力），要么只针对预知的单一固定偏见做固定修正，缺乏灵活性。

Method: BiasReducer仅编辑线性奖励头，并按新数据集选择相关编辑。先用SAE风格编码器学习奖励模型敏感的属性；再学习如何降低对每个属性的依赖，即确定调整奖励头的方向与幅度；面对新数据集时，按属性对奖励分数的影响力排序，选取相关属性并据此编辑奖励模型。

Result: 在五个奖励模型上，BiasReducer持续提升对表层属性偏见的鲁棒性；BiasReducer-M在三个基准上平均提升8.3、18.0、6.9个百分点，优于两个基于训练的基线；收益可迁移至下游，减少不必要的冗长与谄媚，同时保持相当的评价质量。

Conclusion: BiasReducer作为一种轻量、可自适应选择编辑的框架，能有效缓解奖励模型的表层属性偏见，且优于训练类基线，并改善下游LLM行为。

Abstract: Reward models score responses from large language models (LLMs) and guide LLM training toward human preferences. However, reward models can favor superficial attributes such as length or confidence, leading LLMs to produce higher-scoring but not more correct responses. Existing mitigation methods either retrain the reward model or apply a fixed correction to one known bias, such as a preference for longer responses. Retraining requires additional data and computational resources, while existing editing methods require the target bias to be specified in advance and use a fixed edit for that bias. To this end, we propose BiasReducer, a lightweight framework that edits only the linear reward head and selects the relevant edits for each new dataset. First, BiasReducer uses a sparse autoencoder (SAE)-style encoder to learn which attributes (e.g., length and confidence) the reward model is sensitive to. Second, it learns how to reduce the reward model's dependence on each attribute by determining which direction to adjust the reward head and how much to adjust it. Third, for a new dataset, it ranks the attributes by their influence on reward scores, selects the relevant ones, and edits the reward model accordingly. BiasReducer consistently improves reward-model robustness to biases toward superficial response attributes. Across five reward models, BiasReducer-M improves the three benchmarks by 8.3, 18.0, and 6.9 percentage points on average, outperforming the two training-based baselines. The gains transfer downstream, reducing unnecessary verbosity and sycophancy while maintaining comparable judged quality.

</details>


### [298] [Scaling Properties of Same-Family On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32722)
*Yuntai Bao,Qinfeng Li,Guoqing Jiang,Liwei Chen,Zhiheng Qin,Xuanping Li,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在不同规模LLM间的能力迁移规律，发现早期存在"有效迁移"阶段，并拟合出幂律预测峰值表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL能让大模型获得强大推理能力，但这种能力在模型规模间的迁移程度与速度尚不清楚，需要研究其缩放规律。

Method: 研究OPD在弱到强、同基座、强到弱三种师生设定下的缩放性质，用留出准确率（gold score）与KL散度平方根d的关系刻画早期动力学，并拟合幂律描述峰值与斜率随师生参数量和教师分数的变化。

Result: 早期OPD普遍呈现有效迁移区间，gold score随d近似线性上升；所有弱到强配对中，学生峰值gold score都超过其教师，说明小型RL专家可向更大模型迁移能力。幂律显示教师规模只在约达学生规模前有益，且相同gold score下更小的教师迁移效果更好。

Conclusion: 教师的分数本身不能定义其监督价值；研究还考察了两种OPD变体、弱到强OPD自举以及在线策略监督程度的缩放效应。

Abstract: *Reinforcement learning (RL)* can induce substantial reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), but how much of this capability transfers across model scales, and how quickly, remains unclear. We study the scaling properties of *on-policy distillation (OPD)* across *weak-to-strong*, *same-base*, and *strong-to-weak* teacher--student setups. We find that early OPD training dynamics uniformly exhibit a regular *useful-transfer* regime, in which held-out accuracy (the *gold score*, $G$) rises approximately linearly in $d=\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}(π_θ\Vert π_{\mathrm{ref}})}$, the square root of token-level reverse KL divergence from the student initialization. In every observed weak-to-strong pair, the student's peak gold score exceeds its teacher's own, so a compact RL expert can transfer capability to a much larger student via OPD. To estimate OPD outcomes, we fit *power laws* for how $G_{\mathrm{peak}}$ and the slope of the useful-transfer regime scale with student and teacher parameter counts and with teacher gold score. These laws show that peak gold score improves with teacher scale only up to roughly the student's scale, and that at a matched gold score smaller teachers transfer better, so a teacher's score alone does not define its supervision value. We also study the scaling effects of two OPD variants, bootstrapping weak-to-strong OPD, and the degree of on-policy supervision.

</details>


### [299] [Distributionally Robust Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning: Finite-Sample Guarantees under Weak Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32727)
*Chenyu Lu,Zijun Chen,Nian Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究弱通信条件下平均奖励分布式鲁棒强化学习（DR-RL），给出有限样本保证与显式样本复杂度，并验证 n^{-1/2} 收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均奖励、弱通信设定下，鲁棒强化学习的有限样本理论尚不完善，尤其缺乏统一的半径条件与显式样本复杂度分析。

Method: 针对 SA 矩形与 S 矩形结构，分析基于散度（KL、f_k）和基于距离（TV、Wasserstein）的不确定性集；给出鲁棒平均奖励贝尔曼方程存在常增益解的半径条件；提出无需先验知识的算法。

Result: KL 与 f_k 散度球需显式半径条件；TV 与 Wasserstein 球任意正半径即可，且不要求名义 MDP 弱通信。估计鲁棒最优平均奖励的样本复杂度为 Õ(|S||A|p_∧^{-1}Span²(u_δ^*)ε^{-2})，学习 ε-最优策略为 Õ(|S||A|p_∧^{-2}Span²(u_δ^*)ε^{-2})；给出 Span(u_δ^*) 的近紧上界，数值实验验证 n^{-1/2} 收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作为平均奖励弱通信下的分布式鲁棒强化学习建立了有限样本理论，覆盖多种矩形结构与不确定性集，并提供了近乎最优的偏差函数跨度上界。

Abstract: We study distributionally robust reinforcement learning (DR-RL) in the average-reward setting under weak communication. Our main result provides finite-sample guarantees for estimating the robust optimal average reward and learning a near-optimal policy, covering both SA-rectangular and S-rectangular structures with divergence-based and distance-based uncertainty sets. Specifically, for Kullback--Leibler and $f_k$-divergence balls, we establish explicit radius conditions under which the robust average-reward Bellman equation admits a constant-gain solution, while for total variation and Wasserstein balls, any positive radius suffices without requiring the nominal MDP to be weakly communicating. Our algorithm is prior-knowledge-free and achieves sample complexities of $\widetilde O(|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}|p_{\wedge}^{-1}\operatorname{Span}^{2}(u_δ^{\ast})ε^{-2})$ for estimating the robust optimal average reward and $\widetilde O(|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}|p_{\wedge}^{-2}\operatorname{Span}^{2}(u_δ^{\ast})ε^{-2})$ for learning an $ε$-optimal policy. Here, $p_{\wedge}$ is the smallest positive nominal transition probability and $u_δ^{\ast}$ is a robust optimal bias function. We further provide an almost-tight explicit upper bound on $\operatorname{Span}(u_δ^{\ast})$. Finally, we validate the predicted $n^{-1/2}$ convergence rate through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [300] [Predicting the Financial Impact of Supply Chain Risk for Major AI-Related Semiconductor Firms: A Heterogeneous Graph Patch Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32741)
*Jianna Hur,Sagar Samtani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异质图补丁Transformer，提前1-2个季度预测半导体供应链财务压力在财报中的传导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体生产依赖全球多层级供应链，一家企业的财务压力会延迟扩散，最终影响AI芯片设计企业的收入、库存与盈利。多数企业只能看到直接合作伙伴，而既有预测研究主要关注市场类风险指标，缺少从供应链压力到财报表现的前瞻工具。

Method: 构建异质图补丁Transformer：在15186家公司、60个季度的网络上学习；通过学习门控融合季度基本面与宏观贸易、事件、灾害信号；沿供应商、客户、所有权、总部四类关系进行带类型、方向特定的风险传播；用补丁式分词编码传播历史。针对116家焦点半导体企业做初步实验。

Result: 在两个预测时限的所有目标上误差最低；盈利性优势在提前两个季度的时限上进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 该预测可帮助供应链管理者与投资者在扰动反映到财报之前采取行动。

Abstract: Modern semiconductor production relies on a globally distributed, multi-tier supply chain in which financial stress at one firm spreads with a delay and eventually affects the revenue, inventory, and profitability of the companies that design AI chips. Most firms see only their direct partners, and prior predictive research has mainly targeted market-based risk measures, so few tools forecast how supply chain stress will appear in reported financials. In this study, we propose a heterogeneous graph patch transformer that forecasts these quarterly changes one and two quarters ahead. Learning from a 15,186-company network over 60 quarters, the proposed model fuses quarterly fundamentals with macro-trade, event, and disaster signals through learned gates, carries risk across supplier, customer, ownership, and headquarters relations through typed, direction-specific propagation, and encodes the propagated histories with patch-based tokenization. In preliminary experiments on 116 focal semiconductor firms, the proposed model achieves the lowest error on every target at both horizons, and its profitability advantage widens at the two-quarter horizon. These forecasts can help supply chain managers and investors act before disruptions appear in reported financials.

</details>


### [301] [Benchmarking EEG Foundation Models at Scale: Lessons from 20,000 Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32743)
*Zhige Chen,Shu Peng,Chengxuan Qin,Rui Liu,Rui Yang,Kay Chen Tan,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出开源基准EEG-Arena，涵盖30个EEG基础模型和25个监督基线，在23个公开数据集、57个下游任务上进行超2万次评估，系统考察了下游性能、预训练收益、模型规模扩展、数据规模扩展及通道配置鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽被寄予可迁移神经表征的期望，但其相对强监督基线的优势以及进一步扩展的前景尚不明确，缺乏统一的评估框架来回答这些问题。

Method: 构建开源基准EEG-Arena，覆盖30个EEG基础模型与25个监督基线，在23个公开数据集的57个下游任务上，通过五种实验协议开展超20,000次评估，从下游性能、预训练收益、模型参数规模、预训练数据规模、通道配置鲁棒性等维度进行系统分析。

Result: 发现：(1) EEG基础模型在多数任务上优于强任务特定监督基线，尤其在非双极导联设置下；(2) 与架构匹配的从头监督训练相比，预训练能改善早期优化和最终下游性能，且有标签数据越多收益越大越稳定；(3) 现有EEG基础模型的参数数量与下游性能之间无一致正相关；(4) 固定架构下，扩大预训练数据规模可带来持续的下游增益；(5) 通道灵活的模型在多数通道配置下绝对性能更高。

Conclusion: 这些发现证明了EEG基础模型的下游价值，并指出预训练数据扩展是未来进展的有前景方向；同时开源EEG-Arena，为可复现基准测试、模型比较和社区驱动开发提供共享基础设施。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models (FMs) promise transferable neural representations, yet their advantages over strong supervised baselines and their prospects for further scaling remain unclear. To address these questions, we introduce EEG-Arena, an open-source benchmark covering 30 EEG FMs and 25 supervised baselines evaluated on 57 downstream tasks from 23 public datasets. Through more than 20,000 evaluations across five experimental protocols, we assess downstream performance, pretraining benefits, model size scaling, pretraining data scaling, and robustness to channel configuration. We find that (1) EEG FMs outperform strong task-specific supervised baselines on most evaluated tasks, particularly under non-bipolar settings; (2) compared with architecture-matched supervised training from scratch, pretraining improves both early optimization and final downstream performance, with larger and more consistent gains as more labeled downstream data become available; (3) existing EEG FMs do not exhibit a consistent positive relationship between parameter count and downstream performance; (4) under a fixed architecture, increasing the pretraining data scale yields sustained downstream gains; and (5) channel-flexible FMs achieve higher absolute performance than channel-constrained models across most evaluated channel configurations. Together, these findings demonstrate the downstream value of EEG FMs and identify pretraining data expansion as a promising direction for further progress. To support continued research, we release EEG-Arena as an open-source evaluation framework that provides shared infrastructure for reproducible benchmarking, model comparison, and community-driven development.

</details>


### [302] [Reuse or Relearn? A Spectral View of Earth Observation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32756)
*Mehmet Ozgur Turkoglu,Valerio Marsocci,Dominik J. Mühlematter,Dominik Senti,Konrad Schindler,Helge Aasen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用谱诊断方法研究基础模型微调时是否复用了预训练表征，发现地球观测(EO)模型相比自然图像模型(CLIP、DINO)在微调中产生更大、更高秩的更新，保留的预训练结构更少，其下游性能往往以大幅改变预训练权重为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型很少被当作通用的冻结特征提取器，而是针对下游任务进行微调。这一做法在EO领域尤为普遍，但它引出一个仅靠下游精度无法回答的问题：微调究竟是在复用预训练表征，还是重新学习了一个新表征？

Method: 作者提出谱诊断方法，对比模型微调前后的状态，量化三个指标：主导奇异子空间的保留程度、权重更新的分布广度、以及更新幅度。并以CLIP、DINO等自然图像模型作为参照进行对比。

Result: 在评估的微调设置下，EO模型经历远更大、更高秩的更新，预训练结构保留得更少，下游性能常伴随着对预训练权重结构的实质性改变。诊断还揭示了低成本适配的条件：在预训练子空间被保留之处，仅调少量参数即可媲美全量微调；反之则会落后。

Conclusion: 基础模型（尤其是EO基础模型）不应仅以基准精度来评估，还应以其预训练表征的可复用性来衡量。

Abstract: Foundation models are rarely used as generic, frozen feature extractors; instead, they are fine-tuned for the target downstream application. This practice is particularly prevalent in Earth observation (EO), and it raises a question that downstream accuracy alone cannot answer: does fine-tuning reuse the pretrained representation, or does it relearn a new one? We study this with spectral diagnostics that compare a model before and after adaptation, quantifying how well its dominant singular subspaces are preserved, how broadly the weight update is distributed, and how large it is. Using natural image models such as CLIP and DINO as a reference, we find that, under the evaluated fine-tuning settings, EO models undergo far larger, higher-rank updates and retain much less of their pretrained structure, so their downstream performance is often obtained with substantial changes to the pretrained weight structure. The diagnostics further provide insight into how cheaply a model can be adapted: where the pretrained subspaces are preserved, adapting a small fraction of the parameters can match full fine-tuning, and where they are not, it can fall behind. More broadly, foundation models, and EO foundation models in particular, should be assessed not only by benchmark accuracy, but also by how reusable their pretrained representation is.

</details>


### [303] [The Extender: A Log-Structured Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32759)
*Jakob Eriksson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Extender架构——标准Transformer的日志结构化变体，通过增加拼接通道x来传递层间信息，大幅降低注意力内存占用，在短上下文任务上匹配、长上下文任务上超越标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer中，层间仅通过残差h（叠加通道）通信，导致kv投影需持久存储全部层的注意力内存（2Ld_model），内存开销随模型宽度增长。作者希望减少这一持久注意力内存占用，同时保持或提升模型性能。

Method: 在标准Transformer基础上增加拼接通道x：每层ℓ既输出残差更新δ_ℓ加到h，又输出较小的扩展ε_ℓ追加到x。FFN和q以h为输入，而注意力kv投影仅以x为输入。这样完全扩展后的x包含所有层kv投影的完整输入，将持久注意力内存从2Ld_model降至Σ|ε_ℓ|。实验设定|ε_ℓ|=32。

Result: 在|ε_ℓ|=32时，Extender在199M-924M参数下于短上下文（CORE）任务上匹配Transformer精度；在924M参数下于长上下文（RULER）任务上超越Transformer。对于1664宽、924M的模型，Extender持久注意力内存比MHA小104倍，且内存节省随模型宽度增加而增大。

Conclusion: Extender通过日志结构化的拼接通道设计，在保持甚至超越Transformer精度的同时显著降低注意力内存占用，是一种有前景的高效Transformer变体，其内存优势随模型宽度提升。

Abstract: We introduce the Extender, a log-structured variant of the standard Transformer architecture. In a standard Transformer, each layer communicates with subsequent layers exclusively via the residual $\mathbf{h}$, a superposition channel. The Extender adds a concatenation channel $\mathbf{x}$: each layer $\ell$ emits both a residual update $δ_\ell$ which is added to $\mathbf{h}$, and a much smaller extension $ε_\ell$ which is appended to $\mathbf{x}$. While both the FFN and $\mathbf{q}$ see $\mathbf{h}$, the attention $\mathbf{kv}$ projections take only $\mathbf{x}$ as input. As a result, the fully extended $\mathbf{x}$ contains the complete input for the $\mathbf{kv}$ projections of all layers, reducing the persistent attention memory footprint from $2Ld_{model}$ to $\sum|ε_\ell|$. We find that with $|ε_\ell|=32$, the Extender matches Transformer accuracy on short-context (CORE) tasks at 199M-924M parameters, and exceeds Transformer accuracy on long-context (RULER) workloads, again at 924M parameters. For our 1664-wide, 924M model, the Extender's persistent attention memory footprint is $104\times$ smaller than MHA. The memory savings grow with model width.

</details>


### [304] [AnchorMixGAN: Anchor-Aligned Generative Semi-Supervision for DDoS Detection in Cloud-Integrated IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32764)
*Jin Yang,Xufeng Liu,Yong Hu,Xueyang Wang,Honglu Yang,Gang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorMixGAN 生成式半监督框架，通过锚点对齐的目标构建缓解合成视图导致的预测漂移，在云集成 IoT 的 DDoS 检测上显著优于 MixGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云集成 IoT 网络中，标记流量稀缺使 DDoS 检测困难；生成式半监督学习虽可补充训练数据，但合成视图会引发预测漂移，影响为真实无标记流分配的目标。

Method: AnchorMixGAN 含 Anchor-MAS 模块：以每条真实无标记流为锚点，逐个字段组替换为生成流量值构造替代视图；冻结的参考分类器预测锚点及视图，经平均与锐化生成软目标；再将该流与目标同标记样本做 MixUp 进行学习。同时理论分析了参考分类器误差、视图构造与锐化对目标的影响，并推导固定检测器预测下交叉熵变化的界。

Result: 在 90% 训练设置、仅 20% 训练记录有标签时，NSLKDD、BoT-IoT、CICIoT2023 上准确率分别达 97.3%、97.4%、96.5%，较 MixGAN 分别提升 1.6、1.0、4.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 锚点对齐的目标构建有效缓解合成视图带来的预测漂移，使检测器可同时利用原始标记记录与混合样本，在低标注率下取得稳定且更优的 DDoS 检测性能。

Abstract: Detecting distributed denial-of-service (DDoS) attacks in cloud-integrated IoT networks is difficult when labeled traffic is scarce. Generative semi-supervised learning can supplement the available training data, but prediction shifts induced by synthetic views may affect the targets assigned to real unlabeled flows. We propose AnchorMixGAN, a generative semi-supervised framework that addresses this problem through anchor-aligned target construction. Its Anchor-MAS module treats each real unlabeled flow as an anchor and creates alternative views by replacing one field group at a time with values from generated traffic. A frozen reference classifier predicts the anchor and its views; averaging and sharpening these predictions produces a soft target for the original flow. The flow and its target are then mixed with a labeled example using MixUp, allowing the detector to learn from both the original labeled records and the mixed examples. We analyze how reference-classifier error, view construction, and sharpening affect the target, and derive a bound on the resulting change in cross-entropy at a fixed detector prediction. At the reported 90% training setting with 20% of the training records labeled, AnchorMixGAN attains accuracies of 97.3%, 97.4%, and 96.5% on NSLKDD, BoT-IoT, and CICIoT2023, respectively, exceeding the corresponding MixGAN results by 1.6, 1.0, and 4.4 percentage points.

</details>


### [305] [Structuring Relations Among Learning Paradigms via Protocol--Objective--Resource Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32766)
*Junwei Su,Changjie Wang,Dongyang Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协议-目标-资源（POR）框架，将学习范式的表征能力与设计选择分离，通过POR归约证明范式的复杂度支配关系，并实例化出监督、迁移、持续、元学习之间的包含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习涵盖监督、迁移、持续、元学习等多种范式，它们常复用相同的假设类、架构与优化器，但在信息获取、目标、记忆、适应和样本核算上不同。这使得判断某一范式是否真正独立、是否为另一范式的特例、或是否处于更广的结构层级中变得困难。

Method: 提出协议-目标-资源（POR）框架：范式由环境类、观测协议、可采纳学习器、性能泛函和资源核算规则确定；POR归约结合环境嵌入、学习器编译器、阈值映射与校准的资源开销。主要定理表明归约蕴含嵌入比较类上的最坏情况复杂度支配，可向前传递上界、向后传递下界；在带标签样本核算下导出样本复杂度支配。还论证校准的非平凡精度机制是避免空洞比较的必要条件，并说明在协议与学习器类不变时强化目标可严格增加极小极大样本复杂度。

Result: 在监督、迁移、持续、元学习上的实例化得到典型特例关系：持续包含迁移、迁移包含监督、元学习在对齐原始样本核算下包含监督；并导出情节式元学习的非精确情节到样本归约，刻画了重放记忆、任务标识等范式内细化。

Conclusion: 该框架为结构化学习范式、跨范式迁移复杂度保证提供了统一语言。

Abstract: Modern machine learning spans supervised, transfer, continual, meta-learning, and related regimes that often reuse the same hypothesis classes, architectures, and optimizers but differ in information access, objectives, memory, adaptation, and sample accounting. This makes it difficult to determine whether one paradigm is genuinely distinct, a special case of another, or part of a broader structural hierarchy. We introduce a protocol-objective-resource (POR) framework that separates representational capacity from these design choices. A paradigm is specified by an environment class, observation protocol, admissible learners, performance functional, and resource accounting rule. POR reductions combine environment embeddings, learner compilers, threshold maps, and calibrated resource overheads. Our main theorem shows that such reductions imply worst-case complexity domination on embedded comparison classes, transferring upper bounds forward and lower bounds backward; under labeled-example accounting, this yields sample complexity domination. We also show that calibrated nontrivial accuracy regimes are necessary to avoid vacuous comparisons, and that strengthening the objective can strictly increase minimax sample complexity even with unchanged protocols and learner classes. Instantiating the framework for supervised, transfer, continual, and meta-learning yields canonical special-case relations: continual contains transfer, transfer contains supervised, and meta-learning contains supervised under aligned raw-example accounting. We further derive a non-exact episode-to-example reduction for episodic meta-learning and capture within-paradigm refinements such as replay memory and task identifiers. The framework thus provides a unified language for structuring learning paradigms and transferring complexity guarantees across them.

</details>


### [306] [Continual Learning via Self-Probe Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32771)
*Dongkyu Cho,Rumi Chunara,Sungmin Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CPLUS 方法，利用冻结模型对保留样本进行自探测生成新输入并记录预测，将这些自探测结果作为梯度信号约束参数更新，从而在少量旧样本条件下缓解语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型适配新数据时会引发对已有行为的灾难性遗忘；当旧样本很少时，持续学习方法获得的可保留证据稀疏而狭窄，难以有效防止遗忘。

Method: CPLUS 通过自探测（self-probing）让冻结的语言模型从保留样本生成新输入，并记录模型自身的预测结果；与先前把这类数据当作回放训练样本不同，CPLUS 利用自探测结果和旧样本梯度来缩小与先前行为相冲突的参数更新。

Result: 在四种基准、五个语言模型上的实验显示：相同探测作为梯度信号比作为回放数据能保留更多旧行为；CPLUS 在学习新数据的同时比现有基线持续减少遗忘，在旧数据稀缺时尤为明显，且对未参与训练的基准也有保护作用；在 Qwen3 模型家族中，模型规模越大 CPLUS 越有效，能恢复更大比例的由标准微调造成的遗忘。

Conclusion: 将自探测用作梯度约束信号而非回放数据，是一种在旧样本稀缺场景下更有效的持续学习防遗忘策略，且其效果随模型规模增大而提升。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models to new data can cause catastrophic forgetting of previously learned behavior. When only a few past samples remain, they give continual learning methods sparse and narrow evidence about what to preserve. We show that language models can expand this evidence through self-probing, in which the frozen model generates new inputs from the retained samples and records its own predictions on them. Unlike prior work that replays such data as training examples, our method, CPLUS uses self-probe and past-sample gradients to scale down parameter updates that conflict with prior behavior. Experiments with five language models on four benchmarks show three results. First, the same probes preserve more prior behavior as gradient signals than as replay data. Second, CPLUS learns the new data while consistently reducing forgetting more than existing baselines, especially when past data are scarce, and this protection extends to benchmarks not used for training. Third, we observe that CPLUS also becomes more effective as models grow: within the Qwen3 model family, it recovers an increasing share of the forgetting caused by standard fine-tuning.

</details>


### [307] [Beyond Gaussian Assumptions: Distribution-Aware Channel Capacity for Effective Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32774)
*Jianan Jian,Jacob Kang,Nurahmed Multezem,Benjamin Li,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于分布感知与信息论的效应连接估计方法，用归一化流构建双流极小极大估计器，在非高斯残差下估计通道容量，并在仿真与多模态脑信号上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号效应连接估计通常假设残差服从高斯分布，这一假设虽便于计算，但当经验残差非高斯时会丢弃有信息的分布结构，并扭曲推断出的定向交互。作者认为需要在多种模态、物种和实验条件下重新审视高斯假设的合理性。

Method: 作者首先证明脑信号及拟合通道残差普遍偏离高斯性，随后提出一种分布感知、基于信息论的效应连接度量，即一般残差分布下的通道容量。为从经验残差中估计该容量，他们设计了基于归一化流的双流极小极大估计器：生成器在功率约束下搜索可行输入分布，观察者估计输出熵。理论上分析了观察者目标可恢复微分熵（至多差一个KL近似项），并说明该形式在高斯情形下退化为经典高斯容量。

Result: 理论分析给出了估计器的刻画，包括残差熵可在方差之外改变可达信息率，并通过博弈间隙与误差分析刻画优化与近似来源。在已知定向连接的大脑仿真中，涵盖多种网络拓扑、隐驱动、反馈与异质血流动力学的十种条件下，Dual-flow在AUROC和AUPRC上均最高，优于高斯容量、格兰杰因果、VAR-LiNGAM和GIMME。应用于多模态脑信号时，揭示了与已知神经环路一致的时间与条件分辨的定向交互。

Conclusion: 研究建立了超越高斯残差建模的、有原则的分布感知效应连接估计框架。

Abstract: Effective-connectivity estimation from brain signals often relies on Gaussian residual modeling, which enables tractable estimation but can discard informative distributional structure and distort inferred directed interactions when empirical residuals are non-Gaussian. We show across multiple modalities, species, and experimental conditions that both brain signals and fitted channel residuals frequently deviate from Gaussianity. We therefore introduce a distribution-aware, information-theoretic measure of effective connectivity based on channel capacity under general residual distributions. To estimate the resulting capacity from empirical, potentially non-Gaussian residuals, we develop a dual-flow min-max estimator based on normalizing flows, in which a generator searches over admissible input distributions under a power constraint while an observer estimates output entropy.
