We provide a theoretical characterization of the estimator, showing that the observer objective recovers differential entropy up to a KL approximation term, that the formulation reduces to classical Gaussian capacity as a special case, and that residual entropy can alter achievable information rates beyond variance; game-gap and error analyses further characterize optimization and approximation sources. In brain-like simulations with known directed connectivity, Dual-flow achieves the highest AUROC and AUPRC across ten conditions spanning diverse network topologies, hidden drivers, feedback, and heterogeneous hemodynamics, compared with Gaussian capacity, Granger causality, VAR-LiNGAM, and GIMME. Applied to multimodal brain signals, the method reveals time- and condition-resolved directed interactions consistent with known neurobiological circuitry. Together, these results establish a principled distribution-aware framework for effective-connectivity estimation beyond Gaussian residual modeling.

</details>


### [308] [Robust Bayesian Optimization with Q-Exponential Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32775)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Zhidi Lin,Feng Yin,Abdelhak M. Zoubir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出q-ED-BO，用q指数分布替代高斯代理，实现对重尾与离群噪声稳健的贝叶斯优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化依赖高斯过程代理，其高斯假设对离群值和重尾噪声敏感，在受污染数据下性能下降。作者希望在不损失闭式可解性的前提下，增强BO对重尾噪声的稳健性。

Method: 构建代理为单变量q指数分布（q-ED），保留GP-BO的闭式后验均值与方差；引入形状参数q控制尾部：q=2退化为GP，q越小尾部越重、置信界越宽。基于此推导闭式q-UCB（具次线性遗憾）与精确闭式q-EI（q=2时恢复经典EI）。

Result: 在波束形成器与自适应滤波器调参（含脉冲离群）实验中，q-ED-BO在干净数据上达到或超过现有基线；在污染数据下，相比最佳基线在输出SINR上提升约0.7 dB，在失配减少上提升约1.1至1.2 dB。

Conclusion: q-ED-BO通过q指数代理在保持闭式采集函数与理论保证的同时，显著提升了对重尾与离群噪声的稳健性，且不牺牲干净场景下的性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used framework for optimizing expensive black-box objectives, but standard BO methods often use Gaussian process (GP) surrogates whose Gaussian assumption is sensitive to outliers and heavy-tailed noise. We introduce q-ED-BO, a robust BO method whose surrogate follows a univariate q-exponential (q-ED) distribution, preserving GP-BO's closed-form posterior mean and variance while a shape parameter q controls the tail behavior, recovering the GP at q = 2 and growing heavier-tailed with wider confidence bounds as q decreases. This tractability yields a closed-form q-upper confidence bound (q-UCB) with sublinear regret, and an exact closed-form q-expected improvement (q-EI) that generalizes EI to the heavy-tailed predictive, recovering classical EI at q = 2. Experiments on beamformer and adaptive filter tuning with impulsive outliers show that q-ED-BO matches or exceeds existing baselines on clean data, and under corruption, improves the strongest baseline by approximately 0.7 dB in output SINR and 1.1 to 1.2 dB in misalignment reduction.

</details>


### [309] [Learning When to Recur: Token-Adaptive Recursion for Imbalanced Ophthalmic Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32785)
*Nanxi Yu,Kang Li,Ye Du,Xiaowei Hu,Weihua Yang,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ToRe框架，利用冻结的眼科基础模型，通过参数隔离与token自适应递归，解决域增量学习中的标签分布偏移问题，在九个数据集上性能领先且近乎零遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域增量学习方法主要应对风格变化引起的域偏移，但忽略了真实临床场景（如转诊系统）中严重的类别不平衡。机构间类别先验剧烈波动导致标签分布偏移，这是域偏移的关键形式，会引发严重的灾难性遗忘。

Method: ToRe是一种免回放、参数高效的框架，基于冻结的眼科基础模型。它采用参数隔离策略解耦域特定的优化路径，以缓解标签分布偏移和风格变化带来的灾难性遗忘；同时引入token自适应递归，按token自适应地分配额外计算深度，简单token提前退出递归循环，复杂token（如病灶相关）进行更深递归处理，从而增强少数类的特征表示。

Result: 在九个异构数据集上的大量评估表明，ToRe在三个基准的整体性能上持续优于最先进方法，并保持近乎零遗忘。

Conclusion: 结果支持ToRe在动态且不平衡的临床环境中的适用性。代码已在 https://github.com/Nancyolo/ToRe 开源。

Abstract: Domain incremental learning is essential for adapting ophthalmic deep learning models to sequential clinical domains while preserving diagnostic expertise. Existing domain incremental learning methods predominantly address the domain shift induced by style variations. However, they often overlook the severe class imbalance inherent in real-world clinical scenarios, such as clinical referral systems. Institutions in these systems encounter drastic fluctuations in class priors, resulting in label distribution shift, a critical form of domain shift that triggers severe catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose ToRe, a rehearsal-free and parameter-efficient framework that leverages frozen ophthalmic foundation models for robust incremental adaptation. ToRe employs a parameter isolation strategy to decouple domain-specific optimization paths, thereby helping mitigate catastrophic forgetting driven by both label distribution shift and style variations. Simultaneously, it introduces token-adaptive recursion that adaptively allocates additional computational depth across tokens, allowing simple tokens to exit the recursion loop early while subjecting complex tokens, such as those associated with lesions, to deeper recursive processing. This mechanism enhances the feature representations for minority classes, thereby supporting generalization throughout the domain incremental learning process. Extensive evaluations on nine heterogeneous datasets demonstrate that ToRe consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall performance across the three benchmarks, while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Together, these results support the applicability of ToRe to dynamic and imbalanced clinical environments. The code is available at https://github.com/Nancyolo/ToRe

</details>


### [310] [FedHV: Low-Overhead Hypervolume Weighting for Federated Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32790)
*Amirardalan Dehghanpour,Seyed Mohammad Azimi-Abarghouyi,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedHV 通过将参考点相对的目标松弛量映射为闭式逆松弛权重，实现高效的任务级联邦多目标优化，通信量降至 Θ(d+m) 并具备收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级联邦多目标优化方法通常依赖梯度或更新几何来推导任务权重，这需要任务特定信息或服务器端迭代优化，带来较高的通信与计算开销。因此需要一种无需任务特定信息、通信高效且可理论分析的方法。

Method: 提出 FedHV：将参考点相对的目标松弛量映射为闭式逆松弛权重；每个客户端只优化一个加权损失，并在返回模型更新时附带目标估计值。协议每个参与客户端仅增加 2m 个辅助标量，总通信量为 Θ(d+m)，且无需额外同步阶段。作者在客户端异构、多步本地更新、部分参与和有限样本目标报告下分析了由此产生的一轮延迟权重。

Result: 在固定时域正松弛参考条件下，配合时域相关步长与消失的报告误差，FedHV 对平均对数超体积梯度范数平方达到 O(T^(-1/2)) 收敛率，持续报告误差决定平稳性邻域；同一界也控制平方 Pareto 平稳性残差。在四个视觉基准族、六个 Dirichlet 非 IID 划分与三个训练种子上，FedHV 在五个设置中平均精度超过 FSMGDA 与 FedCMOO；在其中四个设置中取得最高最差任务精度，并在两个 CIFAR10-MNIST 设置中改善了困难的 CIFAR-10 目标。

Conclusion: FedHV 以闭式逆松弛权重替代梯度几何推导，在显著降低通信成本的同时保持了收敛性保证与竞争性甚至更优的实验性能，为通信受限的联邦多目标优化提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Task-wise federated multi-objective optimization (FedMOO) trains a shared model for competing prediction objectives under heterogeneous data, partial participation, and communication constraints. Existing methods commonly derive task weights from gradient or update geometry. This requires task-specific information or iterative server-side optimization. We introduce FedHV, which maps reference-relative objective slacks to closed-form inverse-slack weights. Each client optimizes one weighted loss and returns objective estimates with its model update. The protocol adds exactly 2m auxiliary scalars per participating client, yielding Theta(d + m) total per-client communication, compared with the Theta(md) task-specific communication of FSMGDA, and requires no additional synchronization stage. We analyze the resulting one-round-delayed weights under client heterogeneity, multi-step local updates, partial participation, and finite-sample objective reports. Under a fixed-horizon positive-slack reference condition, with the prescribed horizon-dependent step size and vanishing report error, FedHV achieves an O(T^(-1/2)) rate for the average squared log-hypervolume gradient norm; persistent report error determines the resulting stationarity neighborhood. The same bound controls the squared Pareto-stationarity residual. Across six Dirichlet-partitioned non-IID settings from four vision benchmark families and three training seeds, FedHV exceeds FSMGDA and FedCMOO in mean accuracy in five settings. Among these methods and uniform scalarization, it achieves the highest worst-task accuracy in four settings and improves the difficult CIFAR-10 objective in both CIFAR10-MNIST settings.

</details>


### [311] [Understanding and Exploiting Anisotropy in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32792)
*Samyak Jha,Harshvardhan Saini,Yizhen Liao,Yiming Tang,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视LLM残差通道各向异性现象，发现约5%高激活通道构成语言建模的"连贯性基础"，SFT会重塑它们而RL基本不改动、转而适配其余通道；据此提出SphereGate，通过逐通道有界增益的激活加权梯度限制高能通道移动，仅0.1M参数即在多个模型上超越参数高效基线并与全模型GRPO相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练结合了SFT（前向KL、众数覆盖）与RL（反向KL、众数搜索），而频率加权似然训练会留下"各向异性"痕迹——少数残差通道承载异常大的激活。该现象被广泛记录但通常被视为缺陷，其功能及与后训练的相互作用尚不清晰，论文试图弄清这一点并加以利用。

Method: 首先用无标签的离群规则识别约5%的残差通道，通过消融验证其对语言建模的关键性，并比较SFT与RL对这些通道及剩余通道的影响。基于分析提出SphereGate：在冻结主干上为每个残差通道学习一个有界增益，其激活加权梯度可证明地限制高能连贯性通道的移动，同时释放其余通道进行适配，仅需0.1M可训练参数。

Result: 移除约5%离群通道使困惑度从10升至超10^6，而等量随机通道仅升至35，但离群通道几乎无法区分推理对错；SFT重塑这些通道，RL则基本保留它们并适配互补通道。SphereGate在Qwen2.5（0.5B–7B）和Llama-3-8B上于MATH-500超越参数高效基线2.0–7.3分，与全模型GRPO相当或更优。

Conclusion: 各向异性并非缺陷，而是一种分工：少数高能残差通道构成模型的连贯性基础，后训练（尤其RL）可在其他通道上进行推理适配。SphereGate证明了这一机制可被有效利用，以极小参数量实现高效后训练。

Abstract: LLM post-training combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT), a mode-covering forward-KL objective, with reinforcement learning (RL), a mode-seeking reverse-KL objective. Frequency-weighted likelihood training leaves a well-known signature: \emph{anisotropy}, in which a few residual channels carry disproportionately large activations. Anisotropy is widely documented and usually treated as a defect, yet its function and its interaction with post-training remain unclear. We first analyze it. A label-free outlier rule isolates about 5\% of residual channels that are essential for language modeling: removing them raises perplexity from 10 to over $10^6$, versus 35 for count-matched random channels. Yet they barely distinguish correct from incorrect reasoning. SFT reshapes them, whereas RL leaves them largely intact and adapts the complementary channels. These channels therefore form the model's \emph{coherence substrate}, and reasoning adaptation happens elsewhere. We then exploit this. \textsc{SphereGate} learns one bounded gain per residual channel on a frozen backbone. Its activation-weighted gradients provably limit movement of high-energy coherence channels and leave the remaining channels free. With 0.1M trainable parameters, \textsc{SphereGate} outperforms parameter-efficient baselines by 2.0--7.3 points on MATH-500 across Qwen2.5 (0.5B--7B) and Llama-3-8B, is comparable or exceeds full-model GRPO. Anisotropy is not a defect but a division of labor that post-training can exploit.

</details>


### [312] [Derivative-Informed Training of Neural Operators On-the-Fly via Sketched Tangent Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32797)
*Xinhan Yang,Lu Lu,Shancong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出sTCL，一种在线导数信息训练目标，用随机草图扰动直接从控制方程施加物理约束，无需离线切线标签，在多种PDE上达到与离线DIFNO相当的解与雅可比精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为可微代理用于反问题、PDE约束优化、设计与控制时，输入-输出敏感性（导数）的精度至关重要。现有导数信息训练依赖离线生成的导数标签，导致数据生成慢、存储开销大，且难以跨数据集、分辨率或扰动基适配。

Method: 提出草图切线一致性损失（sTCL）：对具有已知可微残差的PDE，利用随机草图化的输入扰动在线构造轻量级导数级物理约束，直接从控制方程强制敏感性一致性，无需离线切线标签，也不改变神经算子架构。针对刚性、病态、非定常或耦合鞍点切线算子下原始前向敏感性残差惩罚失效的问题，引入由简单切线算子判定规则选择的轻量级算子感知损失条件化机制，形成PDE特定的sTCL损失。

Result: 在Helmholtz、非线性扩散-反应、Burgers、Allen-Cahn和Navier-Stokes方程上，所有方法使用相同神经算子骨干，PDE特定的sTCL损失在解与雅可比精度上与离线导数信息训练（DIFNO）相当，同时消除了离线导数数据的生成与存储流程。

Conclusion: 在线导数信息训练不必仅将离线切线求解成本摊销进训练；通过适当的草图化与损失设计，sTCL为导数信息神经算子提供了一条有效的即插即用路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Derivative-informed training improves neural operators by directly supervising their input-output sensitivities, which is crucial when neural operators are used as differentiable surrogates for inverse problems, PDE-constrained optimization, design, and control. However, existing methods rely on offline-generated derivative labels, making data generation slow, storage-intensive, and difficult to adapt across datasets, resolutions, or perturbation bases. We propose sketched tangent consistency loss (sTCL), an on-the-fly derivative-informed training objective that, for PDEs with a known and differentiable residual, enforces sensitivity consistency directly from the governing equation without offline tangent labels or neural-operator architecture changes. sTCL uses randomly sketched input perturbations to provide a lightweight derivative-level physics constraint during training. However, raw forward-sensitivity residual penalties can fail for stiff, ill-conditioned, indefinite, or coupled saddle-point tangent operators. To address this, we introduce lightweight operator-aware loss-conditioning mechanisms selected by a simple tangent-operator decision rule. Across Helmholtz, nonlinear diffusion-reaction, Burgers, Allen-Cahn, and Navier-Stokes, with the same neural-operator backbone for all methods, the PDE-specific sTCL losses achieve solution and Jacobian accuracy comparable to offline derivative-informed training (DIFNO) while eliminating the offline derivative-data generation and storage pipeline. These results show that on-the-fly derivative-informed training need not merely amortize offline tangent-solve cost into training; with appropriate sketching and loss design, sTCL provides an effective drop-in path to derivative-informed neural operators. Code is available at https://github.com/yang9579/Derivative-informed-traning-on-the-fly.

</details>


### [313] [Mind the Spike: Mechanisms and Brittleness of Visual Massive Activations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32808)
*Jonas Ngnawé,Yann Pequignot,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Christian Gagné,Frédéric Precioso,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究LVLM中的视觉激活尖峰：触发方向源自模型权重，尖峰位置由可解释规则决定，且视觉尖峰对图像扰动极度脆弱，可被定向攻击诱发/移除，也可通过预防性干预消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM继承了文本基座的"大规模激活"（少量固定隐藏通道出现远超常规量级的数值）。文本尖峰位置固定且与输入无关，但视觉尖峰是否在各LVLM中形成一致模式、如何响应图像扰动尚不清楚，需要系统性探究。

Method: 覆盖25个adapter-based LVLM（基于10个家族、18个文本基座，2B–72B）。从模型权重识别触发方向，并提出可解释的位置规则：语言模型解码器运行前，最终尖峰token主要限于与图像其余部分共享信息最少的token。据此设计触发引导的尖峰攻击（小ℓ∞预算下故意创建或移除尖峰）及预防性干预（尖峰爆发前移除触发分量）。

Result: 部分LVLM不形成视觉尖峰，其余尖峰发生率不同且多出现在较深层。常见图像损坏频繁创建并迁移尖峰、较少移除尖峰，整体提高尖峰发生率。触发引导攻击在1/255的ℓ∞预算下即可对10个尖峰模型中的9个奏效。预防性干预能在干净和扰动图像上消除或大幅减少尖峰，同时几乎不改变其他图像token。

Conclusion: 视觉尖峰的形成与位置具有可解释且跨模型部分一致的规律，且高度脆弱；基于触发方向的攻击与预防性干预均有效，为理解与调控LVLM内部激活提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) inherit massive activations from their text-only bases: spikes where a few fixed hidden channels receive values thousands of times above the typical magnitude. The text spike systematically appears in early layers at a fixed initial position, independently of input content. Visual spikes vary across images, but whether their formation follows a consistent pattern across LVLMs and how they respond to image perturbations remain open questions. We find that some LVLMs do not form visual spikes, while others spike at different rates, typically in deeper layers. We identify the trigger direction from model weights and an interpretable location rule: before the language model decoder runs, eventual spike tokens are largely restricted to those sharing least with the rest of the image. Crucially, visual spikes are strikingly brittle. Common corruptions frequently create and relocate spikes, and less often remove them, raising overall incidence. Our trigger-guided spike attack deliberately creates or removes spikes under a small $\ell_\infty$ budget, with 1/255 enough in nine of the ten models that spike. Finally, our preventive intervention removes only the trigger component before spikes erupt, eliminating or substantially reducing spikes on clean and perturbed images while leaving the other image tokens nearly unchanged. Our study spans 25 adapter-based LVLMs built on 18 released text-only bases from 10 families, ranging from 2B to 72B parameters.

</details>


### [314] [Change the Product, Keep the Parameters: Associative Algebra Layers for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32814)
*Ilya Koziev,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探索用更廉价的关联代数乘积替代Transformer中的普通矩阵乘法，构造出矩阵维度上二次算术复杂度的方案，并在110M参数模型上验证了可行性与吞吐提升，但下游指标略降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速矩阵乘法算法固定乘积、寻找更廉价求值方式；本文反过来提问：能否让Transformer学习到的投影使用一种不同且更廉价的乘积。

Method: 基于一种关联代数构造，用同一权重分块上更稀疏的交互表替代普通矩阵乘法；在物理块大小固定时构造出矩阵维度上二次算术复杂度的一族方案，并推导GPU执行的有限形状约束；该构造按其双线性秩由Alder–Strassen界可证最优，并可实现为兼容因果掩码和KV缓存解码的行类型矩形投影。用相同配方和12.3B token预算训练两个约110M参数的仅解码器Transformer LM，仅前馈层不同：一个用普通稠密矩阵乘法，另一个用关联代数乘积。

Result: 在四个提示域中，代数模型端到端生成吞吐提升6.2–7.8%，但三项报告的下游指标得分均较低。

Conclusion: 作者将该结果视为所提方法在小规模下的可行性与可训练性检验，后续研究留待未来工作。

Abstract: Fast matrix multiplication algorithms keep the product fixed and search for a cheaper way to evaluate it. We instead ask whether a Transformer's learned projections can use a different, cheaper product altogether. Building on an associative-algebra construction that replaces ordinary matrix multiplication with a sparser interaction table over the same weight blocks, we construct a family with quadratic arithmetic in the matrix dimension when the physical block size remains fixed, and derive finite-shape constraints for GPU execution. The construction is provably optimal for its bilinear rank by the Alder--Strassen bound and can be realized as row-typed rectangular projections compatible with causal masking and KV-cached decoding. We provide an empirical test of this approach by training two approximately 110M-parameter decoder-only Transformer LMs from the same recipe and 12.3B-token budget, differing only in their feed-forward layer: one uses ordinary dense matrix multiplication and the other uses the associative-algebra product. Across four prompt domains, the algebraic model achieves a 6.2--7.8\% increase in end-to-end generation throughput, while obtaining lower scores on all three reported downstream metrics. We treat these results as a feasibility and trainability check for the proposed approach at small scale, leaving further investigation to future work.

</details>


### [315] [Retimed Bellman Flows: Escaping the Impossible Triangle of Velocity Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32828)
*Boyang Xu,Shengzhe Chen,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Retimed Bellman Flows (ReBF)，通过动态调整教师查询的流时间并结合解耦噪声，构造条件无偏的速度目标，在理论上保持Bellman不动点并进行Wasserstein收缩，实验中显著优于现有flow critic方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有flow critic通过连续速度场将高斯噪声传输到Bellman端点来学习回报分布。速度自举（velocity bootstrapping）虽可通过查询后继教师稳定训练，但存在结构性困境：在直线路径上，不存在无可残留的、同时保留高斯初始噪声且维持无偏目标的同时间长仿射映射。

Method: 提出ReBF：在动态偏移的较早流时间查询教师critic，对齐学生与教师的中间轨迹；结合重定时时钟（retimed clock）与全新的解耦噪声生成，构造可证明条件无偏的速度目标，保持Bellman不动点，并在Wasserstein距离下收缩。

Result: 在合成MRP上，ReBF将到真实回报分布的W1距离最多降低7.7倍；在38个具有挑战性的OGBench和D4RL离线强化学习任务上，优于现有flow critic方法。

Conclusion: ReBF通过重定时教师查询与解耦噪声解决了flow critic的结构性困境，在理论与实验上均验证了其有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Flow critics learn return distributions by transporting Gaussian noise to Bellman endpoints via continuous velocity fields. While velocity bootstrapping stabilizes training by querying a successor teacher, existing methods face a structural dilemma: on straight paths, no residual-free same-time affine mapping can preserve Gaussian initial noise while maintaining an unbiased target. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Retimed Bellman Flows (ReBF). ReBF queries the teacher critic at a dynamically shifted earlier flow time, aligning intermediate student and teacher trajectories. By combining this retimed clock with fresh, decoupled noise generation, ReBF constructs a provably conditionally unbiased velocity target that preserves the Bellman fixed point and contracts under Wasserstein distances. Empirically, ReBF reduces $W_1$ distance to ground-truth return distributions by up to $7.7\times$ on synthetic MRPs and outperforms existing flow critics across 38 challenging OGBench and D4RL offline reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [316] [UniCache: Task- and Type-Aware KV Cache Compression for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32831)
*Wanqi Yang,Yuexiao Ma,Mei Xie,Xiawu Zheng,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UniCache，一个免训练的任务感知与类型感知 KV cache 压缩框架，针对统一多模态模型中多任务、多类型 KV cache 的重要性差异进行协调压缩，实现显著压缩与加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型在单一网络中融合理解、生成与编辑任务，但多模态上下文增长导致 KV cache 的存储与访问成本急剧上升。现有 KV cache 压缩方法多针对特定任务和单模态缓存，忽略了缓存重要性随任务和时间步的变化。而在统一多模态模型中，每个任务涉及多种 KV cache 类型，其组成和动态因任务而异，单一压缩策略会丢失关键信息并降低各任务质量。

Method: UniCache 是一个免训练框架，面向任务感知与类型感知的 KV cache 压缩。它通过离线校准识别每个任务激活的缓存片段，并为其分配适合的压缩策略；在共享存储预算下，通过注意力引导的分配和任务感知的时间调度，协调不同策略的并行执行。

Result: 实验表明，UniCache 在理解与编辑任务上实现 5 倍 KV cache 压缩，在生成任务上实现 2.5 倍压缩，且质量损失可忽略；在长上下文场景中吞吐量最高提升 1.78 倍，显著提升了统一多模态模型向更长上下文扩展的实用性。

Conclusion: 针对统一多模态模型中 KV cache 的任务与类型依赖性，UniCache 通过离线校准、注意力引导分配与时间调度，在几乎不损失质量的前提下实现高效压缩与加速，为长上下文多模态应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models combine understanding, generation, and editing within a single network, offering a promising foundation for versatile multimodal applications. However, growing multimodal contexts make KV cache storage and access increasingly costly. Existing KV cache compression methods are typically tailored to specific tasks and single-modality caches, while overlooking changes in cache importance across tasks and timesteps. However, in unified multimodal models, each task involves multiple KV cache types, and both their composition and dynamics differ across tasks. As a result, a single compression policy overlooks task- and type-specific requirements, leading to the loss of critical information and degraded quality across tasks. Based on these findings, we propose UniCache, a training-free framework for task- and type-aware KV cache compression. UniCache identifies the cache segments activated by each task and assigns suitable compression policies through offline calibration. It coordinates their parallel execution under a shared storage budget through attention-guided allocation and task-aware temporal scheduling. Experiments show that UniCache achieves $5\times$ KV cache compression for understanding and editing and $2.5\times$ for generation with negligible quality loss, while increasing throughput by up to $1.78\times$ in long-context settings, significantly improving the practicality of scaling unified multimodal models to longer context.

</details>


### [317] [Permutation-Equivariant Flow Matching for Alignment-Free Neural Weight Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32833)
*Arkadi Piven,Yam Eitan,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Daniel Cremers,Thomas Dagès,Ron Kimmel,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种置换等变的图元网络参数化流匹配速度场，可直接从独立训练的神经网络中学习分布，无需神经元对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维参数空间中学习已训练网络的分布可生成跨任务与架构的新模型，但存在置换对称性难题：交换隐藏神经元会得到代表同一函数却相距甚远的参数向量，给生成建模带来额外变化。现有方法多依赖共同基模型或昂贵的近似神经元对齐，限制了从独立训练网络直接学习的能力。

Method: 用置换等变的图元网络（Graph Meta Network）参数化流匹配（flow-matching）速度场，从而绕开对齐步骤，直接对独立训练得到的网络集合建模。

Result: 方法能很好地复现独立训练集合在准确率、函数相似度和权重相似度上的联合统计，表明生成超越了对检查点的记忆；单一条件模型可在异构架构上生成任务特定网络，并泛化到未见过的隐藏宽度配置；在表格域偏移任务中，中间条件可生成性能媲美跨域logit集成（logit ensemble）的个体网络。

Conclusion: 结果说明置换等变性使模型能够在无需置换对齐的情况下，从多样化的独立训练网络集合中学习其分布。

Abstract: A trained neural network can be represented by a parameter vector in high dimensions. Learning distributions over these vectors enables the generation of new models across various tasks and architectures. A central challenge is permutation symmetry: permuting hidden neurons can produce distant parameter vectors representing the same function. This introduces variations that a generative model must account for when learning from trained networks. Existing methods typically address this using networks derived from a common base model or costly approximate neuron alignment. We instead parameterize a flow-matching velocity field with a permutation-equivariant Graph Meta Network, enabling direct learning from independently trained networks without alignment. Extensive experiments show that our method closely reproduces the joint statistics of accuracy, functional similarity, and weight similarity of independently trained collections, providing evidence of generation beyond checkpoint memorization. A single conditional model also generates task-specific networks on heterogeneous architectures and generalizes to unseen hidden-width configurations. On a tabular domain-shift task, intermediate conditioning produces individual networks with performance comparable to logit ensembles across both domains. Taken together, our results show how permutation equivariance enables learning from diverse collections of independently trained networks without permutation alignment.

</details>


### [318] [HamiFormer: Dual-Expert Diffusion Fields with Affine Symplectic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32838)
*Haoxiang Huang,Xiang Liu,Shuwei Wang,Jingheng Ma,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HamiFormer 是一种双专家扩散场模型，结合全窗口去噪与残差校正的哈密顿传播，配合并行局部仿射扫描（PLAS）与 Regime Model Tree，在长时程动力学与碰撞预测中显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测平滑动力学与碰撞需要同时建模连续演化与突发的状态跃变。现有方法（如 PhysiFormer、DiT）在长时程自回归预测中会累积误差，难以兼顾典型状态精度与大尾部误差，因此需要一种能抑制自回归误差传播、同时保持物理一致性的建模框架。

Method: 提出 HamiFormer，一种双专家扩散场，将全窗口去噪与残差校正的哈密顿传播相结合，通过混合状态反馈削弱继承自回归误差的直接贡献，并让混合状态在联合精化窗口内引导后续传播。引入并行局部仿射扫描（PLAS），在整流流步间摊销迭代精化，并在物理时间上并行求导，其仿射辛映射相比顺序显式欧拉法具有更低求解误差与运行时间。另采用 Regime Model Tree 专门化残差与路由，以平衡典型状态精度与大尾部误差。理论上给出物理一致精化的条件、热启动跟踪及扩散反馈下的有限窗口误差界。

Result: 在 192 步评估中，HamiFormer 相比 PhysiFormer 在 HamiBalls-1 上将归一化相空间 MSE 降低 26.3%，相比 DiT 在 HamiBalls-2 上降低 21.4%，模型容量相当。不相交区间对比显示，在两个数据集上均取得最低的后期时域位置与动量误差。

Conclusion: HamiFormer 通过扩散场与哈密顿传播的混合设计及高效并行扫描，有效缓解长时程自回归误差，在动力学与碰撞预测的精度和效率上优于现有基线，并具备理论一致性保障。

Abstract: Predicting smooth dynamics and collisions requires modeling continuous evolution and abrupt state changes. We introduce HamiFormer, a dual-expert diffusion field combining whole-window denoising with residual-corrected Hamiltonian propagation. Their mixed-state feedback attenuates the direct contribution of inherited autoregressive error: each mixed state guides subsequent propagation within the jointly refined window. Parallel Local Affine Scan (PLAS) amortizes iterative refinement across rectified-flow steps and evaluates derivatives in parallel across physical time. PLAS's affine symplectic maps achieve lower solver error and runtime than sequential explicit Euler in our evaluation. A Regime Model Tree specializes residuals and routing to balance typical-state accuracy against large tail errors. Our analysis gives conditions for physically consistent refinement and warm-start tracking, and finite-window error bounds under diffusion feedback. In 192-step evaluations, HamiFormer reduces normalized phase-space MSE by 26.3% against PhysiFormer on HamiBalls-1 and 21.4% against DiT on HamiBalls-2, with comparable model capacities. Disjoint-interval comparisons show the lowest late-horizon position and momentum errors among baselines on both datasets. Project page: https://hamiformer.github.io/.

</details>


### [319] [Transfer Learning for Edge Classification on Dynamic Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32849)
*Tyler Bonnet,Marek Rei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对动态文本属性图（DyTAGs）提出跨域迁移的边缘分类协议LODO，发现现有自监督方法在域外迁移时表现差，甚至不如忽略结构与时间的BoE基线；据此提出STSA模型，通过时空编码器融合时间差与节点出现频率，并以对比语义预测目标对齐多域文本潜空间，性能超越BoE及所有对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态文本属性图的表示学习需要捕捉跨域持续存在的交互动态，但现有方法易过拟合于特定域的结构、时间与语义模式，导致分布偏移下边缘分类性能下降。作者旨在揭示并解决这一迁移泛化问题。

Method: 1) 形式化建立"留一域"（leave-one-domain-out, LODO）的DyTAG边缘分类迁移学习协议；2) 提出仅使用无序节点与边文本特征序列的Bag of Events（BoE）基线；3) 在BoE基础上提出Spatio-Temporal Semantic Alignment（STSA）：使用时空编码器将时间差与节点出现频率的表示融合到统一流形，并采用对比语义预测（Contrastive Semantic Forecasting）目标，将边缘表示锚定到由预训练语言模型初始化的多域文本潜空间。

Result: 在LODO协议下，现有动态图自监督SOTA方法迁移到未见域时表现不佳，且均不如结构与时序无关的BoE模型；STSA进一步超越BoE以及所有被评估的现有方法。

Conclusion: 动态文本属性图的跨域迁移需要避免过拟合域特定模式。STSA通过时空语义对齐与对比语义预测引入鲁棒的文本先验，在跨域边缘分类中取得更优性能，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Learning transferable representations for dynamic text-attributed graphs (DyTAGs) requires models to capture underlying interaction dynamics that persist across domains. However, existing methods tend to overfit to domain-specific structural, temporal, and semantic patterns, limiting edge classification performance under distribution shifts. To expose and address this, we formally establish a leave-one-domain-out (LODO) transfer learning protocol for edge classification on DyTAGs. Under this protocol, we demonstrate that state-of-the-art self-supervised methods for dynamic graph learning perform poorly when transferred to unseen domains. Strikingly, existing methods underperform a structurally and temporally unaware Bag of Events (BoE) model we introduce, which inputs only unordered sequences of node and edge text features. Proceeding from the BoE, we propose Spatio-Temporal Semantic Alignment (STSA), which integrates a spatio-temporal encoder that fuses representations of time deltas and node occurrence frequencies into a unified manifold. STSA is trained with a Contrastive Semantic Forecasting objective, which anchors edge representations to a multi-domain textual latent space initialized by a pretrained language model, providing a robust prior that outperforms BoE and all existing methods we evaluate.

</details>


### [320] [Logic Gate Networks and Lookup Table Networks as Lightweight Hardware Classifiers for Inter-patient ECG Arrhythmia Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32854)
*Wout Mommen,Lars Keuninckx,Siddharth Patil,Paul Detterer,Achiel Colpaert,Piet Wambacq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将逻辑门网络(LGN)推广到多输入查找表网络(LUTN)，并应用于心电心律失常分类，取得高精度且极低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有CNN、SVM、SNN方法推理功耗和计算量大的问题，探索基于简单二值逻辑运算的低功耗网络在患者间心电(ECG)心律失常分类中的应用潜力。

Method: 将LGN的逻辑门推广到多于两个输入引脚，形成由N输入LUT构成的网络；采用2^N:1多路复用器(MUX)的布尔方程获得可微表达式，在训练中优化LUT输入参数；基于MIT-BIH数据集进行四分类患者间分类。并在Xilinx Zynq-7000 ZedBoard上实现由预处理流水线和6-LUTN分类器组成的系统。

Result: 四分类任务准确率最高达94.41%，jκ指数0.683，与CNN、SVM、SNN方法性能相当。模型仅需约2.89k至6.17k FLOPs(含预处理和读出)，比现有方法低三到六个数量级。完整系统动态能耗为8.25 μJ/次推理，其中LUTN分类器仅用0.46 nJ。

Conclusion: LGN和LUTN可作为轻量级硬件分类器用于患者间心电心律失常分类，兼具竞争性精度与极低功耗。

Abstract: Deep Differentiable Logic Gate Networks (LGNs) and Lookup Table Networks (LUTNs) offer a promising approach for very low power inference due to their use of simple binary logic operations instead of arithmetic. In this work, we generalize the logic gates of LGNs to more than two input pins, naturally arriving at networks consisting of $N$-input LUTs. To obtain a differentiable expression for training the $N$-LUT entries, we adopt the Boolean equation of a $2^N$:1 multiplexer (MUX) and optimize its input parameters during training. We investigate the applicability of LGNs and LUTNs to inter-patient ECG arrhythmia classification using the MIT-BIH data set. The proposed models achieve up to 94.41\% accuracy and a $jκ$ index of 0.683 on a four-class task, showing a competitive performance compared to existing CNN-, SVM- and SNN-based methods. Our LGNs and LUTNs only require an estimated 2.89k to 6.17k FLOPs, including preprocessing and readout, which is three to six orders of magnitude less than state-of-the-art methods. We verified our design, which consists of the preprocessing pipeline and a 6-LUTN classifier, by implementing it on a Xilinx Zynq-7000 ZedBoard. The complete system consumes a dynamic energy of 8.25 $μ$J/inference, of which only 0.46 nJ is utilized by the LUTN classifier. These results show that both LGNs and LUTNs can be employed as lightweight hardware-based classifiers for inter-patient ECG arrhythmia classification.

</details>


### [321] [Muon Under Gradient Noise and the Limits of Orthogonalization Near Optima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32861)
*Xiaohui Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了Muon优化器在最优解附近的噪声主导行为，发现其正交化更新在期望意义下等价于匹配学习率的线性梯度步，但保留了与输入噪声不相关的非线性残差，该残差会增加稳态协方差并抬高稳态损失下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon用权重矩阵的正交极因子替代动量缓冲。作者想探究在最优解附近、小批量噪声主导梯度时，这种正交化究竟起什么作用，从而判断噪声主导阶段是否仍需正交化。

Method: 在高斯噪声模型下推导Muon的期望更新；用Hermite展开分析动量对非线性残差的作用；构造局部二次代理模型，在稳态噪声缓冲上评估残差，并与全非线性递推仿真及冻结Transformer梯度测量对照验证。

Result: 期望Muon更新退化为缩放梯度步，匹配学习率的线性方法可复现其一阶均值响应；匹配响应后Muon留下与输入噪声不相关却贡献额外协方差的非线性残差。Hermite展开显示高阶分量比线性分量去相关更快，动量压制但无法消除残差累积协方差。局部二次代理中，残差增加稳态协方差并在每个稳定步长上抬高稳态损失下界，而收缩动态不变。仿真与实测均支持该图景。

Conclusion: 当优化进入噪声主导阶段，正交化的额外收益有限，结果从理论上支持用响应匹配的动量SGD替代正交化。

Abstract: Muon replaces the momentum buffer of each weight matrix by its orthogonal polar factor. We ask what this orthogonalization does near an optimum, where minibatch noise dominates the gradient. Under a Gaussian noise model, the expected Muon update becomes a scaled gradient step, so a linear method with a suitably matched learning rate reproduces Muon's first-order mean response. The stochastic update is a different matter: after the response is matched, Muon retains a nonlinear residual that is uncorrelated with the input noise and contributes additional covariance. A Hermite expansion shows how momentum acts on this residual. Its higher-order components decorrelate faster than the linear component, so momentum suppresses the residual's accumulated covariance relative to the linear part, but never eliminates it. In a local quadratic surrogate that evaluates the residual on the stationary noise buffer, the residual adds stationary covariance and raises the stationary loss floor at every stable step size, while leaving the contraction dynamics unchanged. Simulations of the full nonlinear recursion on quadratics and measurements on frozen transformer gradients support each step of this picture. Together, the results make a theoretical case for replacing orthogonalization by response-matched momentum SGD once optimization becomes noise-dominated.

</details>


### [322] [Staying on the Attractor: Supervising Neural Surrogates of 3D Turbulence Where They Leave It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32864)
*Yilong Dai,Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Jiaqi Gong,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi,Xiaowei Jia,Runlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Off-Attractor Supervision (OAS)，对神经代理在偏离吸引子处用 DNS 计算真实演化进行离线重标注监督，显著延长 3D 湍流预测的失效时间并提升长时统计保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理用于替代 DNS 预测 3D 湍流，目标是短期逐点准确、长期物理与统计保真。但混沌流中微小误差会将代理推离吸引子，而偏离态在训练数据中覆盖稀疏，演化约束弱，导致误差被放大并出现爆炸、冻结或统计漂移。因此需要在代理可能失败的区域补充监督。

Method: 提出 OAS：在代理离开吸引子的状态上施加监督，用 DNS 计算这些状态的真实未来并配对重标注。三种生成器选取数百个状态：1) 从代理自身 rollout 中采集；2) 通过代理对抗攻击针对冻结、过度放大、不可压缩性与能量平衡违背；3) 沿动力学的放大方向与强阻尼随机方向扰动训练状态。所有攻击仅在代理上运行，DNS 重标注每个状态仅离线执行一次。

Result: 在 128³ 湍流实验中，OAS 将失效时间的位数从 21 步提升到 721 步；对比基线中位数至多 110 步，且优势在多个训练随机种子下保持一致。OAS 在第 15 步取得最低逐点误差，并在对比方法中取得最佳长时统计表现。

Conclusion: OAS 将物理模型融入神经模拟，把监督从固定参考轨迹扩展到代理可能失败的状态，为部署时超出训练数据覆盖的科学代理提供更可靠的设计原则。

Abstract: Neural surrogates are trained to predict 3D turbulent flows in place of direct numerical simulation (DNS). For chaotic flows, the goal is short-term pointwise accuracy followed by long-term physical and statistical fidelity. However, small prediction errors can carry a surrogate away from the flow's attractor. Off-attractor states are poorly represented in training data, leaving their evolution weakly constrained. The learned dynamics can then amplify deviations and lead to blow-up, freezing, or statistical drift. We propose off-attractor supervision (OAS) to supervise neural surrogates where they leave the attractor. OAS teaches the model how the true Navier-Stokes dynamics would evolve from these states. Each selected state is paired with its own future computed by DNS. Three generators select a few hundred states for relabeling. The first collects states from the surrogate's own rollouts. The second uses surrogate attacks to target freezing, excessive amplification, and violations of incompressibility and energy balance. The third perturbs training states along an amplified direction and a strongly damped random direction of the dynamics. All attacks run on the surrogate alone, and DNS relabeling is performed offline once per selected state. Experiments on $128^3$ turbulence show that OAS increases the median time to failure from 21 to 721 steps. The compared baselines achieve medians of at most 110 steps, and the advantage holds across training seeds. OAS also achieves the lowest pointwise error at step 15 and the best long-horizon statistics among the compared methods. OAS integrates physical models into neural simulation by extending supervision from fixed reference trajectories to states where the surrogate is likely to fail. This principle can guide the development of more reliable scientific surrogates when deployment takes models beyond the coverage of their training data.

</details>


### [323] [Refreshing Less, Selecting Better: Reusing Stale Gradient Features for Efficient Influence-Based Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32888)
*Jianchang Su,Yifan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 缓存梯度特征在多个设定下能保持排序（Spearman 0.952–0.991），据此提出 CDIS：只对排名靠前的候选重算梯度、做仿射校准并按来源与长度配额选取子集，大幅降低选择成本且性能接近全量重算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度类数据选择方法（如 LESS）需为每个候选样本计算其梯度与目标验证梯度的对齐度，且在每次新检查点都要重新计算逐样本梯度特征，这成为主要开销。作者希望验证缓存特征能否在检查点变化后仍然保持排序质量，从而据此设计一种低成本的选择方案。

Method: 先在预热后的检查点缓存梯度特征，并在三个选择种子、两个模型家族、两个候选池与两个目标任务上，将缓存特征与新鲜验证梯度配对，考察排序保持性。基于观察提出 CDIS：在陈旧分数下对排名前 p 的候选重算梯度特征，在重算样本上拟合仿射校准，再在来源与长度配额下选取最终子集；并将缓存迭代跨四个检查点。

Result: 缓存特征在 40 个优化器步后仍能保持排序，Spearman 相关为 0.952–0.991，但其诱导的前 10% 子集会漏掉全量重算所选的 10–22%。当刷新比例不低于选择比例时，若校准后的陈旧分数误差有界，可精确恢复 top-k：p=0.3 时所有设定下恢复子集与全量重算一致；分层预算恢复率 0.92–1.00，梯度阶段墙钟时间下降 3.5–3.6 倍。跨四个检查点迭代缓存时 top-k 重叠 ≥0.98，每样本仅需 1.9 个梯度特征（全量重算为 4）。下游任务中，无约束 top-k 会坍缩到单一数据源，GSM8K 上比随机选择低 13 分；CDIS 比随机选择高 12 分（配对置信区间不含零），仅比全量重算低 4.3 分（约一个训练运行标准差），而选择成本低 3.4 倍。

Conclusion: 缓存梯度特征在检查点漂移下仍能保持高质量排序，CDIS 通过局部重算与仿射校准在不牺牲太多效果的前提下显著降低选择成本，并能通过重算样本上陈旧与新鲜分数的一致性作为免费的安全复用检查；同时说明下游选择必须施加来源与长度配额，否则易坍缩到单一数据源。

Abstract: Gradient-based data selection methods such as LESS score each candidate by the alignment between its gradient and a target validation gradient, and recomputing per-example gradient features at every new checkpoint dominates their cost. Across three selection seeds, two model families, two candidate pools, and two target tasks, features cached at a post-warmup checkpoint and paired with fresh validation gradients preserve the ranking 40 optimizer steps later with Spearman correlation from 0.952 to 0.991, while the top-10% subset they induce misses 10 to 22% of the examples that full recomputation selects. We therefore propose Cached Diverse Influence Selection (CDIS), which recomputes gradient features for the top-ranked fraction $p$ of candidates under the stale scores, fits an affine calibration on the recomputed examples, and selects the final subset under source and length quotas. A refresh fraction at or above the selection fraction recovers the exact top-$k$ subset whenever the calibrated stale scores have bounded error, and the budget curves follow this rule: at $p=0.3$ the recovered top-$k$ subset coincides with full recomputation in every setting, allocating the same budget per stratum recovers the stratified subset at 0.92 to 1.00, and gradient-stage wall-clock drops 3.5 to 3.6 times. Iterating the cache over four checkpoints keeps top-$k$ overlap at 0.98 or higher at 1.9 gradient features per example against 4 for full recomputation, and stale-to-recomputed agreement on the refreshed examples provides a free check for unsafe reuse. Downstream, unconstrained top-$k$ selection collapses to a single data source and scores 13 points below random selection on GSM8K. CDIS scores 12 points above random selection with paired confidence intervals that exclude zero and trails full recomputation by 4.3 points, one training-run standard deviation, at 3.4 times lower selection cost.

</details>


### [324] [Measurement-Gated Provenance Attenuation for Frozen EEG Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32889)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Yankai Chen,Ayana Mussabayeva,Nurdaulet Akhanov,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MGPA方法，用测量证据决定校正位置、保留信息决定目标值，在不重训编码器的前提下减弱EEG表征中的采集签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的EEG表征会同时保留采集签名与神经活动，而仅凭"来源可预测性"无法判断该移除什么，因为它可能源于测量效应，也可能源于真实的生物或群体差异，后者不应被抹除。

Method: Measurement-Gated Provenance Attenuation (MGPA)：成对测量对比定义一个门控区域，区域外不做改动；区域内将来源分数移动到保留坐标已预测的值。对固定仿射分数，这保持与那些坐标设定的任意目标相同的信息，且所需期望平方移动最小。提供闭式解与critic引导的迭代构造，无需来源身份或编码器重训。

Result: 在受控参考变化下（来源-任务关联已知），MGPA将来源降至接近随机水平且任务性能不变；而LEACE使冻结任务AUROC从.753降至.656却几乎未触及来源；消融显示衰减来自门控方向，且条件目标移动少2.7倍。跨设备与电极录音中，迭代校正降低来源可及性并保持或提升任务性能。一个在某一任务上选定并复用的迭代映射使另两个任务的worst-association AUROC较LEACE分别提升.057和.019。

Conclusion: 可复用的校正，其价值体现在采集线索不再可靠时已有预测器的表现，而不仅是探针能读出什么。

Abstract: Frozen EEG representations retain acquisition signatures as well as neural activity. Source predictability alone does not identify what should be removed: it can reflect measurement effects or genuine biological and population differences, which should not be erased. We propose Measurement-Gated Provenance Attenuation (MGPA), built on one principle: measurement evidence determines where correction may act, and preserved information determines what it should aim for. Paired measurement contrasts define a gate outside which nothing changes; inside it, the source score is moved to the value the preserved coordinates already predict: for a fixed affine score, this keeps the same information as any target set by those coordinates and needs the least expected squared movement. Closed-form and critic-guided iterative constructions apply it without source identity or encoder retraining. Three studies test the principle at increasing distance from its assumptions. Under controlled reference changes, where the source-task association is known, MGPA brings source to near chance with task performance unchanged, whereas erasing what predicts source (LEACE) lowers frozen-task AUROC from .753 to .656 while barely touching source; ablations attribute the attenuation to the gate's directions and 2.7x less movement to the conditional target. Across recordings from different devices and electrodes, iterative correction lowers source accessibility while preserving or improving task performance. Finally, one iterative map selected on one task and reused unchanged on existing heads for two others raises their worst-association AUROC (lowest over device-label shifts) by .057 and .019 over LEACE, at a cost to those heads while the training association holds. A reusable correction shows its value in how an existing predictor behaves once acquisition cues stop being reliable, not only in what a probe can read.

</details>


### [325] [A Function-Level Vulnerability Score Measures Flag Rate More Than the Model: Protocol Effects on Paired Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32890)
*Maciej Cichoń,Bartłomiej Dmitruk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过固定模型输出、仅变动评测协议（指标、判定提取、输出预算），量化了"评测协议"能解释多少模型间分数差异，并揭示函数级F1主要测量的是"标记率"而非模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多被用作漏洞检测器，但相似模型在不同论文中报告的分数差异很大，且尚不清楚这种差异在多大程度上来自评测协议本身而非模型本身。

Method: 采用配对测试：模型需标记存在漏洞的函数，并在修复提交后放行其修复版本。逐个变动三项被已发表评测做出不同选择的设计：指标、判定提取、输出预算。评估了7个前沿及大型开源模型（5个已发布配对基准+本文新汇总集）以及61个1.5B–36B参数的开源模型（仅汇总集）。分析方法包括Spearman相关、置信区间、独立性检验、长度匹配配对上的激活线性探针与tf-idf基线对比。

Result: 函数级F1与"模型是否同时标记一对中的两个函数"高度相关（42种组合上Spearman +0.86），而与配对级正确性几乎无关（+0.16）。配对分数上，中位数处提取带来的变化仅+0.001，输出预算为+0.02且区间跨零，而模型对基准分数的影响可达0.18、基准对模型的影响可达0.16。68个模型中有37个的"正确vs反向配对"差异其95%区间包含零（即零模型表现），而双标记与双放行率中位数分别超零模型0.055；64/68个模型中，一对函数得到相同答案的频率高于独立性预测。长度匹配配对上，激活线性探针组内排序准确率0.78，tf-idf为0.64，长度为0.5；六个模型中三个生成判定接近随机，另三个为0.55–0.57，prompted logit在全部六个模型上均为随机水平。

Conclusion: 模型间分数差异主要来自模型与基准的交互而非协议细节（提取、预算），但函数级分数主要测量的是"标记率/标记倾向"而非模型判别能力；模型判定大多由一对函数共有的文本决定，而非真正区分漏洞函数与其修复版本。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly evaluated as vulnerability detectors, and scores reported for similar models differ widely between papers. We measured how much of that difference evaluation protocol accounts for, with model outputs held fixed. In a paired test, a model must flag a vulnerable function and clear its version after a fixing commit. Three choices that published evaluations make differently were varied one at a time: metric, verdict extraction and output budget. Seven frontier and large open models were evaluated on five released pair benchmarks and a set pooled for this work under one protocol, and 61 open models of 1.5B to 36B parameters on the pooled set. Function-level F1 follows how often a model flags both functions of a pair (Spearman $+0.86$ over 42 combinations) and is nearly unrelated to pair-level correctness ($+0.16$). On the pair score, extraction changes a model's number by $+0.001$ at the median and budget by $+0.02$ with an interval through zero, whereas the model changes a benchmark's number by up to 0.18 and the benchmark a model's by up to 0.16; a function-level score therefore measures flag rate more than model. For 37 of 68 models the difference between correct and reversed pairs is within its 95% interval of zero, the value for a null model that flags each function at a fixed rate, while both-flagged and both-cleared rates exceed that null by 0.055 on median, and for 64 of 68 both functions of a pair receive one answer more often than independence predicts: verdicts are determined by the text common to both functions. On length-matched pairs a linear probe on activations separates 0.78 by within-pair ranking, against 0.64 for a tf-idf baseline and 0.5 for length; the generated verdict is near chance for three of six models and at 0.55 to 0.57 for the other three, and a prompted logit is at chance for all six.

</details>


### [326] [Optimizing H-Graph Hybridization for Diffusion-Guided RRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32897)
*Omer Talmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种推理时多样化策略（factorial diversity 与 refinement-only diversity），并通过 H-Graph 杂交结合，在 AntMaze 上验证可将采样多样性转化为更短更优的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的采样运动规划器单次运行即可生成高质量轨迹，但其推理阶段的随机多样性几乎未被利用，作者希望无需再训练地挖掘这一多样性来提升轨迹质量。

Method: 针对固定的预训练 DiTree 模型，设计两种推理时多样化策略：一是 factorial diversity，扫描随机种子与 Diffusion Goal Bias（DGB）参数；二是 refinement-only diversity，扫描扩散精修强度（RS，控制对 RRT 生成轨迹的编辑程度）；随后用 H-Graph 杂交将两者结合。

Result: 在 15 个迷宫场景的 holonomic AntMaze 机器人上，H-Graph 使 factorial 与 refinement-only 多样性的平均池长度分别提升 18.8% 与 14.5%；最佳单候选长度分别提升 9.7% 与 6.8%；相比成功基线轨迹长度分别改进 18.2% 与 19.9%。

Conclusion: 推理时参数变化是一种可靠、免训练的路径多样性来源，H-Graph 杂交能稳定地将这种多样性转化为更短、更高质量的轨迹。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners guided by diffusion models produce high-quality trajectories in a single run, yet the stochastic diversity available at inference time is left largely unexploited. We present two inference-time diversification strategies for a fixed, pretrained DiTree model, combined via H-Graph hybridization, and evaluate them on a holonomic AntMaze robot across 15 maze scenarios. The first, factorial diversity, sweeps the random seed and Diffusion Goal Bias (DGB) parameter, the second, refinement-only diversity, sweeps the diffusion refinement strength (RS) that controls how much an RRT-generated trajectory is edited. Because a single-run baseline only partially succeeds, we additionally compare H-Graph results with pool-based statistics. H-Graph improves the mean pool length of the factorial and refinement-only diversities by 18.8% and 14.5%, respectively. In addition, it also improves the best individual candidate's lengths by 9.7% and 6.8%, respectively. And last, compared with the successful baseline's trajectory length, it improves the results by 18.2% and 19.9%, respectively. These results show that inference-time parameter variation is a reliable, training-free source of path diversity, and that H-Graph hybridization reliably converts this diversity into shorter, higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [327] [When Less Compute Is More: Adaptive Early Exit Improves Pretrained Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32898)
*Tianyang Zhou,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个针对预训练异常检测模型的深度自适应早退研究：早退不仅提速，还能因缓解"上下文污染"而提升检测性能，并训练即插即用路由器自动选择数据集级退出层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表格基础模型以固定深度处理所有数据集，推理成本随数据规模增长。作者希望引入深度自适应早退以提升效率，同时探究早退对检测性能的影响及其机理。

Method: 在三种预训练异常检测基础模型上开展深度自适应早退研究；分析各层预测中近邻上下文样本的影响以识别"上下文污染"机制；以查询异常标签作为仅训练期可用的特权信息，预训练一个即插即用路由器，事后选择数据集特定的退出层，保持基础模型参数与预测头不变。

Result: 在最优中间层早退平均提升检测性能4.7–7.3%，跨三种基础模型一致；当上下文污染与自然异常率匹配时增益达13–21%。在三个大规模真实基准上，干净上下文时路由器可恢复最高45%的oracle增益并带来最高1.8倍加速，污染越重增益越大。

Conclusion: 早退在异常检测中兼具效率与性能双重收益，其机理在于缓解检索到"准确但被污染"邻居的负面影响；所提出的即插即用路由器可有效逼近oracle退出层选择，随上下文污染加剧收益更明显。

Abstract: Pretrained tabular foundation models process every dataset at a fixed depth, with inference costs growing with dataset size. To address this, we present the first study of depth-adaptive early-exit for pretrained outlier detection models. While early-exit is typically motivated by efficiency, we uncover a surprising benefit: exiting at the optimal intermediate layer can also improve detection performance on diverse real-world benchmarks by 4.7-7.3% on average, consistent across three distinct foundation models. First, we investigate the factors driving these gains, and identify a key mechanism: context pollution, i.e., the presence of outliers among in-context samples. Our analysis reveals that nearby in-context samples exert increasing influence on query predictions at greater depths, consistent with a retrieval-based view of these models. In effect, early-exit alleviates the adverse effects of retrieving accurate-yet-polluted neighbors, with gains of 13-21% when context pollution matches the natural outlier rate. Motivated by these findings, we pretrain a plug-in router to select a dataset-specific exit layer, using query outlier labels as privileged information available only during router training. The router operates post hoc, leaving the base model parameters and prediction head unchanged. Experiments on three large real-world benchmarks show that, on clean context, the router recovers up to 45% of the oracle gain with up to 1.8x speedup across three pretrained backbones, with larger gains as context pollution increases.

</details>


### [328] [Predicting the Next State Is Not Enough: JEPA Representations for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32908)
*Aarnav Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一步 Lean 状态转移能否为分支排序提供自监督信号，发现 JEPA 式预测在单步排名上优于 InfoNCE，但在长程搜索中反而不如 proposer 排序，说明准确的一步转移排序不足以作为长程搜索价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经定理证明器既需要提出策略，也需要决定探索哪些有效后继状态。作者希望验证一步 Lean 转移是否能作为分支排序的自监督信号，从而将表示质量与提案质量分离评估。

Method: 采用 JEPA 式模型预测潜在后继表示，仅对由固定预训练 ByT5 proposer 生成的、经内核验证且非终止的后继进行打分，并与匹配的 InfoNCE 方法在同一 theorem 排名诊断上比较。

Result: JEPA 在同一 theorem 排名诊断上 Top-1 为 50.18%，高于 InfoNCE 的 31.55%；但在三个随机种子下平均解决 282.3/987 个定理，低于 proposer 排序的 308，且需要更多策略检查。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，准确的一步转移排序不足以构成长程搜索价值；受控评估将表示质量与提案质量分离，并以内核验证的证明完成为主要终点。

Abstract: Neural theorem provers must both propose tactics and decide which valid successor states to explore. We study whether one-step Lean transitions provide a self-supervised signal for branch ordering. A JEPA-style model predicts latent successor representations and scores only kernel-validated, nonterminal successors generated by a fixed pretrained ByT5 proposer. JEPA achieves higher Top-1 than matched InfoNCE on a same-theorem ranking diagnostic(50.18% versus 31.55%), but averages 282.3 of 987 solved theorems across three seeds versus 308 for proposer ordering, while requiring more tactic checks. In this setting, accurate one-step transition ranking is therefore insufficient as a long-horizon search value. The controlled evaluation separates representation from proposal quality and treats kernel-checked proof completion as the primary endpoint.

</details>


### [329] [Allspark: Weak to Strong Transfer via Alternating Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32913)
*Kaizhao Liang,Junxiong Wang,Chen Liang,Zhendong Wang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Allspark 框架，通过"交替思维链"实现弱到强迁移：弱教师模型与冻结的同款模型交替推理，推理时用更强的学生模型替换冻结伙伴，无需强模型 rollout 即可提升强模型推理能力，并在 Qwen 与 ARC-AGI-2 实验上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型进展重新激发了对大规模强化学习的兴趣，但大模型 rollout 生成成本高昂，使 RL 方案测试代价过大。因此作者希望探究：能否在不使用强模型 rollout 训练的前提下，让弱模型学到的推理改进迁移到更强的模型上。

Method: 提出 Allspark 训练与推理框架，基于交替思维链实现弱到强迁移。训练时，弱教师与同款模型的冻结副本交替生成推理片段，由冻结模型给出最终答案；推理时，更强的学生模型替换冻结伙伴，且两个模型均保持固定。由于模型间通过文本通信，教师可跨模型家族、跨分词器地引导学生。实验在数学与推理的 Qwen 受控设置，以及 ARC-AGI-2 上的更大规模 Inkling 实验两个尺度展开。

Result: Inkling 实验在家族内与跨家族设置中均取得准确率提升，包括向 Kimi 和 Nemotron 的迁移，且收益在不同推理设置下有所差异。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持复用已训练的弱教师来服务多个强学生模型，并进一步研究由此产生的准确率—token 权衡。

Abstract: Recent progress in frontier models has renewed interest in large-scale reinforcement learning (RL), but the cost of generating large-model rollouts makes even testing RL recipes expensive. We ask whether reasoning improvements learned by a small, weak model can benefit a larger, stronger model without using the strong model's rollouts during training. We introduce Allspark, a training and inference framework for weak-to-strong transfer through alternating chains of thought. A weak teacher is trained alongside a frozen copy of the same model; the two alternate reasoning segments, and the frozen model produces the final answer. At inference time, a stronger student replaces the frozen training partner, while both models remain fixed. Because they communicate through text, the teacher can steer students from different model families and with different tokenizers. We study Allspark at two scales: controlled Qwen experiments across math and reasoning, and larger-scale Inkling experiments on ARC-AGI-2. The Inkling experiments show accuracy gains in within-family and cross-family settings, including transfer to Kimi and Nemotron, with benefits that vary across inference settings. These findings motivate reusing a trained weak teacher across strong students and examining the resulting accuracy--token tradeoff.

</details>


### [330] [Adaptive Latent Capacity for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32921)
*Idan Achituve,Lior Dikstein,Idit Diamant,Arnon Netzer,Hai Victor Habi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ALeWM，一种基于 JEPA 的世界模型，通过前缀条件分布与 MixSIGReg 正则，将预测信息集中到潜表示的紧凑前缀中，实现更高效规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准抗坍缩目标仅鼓励潜坐标间的变化，未按预测重要性组织坐标，导致潜表示信息分布无序，不利于递归规划与高效预测。

Method: ALeWM 学习前缀长度上的序列条件分布，训练预测器从采样前缀估计完整下一嵌入；并提出 MixSIGReg，用高斯活跃前缀加零填充的混合先验对掩码嵌入做正则，促使早期坐标保留预测关键信息。

Result: 分析表明 MixSIGReg 的混合分布对早期坐标块赋予更高方差；在特定假设下，将最有用于预测的信息置于早期块可最小化预测误差。在已知状态变量的受控动力系统和目标条件视觉控制中，ALeWM 平均成功率达固定宽度 LeWM 更高，且平均规划容量更低。

Conclusion: 通过有序化潜表示的前缀信息组织，ALeWM 在更少规划容量下取得更高成功率，验证了按预测重要性组织潜坐标的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Adaptive LeWorldModel (ALeWM), a world model based on a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that learns to concentrate predictive information in compact prefixes of a wide latent representation. To encourage this ordering, ALeWM learns a sequence-conditioned distribution over prefix lengths and trains the predictor to estimate the full next embedding from a sampled input prefix. As standard anti-collapse objectives encourage variation across latent coordinates and do not organize them by predictive importance, we also introduce MixSIGReg. MixSIGReg regularizes the masked embeddings against a prior-weighted mixture with Gaussian active prefixes and zeros in the remaining coordinates. As a result, the ALeWM objective encourages early coordinates to retain information useful for prediction and recursive planning. Our analysis shows that the mixture distribution used by MixSIGReg assigns higher variance to earlier coordinate blocks and lower variance to later ones. In addition, we show that, under specified assumptions, prediction error is minimized by placing the information most useful for prediction in earlier blocks. Empirically, we study the behavior of ALeWM in a controlled dynamical system with known state variables and in goal-conditioned visual control. We show that ALeWM consistently achieves higher mean success rates than tuned fixed-width LeWM, with lower planning capacity on average.

</details>


### [331] [The Geometry of Logic: Stratification Induces Semantic Structure and Robust Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32927)
*Cristina V. Lopes,Yuangang Li,Justin Tian Jin Chen,Alberto Krone-Martins,Iris Ma,Md Rakib Hossain Misu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 STRAT 架构，将残差流分为 Data 与 Type 正交子空间，用基于类型的注意力与门控控制数据变换，从而提升逻辑机制的可学习性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 在符号任务上表现良好，但其究竟学到可泛化规则还是依赖统计捷径尚不明确；机制研究提示，将"值"与"控制其操作的"类型"分离，可能有助于稳健推理，因此作者希望将这种分离作为架构原语来提升逻辑机制的可学习性与泛化。

Method: 提出 STRAT（Stratified Registers And Types），把残差流划分为正交的 Data 与 Type 子空间，并通过基于类型的注意力与门控来支配 Data 变换；同时通过受控算术消融实验识别出数据—控制干扰导致的三种失效模式：Linear Trap、Gradient Wall 与 Open Gate Trap。

Result: 机制分析显示模型具有可解释的逻辑结构；在算术任务上，STRAT 相对 Transformer 基线将 OOD 中位误差降低约 35 倍。在覆盖 10 个任务的 11 个数据集上，STRAT 的平均准确率均优于 Transformer 基线，平均高出 26 个百分点（两者均仅用每个数据集 10 个基示例训练，并使用相同的任务特定增强）。在分布偏移下，STRAT 平均准确率仅下降 2.39 个百分点，而 Transformer 下降 11.75 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将值与类型分离作为架构原语，能有效缓解数据—控制干扰，提升逻辑机制的可学习性与泛化能力；STRAT 在多种任务与分布偏移下均显著优于 Transformer 基线，并展现出可解释的内部逻辑结构。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models perform well on symbolic tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they learn generalizable rules or rely on statistical shortcuts. Mechanistic studies link algorithmic behavior to structured internal representations, motivating the hypothesis that robust reasoning benefits from separating values from the types that control their manipulation. Can making this separation an architectural primitive improve the learnability and generalization of logical mechanisms? We introduce \textbf{STRAT} (\textbf{ST}ratified \textbf{R}egisters \textbf{A}nd \textbf{T}ypes), which partitions the residual stream into orthogonal Data and Type subspaces and uses Type-based attention and gating to govern Data transformations. Controlled arithmetic ablations identify three failure modes associated with data-control interference: the Linear Trap, Gradient Wall, and Open Gate Trap. Mechanistic analysis reveals interpretable logical structure, and in arithmetic, STRAT reduces median OOD error 35-fold relative to a Transformer baseline. On each of 11 datasets spanning 10 tasks, STRAT outperforms the Transformer baseline in mean accuracy, by 26 percentage points on average, with both models trained from 10 base examples per dataset using identical task-specific augmentation where applicable. Under distribution shift, STRAT's mean accuracy drops by only 2.39 percentage points, compared with 11.75 for the Transformer.

</details>


### [332] [Phenomenon-Graph JEPA: Label-Efficient Representation Learning for Contactless Cardiorespiratory Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32928)
*Constantino Álvarez Casado,Nhi Nguyen,Mohammad Rakibur Rahman,Le Nguyen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Sasan Sharifipour,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Phenomenon-Graph JEPA 自监督预训练框架，利用毫米波雷达与 RGB-D 相机采集的心肺信号，通过生理现象图上的边预测目标嵌入，无需负样本或合成增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肺波形标注数据稀缺，每个标签都需监督采集；对比学习依赖信号变换与负样本，而时间扭曲会改变呼吸率、远距离窗口也可能共享同一生理状态，因此这些假设对心肺数据有效性存疑。

Method: 联合嵌入预测架构，输入四路处理后的一维流，每流由时序卷积分支与带限谱分支编码；预训练时沿两类带类型边（同一生理现象的流、同一状态片段内向前时间）预测被停止的目标嵌入；无负样本与合成增强。将生理类型化视为可检验假设，与错误边图、全对图对比；可选引入 Takens 式延迟坐标。

Result: OMuSense-23 上预训练相比匹配监督使标签效率面积提升 3.91 个百分点（95% 区间 2.08–5.80，Holm 校正 p=0.006），第二配置提升 3.74 点；但错误边与全对控制未能证明生理类型化的收益。延迟坐标在与学习历史的验证比较中有所改善，两个仅手腕 WESAD 协议未显示预训练优势。

Conclusion: 研究将预测性表示的实测收益与用于组织训练的生理先验区分开来：预训练收益可复现，但生理类型图本身的价值尚未被证实。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar and RGB-D cameras can record cardiac and respiratory waveforms continuously and without contact, but labeled recordings remain scarce because every label requires a supervised acquisition session. Self-supervised pretraining can exploit the unlabeled signals, yet contrastive methods depend on signal transformations and negative pairs whose validity is uncertain for cardiorespiratory data, where time warping changes breathing rate and distant windows can share the same physiological state. We present Phenomenon-Graph JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that learns from four processed one-dimensional streams without negative pairs or synthetic augmentation in its base configuration. Each stream is encoded by a temporal convolutional branch and a band-limited spectral branch. During pretraining, the model predicts stopped target embeddings along typed edges, which connect streams assigned to the same physiological phenomenon, and forward in time within a state episode. We treat this physiological typing as a testable hypothesis and compare it with wrong-edge and all-pairs prediction graphs. In the OMuSense-23 dataset, pretraining improves label-efficiency area over matched supervised training by 3.91 percentage points (95% interval 2.08 to 5.80, Holm-adjusted p = 0.006), and by 3.74 points under a second configuration evaluated on the same test participants. However, the wrong-edge and all-pairs controls do not establish a benefit from physiological typing. Optional Takens-inspired delay coordinates improve a validation comparison with learned history, whereas two wrist-only WESAD protocols do not establish a pretraining advantage. The study therefore separates the measured benefit of predictive representations from the physiological prior used to organize their training.

</details>


### [333] [Efficient Dynamic Algorithms for Graph Neural Networks with Non-Linear Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32929)
*Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Silvio Lattanzi,Danny Mittal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究动态图边插入/删除下非线性GNN传播的高效节点表示维护问题，提出基于残差的动态算法，在度归一化误差保证下实现每次图变更摊销O(1/ε)更新时间，并通过实验验证非线性提升精度且保持高效更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多GNN方法假设拓扑静态，在边随时间变化的演进网络上效率低；现有动态方法要么侧重时序GNN架构而不关注高效动态维护，要么局限于基于Personalized PageRank的线性传播模型，缺乏对非线性传播的高效维护研究。

Method: 针对无学习参数、仅训练后续分类器的非线性GNN传播，在边插入与删除下维护节点表示。对一类广泛的激活函数，提出基于残差的动态算法：通过push操作选择性传播局部误差，维护演化不动点的近似，避免全量重算。采用度缩放范数下的势能分析。对线性特例，额外给出基于低秩矩阵逆更新的精确动态算法。

Result: 在度归一化误差保证下，证明每次图变更的摊销更新时间为O(1/ε)；相比线性情形的先前工作，无需对更新序列或输入向量作随机性假设。基准数据集实验表明，引入非线性提升精度，同时保持高效更新性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展且具理论基础的动态图传播维护方法，实现了非线性GNN传播的高效动态更新，并兼顾精度提升。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used for representation learning on graphs, but most methods assume static topologies, making them inefficient on evolving networks where edges change over time. Existing dynamic approaches either model graph evolution through temporal GNN architectures without focusing on efficient dynamic maintenance, or are restricted to linear propagation models based on Personalized PageRank.
  In this work, we study how to efficiently maintain node representations for non-linear GNN propagation under edge insertions and deletions. The propagation has no learned parameters, and only a classifier applied afterward is trained. For a broad class of standard activation functions, we develop a residual-based dynamic algorithm that selectively propagates local errors via push operations, maintaining an approximation to the evolving fixed point without full recomputation. We prove that our method achieves amortized $O(1/ε)$ update time per graph change under a degree-normalized error guarantee. Our approach uses a potential-based analysis in a degree-scaled norm and, in contrast to prior work on the linear case, requires no randomness assumptions on either the update sequence or the input vector. For the linear special case, we additionally provide an exact dynamic algorithm via low-rank matrix inverse updates. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that incorporating non-linearity improves accuracy while preserving efficient update performance, yielding a scalable and theoretically grounded method for maintaining this propagation on dynamic graphs.

</details>


### [334] [Counting on Thinking: Tracing Evidence Integration in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32932)
*Jingming Xue,Robert C. Wilson,Huadong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过证据整合任务揭示:LLM直接作答无法自动完成计数,需依赖"思考"逐步构建累计量,且反馈学习难以将其内化为自动过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限算力下需在自动化的System 1与高成本的System 2思维间权衡。人类与动物能自动完成计数等基本运算,但LLM即便面对计数这类基础任务,直接作答也困难,本文追问其为何需要"思考",并探究哪些操作可通过学习变为直接可用。

Method: 借用心理学与神经科学的证据整合范式,设计任务:每轮对话呈现一个字母,询问两个目标字母中哪个出现更多。最优策略为维护"计数差"并为每个字母等权更新。对比直接作答与思考模式,分析整合权重、末轮注意力分布、推理轨迹,以及上下文强化学习(ICRL)下的表现变化。

Result: 直接作答对证据加权不均、呈强近因效应,且难度升高时对正确答案赋概率更低;思考模式提升表现并使整合权重近乎均匀,但两种模式下末轮注意力仍集中于序列两端。推理轨迹显示模型反复回看输入、重数并校验中间计数。推理token开销随字母数量增长远快于随连贯性增长。ICRL下反复博弈性能恶化、近因效应增强,模型却更自信。

Conclusion: 思考是在构建直接作答所缺失的累计量,而非读取已有结果。人类与动物将此类计算摊销为自动过程,而当前LLM每次试验仍需付出思考代价。哪些操作能被学习直接化,是未来模型分配算力的核心问题。

Abstract: Finite computational resources force a tradeoff between automatic System 1 processes and costly System 2 thinking. Large language models (LLMs) can spend extra computation on hard problems, yet direct answers struggle even with counting, an elementary operation humans and animals perform automatically. We ask why this requires thinking in LLMs. Evidence integration has long been used in psychology and neuroscience to probe decision-making. Our evidence-integration task presents one letter per conversational turn and asks which of two target letters appeared more often. A running count difference solves the task optimally by weighting every letter equally; tokens at each turn could represent and update this difference. Direct responses instead weighted evidence unevenly, with strong recency effects, and assigned less probability to the correct answer as difficulty increased. Thinking improved performance and made integration weights nearly uniform, yet final-query attention remained concentrated on the sequence ends in both modes. Reasoning trajectories showed models revisiting input, recounting letters, and checking intermediate counts that informed the answer, suggesting that thinking constructs the accumulated count that direct responses lack rather than reading out one already formed. Reasoning-token costs grew with the number of letters far more than with coherence. Outcome feedback did not bring this computation into direct responses: under in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL), performance deteriorated over repeated games and recency effects strengthened, yet models grew more confident. Humans and animals amortize such computations into automatic processes, whereas current LLMs still pay for them with thinking on every trial. Which operations learning can make directly available remains central to how future models allocate computation.

</details>


### [335] [Last-Iterate Guarantees for Online Reinforcement Learning in Structured Constrained MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32933)
*Nam Phuong Tran,Trinh Ha Mai Huynh,Tuyen Pham Le,Van-Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在结构化约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中实现末次迭代收敛的通用且统计高效的框架，支持基于结构化函数逼近的无模型在线与离线学习，并在线性CMDP上给出依赖于表示复杂度的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中，部署时使用的是单一策略，其性能与约束满足应直接成立，而不仅是对训练策略的平均或混合成立。这促使研究约束强化学习中的末次迭代保证。已有工作多在精确梯度或表格在线设定下建立此类保证，但面向结构化、大状态问题的可扩展结果仍然缺失。

Method: 作者提出一个通用的统计分析框架，将正则化原始-对偶动力学的收缩性与策略评估中的actor逼近误差、统计误差分离。该框架支持无模型在线与离线学习：采用乐观策略评估以避免显式构造转移模型，并使用紧凑的参数化actor，避免维护过去策略的混合或历史。作者将其实例化到线性CMDP与一般函数逼近。

Result: 该框架获得了依赖于表示复杂度的复杂度界，并在目标精度依赖方面优于先前的乐观正则化原始-对偶分析。作者进一步在合成的线性CMDP上验证了理论预测的稳定效应：正则化方法展现稳定的末次迭代行为，而非正则化方法则出现更大的振荡。

Conclusion: 该工作为结构化CMDP提供了可扩展的末次迭代收敛保证，兼具统计效率与无模型实现优势，并通过实验验证了正则化对末次迭代稳定性的关键作用。

Abstract: In safety-critical applications, deployment uses a single policy, whose performance and constraint satisfaction should hold directly rather than only for an average or mixture of training policies. This motivates last-iterate guarantees in constrained reinforcement learning. Recent progress has established such guarantees in exact-gradient or tabular online settings, yet scalable results for structured large-state problems remain open. We develop a general, statistically efficient framework for last-iterate convergence in structured Constrained MDPs (CMDPs). Our analysis separates contraction of the regularised primal-dual dynamics from actor approximation and statistical errors in policy evaluation under online exploration. This enables model-free on- and off-policy learning with structured function approximation: optimistic policy evaluation avoids explicit transition-model construction, while a compact parametric actor avoids maintaining mixtures or histories of past policies. We instantiate the framework for linear CMDPs and general function approximation, obtaining representation-dependent complexity and improved target-accuracy dependence over prior optimistic regularised primal-dual analyses. We further validate the stabilising effect predicted by our theory on a synthetic linear CMDP: the regularised method exhibits stable last-iterate behaviour, whereas its unregularised counterpart shows larger oscillations.

</details>


### [336] [The Impact of Stochasticity on the Rashomon Effect in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32934)
*Andrea Apicella,Francesco Isgrò,Andrea Pollastro,Roberto Prevete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究神经网络训练随机性（权重初始化、小批量数据顺序、Dropout）如何分别影响Rashomon现象的三个方面：解空间多重性、预测多重性与决策依据多重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练本质上是随机的，性能相近的模型可能因初始化等因素而不同，这一现象与Rashomon效应相关。尽管该效应受到越来越多关注，但不同训练随机性来源是否以相似或不同方式影响其表现尚不清楚。

Method: 沿三个互补维度展开实证研究：解空间多重性、预测多重性与决策依据多重性，分别用经验Rashomon集大小、预测模糊度和XAI归因图一致性来量化。独立控制三种标准随机性来源：权重初始化、小批量数据顺序和Dropout，以分离各自贡献。在表格和图像分类基准上进行实验。

Result: 三种随机性来源以不同方式影响三个维度。特别是，更大的经验Rashomon集不一定对应更大的预测分歧或更低的解释一致性，说明解空间、预测与决策依据多重性捕捉的是Rashomon效应互补而非可互换的方面。

Conclusion: 训练随机性不仅影响预测性能，也影响预测与解释的稳定性，凸显在评估神经网络可靠性、可复现性和可解释性时，识别导致Rashomon现象不同表现的具体随机性来源的重要性。

Abstract: Neural network training is inherently stochastic, with factors such as weight initialization leading to distinct models despite comparable predictive performance. This phenomenon is commonly associated with the Rashomon effect, which describes the existence of multiple near-optimal models for the same task. Although the Rashomon effect has received increasing attention, it remains unclear whether different sources of training stochasticity contribute similarly or differently to its manifestations. In this work, we present an empirical study of the Rashomon phenomenon along three complementary dimensions: solution-space multiplicity, predictive multiplicity, and decision-basis multiplicity. These dimensions are quantified through the size of the empirical Rashomon set, predictive ambiguity, and agreement between XAI attribution maps, respectively. By independently controlling three standard sources of stochasticity, namely weight initialization, mini-batch data ordering, and dropout, we isolate their respective contributions to each dimension of the Rashomon phenomenon. Experiments on tabular and image classification benchmarks reveal that these sources affect the three dimensions in different ways. In particular, larger empirical Rashomon sets do not necessarily correspond to greater predictive disagreement or lower explanation agreement, indicating that solution-space, predictive, and decision-basis multiplicity capture complementary rather than interchangeable aspects of the Rashomon effect. Overall, our results show that training stochasticity influences not only predictive performance but also the stability of predictions and explanations, highlighting the importance of identifying the specific sources of stochasticity responsible for different manifestations of the Rashomon phenomenon when assessing the reliability, reproducibility, and interpretability of neural network models.

</details>


### [337] [Theory of Scene: Breaking the Symmetry Trap in Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32939)
*Liangqi Yuan,Wenzhi Fang,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出基于大语言模型的多智能体系统存在"对称性陷阱"，提出无需训练的心智场景理论（ToS）推理范式，将同质性从陷阱根源转变为解决之道，并在多个基准上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统高度同质化，即便使用不同LLM，智能体行为也彼此相似。作者发现当这类智能体在无通信情况下并发行动时，会在需要分割的目标上碰撞、在需要共同占据的目标上分道扬镳，即"对称性陷阱"。常用于无通信协调的心智理论（ToM）无法逃脱该陷阱，因为同质智能体会对彼此形成相同预测并作出相同反应，因此需要新的协调范式。

Method: 提出心智场景理论（ToS），一种无需训练的推理范式：每个智能体读取其公共角色（智能体之间唯一的差异）以及所有智能体共同观察到的任务上下文。通过"角色门控"判断归属是重叠还是已分割，通过"任务耦合"推断团队应对每个目标是收敛、分割还是按阶段依次处理，从而使同质智能体推导出统一的分工。同时引入受控环境DivvyBench，其目标类型使回合呈现竞争、合作或混合特性，覆盖桌面、空域和家庭场景，并在GovSim与Overcooked两个已有基准上评估。

Result: ToS在全部基准上超越全部六个基线，且每个基线至少在一种设置上远落后于它。在相同输入下与ToM相比，ToS将DivvyBench成功率从71.1%提升至99.6%，GovSim总收益从207提升至400，Overcooked关卡归一化吞吐量从1.41提升至1.67。

Conclusion: 通过让同质智能体依据公共角色和任务场景推导统一分工，ToS成功将同质性由对称性陷阱的成因转化为解决方案，在竞争、合作与混合任务中均显著优于现有方法，为无通信多智能体协调提供了有效且无需训练的新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models (LLMs) are largely homogeneous, as their agents behave alike even across distinct LLMs. We show that when such agents act concurrently without communication, they collide on targets they must split and diverge on targets they must take together, a double failure we term the symmetry trap. Theory of Mind (ToM), widely used for coordination without communication, cannot escape this trap, since homogeneous agents form the same prediction of one another and respond to it in the same way. We propose Theory of Scene (ToS), a training-free reasoning schema in which each agent reads its public role, the only difference between the agents, and the task context they all observe. Homogeneous agents thereby derive one division of labor, each taking the part its role fixes, which turns homogeneity from the cause of the trap into the cure. ToS reads the role together with the scene through role gating, which determines whether ownership overlaps or is already divided, and the task context through task coupling, which infers whether the team must converge on each target, divide it, or take its stages in turn. We evaluate on DivvyBench, a controlled environment we introduce, whose target types make an episode Competitive, Cooperative, or Mixed across Tabletop, Airspace, and Household scenarios, and on two established agentic benchmarks, GovSim and Overcooked. ToS outperforms all six baselines on every benchmark, and each baseline falls far behind it in at least one setting. Against ToM given the same inputs, ToS raises the DivvyBench success rate from 71.1% to 99.6%, the GovSim total gain from 207 to 400, and the Overcooked level-normalized throughput from 1.41 to 1.67.

</details>


### [338] [TwinS-GCN: Spectral conjugate for Spectral Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32940)
*Chun Hei Michael Chan,Flavia Petruso,Dimitri Van De Ville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TwinS-GCN，通过谱共轭将图移位算子分解为耗散与非耗散部分，得到 sum filter 与 ratio filter，在降低深度的同时实现长程信息传输并缓解过平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图卷积网络通过图移位算子的反复局部聚合传播信息，$K$ 层网络只能覆盖 $K$ 跳邻域：加深会导致过平滑，而长程依赖又需要深度。要在长距离上无衰减地传输信息，移位算子需能区分流的方向，对称算子无法做到，有向算子可以。直接取移位算子的斜对称部分虽可提取纯方向性，但其一般与移位本身不对易，导致基于其构造的滤波器不具有移位不变性。

Method: 作者改用谱共轭，即算子在下映射 $τ:z\mapsto \bar{z}$ 下的像，它与移位对易，并将移位拆分为耗散部分与非耗散部分。由此得到两类滤波器：sum filter，其非耗散分量可无能量损失地传输信号；ratio filter，其形式为两分量的比值而非移位的多项式。二者均源自非全纯核，因此位于经典谱卷积所依赖的全纯类之外。为落地为稳定的顶点域层，作者采用 Chebyshev 重参数化，得到具有实系数的 TwinS-GCN。

Result: 在有向环上，ratio filter 化为具有全局脉冲响应的 IIR 滤波器，作者证明其相对于任意 $K$ 次多项式滤波器存在长程可达性差距。Chebyshev 重参数化给出稳定的顶点域层与实系数，TwinS-GCN 能以更小深度解决图迁移任务，并在节点分类基准上与最先进的图卷积网络相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过谱共轭而非斜对称分解来获得移位不变的方向性滤波，TwinS-GCN 在保持深度较浅的同时实现了长程、无衰减的信息传输，兼顾了过平滑缓解与长程依赖建模。

Abstract: Graph convolutional networks propagate information by repeated local aggregation through a graph shift operator; i.e., a $K$-layer network reaches $K$ hops neighborhood. On the one hand, such spreading can lead to oversmoothing. On the other hand, long-range dependencies demand the depth. Transporting information on long distances and without attenuation requires the shift to distinguish a direction of flow, which a symmetric operator cannot perform but a directed one can fulfill. A natural way to extract pure-directionality is to take the skew-symmetric part of the shift operator through the Cartesian split, which, however, generally does not commute with the shift itself, meaning that the filters built on it are not shift-invariant. We instead use the spectral conjugate; i.e., the image of the operator under $τ:z\mapsto \bar{z}$, which commutes with the shift and splits it into a dissipative and a non-dissipative part. Two filter families follow: a sum filter, whose non-dissipative component transports signal without energy loss, and a ratio filter, ratio in the pair of components rather than polynomial in the shift. Both arise from non-holomorphic kernels, placing them outside the holomorphic class underlying classical spectral convolution. On the directed cycle, the ratio filter becomes an IIR filter with global impulse response, for which we prove a long-range reach gap against every degree-$K$ polynomial filter. Chebyshev reparameterization gives stable vertex-domain layers with real coefficients, yielding TwinS-GCN, which solves graph transfer tasks at reduced depth and is competitive with state-of-the-art graph convolutional networks on node classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [339] [Generative Priors Conditioned on Natural Language for Bayesian Inversion in PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32941)
*Pengyu Zhang,Mark Girolami,Arnaud Vadeboncoeur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用配对文本与定量数据学习自然语言条件贝叶斯先验，结合新系统文本描述与少量噪声传感读数进行推断和不确定性量化，采用条件扩散与条件自编码器两种方法并在热方程、Helmholtz方程和英国气象再分析数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与工程中的量（QoI）推断常同时拥有定量数据（传感器、仿真、再分析）和定性数据（实验设置、预期结果、人类感知行为的文本描述）。作者希望利用两种模态的固有相关性，让文本描述为新物理系统的推断提供信息，从而在仅有少量噪声传感读数时也能更好地推断QoI并量化不确定性。

Method: 给定定性文本与定量QoI配对的训练集，学习一个信息丰富的、以自然语言为条件的贝叶斯先验；面对新系统时，将该先验与训练数据、新系统的定性文本描述以及少量噪声传感读数一致地结合进行推断与UQ。开发两条并行路线：条件扩散模型与条件自编码器，并与经典贝叶斯方法、无条件生成模型和确定性监督方法进行比较。

Result: 在稳态热方程、阻尼Helmholtz方程和英国气象再分析数据上进行测试。条件自编码器具有理论可处理性、后验采样快、UQ校准更好；条件扩散模型表达能力更强，可捕捉具有不规则QoI场的复杂后验。两者各有优势与权衡。

Conclusion: 文本条件先验能有效整合定性文本与少量定量观测用于QoI推断与不确定性量化；条件自编码器适合快速且校准良好的UQ，条件扩散适合复杂不规则后验的表达，实际应用需根据需求在二者间权衡。

Abstract: Inferring quantities of interest (QoI) from data is a central task in Science and Engineering. In such contexts, we often have access to both quantitative data and qualitative data. Quantitative data may be represented by noisy sensor measurements, simulation data, re-analysis data; qualitative data may be in the form of text descriptions of experimental setups, expected experiment outcomes, and human-perceived system behaviours. The task we address in this paper is the following. Given a training set of paired qualitative text and quantitative QoI data, we learn to exploit the inherent correlation between the two modalities to learn a highly informative data-driven natural-language-conditional Bayesian prior, such that when presented with a new physical system, we can coherently combine (i) the training dataset, (ii) qualitative text describing the new system, and (iii) a small number of noisy sensor readings from that new system, to perform inference and uncertainty quantification (UQ) over the QoI. To achieve this task, we develop two parallel approaches, one uses conditional diffusion and the other conditional autoencoders, and compare both against classical Bayesian methodology, unconditional generative models and deterministic supervised methods. Each approach has specific strengths and tradeoffs; conditional autoencoder offers theoretical tractability, allows for fast posterior sampling, and provides better-calibrated UQ, whereas conditional diffusion is explored for greater expressiveness and capturing complex posteriors with irregular QoI fields. The approach is tested on the steady-state heat equation, damped Helmholtz equation, and UK weather reanalysis data.

</details>


### [340] [Optimal Nonparametric Dynamic Pricing with Censored Demand and Adversarial Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32949)
*Mengxiao Zhang,Yingfei Wang,Haipeng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带有删失需求的在线动态定价问题，将已有线性假设推广到非参数设定，提出Double-Grid-UCB和Threshold-UCB算法，后者达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾并证明其极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作Xu et al. [2026]在$\sqrt{T}$遗憾下依赖严格的结构假设（线性需求、价格无关加性噪声、库存与噪声支撑的关系），适用范围受限。本文希望推广到更一般且统计上更难的模型，仅要求期望销量关于价格非增，允许非线性需求曲线和价格相关噪声。
