Method: 作者首先提出基线算法Double-Grid-UCB，对价格和库存同时离散化，对每个价格-库存网格对分别进行收益估计，达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$期望遗憾；随后提出Threshold-UCB，通过共享的需求尾部概率估计在不同库存水平间复用销量观测，使同一数据可支撑多个库存的收益上界，从而将遗憾改进到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$；并通过从随机发布定价问题的归约给出$\Omega(T^{2/3})$下界。

Result: Double-Grid-UCB达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$期望遗憾；Threshold-UCB达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$期望遗憾；通过归约得到$\Omega(T^{2/3})$下界，说明Threshold-UCB的极小极大最优性。大量实验表明Threshold-UCB在多种库存过程、需求函数和噪声模型下均优于基准算法。

Conclusion: 在更一般的非参数删失需求动态定价框架下，Threshold-UCB通过复用跨库存的销量观测实现$T^{2/3}$遗憾，并达到极小极大最优，实验验证其一致优越性。

Abstract: We study online dynamic pricing with censored demand, where an arbitrary inventory level is revealed before pricing and may adapt to past observations, while demand follows an unknown, price-dependent distribution that is stationary over time. For a horizon of $T$ rounds, Xu et al. [2026] achieved $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under restrictive structural assumptions including linear demand, price-independent additive noise, and conditions relating inventory levels to the noise support. Our first contribution is to extend this framework to a substantially more general and statistically harder nonparametric setting, requiring only the natural assumption that expected sales are nonincreasing in price and allowing nonlinear demand curves and price-dependent noise. For this model, we first propose a simple baseline, Double-Grid-UCB, which discretizes both price and inventory and achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret using separate revenue estimates for each price-inventory grid pair. Then, we develop Threshold-UCB, which improves the expected regret to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$. Unlike Double-Grid-UCB, Threshold-UCB reuses sales observations across inventory levels through shared estimates of demand-tail probabilities, allowing the same data to support revenue upper bounds for multiple inventories rather than a single inventory bin. We also complement this upper bound with an $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound via a reduction from stochastic posted pricing, establishing its minimax optimality. Finally, extensive experiments across inventory processes, demand functions, and noise models demonstrate consistently superior performance of Threshold-UCB over benchmark algorithms.

</details>


### [341] [Constrained Flow Policy Updates: A Generalized Schrödinger Bridge View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32952)
*Boyang Li,Matthew Kim,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RAFALE，一种面向在线安全强化学习的离策略 actor-critic 方法，通过流策略直接对增广目标求导，避免 score 估计，并用无密度动能正则替代熵正则，在七个 Safety-Gymnasium 任务上兼顾奖励与安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线安全 RL 需在满足安全约束下最大化奖励。奖励与安全约束会使动作分布呈现多峰，主流 primal-dual 方法中的高斯 actor 易坍缩到单一次优模式，且在非凸 Lagrangian 景观上优化不稳定。扩散/流策略虽能表示此类分布，但已有扩散 actor 需要估计并匹配增广 Lagrangian 目标策略的 score，增加复杂度与误差。此外流策略缺少可用的动作对数密度，难以做熵正则。

Method: 直接沿流策略的生成路径对增广目标求导，无需估计 score；沿用 FLAC 的无密度动能正则器处理熵正则缺失；提出 Reparameterized Augmented-Lagrangian Flow Actor with Least Energy（RAFALE）。将更新形式化为受限的单端广义薛定谔桥，并证明对每个源采样，该路径空间问题等价于动作空间中的熵正则问题。

Result: 在正噪声下，其解仅在估计代价超过由拉格朗日乘子设定的阈值处，对纯奖励动作分布进行重加权；噪声趋于零时，最优值收敛到流策略直接优化的最小能量映射目标。在七个 Safety-Gymnasium 任务上，RAFALE 取得有竞争力的奖励，且每个任务的平均最终代价均在预算内，而强基线往往在奖励与代价之间取舍。

Conclusion: RAFFALE 通过流策略直接优化增广目标、结合无密度动能正则，实现了稳定且兼顾安全约束的在线安全 RL；消融实验支持其增广目标与流 actor 两者的必要性。

Abstract: Online safe reinforcement learning (RL) seeks policies that maximize reward while satisfying safety constraints. Reward and safety can induce multimodal action distributions, challenging the prevailing primal-dual methods: Gaussian actors may collapse onto a single suboptimal mode, and optimization over the nonconvex Lagrangian landscape can be unstable. Diffusion and flow policies can represent such distributions, but recent work with a diffusion actor relies on estimating and matching the score of an augmented-Lagrangian target policy. Instead, we differentiate the augmented objective directly through the generation path of a flow policy, so no score needs to be estimated. Because a flow policy lacks a readily available action log-density for entropy regularization, we build on the density-free kinetic-energy regularizer of FLAC, a recent reward-only method, and propose Reparameterized Augmented-Lagrangian Flow Actor with Least Energy (RAFALE), an off-policy actor-critic method for safe RL. We formulate its update as a constrained one-ended generalized Schrödinger bridge and show that, for each source draw, this path-space problem is exactly an entropy-regularized problem in action space. At positive noise, its solution reweights the reward-only action distribution only where the estimated cost exceeds a threshold set by the Lagrange multiplier. As the noise vanishes, the optimal value converges to that of a least-energy map objective that the flow policy optimizes directly. Across seven Safety-Gymnasium tasks, RAFALE achieves competitive reward with mean final cost within budget on every task, whereas strong baselines trade one for the other; ablations support the necessity of both its augmented objective and its flow actor.

</details>


### [342] [Efficient Message Passing for Partial Differential Equation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32956)
*Anna Kazachkova,Leonhard Hennicke,Rainer Schlosser,Ralf Herbrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于因子图的概率推断方法来求解偏微分方程（PDE），通过消息传递与矩匹配近似参数后验，无需后验采样或全局梯度优化，在一阶平流方程和二阶半线性Fisher-KPP方程上取得与基线相当的预测精度，并提供结构化预测不确定性，推断速度接近变分推断，训练时间最多减少10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实物理量的先验信息最适合用偏微分方程（PDE）表达，但如何将PDE作为显式先验知识融入概率模型、高效求解并量化不确定性，仍是一个挑战。现有方法往往依赖后验采样或全局梯度优化，计算代价高。

Method: 提出用因子图上的概率推断来求解PDE：将主导PDE编码为显式因子以约束解空间，观测数据进一步塑造参数后验；通过基于矩匹配的消息传递推断近似参数后验，不进行后验采样，也不做全局梯度优化。

Result: 在一阶平流方程和二阶半线性Fisher-KPP方程上，方法达到与标准基线相当的预测精度，同时提供结构化预测不确定性；推断出的后验边缘均值与不确定性结构比所评估的均值场变分推断基线更接近Hamilton Monte Carlo结果，且实验中训练时间最多节省10倍，推断速度与变分推断相当。

Conclusion: 因子图上的消息传递概率推断为将PDE先验与观测数据结合提供了一种高效、无需采样的求解框架，在保持预测精度的同时给出可靠的不确定性量化，并在计算效率上优于采样方法、在不确定性刻画上优于均值场变分推断。

Abstract: Prior information for real-world physical quantities is most elegantly expressed via partial differential equations (PDEs). In this paper, we propose a novel way to solve PDEs using probabilistic inference on a factor graph. In general, factor graphs provide a natural way to encode prior knowledge into a model as explicit factors; here, this knowledge is provided by a governing PDE, which narrows the solution space, while observed data further shape the posterior over the parameters. The approximate parameter posterior is inferred using message passing based on moment matching, without posterior sampling or global gradient-based optimization. We demonstrate our approach on the first-order advection and the second-order semi-linear Fisher-KPP equations, where it achieves predictive accuracy comparable to a standard baseline while providing structured predictive uncertainty. Moreover, the inferred posterior marginal means and uncertainty structure match more closely those obtained using Hamiltonian Monte Carlo than the evaluated mean-field variational inference baseline, while requiring up to 10x less training time in our experiments, with inference speed comparable to variational inference.

</details>


### [343] [Beyond Token Savings: A Systematic Study of Context Compression in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32961)
*Ritul Satish,Prasoon Sinha,Akiho Kawada,Neeraja J. Yadwadkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统性地变化LLM智能体的历史压缩决策（压缩什么、何时压缩、压缩多少），在3个开源模型、SWE-bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 1.0上运行近35,000次，发现更少token未必更快更省，压缩策略的效果高度依赖任务、模型与负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体处理更长任务，推理、动作与工具输出的历史不断增长，需要压缩。压缩能减少token，但也会改变后续决策可用的信息。现有智能体框架把"压缩什么、何时压缩、压缩多少"捆绑为固定策略，缺乏系统性刻画来分离这些决策并揭示各自对任务成功率与执行成本的影响。

Method: 在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 1.0上，针对三个开源权重模型，系统性地独立变化压缩相关决策（压缩内容、时机、比例），共计约35,000次智能体运行，并测量任务成功率、token使用量、端到端延迟与估计成本。

Result: 更少的token不一定意味着更快或更便宜的执行：在Terminal-Bench上用Qwen时，仅使用约三分之一token的策略反而比未压缩的智能体慢20-80%。总体成功率相近的策略可能解决不同的任务；同一策略在不同模型上表现也差异明显。

Conclusion: 应基于压缩对智能体执行过程的影响来评估压缩，而非仅看token节省；压缩策略需针对具体任务、模型与工作负载进行定制化设计。

Abstract: As LLM agents tackle longer tasks, they increasingly compress growing histories of reasoning, actions, and tool outputs. Compression can reduce token use, but it also changes the information available for later decisions. Existing agentic harnesses bundle decisions about what to compress, when to compress, and how much to remove into fixed policies. A systematic characterization is needed to disentangle these decisions and reveal how each affects task success and execution cost. We systematically vary these decisions across three open-weight models on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 1.0. Across nearly 35,000 agent runs, we measure task success, token use, end-to-end latency, and estimated cost. We find that fewer tokens need not mean faster or cheaper execution: on Terminal-Bench with Qwen, policies using roughly one-third as many tokens can take 20-80% longer than the uncompressed agent. Policies with similar overall success can solve different tasks, while the same policy can perform quite differently across models. Our results motivate evaluating compression by its effects on agent execution and tailoring policies to the task, model, and workload.

</details>


### [344] [Clipped or Unclipped? Finite-Sample Trade-offs for Averaged SGD under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32962)
*Alexandra Suvorikova,Egor Gladin,Darina Dvinskikh,Artem Agafonov,Mohammad Alkousa,Yuriy Dorn,Vladislav Matyukhin,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有限条件p阶矩（p≥2）下，对裁剪与未裁剪的Polyak-Ruppert平均SGD进行有限样本比较，给出裁剪未必改善统计精度的显式条件，并指出裁剪的具体代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度裁剪常用于稳定训练，但已有认知中它能否提升平均SGD的统计精度并不明确，尤其（即使在）重尾噪声下。本文希望澄清裁剪与未裁剪平均SGD在精度与置信度上的差异，并量化裁剪的代价。

Method: 推导裁剪与未裁剪Polyak-Ruppert平均SGD的有限样本偏差界，假设有限条件p阶矩（p≥2）；通过平衡裁剪偏差与集中性获得新界；利用一维二次递推建立未裁剪重尾项的紧致性；并将比较扩展到投影凸SGD；同时分析固定阈值的渐近方差以及整体梯度裁剪在非对称噪声下的极限点偏移。

Result: 给出显式精度与置信条件：当p>2时，高斯项主导未裁剪偏差界，裁剪未必改善其主导阶；通过平衡裁剪偏差与集中性，得到重尾修正对逆失败概率呈对数而非多项式依赖的界；在p=2时改进主导界的置信依赖；通过一维二次递推证明未裁剪重尾项的紧致性；并将比较推广到投影凸SGD；证明裁剪代价：任意固定有限阈值在标量高斯二次问题上增大渐近方差，整体梯度裁剪在非对称噪声下可移动极限点。

Conclusion: 裁剪对平均SGD的统计精度并非必然有益；其主导阶在p>2时可能不受影响，且存在明确的代价（增大渐近方差、在非对称噪声下移动极限点）。所得界在置信依赖上更优，并提供了未裁剪重尾项的紧致刻画。

Abstract: Gradient clipping is widely used to stabilize training, but it need not improve the statistical accuracy of averaged SGD, even under heavy-tailed noise. We derive a finite-sample comparison of clipped and unclipped Polyak-Ruppert averaged SGD under finite conditional $p$-th moments, $p\ge2$. Our main result gives explicit accuracy and confidence conditions under which, for $p>2$, the Gaussian term dominates the unclipped deviation bound, so clipping need not improve its leading order. By balancing clipping bias and concentration, we obtain a bound in which the heavy-tail correction depends logarithmically rather than polynomially on the inverse failure probability. At $p=2$, this improves the confidence dependence of the leading bound. We establish sharpness of the unclipped heavy-tail term through an exact one-dimensional quadratic recursion and extend the comparison to projected convex SGD. We also prove concrete costs of clipping: every fixed finite threshold increases asymptotic variance on a scalar Gaussian quadratic, while whole-gradient clipping can shift the limiting point under asymmetric noise.

</details>


### [345] [Self-Confirming Superposition Traps in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32966)
*Dai Shi,Andi Han,Feng Chen,Yiqun Duan,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示强化学习中"自我确认叠加陷阱"：最优表征拟合也可能固化低回报策略，并提出保留被忽视状态、改进回放权重等干预以提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中，策略选择数据、数据塑造表征、表征影响回报、回报再指导策略，形成闭环。作者想探究：即使表征拟合在当前数据上全局最优，这个闭环是否仍可能维持一个低回报策略。

Method: 在绑定两步模型中刻画陷阱存在的维度；证明等特征频率、持续访问与独立控制器学习未必能避免陷阱；在有限动作模型中界定回报扭曲并推导回放条件；用Neural PPO实验观察反馈演化，并测试保留被忽视状态、调整回放权重、降低特征重叠等干预，在MiniGrid、DMControl及基于世界模型的DreamerV3–Crafter上验证。

Result: 实验显示，初始偏向不同动作的智能体在相同容量下形成不同干扰模式与相反的回报排名，最终策略不同。保留被忽视状态可降低实测干扰并改善序贯回报，即使编码器冻结也有增益；相关干预在MiniGrid与DMControl上改善控制，保护性拟合在容量不变时提升DreamerV3–Crafter的累计训练得分。

Conclusion: RL的策略–表征反馈闭环可产生自我确认的叠加陷阱，使最优拟合固化为次优控制。通过保留被忽视状态与改进回放/拟合方式，可缓解干扰并提升控制表现，说明状态访问与拟合权重设计对稳定学习至关重要。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) trains representations on data selected by the agent's policy, which then uses the resulting returns to guide its next choices. We show that this loop can sustain a lower-return policy even when representation fitting is globally optimal on those data. In a self-confirming superposition trap, every optimal code assigns overlapping directions to features that rarely occur together under the current policy. An alternative action brings them together, causing interference that lowers its return and reinforces avoidance, although refitting to that action would yield more return at the same capacity. We characterize the dimensions admitting a trap in a tied two-step model and show separately that equal feature frequencies, continued visitation, and independent controller learning need not prevent it. Because fitting weights errors by visitation, an avoided action can lose its return advantage at little cost to the objective. In a finite-action model, we bound this distortion and derive a replay condition: sufficient training weight on the best separately adapted action preserves its ranking despite residual error. Neural PPO experiments show how the feedback develops during learning: agents initialized toward different actions develop different interference patterns, opposite mean return rankings, and different final policies at the same capacity. We therefore test whether retaining access to neglected states can improve control. Keeping these states in training reduces measured interference and improves sequential return, with gains even when the encoder is frozen. Related interventions on state access, replay weights, and feature overlap improve control on MiniGrid and DMControl. For agents that learn through a world model, protected fitting improves DreamerV3--Crafter's cumulative training scores at unchanged capacity.

</details>


### [346] [Adaptive Ensemble Selection for Noisy Labels on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32976)
*Faizaan Ali,Inwon Kang,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向AI数据科学系统的数据中心推理模块，通过元模型自适应地为多种标签噪声检测器分配权重，实现数据集质量诊断与清洗策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据集中错误或损坏的标签会显著降低监督学习性能，尤其当误标较隐蔽、仅凭特征空间难以检测时。在自动化或AI增强的数据科学流程中，鲁棒地检测这类标签噪声对构建可靠模型至关重要。

Method: 提出一个数据中心推理模块，用于自动诊断数据集质量并选择清洗策略。给定数据集，由元模型预测一组多样化检测器（包括置信度类、邻域类、分布类方法）的权重，实现描述符驱动的集成。

Result: 在受控噪声的基准数据集上，该方法平均性能与Confident Learning基线相当，在异构情形下存在依赖数据集的正负增益。进一步表明检测器有效性与数据集属性存在系统性关联。

Conclusion: 验证了描述符驱动的数据中心集成方法作为AI辅助数据科学流水线组件的价值，可用于鲁棒的数据集评估与模型可靠性提升。

Abstract: Incorrect or corrupted labels in tabular datasets can significantly degrade supervised learning performance, particularly when mislabeling is subtle and not easily detectable from feature space alone. In the context of automated or AI-augmented data science workflows, robust detection of such label noise is critical for building reliable models. We propose a data-centric reasoning module for AI data science systems that automatically diagnoses dataset quality and selects appropriate cleaning strategies. Given a dataset, a meta-model predicts weights over a diverse set of detectors, including confidence-based, neighborhood-based, and distributional methods. Across benchmark datasets with controlled noise, our approach achieves performance comparable to a Confident Learning baseline on average, with dataset-dependent gains and losses, particularly in heterogeneous regimes. We further show that detector effectiveness is systematically linked to dataset properties. These results demonstrate the value of descriptor-driven, data-centric ensembling as a component of AI-assisted data-science pipelines for robust dataset assessment and model reliability.

</details>


### [347] [Feasible Flow Matching for Graph Reconstruction via Within-Sampling Primal-Dual Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32980)
*Haoming Chen,Nicolas Zilberstein,Santiago Paternain,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Constrained Primal-Dual PIFM (CPD-PIFM)，通过在采样轨迹中演化拉格朗日乘子，将结构约束（度界、三角计数、边密度带）纳入图重建，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图重建从部分观测出发时通常带有结构侧信息（度界、三角计数、边密度带），而先验引导的流匹配（PIFM）虽能将局部先验输运到图分布，却缺乏纳入此类侧信息的机制。

Method: 在 PIFM 采样器上增广沿每条轨迹演化的拉格朗日乘子；乘子根据预测端点处的约束违反情况作出响应，并引导后续采样步骤，且无需重新训练。

Result: 理论上证明采样器继承 PIFM 的置换等变性，并将期望终端松弛界定为随步数平方根倒数衰减的项加两个近似项。实验上，在三个链接预测基准、九种数据集与约束组合中，可行性提升 11–26 个百分点，且与固定引导方法相比具有竞争力，无需为每个约束单独选择乘子。

Conclusion: CPD-PIFM 为带结构侧信息的图重建提供了一种无需重训练、参数选择简便的约束采样方案，兼顾理论保证与实证可行性提升。

Abstract: Graph reconstruction from partial observations often comes with structural side information, such as degree bounds, triangle counts, or an edge-density band. Prior-Informed Flow Matching (PIFM) reconstructs graphs by transporting a local prior toward the graph distribution, but it provides no mechanism to incorporate this side information. We put forth Constrained Primal-Dual PIFM (CPD-PIFM), which augments the sampler with Lagrange multipliers that evolve along each trajectory. The multipliers respond to constraint violations at a predicted endpoint and guide subsequent sampling steps without retraining. We prove that the sampler inherits PIFM's permutation equivariance and bound its expected terminal slack by a term that decays as the inverse square root of the number of steps, plus two approximation terms. On three link-prediction benchmarks and nine combinations of datasets and constraints, CPD-PIFM raises feasibility by 11-26 percentage points and remains competitive with fixed guidance without selecting a separate multiplier for each constraint.

</details>


### [348] [What Should Data Teach? Moving Bottlenecks Across Circuit, Store, and Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32991)
*Yixiao Chen,Ke Cheng,Jiangtao Guan,Shuo Huang,Yue Liu,Jun Zhang,Yuhong Liu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从电路视角揭示语言模型训练中的三个瓶颈（计算形成、内容可用性、路由选择），并提出"诊断到数据"的共享原则来针对性设计监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是回答"在训练的特定阶段，数据应该教给语言模型什么"。现有做法往往只是对困难样本重新排序，而作者认为当限制性操作发生变化时，需要改变监督方式而非仅重新排序难度样本。

Method: 提出诊断到数据原则：定位缺失操作、保留其因果关系、变化含捷径的上下文、重新审计残差。据此设计三类干预：形成敏感的选择与先决条件排序（加速绑定-匹配-传输路径）、可用性反事实（区分写入内容与调用记忆）、配对监督与上下文机会排序（改进路由决策）。在同一个350M模型上做连续实验连接三种干预。

Result: 早期电路训练改善了后续学习；完整干预序列在被"使用教学"排除的事实上优于阶段替换对照组；同一训练多重集的早期前缀在100B token内保持验证优势；配对监督与上下文机会排序提升匹配路由决策和长上下文答案可能性。

Conclusion: 独立查询表面与对立来源决策表明条件仲裁是尚待解决的边界问题。核心结论是：限制性操作的变化要求监督方式的改变，而非仅仅对困难样本重新排序。

Abstract: What should data teach a language model at a particular point in training? A circuit view reveals three distinct bottlenecks: forming a computation, making its required content available, and selecting among available routes. A shared diagnosis-to-data principle connects them: localize the missing operation, preserve its causal relation, vary shortcut-bearing context, and re-audit the residual. Formation-sensitive selection and prerequisite ordering accelerate a binding-matching-transport path; a brief early prefix from the same training multiset retains a validation advantage through 100B tokens. Availability counterfactuals then distinguish writing content from invoking available memory, while paired supervision and context-opportunity ranking improve matched route decisions and long-context answer likelihood. A continuous 350M-model experiment connects the three interventions on the same facts: early circuit training improves subsequent learning, and the complete sequence outperforms stage-replacement controls on facts withheld from Use teaching. Independent query surfaces and opposed-source decisions expose conditional arbitration as the remaining frontier. Together, these results show why a change in the limiting operation calls for a change in supervision, not merely a new ranking of difficult examples.

</details>


### [349] [Saturation-Insensitive Dueling Bandits with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33011)
*Chenggong Zhang,Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Weitong Zhang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究一般函数逼近下的上下文对决老虎机，提出SI-CDB算法以解决BTL偏好模型饱和导致的奖励学习困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BTL偏好模型下，当当前奖励模型已能以高置信度区分两个动作时，偏好反馈变得信息量很弱，导致奖励估计难以进一步提升。这种模型饱和现象使得现有样本复杂度分析依赖于逆导数因子1/σ'[Δ_r*]，当链接函数σ在大奖励差距处饱和时该因子会变得极大，从而带来不利的复杂度依赖。

Method: 作者提出SI-CDB算法，通过精心设计的启发式策略选择对手臂（opponent arms），从而实现对饱和不敏感的奖励学习。理论分析的核心是一个针对一般函数逼近对决老虎机定制的局部化Eluder维数（localized Eluder dimension）框架。

Result: SI-CDB恢复了线性奖励类下的近最优依赖，消除了不利的1/σ'(·)因子。理论结果还解释了为何双臂遗憾分析对于提升对决老虎机中单臂性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该工作通过SI-CDB算法和局部化Eluder维数框架，有效解决了BTL偏好模型饱和问题，为一般函数逼近下的上下文对决老虎机提供了对饱和不敏感的样本复杂度分析。

Abstract: We study contextual dueling bandits with general function approximation under the Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) preference model. A key challenge in this setting is the saturation of the preference model: when the current reward model can already distinguish two actions with high confidence, the resulting preference feedback becomes weakly informative, making it difficult to further improve reward estimation. Consequently, existing sample-complexity analyses often depend on the inverse-derivative factor $1 / σ'[Δ_{r^\ast}]$ which can be prohibitively large when the link function $σ$ saturates for large reward gaps $Δ_{r^\ast}$. To address this issue, we introduce `SI-CDB`, an algorithm that selects opponent arms using a carefully designed heuristic for arm selection. This design enables saturation-insensitive reward learning and recovers the near-optimal dependence for linear reward classes, eliminating the unfavorable $1/σ'(\cdot)$ factor. The core of our analysis is a localized Eluder dimension framework tailored to dueling bandits with general function approximation. Our theoretical results also explain why two-arm regret analysis is crucial for improving single-arm performance in dueling bandits.

</details>


### [350] [Relative Generalization Invariance of LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33016)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Shuche Wang,Shenggui Li,Tianyu Ruan,Jianliang He,Ivor Tsang,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Tianwei Zhang,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"相对泛化不变性"(RGI)概念，用于区分优化器、模型架构与训练数据流对LLM预训练性能的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练性能由优化器、模型架构和训练数据流三部分共同决定，但三者各自如何影响性能尚不清楚。作者希望通过研究相对泛化来隔离这些因素的影响。

Method: 引入相对泛化不变性(RGI)，即在任意两个token之间，不同模型的验证损失差异保持不变的特性。作者在多种优化器和适度的架构变化下验证RGI是否成立，并检验数据流变化对RGI的影响，同时与神经正切核及平均场理论进行对比，并在过参数化二次模型中给出理论证明。

Result: RGI在广泛的优化器和适度架构变化下近似成立，说明这些选择对token级损失带来近似均匀的偏移；而改变训练数据流则会显著改变相对泛化。RGI无法仅由神经正切核或平均场机制解释，但可在过参数化二次模型中自然涌现。

Conclusion: RGI作为LLM预训练中的一种新现象，有助于将优化器和架构的影响与训练数据的影响区分开来。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) pretraining performance is jointly shaped by three components of the training triplet: the optimizer, model architecture, and training data stream. However, how these components influence performance in distinct ways remains unclear. We take a first step toward isolating their effects by studying relative generalization. We introduce Relative Generalization Invariance (RGI), the invariance of the validation-loss difference between any two tokens across models. We show that RGI approximately holds across a wide range of optimizers and moderate architectural variations, suggesting that these choices induce an approximately uniform shift in token-wise losses. In contrast, changing the training data stream can substantially alter relative generalization. We further show that RGI cannot be explained by the neural tangent kernel or mean-field regimes alone and prove that it can emerge in an overparameterized quadratic model. Overall, our work identifies RGI as a new phenomenon in LLM pretraining that helps distinguish the effects of optimizers and architectures from those of training data.

</details>


### [351] [Low-Rank Single-Index Bandits with Unknown Links: From Matrices to Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33025)
*Zhongxuan Liu,Yue Kang,Thomas C. M. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有未知共享Lipschitz链接函数和低秩索引参数的随机矩阵与张量bandit问题，提出T-ESTOR和T-BSTOR算法，分别针对单调与非单调链接实现最优遗憾率，并通过合成与CCLE实验验证结构估计的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩矩阵/张量bandit虽能利用结构交互，但通常假设奖励链接已知；而现有单索引bandit方法虽能处理未知链接，却未直接利用矩阵或张量的低秩结构。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在已知正则候选分布和有限方差噪声下，研究具有未知共享Lipschitz链接与低秩索引参数的随机矩阵/张量bandit。对单调链接，提出T-ESTOR，将鲁棒且秩自适应的Stein估计与基于epoch的贪心选择结合；对非单调链接，在非零基律Stein信号下提出T-BSTOR，将结构估计与鲁棒分箱学习结合。

Result: 在精确选中分数访问与一致正选中设计Stein信号下，T-ESTOR达到平方根遗憾，维度依赖由低秩结构决定；对每个可容许设计，单调下界在大horizon下匹配秩、维度和horizon依赖（至对数因子）。T-BSTOR在固定维度、菜单大小和模型/设计常数下达到最优Õ(T^{2/3})速率。合成与CCLE实验显示结构估计优于向量化及竞争的单索引基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为未知链接下的低秩矩阵与张量bandit提供了理论与算法框架，T-ESTOR与T-BSTOR分别在单调和非单调场景达到最优遗憾率，实验验证了利用低秩结构估计的有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank matrix and tensor bandits exploit structured interactions but typically assume a known reward link. Recent single-index bandit methods accommodate unknown links without directly exploiting matrix or tensor rank. We address this gap by studying stochastic matrix and tensor bandits with an unknown shared Lipschitz link and a low-rank index parameter under known regular candidate distributions and finite-variance noise. For monotone links, T-ESTOR combines robust, rank-adaptive Stein estimation with epoch-based greedy selection. Under exact selected-score access and a uniformly positive selected-design Stein signal, it achieves square-root regret with dimension dependence determined by the low-rank structure. For every admissible design, the monotone lower bound matches the rank, dimension, and horizon dependence up to logarithmic factors at large horizons, for fixed menu size and model/design constants. For nonmonotone links under a nonzero base-law Stein signal, T-BSTOR combines structured estimation with robust bin-based learning and attains the optimal $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ horizon rate for fixed dimensions, menu size, and model/design constants. Synthetic and CCLE-based experiments illustrate the benefits of structured estimation relative to vectorized and competing single-index baseline methods.

</details>


### [352] [Łukasiewicz Neural Networks Extended: Residual Architectures and Crystallization Strategies for Interpretable Rule Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33028)
*Carlos Leandro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入残差连接，将Łukasiewicz神经网络扩展到任意深度并保持符号化知识抽取能力，同时分析三种结晶策略的理论保证与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有Łukasiewicz神经网络虽能实现符号知识抽取（训练得到逻辑公式而非黑箱模型），但存在两个障碍：结晶（强制权重为整数）仅在概率上成功，且理论保证随网络加深而失效，限制了其在浅层架构和小数据集上的应用。

Method: 证明残差连接（类似ResNet的跳跃连接）可将Łukasiewicz神经网络扩展到任意深度，并通过构造保证在合并神经元处保持符号对应：Łukasiewicz残差块中的合并神经元自动满足神经元分类命题，不受内层权重影响；内层神经元则通过结晶策略训练至可表示性。同时分析三种结晶策略：修正的Levenberg--Marquardt、直通估计（STE）和近端正则化。

Result: 残差连接在构造上保证了合并神经元的符号对应关系，不受内层权重影响；内层神经元可通过结晶策略训练至可表示。三种结晶策略的理论保证、失败模式和可解释性权衡得到刻画。

Conclusion: 残差连接可将Łukasiewicz神经网络的符号对应扩展到任意深度，克服了原方法仅适用于浅层架构的限制；三种结晶策略各有其理论保证、失败模式与可解释性权衡。

Abstract: A feed-forward neural network whose weights are integers and whose activation is the truncated identity implements, neuron by neuron, the connectives of Łukasiewicz many-valued logic. This exact correspondence --- established theoretically by Castro and Trillas and developed into a training algorithm by Leandro --- enables \emph{symbolic knowledge extraction}: training produces not a black-box model but a logical formula. Two obstacles have limited the approach to shallow architectures and small datasets: crystallization (forcing weights to integers) succeeds only probabilistically under the original Levenberg--Marquardt training scheme, and the theoretical guarantees break down as networks grow deeper.
  This paper addresses both obstacles. First, we prove that \emph{residual connections} (skip connections of the kind used in ResNets) extend Łukasiewicz neural networks to arbitrary depth while preserving the symbolic correspondence \emph{at merge neurons} by construction: merge neurons in a Łukasiewicz residual block automatically satisfy the neuron-classification proposition, regardless of the inner layer weights; inner-layer neurons are trained toward representability by the crystallization strategy. Second, we analyse three crystallization strategies --- Levenberg--Marquardt (corrected), straight-through estimation (STE), and proximal regularization --- characterizing their theoretical guarantees, failure modes, and interpretability trade-offs.

</details>


### [353] [What Must a World Model Distinguish for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33030)
*Rongzhe Wei,Hans Hao-Hsun Hsu,Peizhi Niu,Yifan Li,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出机制充分性、响应充分性与决策充分性的层级框架，指出世界模型需保留的信息取决于规划查询、候选集与规划器，并在碰撞系统、非线性动力学与机器人规划中验证；实验显示查询条件联合生成模型在已见目标上遗憾更低，但泛化到未见目标时优势消失，因此提出"查询决定看哪里、动作条件模型预测发生什么"的模块化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型用于模拟动作候选的后果，但良好规划并不需要保留准确预测所需的全部物理区分。论文旨在形式化预测需求与决策需求之间的差距，并厘清世界模型究竟需要保留哪些信息。

Method: 提出机制、响应、决策三层充分性层级，用以刻画给定候选集下规划查询决定哪些物理变化重要及其需保留的精度；在碰撞系统、非线性动力学与机器人规划中进行研究，并对比查询条件联合生成模型与动作条件世界模型的表现。

Result: 在已见目标上，联合生成动作与结果的查询条件模型遗憾低于动作条件世界模型，但该优势在泛化到未见目标时大幅消失；规划器自适应搜索时，最终选择不需要的信息仍可能是发现优质候选所必需的。

Conclusion: 世界模型须保留的内容取决于查询、候选集与规划器。据此提出模块化设计：由查询决定"往哪里看"，由动作条件模型预测"会发生什么"，使同一预测可跨目标复用。

Abstract: World models simulate the consequences of action candidates, but good planning need not preserve every physical distinction required for accurate prediction. We formalize this gap through a hierarchy of mechanism, response, and decision sufficiency. Given a candidate set, the planning query determines which physical variations matter and how precisely they must be preserved: coarse decisions can discard much of the information needed for prediction, whereas fine decisions may require nearly the same resolution. In practice, planners often adaptively search to construct candidates, and information unnecessary for final selection may still be needed to discover good candidates. What a world model must preserve therefore depends on the query, the candidate set, and the planner. We study these effects in a collision system, nonlinear dynamics, and robotic planning. These varying requirements raise a design question: where should query information enter the planning system? A model that jointly generates actions and outcomes conditioned on the query achieves lower regret than an action-conditioned world model on seen objectives, but this advantage largely disappears when generalizing to unseen objectives. Motivated by this, we propose a modular design in which the query determines where to look and an action-conditioned model predicts what will happen, allowing the same predictions to be reused across objectives.

</details>


### [354] [Balancing Early Performance Sacrifices with Long-Term Gains: Scaling Learning-Rate Warmup Duration Across Training Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33041)
*Kristi Topollai,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型训练中学习率预热时长的设定问题，通过二次模型提出一个与训练时长相关的预热时长缩放定律，说明预热应视为依赖训练时长的超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率预热是语言模型训练的标准技术，但其时长设定基本靠启发式：要么固定步数，要么固定训练时长比例，两者在训练变长时的缩放行为差异很大。作者希望回答预热何时应固定、何时应随训练时长增长。

Method: 作者构建了一个二次模型，其中不同模式对峰值学习率的响应不同：预热会减慢那些在峰值学习率下已良好收缩方向的进展，但能消除接近稳定性边界方向上的持续误差；峰值学习率越高，平衡越倾向于更长的预热时长。

Result: 得到一个紧凑的训练时长缩放定律，涵盖几乎不预热、固定时长预热、以及随训练时长增长预热等多种情形，并解释了偏好的预热机制如何随峰值学习率变化。该定律可用较短训练拟合，并预测更长训练时长的预热需求。

Conclusion: 研究通过单一权衡解释了预热的若干常见性质，并建议将预热时长视为依赖训练时长的超参数，而非固定的训练启发式设定。

Abstract: Learning-rate warmup is a standard technique in language-model training, yet its duration remains largely heuristic. Common approaches use either a fixed number of updates or a fixed fraction of the training horizon, two choices that imply very different scaling as training gets longer. When should warmup stay fixed, and when should it grow with the horizon? We address this question with a quadratic model whose modes respond differently to the peak learning rate. Warmup slows progress in directions that already contract well at the peak rate, but can remove persistent error in directions near the stability edge, with higher peak rates shifting the balance toward longer warmup durations. This yields a compact horizon scaling law that captures regimes ranging from essentially no warmup, through fixed-duration warmup, to durations that grow with the training horizon, and explains how the preferred regime changes with peak learning rate. Because the law captures the tradeoff between giving up early progress and improving the trajectory that follows, it can be fit using shorter runs and used to predict warmup at substantially longer horizons. Together, our results explain several familiar properties of warmup through a single tradeoff and suggest treating warmup duration as a horizon-dependent hyperparameter rather than a fixed training heuristic.

</details>


### [355] [Cost-free Spectral Estimation for Adaptive Newton--Schulz in Matrix Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33047)
*Kristi Topollai,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种谱自适应方法，利用 Newton-Schulz 迭代内部 Gram 矩阵的低成本标量缩减来估计奇异值分布，从而为每个矩阵选择专门的多项式例程，将正交化变为随层和时间自适应的过程，在固定迭代预算下降低正交化误差并在 GPT 预训练中降低验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵优化器（如 Muon）通过近似正交化（通常用少量 Newton-Schulz 矩阵乘法）处理动量矩阵。该近似的质量与成本强烈依赖于输入的奇异值谱，但现有实现对所有层和整个训练过程都使用同一个固定多项式例程，这种统一处理并不必要。

Method: 作者指出 Newton-Schulz 迭代中形成的 Gram 矩阵可通过廉价的标量缩减获得谱矩，无需额外矩阵乘法。基于这些矩，他们恢复经验奇异值分布的估计，并据此选择针对当前矩阵特化的多项式例程，将 Newton-Schulz 正交化转变为谱自适应过程，能够响应不同层和训练时间上的谱差异。

Result: 在保存的动量矩阵上，谱估计在固定迭代预算下显著降低正交化误差，或以更少迭代达到相同精度；在高达 10 亿参数的 GPT 预训练中，该方法降低了两种矩阵优化器的验证损失。

Conclusion: 优化器内部的矩阵函数操作不必针对保守的最坏情况谱进行设计：它们可以廉价地测量自身正在处理的谱，并据此特化计算。这为设计更高效、自适应的矩阵优化器提供了新思路。

Abstract: Matrix optimizers such as Muon transform each momentum matrix through an approximate orthogonalization, typically implemented by a small number of Newton-Schulz matrix multiplications. The quality and cost of this approximation depend strongly on the singular-value spectrum of its input, yet existing implementations use the same fixed polynomial routine for every layer and throughout training. We show that this uniform treatment is unnecessary: the computations in the Newton-Schulz method already reveal enough information to make the method adaptive. The Gram matrices formed inside Newton-Schulz iterations yield spectral moments through inexpensive scalar reductions, requiring no additional matrix multiplications. From these moments, we recover an estimate of the empirical singular-value distribution and use it to select a polynomial routine specialized to the current matrix. This turns Newton--Schulz orthogonalization into a spectrum-adaptive procedure that responds to differences across both layers and training time. On saved momentum matrices, spectral estimation substantially reduces orthogonalization error at a fixed iteration budget or reaches the same accuracy with fewer iterations, and in GPT pretraining up to 1B parameters it lowers the validation loss of two matrix optimizers. Our results suggest that matrix-function operations inside optimizers need not be designed for a conservative worst-case spectrum: they can cheaply measure the spectrum they are already processing and specialize computations accordingly.

</details>


### [356] [DevelopmentODE: Structured Neural ODEs for Early Brain Development Dynamics Across a Decade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33048)
*Kaiqiao Han,Haitao Chen,Bryan Quah,Xiaoda Wang,Janelle Liu,John H Gilmore,Wei Gao,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DevelopmentODE，一种结构化的连续时间框架，用于从稀疏纵向观测中建模儿童脑发育轨迹。该模型以"发育渠化"为共享几何参考，组织群体与个体变异，并通过随年龄变化的向量场形变刻画发育非平稳性，在纵向fMRI功能连接预测任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童脑发育研究依赖稀疏且时间间隔不规则的纵向观测，而发育动态又随个体和年龄变化。通用连续时间模型虽能处理不规则采样，但往往把这些因素统统塞进单一灵活的转移函数，缺少对群体进程、个体差异与发育年龄如何塑造动态的结构化刻画。

Method: 提出DevelopmentODE：在共享发育几何中组织群体与个体变异。以"发育渠化"表示群体轨迹，其局部方向作为组织个体变异的参考；个体偏差速度相对于该方向受约束，共享的非线性偏差场刻画个体发育运动而不干扰群体进程。同时用有序的、依赖年龄的共享向量场形变建模发育非平稳性，使之随年龄平滑演化，积分的时长由流逝时间决定。

Result: 在纵向fMRI数据上，用儿童早期观测预测其未来功能连接，DevelopmentODE在短期和长期预测上均持续优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: DevelopmentODE利用群体层面的发育结构来引导稀疏个体轨迹的学习，同时允许动态随年龄平滑变化，为长期神经发育预测提供了一种结构化、可解释的连续时间建模方案。

Abstract: Understanding how individual brain development unfolds over childhood requires modeling developmental trajectories from sparse longitudinal observations. Long-term neurodevelopmental forecasting is challenging because each child is typically observed at only a few irregularly spaced visits, while developmental dynamics vary across individuals and age. Generic continuous-time models accommodate irregular timing but often absorb these factors into a single flexible transition function, providing little structure for how population progression, individual variability, and developmental age shape the dynamics. We propose DevelopmentODE, a structured continuous-time framework that organizes population- and subject-specific variation within a shared developmental geometry while allowing the governing dynamics to evolve with age. The model builds this geometry around a developmental canal representing the population trajectory, whose local direction provides a reference for organizing subject-specific variation. Subject deviation velocities are constrained relative to this direction, while a shared nonlinear deviation field captures individual developmental motion without disrupting population-level progression. DevelopmentODE further models developmental non-stationarity through ordered age-dependent deformations of the shared vector field, progressively adapting a common dynamical structure as age changes, while elapsed time determines the integration horizon. This formulation uses population-level developmental structure to guide learning from sparse individual trajectories while allowing dynamics to evolve smoothly with age. We evaluate DevelopmentODE on longitudinal fMRI by predicting future functional connectivity of the same child from earlier observations. DevelopmentODE consistently outperforms competing baselines across short- and long-horizon predictions.

</details>


### [357] [Deep Learning Techniques for Phoneme Recognition in Italian Children' s Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33060)
*Nicola Barbaro,Cristina Gena,Francesco Petriglia,Andrea Meirone,Alessandro Mazzei,Arianna Viotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Broca系统，基于Conformer，预训练8天成人语音并微调165分钟意大利儿童语音数据，在国际音标转写任务上取得13.36%的加权音素错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 言语治疗师在诊断障碍时缺乏高效工具将语音转写为国际音标（IPA），儿童语音存在音调、口音和语音错误等变异，且临床场景数据资源有限。

Method: Broca系统采用Conformer架构，先在8天成人语音上预训练，再在165分钟意大利儿童语音数据集（来自3.5-6.5岁儿童标准化诊断测试）上微调，并针对儿童语音的音调、口音和语音错误等语音变异性进行优化。

Result: 在意大利语上取得最先进的13.36%加权音素错误率（Phoneme Error Rate），且仅使用不到3小时的儿童特定数据。

Conclusion: 准确的、词汇无关的语音到IPA转写可在极少量数据下实现，为更可及、数据高效的语音评估与诊断工具奠定基础。

Abstract: Speech therapists often face difficulties diagnosing impairments due to the lack of efficient tools for transcribing speech into the International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA). This work addresses this challenge with Broca, a Conformer-based deep learning system pretrained on 8 days of adult speech and fine-tuned on a 165-minute dataset of Italian child speech collected through a range of standardized diagnostic tests for children aged 3.5-6.5. Broca was optimized to handle phonetic variability in children's speech, including tone, accent, and speech errors, and achieved a state-of-the-art weighted Phoneme Error Rate of 13.36% on Italian speech. Remarkably, this performance was obtained using less than three hours of child-specific data, underscoring the model's efficiency and robustness in low-resource clinical settings. This work demonstrates that accurate, vocabulary-independent speech-to-IPA transcription can be achieved with minimal data, paving the way for more accessible, data-efficient tools to support speech assessment and diagnosis.

</details>


### [358] [Zero-Storage Procedural Neural Synthesis via Boundary Dynamics: Formal Verification in Lean 4 and Bare-Metal Gauntlet Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33066)
*Volkan Dağlı,Zerrin Dağlı,Dağhan Dağlı*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对WERR（Waves & Errors）与Phase III轨道误差动力学（OED）这一非张量决策范式进行了形式化验证与裸机实证，利用Mandelbrot集边界上的24字节坐标种子按需程序化合成非线性决策边界，在Lean 4中机器验证了10条定理，并在多核服务器上完成了CPU内核的性能与鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代神经推理依赖存储于高带宽显存中的稠密浮点权重矩阵，导致严重的内存墙瓶颈，且无法在EVM等确定性虚拟机中本地执行；同时，连续域上递归动力系统的终止性与算术不变量验证在BSS模型下一般不可判定。因此需要一种无需张量、无需显存且可形式化验证的决策范式。

Method: 将递推 z_{n+1}=z_n^2+c 投影到模剩余环 Z/9Z 与定点域 Q_{16.16}，基于24字节坐标种子 Θ=(c_x,c_y,zoom) 沿 Mandelbrot 集边界程序化合成非线性决策边界；在 Lean 4（v4.34.1）与 Mathlib4 中建立10条机器验证定理，无未证明猜想（sorry）；并在40核双路Intel Xeon服务器上运行向量化36次迭代CPU内核。

Result: 在Lean 4中证明了 I_3={0,3,6} 的理想封闭性、通用燃料受限停机（≤9与≤12步）、Q_{16.16} 下低于 2^{63}-1 时无平方溢出、非恒定边界逃逸敏感性以及参数化EVM gas界（≤22,557≤24,000 gas）。实测CPU内核100,000次决策耗时6.49秒（15,397决策/秒，0字节VRAM，15.15倍加速），S型离群门控抑制100.00%对抗性尖峰并保留89.60%干净基线信号。

Conclusion: WERR/OED 提供了一种可形式化验证、零显存占用且可在EVM内执行的非张量决策范式，兼具机器验证的数学保证与实测的高吞吐和强鲁棒性，为规避内存墙与确定性虚拟机兼容问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Contemporary neural inference architectures rely on dense floating-point weight matrices stored in high-bandwidth memory (VRAM), incurring severe memory-wall bottlenecks and preventing native execution inside deterministic virtual machines like the Ethereum Virtual Machine (EVM). Verifying termination and arithmetic invariants for recursive dynamical systems over continuous domains is generally undecidable in the Blum-Shub-Smale model. Here, we present the formal verification and bare-metal empirical validation of WERR (Waves & Errors) and Phase III Orbital Error Dynamics (OED), a non-tensor decision paradigm that procedurally synthesizes non-linear decision boundaries on demand from a 24-byte coordinate seed $Θ= (c_x, c_y, \text{zoom})$ along the boundary of the Mandelbrot set ($\partial\mathcal{M}$). By projecting the recurrence $z_{n+1} = z_n^2 + c$ onto the modular residue ring $\mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z}$ and the fixed-point domain $\mathbb{Q}_{16.16}$, we establish ten machine-verified theorems in Lean 4 (v4.34.1) with Mathlib4 and zero unproven conjectures (sorry): proving $\mathcal{I}_3 = \{0,3,6\} \subset \mathbb{Z}/9\mathbb{Z}$ ideal closure, universal fuel-bounded halting ($\le 9$ and $\le 12$ steps), absence of $\mathbb{Q}_{16.16}$ square overflow below $2^{63}-1$, non-constant boundary escape sensitivity, and a parametric EVM gas bound ($\le 22,557 \le 24,000$ gas). Evaluated on a 40-core Dual Intel Xeon server, the vectorized 36-iteration CPU kernel processes 100,000 decisions in 6.49 s (15,397 decisions/s, 0 Bytes VRAM, 15.15x speedup), while a sigmoidal outlier gate suppresses 100.00% of adversarial spikes while preserving 89.60% of clean baseline signals.

</details>


### [359] [The limits of exactness: On the failure of automatic differentiation in physics-informed machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33078)
*Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自动微分（AD）虽能精确计算机器精度导数，但数学上的精确不等于物理上的忠实；AD缺乏对物理结构（如对流方向性、扩散、守恒、对称性等）的认知，可能给出"数值完美却错误"的导数。这一认识为构建下一代PDE驱动的神经代理模型提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD已成为物理信息机器学习的核心计算工具，因其能为控制方程提供机器精度导数。然而作者指出"数学意义上的精确"与"物理保真度"并不等同，该混淆可能导致在求解PDE问题时使用物理上错误的导数，因此需要重新审视AD在物理建模中的根本局限。

Method: 这是一篇观点性（position/argument）论文。作者通过概念论证的方式，指出AD在构造上不具备对解必须遵循的物理结构的感知能力，并系统列举跨越各计算科学与工程分支的物理属性清单（对流方向性、扩散、色散、守恒、热力学一致性、对称性、辛结构、正性、单调性、有界性等）来支撑论点。

Result: 论文未提供数值实验或定量结果。其论点结果为：对流及其方向性、扩散与色散只是更广泛物理结构缺失中最显著的实例；这一问题贯穿所有计算科学与工程分支，说明这是一个系统性、根本性的缺口，而非个别案例。

Conclusion: 认识到AD与物理保真度之间这一更广泛的差距，将重新定义该领域如何构建下一代PDE驱动的神经代理模型——未来模型设计需要显式纳入物理结构约束，而不能仅依赖AD的数学精确性。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation (AD) lets neural networks compute derivatives of governing equations to machine precision, and this precision has made it the computational backbone of physics-informed machine learning. Yet exactness in the mathematical sense is not the same as fidelity to the physics. Here I argue that a derivative can be numerically perfect and still be the wrong derivative for the problem at hand, because AD, by construction, has no notion of the physical structure a solution must obey. Convection and its associated directionality, diffusion, and dispersion are only the most visible instances of a much longer list that spans all branches of computational science and engineering, including conservation, thermodynamic consistency, symmetry, symplectic structure, positivity, monotonicity, and boundedness. Recognizing this broader gap reframes how the field should build the next generation of PDE-driven neural surrogates.

</details>


### [360] [From Constitutions to Control: Interpretable Rewards for Aligning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33086)
*Johann D. Gaebler,Calvin Isley,Max Lamparth,Stephen Casper,Sharad Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于评分标准（rubric）的对齐框架，将通用宪法转化为可解释、可调优的奖励模型，通过重加权各维度实现对模型行为的精准控制，并能缓解偏好标签中的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法存在不足：基于偏好的方法将多种考量合并为整体人类判断，难以知晓奖励来源、难以针对性调整；基于原则的方法虽指定高层价值观，却未充分将其操作化。因此需要一个既透明又可调控的奖励机制。

Method: 构建基于rubric的框架，将通用宪法转化为可解释、可调优的奖励模型；利用宪法引导的AI反馈为各rubric条目估计初始权重；随后对这些维度进行重加权，构造修改后的训练奖励。

Result: 在政治对齐与安全-有用性权衡实验中，重加权单个维度能可预测地、基本独立地改变目标行为，并在冲突的对齐目标间进行权衡；同一框架还能通过降低偏好判断中标签偏见（如谄媚与人口统计偏见）的影响来缓解这些偏见。

Conclusion: 由宪法衍生的可解释奖励能够将高层对齐原则转化为更透明、更可控的模型行为。

Abstract: Current approaches to aligning language models often make it hard to know what behavior is being rewarded or to change that reward in a targeted way. In particular, standard preference-based methods collapse multiple considerations into aggregate human judgments, obscuring what drives the resulting reward, while principle-based methods specify high-level values without fully operationalizing them. To address this gap, we develop a rubric-based framework to transform a general-purpose constitution into an interpretable and tunable reward model, using constitution-guided AI feedback to estimate initial weights for the constituent rubric items. We then reweight those dimensions to construct modified rewards for training. Across experiments on political alignment and safety-helpfulness tradeoffs, reweighting individual dimensions predictably changes targeted behaviors largely independently while navigating tradeoffs between conflicting alignment objectives. We show that the same framework can mitigate label bias encoded in preference judgments -- including sycophancy and demographic bias -- by reducing their influence on the training reward. Our results demonstrate that constitution-derived, interpretable rewards can translate high-level alignment principles into more transparent and controllable model behavior.

</details>


### [361] [Geometry-Aware Operator Families for Structured Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33089)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Geometry-Induced Operator Families (GIOF) 框架，将固定几何结构转化为结构化传播算子族，并依据上下文动态选择算子，在多个理论与实验中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经架构多依赖通用算子模板或针对特定几何的构造，缺乏一个统一框架，能从固定结构信息中直接推导合法算子，同时适应变化的上下文。几何作为潜在表征的基础，决定了组件交互与信息传播方式，因此几何感知的算子设计是结构化深度表征学习的关键。

Method: GIOF 首先将几何导出的交互通道转换为可复用的生成器基，然后通过上下文相关的选择器和自适应传播尺度进行组合，最后借助稳定的连续时间传播与瓶颈残差层实现所选算子。

Result: 理论层面给出了参数压缩、可识别性、稳定性、局部性、组合结构与过平滑行为等保证；受控实验验证了相关机制；在 PEMS-BAY 和 METR-LA 上，于所有报告的区域性中断设置中取得最低平均 MAE，在 30% 传感器缺失时相较最强保留基线提升 2.4%--8.8%。

Conclusion: GIOF 提供了一个统一、几何感知且上下文自适应的算子设计框架，兼具理论保证与实证优势，适用于结构化深度表征学习及区域中断场景下的时空预测。

Abstract: The geometry of latent representations governs which components should interact and how information should propagate, making geometry-aware operator design a fundamental ingredient of structured deep representation learning. However, existing neural architectures typically rely on generic operator templates or geometry-specific constructions, creating a need for a unified framework that can derive admissible operators directly from fixed structural information while remaining adaptive to changing contexts. We introduce \emph{Geometry-Induced Operator Families} (GIOF), a general framework that converts fixed geometry into a structured family of propagation operators and dynamically selects an appropriate member of this family according to the current context. GIOF first transforms geometry-derived interaction channels into reusable generator bases, then combines them through a context-dependent selector and adaptive propagation scale, and finally realizes the selected operator through stable continuous-time propagation and a bottleneck residual layer. We establish theoretical guarantees covering parameter compression, identifiability, stability, locality, compositional structure, and oversmoothing behavior, while controlled experiments validate these mechanisms and experiments on PEMS-BAY and METR-LA achieve the lowest mean MAE across all reported regional-outage settings, improving over the strongest retained baseline by 2.4\%--8.8\% at 30\% missing sensors.

</details>


### [362] [How Linear Attention Remembers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33093)
*Kichang Lee,JaeYeon Park,Songkuk Kim,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将线性注意力的固定大小循环状态视为一种记忆系统，通过分解分析与因果干预，揭示其选择性回忆机制及其与KV记忆的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定大小的循环状态替代标准注意力中不断增长的KV缓存，虽降低内存增长，但改变了历史信息的存储方式：许多token必须共享并反复更新同一记忆。作者希望弄清这种循环状态作为记忆系统是如何工作的。

Method: 采用解析分解方法，并在预训练的GLA与GDN模型上进行受控因果干预，追踪被回忆信息的写入、保持与访问过程。

Result: 事实特定信息通过集中的、内容依赖的写入进入循环记忆，并通过集中的查询时读取路径被访问；多个事实可在同一状态中保持选择性可访问，但其内部表示存在跨事实因果耦合而非独立KV式存储；随记忆负载增加，回忆与定向编辑能力均退化，而单纯上下文长度的影响较小；后续写入的因果干预表明干扰由与既有记忆的重叠程度决定；在混合架构中，支持回忆的运行时记忆主要转移到全注意力的KV状态。

Conclusion: 固定大小循环记忆在共享存储下仍能支持选择性回忆，但也暴露出与token可寻址KV记忆不同的干扰与容量限制。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the growing key--value (KV) cache of standard attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, substantially reducing memory growth with context length. This efficiency, however, changes how past information is stored: many tokens must share and repeatedly update the same memory. We study how this recurrent state functions as a memory system. Using an analytical decomposition together with controlled causal interventions in pretrained GLA and GDN models, we trace how recalled information is written, retained, and later accessed. We find that fact-specific information enters recurrent memory through concentrated, content-dependent writes and is later accessed through concentrated query-time read pathways. Multiple facts can remain selectively accessible within the same state, yet their internal representations exhibit cross-fact causal coupling rather than independent KV-like storage. As memory load increases, both recall and targeted editability degrade, whereas elapsed context alone has a substantially smaller effect within the tested regime. Causal interventions on subsequent writes further show that interference is shaped by their overlap with existing memory. Finally, in hybrid architectures that combine recurrent layers with full attention, the runtime memory directly supporting recall shifts predominantly to the full-attention KV state. Together, these results reveal how fixed-size recurrent memory supports selective recall despite shared storage, while exposing the interference and capacity limits that distinguish it from token-addressable KV memory.

</details>


### [363] [CARVE: Breaking Data Barriers in Chip Placement by Harnessing Reusable Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33106)
*Jiefu Zhang,Haixiang Sun,Yang Xu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Zishen Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARVE 将累积经验表示为冻结基策略、不可变专家与经本地验证的任务凭据，实现专家复用；宏布局中复用优先流程将训练时间减少 58.5%，HPWL 收益仅从 8.41% 降至 7.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练宏布局策略可减少跨电路重复优化，但部署时常遇到新设计且原始训练数据不可用；反复微调单一服务模型会覆盖早期改进，而仅保存检查点又无法确定其可靠复用位置，因此需要一种可验证、可复用累积经验的持续适应框架。

Method: 提出 CARVE 框架，将累积经验组织为冻结基策略、不可变专家以及通过本地验证获得的任务特定凭据。面对新任务，先检查现有专家，仅当无专家合格时才从冻结基策略训练新专家。在固定任务分布和可控制累积误差的验证规则下，建立重复复用的期望性能保证；对有界损失推导可靠复用所需本地样本的匹配最坏情况界。

Result: 在宏布局任务中，复用优先的后续流程将记录训练时间从 9.66 小时降至 4.01 小时，减少 58.5%，而平均 HPWL 增益仅从 8.41% 变为 7.86%。在模拟接收部署中，导入的专家在七个新 IBM 电路中的六个上被复用，无需接收方训练，实现 5.76% 的平均 HPWL 增益。导航研究为修复保留和重复适应提供了补充证据。

Conclusion: CARVE 通过冻结基策略、不可变专家和本地验证凭据，在几乎不损失性能的情况下显著降低重复训练成本，并支持无需接收方训练的知识复用，为宏布局等任务的持续适应提供了可验证的复用机制与理论保证。

Abstract: Pretrained macro-placement policies can reduce repeated optimization across circuits, but deployment often exposes them to unfamiliar designs when the original training data are unavailable. Repeatedly fine-tuning a single serving model can overwrite earlier improvements, while simply saving checkpoints does not determine where they can be reliably reused. We introduce Continual Adaptation through the Reuse of Validated Expertise (CARVE), a framework that represents accumulated expertise as a frozen base policy, immutable specialists, and task-specific credentials obtained through local validation. For a new task, CARVE first checks existing specialists and trains a new specialist from the frozen base only when none qualifies. Under fixed task distributions and validation rules that control cumulative error, we establish expected-performance guarantees for repeated reuse. For bounded losses, we also derive matching worst-case bounds on the local samples needed for reliable reuse. In macro placement, a reuse-first follow-up reduces recorded training time by 58.5% (9.66 to 4.01 hours), while mean HPWL gain changes only from 8.41% to 7.86%. In a simulated receiving deployment, imported specialists are reused on six of seven new IBM circuits with no receiver-side training, achieving a 5.76% mean HPWL gain. Navigation studies provide complementary evidence on repair retention and repeated adaptation.

</details>


### [364] [D-JEPA: Design-Recoverable JEPA Representation with Swappable Physics Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33110)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Aashwin Anand Mishra,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-JEPA，以几何为中心的JEPA框架，从几何单独学习可复用表示，通过轻量物理解码器跨工况与响应空间复用，实现设计参数近乎完美的线性可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法在参数化物理系统中，学到的表示会将几何与工况、任务特定的物理响应纠缠在一起，限制了跨预测任务的复用能力，且不利于设计分析与优化。

Method: D-JEPA仅从几何计算紧凑表示，并用轻量级物理专用解码器在不同工况与物理响应空间复用；引入显式的"设计可恢复性"目标，使几何潜变量保留底层设计变量信息；同时识别出case-level collapse失效模式，通过case-level变化约束与辅助目标重建来缓解。

Result: 在四个3D气动、水动力与结构基准上，D-JEPA保持或提升了全场预测精度，同时实现设计参数的近乎完美线性可恢复性；冻结的几何表示可复用于未见工况并迁移到结构响应任务，且可训练参数更少；表示还支持可微设计优化，经高保真CFD验证，能保持候选设计的预测排序。

Conclusion: 将可复用的几何表示与物理特定预测分离，为科学代理建模与设计提供了一种实用的表示方案。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide a framework for learning compact representations without directly reconstructing high-dimensional observations. However, in parameterized physical systems, learned representations can entangle geometry with operating conditions and task-specific physical responses, limiting their reuse across prediction tasks. We introduce D-JEPA (Design-recoverable JEPA), a geometry-centric JEPA that computes a compact representation from geometry alone and reuses it across operating conditions and physical response spaces through lightweight physics-specific decoders. An explicit design-recoverability objective encourages the geometry latent to preserve information about the underlying design variables, enabling the representation to support design analysis and optimization. We further identify a case-level collapse failure mode in which target representations become nearly invariant across distinct geometries despite low reconstruction error, and mitigate it using case-level variation constraints and auxiliary target reconstruction. Across four 3D aerodynamic, hydrodynamic, and structural benchmarks, D-JEPA maintains or improves full-field prediction accuracy while achieving near-perfect linear recoverability of design parameters. The frozen geometry representation can be reused at held-out operating conditions and transferred to a structural response task with fewer trainable parameters. Finally, the representation supports differentiable design optimization, with designs validated using high-fidelity CFD, preserving the predicted ranking of candidate designs. These results demonstrate that separating a reusable geometry representation from physics-specific prediction provides a practical representation for scientific surrogate modeling and design.

</details>


### [365] [Simulation-Free Learning of GP-SDEs from Irregular Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33112)
*Zhidi Lin,Yuhao Liu,Ying Li,Edwin Fong,Petar Djurić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GP-SDE Matching，一种无模拟的变分框架，用于贝叶斯 GP 漂移学习与连续时间状态平滑，可处理噪声且不规则的观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-SDE 能对未知连续时间状态动态进行贝叶斯建模并提供不确定性量化，但从噪声和不规则观测中学习与推断在计算上仍具挑战性。

Method: 通过解析地边缘化稀疏 GP 后验，推导出可处理的漂移匹配目标，同时考虑未知漂移的后验均值与不确定性；并引入感知不规则时间的变分状态后验，在编码与连续时间边缘查询中融入真实观测时间，实现无模拟的变分推断。

Result: 在随机 Lorenz-63 系统上，不规则观测下的漂移恢复与状态重建显著提升；在五个系统辨识基准上，随观测稀疏度增加仍保持稳健预测，性能与现有潜 SDE 及状态空间方法相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: GP-SDE Matching 为贝叶斯 GP 漂移学习与连续时间状态平滑提供了高效且有效的无模拟变分方案，尤其适合噪声、不规则及稀疏观测场景。

Abstract: Gaussian process stochastic differential equations (GP-SDEs) provide a flexible Bayesian model for unknown continuous-time state dynamics with uncertainty quantification, but learning and inference from noisy and irregular observations remain computationally challenging. To address this issue, we propose GP-SDE Matching, a simulation-free variational framework for Bayesian GP drift learning and continuous-time state smoothing. We analytically marginalize the sparse GP posterior to derive a tractable drift-matching objective that accounts for both the posterior mean and uncertainty of the unknown drift. To handle irregular observations, we further introduce an irregular-time-aware variational state posterior that incorporates the actual observation times during both encoding and continuous-time marginal querying. Experiments on the stochastic Lorenz--63 system demonstrate substantially improved drift recovery and state reconstruction under irregular observations, while five system identification benchmarks show robust forecasting under increasing observation sparsity and competitive performance against existing latent-SDE and state-space methods.

</details>


### [366] [ECG-Scroll: A Long-Horizon, Streaming Benchmark and Agent Environment for Interpretation of Ambulatory Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33117)
*Haitao Li,Chenglin Li,Zhengyao Ding,Ziyu Li,Yiheng Mao,Zhengxing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ECG-Scroll，将长时程动态心电图判读重构为长时程、在线（流式、因果）序贯决策过程，构建包含390个完整记录、2536小时双导联动态心电的基准与环境，评估流式场景下智能体的记忆、工具使用与规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM已能解读标准十秒十二导联心电图，但真实心脏监测（动态Holter与遥测）时长数小时至数天，需在线流式判读，关键发现常为短暂阵发性事件，淹没于平淡波形中。此类记录无法以诊断分辨率放入单一上下文，且未来尚未发生，判读者必须在线决策：此刻测量什么、将证据写入记忆、事件发生时即时报告。批量离线评估无法刻画这种在线因果场景。

Method: 将长时程心电解读重铸为长时程、在线（流式、因果）序贯决策过程，提出ECG-Scroll。基准以分块方式将长时程动态记录流式输入智能体，要求其在无未来信号的前提下定位、量化并即时标记阵发性事件；因保留原始信号，所有答案可与客观真值核验，采用规则式而非评判式奖励；并引入批量评估无法表达的指标——事件起点到智能体记录时刻的检测延迟。作为智能体环境，它是固定gym风格交互层，考察三种能力：记忆、基于信号测量的工具使用（而非读取像素）、以及“此刻测什么、何时提交”的规划。

Result: 发布390个完整记录实例，覆盖2536小时双导联动态心电；在线评估了信号阈值规则智能体与现成LLM智能体，刻画其如何使用记忆、工具与规划，并指出基准的剩余提升空间。

Conclusion: ECG-Scroll将长时程心电图判读从批量离线范式推进到在线流式序贯决策范式，提供可核验的规则奖励与检测延迟指标，为评估智能体的记忆、信号级工具使用与规划能力建立了新基准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can now interpret a standard ten-second, twelve-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) with clinically grounded, reward-verified reasoning. Real cardiac monitoring is different. Ambulatory (Holter) and telemetry recordings span hours to days and are read as they stream in, and their clinically decisive findings are paroxysmal, brief episodes buried in an otherwise unremarkable trace. Such a recording cannot be held in one context at diagnostic resolution, and its future has not yet happened, so a reader must work online, deciding what to measure now, committing evidence to memory as it passes, and reporting events as they occur. We recast long-duration ECG interpretation as a long-horizon, online (streaming, causal) sequential decision process and introduce ECG-Scroll. As a benchmark, long ambulatory recordings are streamed to an agent chunk by chunk, and it must localize, quantify, and promptly flag paroxysmal events without access to future signal; because the underlying signal is retained, every answer is checkable against objective ground truth, giving rule-based rather than judge-based rewards, and the streaming formulation adds a metric batch evaluation cannot express, the detection latency between an event's onset and the moment the agent records it. As an agent environment, it is a fixed, gym-style interaction layer that exercises three competencies single-glance ECG models never touch: Memory, Tool use through signal-grounded measurement rather than reading pixels, and Planning of what to measure now and when to commit. We release 390 whole-recording instances spanning 2,536 hours of two-lead ambulatory ECG and evaluate a signal-threshold rule agent alongside off-the-shelf LLM agents online, characterizing how they use memory, tools, and planning and where the benchmark's head-room lies.

</details>


### [367] [Mycelium: A Generalizable Cross-Grid Multi-Task Model for Electrical Distribution Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33120)
*Zhengyang Wei,Shourya Bose,Helgi Hilmarsson,Elena Carnio,Dhruv Suri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对配电网络推理中测量稀疏、含噪、不完整及网络异构的问题，提出统一电网本体（将变规模配网表示为异构图）与物理数据仿真流程，并开发了异构图Transformer模型Mycelium，在未见过的基准网络上多数指标优于任务专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络运行需要在异构网络间进行推理，但时间序列测量稀疏、含噪且不完整。作者希望构建一个统一模型，既能完成根植于电网物理的多样任务，又能泛化到未见过的配电网络，这是统一电网模型面临的重要挑战。

Method: 1) 定义统一电网本体，将变规模配网表示为异构图，保留原生拓扑、设备类型和电气关系；2) 构建基于物理的数据仿真流程，结合参考网络与程序化生成网络、网络重构、故障场景及可配置的传感条件；3) 提出Mycelium——一种异构图Transformer，含结构感知的通信边、编码网络位置与标称相位方向的电气参考特征，以及生成各任务输出的任务专用时序读出模块；4) 在参考与合成电网上训练，并在完全排除于训练和验证之外的基准网络上研究泛化。

Result: 在多数报告的基准指标上，Mycelium性能优于任务专用神经网络基线。架构消融实验及上述研究揭示了Mycelium能够学习底层物理的表征，从而提升跨任务性能。

Conclusion: Mycelium通过学习电网底层物理表征增强了跨任务表现，为统一电网模型的关键挑战提供了可行方案，并展现出对未见过配电网络的泛化能力。

Abstract: Electrical distribution grid operations require inference across heterogeneous networks from sparse, noisy, and incomplete time series measurements. In this work, we identify challenges and explore solutions towards a unified model that can perform diverse tasks grounded in the physics of the electric grid and generalize to unseen distribution networks. We define a unified grid ontology that represents variable sized distribution networks as heterogeneous graphs while preserving native topology, asset types, and electrical relationships across networks. We develop a physics based data simulation pipeline that combines reference and procedurally generated distribution networks with network reconfigurations, fault scenarios, and configurable sensing conditions. We present Mycelium, a heterogeneous graph transformer with structure aware communication edges and electrical reference features that encode network position and nominal phase orientation, together with task specific temporal readouts which generate per task outputs. We train Mycelium on reference as well as synthetic grids, and study its generalization on benchmark networks completely excluded from training and validation. Mycelium is observed to outperform task specific neural baselines on most reported benchmark metrics. Architectural ablations and the aforementioned studies reveal Mycelium's capability to learn representations of the underlying physics which serves to enhance cross-task performance, thereby addressing a significant challenge in unified grid models.

</details>


### [368] [Save Your Saturated Data: Learning Beyond Reward Saturation in Group-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33126)
*Ziyuan Yang,Yike Wang,Shangbin Feng,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究如何在奖励饱和数据上恢复有用的强化学习信号，发现在rollout层面注入"高质量但错误"的负样本最有效，可将GRPO性能提升6.4%–9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型能力增强，同一问题的多个采样回答可能都获得同样高的奖励，导致组相对强化学习（如GRPO）的优势估计趋近于0、学习信号消失，使原本有用的训练数据变得过时。作者希望探究能否从这类"奖励饱和"数据中重新提取有效的学习信号。

Method: 在组策略RL流程的四个层面（数据、rollout、奖励、优势）设计干预措施，并仅使用饱和的推理数据进行大规模RL训练。核心策略是在rollout生成阶段诱导模型产出"高质量但错误"的解，从而向饱和组中引入低奖励的负样本来恢复非零优势；同时对比了提高rollout温度、添加辅助奖励等其他干预。

Result: 标准GRPO在饱和数据上几乎总是产生接近0的优势和近似噪声的信号；而各类干预能成功回收利用这些数据。其中rollout生成层面的干预效果最稳定最有效，在Qwen3-1.7B和4B上使GRPO提升6.4%至9.0%。提高温度或添加辅助奖励也能恢复非零优势，但收益不够一致。分析还表明有效的负样本需要具备信息量的负轨迹，该方法在混合非饱和数据时依然有效，并支持对新饱和样本的迭代回收。

Conclusion: 不要浪费你的饱和数据——在数据日益稀缺的背景下，通过恰当的策略可以将奖励饱和数据回收为有用的RL训练信号，且rollout层面的负样本注入是最优方案。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning (RL) relies on reward variation among sampled responses to estimate informative relative advantages. As language models become increasingly capable, existing training data can become reward-saturated: all sampled responses to the same problem might receive equally high rewards, where the group-relative learning signals vanish and leave previously useful data obsolete. In this work, we investigate whether useful learning signals can be recovered from such saturated data. We study interventions at four levels of group-policy RL pipelines---data, rollout, reward, and advantage---and conduct extensive RL training on saturated reasoning data only. While standard GRPO on saturated data would almost always yield near-0 advantages and near-noise signals, diverse interventions successfully recycle and repurpose such data: among the proposed strategies, interventions at rollout generation are consistently most effective: nudging the policy to generate ``high-quality'', incorrect solutions introduces rollouts with poor rewards into saturated groups as negative samples, which turns out to improve GRPO by 6.4% to 9.0% across Qwen3-1.7B and 4B. Other interventions such as increasing rollout temperature or adding auxiliary rewards can also restore non-zero advantages, but yield less consistent gains. Further analyses show that effective negative rollouts require informative negative trajectories, that the method remains effective alongside unsaturated data, and that it supports iterative recycling of newly saturated examples. While increasingly stronger LLMs would render more data as saturated, our results demonstrate that don't waste your saturated data: with the right strategies they can be recycled into useful RL training signals in an increasingly data-scarce world.

</details>


### [369] [Policy Plasticity Matters in Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning: Refitting Offline Policies for Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33127)
*Yuheng Huang,Yunpeng Qing,Yixiao Chi,Yilun Kong,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从网络可塑性角度研究离线到在线强化学习(O2O RL)的迁移问题，发现静态离线数据上的长期优化会在性能饱和后逐渐降低网络可塑性，进而削弱在线提升能力；提出轻量级方法REFIT，通过"新鲜初始化"与策略蒸馏恢复可塑性，在D4RL与OGBench上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O2O方法主要通过价值校准来处理离线到在线的过渡，普遍将离线训练得到的策略视为给定的初始化而不加质疑。本文质疑该假设：离线策略是否仍具备足够的可塑性以供在线学习？受控实验表明，在静态离线数据上过度优化虽使离线性能饱和，却持续降低网络可塑性，且可塑性越低，后续在线提升越弱。因此需要一种显式恢复可塑性的方法。

Method: 提出REFIT(REstoring plasticity via Fresh Initialization and policy Transfer)，一种轻量级的模型层面方法，用于O2O过渡阶段。在在线微调之前，将离线策略蒸馏到一个全新初始化的学生网络中，并在蒸馏过程中临时冻结学生网络的一个随机子集单元，从而把学到的离线行为迁移到一个更具可塑性的策略初始化上。

Result: 在D4RL和OGBench上的大量实验表明，REFIT在Cal-QL与IQL两种骨干网络上，均持续取得高于现有O2O即插即用方法的总体性能；可塑性诊断与消融实验进一步为网络可塑性的恢复提供了证据。

Conclusion: REFIT表明，将O2O适配问题视为可塑性恢复问题而非单纯的价值校准是有效的；通过新鲜初始化加策略蒸馏，能够在不改变原有算法框架的前提下作为轻量插件提升离线到在线迁移的最终性能，并保持网络的可塑性。

Abstract: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning (O2O RL) has emerged as a practical paradigm that pre-trains the policy using static offline datasets and subsequently adapts the policy through online interactions. Existing O2O methods primarily address the transition through value calibration, while generally treating the offline-trained policy as a given initialization. We instead study O2O adaptation from the perspective of network plasticity, asking whether the offline-trained policy remains sufficiently adaptable for online learning. Controlled experiments show that prolonged optimization on static offline data progressively reduces network plasticity even after offline performance has largely saturated, and that lower plasticity is associated with weaker subsequent online improvement. Motivated by these observations, we propose REstoring plasticity via Fresh Initialization and policy Transfer (REFIT), a lightweight model-level method for the O2O transition. Before online fine-tuning, REFIT distills the offline policy into a freshly initialized student while temporarily freezing a random subset of student units, transferring the learned offline behavior to a more plastic policy initialization. Extensive experiments on D4RL and OGBench demonstrate that REFIT consistently achieves higher aggregate performance than existing O2O plug-in methods across both Cal-QL and IQL backbones, while plasticity diagnostics and ablations provide further evidence of restored network plasticity.

</details>


### [370] [Flow-Matching-Based Protein Structure Tokenizer Made Efficient and Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33129)
*Zhe Zhang,Yikai Zhang,Jiangtao Feng,Ya-Qin Zhang,Wei-Ying Ma,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProFiT 是一种轻量级流匹配蛋白质结构分词器，无需复杂训练目标和手动语义对齐即可高效训练，并在重建质量与泛化性上匹配或超越更大模型，可作为即插即用分词器适配多种下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构分词方法连接蛋白质模态与离散建模时，过度依赖为特定下游任务量身定制的复杂训练目标和大规模训练数据集，导致难以迁移到更广泛的应用场景。因此作者希望提出一种更轻量、训练简单且泛化性强的方案。

Method: 提出 ProFiT，一种轻量级流匹配（flow matching）分词器。通过简单的训练策略促进码本的健康利用，从而高效训练，并在无需任何人工语义对齐的情况下自然学习到语义有意义的表示。

Result: ProFiT 的重建质量和泛化能力匹配或超越规模显著更大的分词器。作者在广泛设置下进行了评估，证明其可作为即插即用分词器适配多种下游任务。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了流匹配分词器范式的巨大潜力，代码已公开。

Abstract: As the bridge between protein modality and discrete modeling, protein structure tokenization still largely relies on heavily engineered training objectives tailored to specific downstream tasks and large training datasets, which hinders its transfer to broader application scenarios. To address this issue, we propose ProFiT, a lightweight flow matching tokenizer. With simple training strategies that encourage healthy codebook utilization, ProFiT can be trained efficiently and naturally learns semantically meaningful representations without any manual semantic alignment, while achieving reconstruction quality and generalization that match or surpass those of substantially larger tokenizers. We conduct extensive evaluations across a wide range of settings and demonstrate that ProFiT is a plug-and-play tokenizer adaptable to diverse downstream tasks. This study further reveals the significant potential of the flow matching tokenizer paradigm. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/QDKStorm/ProFiT.

</details>


### [371] [ILP-BO: Integer Linear Programming-Based Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33131)
*Hyakka Nakada,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ILP-BO，将离散域上基于核的代理优化精确转化为整数线性规划问题，实现带最优性证书的候选选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑箱优化（如贝叶斯优化）在优化基于代理模型的采集准则时，通常依赖非线性优化或启发式搜索，无法保证候选选择的全局最优性。

Method: 通过引入二元one-hot辅助变量，在有限离散距离层级上精确表示非线性核函数，把非线性代理转为带线性约束的线性目标；进一步引入汉明距离边距以排除已观测点邻域来保持探索性并维持线性结构；对多种标准核推导该形式，并基于二值搜索空间测度给出汉明距离阈值的解析上界；最终用整数规划求解器求解候选选择问题并获得最优性证书。

Result: 在合成与离散优化基准实验上，ILP-BO与实用贝叶斯优化方法相比取得了具有竞争力的优化性能。

Conclusion: ILP-BO是一种准贝叶斯优化框架，使候选选择可获最优性证书，有望成为一种高度透明的黑箱优化框架。

Abstract: Black-box Optimization (BO) is a powerful framework for optimizing expensive objective functions or unknown functions with a limited number of evaluations. A central step of standard BO such as Bayesian optimization is the optimization of a surrogate-based acquisition criterion, which is commonly performed using nonlinear optimization or heuristic search. Therefore, conventional black-box optimization generally does not guarantee global optimality in candidate selection. In this study, we propose Integer Linear Programming-based Black-box Optimization (ILP-BO), a quasi-Bayesian optimization framework that transforms kernel-based surrogate optimization over discrete domains into an Integer Linear Programming (ILP) problem. The key idea is to represent nonlinear kernel functions exactly on finite discrete distance levels by introducing binary one-hot auxiliary variables. This transformation converts the nonlinear surrogate into a linear objective with linear constraints and binary variables. To incorporate exploration while preserving the linear structure, we further introduce a Hamming-distance margin that excludes neighborhoods around previously observed points. We derive the proposed formulation for several standard kernels and obtain an analytical upper bound on the Hamming-distance threshold based on the measure in the binary search space. The resulting candidate-selection problem can be solved by integer programming solvers with certificates of optimality. Thus, our methodology has the potential to serve as a highly transparent black-box optimization framework. Experiments on synthetic and discrete optimization benchmarks show that ILP-BO achieves competitive optimization performance compared with practical Bayesian optimization methods.

</details>


### [372] [Leaky Students: Membership Inference against On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33136)
*Zhexi Lu,Mingzhi Zhu,Stacy Patterson,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的成员推断问题，发现学生模型会泄露教师监督所用记录的成员信息，并提出 Leaky 方法实现有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD 训练中教师获得的特权信息可能包含敏感数据，但学生模型在蒸馏过程中是否泄露这些记录的隐私信息尚不清楚，此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出 Leaky：从目标模型采样新轨迹，将其 token 对数概率与在不含候选记录上训练的多个匹配参考模型的最大值进行比较，再对差距应用 Leaky ReLU，保留正差距并降低负差距权重，以近似校正非成员的偶然正差距。

Result: 在涵盖数学、医学问答和代码生成的 15 个目标上，Leaky 优于所有基线，平均 AUROC 达 0.875；而主评估中最强基线为 0.614，在同一采样轨迹上最强基线为 0.826。

Conclusion: 通过 OPD 训练的学生模型可以泄露教师监督所用记录的成员身份，即便固定参考答案损失几乎无法提供成员证据。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated trajectories. However, privileged information supplied to the teacher for OPD training may contain sensitive data. Whether the student leaks private information about the records supplied to the teacher during distillation remains poorly understood. To the best of our knowledge, we present the first systematic study of membership inference in this setting. We find that fresh student trajectories expose sparse membership signals that fixed reference-answer losses often miss. These signals are mixed with probability changes caused by training on other records. We introduce Leaky, which samples fresh trajectories from the target model and compares its token log-probabilities with the maximum across matched reference models trained without the candidate records. It applies Leaky ReLU to the resulting gaps, preserving positive gaps and downweighting negative gaps as an approximate correction for incidental positive gaps in non-members. Across fifteen targets spanning mathematics, medical question answering, and code generation, Leaky outperforms all evaluated baselines and achieves mean AUROC 0.875, compared with 0.614 for the strongest baseline on each target in the main evaluation. On the same sampled trajectories, the strongest baseline achieves mean AUROC 0.826. These results show that students trained through OPD can expose the membership of records used for teacher supervision, even when fixed reference-answer losses provide little evidence of membership.

</details>


### [373] [Beyond the Training Horizon: Mechanisms and Limits of Length Generalization in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33144)
*Jia Liang,Xi Jin,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析揭示循环Transformer长度泛化的内在机制与局限：MR-Loop在固定读出位置更新状态并通过相邻词交互推进关系选择，DR-Loop跨关系位置传播状态形成推进式计算前沿；两者在更深推理链上均失效，但共享"活跃-消耗/新鲜-老化"残差方向编码计算状态的原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer能够泛化到比训练时更长的推理链，但支撑这一行为的具体计算机制以及限制其泛化范围的原因尚不清楚。作者希望从机制层面理解循环Transformer为何能长度泛化、以及在多大程度上泛化。

Method: 对比两种循环Transformer配置：匹配循环（MR-Loop）与解耦循环（DR-Loop），评估多项式迭代、有限状态组合与知识图谱遍历三类任务。采用注意力分析、中间状态解码与因果干预等手段，在最终答案监督下剖析其学习到的机制。

Result: MR-Loop在固定读出位置更新中间状态，通过相邻词交互与可迁移的进度线索推进关系选择；DR-Loop则跨关系位置传播中间状态，形成推进式计算前沿。两者在深度增大时都失效：MR-Loop的读出状态与进度线索退化，DR-Loop的状态传播可靠性下降，且自纠正能力有限导致局部错误持续并累积。

Conclusion: 两类模型共享同一表征原则：循环状态既编码任务相关内容，也编码其计算状态（即该内容是否仍支持后续计算）。"活跃-消耗"与"新鲜-老化"残差方向可因果控制信息能否参与后续计算，甚至超越训练范围。此外，长度泛化未必依赖忠实的分步推理，循环Transformer可借助任务结构而不显式表示每个中间状态。

Abstract: Looped Transformers can generalize to reasoning chains longer than those encountered during training, but the computations enabling this behavior and limiting its extent remain unclear. We mechanistically compare two looped-Transformer configurations, which we call the Matched-Recurrence Looped Transformer (MR-Loop) and Decoupled-Recurrence Looped Transformer (DR-Loop), reflecting their respective recurrence-training schemes. We evaluate polynomial iteration, finite-state composition, and knowledge-graph traversal using detailed mechanistic analysis. Attention analysis, intermediate-state decoding, and causal interventions reveal distinct mechanisms learned under final-answer supervision. MR-Loop updates an intermediate state at a fixed readout while advancing relation selection through adjacent-token interactions and a transferable progress cue. DR-Loop instead propagates intermediate states across relation positions, forming an advancing computational frontier. However, both mechanisms become unreliable at greater depths: MR-Loop exhibits degradation of its readout state and progress cues, while DR-Loop exhibits declining reliability of state propagation. Limited self-correction allows local errors to persist and compound. Across both models, we uncover a common representational principle: recurrent states encode not only task-relevant content but also its computational status, whether that content remains in a form that can support subsequent computation. Transferable live-consumed and fresh-aged residual directions causally control whether represented information can participate in subsequent computation, including beyond the training horizon. We further show that length generalization need not rely on faithful step-by-step reasoning, as Looped Transformers can exploit task structure without explicitly representing every intermediate state.

</details>


### [374] [CFLoRA: Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs with Complementary Factors for Error-free Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33147)
*Yanan Ma,Qiyuan Chen,Zihan Fang,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CFLoRA，通过每轮将 LoRA 隐通道划分为两个互补集合，消除双线性项，实现无聚合误差且无需扩展通信秩的联邦 LoRA 精确聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦 LoRA 中，分别平均两个低秩因子不等于平均其乘积，导致联邦平均存在结构性不匹配。现有精确方法主要依赖冻结整个因子或跨轮交替更新因子，但都无法在无聚合误差或不扩展通信秩的情况下同时更新两个因子。

Method: CFLoRA 在每个通信轮将 LoRA 隐通道划分为两个互补集合，通过保证因子间列与行的互补性，消除矩阵乘法中的双线性项，从而使联邦聚合精确。该框架还支持客户端具有异构秩预算。

Result: 收敛性分析表明，在同构秩情况下，CFLoRA 对原始 LoRA 目标达到 O(1/√T) 的收敛速率。在 GLUE 基准上使用 RoBERTa、在常识推理任务上使用 LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct 的大量实验显示，CFLoRA 相比最先进的联邦 LoRA 基线具有更优性能和训练效率。

Conclusion: CFLoRA 解决了联邦 LoRA 聚合中的结构性不匹配问题，实现精确聚合且支持异构秩，并在理论与实验上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Federated low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables collaborative fine-tuning of large language models without centralizing private client data. Its factorized update, however, creates a structural mismatch in federated averaging: averaging the two LoRA factors separately does not equal averaging their products. Existing exact methods resolve this issue mainly by freezing an entire factor or alternating factors across rounds, but none can update factors simultaneously without aggregation errors or expanding communication ranks. To address this fundamental problem, we present \texttt{CFLoRA}, a federated LoRA scheme that partitions latent LoRA channels into two complementary sets in every communication round. By ensuring that columns and rows are complementary across factors, we eliminate bilinear terms in matrix multiplications, making federated aggregation exact. Crucially, our framework also supports clients with heterogeneous rank budgets. Convergence analysis validates \texttt{CFLoRA} achieves $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence rate of the \textit{original} LoRA objective in homogeneous-rank cases.
