Extensive experiments with RoBERTa on the GLUE benchmark and with LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct on commonsense reasoning tasks demonstrate that \texttt{CFLoRA} achieves superior performance and training efficiency compared to state-of-the-art federated LoRA baselines.

</details>


### [375] [Convergence of Practical Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33152)
*Haonan Wang,Yu Wu,Minghui Liwang,Xinlei Yi,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为实用Muon优化器提供了优化解释并首次在随机非凸设定下建立了收敛保证，联合考虑了Newton–Schulz迭代与解耦权重衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为AdamW在大规模神经网络训练中的替代方案日益受到关注，但现有理论分析常简化或忽略两个关键组件：带有经验调节多项式系数的实用Newton–Schulz迭代，以及用于正则化的解耦权重衰减，导致对其实际实现的理论理解不完整。

Method: 将实用Muon解释为对原始损失的右预条件优化，并引入随接近驻点而消失的动态加权ℓ2正则化器，从而保持优化目标为原始目标；在此框架下联合分析Newton–Schulz迭代与解耦权重衰减。

Result: 建立了实用Muon在随机非凸设定下的首个收敛保证，以期望Frobenius范数梯度衡量达到O(T^{-1/4})的收敛速率，相比最佳已知AdamW收敛速率的维度依赖改进了√d倍（T为迭代轮数，d为参数维度）；实验进一步支持理论收敛结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为实用Muon提供了理论解释与收敛保证，说明其在维度依赖上优于AdamW，并为其实践应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Muon is emerging as a promising alternative to AdamW for large-scale neural network training, yet theoretical understanding of its practical implementation remains incomplete, as existing analyses often simplify or omit two key components: (i) practical Newton--Schulz iterations with empirically tuned polynomial coefficients $(3.4445,-4.7750,2.0315)$; and (ii) decoupled weight decay for regularization. In this paper, we provide an optimization interpretation and establish convergence for practical Muon, jointly accounting for both components. Specifically, we interpret practical Muon as right-preconditioned optimization of the original loss with a dynamic weighted $\ell_2$ regularizer that vanishes as stationarity is approached, so that the optimization target remains the original objective. We then establish, to our best knowledge, the first convergence guarantee for practical Muon in the stochastic nonconvex setting, with an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ convergence rate in terms of the expected Frobenius norm of the gradient, improving the dimension dependence of the best known AdamW's convergence rate by a factor of $\sqrt{d}$, where $T$ is the iteration horizon and $d$ is the parameter dimension. Experiments further support the theoretical convergence results.

</details>


### [376] [Downstream-Aware Context Selection for Online In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33166)
*Ruihan A. Li,Shangtong Zhang,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出有界历史上下文管理框架，通过预测删除历史交互的任务相关下游效应来指导历史选择与决策相关预算，在保持性能的同时显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICRL让大模型智能体无需更新参数即可用交互历史适应新环境，但不断增长的上下文历史会带来高昂的token成本，需要在保持性能的前提下进行有效的历史管理。

Method: 以完整滚动历史为参照，用预测器评估删除效应、定义删除顺序，并应用共享选择准则决定每次决策保留多少历史，从而确定决策相关的上下文预算。

Result: 在SUMO闭环驾驶的分布内、未见域、未见路线三种设置下分别减少25.7%、25.8%、23.2%的token使用并保持相近驾驶性能；在ScienceWorld的持续ICRL协议下比全上下文减少52.1%token，比Recent和Similarity基线分别少用30.2%和37.8%token且保持性能。

Conclusion: 该有界历史上下文管理框架能够在维持智能体任务性能的同时大幅降低token开销，是一种有效的ICRL上下文管理方案。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) enables large language model agents to adapt to new environments using their interaction history without updating model parameters. However, repeatedly conditioning on growing histories can lead to substantial token cost. We propose a bounded-history context-management framework that predicts the task-dependent downstream effect of removing historical interactions to guide history selection and determine a decision-dependent context budget. Formally, our framework uses the full rolling history as a reference. The predictor evaluates removal effects, defines a deletion ordering, and applies a shared selection criterion to determine how much history to retain at each decision. We evaluate the method in closed-loop SUMO driving under held-out in-distribution, unseen-domain, and unseen-route settings, and in ScienceWorld under a continual ICRL protocol. Relative to a baseline using the full context, our method reduces total token usage by 25.7%, 25.8%, and 23.2% across the three driving settings while maintaining comparable closed-loop driving performance. In ScienceWorld, it reduces total token usage by 52.1% compared to full context and uses 30.2% and 37.8% fewer tokens than the Recent and Similarity baselines, respectively, while maintaining performance.

</details>


### [377] [When Does Backpropagating Through Policy Memory Matter? Physical Credit, Optimizer Updates, and Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33169)
*Xingjian Li,Yi Han,Jianhua Z. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带记忆策略的反向传播路径被截断（如Transformer-XL）时的影响，通过固定前向计算、仅改变导数边，测量梯度、优化器更新和持续训练表现，发现优化器和训练阶段决定了记忆截断的真实成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带记忆的策略沿两条反向路径学习：通过动作产生的物理状态，以及通过存储的表示。Transformer-XL和截断时间反向传播（TBPTT）在保留存储历史值的同时切断了第二条路径。作者想探究这种切断何时才真正重要。

Method: 保持前向计算固定，仅改变导数边（如分离键值缓存），在Transformer船舶轨迹模型和四旋翼跟踪策略上测量参数梯度、优化器应用的更新以及持续训练。对比全局范数裁剪与AdamW的影响，测试不同噪声水平、不同截断窗口长度（两步、八步）以及仅在训练后期启用截断的情形。

Result: 在船舶模型中，当梯度流经所有早期物理状态时，分离键值缓存使梯度范数降至约十分之一，但方向几乎不变；在单步物理信用下几乎无变化。全局范数裁剪消除了记忆截断图之间的大部分梯度差异，而AdamW把2%的梯度差异放大为高达31%的更新差异。四旋翼在0.20 m/s噪声下，移除记忆使跟踪误差上升43%，切断记忆梯度上升32%；低噪声下切断的平均代价超过记忆的价值。两步截断无显著增益，八步截断消除了二分之一到四分之三的代价。

Conclusion: 测量记忆截断的代价应从初始化开始训练并带上截断；比较反向图时应依据优化器应用的更新，而非原始梯度。后期才启用截断会低估其代价约三倍。

Abstract: Policies with memory can learn along two backward paths: through the physical states their actions produce and through the representations they store. Transformer-XL and truncated backpropagation through time cut the second path at stored history while keeping its values. We ask when this cut matters. Holding the forward computation fixed and varying only derivative edges, we measure parameter gradients, the updates the optimizer applies, and continued training in a Transformer vessel-trajectory model and a quadrotor tracking policy. In the vessel model, detaching the key-value cache shrank the gradient to about a tenth of its norm, with little rotation, when gradients flowed through all earlier physical states, but barely changed it under one-step physical credit. In this strongly clipped regime the optimizer, not the gradient, set how far updates differed: global-norm clipping removed most of the gradient difference between memory-cut graphs, whereas AdamW turned a 2% gradient difference between two placements of the cut into update differences of up to 31% at the step where the placement was switched. In a quadrotor trained from initialization with 0.20 m/s velocity noise, removing memory raised tracking error by 43% and cutting memory gradients raised it by 32%; at low noise the cut's mean cost exceeded the value of memory. Two-step truncation segments gave no measurable gain, although with hidden velocity a two-step window captured most of the value of memory; eight-step segments removed half to three quarters of the cost. Switching the cut on only for the last fifth of training understated its cost about threefold at 0.20-0.30 m/s, but not at low noise or with hidden velocity. These results suggest measuring the cost of a memory cut by training with it from initialization, and comparing backward graphs by the updates the optimizer applies rather than by raw gradients.

</details>


### [378] [Perturb-and-Solve: Efficient Learned-Operator Conditioning for Latent Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33171)
*Abduragim Shtanchaev,Arip Asadulaev,Luiza Labazanova,Aidar Alimbayev,Karim Salta,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PASEO提出一种高效算子条件方法，用1M参数网络在潜空间扰动扩散模型预测，无需反传扩散网络即可支持任意退化算子的逆问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型是图像复原逆问题的强先验，但现有方法在通用性与效率间存在权衡：固定退化算子求解器快但通用性差，支持任意算子的方法慢且需通过扩散网络反向传播梯度。目标是打破这一瓶颈。

Method: PASEO用一个1M参数的小型学习网络在潜空间对扩散模型预测进行退化扰动，支持学习退化算子而无需反传扩散网络。通过加噪并基于该网络的局部线性近似求解线性方程，从近似后验中高效采样重建结果，避免构建或求逆大型协方差矩阵。

Result: 在FFHQ和COCO上的超分辨率、去模糊和修复任务中，PASEO在相同或更少模型评估次数下取得强感知质量，相比基线最高快9倍，峰值内存最多减少34%。

Conclusion: PASEO在保持高感知质量的同时显著提升效率并降低内存开销，为潜扩散模型求解任意退化逆问题提供了通用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models serve as powerful priors for solving inverse problems in image restoration, such as deblurring, inpainting, and super-resolution. Current methods have a trade-off between generality and efficiency. Solvers that are restricted to a fixed set of degradation operators are fast and efficient. Methods that support arbitrary degradation operators are slow and require gradients through the diffusion network. To break this bottleneck, we introduce PASEO (Perturb-And-Solve for Efficient Operator conditioning), a method that uses a small (1M parameters) learned network to degrade diffusion model predictions in latent space. PASEO supports learned degradation operators without back-propagating through the diffusion network. We efficiently sample reconstructions from an approximate posterior by combining the diffusion model's prediction with the observed image. We do this by adding noise and solving linear equations based on a local linear approximation of the learned network, without building or inverting large covariance matrices. Across super-resolution, deblurring, and inpainting on FFHQ and COCO, PASEO achieves strong perceptual quality while running up to 9x faster and using up to 34% less peak memory than the tested baselines, with the same or fewer model evaluations.

</details>


### [379] [When an Evaluation Rule Writes Training Labels: Measuring Human-Reference Forgiveness in NAVSIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33189)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Jingxin Yang,Jiaqi Ye,Youran Sun,Shuo Xin,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析 NAVSIM 中 GTRS-Dense 标签生成器的人为"宽容"规则，发现该规则抹除了大量候选轨迹的区分信息，影响监督训练与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAVSIM 作者为避免在单条轨迹评分时惩罚情境合理的机动，引入了"人类参考分数为零即全部标记通过"的宽容机制，并警告其可能掩盖重要失败。论文旨在量化该规则对监督信号的实际影响。

Method: 在全部 103,288 个 navtrain 场景上，关闭覆盖规则重新运行生成器，比较覆盖前目标与已发布标签；分析受影响列的比例与分布，并在 navtest 上比较宽容场景与匹配的非宽容场景中车道保持头的 AUC；同时对 Hydra-MDP 检查点、续训 300 步（三个配对种子）以及禁用宽容后的评分进行对比实验。

Result: 该规则在 11,237 个场景（10.8793%）上抹除了训练损失所依赖的候选区分；其中车道保持涉及 13,042 个被宽容损失列中的 9,982 个，宽容列中位数有 14,391/16,384 个候选在覆盖前为失败。navtest 上车道保持头在宽容场景的 AUC 中位数为 0.7095，匹配非宽容场景为 0.9807；Hydra-MDP 相应为 0.6627 与 0.9761。续训后宽容场景 AUC 高出 0.1086–0.1251，组间差距虽缩小但仍大于零。禁用宽容评分时，车道保持高出 2.478–3.524 分，相邻帧规划一致性降低，且这些变化在宽容场景上更大。EPDMS 无法区分两组目标。

Conclusion: 人为宽容覆盖显著损害了监督信号的质量与模型在宽容场景上的表现；禁用宽容会改变评分结果并降低规划一致性，而 EPDMS 对该差异不敏感，说明需要重新审视该标签生成规则。

Abstract: When the human reference scores zero on a metric, the released GTRS-Dense label generator for NAVSIM marks every candidate trajectory in the scene as passing it. NAVSIM's authors introduced this human-reference forgiveness to avoid penalizing contextually justified maneuvers when scoring one trajectory, and warned that it could overlook important failures. In label generation it sets a whole column of 16,384 candidate targets to passing. To measure the consequences for supervision, we re-run the generator with the overwrite disabled and compare the pre-overwrite targets with the released labels on all 103,288 navtrain scenes. The rule erases a candidate distinction that the training loss reads on 11,237 of them (10.8793%). Firing usually changes most of a column: lane keeping carries 9,982 of the 13,042 forgiven loss columns, and its median forgiven column had 14,391 of 16,384 candidates failing before the overwrite. On held-out navtest scenes forgiven on lane keeping, the released lane-keeping head's median AUC against the pre-overwrite outcome is 0.7095; on unforgiven scenes matched on failing-candidate count it is 0.9807. For the Hydra-MDP checkpoint released with GTRS, whose configuration takes the same label file, the two values are 0.6627 and 0.9761. Continuing the released GTRS-Dense checkpoint for 300 optimizer steps with three paired seeds, we observe the forgiven-scene AUC 0.1086-0.1251 higher with pre-overwrite than with published targets, and a narrower gap between matched groups, still above zero. Scoring with forgiveness disabled, we observe lane keeping higher by 2.478-3.524 points on navtest scenes forgiven on any of five loss metrics, with lower adjacent-frame plan consistency. Both changes are larger there than on the rest. EPDMS, scored the same way, does not separate the two target sets.

</details>


### [380] [AG-CoT: Verified Algorithmic Traces for LLM Program Synthesis on Clifford Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33192)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Chenfeng Cao,Lu Pang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究科学代码生成中"程序可执行但计算错误"的问题，以Clifford电路生成为例，提出基于精确验证器的AG-CoT监督与验证器过滤续训框架，显著提升量子态等价准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码生成模型可能产出可运行但无法计算正确科学对象（此处为正确量子态）的程序。Clifford电路可精确经典验证，适合作为研究该问题的试验台，且对量子纠错中的稳定子态制备有实际意义。

Method: 目标条件化框架：目标以紧凑的有符号稳定子生成元给出，用精确验证器检查生成的OpenQASM电路。用验证器校验的Aaronson-Gottesman思维链（AG-CoT）轨迹监督模型，并对验证器接受的模型生成结果进行继续训练。

Result: 在两个独立训练的模型族（3B与7B）中，AG-CoT监督使贪心解码的状态等价准确率相比仅用电路的基线提升4至6倍，验证器过滤续训在此基础上带来一致增益。32B补充研究显示，监督模型语法与Clifford有效性接近完美，最强直接模型每目标状态等价率达6.14%，多候选验证器引导选择下超过10%。

Conclusion: 算法轨迹监督在两个模型族中均带来大幅且统计显著的提升，验证器过滤续训再添增益。Clifford有效性与状态等价之间的持续差距表明精确验证是必要的：电路可以在语法和物理上有效，却制备错误的量子态。

Abstract: Scientific code generation can produce executable programs that fail to compute the intended scientific object. We study this problem in language-model synthesis of Clifford circuits, which prepare the stabilizer states used in quantum error correction and admit exact classical verification. In our target-conditioned framework, each target is given as compact signed stabilizer generators, and an exact verifier checks the generated OpenQASM circuits. We supervise models with Aaronson-Gottesman chain-of-thought (AG-CoT) traces checked by the verifier, and continue training on model generations that the verifier accepts. Across two independently trained model families (3B and 7B), AG-CoT supervision multiplies greedy-decode state-equivalence accuracy by four to six times over circuit-only baselines, and verifier-filtered continuation training adds a further consistent gain atop both. A complementary 32B study shows that supervised models achieve near-perfect syntax and Clifford validity while the strongest direct model reaches 6.14% state equivalence per target, rising to over 10% under verifier-guided selection with multiple candidates. These results show that algorithmic trace supervision gives a large, statistically significant gain in both model families and that verifier-filtered continuation adds a further repeated gain. The persistent gap between Clifford validity and state equivalence confirms that exact verification is necessary: a circuit can be syntactically and physically valid yet prepare the wrong quantum state.

</details>


### [381] [Apparent Compression, Real Stability: The Intrinsic Dimension of Learning a Quantum Wavefunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33193)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Chenfeng Cao,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文测度了变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）训练的内在维度，发现小维度可能具有误导性，但其带来的稳定性是真实的，且内在维度能追踪量子相变难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于探究训练到底需要权重空间中多少个方向，即内在维度，这一概念曾推动LoRA等参数高效方法。作者选择VMC作为测试，因为它训练神经网络表示量子多体系统基态，网络自生成训练样本、梯度含噪，且已知精确答案、每次运行可评分，是严苛且有揭示性的测试。

Method: 方法上，仅训练一个小潜在向量，由冻结的随机映射转换为网络权重，不改变标准自然梯度优化器。在具有困难符号模式的磁体上实验，并观察跨量子相变的内在维度变化，同时对比子空间训练与全参数训练及匹配求解器的控制实验。

Result: 结果显示：在符号不可表示的网络上，最佳能量出现在28642个方向中的8个，仅因无网络能更低；一旦符号可学习，符号和幅度都不廉价。维度跨量子相变升高，能以远低计算量追踪状态难度，但从不低于随机子空间设定的下限。子空间训练从未发散，而相同设置的全参数训练发散，匹配求解器控制表明差异源于维度降低。

Conclusion: 结论是内在维度小可能误导，但其稳定性真实；内在维度可低成本追踪量子相变难度，且子空间训练具有稳定性优势。

Abstract: How many directions in weight space does training need? The intrinsic dimension answers this with the smallest number of random directions in which training still reaches a target accuracy, and small values have motivated parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA. We measure it for variational Monte Carlo (VMC), which trains a neural network to represent the ground state of a quantum many-body system. VMC is a demanding test, because the network generates its own training samples and every gradient is noisy, and a revealing one, because the exact answer is known and every run can be scored. We train only a small latent vector that a frozen random map turns into the network's weights, with no change to the standard natural-gradient optimizer. We find that a small dimension can be misleading, while the stability it brings is real. On a magnet with a hard sign pattern, a network that cannot represent signs reaches its best energy in 8 of 28,642 directions, but only because no such network can go lower; once signs are learnable, neither the signs nor the magnitudes are cheap. The dimension rises across a quantum phase transition, so it tracks how difficult a state is at far less compute than fitting a scaling law, yet it never falls below a floor set by the random subspace itself, even where the ground state is nearly trivial. Training in the subspace, in contrast, never diverged in our experiments, whereas full-parameter training with the same settings did, and a control with matched solvers attributes the difference to the reduced dimension.

</details>


### [382] [Orthogonal Witness Control for Muon Optimization via Sigmoid Spectral Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33194)
*Dat Phi Van,Ngo Vu Minh,Tuc Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Ngoc-Thanh Dinh,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Soren 矩阵优化器，用有界单调 sigmoid 变换谱值以保留梯度奇异子空间和相对幅度信息，并用 Soft Newton-Schulz 多项式高效实现，实验验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Muon 等矩阵优化器通过 Newton-Schulz 正交化利用更新谱结构，但近乎压平奇异谱，丢弃了不同梯度模态之间的相对幅度信息。

Method: Soren 保留梯度奇异子空间，对奇异值施加有界单调 sigmoid 变换，平滑压缩主导模态而不完全压平谱；将其解释为正定预条件梯度方法，在相对光滑性与度量 Polyak-Łojasiewicz 几何下建立收敛保证；提出有限深度 Soft Newton-Schulz 多项式实现，避免显式 SVD，并刻画谱近似对收敛几何的影响。

Result: 在 LLM 预训练、监督微调和直接偏好优化实验中，Soren 相较已有优化器展现出有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 保留谱相对幅度信息的软谱变换是提升矩阵优化器性能的有效途径，且可由多项式近似高效实现并具备理论收敛保障。

Abstract: Matrix-valued optimizers such as Muon exploit the spectral structure of neural network updates through Newton--Schulz orthogonalization, but their near-flattening of the singular spectrum discards relative magnitude information across gradient modes. We introduce \emph{Soren} (\textbf{S}pectral \textbf{O}rthogonal \textbf{Re}shapi\textbf{n}g), a matrix-valued optimizer that preserves the singular subspaces of the gradient while applying a bounded, monotone sigmoid transformation to its singular values. This smoothly compresses dominant modes without fully flattening the spectrum. We interpret Soren as a positive-definite preconditioned gradient method and establish convergence guarantees under relative smoothness and metric Polyak--Łojasiewicz geometry. To avoid explicit singular value decomposition, we further develop a finite-depth Soft Newton--Schulz (SNS) polynomial realization of the sigmoid spectral map and characterize how its spectral approximation affects the induced convergence geometry. Experiments across LLM pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and direct preference optimization demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of Soren against established optimizers.

</details>


### [383] [Teach Yourself Where to Look: On-Policy Attention Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33200)
*Safaeid Hossain Arib,Rabeya Akter,Ismam Nur Swapnil,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Md Mofijul Islam,Tasnim Mohiuddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OPASD，将特权教师的注意力分布经投影重归一化后对齐到学生可见位置，补充token级监督，在多个数学基准上一致超越纯token的OPSD，并显著降低长度膨胀与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy自蒸馏只传递教师预测的token分布，却不传递教师在上下文中的注意力位置，缺少这一互补监督信号。

Method: OPASD在token级监督之外，引入解条件化的注意力蒸馏：由于特权教师可访问学生看不到的验证解token，先将其注意力投影到学生可见位置并重归一化，再进行对齐。

Result: 在3种模型规模与4个竞赛级数学基准上，平均准确率较纯token OPSD提升4.98至8.40个百分点；生成rollout token减少73.9%，估算计算量减少72.6%，训练速度提升1.53倍，并避免长度膨胀与性能退化。

Conclusion: 解条件化注意力提供了互补监督信号，使on-policy自蒸馏更准确、更稳定且更计算高效。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation trains reasoning models on their own trajectories using dense token distribution guidance from a privileged teacher with access to a verified solution. This supervision transfers what the teacher predicts without directly transferring where it attends within the preceding context. We introduce On-Policy Attention Self-Distillation (OPASD), which complements token-level supervision with solution-conditioned attention distillation. Because the privileged teacher can attend to verified solution tokens unavailable to the student, OPASD projects teacher attention onto student-visible positions and renormalizes the resulting distribution before alignment. Across three model sizes and four competition-level mathematics benchmarks, OPASD consistently outperforms token-only OPSD, improving average accuracy by 4.98 to 8.40 percentage points. OPASD also avoids the response-length inflation and performance degradation observed with token-only distillation, reducing generated rollout tokens by 73.9% and estimated model compute by 72.6% while training 1.53x faster. These results show that solution-conditioned attention provides a complementary supervision signal that makes on-policy self-distillation more accurate, stable, and compute-efficient.

</details>


### [384] [SMORE: Stability-Promoting Mesh-Agnostic Model Reduction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33205)
*Yangyuan Li,Weichao Li,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMORE——一个与网格无关的时空PDE降阶框架，采用李雅普诺夫引导的稳定性正则化训练潜动力学，实现稳定的长时间预测，并在多个PDE问题上优于DINo、FNO、CNO、Transolver等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真求解时空PDE计算代价高昂，需要数据驱动的降阶代理模型用于不确定性量化、设计优化、数据同化和最优控制等多次查询任务。但现有代理模型时序稳定性差，在多步长时程预测中易出现不稳定推演和反向传播梯度爆炸。

Method: 提出SMORE框架：潜动力学采用李雅普诺夫引导的稳定性正则化进行训练，以促进稳定的长时程推演；在给定结构假设下提供理论保证；学习的潜动力学具有可解释性且为线性或线性二次形式；框架还能从初始条件的稀疏测量预测连续PDE解场；且与网格无关。

Result: 在波动传播、Navier-Stokes方程和浅水方程等问题上评估，SMORE提升了长时程推演的泛化性与经验鲁棒性，在相近参数预算下取得与DINo、FNO、CNO、Transolver等竞争基线相当甚至更优的精度。

Conclusion: SMORE通过在训练中引入稳定性正则化有效缓解了代理模型的长时程不稳定问题，其可解释的线性/线性二次潜动力学还有望服务于数据同化和最优控制等下游任务。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations of time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are computationally expensive, motivating data-driven reduced-order surrogates for many-query tasks such as uncertainty quantification, design optimization, data assimilation, and optimal control. However, existing surrogate models often exhibit poor temporal stability, which can lead to unstable rollouts and exploding gradients during backpropagation, especially in multistep long-horizon forecasting. To address this, we propose SMORE, a mesh-agnostic framework for model order reduction of time-dependent PDEs. Its latent dynamics are trained with Lyapunov-guided stability regularization, which promotes stable long-horizon rollouts. We provide theoretical guarantees under the stated structural assumptions. Beyond forecasting PDE evolution, the learned latent dynamics, which are interpretable and linear or linear-quadratic, could bring benefits for downstream tasks such as data assimilation and optimal control. Moreover, our framework is capable of predicting continuous PDE solution fields from sparse measurements of the initial condition. We evaluate SMORE on a range of problems, including wave propagation, the Navier-Stokes equations, and the shallow water equations. Our results show that it improves long-horizon rollout generalization and empirical robustness, and achieves competitive accuracy at comparable parameter budgets relative to competitive baselines including DINo, FNO, CNO, and Transolver.

</details>


### [385] [When Do Models Admit They Are Wrong? Failure Disclosure Is Unstable Under Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33220)
*Steven Y. Feng,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank,Evan Hubinger,Paul C. Bogdan,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 仅用结果反馈的强化学习可以稳定任务准确率，但模型是否承认解题失败（失败披露）在多次训练运行间差异极大；该行为受微小浮点与采样差异影响，并可分解为多个决策阶段，需要在训练中直接测量与约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：即使任务表现相近，模型在"如何报告自身错误"上也可能截然不同。作者关注失败披露（失败时是否承认失败，而非沉默或谎称成功）这一与安全相关的行为，指出稳定任务准确率并不能保证稳定可靠的安全相关行为，因此需要测量跨运行的这些行为并设计让其可靠的训练方法。

Method: 采用仅基于结果的GRPO进行多次重复训练运行，比较失败披露与任务准确率的变异性；在第二个推理任务和稳定化的PPO上复现；在7B规模验证，并在指令条件下的32B设置中检验。通过引入微小浮点与采样差异、固定任务目标与早期训练历史进行对照。设计中性对照实验，检验训练约束较弱的行为更易跨运行波动。另设实验：通过抑制模型在失败但格式良好的回答上偏离初始策略，来降低失败披露的变异性。

Result: 失败披露的跨运行变异远大于任务准确率；该模式在第二个推理任务、稳定化PPO、7B以及32B指令条件下均出现。训练中微小的浮点与采样差异即可改变报告行为。失败披露不是单一决策，可分离为检查答案、进入报告、完成承认三个阶段，薄弱环节随任务与回答格式而变。中性对照显示训练约束较弱的行为普遍易波动。抑制在失败且格式良好回答上偏离初始策略可显著提升报告一致性，但对任务表现的影响依设置而定。

Conclusion: 稳定的任务准确率不等于稳定的安全相关行为。研究者应跨运行直接测量失败披露等行为，并设计能保持其可靠性的训练方法。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning can produce models with similar task performance but very different ways of communicating about their mistakes. We study failure disclosure: whether a model admits that an attempted solution failed rather than staying silent or presenting it as successful. Across repeated outcome-only GRPO training runs, failure disclosure varies far more than task accuracy. The pattern extends to a second reasoning task and stabilized PPO, persists at 7B, and also appears in an instruction-conditioned 32B setting. We also find that small floating-point and sampling differences during training can redirect reporting behavior even when the task objective and earlier training history are held fixed. Additional tests show that failure disclosure is not a single decision: Checking the answer, entering a report, and completing the admission can separate, and the weak point depends on the task and response format. Further, experiments with neutral controls show more broadly that behaviors left weakly constrained by training are especially likely to vary across runs, of which failure disclosure is an example. We can reduce variability in failure disclosure by discouraging the model from drifting from its starting policy on failed, well-formed responses. This makes reporting substantially more consistent, though its effect on task performance depends on the setting. Stable task accuracy therefore does not guarantee stable safety-relevant behavior: Researchers should measure these behaviors directly across runs and design training methods that keep them reliable.

</details>


### [386] [RMB: Reward Model Boosting Mitigates Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33221)
*Jiabin Fan,Dezhi Ye,Yongchang Hao,Lili Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reward Model Boosting (RMB)，通过训练多样性奖励模型集并学习轻量级聚合器，缓解 RLHF 中的奖励破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 能对齐大语言模型与人类偏好，但代理奖励模型不完美，导致奖励破解：策略优化提升了代理奖励却损害真实人类偏好表现。

Method: RMB 分两步：先用多样性促进正则项训练一组奖励模型，使其学习互补的奖励景观；再基于 boosting 思想学习一个轻量级聚合器，将多个奖励模型输出融合为更准确稳健的奖励信号。

Result: 大量实验表明 RMB 在分布内与分布外数据集上均显著提升奖励准确率，明显缓解奖励破解问题，并最终改善 RLHF 表现。

Conclusion: RMB 通过多样化奖励模型与 boosting 聚合，为 RLHF 提供更稳健可靠的奖励信号，有效缓解奖励破解并提升整体性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is a powerful technique for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preference. However, it often suffers from the reward hacking issue, where policy optimization improves the proxy reward model while actually degrading performance with respect to the true human preference, due to the imperfection of the proxy. To address this, we propose Reward Model Boosting (RMB), a novel approach that enhances the robustness and reliability of the reward signal for RLHF. RMB first trains a set of reward models with a diversity-promoting regularizer. This encourages each model to learn complementary aspects of the reward landscape. Then, RMB learns a lightweight aggregator in the principle of boosting to aggregate the outputs of the diverse reward models into a more accurate and robust reward signal. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that RMB significantly improves reward accuracy on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets, substantially mitigating the reward hacking issue and ultimately improving RLHF performance.

</details>


### [387] [MTLiquid: Enabling Efficient Multi-Task Learning using Liquid Neural Networks for Lightweight Healthcare Monitoring Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33232)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Fahad Abdul Rauf,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MTLiquid，一种在连续时间神经网络中实现高效多任务学习的方法，用于医疗健康监测，通过多输入/输出头和有效训练策略，在保持强性能的同时显著降低内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗监测中的生理信号常以不规则时间间隔采样，需连续时间处理以保证准确预测；同时系统往往需解决多个检测/预测任务以全面评估患者。然而现有连续时间神经网络通常每个网络只解决单一任务，限制了效率提升。

Method: MTLiquid采用：(1)多个输入和输出头以适应不同任务，同时跨任务共享同一骨干网络；(2)有效的训练策略，利用损失加权技术平衡不同任务的学习更新，并采用比例数据呈现技术应对数据集规模不平衡问题。

Result: 在ICU患者死亡率预测(P12)和脓毒症早期检测(P19)任务上，MTLiquid取得强性能（AUROC：P12为0.84，P19为0.94），与最先进的单任务学习在连续时间网络（P12 0.84，P19 0.95）和离散时间网络（P12 0.79-0.82，P19 0.92-0.94）中相当，同时内存成本显著降低44%-94%。

Conclusion: MTLiquid方法有望实现轻量级连续时间医疗监测系统，支持更好的决策。

Abstract: Continuous-time sensing and monitoring with timely and accurate decision-making are critical for many real-world applications. In healthcare monitoring systems, physiological signals are often available or sampled at irregular time intervals, hence requiring continuous-time processing to provide accurate prediction. Moreover, such systems often need to solve multiple detection/prediction tasks to provide a comprehensive patient review from different physiological aspects for more accurate decision-making. To solve this, continuous-time neural networks (CTNNs) can be employed. However, state-of-the-art works typically solve only one task at each network, thereby limiting their efficiency gains. To address this limitation, we propose MTLiquid, a novel methodology to enable efficient multi-task learning in continuous-time processing for healthcare monitoring systems through effective network design and training strategy. MTLiquid employs: (1) multiple input and output heads to accommodate different tasks, while sharing the same backbone network across tasks; as well as (2) an effective training strategy that leverages a loss-weighting technique to balance learning updates across different tasks and a proportional data presentation technique to address imbalanced dataset sizes. Experimental results for mortality prediction (P12) and sepsis early detection (P19) tasks for ICU patients show that, MTLiquid achieves strong performance (AUROC: 0.84 for P12 and 0.94 for P19) comparable to the state-of-the-art single-task learning in both continuous-time networks (AUROC: 0.84 for P12 and 0.95 for P19) and discrete-time networks (AUROC: 0.79-0.82 for P12 and 0.92-0.94 for P19), while incurring significantly smaller memory cost by 44%-94%. These results highlight the potential of our MTLiquid methodology to enable lightweight continuous-time healthcare monitoring systems for better decision-making.

</details>


### [388] [The Price of Locality: Why Forward-Forward Underperforms Backpropagation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33240)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Haichuan Liu,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文诊断了前向-前向算法（FFA）相较反向传播（BP）性能差距的两个结构性失效模式：并发局部更新导致的优化误差下限，以及层表示几何坍缩；理论上证明FFA每层损失满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式，但层间分布漂移造成误差地板，且表示相似核随深度指数坍缩至秩一，使FFA有效学习容量与深度无关，而BP容量随深度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFA以逐层局部对比目标替代BP，免去反向传播与中间激活的保存，但与BP存在持续性能差距，且该差距随深度加深而扩大。论文旨在从优化与表示两个方面诊断这一差距的根本结构性原因。

Method: 从优化角度，证明FFA损失在每层满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式，但并发逐层更新引发层间表示分布漂移，使每层面对移动的输入分布优化，产生误差下限。从表示角度，分析层表示的成对相似核，揭示其随深度增加向秩一指数收缩，坍缩了逐层误差信号的多样性。并引入"有效学习容量"（跨层梯度信息多样性）进行理论刻画，与BP的链式法则信号对比。

Result: 理论上证明：FFA每层损失满足PL不等式，但并发更新带来不可消除的误差地板；层表示相似核随深度指数坍缩至秩一，限制逐层误差信号多样性；因此FFA的有效学习容量受深度无关的上界约束，而BP因链式法则保留逐层多样性，容量随深度增长。这从理论层面解释了FFA深度越深性能差距越大的现象。

Conclusion: FFA与BP的性能差距源于两个结构性失效：优化层的并发更新误差地板与表示层的几何坍缩。二者共同使FFA有效学习容量不随深度增长，而BP容量随深度扩展，从而解释了深度增加时差距扩大的原因。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward Algorithm (FFA) replaces backpropagation (BP) with layer-wise local contrastive objectives, eliminating the backward pass and the need to retain intermediate activations, yet suffers a persistent performance gap with BP that worsens with depth. This paper diagnoses two structural failure modes: an optimization floor arising from concurrent local updates; and a geometric collapse of layer representations driven by the local update mechanism. On the optimization side, we prove that the FFA loss satisfies the Polyak--Lojasiewicz inequality at each layer; however, simultaneous layer updates induce inter-layer representation-distribution drift, so each layer optimizes against a moving input distribution and incurs an error floor. On the representational side, the pairwise similarity kernel of layer representations contracts exponentially toward rank one as depth increases, collapsing the diversity of per-layer error signals. This collapse bounds FFA's effective learning capacity, which measures the diversity of gradient information across layers, independently of depth, whereas BP's chain-rule signal preserves per-layer diversity, yielding a capacity that scales with depth.

</details>


### [389] [Minimax-Optimality of Posterior Sampling for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33246)
*Taewon Goo,Kihyuk Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明未修改的标准PSRL（后验采样强化学习）在任意相关先验下即可达到极小极大最优的贝叶斯遗憾，无需对先验做结构假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSRL是最简单有效的探索方法之一，但一个基本问题长期未解：未修改的PSRL能否在不对先验做结构假设的情况下达到极小极大遗憾？本文回答"是"。

Method: 核心难点在于后验采样的转移模型与其自身的续期价值相互耦合。作者引入一个共同的"经验转移参考"来分离由此产生的价值失配，并用基于Bellman的方差论证加以控制，且不引入额外的主阶状态空间因子。

Result: 对未知随机奖励的有限时域、时间非齐次表格MDP，在奖励与转移的任意联合先验下，得到极小极大 regret 率 Õ(√(SAH³K))；同一证明原理还在线性混合MDP、任意联合参数先验下给出极小极大 Õ(d√(H³K)) 率。

Conclusion: 精确的vanilla PSRL在任意相关先验下即达到主阶贝叶斯遗憾的极小极大最优，说明无需对先验施加结构假设；该证明原理具有可推广性。

Abstract: Posterior sampling for reinforcement learning (PSRL) is one of the simplest and most effective exploration methods, but a basic question has remained open: does unmodified PSRL achieve minimax regret without structural assumptions on the prior? We answer yes. Exact vanilla PSRL is minimax optimal in leading-order Bayesian regret under arbitrary correlated priors. The difficulty is that a posterior-sampled transition model is coupled with its own continuation value. We overcome this with a common empirical transition reference that isolates the resulting value mismatch and a Bellman-based variance argument that controls it without an extra leading-order state-space factor. For finite-horizon, time-inhomogeneous tabular MDPs with unknown stochastic rewards, this yields the minimax $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ regret rate under arbitrary joint priors over rewards and transitions. The same proof principle gives the minimax $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{H^3K})$ rate for linear-mixture MDPs under arbitrary joint parameter priors.

</details>


### [390] [Feedback-Robust AI for Patient Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33248)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ClosedLoopBench 基准，用负对照动作流检验患者知识图谱关系是否具有物理/药理/临床确定的符号，并给出反馈鲁棒的图构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 床旁监测患者知识图谱需为关系定类型并声明数据是否支持其符号，但麻醉与重症中药物与通气随生理反馈滴定，时间关系混淆了患者响应与临床医生策略，导致估计出的符号被医生政策污染。

Method: 构建 ClosedLoopBench：3,442 例 VitalDB 手术病例（12,653 小时），29 种关系，符号由物理、药理或临床实践确定，并配负对照动作流（将患者动作替换为他人动作）。评估 12 种估计器；提出反馈鲁棒患者图：概念节点+证据指针、经负对照准入的定类型关系、搏动级耦合。

Result: 未校准时 6/12 估计器平均将 19-29 个关系估计中的 11-18 个判为显著；校准后互相关与 Granger 检验仍将呼吸机频率 -> 呼气末 CO2 的符号判为医生政策方向。所提图在零流下每图 0.06-0.10 个虚假概念级关系实例，相关法为 10-12 个。11 个慢药物与呼吸机响应的个体化估计预测后续数据不优于群体估计；脉搏到达时间-收缩压斜率为负者占 94.3%（2,884 例），个体化早期-晚期相关 0.67 [0.63, 0.70]。

Conclusion: 临床反馈回路会使常规相关与因果方法产生系统性错误符号，需以定类型、负对照准入和搏动级耦合构造反馈鲁棒患者图；多数慢响应个体化建模收益有限。

Abstract: Patient knowledge graphs from bedside monitoring should type their relations and state whether the data support their signs. In anesthesia and intensive care, clinicians titrate drugs and ventilation in response to the physiology, so temporal relations mix the patient's response with the clinician's policy. We introduce ClosedLoopBench: 29 relations with signs fixed by physics, pharmacology or clinical practice, on 3,442 VitalDB surgical cases (12,653 h) with negative-control action streams. When each patient's actions are replaced by another patient's, six of 12 estimators declare on average 11-18 of their 19-29 distinct relation estimates significant without calibration, and after calibration cross-correlation and Granger tests still assign ventilator rate -> end-tidal CO2 the sign of the clinician's policy. We propose feedback-robust patient graphs that combine concept nodes with evidence pointers, typed relations admitted against negative controls, and beat-level couplings. On VitalDB under null streams, our graphs contain 0.06-0.10 false concept-level relation instances per graph, versus 10-12 for correlational construction. Patient-specific estimates of 11 slow drug and ventilator responses predict later data no better than population estimates, whereas the pulse-arrival-time-systolic-pressure slope is negative in 94.3% of 2,884 cases and patient-specific (early-late correlation 0.67 [0.63, 0.70]).

</details>


### [391] [BERT4DTI : BERT-based Model for Predicting Drug-Protein Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33254)
*Thanina Hamitouch,Khadidja Henni,Abdelkrim Arie,Amina Selma Haichour,Neila Mezghani,Lina Abou-Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BERT4DTI：ChemBERTa+ProtBERT双向互注意力+卷积/MLP分类，仅微调最后两层，参数量125M，在BIOSNAP等基准上竞争力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-靶点相互作用预测对药物发现与重定位至关重要。现有序列型DTI模型存在三大局限：标注数据稀缺且分布不均、大型预训练编码器端到端微调成本高、独立编码序列无法捕捉配对特异性依赖。

Method: 用ChemBERTa编码SMILES、ProtBERT编码氨基酸序列；在token级表示上施加双向互注意力；用卷积层与多层感知机对交互特征分类。为降低可训练规模，ProtBERT截断至保留18层，且每个编码器仅微调最后两层。

Result: 在BIOSNAP、DAVIS、BindingDB上具竞争力：BIOSNAP取得最佳ROC-AUC与PR-AUC，三个基准上灵敏度最高。DAVIS消融显示互注意力提升PR-AUC与特异性。可训练参数125M，而全量BERT微调为353M。

Conclusion: BERT4DTI在序列型DTI筛选中提供较优的性能-参数权衡；运行时分析、校准与泄漏审计的验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: Understanding how drugs interact with protein targets is fundamental to drug discovery, drug repurposing and the early identification of promising therapeutic candidates before costly experimental testing. Sequence-based DTI models face three practical limitations: labelled interactions are scarce and unevenly distributed, large pretrained chemical and protein encoders are expensive to fine-tune end-to-end, and independently encoded sequences do not capture pair-specific dependencies. We present BERT4DTI, which encodes SMILES strings with ChemBERTa and amino-acid sequences with ProtBERT, applies bidirectional mutual attention between token-level representations, and classifies the resulting interaction features using convolutional layers and a multilayer perceptron. To reduce trainable size, ProtBERT is truncated to 18 retained layers and only the last two layers of each encoder are fine-tuned. On BIOSNAP, DAVIS and BindingDB, BERT4DTI is competitive, achieving the best ROC-AUC and PR-AUC on BIOSNAP and the highest sensitivity on all three benchmarks. An ablation on DAVIS shows that mutual attention improves PR-AUC and specificity. With 125M trainable parameters compared with 353M for full BERT fine-tuning, BERT4DTI provides a favourable performance-parameter trade-off for sequence-based DTI screening, while leaving runtime profiling, calibration and leakage-audited validation for future work.

</details>


### [392] [Two Heads Are Better Than One: Aggregating Weaker LLMs for Better Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33257)
*Cheng Peng,Ruixi Luo,Zhi Chen,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 弱LLM预测者可被聚合成超越更强单一预测者的集成，为受限场景提供替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强单一预测者成本高或受限制，需研究能否用较弱LLM预测者聚合出更优表现。

Method: 基于ForecastBench，评估70个LLM预测者、16个比较组、1121个弱模型对；用测试Brier分数识别最强个体，用训练数据学习聚合权重，构建仅含弱预测者的聚合。

Result: 学习式线性池化在16组中11组达到或超越最强个体，全部16组Brier分数差距在5%内；提升不依赖接近最优的成员，且校准良好；增加模型数量不一定提升性能，实际约束下仍可获得有竞争力的弱模型聚合。

Conclusion: 弱到强预测聚合可行，弱LLM组合可接近甚至超越强单一预测者，具实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to forecast real-world events, but access to the strongest individual forecaster may be costly or otherwise constrained. We study weak-to-strong forecast aggregation: can individually weaker LLM forecasters be aggregated to outperform a stronger forecaster? Using ForecastBench (Karger et al., 2025), we evaluate 70 LLM forecasters across 16 comparison groups, each with more than 1,000 shared subquestions, yielding 1,121 weaker-model pairs. Within each group, we identify the strongest individual by test Brier score and evaluate aggregates composed exclusively of weaker forecasters, with aggregation weights learned on separate training data. We find substantial evidence of weak-to-strong improvement. Learned linear pooling identifies a weaker pair that matches or outperforms the strongest individual in 11 of 16 groups and comes within 5% of its Brier score in all 16 groups. We also find that these improvements do not rely on having a near-best constituent and are generally accompanied by good calibration. Additional analyses show that adding more models does not consistently improve performance, and competitive weaker-model aggregates also remain available under practical constraints.

</details>


### [393] [GTRL: Grounding Divide-and-Conquer Value Learning with Temporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33259)
*Abdul Monaf Chowdhury,MD Sameer Iqbal Chowdhury,Shifat E Arman,Md Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GTRL：用一步TD目标为分治式价值更新提供接地，并校正后见重标定偏差，在十九个OGBench任务上取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中，分治策略通过子目标拼接两段较短轨迹以扩展到长时域，但在随机动力学下存在问题：一是其基础情形只通过数据中"最幸运"的轨迹来估值（乐观偏差），二是子目标必须位于共享轨迹上，导致没有轨迹连接的状态-目标对完全得不到价值更新。

Method: 提出Grounded Transitive RL（GTRL）。核心是把一步TD目标加入分治组合更新中而非替代它：单步TD目标对后继求平均、无需子目标，因而在随机动力学下是正确的；两者相加使每个状态-目标对都能获得更新，同时仍保留组合带来的长时域能力。此外，针对后见重标定（hindsight relabeling）带来的偏差，GTRL按每个目标从其他后继被到达的难易程度进行重加权校正。

Result: 在覆盖随机、确定性和stitching（拼接）环境的十九个OGBench任务上进行评估，GTRL取得最高的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 将一步TD目标与分治式组合相结合，可在保留长时域优势的同时修正随机动力学下的乐观偏差，并使所有状态-目标对均能获得价值更新；重加权机制进一步缓解后见重标定偏差，整体在多个基准上表现最优。

Abstract: In offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), divide-and-conquer scales to long horizons by joining two shorter segments at a subgoal. However, under stochastic dynamics, the base case of this rule values the luckiest trajectories through the data. The subgoal must also lie on a shared trajectory, so a state-goal pair that no trajectory connects gets no value update at all. To address both, we present Grounded Transitive RL (GTRL), an offline GCRL value learning algorithm that grounds the divide-and-conquer update with a one-step TD target. Over a single step, TD is correct, as its target averages over the successors and needs no subgoal. GTRL adds this target to the composition rather than replacing it, so every pair receives an update, and the composition still carries the long horizon. GTRL also corrects the bias from hindsight relabeling by reweighting each goal against how reachable it was from other successors. We evaluate our algorithm on nineteen OGBench tasks spanning stochastic, deterministic, and stitching environments, where it achieves the highest average success rate. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [394] [Towards Identifiable Representations under Misspecified Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33273)
*Yuke Li,Yujia Zheng,Ziyi Chen,Kun Zhang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究隐变量相关噪声下的可识别性问题，放宽了观测在给定隐变量时条件独立的假设，提出更一般的错误指定结构，给出精确与近似可识别性保证，并提出无监督变分估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可识别性结果依赖观测在给定隐变量条件下相互独立（条件独立）的假设，而依赖隐变量的噪声对可识别性构成根本挑战，该假设在实际中往往不成立，因此需要研究更一般的错误指定结构。

Method: 将结构错误指定刻画为受扰动的因子分析问题；在谱分离与受控扰动条件下建立子空间可识别性，并在结构稀疏性下建立逐分量可识别性；当精确条件不满足时，推导近似子空间可识别性定理；基于上述结果设计无监督变分估计器来恢复隐变量。

Result: 得到了精确的子空间与逐分量可识别性保证，以及近似子空间可识别性定理；实验验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 在条件分解不成立的更一般错误指定结构下，仍可获得精确与近似的可识别性保证，并据此构建有效的无监督变分估计方法。

Abstract: The presence of noise that depends on the latent variables poses a fundamental challenge to identifiability. Existing results rely on conditional independence among the observations given the latent variables. We study a more general \emph{misspecified structure}, where this conditional factorization does not hold, and establish both precise and approximate identifiability guarantees. We characterize structural misspecification as a perturbed factor analysis problem. For precise identifiability, we establish subspace identifiability under spectral separation and controlled perturbation, followed by component-wise identifiability under structural sparsity. When the precise condition is not guaranteed, we derive an approximate subspace-identifiability theorem. Based on these results, we develop an unsupervised variational estimator for recovering latent variables. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [395] [Domain Generalization under Sampling Pattern Shifts in Irregular Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33279)
*Changhun Kim,Joohyung Lee,Kwanhyung Lee,Donghwee Yoon,Grigorios Chrysos,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对不规则采样多元时间序列(ISMTS)中采样模式偏移问题，提出首个受控基准 HAR-C，并揭示采样偏移会导致性能显著下降与捷径依赖；进而提出域泛化框架 PRISM，通过无标签学习特征中心与采样中心互补表示，再进行鲁棒监督训练，在受控与真实基准上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则采样多元时间序列广泛存在于现实应用中，观测时间与可用测量在不同领域差异显著。现有模型虽利用采样信息进行预测，但在采样模式偏移下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。作者希望系统地评估并解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出 HAR-C：首个针对 ISMTS 采样模式偏移的受控基准。2) 提出 PRISM 域泛化框架：先在没有任务标签的情况下学习互补的"特征中心"与"采样中心"表示，再在多样采样变化下进行鲁棒监督训练，以抑制脆弱的捷径依赖。

Result: 实验表明：仅采样偏移即可显著降低性能，诱发采样相关的捷径，且现有域泛化方法难以应对。PRISM 在受控与真实 ISMTS 基准上均能持续提升对未见采样偏移的鲁棒性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 采样模式偏移是不规则采样多元时间序列中一个被忽视但关键的问题；通过互补表示学习与鲁棒监督训练，PRISM 能有效提升对该偏移的泛化能力，为 ISMTS 域泛化研究提供了基准与解决方案。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate time series (ISMTS) are prevalent in real-world applications, where both observation times and available measurements can vary substantially across domains. While recent models increasingly exploit such sampling information for prediction, its robustness under sampling pattern shifts remains underexplored. We introduce HAR-C, to the best of our knowledge the first controlled benchmark for sampling pattern shifts in ISMTS, and show that sampling shifts alone can substantially degrade performance, induce sampling-specific shortcuts, and remain challenging for existing domain generalization (DG) methods. Motivated by these findings, we propose PRISM, a DG framework that first learns complementary feature-centric and sampling-centric representations without task labels, and subsequently performs robust supervised training across diverse sampling variations to discourage brittle shortcut reliance. Extensive experiments on controlled and real-world ISMTS benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently improves robustness to unseen sampling shifts over existing methods. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PRISM.

</details>


### [396] [Direct Hidden-State Alignment: Mapping and Controlling Preference Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33298)
*Fansheng Zhang,Shengran Guo,Zexiao Wang,Liang Yuan,Jiyuan Chen,Ruikun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差竞争图（RCM）来刻画偏好行为在模型原生计算中的表征，并在此基础上提出直接隐状态对齐（DHSA）与RCM引导的因果激活状态转移（CAST），以极少参数实现推理期可控的偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多场景下后训练并不需要从零构建目标行为，基座模型其实已具备该能力，只是不能稳定表达。因此偏好对齐的部分问题从"能力获取"转变为"行为表达"。作者希望弄清：特定偏好在模型原生计算中如何表征、是什么阻碍了支持目标的计算稳定主导生成、以及这种结构能否直接指导控制。

Method: 1）提出残差竞争图（RCM），将某一行为偏好映射为原生残差计算的有符号因果效应；2）基于RCM分析揭示支持目标与竞争目标的效应共存、组件角色依赖输入、以及单一原生组件干预即可逆转偏好结果的现象；3）提出直接隐状态对齐（DHSA），把推理期隐状态而非基座权重作为直接适配空间；4）用RCM引导的因果激活状态转移（CAST）在少量偏好相关接口处做局部状态干预，同时冻结基座模型，控制器参数仅256–16,384个。

Result: RCM在多个偏好域中揭示了共存的靶向支持与竞争效应、输入依赖的组件角色，以及单组件干预即可翻转偏好结果的情形；DPO会大幅重组这些效应，可削弱对抗性但不保证其消除。CAST仅用256–16,384个控制器参数，在三个偏好域上达到与DPO相当的操作点，可与DPO训练模型互补，并能在推理时启用或移除。

Conclusion: 偏好对齐可视为对已有能力的表达调控而非全新能力习得。RCM提供了理解原生计算中偏好表征的工具，DHSA/CAST则表明以推理期隐状态为适配空间、以极少参数做局部状态干预，即可实现可控、可插拔且与DPO竞争的对齐效果。

Abstract: In many settings, post-training need not create the target behavior from scratch: the base model can already produce it, but not reliably. This shifts part of preference alignment from capability acquisition to behavioral expression. We ask how a specified preference is represented in native model computation, what prevents target-supporting computation from reliably dominating generation, and whether this structure can directly guide control. We introduce Residual Competition Maps (RCMs), which map a behavioral preference onto signed causal effects of native residual computation. Across preference domains, RCMs reveal coexisting target-supporting and target-competing effects, input-dependent component roles, and cases where a single native-component intervention reverses the preference outcome. DPO substantially reorganizes these effects and can weaken opposition without guaranteeing its removal. We then propose Direct Hidden-State Alignment (DHSA), which treats inference-time hidden states rather than base-model weights as the direct adaptation space. RCM-guided Causal Activation State Transition (CAST) implements DHSA through local state interventions at a small number of preference-relevant interfaces while freezing the base model. With only 256-16,384 controller parameters, CAST reaches DPO-competitive operating points across three preference domains, can complement DPO-trained models, and can be enabled or removed at inference time.

</details>


### [397] [BITS: Rethinking Fair and Comprehensive Evaluation for Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33303)
*Kangjia Yan,Linfeng Wang,Tianen Shen,Xiangfei Qiu,Ruitong Zhang,Hao Miao,Jilin Hu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BITS，一个针对不规则时间序列预测的标准化、可复现、可扩展基准，涵盖十一个数据集、九个领域，并提供统一的数据预处理、模型集成与评估流程，支持误差与非误差指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不规则时间序列预测研究缺乏统一基准，评估多局限于少数数据集，实验协议不一致，且以误差指标为主，难以公平、全面地比较不同方法。为此需要构建标准化基准以加速研究进展。

Method: BITS 覆盖九个领域共十一个数据集，依据缺失率、缺失模式复杂度、采样不规则性和偏度刻画数据特征；提供统一的数据预处理、模型集成、评估与报告流程；在一致设置下支持规则与不规则时间序列预测方法（含时间序列基础模型），并同时纳入误差类与非误差类评估指标。

Result: 实验发现方法性能在不同不规则特征下差异显著，没有单一建模策略能持续占优；使用误差指标与非误差指标会得到不同的模型排名，说明需要多维度评估。

Conclusion: BITS 为不规则时间序列预测提供了标准化、可复现且可扩展的评测平台，揭示了单一指标评估的局限，强调了多维度评估的必要性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in irregular time series forecasting, the field still lacks a unified benchmark for fair and comprehensive evaluation. Existing evaluations are often conducted on a limited set of datasets with inconsistent experimental protocols and predominantly error-based metrics, rendering it difficult to compare and assess methods fairly and comprehensively across diverse settings. To eliminate these limitations and accelerate progress, we propose BITS, a standardized, reproducible, and extensible benchmark for advancing research on irregular time series forecasting. BITS covers eleven datasets from nine domains with diverse irregularity characteristics, and it characterizes the datasets according to their missing rate, missing pattern complexity, sampling irregularity, and skewness. Further, it offers a unified pipeline for data preprocessing, model integration and evaluation, and reporting. It accommodates regular and irregular time series forecasting methods, including time series foundation models, under consistent settings, incorporating both error-based and non-error-based evaluation metrics. Findings include that method performance varies substantially across irregularity characteristics, with no single modeling strategy consistently dominating. We also find that using error-based or non-error-based metrics can yield different model rankings, highlighting the need for multi-dimensional evaluation. The code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BITS-8F2E/.

</details>


### [398] [ZeroGAR: Benchmarking the Adversarial Robustness of Zero-Shot Graph Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33314)
*Zhongjian Zhang,Xiao Wang,Busheng Zhang,Bo Yan,Xingtong Yu,Yue Gao,Jia Li,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZeroGAR，首个系统评估零样本图模型（ZGM）对抗鲁棒性的基准，揭示干净性能强不代表鲁棒，并总结三大关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本图模型多在干净图上评估，而图鲁棒性基准主要针对监督设置，因此"当未见目标图遭遇对抗操纵时 ZGM 是否鲁棒"这一根本问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 ZeroGAR 基准：评估来自 3 种范式的 13 个代表性 ZGM，覆盖 8 个数据集、4 个领域，包含域内与跨域迁移，涵盖结构、文本和节点注入三类攻击及多种扰动预算；并考察现有图防御方法在零样本设置下的有效性。

Result: 实验发现干净零样本性能强并不能保证对抗鲁棒性，并得到三点关键结论：(1) 脆弱性模式与模型预测机制相关，GNN 类方法易受结构与节点注入攻击，LLM 类方法易受文本攻击；(2) 更强的 LLM 骨干带来结构—文本鲁棒性权衡；(3) 现有图防御方法无法稳定提升零样本鲁棒性，甚至可能损害干净性能。

Conclusion: ZeroGAR 可促进快速、公平的评估，并激发 ZGM 安全领域更多创新研究。

Abstract: Zero-shot graph models (ZGMs), which learn transferable knowledge from source graphs and directly apply to unseen target graphs without any adaptation, have achieved promising performance and attracted considerable attention. Despite their proliferation, existing ZGMs are predominantly evaluated on clean graphs, while existing graph robustness benchmarks mainly focus on supervised settings, leaving a fundamental question largely unexplored: How robust are ZGMs when their unseen target graphs are exposed to adversarial manipulation? In this paper, we answer this question by proposing ZeroGAR, the first systematic benchmark for evaluating the adversarial robustness of ZGMs. ZeroGAR evaluates 13 representative ZGMs from 3 different paradigms on 8 graph datasets across 4 domains, covering both in-domain and cross-domain transfer under structural, textual, and node injection attacks with multiple perturbation budgets. It further investigates whether existing graph defenses remain effective in the zero-shot setting. Extensive experiments reveal that strong clean zero-shot performance does not guarantee adversarial robustness, with three key findings: (1) Vulnerability patterns are related to model prediction mechanisms: GNN-based methods are particularly vulnerable to structural and node injection attacks, whereas LLM-based methods are more vulnerable to textual attacks; (2) Stronger LLM backbones introduce a structure-text robustness trade-off; (3) Existing graph defense methods do not consistently improve zero-shot robustness and may compromise clean performance. We hope that ZeroGAR will facilitate rapid, equitable evaluation and inspire further innovative research in ZGM security.

</details>


### [399] [When to Evict, Not What to Keep: Draft-Guided Eviction for Training-Free KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33334)
*Haeyong Kang,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出免训练KV缓存压缩方法在预填充结束即逐出token存在两类失败，并提出将逐出延后到生成前k=2个草稿token之后的DGE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SnapKV、H2O、PyramidKV等免训练KV缓存压缩方法在预填充结束时逐出token，目标是保留未来查询预期使用的注意力质量，即优化“保留什么”。作者表明该目标在两方面失败：(1)补偿性——恢复被逐出的注意力质量可恢复注意力层面目标但无法恢复任务质量；(2)选择性——当恢复的质量是碎片化而非集中于连贯片段时，覆盖更多真实解码查询质量反而损害质量。这些失败的共同原因是逐出发生在决定答案轨迹的查询出现之前。

Method: 提出Draft-Guided Eviction (DGE)，使用完整缓存草拟前k=2个答案token后再执行逐出，仅比预填充多一个解码步骤。由于草稿由答案自身前缀生成，在该轨迹信号可用前不丢弃任何缓存条目。每头缓存预算不变，DGE可直接应用于SnapKV、PyramidKV、H2O和StreamingLLM而无需修改其逐出分数。与额外前向传播方法不同，DGE改变的是逐出发生的时机而非所选缓存条目。

Result: 在六个 instruct 微调主干中的五个上，DGE 在每个评估预算下均优于先前方法，在 LongBench 上达到 44.2，接近 FullKV 的 44.3。仅改变时机的对照方法 DGE-W 取得相同分数，表明增益来自逐出发生的时机而非所选内容。

Conclusion: 增益来源于逐出时机而非选择内容，作者将这一效应称为轨迹锚定(trajectory anchoring)。

Abstract: Training-free KV-cache compression methods such as SnapKV, H2O, and PyramidKV evict tokens at the end of prefill, aiming to preserve the attention mass that future queries are expected to use -optimizing what to keep. We show that this objective fails in two distinct ways. (1) Compensation: restoring the evicted attention mass can recover the attention-level target without recovering task quality. (2) Selection: covering more of the true decode-query mass can hurt quality when the recovered mass is fragmented rather than concentrated in coherent spans. These failures share a common cause: eviction occurs before the queries that determine the answer trajectory exist. We propose Draft-Guided Eviction (DGE), which defers eviction until after drafting the first k=2 answer tokens using the full cache - just one decode step beyond prefill. Because the draft is generated from the answer's own prefix, no cache entries are discarded before this trajectory signal becomes available. The per-head cache budget remains unchanged, and DGE can be applied directly to SnapKV, PyramidKV, H2O, and StreamingLLM without modifying their eviction scores. Unlike extra-pass methods, DGE changes when eviction occurs rather than what cache entries are selected. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGE outperforms prior methods at every evaluated budget on five of six instruct-tuned backbones, achieving 44.2 on LongBench, nearly matching FullKV at 44.3. The timing-only control DGE-W achieves the same score, demonstrating that the gain comes from when eviction occurs rather than what is selected - an effect we term trajectory anchoring.

</details>


### [400] [Beyond Conservatism: Recoverability-Conditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33336)
*Xuanlin Chen,Ziyue Wang,Xunlan Zhou,Yuan-yih Shang,Qiang Wu,Shenghua Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RECON 方法，通过可恢复性条件化的探索与保守策略学习分离，提升基于模型的模仿学习的交互效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的模仿学习（MBIL）虽能通过在世界模型生成的想象轨迹上优化策略来提升真实环境交互效率，但模型诱导分布与真实环境占用之间的差距使策略学习对模型误差敏感。保守 MBIL 能缓解策略优化中的模型利用问题，但当真实交互由同一保守策略采集时，专家分布周围的不确定区域采样不足；而通用不确定性驱动探索又可能把交互分配到新颖但与任务无关的动态上。

Method: RECON 将保守策略学习与主动数据采集解耦：维护一个用于任务执行的主策略和一个用于真实环境交互的探索者。探索者基于认知不确定性进行优化，并以从多步主策略想象中估计的可恢复性为条件，从而将数据采集聚焦于主策略仍能返回专家行为的未知状态。

Result: 在运动、导航和操作任务上的实验显示，交互效率、模仿性能和鲁棒性均获得一致性提升。

Conclusion: RECON 将真实环境交互导向专家分布周围先前方法探索不足的可恢复区域，从而学习到更适合模仿的世界模型。

Abstract: Model-based imitation learning (MBIL) improves real-environment interaction efficiency by optimizing policies on imagined rollouts from a learned world model. However, the gap between model-induced and real-environment occupancies makes policy learning sensitive to model error. Conservative MBIL mitigates model exploitation during policy optimization, but when real-environment interactions are collected by the same conservative policy, uncertain regions around the expert distribution remain insufficiently sampled. Generic uncertainty-driven exploration, on the other hand, may allocate interaction to novel but task-irrelevant dynamics. We propose REcoverability-CONditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning (RECON). RECON separates conservative policy learning from active data collection by maintaining a main policy for task execution and an explorer for real-environment interaction. The explorer is optimized based on epistemic uncertainty conditioned on recoverability estimated from multi-step main-policy imagination, focusing data collection on unknown states from which the main policy can still return toward expert behavior. Experiments on locomotion, navigation and manipulation show consistent gains in interaction efficiency, imitation performance, and robustness, indicating that RECON directs real-environment interaction toward recovery regions around the expert distribution that are underexplored by prior methods, and thereby learns a world model better suited for imitation.

</details>


### [401] [Safe Score Matching: Diffusion Policies with Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Online Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33337)
*Boyang Li,Matthew Kim,Sylvia Lee Herbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Safe Score Matching（SSM），将 Q-score matching 扩展到硬约束安全强化学习，通过 HJ 可达性门控双分支分数目标，在可行域内做奖励最大化、在域外引导恢复，在多个基准上取得更优性能与更低不安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线安全 RL 需要最大化奖励同时满足安全约束。现有方法存在局限：原始-对偶拉格朗日方法只施加"平均"安全约束，较为宽松；HJ 可达性方法虽能施加硬性的状态级约束，但可行域与不可行域的目标不同（前者奖励最大化，后者恢复至可行域），导致目标动作分布本质上是多峰的，而现有 HJ 安全 RL 使用的单峰或确定性 actor 难以表达这种分布，常坍缩到次优模态。

Method: 提出 SSM，一种离策略 actor-critic 方法。核心是用 HJ 可达性对双分支分数目标进行门控：在可行集内，去噪过程退化为对 HJ critic 判定为可行动作的 Q-score matching；在可行集外，恢复分支将去噪偏向最坏情况违反更低的区域。借助扩散策略的表达能力来建模多峰目标动作分布。

Result: 在四旋翼与固定翼轨迹跟踪、稳定与避障基准上，SSM 取得最佳或接近最佳的任务性能，且误安全率低；原始-对偶基线出现更多不安全行为，可达性基线偏保守。在 Safety-Gymnasium 速度任务上，SSM 取得最低成本且奖励具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SSM 通过将 Q-score matching 与 HJ 可达性结合，成功应对硬约束安全 RL 中的多峰动作分布难题，在保证安全的同时维持良好任务表现，优于现有基线的安全-性能权衡。

Abstract: Online safe reinforcement learning (RL) seeks policies that maximize reward while satisfying safety constraints. A popular line of research in safe RL relaxes safety to a soft expected-cost constraint and solves the resulting Constrained Markov Decision Process via primal-dual Lagrangian updates that only enforce safety on average. To address this limitation, hard, state-wise constraints are introduced and often imposed through Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability. Yet such constraints require solving different objectives in the feasible and infeasible regions: reward maximization in the former, recovery toward the feasible regions in the latter. The resulting target action distributions are inherently multimodal, and this structure poses a fundamental challenge for the Gaussian or deterministic actors used in existing HJ-based safe RL, which often collapse onto suboptimal modes. Diffusion policies provide the expressiveness needed to represent such distributions, and recent work on Q-score matching offers a route to training them for online RL by score regression -- but has been applied only to reward maximization. We propose Safe Score Matching (SSM), an off-policy actor-critic method that adapts Q-score matching to hard-constrained safe RL by gating a two-branch score target with HJ reachability: inside the feasible set, the denoising process degenerates to Q-score matching on actions classified as viable by the HJ critic; outside, a recovery branch biases denoising toward regions with lower worst-case violation. On quadrotor and fixed-wing trajectory-tracking and stabilize-and-avoid benchmarks, SSM attains the best or near-best task performance with low false-safe rates, whereas the primal-dual baseline admits more unsafe behavior and reachability-based baselines tend to be more conservative; on Safety-Gymnasium velocity tasks, SSM attains the lowest cost with competitive reward.

</details>


### [402] [RAEGL: Risk-Aware Evidence-Gated Learning for Selective Contextual Routing under Temporal Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33340)
*Yifan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEGL——一种风险感知的证据门控学习框架，用于选择性上下文预测，通过默认保留全局预测器、仅在部署前证据支持时才激活上下文残差，从而在时序分布偏移下管理上下文部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文专门化可提升预测精度，但在某一历史区间选出的校正项在时序分布偏移下可能变得不可靠。传统的基于验证集驱动的上下文选择容易带来有害的上下文部署，因此需要一个可审计、基于证据的机制来管理部署风险。

Method: RAEGL框架默认保留经校验的全局预测器，仅当部署前证据支持时才激活上下文残差；框架将候选选择与门控校准分离，并联合评估随机化显著性、实际有意义的增益以及时间稳定性；还提出了稳定性与支持感知的扩展版本。

Result: 在重建的Our World in Data审计中，精确回退避免了由两个验证集选出的校正项导致的RMSE恶化（0.0960和0.0239）；在封闭的世界发展指标评估中，基于区域的校正通过了随机化检验，但因增益仅0.000092、国家聚类95%置信区间跨零、仅0.02%的自助法重复达到实际阈值而被拒绝激活；在受控面板中，稳定性与支持感知扩展在97.2%的强稳定上下文运行中激活，同时拒绝了所有高漂移设置。

Conclusion: RAEGL可作为管理上下文部署风险的可审计、基于证据的机制，也是验证集驱动上下文选择的一种保守替代方案。

Abstract: Contextual specialization can improve forecasting accuracy, but a correction selected on one historical interval may become unreliable under temporal distribution shift. To address this issue, we propose RAEGL, a Risk-Aware Evidence-Gated Learning framework for selective contextual forecasting. RAEGL retains a validated global predictor by default and activates a contextual residual only when pre-deployment evidence supports its use. The framework separates candidate selection from gate calibration and jointly evaluates randomization significance, practically meaningful gain, and temporal stability. Experiments on real-world audits and controlled panels show how RAEGL can prevent harmful contextual deployment while making conservative opportunity costs explicit. In a reconstructed Our World in Data audit, exact fallback avoids RMSE degradations of 0.0960 and 0.0239 caused by two validation-selected corrections. In a sealed World Development Indicators evaluation, a region-based correction passes the randomization test but is withheld because its gain is only 0.000092, its country-clustered 95% confidence interval crosses zero, and only 0.02% of bootstrap replicates reach the practical threshold. In controlled panels, the stability- and support-aware extension activates in 97.2% of strong, stable-context runs while rejecting all high-drift settings. These results support RAEGL as an auditable, evidence-based mechanism for managing contextual deployment risk and as a conservative alternative to validation-driven contextual selection.

</details>


### [403] [CalibHyper: Chance-Corrected Relational Hypergraphs for Few-Shot Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33342)
*Linyu Li,Zhi Jin,Yuanpeng He,Dongming Jin,Huanyu Liu,Huanyao Zhang,Haoran Duan,Heng Tian,Gadeng Luosang,Nyima Tashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CalibHyper，一种基于联合标签分布的机会校正关系超图方法，用于分子性质少样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测在药物开发和材料发现中至关重要，但实验成本高、标签数据稀缺。现有的上下文感知方法虽利用辅助测定标签支持少样本预测，并通过标签一致性监督性质关系，但标签一致性对类别边缘分布敏感，且无法直接刻画性质间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出 CalibHyper 方法：从有序四态标签分布中减去独立性基线，并根据联合观测数量对残差进行收缩；使用交换等变的关系头估计这些残差，为每个分子选择辅助性质，并设定其超边消息的符号与权重。

Result: 在来自五个基准的十三个数据集上，于 1-shot 和 10-shot 设置下，CalibHyper 及其消融设置取得了与最强已报道结果相当的 ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 通过基于联合标签分布的机会校正与超图关系建模，CalibHyper 有效提升了少样本分子性质预测的性能，验证了直接刻画性质依赖关系的优势。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is central to drug development and materials discovery, but experiments are costly and labeled data are scarce. Context-aware methods use auxiliary assay labels to support few-shot prediction, and recent work supervises property relations with label agreement. However, label agreement is sensitive to class marginals and does not directly capture dependence between properties. We propose CalibHyper, a chance-corrected relational hypergraph method based on the joint label distribution. CalibHyper subtracts an independence baseline from the ordered four-state label distribution and shrinks the residual according to the number of joint observations. A swap-equivariant relation head estimates these residuals, which choose the auxiliary properties for each molecule and set the sign and weight of their hyperedge messages. On thirteen datasets from five benchmarks, in both 1-shot and 10-shot settings, CalibHyper and its ablation settings achieve ROC-AUC competitive with the strongest reported results.

</details>


### [404] [MultiEcho: An Experimental Science of Learned Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33347)
*Meng Zhu,Airui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiEcho框架，通过受控反事实干预估计世界模型的响应规律，区分响应可预测性、干预可读性与物理准确性，并在九个模拟物理系统与七个冻结模型配置上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型可被视为具有自身响应规律的实验系统，但现有研究常将响应可预测性与物理准确性混为一谈，缺乏在受控干预下界定规律适用范围并单独检验其物理对应性的框架。

Method: MultiEcho框架：通过受控反事实干预估计响应规律、界定适用范围并独立检验物理对应性；采用三参考估计器预测完整干预响应并恢复干预参数；估计器选择仅使用发现数据，冻结拟合在验证与确认情境下评估；设计事件窗口、可见性与相机干预，配对生成器配置，幅度扫描，精确复位材料实验，以及精确有限尺度恒等式与一阶余项界。

Result: 响应可在局部被描述，却在同一目标坐标下与物理效应匹配不佳；事件窗口、可见性与相机干预揭示条件适用性；更强引导的场景提示下配对生成器配置可读性下降；幅度扫描显示图像误差小而相对效应误差大；精确复位材料实验将可见响应成功与固定读出失败分离；精确有限尺度恒等式可解析奇偶响应误差，一阶余项界界定精化校准的收敛条件。

Conclusion: MultiEcho为独立于物理定律、并相对于物理定律研究学习到的世界规律提供了实验基础。

Abstract: World models can be studied as experimental systems with response laws of their own. We introduce MultiEcho, a framework for estimating these laws through controlled counterfactual interventions, delimiting their applicability, and separately testing their physical correspondence. Across nine simulated physical systems and seven frozen model configurations, three-reference estimators predict complete intervention responses and recover intervention parameters. Estimator selection uses discovery data only; frozen fits are evaluated on validation and confirmation contexts. The experiments distinguish response predictability, intervention readability and physical accuracy. Responses can be locally describable yet poorly match physical effects in the same target coordinates. Event-window, visibility and camera interventions reveal conditional applicability, and paired generator configurations show reduced readability under a scene prompt with stronger guidance. Magnitude sweeps expose small image errors alongside large relative effect errors. An exact-reset material experiment separates registered visible-response success from fixed-readout failure on material-dependent futures at matched positions and velocities. Exact finite-scale identities resolve odd and even response errors; first-order remainder bounds specify when refined calibration converges. MultiEcho provides an experimental basis for studying learned-world laws independently of, and in relation to, physical laws.

</details>


### [405] [KoopCell: Koopman-Based Generative Model for Learning Single-Cell Dynamics from Distribution Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33350)
*Wanfeng Lu,Yutong Zhang,Keyi Zhou,Chenxin Ge,Wei Lin,Qunxi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 KoopCell，一个基于 Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig 理论的统一生成框架，联合学习表征与线性潜动力学，用于从时间稀疏、未配对的分布快照中学习群体动力学，并扩展出 KoopCell-M 以建模分支动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从时间稀疏、未配对的分布快照中学习群体动力学是发育生物学的核心挑战。现有的神经微分方程和流匹配方法虽能插值观测快照，但难以外推到训练范围之外，且缺乏显式建模发育分支的机制。

Method: 基于 Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig 理论构建统一生成框架。利用弱连续性方程推导出从分布快照估计 Koopman 生成元的闭式最小二乘估计量，并在适当假设下建立收敛保证；进一步通过马尔可夫嵌入将非马尔可夫记忆引入潜 Koopman 动力学，得到 KoopCell-M 以建模分支动力学。

Result: 在合成系统和三个 scRNA-seq 数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够恢复 Koopman 谱、通过记忆建模分支，并可扩展到预测高维基因表达分布，在所评估方法中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: KoopCell 通过结合表征学习与线性潜动力学，为从稀疏快照中建模群体动力学提供了具有理论保证的统一框架，并借助记忆机制有效处理发育分支，具备良好的扩展性与预测能力。

Abstract: Learning population dynamics from temporally sparse, unpaired distribution snapshots is a fundamental challenge in developmental biology. Recent approaches based on neural differential equations and flow matching can interpolate between observed population snapshots, but may struggle to extrapolate beyond the training horizon and often lack an explicit mechanism for modeling developmental branching. We propose KoopCell, a unified generative framework based on Koopman-Mori-Zwanzig theory that jointly learns representations and predictive linear latent dynamics. Theoretically, using the weak continuity equation, we derive a closed-form least-squares estimator for the Koopman generator from distribution snapshots and establish convergence guarantees under suitable assumptions. To model branching dynamics, we further develop KoopCell-M, which incorporates non-Markovian memory into the latent Koopman dynamics through a Markovian embedding. Experiments on synthetic systems and three scRNA-seq datasets demonstrate the ability of our framework to recover Koopman spectra, model branching through memory, and scale to predicting high-dimensional gene expression distributions, achieving state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [406] [How Much Imprecision is Enough Imprecision in my Classifier? A Practical Elicitation Procedure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33352)
*Victor F. Lopes de Souza,Sébastien Destercke,Abdelhak Imoussaten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出实用的引导(elicitation)程序，用于度量用户对集合值预测(即接受不精确性)的偏好程度，并在表格与图像数据集上验证了迭代引导程序收敛到目标参数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合值分类器（源自精确概率+代价函数、凸集+鲁棒推断、或conformal方法）是获得更鲁棒、可信预测的常用手段，但目前缺乏操作性工具来度量用户在接收预测时愿意接受多大不精确性、愿意用多少精度换取多少准确性。

Method: 提出实用且可操作的引导程序(elicitation procedures)来度量用户对集合值预测的倾向，并采用迭代式引导过程使其收敛到目标参数值。

Result: 在标准机器学习基准的表格数据集与图像数据集上验证了迭代引导程序能收敛到目标参数值；同时该程序向用户展示的实例数量较少。

Conclusion: 该方法具有实用性，适用于识别决策者在面对不精确性时的最优行为。

Abstract: Set-valued classifiers, whether derived from precise probabilities and an adapted cost function, from convex sets with a robust inference mechanism, or from conformal methods, are routine options to obtain more robust, trustworthy predictions. However, there is a lack of operational tools to measure how robust or imprecise a given user is ready to be when receiving predictions, that is how much precision he/she is ready to let go in exchange of more accuracy. This is why we propose, in this paper, practical and operational elicitation procedures to measure the user proneness to set-valued predictions. The effectiveness of the iterative elicitation procedure in converging to the target parameter value is demonstrated on both tabular and image datasets drawn from standard machine learning benchmarks. The results show that the procedure also presents the user with a small number of instances, highlighting the practicality of the approach for real-world applications aimed at identifying the decision maker's optimal behavior when faced with imprecision.

</details>


### [407] [The Selection Rule Decides the Winner: A Pre-Registered Audit of Open-Set Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33370)
*Farhan Shahriyar Hossain,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Abrar Jahin,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过统一协议重跑DEMO、NSReg与自建OUTPOST，检验开放集图异常检测中三项报告惯例（最优epoch取分、基线数字直接沿用、异常多为小类重标注）对排名结论的实际决定作用，并报告40条预注册预测中12条被证伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集图异常检测的已发表结果普遍依赖三种惯例：在测试集上按最优epoch读取分数、基线数值从旧论文复制、以及大多数异常来自被重标注为异常的少数类。作者想知道这些惯例在多大程度上决定了所报告的排名，因此需要一次协议统一、种子与划分一致的受控重跑与预注册检验。

Method: 在八张图（基线方法因无法在ogbn-mag上运行而只用其中七张）上用完全相同的种子与划分，各跑十个种子，重跑DEMO、NSReg，并加入为该研究设计的轻量一阶检测器OUTPOST。每次运行同时按“最优epoch”规则与可部署的验证集规则评分；在检验这些惯例的运行之前预先注册40条预测；并检验0.002的超参选择平局带是否低于成对标准误。

Result: 第一，评分规则会改变领先者：最优epoch规则下OUTPOST与NSReg在七张图中各领先三张，验证规则下NSReg领先五张。第二，最优epoch带来的增益取决于基准构建方式：三张小规模重标注类图为0.045–0.080 AUC-ROC，三张真实欺诈图为0.002–0.014。第三，OUTPOST中的伪标签在同样三张图上贡献0.038–0.065 AUC-ROC，但在任何真实欺诈图上均无收益。此外，0.002的平局带在全部六张受测图上都低于成对标准误，即使使用十个种子时亦然。40条预测中有12条被证伪，作者一并报告。

Conclusion: 所报告排名在很大程度上由评分规则与基准构建惯例决定，而非仅由方法本身决定；伪标签类技巧的收益集中在重标注类图上、在真实欺诈图上消失；0.002的平局带不足以支撑超参选择。作者据此提出一份简短的报告清单，并要求公开被证伪的预注册预测。

Abstract: Open-set graph anomaly detection trains on a few labeled anomalies from one class and must also find anomaly classes that were never labeled. Published results share three conventions: the test score is read at the best epoch on the test set, baseline numbers are copied from earlier papers, and most anomalies are minority classes relabeled as anomalous. We ask how much of the reported ranking these conventions decide. We re-run two recent methods, DEMO and NSReg, together with OUTPOST, a small first-order detector built for this study. All three use one protocol with identical seeds and splits on eight graphs (seven for the baselines, which cannot run on ogbn-mag), ten seeds each, and every run is scored under both the best-epoch rule and a deployable validation rule. Before the runs that test them, we registered 40 predictions. Three findings hold. First, the rule changes the leader: under the best-epoch rule, OUTPOST and NSReg each lead three of seven graphs, while under the validation rule, NSReg leads five. Second, the best-epoch bonus depends on how the benchmark was built: 0.045--0.080 AUC-ROC on the three small relabeled-class graphs and 0.002--0.014 on the three real fraud graphs. Third, pseudo-labeling in OUTPOST is worth 0.038--0.065 AUC-ROC on the same three graphs but gives no benefit on any real fraud graph. We also show that a 0.002 tie band for hyperparameter selection lies below the paired standard error on all six graphs tested, even at ten seeds. Twelve of our 40 predictions were falsified, and we report them. We close with a short reporting checklist.

</details>


### [408] [TNF based Spectral Embedding for Effective Application of Supervised Machine Learning Techniques in Automobile Insurance Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33376)
*Rohan Yashraj Gupta,Lalith Srikanth Chintalapati,Satya Sai Mudigonda,Pallav Kumar Baruah,Raghunatha Sarma Rachakonda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对汽车保险欺诈检测，系统组合了不平衡处理、图谱嵌入与多种分类模型（共65种组合），发现MWMOTE + TNFSE2 + Random Forest的组合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险欺诈检测具有重要的财务影响，模型需在准确率、灵敏度、特异度、F1、AUC等多指标上表现良好。但欺诈数据存在类别不平衡问题，且需要合适的分类模型与低维表示方法，因此需要系统探索最优组合。

Method: 使用汽车保险数据集，考察决策树、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM、GBM等分类模型；采用MWMOTE和TGAN处理数据不平衡；使用基于拓扑节点特征（TNF）的谱嵌入以及MDS、Isomap、t-SNE等流行嵌入方法进行低维表示；共评估65种组合。

Result: 在所有65种组合中，MWMOTE（第一阶段不平衡处理）+ TNFSE2（第二阶段嵌入）+ 随机森林（第三阶段分类）取得最佳结果，优于其他所有组合，体现了TNF谱嵌入在汽车保险数据集上的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种创新的汽车保险欺诈检测方法，验证了TNF谱嵌入结合不平衡处理与随机森林的有效性，可为保险欺诈检测提供参考。

Abstract: Fraud detection is an important area of research in the insurance business due to its financial implications. The primary aim of a fraud detection model is to identify fraud and non-fraud cases with high accuracy along with other important metrics such as Sensitivity, Specificity, Precision, F1-score, False Positive Rate, False Discovery Rate, AUC etc. To achieve this, we need to explore a suitable classification model to identify fraud and non-fraud cases. In this work, we have used auto insurance data set and explored classification models such as Decision Tree (DT), Random Forest (RF), XGBoost, LightGBM and Gradient Boosting Machine (GBM). To overcome the problem of data imbalance, we have employed MWMOTE and TGAN techniques. We have used Topological Node Feature(TNF) based spectral embedding for low dimensional data representation along with some popular embedding methods like MDS, Isomaps and t-SNE. After studying all the 65 possible combinations of these models, we have proposed an innovative method for effective automobile insurance fraud detection. For the given dataset, our results show that using a combination of MWMOTE as a data imbalance handling technique (Phase I), TNFSE2 as data embedding (Phase II) and Random Forest as classification (Phase III) provides the best result in comparison to all other combinations. This work also highlights the efficacy of TNF based spectral embedding in automobile insurance dataset

</details>


### [409] [Optimal Transport Dropout for Structured Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33377)
*Giacomo Lorenzon,Francesco Regazzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Optimal Transport Dropout (OTD)，通过联合学习预测映射与潜在扰动的分布规律，构造结构化的预测不确定性，无需后验推断或集成多个独立网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性神经网络和神经算子只给出点预测，缺乏可靠性的度量。预测不确定性可能来自不可约的结果变异性、有限数据或模型类别的局限。蒙特卡洛 dropout 虽计算方便，但其扰动规律大多先验给定，且通常在潜在坐标上是因子化的，难以刻画结构化不确定性。

Method: OTD 从简单的独立参考分布出发，通过可学习的流（flow）对潜在扰动进行最优传输，再将其传播至预测神经网络，从而诱导出结构化的预测分布。训练采用严格 proper 的 Energy Score 作为目标，并加入动能作用项（kinetic-action term）对传输进行几何正则化。预测映射与扰动规律联合学习。

Result: 合成基准上，OTD 能捕获多模态预测分布，在模型误设时产生有意义的离散度，并随训练数据增多或模型容量增大而呈现收缩的离散度。在场值 PDE 代理任务中，预测离散度与预测误差的空间模式高度一致；相比蒙特卡洛 dropout，预测更准确、校准更好且区间显著更窄。在真实回归基准上，精度具竞争力，概率预测优于已有基线。

Conclusion: OTD 提供了一种在无需显式后验推断或独立训练预测器集成的情况下学习结构化预测不确定性的方法。

Abstract: Deterministic neural networks and neural operators provide point predictions with no intrinsic measure of reliability. Yet, predictive uncertainty may stem from irreducible outcome variability, finite data, or limitations of the chosen model class. Monte Carlo dropout offers a computationally convenient way to construct a predictive distribution through stochastic feature masking, without training multiple independent networks or explicitly inferring a posterior over model parameters. However, its perturbation law is largely prescribed a priori and typically factorised across latent coordinates. We introduce Optimal Transport Dropout (OTD), which instead learns the predictive mapping and the law of its latent perturbations jointly. Starting from a simple independent reference distribution, OTD transports latent perturbations through a learnable flow and propagates them through the predictive neural network, thereby inducing a structured predictive law. Training uses the strictly proper Energy Score, while a kinetic-action term geometrically regularises the transport. Synthetic benchmarks show that OTD captures multimodal predictive distributions, generates meaningful dispersion when the model is misspecified, and exhibits contracting dispersion as more training data or greater model capacity are provided. For a field-valued partial differential equation surrogate, predictive dispersion strongly aligns with the spatial pattern of prediction errors. On this task, compared with Monte Carlo dropout, OTD yields more accurate predictions and better-calibrated, substantially narrower intervals. On real-world regression benchmarks, it further shows competitive accuracy and better probabilistic predictions compared to established baselines. OTD therefore offers a way to learn structured predictive uncertainty without explicit posterior inference or ensembles of independently trained predictors.

</details>
