### [410] [From Grey-Box to Green-Box: When can Physics-Informed Machine Learning Reduce Carbon Footprints in Structural Health Monitoring?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33387)
*Daisy R. Bradley,Nathan A. Hinchliffe,Daniel J. Pitchforth,Matthew R. Jones,Elizabeth J. Cross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨物理信息机器学习（灰箱模型）是否具有降低环境影响的额外优势，在结构健康监测场景下比较了四种物理信息方法的训练碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在工程中应用日益广泛，但计算时间增加带来环境成本。物理信息机器学习或"灰箱"模型利用工程师对建模结构的物理洞察，克服传统黑箱学习器的一些局限，在结构工程等领域表现良好。本工作旨在探究其是否还具备减少环境影响的额外优势。

Method: 在结构健康监测背景下，评估四种物理信息机器学习方法（涵盖高斯过程与神经网络）：残差建模、输入增强、混合建模和约束学习。比较各模型训练至达到给定误差阈值时的碳排放，并考虑训练数据量与训练时间的关系，将训练时长与计算产生的碳排放挂钩。

Result: 在多数示例中，物理信息模型的训练碳排放低于传统模型（输入增强模型除外）。这种训练排放的降低进一步叠加了因采集和存储更少数据而带来的环境节省。

Conclusion: 物理信息机器学习在减少环境影响方面展现出潜力，但并非万能方案。案例研究表明，需要在引入物理知识所带来的复杂性增加与减少训练数据需求所获得的收益之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Machine learning plays an increasingly vital role in engineering, but the corresponding increase in compute time is not without environmental cost. Physics-informed machine learning or "grey-box" models have been developed to overcome some of the limitations of traditional black-box learners, utilising the physical insight that an engineer would have about the structure they are modelling and have shown promising results in the structural engineering field among many others. This work explores whether an additional advantage could be a reduced environmental impact, considering the relationship between training data quantity and training time, linking this duration to carbon emissions from computing.
  In a structural health monitoring context, four physics-informed machine learning approaches - spanning Gaussian processes and neural networks - are evaluated: residual modelling, input augmentation, hybrid modelling, and constrained learning. The emissions for training each of the models to reach a given error threshold is compared, and in most examples, shown to be lower for the physics-informed models (with input augmented models being an exception). This reduction in training emissions further compounds the environmental savings achieved by collecting and storing less data. Although promising results, we cannot expect a silver bullet and the case studies demonstrate that a trade-off is needed between the increased complexity that comes from introducing physics into a machine learner, against the gain from reduced training data requirements.

</details>


### [411] [Beyond Timestamps: Decision-Aligned On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33391)
*Mingju Chen,Can Lv,Jinrong Liu,Huan Zhang,Heng Chang,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出AlignOPSD方法，通过决策对齐监督纠正与半马尔可夫分层信用分配，解决RLVR中on-policy自蒸馏的"决策—时间戳错配"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR依赖稀疏结果奖励，对长时程智能体的监督过于粗糙；on-policy自蒸馏虽提供稠密特权反馈，但存在决策—时间戳错配：特权指导可能与学生功能决策错位，因为决策可能发生在不同时间步，且学生决策本身可跨多个时间步，导致时间戳局部的监督使上下文与信用时间范围双重错位。

Method: 提出AlignOPSD，遵循"先对齐监督、再分配信用"的原则。包含两部分：(1) 决策对齐监督纠正（Decision-Aligned Supervision Rectification），在兄弟rollout中功能匹配的上下文里对同一学生采样响应重新打分，以校准局部教师证据；(2) 半马尔可夫分层信用分配（Semi-Markov Hierarchical Credit Assignment），从对应关系变化中推导变时长决策跨度，并利用纠正后的证据在跨度及其组成轮次上分配基于结果的信用。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上，用Qwen2.5-3B与Qwen2.5-7B对代表性基线进行评估。AlignOPSD在所有八项骨干—聚合指标对比中均优于GRPO和StepOPSD，相比GRPO提升5.5–8.7%，并在六项中排名第一。附加分析考察了两个对齐阶段及跨任务的超参数敏感性。

Conclusion: AlignOPSD有效解决了on-policy自蒸馏中的决策—时间戳错配问题，在多个任务与模型规模上稳定超越GRPO与StepOPSD，验证了"先对齐监督、再分配信用"原则的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often relies on sparse outcome rewards, providing coarse supervision for long-horizon agents. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) complements this signal with dense privileged feedback. However, we identify \emph{Decision--Timestamp Mismatch}: privileged guidance may be misaligned with the student's functional decision because the corresponding decision can occur at a different timestep, while the student's decision itself may span multiple timesteps rather than being tied to a single timestamp. Thus, timestamp-local supervision can misalign both the context and the temporal scope of credit. To address this mismatch, we introduce \textsc{AlignOPSD}, following the principle of aligning supervision before assigning credit. Decision-Aligned Supervision Rectification re-scores the same student-sampled response in functionally matched contexts across sibling rollouts to calibrate local teacher evidence. Semi-Markov Hierarchical Credit Assignment then derives variable-duration decision spans from correspondence changes and uses rectified evidence to allocate outcome-grounded credit across spans and their constituent turns. We evaluate \textsc{AlignOPSD} with Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-7B on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA against representative baselines. \textsc{AlignOPSD} outperforms both GRPO and StepOPSD across all eight backbone--aggregate-metric comparisons, improving on GRPO by 5.5--8.7 \% and ranking first in six. Additional analyzes examine the two alignment stages and hyperparameter sensitivity between tasks. Our code is avaliable at https://github.com/mingju-c/Align-OPSD

</details>


### [412] [AutoHGNN: Robust and Efficient Neural Architecture Search for Hypergraph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33392)
*Sirui Li,Pietro Liò b,Xinsheng Li,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoHGNN，一个面向超图神经网络的神经架构搜索框架，通过引入超交互模块（HIM）和超图稳定拓扑距离（HyperSTD）准则，自动发现更优的超图网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络虽取得显著成功，但人工设计架构耗时耗力，且难以捕捉复杂的高阶关系；同时传统图神经网络设计范式与超图数据存在不匹配问题，因此自动化超图网络结构设计至关重要。

Method: 提出 AutoHGNN 搜索框架：一是在搜索空间中引入超交互模块（HIM），以缓解传统图神经网络设计与超图数据之间的不匹配；二是提出超图稳定拓扑距离（HyperSTD）作为结构选择准则，在可微搜索过程中识别最能保留原超图内在结构亲和性的架构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AutoHGNN 在分类准确率和时间效率上一致优于人工设计和自动搜索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明所发现的架构显著更有效，验证了 AutoHGNN 在提升超图学习的自动化与适应性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks have achieved significant success in recent years. However, manual architecture crafting is labor-intensive and often fails to capture complex, higher-order relations, making the automation of hypergraph neural network structure design crucial. To improve the automation and adaptability of hypergraph learning, this paper proposes AutoHGNN, a neural architecture search framework tailored for hypergraph neural networks. First, we introduce a Hyper-Interaction Module (HIM) into the search space to address the mismatch between conventional graph neural network designs and hypergraph data. Second, we propose Hypergraph Stable Topological Distance (HyperSTD) as a structural selection criterion to identify architectures that best preserve the intrinsic structural affinities of the original hypergraph during differentiable search. Extensive experiments on various benchmark datasets demonstrate that AutoHGNN consistently outperforms manually designed and automatically searched baselines in classification accuracy and time efficiency, proving that the discovered architectures are significantly more effective.

</details>


### [413] [Decoupling Token Roles in Autoregressive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33405)
*Suqin Yuan,Runqi Lin,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Junchi Yu,Lei Feng,Chris Russell,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控破坏实验解耦了token的两种角色（预测目标与上下文），发现二者存在反转关系：降低噪声token的可预测性会减少其作为目标的损害，却增加其作为上下文的损害。这一解耦还解释了语言模型生成文本的特性，并表明仅移除token自身损失不足以控制损害，需要解耦其角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归预训练日益依赖异构数据，因此理解模型如何从单个token学习变得很重要。现有方法用token自身的损失来定义其贡献，但忽略了token不仅是预测目标，也是后续内容的上下文。作者希望揭示这两种角色的区别及其影响。

Method: 采用受控破坏（controlled corruption）方法，将token作为预测目标（target）和作为上下文（context）两种角色进行解耦；并通过分析语言模型生成的文本，检验生成过程中token选择机制与上下文角色的差异。

Result: 发现反转现象：使噪声token更易预测会降低其作为目标的损害，但增加其作为上下文的损害。在已知的破坏位置上，通过上下文途径作用可以减少损害，而仅移除token自身损失无法做到。生成文本中，每个token依据其与前缀的契合度被选择，但其作为上下文的作用从未经过独立确定续写的检验。

Conclusion: 理解和控制模型从token中学习的内容，必须解耦token的预测目标角色与上下文角色，二者的贡献方向可能相反，且仅关注token自身损失不足以全面刻画其影响。

Abstract: Autoregressive pretraining increasingly draws on heterogeneous data, making it important to understand how a model learns from an individual token. The next-token prediction objective naturally identifies a token's contribution with its own loss. However, each token is not only a prediction target but also context for what follows. Using controlled corruption, we decouple these two roles and find a reversal: making a noisy token easier to predict reduces its damage as a target but increases it as context. The same decoupling helps explain text generated by language models: generation selects each token by its fit to the prefix, while its role as context is never tested against an independently determined continuation, because that continuation is generated to fit it. At known corrupted positions, acting through the context can reduce damage that removing the token's own loss does not. Understanding and controlling what a model learns from a token therefore requires decoupling its roles.

</details>


### [414] [StarBOA: Real-Time Mamba State-Space Unrolling for Sparse Radar Micro-Doppler in ISAC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33408)
*Mustafa Bora Çelik,Ceren Çelik,Orhan Gazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StarBOA，用因果Mamba状态空间模型替代注意力机制，在无需回扫历史重建的情况下实现超稀疏chirp子采样下的高效因果雷达重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC场景中雷达感知需在最多90%数据缺失的chirp子采样下工作。基于注意力的基线受限于52ms缓冲区，随稀疏度增加会崩塌至最大均匀熵（H=2.584 bits），无法捕捉长程步态周期上下文。

Method: StarBOA以因果Mamba状态空间模型替换注意力机制，按窗口增量更新，无需重新扫描过去的重建结果；通过维护持久状态，在不增加每步计算成本的前提下整合超过100倍的时间历史。

Result: 在所有稀疏度水平上均优于基线已发表结果，SSIM增益从50%缺失时的+0.0379提升至90%缺失时的+0.2472；每个窗口处理仅需1.53ms，零前瞻。

Conclusion: StarBOA证明在极端chirp子采样条件下可实现高效、因果的重建，突破注意力基线的历史长度限制。

Abstract: In Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC), radar sensing must operate under chirp subsampling with up to 90\% missing data. An attention-based baseline, limited to a 52~ms buffer, collapses toward maximum uniform entropy ($H=2.584$ bits) as sparsity increases, failing to capture long-range gait-cycle context. We propose StarBOA, which replaces attention with a causal Mamba state-space model that updates incrementally on a per-window basis without re-scanning past reconstructions. By maintaining a persistent state, StarBOA integrates over $100\times$ more temporal history at no additional per-step computational cost. StarBOA outperforms the baseline's published results across all sparsity levels, with SSIM gains increasing from $+0.0379$ at 50\% missing data to $+0.2472$ at 90\%. Each window is processed in 1.53~ms with zero lookahead, demonstrating efficient causal reconstruction under extreme chirp subsampling.

</details>


### [415] [Investigating the Effect of k-NN Preprocessing on Developing Graph Neural Networks: A Fairness-Based Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33416)
*Nikolaos Zafeiropoulos,Emmanouil Mavrikos,George E. Tsekouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种公平图卷积神经网络(GCN)设计方法，通过k-NN构图并将均等机会准则嵌入优化目标，研究邻域参数k对分类性能与公平性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN已广泛用于图数据学习，但公平性问题尚未充分解决。图构建过程（如k-NN中的k值）与公平性约束之间如何相互影响缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用基于k-最近邻的预处理方式构建输入图；以均等机会(equalized odds)作为公平性准则，通过额外公平性损失项将其直接嵌入模型优化目标；系统变化k值，在三个真实表格数据集上考察图结构变化与公平性约束的交互作用。

Result: k值对性能趋势起关键作用，随数据集特性表现为持续提升或在中段取值达到峰值；引入公平性约束显著缩小了按受保护变量划分的各组在假阳性率与假阴性率上的差异，且整体准确率未出现重大损失。

Conclusion: 研究强调在图卷积学习中联合优化图构建过程与公平性目标的重要性，为构建更公平有效的图模型提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: In this paper, a methodology to design fair graph convolutional neural networks (GCNs) is developed and tested over several application data sets. The graphs that are used as inputs to the network are constructed by a k-nearest neighbor-based preprocessing procedure, while fairness issues are considered in terms of the equalized odds criterion. To effectively incorporate the above heterogenous information, the equalized odds criterion is directly embedded into the model's optimization objective through an additional fairness-driven loss functional term. The proposed methodology investigates how varying the neighborhood size in the k-NN algorithm during graph construction influences both the classification performance and the fairness of the resulting models. Extensive experimentation is conducted on three real-world tabular datasets with known biases, evaluating the interplay between graph structure and fairness enforcement. The results demonstrate that the choice of the value of the parameter k critically impacts the performance trends, either steadily improving or peaking at intermediate values depending on dataset characteristics, while the application of fairness constraints significantly mitigates disparities in false positive and false negative rates across groups defined by the protected variable at hand, without incurring major sacrifices in overall accuracy. This study highlights the importance of jointly optimizing the graph construction process and fairness objectives in GCN-based learning, providing a systematic approach toward building more equitable and effective graph-based models.

</details>


### [416] [A Light Bilevel Refinement Aligns Self-Supervised Representations for Stronger Task-Specific Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33424)
*Gustav Wagner Zakarias,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSSLight 通过 M-FAC 隐式梯度近似与 LoRA 参数高效微调改造 BiSSL 双层优化框架，使自监督预训练与下游任务对齐更高效可扩展，计算时间较前代减少十倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练学到的表征虽具广泛迁移性，但直接微调可能因自监督目标与下游任务目标错位而次优，甚至破坏对下游有用的预训练特征。BiSSL 通过双层优化引入过渡训练阶段来缓解该问题，但其依赖传统双层优化求解，隐式超梯度近似代价高昂，难以适用于现代模型架构。

Method: 提出 BiSSLight：将基于 M-FAC 的隐式梯度近似与 LoRA 参数高效微调相结合，替代 BiSSL 中昂贵的隐式超梯度近似，从而实现更大规模下的高效应用。

Result: 在多种下游任务与现代模型架构上的评估表明，BiSSLight 持续提升下游性能；随模型规模增大，尽管基线更强，其增益仍更明显。在 ViT-H 主干上，计算时间较前代减少十倍以上，计算效率显著提升。

Conclusion: BiSSLight 使双层优化的预训练-微调对齐方法变得高效且可扩展，在保持性能增益的同时大幅降低计算成本，为大规模自监督预训练的表征精炼提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining learns representations that are broadly transferable across downstream tasks, yet direct fine-tuning can be suboptimal due to misalignment between self-supervised and downstream task objectives, potentially degrading pretrained features beneficial to the downstream task. The BiSSL framework addressed this by introducing a transitional training stage formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the downstream task objective guides the self-supervised learning process in refining pretrained representations to better facilitate subsequent fine-tuning. However, BiSSL relies on conventional bilevel optimization solving techniques whose costly implicit hypergradient approximations render the method increasingly impractical for contemporary model architectures. To make it efficient and scalable, we introduce BiSSLight, which combines M-FAC-based implicit gradient approximation with parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA, enabling efficient application at larger scales that were previously impractical. Evaluation across multiple downstream tasks and contemporary model architectures shows that BiSSLight consistently improves downstream performance, with gains becoming more pronounced as model size increases despite stronger baselines. The method is highly computationally efficient, reducing computation time by more than a factor of ten compared to its predecessor on a ViT-H backbone.

</details>
